patippuri
tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟi  eṉum innūl tamiḻaṟiñar 
na. cuppureṭṭiyār avarkaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭu 1957 il mutal patippākavum, 1964 il tiruttam ceyyappaṭṭu iraṇṭām patippākavum veḷi vantuḷḷatu. itaṉi empatippakattiṉ vāyilāka veḷiyiṭa muṉvantuḷḷōm.
tāymoḻivaḻik kalviyiṉ mēṉmi kuṟittum ataṉ payaṉpāṭu kuṟittum, vaḷarccik kuṟittum tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷum, araciya lāḷarkaḷum atikam pēci varum kālamitu. inta nērattil innūl veḷivaruvatu mika poruttamāka irukkum eṉṟu karutukiṟōm.
tamiḻmoḻiyip payiṟṟum nōkkaṅkaḷ  toṭaṅki, muṭivāka kalvittuṟi aḷaviyal eṉṉum 16 talippukaḷi uḷḷuṟiyākak koṇṭu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. innūliluḷḷa ceytikaḷ kalviyāḷar kaḷukkum, māṇavarkaḷukkum peritum payaṉpaṭattakkavi.
innūliṉ arumiperumikaḷi  mēṉāḷ kalviyamiccar aviṉācaliṅkam  avarkaḷiṉ aṇinturiyilum, āṅkila muṉṉuri yil  tiruvēṅkaṭaccāri avarkaḷum, nūlmukam talippil in nūlāciriyariṉ kuṟippukaḷum innūlip paṭikkum muṉ  aṉivarum paṭikka vēṇṭiya ceytikaḷākum. tāymoḻivaḻikkalviyiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ kuṟittum iṉṟiyamiyāmi kuṟittum pēcappaṭum ikkāla
kaṭṭattil innūli veḷiyiṭuvatik kaṭamiyāka koḷkiṟōm.

eṉ aṉṉikku
aṉpup paṭiyal

	naṟṟavat tāyē! vāḻi! pallāṇṭu
		nalaṉuṟap peṟṟeṉi vaḷarttuk
	kaṟṟava ṉākkip pēṇiṉi ! ulakiṟ
		kāṇuṟu poruḷelām koṇarntuṉ 
	ciṟṟaṭip puṟattil vippiṉum aviniṉ 
		cīraruṭ koppeṉa lāmō? 
	maṟṟiti yuṇarvēṉ ! eṉiṉumin nūlniṉ 
	malarppatat turimicey taṉeṉē.

ceṉṉi  mānila muṉṉāḷ kalviyamiccar
uyar tiru. ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam avarkaḷ
maṉamuvantu aḷitta 

aṇinturi
oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ mēṉmikku aṟikuṟiyāka iruppatu avarkaḷ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappu, piṟa moḻikaḷiṉ mūlam aṟivu peṟalām. āṉāl maṉap paṇpum cīriya cintiyum tam conta moḻiyi āḻntu payiluvataṉāltāṉ peṟa muṭiyum. eṉavē, nam nāṭu putu vāḻvu peṟṟip peru vāḻvi nōkkic cellum ikkālattil namatu moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcciyippaṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ niṉippatil āccariyam illi.
moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci eṉpatu veṟum vāyppēccāl maṭṭilum uṇṭāvatilli. ataṟkāka moḻiyiṉpāl pakti tūṇṭappeṟṟu māḻiyiṉ cēviyē payaṉum paṇpum eṉakkaruti uḻippavarkaḷiṉ muyaṟciyiṉāltāṉ aḵtu uṇṭākum. ellāt tuṟikaḷilum nalla tamiḻp puttakaṅkaḷ veḷivara vēṇṭum. entat tamiḻaṉum tamiḻilēyē paṭittu ettakiya uyarnta ñāṉattiyum peṟa vēṇṭum. tamiḻil varum puttakaṅkaḷ ārampattil moḻi peyarppāka amiyalām. āṉāl, moḻi peyarppu nūlkaḷ mutaltaramāṉaviyāka irukka muṭiyātu. mutaltaramāṉa puttakaṅkaḷāka irukak vēṇṭumāṉāl, makkaḷ piṟatuṟi aṟivit tāṅkaḷē peṟṟu avvaṟivuṭaṉ oṉṟi ataṉ piṉṉar avvaṟivu avarkaḷiṉ conta aṉupavamāy veḷivara vēṇṭum. ittakiya puttakaṅkaḷ ellā aṟivut tuṟikaḷilum ippoḻutu mikamikat tēvi.
in nōkkaṅkaḷi niṟivēṟṟuvataṟkākap palavitamāṉa muyaṟcikaḷ ippoḻutu mēṟkoḷḷappeṟṟu varuvatu mikavum makiḻcci tarakkūṭiyatu. tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam veḷiyiṭum kalikkaḷañciyam  ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. kalikkatir pōṉṟa māta veḷiyīṭukaḷum piṟa pattirikikaḷum aṟivut tuṟiyil kaṭṭurikaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi veḷiyiṭuvatu maṟṟoṉṟu. ivi tavira, pala tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ peṟṟa ñāṉatti eḷiya tamiḻil, aṉivarum uṇarattakka muṟiyil, eḻuti varuvatum mikavum nampikki tarakkūṭiya nilimiyākum.
kalvit tuṟiyil pāṭa pōtaṉiyiṉ taram uyara vēṇṭu meṉpatu aṉivaruṭiya viruppam.  namatu uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil pāṭapōtaṉi tamiḻil amintirukkiṉṟatu. āṉāl, intap pāṭaṅkaḷip pōtikkum āciriyarkaḷukkup payiṟciyum pāṭamum āṅkilattil aḷikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉar avarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷil mēṟkoḷḷavirukkum paṇikaḷil avarkaḷ peṟṟa aṟivum payiṟciyum muṟṟilum payaṉpaṭāmal pōkiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē, mutalil āciriyark kallūrikaḷil pāṭaṅkaḷ tamiḻil collittarappaṭa vēṇṭiyatu mikavum avaciyam. itaṟkut tēviyāṉa puttakaṅkaḷ tamiḻil illiyē eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. intak kuṟiyi nīkka tiru. cuppu reṭṭiyār muṉ vantiruppatu pārāṭṭat takkatu.
avar eḻutiyirukkum tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟi periya aḷavu nūlāka amintirukkiṉṟatu. ati muṟṟilum pārkka eṉakku nēramum ōyvum kiṭṭavilli. eṉṟālum, pārtta aḷavil tāy moḻip payiṟciyit tavira attuṭaṉ toṭarpāka irukkavēṇṭiya navīṉa muṟikaḷ paṟṟiya piṟa ceytikaḷum cērkkap peṟṟiruppatāka aṟikiṉṟēṉ. avaruṭiya puttakam āciriyar payiṟcik kallūrikaḷilum āciriyar payiṟcip paḷḷikaḷilum payiṟci peṟum māṇakkarkaḷukkup peritum payaṉpaṭum eṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ.
iṉṟu namatu uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil pāṭapōtaṉi tamiḻil amintirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉ kāraṇamāka avarkaḷuk kirukkum āṅkila māḻiyiṉ ñāṉam ceṉṟa talimuṟiyil āṅkilam pōtaṉāmoḻiyāka irunta kālattil iruntamātiri illi eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. āṉāl, palatuṟikaḷilum aṟivuttaram mikavum uyarntirukkiṉṟateṉpatu pārapaṭca millāmal pārppavarkaḷ aṉivarukkum teḷivākak teriyum. tām paṭippatu, āṅkilattip paṭippatukūṭa etaṟkāka? pala tuṟikaḷilum aṟivu peṟuvataṟkāka, tāymoḻimūlam kalvi pukaṭṭuvatutāṉ kuṟinta kālattil atika aṟivu peṟuvataṟ kēṟṟa vaḻi eṉpatu ulaka muḻuvatilumuḷḷa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ aṉupavam. āṉāl, iṉṉum ēṉ kallūrikaḷilum pōtaṉā muṟi tāymoḻi mūlam amiyavilli eṉṟa viṉā eḻukiṉṟatu.
intak kāraṇaṅkaḷi iṅku ārāyavēṇṭiya avaciyam illi. iṉṟilliyāṉāl, nāḷi, allatu oṉṟiraṇṭu, allatu aintāṇṭukaḷil paṭippaṭiyākat tamiḻ pōtaṉā moḻiyāvatu uṟuti. puttakaṅkaḷ illiyeṉṟu collu kiṉṟavarkaḷ, atu pōtaṉā muṟiyāṉālum, veḷivarum puttakaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu āccariyappaṭuvārkaḷ.
	āṉāl, ataṟku muṉṉamēyē ip puttakaṅkaḷi eḻuta muṉ vantiruppavarkaḷ mikavum pārāṭṭutalukkuriyavarkaḷ. tiru. cuppu reṭṭiyār mikunta ciramattuṭaṉum cirattiyuṭaṉum paktiyuṭaṉum ip paṇiyic ceytirukkiṉṟār. tamiḻ aṉṉiyiṉpāl tāṉ koṇṭuḷḷa pēraṉpāl, tamiḻakkuc cēvi ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟa pērārvattāl tūṇṭap peṟṟu tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟi eṉṟa innūli eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār. avar paṇi aṉivarukkum payaṉpaṭuvatākuka. avar mēṟkoṇṭirukkum muyaṟcikaḷil āṇṭavaṉ tuṇi niṟpāṉāka.
					iṅṅaṉam,
ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam. 

srī rāmakiruṣṇa miṣaṉ vityālayam, 
periyanāyakkaṉ pāḷiyam, 
30-9-57.
FOREWORD
Professor N. Subbu Reddiar has done much more than merely put out one more book on the pedagogy of Tamil. Most of the books on the teaching of Tamil appear to present techniques and procedures ill-suited to the teaching of the mother-tongue, based on ideas appropriate to the teaching of foreign languages. Professor Reddiar has furnished almost the first book on Tamil teaching based on principles most appropriate to it. I know that he has developed many of these principles from his careful studies of Tamil grammar and prosody. It is a pity that most previous writers have ignored the basic material contined in the Tolkappiam which, incidentally, is a mine of information on the pedagogy of Tamil. Professor Reddiar has broken new ground by resisting the temptation to which most earlier writers have succumbed, and resuscitated those educational principles and procedures outlined in Classical Tamil Literature that are still valid according to the criteria of what goes by the name of Progressive Education. The Principles handled in the book are illustrated by apt examples from Tamil literature. As Professor Reddiar has given me a carte blanche in respect of the length of the Foreword, I am permitting myself to attempt an analysis of the original contribution he has made to the teaching of Tamil, chiefly by way of helping teachers of other Indian languages to perceive the possibility of new dynamic approaches to the teaching of their own languguages.
	Professor Reddiar has identified the objectives of the learning of the mother-tongue from the mass of general objectives which hold good for all languages. He has rightly emphasised the role of the mother-tongue in providing wholesome emotional sustenance to the growing learners. The distinction between a foreign language and the mother-tongue seems to lie in the possibility of greater emotionalisation through the mother-tongue. The mother-tongue patently serves the functional purpose of communication ; but a subtle principle implied in its character, which is often missed is, that it conveys something most intimate to the life and experience of the learner, while the experiences gined through a foreign language are exotic and vicarious, though sensible by the process of comparison and imagination. The art of translation must remin an imperfect art even with the most perfect of multi-linguists becuse, while words may be translatable for practical purposes, most concepts must remin untranslated. It is not the perfect nature of the translation in any accredited translation of a great work that helps one to see the beauty of the original in the translation. The superior education and imagination of the reader and the circumstances of his being a good student of the original make him see in the translation what does not really exist ; viz., the beauty of the original. G. U. Pope’s translation of the Kural, for instance, can never be as perfect as Tiruvalluvar’s Tamil Kural. But if Pope is remembered better than any other translator of the Kural it is becuse the translation has literary value in English. It seems to be an axiom that to enjoy a work of literature there can be no short-circuiting of the process by reading it in translation. There is as much difference between an original work and its best translation as there is between an actual visit to a place and seeing the place in a three-dimensional picture.
	I have sid so much about translation only to come back to the position that the study of the mother-tongue is most necessary for the proper building up of the emotions of the children. Psychic imbalance, lack of character, a negative, nihilistic attitude and frustration, result from a wrong approach to the question of the education of the emotions. It seems to be true that character is the product of a good knowledge of the mother-tongue. The Foreign language can undoubtedly enlarge the imaginative horizon of the learner, and add to his fund of knowledge; but it can never substitute the mother-tongue as the most nourishing sustenance for emotional development.
Professor Reddiar has developed this theme in the chapter on ‘Objectives’ by discussing the personality values of the mother-tongue. The mother-tongue is the entrance to the true life of the community which has a direct and permanent influence on the individual. It is a means of enriching one’s social usefulness and culture. It is also a means of self-correction and self-education. Language necessarily leads to literature, and also to the whole literary heritage. A thorough grasp of the mother-tongue is a means to participation in the pride and glory of several generations which the heritage mirrors up before the eager participant. A nation of character can be guaranteed if the mother-tongue is given the first place in the educational system, and every citizen is helped to participate in the enjoyment of the literary heritage of the mother-tongue.
Discussing the value of oral work, Professor Reddiar has rightly emphasised the role of questioning as an important technique in the promotion of oral competencies. I remember a teacher of mine in the Columbia University telling me that the correct measure of the best class-room teaching is the skill to use the question technique. How many teachers use the technique for any other purpose than for killing time ruthlessly? Good questioning is mutual inspring. While the teachers’ questions, in the ideal set-up, result in both learning and internalisation of knowledge, the students’ questions, whatever they are, result in improved teaching. Questioning is not the prerogative of the teacher. Unfortunately, in the Western pedagogical tradition, questioning is indicated mostly as a weapon in the hands of the teacher. Questioning, in its technical connotation, means mostly the questioning by the teacher. There is very little sid about student-questioning. No doubt, in recent times, especially after Dewey codified the scattered bits of the democratic philosophy of education and made it the basis of a number of practicable class-room techniques, American teachers have realised the educational value of student-questioning. But it was only in India that the true value of questioning as an educational technique was developed. The concepts of vina and prasna are at least as old as the earliest books in Tamil and Sanskrit literatures extant. Much in the heritage of Tamil literature has been the inspiration of student-questioning. There is the convention of many books being in the question-answer form ; and one would suspect that the convention itself developed from a real practice. Parvati is the questioning student par excellence, and Iswara is the answering teacher par excellence, be it Astrology, Astronomy, Medicine, Physics or Metaphysics. The author has done very well by giving this technique the pride of place in oral work.
Another aspect of oral work which has come in for detiled attention at the hands of our author relates to the need for speech education as an integral part of language education. Not many, even among Tamilians, may know that the Tamil language is among the first languages of the world to have systematised its phonetics in the manner found in the Tolkappiam and later gramatical works. Ignorance of this has led to disastrous consequences. Though, unlike in Sanskrit, the letter symbols of Tamil do not cover all sounds, some of the letters in combination in a word do have, sounds other than the basic ones for which they stand in the alphabetical system. Thus, we write in Tamil Ka, m, pa, n. In combination as a word unit we do not pronounce it or transliterate it as ‘Kampan’ but as ‘Kamban’. Another instance is Pa, n, ta, m. This is not transliterated as ‘Pantam’ but as ‘Pandam’, Ko, n, tu, is not to be transliterated as ‘Kontu’ but as ‘Kondu’. Va, n, t, aa, n is not ‘Vantaan’ but ‘Vandaan’. We could multiply instances in respect of the multiple sound-purposes of certin letters in combination in words. Professor Reddiar has devoted the requisite length to the importance of the ‘Oli Iyal’ in Tamil. I am not aware of any other scholar in Tamil Who has emphasised the need for codifying the phonetic principles of Tamil as they are avilable in the earliest grammatical works ; and the day may not be far off when Professor Reddiar’s inspiration produces a Daniel Jones for Tamil.
The discussion on oral and silent reading is really scholarly. The author has distinguished three types of reading: developmental, recreational and functional. Language education is, to a large extent, self-education in so far as it relates to the mother-tongue. The role of the teacher will be as much a negative one as it would be a positive one. The negative aspect of the teaching work will include the undoing of the wrong influences of the home and the community in speech and writing. The mother-tongue, in its spoken form at home and in the community, is not only full of colloquialisms but also regional variations. Before my visit to England I had imagined that in the whole of the British language there was one uniform way of speaking in English Languages. But when I visited the different parts of the country, I discovered there were regional variations. I was left wondering which was the standard form. In the United States where English has been accepted as the language of the nation with certin modifications, the variations in speech result from two factors. One is, that the Americans have developed a basic speech pattern with inflections, modulations and puses, determined more or less uniformly. Another is, that even within this uniformity there is influence of the original mother-tongue of the community. A predominantly Italian community domiciled in an area shows itself up by the way it speaks the national language which has also become the community’s mother tongue. There can be no denying the fact that there are regional variations in respect of Tamil speech also. Certin mistakes of speech are perpetuated in each area, and Professor Reddiar has listed these. The function of the teacher is to wean the learner from such incorrect habits as may be inculcated in the home and community environment of the learner. The positive role related to the inculcation of the right language habits. But even the best teaching by the ablest teacher can only be a beginning. It must be completed by the reading of the three types of reading material suggested by Professor Reddiar. No modern educationist will underrate the value of library reading as integral to the process of learning. What the teacher initiates, the library completes. The pity of it, however, is that, even in the year of grace 1964, the library is considered a luxury in not a small number of high schools in Tamilnad. A year ago I assisted in a survey of libraries, and the revelation shocked me. Only a few schools could boast of anything like a library. Some schools have a few almirahs contining books most of which are of no use either to the teachers or to the students. Even if we should concede that a collection of books, whatever they are, is a library, and apply this definition to defend an unhappy situation in our schools, there can be no escaping the fact that the number of good Tamil books in the libraries of most schools can be counted on the fingers of both hands. The current harvest in the field of Tamil literature is as rich as it should be. Excellent reading material has been put out in recent years in Tamil; but very few schools have bestowed any thought on the question of organizing a good Tamil section in the library.
The section on Tamil literature appears to be the best part of the book. I am particularly impressed with the author’s treatment of poetry. He rightly emphasizes the role of poetry as an instrument of educating the emotions. He warns the pedagogical surgeons among Tamil Pandits aginst performing purposeless surgery on beautiful poems in the name of grammar and paraphrase. Poetry is not for dissection; it is for enjoyment. It is one of the major ingredients in aesthetic education.
In this connection, I would commend the following books of Professor Reddiar for careful study; Kavingnan Ullam, Kalingathupparani Aarichi, Kaathal Oviyangal, and Kavithi Anubhavam.
This place of grammar, the need to promote expression through the written word and the development of creative writing in Tamil have received really expert treatment at the author’s hands. I am fascinated by the section on ‘Teacher Planning’ in which he advocates the preparation of a blue-print of the whole year’s work by the teacher. I should like to congratulate the learned author on his masterly treatment of the concept of Evaluation in its appropriateness to Tamil.
Profesor Reddiar has not, however, discarded whis of essence in the writings of experts in Linguistics. The merit of his work lies in its eclectic approach, coalescing what is still valid in the traditional approach and what Western educational thinking has to furnish as its cream. The bibliography at the end of the book is the key to the way the author’s mind must have worked at the time of his writing the book.
The glossary of technical terms, many of which he has coined, must prove the forerunner to more exhustive glossaries.
I am thankful to Professor Reddiar for the honour he has done me by asking me to write the Foreword to his magnum opus. The value of this work is bound to increase with the greater realization of the new contribution it has made and the new vistas it has opened up to the teachers of Tamil for whom it is primarily intended.

S. THIRUVENKATACHARI
Professor of Education
Dr. Alagappa Chettiar Trining College.
Karikudi,
20-2-1964

tēṭik koḻikkum kavivāṇar nāvum ceḻuṅkaruṇi
ōṭip perukum aṟivāḷar neñcum uvantunaṭam
āṭik kaḷikkum mayilē! uṉ pātam aṭikkalē! 
î tēcika vināyakam piḷḷi
tāy moḻiyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi ellāt tuṟikaḷi luḷḷavarkaḷum uṇarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.  makkaḷāṭciyil tāy moḻi perumpaṅku koḷḷavēṇṭiya ciṟantatoru karuviyākum.  kōṉāṭciyiṉ nōkkam vēṟu; kuṭiyāṭciyiṉ nōkkam vēṟu. kōṉāṭciyil āḷvōr tamāṇiyik kuṭimakkaḷiṭam aṭiyac ceyvatu; kuṭiyāṭciyil makkaḷ tamviruppatti āḷvōr aṟiyacceyvatu. itaṟku makkaḷ aṟinta moḻitāṉ takkatoru karuviyāka irukka muṭiyum. ēṉavē, aṉivarum tāy moḻiyil teḷivākap pēcavum eḻutavum āṟṟal peṟavēṇṭum eṉpatik kūṟavā vēṇṭum? iti mukkiya nōkkamākak koṇṭu toṭakka, iṭi, uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟum tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ moḻiyik kaṟpikka vēṇṭum; tāy moḻiyilēyē ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷum kaṟpikkappeṟa vēṇṭi yatāyiruttaliṉ piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷum in nōkkatti niṉivil vittuk koṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟavēṇṭum.
ip puttakam tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟiyik kūṟuvatu. uḷaviyal uṇmikaḷi aṭippaṭiyākakkoṇṭu mēṟpula aṟiñarkaḷ ettaṉiyō payiṟṟu muṟikaḷik kaṇṭaṟin tuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟiyellām moḻiyiṉ marapu keṭātu ententa aḷavil nantamiḻ moḻiyik kaṟpippatil mēṟkoḷḷalām eṉpati ōraḷavu ārāyntuḷḷēṉ. potuvāka ellā āciriyarkaḷum, ciṟappākat tamiḻāciriyarkaḷum, kalvit tuṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟum piṟarum, potumakkaḷum cintikkavēṇṭiya pala karuttukkaḷ itil kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. iviyē muṭinta muṭipeṉṟu aṟutiyiṭṭu urittēṉilli. avaciyam ēṟpaṭil māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceytukoḷḷalām; ceytu koḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum. tāymoḻiyaṟivu cemmiyāka vāyttāltāṉ āṭci pīṭattil amarntiruppōrukkum nāṭṭu makkaḷukkum iṭiyil nalla toṭarpu ēṟpaṭum mēlum araciyal pōkki makkaḷ naṉkuṇaravum, taṅkaḷ viruppatti araciṉarukkup pulappaṭuttikkoḷḷavum iyalum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, tāy moḻiyiṉ vaḷantāṉ araciṉarukkum makkaṭkum nēraṭiyāṉa toṭarpi uṇṭākka muṭiyum.
tāymoḻiyi ellōrum eḷitil kaṟṟukkoḷḷalāmē; piṟanta cila āṇṭukaḷil kuḻanti, tāṉ vāḻum cūḻniliyil peṟṟōr, uṟṟār, uṟaviṉar camūkam ākiyōriṉ mūlamākat tāymoḻiyi mika virivākak kaṟṟukkoḷvatiliruntu ivvuṇmi teḷivākumē; ivvaḷavu eḷitākakak kaṟkakkūṭiya tāymoḻiyik kaṟpittaṟku ivvaḷavu ārppāṭṭam tēviyilliyē eṉṟu cilar karutalām; uṇmiyākap palar avvāṟutāṉ karutukiṉṟaṉar. oru kuḻantiyō allatu paṭikkātu vaḷarntavarō tam aṉṟāṭat tēvikkākak kaṟkum nilivēṟu; pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap paṇpaṭṭu ilakkiya ilakkaṇa vaḷam paṭitta moḻiyi aṟiviyal niliyil vittuk kaṟpatu vēṟu; eṉpati, eṇṇip pārkkum aṉivarum eḷitil uṇarvar. uḷaviyal uṇmikaḷ palavaṟṟik kaṇṭaṟinta piṟaku iḵtu iṉṉum teḷivākap pulaṉākiyuḷḷatu.
nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷākavē navīṉa muṟiyil tamiḻ kaṟpippatip paṟṟi oru puttakam eḻuti veḷiyiṭa eṇṇiyiruntēṉ. ōṉpatu āṇṭukaḷ uyarnilip paḷḷiyil talimiyāciriyaṉāka iruntapoḻutu nēril kavaṉitta palvēṟu aṉupavaṅkaḷik koṇṭum, eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ pi. ṭi. māṇākkarkaṭkut tamiḻ kaṟpippatip paṟṟip pōtittuvanta aṉupavattik koṇṭum ipputtakam eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu. eḻuti muṭikka iraṇṭāṇṭukaḷ āyiṉa. ipputtakattil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa pala karuttukkaḷiyum naṉku cintittu naṭimuṟiyil ceyalukkuk koṇṭuvantāl māṇākkarkaḷiṭam tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivu naṉmuṟiyil amivatu uṟuti eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ; nampikkiyum koḷkiṉṟēṉ. muṟikaḷik kūṟuvatu eḷitu; avaṟṟi naṭimuṟikkuk koṇṭuvantu; veṟṟi kāṇpatu eṉpatu moḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷum muyaṟciyiyum, avarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉiyum, īṭupāṭṭiyum poṟuttuḷḷatu.
ipputtakam patiṉāṟu attiyāyaṅkaḷākap pirikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. mutal attiyāyattil tamiḻ kaṟpippataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ ōraḷavu virivāka ārāyappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭāvatu attiyāyattil innōkkaṅkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil niṟivēṟuvataṟkāṉa pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum; naṭi muṟiyiluḷḷa toṭakka, iṭi, uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil kalvi peṟum māṇākkarkaḷ payilavēṇṭiya moḻikaḷpaṟṟiyum viḷakkam koṭukkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. mūṉṟiliruntu āṟu muṭiyavuḷḷa attiyāyaṅkaḷil payiṟṟuvataṟku mēṟkoḷḷap peṟum palvēṟu muṟikaḷippaṟṟiya vitikaḷum, vivaraṅkaḷum tarappeṟṟiruppatuṭaṉ; tamiḻmoḻi payiṟṟuṅkāl avaṟṟi evvevvāṟu kiyāḷalām eṉpatum viḷakkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. ēḻiliruntu paṉṉiraṇṭu muṭiyavuḷḷa attiyāyaṅkaḷil mēṟkūṟappeṟṟa vitikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil evvevvāṟu kiyāḷalām eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkap paṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. ivi yāvum muṭinta muṭipukaḷ eṉṟu evarum niṉittal vēṇṭā. ivaṟṟi naṭimuṟiyil koṇṭuvarumpoḻututāṉ uṇmiyāṉa caṅkaṭaṅkaḷ teṉpaṭum. muṟikaḷiyum avaṟṟiṟkēṟpa māṟṟiyamittuk koḷḷavum nēriṭum. patiṉmūṉṟāvatu attiyāyattil māṇākkarkaḷiṭam nalla kiyeḻuttu amivatupaṟṟiyum avarkaḷ eḻuttu vēlikaḷil kāṇakkūṭiya piḻikaḷpaṟṟiyum avaṟṟit tavirppatupaṟṟiyum cila karuttukkaḷ terivikkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. patiṉāṉkāvatu attiyāyattil tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ tam paṇiyit tiṟamiyuṭaṉ āṟṟuvataṟku vakuttukkoḷḷavēṇṭiya tiṭṭaṅkaḷpaṟṟiyum pāṭak kuṟippukkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmipaṟṟiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷuṭaṉ viḷakkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu. patiṉintāvatu attiyāyattil paḷḷiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varum puttakaṅkaḷiṉ vakikaḷiyum, nalla puttakaṅkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷiyum paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷum, tuṇippāṭap puttakaṅkaḷip payiṟṟum muṟikaḷum, nūlakap paṭippi mēṟkoḷḷum vaḻikaḷum ōraḷavu viḷakkappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. navīṉa muṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭu kaṟpippataṉāl tamiḻmoḻi kaṟpataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷiyoṭṭi māṇākkar peṟum moḻiyaṟiviyum ilakkiya aṟiviyum navīṉa aḷaviyal muṟikaḷikkoṇṭē aḷappatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu. cariyāṉa aḷavu kōlikkoṇṭu aḷantāltāṉ uṇmiyāṉa nili pulaṉākum. uḷaviyaṟkali vaḷarcciyiṉ viḷivāka kalvittuṟi aḷaviyal kuṟittu virivāṉa nūloṉṟit tamiḻil eḻuta eṇṇiyuḷḷēṉ. aḷaviyalpaṟṟiya ellāk karuttukkaḷum atil tokuttuk kūṟappeṟum.
puttakattiṉ iṟutiyil  ip puttakattil mēṟkoṇṭa kaliccoṟkaḷum puttakam eḻutap payaṉpeṟṟa nūlkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum poruṭ kuṟippu akarātiyum piṉṉiṇippukkaḷākac cērkkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa. kaliccoṟkaḷ āṅkilattil paṭikkum payiṟṟu muṟikaḷit tamiḻmoḻiyil amittuk kōṭalukkum, mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷ ipputtakattip paṭippōri mēlum paṭippilkoṇṭu celuttuvataṟkum, poruṭ kuṟippu akarāti pala ceytikaḷi avaravar virumpukiṟavaṇṇam tokuttaṟivutaṟkum tuṇiyāka irukkum eṉṟē avi cērkkappeṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉa.
puttakam eḻuti muṭintu accuvākaṉam ēṟuvataṟkumuṉ eṉ ariya naṇparum aḻakappā payiṟcik kallūri mutalvarumāṉa tiru. pa. turikkaṇṇu mutaliyār avarkaḷ pirati muḻuvatiyum mikap poṟumiyuṭaṉ paṭittup pārttuc cila kuṟippukkaḷik kūṟiṉārkaḷ. puttakam accākum   poḻutu purūp; pārttu palavita tiruttaṅkaḷic ceytu puttakattiṉ akattōṟṟamum puṟattōṟṟamum aḻakāka amivataṟku mutaṟ kāraṇamāka iruntavar eṉ naṇpar tiru. e. tiruvēṅkaṭāccāri avarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ iruvarukkum eṉ uḷaṅkaṉinta naṉṟi.
ip puttakam eḻutik koṇṭiruntapoḻutu nōyvāyppaṭṭēṉ. uṭkārntu eḻuta muṭiyāta niliyil iruntēṉ. ēṉ arumi māṇākkarāyirunta tiru. ci. irāmacāmi pi. ō.el.,pi.ṭi., (ippoḻutu teṉkāciyil tamiḻāciriyarākap paṇiyāṟṟupavar) yāṉ collac colla eḻuti utaviṉār. kiṭṭattaṭṭa kiyeḻuttuppaṭiyil pāti avar kiyeḻut tilē amintatu. avarukkum atupōla poruṭ kuṟippu akarāti tokuppatu pōṉṟa piṟavakikaḷil tuṇi ceyta piṟa māṇākkarkaḷukkum eṉ naṉṟi urittākum.
ip puttakam accu vaṭivam peṟuvataṟkup pallāṟṟāṉum utaviyaruḷiyavar kampaṉ pukaḻ pāṭik kaṉṉit tamiḻvaḷarppavarum; eṉmītu aḷavaṟṟa aṉpukāṭṭi varupavarumāṉa uyartiru. cā kaṇēcaṉ avarkaḷ. avarkaṭku ellāvaṟṟālum eṉṟeṉṟum kaṭamippaṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉ. tiru. kaṇēcaṉ avarkaḷ elliyaṟṟa tamiḻppaṟṟuḷḷavar; tamiḻ moḻi ellāvita vaḷattiyum peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟu tuṭittu niṟpavar; tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcciyilum ataṉik kaṟpittayilum aṭippaṭiyāṉa māṟutalkaḷi avāvi niṟpavar; tamiḻmoḻi āṅkila moḻipōl virivil vaḷampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu āci koḷpavar. ittakiya uyar karuttukkaḷikkoṇṭa oru pēraṉpar eṉṉi ūkkuvittu innūl veḷivaruvataṟku utaviṉār eṉṟāl ataṉi nāṉ peṟṟa pēṟu eṉṟē karutukiṉṟēṉ. aṉṉārukku eṉ naṉṟi kalanta vaṇakkam urittākum.
uyartiru. ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam avarkaḷi aṟiyāta tamiḻ makkaḷē irukka muṭiyātu; tamiḻ moḻikkum kalvikkum avar ceyta, ceytu varukiṉṟa toṇṭukaḷi tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟil poṉṉeḻuttukkaḷāl poṟikka vēṇṭum. elliyaṟṟa tamiḻk kātalu ṭiya ap periyār innūlukku muṉṉuri vaḻaṅki āci kūṟiyati yāṉpeṟṟa perumpēṟu eṉṟē karutukiṉṟēṉ. ap periyārukku eṉ uḷaṅkaṉinta naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatu.
ip puttakatti acciṭuvatāl nēriṭum perum poruṭ celaviyum poruṭpaṭuttātu iti maṉamuvantu ēṟṟu mikavirivil acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭavarkaḷ tiruvāḷarkaḷāṉa e. ār. cuppiramaṇya piḷḷi patippakattār; kālattiṟku vēṇṭiya pala tamiḻ nūlkaḷi aḻakuṟa acciṭṭut tamiḻp paṇiyāṟṟukiṉṟavarkaḷ avarkaḷ. avarkaṭku eṉ maṉamuvanta naṉṟi urittākum.
mūṉṟāṇṭup paruvattilēyē tantiyi iḻantavaṉ yāṉ. tantiyōṭu kalvi pōm eṉṟu periyōr kūṟuvar. eṉṟālum, pala iṉṉalkaḷukkiṭiyē ciṟitum taḷarātu  ammiyāka iruntu eṉṉi vaḷarttum appaṉāka iruntu kalvi payiṟṟuvittum ammiyum appaṉumāka iruntu eṉakku ellā nalaṉkaḷi aruḷiyum eṉṉi in nilikkuk koṇṭuvantu kaṇṭu makiḻum eṉ arumi aṉṉiyiṉ poṉṉār tiruvaṭikaṭku innūlic camarppit tuḷḷēṉ. tāymoḻiyic ciṟārkaḷiṭam vaḷamāka vaḷarkkum vaḻiyik kūṟum tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟi yām in nūlit tāykkuc camarppippatu mikavum poruttam eṉṟu karutiyē avvāṟu ceytuḷḷēṉ.
ēṉakkuḷḷa palavakiyāṉa kuṟikaḷāl ip puttakattila palavita kuṟipāṭukaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruttal kūṭum. cila karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷum irukkalām. āvaṟṟi aṉparkaḷ poṟutturuḷumāṟu vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ. puttakatti aṉparkaḷ ūṉṟip paṭittuk kuṟipāṭukaḷiyum karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum terivippārkaḷāyiṉ aṭutta patippil avaṟṟit tiruttik koḷvēṉ. ip puttakatti eḻuti veḷiyiṭuvataṟku yāṉ mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟciyi niṟi vēṟṟivitti ellām valla tiruvaruḷi vāḻtti vaṇaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
kuṟṟam kaḷintu kuṟipeytu vācittal
kaṟṟaṟinta māntar kaṭaṉ
na. cuppu reṭṭiyār,

aḻakappā payiṟcik kallūri kārikkuṭi
akṭōpar 1, 1957
tiruntiya (iraṇṭām) patippu
nūliṉ aṭippaṭik karuttukkaḷil atikamāṟṟam oṉṟum illi. kaliccoṟkaḷilum iyal amippukkaḷilum māṟṟam ceyyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. naṭiyilum aṭikkuṟippukkaḷilum tiruttam ceyyappeṟṟuḷḷatu. nūliṉ uṭalil iṭiyiṭiyē cila āṅkilac coṟkaḷ viḷakkattiṟkāka nuḻikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
	intappatippiṉi eṉ ariya naṇpar pērāciriyar e. tiruvēṅkaṭāc cāriyār avarkaḷiṉ āṅkila muṉṉuri aṇiceykiṉṟatu. pērāciriyar āccāriyār avarkaḷuṭaṉ patiṉāṉku āṇṭukaḷ neruṅkip paḻakiyuḷḷēṉ; pattu āṇṭukaḷ aḻakappā āciriyar payiṟcikkallūriyil avaruṭaṉ cērntup paṇiyāṟṟiyuḷḷēṉ. irupattintu āṇṭukaṭkumēl kalvittuṟiyil pala nilikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟi aṉupavam peṟṟa eṉ naṇpar iṅkilāntu, amerikkā pōṉṟa mēṉāṭukaḷil pala kalvi niliyaṅkaḷip pārviyiṭṭuk kalvipaṟṟiya pala karuttukkaḷi aṟintavar. vaṭamoḻip pulami mikkavar; tamiḻilum nalla payiṟciyuṭiyavar. ēntap piracciṉiyiyum nuṇukiyaṟintu teḷivākappiṟarkku uṇarttum peṟṟiyar. āṅkilattilum aruntamiḻilum  kēṭṭār piṇikkum takiyarāyk kēḷārum vēṭpa moḻiyum colvaṉmi vāyntavar. pala nūlkaḷiṉ āciriyar; ciṟanta āyvāḷar. ittakiya eṉkeḻutaki naṇpar inta nūlukku āṅkila muṉṉuri vaḻaṅkiyamikku eṉ uḷaṅkaṉinta naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatu.
it tiruntiya patippu veḷivaruvataṟku eṉ muyaṟciyi niṟivēṟṟi vitta  pintamiḻp piṉ ceṉṟa paccip pacuṅ koṇṭaliṉ tiruvaṭikaḷi niṉintu pōṟṟukiṉṟēṉ.
na. cuppu reṭṭiyār, 
tiruppati 
15-3-64
tamiḻ payiṟṟum muṟi
pirivu-1 : nōkkaṅkaḷ

oruvar piṟantatu mutal iṟakkum vari pēci varuvatu tāy moḻi.  kuḻantiyiṉ iyalpāṉa moḻiyum atuvēyākum.  potuvāka nām namatu eṇṇaṅkaḷip pēccu mūlamākavum eḻuttu mūlamākavum veḷiyiṭuvataṟkut tāymoḻi ciṟanta karuviyākap payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.  atuvum viṭutali peṟṟa nam nāṭṭil iṉṟu tāy moḻiyiṉ tāṇṭavatti emmaruṅkum kāṇalām. uyarnta maṉaveḻuccikaḷiyum kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟaliyum koṇṭa tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷic cuvippatupōl ayalmoḻi ilakkiyaṅkḷic cuvikka muṭiyātu.  tāymoḻi eḻiluṇar tiṟaṉiyum, kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟaliyum naṉmuṟiyil vaḷarkkiṉṟatu; vaḻi vaḻiyāka varum camūka iyaliyum, paṇ pāṭṭiyum, aṟiyum tiṟavukōlākavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu; vāḻkkiyi vaḷappaṭutti nītiyi iṉiya muṟiyil pukaṭṭu kiṉṟatu.  tāymoḻi mūlam kalvi peṟuṅkāl kālac celavu. paṇac celavu. muyaṟci ākiyavi kuṟikiṉṟaṉa.  tāy moḻi payilvatāl moḻip paṟṟum nāṭṭup paṟṟuṅkūṭa naṉmuṟiyil amikiṉṟaṉa.  ittakiya nōkkaṅkaḷi inta iyal virivāka viḷakkukiṉṟatu.  ivaṟṟi niṟivēṟṟa vēṇṭiya tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷiṉ poṟuppukaḷiyum, avarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷiyum, cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvatuṭaṉ tāymoḻik kalviyil piṟa pāṭa āciriyarkaḷiṉ paṅkiṉiyum niṉivūṭṭu kiṉṟatu ip pirivu.
1. tamiḻ moḻiyip payiṟṟum nōkkaṅkaḷ
ivvulakil nuṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa moḻikaḷ pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṟantatu mutal iṟakkumvari. toṭṭililiruntu cuṭukāṭuvari, oruvar kēṭṭum, pēciyum, varum moḻiyē tāymoḻi eṉappaṭum.  kuḻantikku iyalpāṉa moḻiyum atuvē.  oru tāy taṉ kuḻantikkup pāluṭṭum poḻutum, tālāṭṭum poḻutum, cīrāṭṭip, pārāṭṭum poḻutum, pēcum moḻiyit tāṉē tāymoḻi eṉṟu collavēṇṭum?  ic ciṟappuk karutiyē namatu tāymoḻiyākiya tamiḻ moḻiyi āṉṟōr pālvāyp pacuntamiḻ eṉṟu ciṟappittuḷḷaṉar.
moḻi camūkattiltāṉ vaḷarccipeṟum:  moḻi iṉṟēl makkaḷ camūkamāka .iyaṅka muṭiyātu.  eṉavē oṉṟupaṭṭa potu vāḻkkikku moḻi iṉṟiyami yātatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil oruvarōṭu oruvar uṟavāṭuvataṟku moḻiyē ciṟanta karuviyāka amikiṉṟatu.  cātāraṇa makkaḷ mutalil pēcat toṭaṅkiya tāy moḻiyiyē oru ciṟanta karuviyākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.  potuvāka nām namatu eṇṇaṅkḷip  pēccu mūlamākavum eḻuttu mūlamākavum veḷiyiṭuvataṟku namatu tāymoḻiyiyē kiyāḷukiṉṟōm.  palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa koḷkikaḷ, camayaṅkaḷ, poruḷātāra ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷ, kulaṅkaḷ, nilikaḷ, toḻilkaḷ, ākiyavaṟṟāl vēṟṟumiyuṟṟa pala piriviṉarkaḷum pēcum tāymoḻiyāl oṉṟupaṭuvatik kāṇumpoḻutu tāymoḻiyiṉ pērāṟṟali eṉṉaveṉṟu kūṟuvatu?!  ittakiya ciṟappu vāynta tāymoḻi, kalvit tiṭṭattil mutaliṭam peṟuvatil viyappoṉṟum illi.  eṉavē, tāymoḻiyāciri yarkaḷ itik kaṟpikkum muṉṉar, kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya nōkkaṅkaḷi, aṟintu kaṟpittal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  annōkkaṅkaḷ yāvi eṉpati īṇṭu nōkkuvōm.
maṉattil uḷḷavaṟṟi veḷiyiṭal:  moḻiyiṉ mūlamākattāṉ namatu eṇṇaṅkaḷ, karuttukkaḷ, uṇrccikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit terivikkiṉṟōm.  pēccālum eḻuttālum piṟar karuttukkaḷi aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  makkaḷiṉ potu vāḻkkikku iṉṟiyamiyāta tāymoḻiyippayaṉ eytum muṟiyil māṇakkarkaḷ tiṟamiyuṭaṉ kiyāḷak kūṭiyavāṟu kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.  mutalil vēṇṭappaṭuvatu teḷivē:  cariyākac colvati aṭuttatākak koḷḷalām.  ivviṭattil jārj cāmcaṉ eṉṟa āṅkilak kalvi nipuṇar kūṟiyiruppatit tirumpak kūṟutal poruttamākum: iṅṅaṉam nuṇṇiya eṇṇaṅkaḷum teḷivāṉa karuttukkaḷum tōṉṟuvataṟku āciriyar māṇākkarkaḷiṭam (1)eḷiya muṟiyil oru poruḷik kuṟittut teḷivākap pēcavum. (2) teḷivāṉa muṟiyil oruvar oru poruḷippaṟṟik kēṭkavum kēṭṭup poruḷuṇaravum., (3) oru poruḷit teḷivāṉa muṟiyil paṭikkavum, paṭittup poruḷuṇaravum (4) oru poruḷippaṟṟit teḷivāṉa, eḷiya muṟiyil, eḻutavumāṉa āṟṟalkaḷi uṇṭu paṇṇuvatākum.  piṟarukkuk kaṟpikkum āciriyariṭam ippaṇpukaḷ amiya vēṇṭum1 tāymoḻiyil teḷivākap pēcavum, paṭikkavum, eḻutavumāṉa payiṟcikaḷip paḷḷiyil aḷittu ivvāṟṟalkaḷi vaḷarttāl avarkaḷ vāḻkkit tuṟiyil iṟaṅkic ceyalāṟṟum poḻutu avi peritum tuṇiyāka irukkum.  makkaḷāṭci nilavum nam nāṭṭil oru ciṟanta kuṭimakaṉāka vāḻvataṟku ivvāṟṟalkaḷ mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātavi.  oru nalla kuṭimakaṉukkut teḷivāṉa cintaṉi, teḷivāṉa pēccu, teḷivāṉa eḻuttu ākiya tiṟaṉkaḷ vēṇṭappaṭupavi allavā?  vaḷḷuvap peruntakiyum, 
	eṇporuḷ ākac celaccollit tāṉpiṟarvāy
 nuṇporuḷ kāṇpatu aṟivu. 2
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu īṇṭu cintittuṇaraṟpālatu.  tāymoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyar ivvāṟṟalkaḷi vaḷarppatil takka kavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum.
tāymoḻiyil nalla pulami peṟṟāltāṉ oruvar tam uṇarccikaḷiyum, uyar karuttukkaḷiyum teḷivāka veḷiyiṭa muṭiyum;   avaruṭiya paṭippāṟṟalilum oru vaḷarcciyik kāṇa muṭiyum.  māṇākkarkaḷ muḻu vaḷarccikkum tāymoḻip payiṟciyē ciṟantatōr aṭippaṭiyāka amikiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.  tam uṇarccikaḷi veḷiyiṭuvatil avarkaḷukku oru taṉippaṭṭa makiḻcci ēṟpaṭuvatuṭaṉ, avarkaḷuṭiya maṉam, oḻukkam, mutaliyavi paṇpaṭṭu āḷumi (Personality) ciṟakkavum iṭamuṇṭu.  tāymoḻiyil uḷḷa ciṟanta ceyyuṭkaḷ, kāviyaṅkaḷ, vēṟu pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷ maṉatti virivaṭiyac ceytu avarkaḷuṭiya taṉivīṟu ciṟakkavum vaḻikōlum.  ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ  tam kāviya ulakil ulavac ceyyum kāviyamāntarkaḷiṭam māṇākkarkaḷ paḻaka vāyppukaḷ peṟumpoḻutu ammāntarkaḷiṉ ilaṭciya vāḻkki avarkaḷ  vāḻvukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amiyum.  ivvulaka  vāḻkkiyil makkaḷ nāḷtōṟum kāṇa nēriṭum ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷ, ēkkaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, avarkaḷiṭam aṭikkaṭi eḻum itayattuṭippukaḷ, iṉpakkaṉavukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ tām paṭitta māntarkaḷiṭam niṟivuceytu kāṭṭiyuḷḷatip paṭikkumpoḻutu avarkaḷ uḷḷam kaḷip paṭikiṉtu:  maṉam amitiyuṟu kiṉṟatu.  puṟanāṉūṟu pōṉṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil paṭittiṟam vāynta peru vēntarkaḷiyum, koṭittiṟam mikka vaḷḷalkaḷiyum, kaṟṟaṟintu aṭaṅkiya cāṉṟōr kaḷiyum kāṇumpoḻutu iḷam māṇākkarkaḷiṉ etirkāla vāḻvu ciṟakkum vaḻikaḷ tōṉṟum; aṅkuk kāṇap peṟum nikaḻccikaḷ avarkaḷ vāḻvic ciṟappikkavum kūṭum.
kaṭanta aimpatu āṇṭukaḷil makkaḷ vāḻkkiyilum, karuttulakilum, aṉupavattilum ettaṉiyō māṟṟaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  avi makkaḷ maṉappāṉmiyit talikīḻāka māṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām.  putiya maṉappāṉmiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟiyavaṟṟil mukkiya māṉatu  tāymoḻippaṟṟu.  viṭutali peṟṟa nam nāṭṭil iḵtu emmaruṅkum kāṇappeṟum paṇpākak kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟatu.  iṉṟu eṅkum tamiḻ muḻakkam kēṭkappaṭukiṉṟatu; ellāt tuṟikaḷilum tamiḻ maṇam kamaḻkiṉṟatu.  tēciya iyakkam tōṉṟiyatiliruntu tāymoḻi iyakkamum tōṉṟi vaḷarntu, nāṭu viṭutali eytiya piṟaku aḵtu eṉṟumillāta putiya vēkatti iṉṟu peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  tāymoḻiyil eṇṇaṟṟa nāḷitaḻkaḷ, kiḻami itaḻkaḷ, piṟiitaḻkaḷ, tiṅkaḷitaḻkaḷ tōṉṟi vēkamākap paravi makkaḷ maṉatik kuvikkoṇṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa.  itukāṟum potumakkaḷ vāḻvuṭaṉ neruṅkic cērāmalirunta ilakkiya vāḻvu avarkaḷ vāḻvuṭaṉ cērat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.  ilakkiyac cuviyum, kaviti iṉpamum pāmaramakkaḷukku illiyeṉṟum, kaṟṟaṟinta pulavarkaḷukku maṭṭumē ilakkiyam uriya poruḷeṉṟum nāṭṭiṭiyē vērūṉṟi nilaviyirunta karuttu māṟiviṭṭatu.  iṉṟu pāmaramakkaḷ maṉattiṉmītum ilakkiya vāṉam kavintu avarkaḷ cuvittu aṉupavikkakkūṭiya muṟiyil kaviti maḻi poḻiyavēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttu mella eḻuntu veṟṟiyum peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  ittuṟiyil mutaṉmutalāka vaḻikōliya pāratiyiṉ aṭic cuvaṭṭil pala kaviñarkaḷ talikāṭṭi varukiṉṟaṉar.  tamiḻ urinaṭiyum puttuṇarcci peṟṟup pāmararkaḷum tuykkum niliyil katikaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa putiya maṟumalarcci ilakkiya vakikaḷ ērāḷamākat tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa.
paṭakkāṭcikaḷ tāymoḻikku ēṟṟam aḷittirukkiṉṟaṉa.  kalittuṟi yil tamiḻppaṭaṅkaḷ ciṟitu ciṟitāka muṉṉēṟi, eṇṇaṟṟa pēcum paṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi, eḻuttu maṇam aṟiyāta pāmaramakkaḷiṉ karuttulakattic ciṟitu vaḷarttirukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟē colla vēṇṭum.   ivi ciṟṟūrkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷuṭiya naṭi uṭi pāvaṉikaḷiyum māṟṟiviṭṭaṉa.  nāḷitaḻkaḷilum piṟa veḷiyīṭukaḷilum veḷivarum viḷamparaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ nillātu tāymoḻi mūlam pala vaṇikaviḷamparaṅkaḷum pēcum paṭaṅkaḷ vaṭivil tōṉṟip pāmaramakkaḷiṉ karut tulakattip paṉmukavirivaṭiyac ceytuvarukiṉṟaṉa.  maruttuvam, cukātāram, kalvi, nāṭṭu naṭappukaḷ ākiya tuṟikaḷilum ippēcum tamiḻpaṭaṅkaḷ peruntoṇṭāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉa.
vāṉoli, tolipēci, oliperukki, kirāmaḵpōṉ pōṉṟa pēcumpoṟikaḷ tōṉṟiya piṟaku makkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavu neruṅki varukiṉṟatu.  tolikkāṭciyik kaṇṭaṟinta piṟaku ivvuṟavu mēlum neruṅkukiṉṟatu.  iṭattālum, kālattālum cēymiyilirukkum makkaḷiṭamum nām ētō oru vitattil uṟavu koḷḷukiṉṟōm.  kālañceṉṟa pāṭakarkaḷiṉ kuralkaḷiyum kirāmaḵpōṉ mūlam kēṭka muṭikiṉṟatu.  pala āyiram milukku appālum irukkiṉṟa makkaḷiṭam tolipēci mūlam pēcukiṉṟōm.  kampiyillāt tanti namakku nalkum vacatikaḷō collum taramaṉṟu.
intap putiya cātaṉaṅkaḷilellām iṉṟu tāy moḻiyiṉ tāṇṭavattik kāṇkiṉṟōm.  vaṇikarkaḷum, piṟarum toli pēcimūlam tāymoḻiyil pēcippala ceyalkaḷi niṟivēṟṟik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  perumpālāṉa vāṉoli nikaḻccikaḷ tāymoḻiyil amikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  oliperukki vanta piṟaku oruvar pēcuvati orē kālattil ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ kēṭka muṭikiṉṟatu.  nāṭu viṭutalip peṟuvataṟkuc cila āṇṭukaḷ muṉpiruntē potuppēccu mēṭiyit tāymoḻi taṉakkē eṉṟu urimiyākkik koṇṭuviṭṭatu.  vayatu vantavarkaḷukku vākkurimi vanta piṟaku potumēṭiyil makkaḷ maṉatti orumukappaṭuttum palvēṟu potukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ, mānāṭukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nikaḻccikaḷ tāymoḻiyilēyē naṭi peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  caṭṭacapi nikaḻccikaḷum tāy moḻiyāṉa tamiḻilēyē naṭipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam iṉṟu ceyaṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
potumēṭiyit tāymoḻi kippaṟṟiya kāraṇattāl iṉṟu nāṭṭil palvēṟu iyakkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi vaḷarntu varukiṉṟaṉa.  makkaḷiṉ etirkāla vāḻvi makkaḷē vakuttuk koḷḷavēṇṭu meṉkiṟa kural emmaruṅkum eḻuppappaṭukiṉṟatu.  moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil mānilap piriviṉi  ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ uṇarntuvarukiṉṟaṉar.  ataṟkeṉa ēṟpaṭṭup paṇiyāṟṟivarum kuḻuviṉ aṟikkiyiyum atupaṟṟiya palvēṟu tiṟaṉāyvukaḷiyum pala aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷiyum ceytittāḷkaḷil veḷivantatik kaṇṭōm.  moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyiltāṉ āntira mānilap piriviṉiyum ēṟpaṭṭatu;  pampāy mānilamum iraṇṭākap pirintatu.
ilakkiya nayaṅkaṇṭu iṉpuṟal: tamiḻmoḻiyil eṇṇaṟṟa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa;  namatu mūtātiyar namakku kuṭivaḻic cottāka vittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟa aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷ avi.  iḷamiyiliruntē māṇākkarkaḷi ilakkiya nayamuṇarntu cuvittalil īṭupaṭutta vēṇṭum.  ilakkiyac cuviyil tiḷikkum āṟṟali māṇākkarkaḷiṭam vaḷarttu viṭṭāl atuvē tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ tām kaṟpittalil kaṇṭa veṟṟiyākum.  aṟitōṟum aṟiyāmiyik kāṭṭum  ilakkiyac cuviyil tiḷitta oruvar.
	iruntamiḻē uṉṉāl iruntēṉ; imiyōr
 viruntu amiḻtam eṉṟālum vēṇṭēṉ3 
eṉṟallavō kūṟippōntōr? ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷiṉ paṭippukkaḷ yāvum toṭṭaṉittuṟum maṇaṟkēṇi pōl payiluntōṟum putupputup poruḷ curantu iṉpamūṭṭum teyva ūṟṟukkaḷākum.  ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ilimaṟi kāykaḷākac ceṟintu kiṭakkum uḷḷuṟi uvamam iṟiccip poruḷ pōṉṟa poruḷ nayaṅkaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṉ veḷit tōṟṟaṅkaḷākak kāṭciyaḷikkum mōṉi etuki maṭakku yamakam tiripupōṉṟa col nayaṅkaḷiyum kaṇṭuṇarntu cuvikkumpoḻutu eṉṟumē kāṇāta iṉpam payattali ilakkiyac cōlikaḷil pukuntu pārppavarkaḷ eḷitil uṇarvar.
poruḷ kāraṇamāka, poṟi karuviyāka, nukarappaṭum iṉpam poruḷaṟṟa niliyil iḻakkappaṭukiṉṟatu.  poruḷāl peṟappaṭum iṉpam vaḷaravaḷara ataṟkuk kāraṇamākavuḷḷa poruḷ curuṅkik kaṭiciyil atuvum illiyākiviṭum.  āṉāl, ciṟupāṉmi poṟikaḷ tuṇiniṟpa, nuṇ māṇuḻipulaṅ karuviyāka nukarappaṭum ilakkiya iṉpam, iṉpaṅkaḷ aṉittilum taliciṟantatu eṉpatu aṟiñar kaṇṭa uṇmi; aṟivāl paṭikkappeṟum ivviṉpam vaḷaravaḷara ataṟkuk kāraṇamāya aṟivum mēṉmēlum vaḷarum.  nāṉum vaḷarntu taṉmūlattiyum vaḷarkkum ilakkiya iṉpam makkaḷ naṉmikku iṉṟiyamiyātu vēṇṭappaṭuvatu. kampanāṭarum,  
kavippā amutam iciyiṉ kaṟiyoṭu
	kaṇṇaṉ uṇṇak 
kuvippāṉ 4
eṉṟu kavippā iṉpaamiḻta māvatiyum, aḵtu iciyuṭaṉ cērttu uṇṇappaṭumpoḻutu teviṭṭāta teḷḷamuta māvatiyum kūṟiyiruttal īṇṭu cintikkaṟpālatu.  tiruvācakat tēṉil tiḷitta irāmaliṅka vaḷḷalum tām peṟṟa iṉpatti,
	vāṉkalanta māṇikka vācaka! niṉ vācakatti
nāṉkalantu pāṭuṅkāl naṟkaruppañ cāṟṟiṉilē 
tēṉkalantu pālkalantu ceḻuṅkaṉittīñ cuvikalantu
ūṉkalantu uyirkalantu uvaṭṭāmal iṉippatuvē. 5
eṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟār.  ivviṉpatti aṭiyum vaḻiyi naṉku aṟintu koṇṭāl, vēliyiliruntu nīṅki ōyvu peṟuṅ kālattilum, mutumi kāraṇamāka avalakkavaliyālum taḷarcciyālum tuṉpappaṭum poḻutum ivviṉpam iḷami yuṇarcciyiyūṭṭi maṭṭillāta makiḻcciyiyum aḷikkum.
aṉṟiyum, ivvulaka vāḻvil vayiṟṟup piḻippukkākap pala tuṟikaḷil kiṭantuḻaṉṟu uḻittu aluttuppōkum poḻutu tōṉṟakkūṭiya veṟuppi ivvilakkiya iṉpam pōkkiviṭum.  kīḻmakkaḷ kūṭṭuṟavu, cōmpal, vīṇkālap pōkku mutaliya pala kuṟikaḷi nīkkavum ilakkiyap payiṟci tuṇi purikiṉṟatu.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil nēriṭak kūṭiya ēmāṟṟaṅkaḷiyum iṭarp pāṭukaḷiyum iṉpamāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇmiyi ivvilakkiyaṅkaḷ namakkuk kaṟpikkum.
	naṉṟāmkāl nallavāk kāṇpavar aṉṟāmkāl
 allaṟ paṭuvatu  evaṉ? 6
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa poṉmoḻikaḷ maṉa amitikkuc ciṟanta maruntupōl utavum.  paranta aṟiviyum, virinta nōkkattiyum nalki nammip putiyatōr maṉitaṉākkavalla ivvilakkiya iṉpatti virumpātār yār? āṅkilat tiṟaṉāyvāḷar mātyū ārṉālṭu eṉpār kūṟiyapaṭi.  vāḻkkiyiṉ tiṟaṉāyvutāṉē ilakkiyam? tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paṭittuc cuvippatupōl ayal moḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷic cuvikkamuṭiyātu.  eṉavē, iḷamitoṭṭē tāymoḻip payiṟci aḻuttamāka vēṇṭap paṭuvatoṉṟu.
kaṟpaṉi yāṟṟaliyum murukuṇarcciyiyum vaḷarttal:   tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷit tuyppatāl ivi iraṇṭum oruvarpāl tāmāka vaḷarak kūṭiyavi.  tāy moḻiyāciriyar ceyyuṭ pāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu ivi naṉku vaḷarum cūḻnilikaḷi uṇṭākkalām.  ilakkiyattiṟkuk karuvūlamākat tikaḻvatu kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟal;  kavitikku iṉṟiyami yātatāka vēṇṭappaṭuvatum atuvē.  murukuṇarcci eṉpatu aḻakuḷḷavikaḷit tuykkum oruvaki āṟṟal:  aḻakuḷḷa vaṟṟip poṟi karuviyākak koṇṭum tuykkalām;  kaṟpaṉic ciṟaku peṟṟa maṉattālum tuykkalām.  ilakkiya āciriyaṉ pulaṉkāḷālum kaṟpaṉiyālum kaṇṭavaṟṟiyē avaṉ paṭitta ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paṭikkumpoḻutu nammiyum kāṇac ceykiṉṟāṉ.
caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷ, kamparāmāyaṇam, kaliṅkattupparaṇi pōṉṟa tīñcuvi mikka ilakkiyaṅkaḷippaṭikkumpoḻutu eṇṇaṟṟa kaṟpaṉik kāṭcikaḷik kāṇkiṉṟōm;  ak kāṭcikaḷil tōyntu kiṭakkum murukuṇarcciyil nāmum īṭupaṭukiṉṟōm.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟali nammiṭiyē tōṟṟuvikkum oṉṟiraṇṭu kāṭcikaḷik kāṇpōm. cāṭṭi koṇṭu pamparamviṭṭu viḷiyāṭum ciṟuvarkaḷi  nām pārttirukkiṉṟōm.  alakilā viḷiyāṭṭuṭi āṇṭavaṉ aṇṭaṅkaḷi āṭṭivippati iv viḷiyāṭṭukku oppumi kāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ oru kaviñaṉ.
cāṭṭi niṟkum aṇṭam elām
	cāṭṭi ilāp pamparam pōl
āṭṭuvikkum kuṟṟālattu
	aṇṇalār. 7
eṉṟu kūṟuvatil kaṟpaṉit tiṟaṉik kāṇka.  aṇṭaṅkaḷ cuḻalvatiyum pamparam cuḻalvatiyum oppiṭṭa murukuṇarcciyi eṇṇi makiḻka.  kāli iḷamparitiyiṉ eḻilik kāṭṭum maṟṟoru kaviñaṉ.
	taṅkam urukkit taḻalkuṟittut tēṉākki
eṅkum parappiyatōr iṅkitamō? 8
eṉṟu kūṟuvatil potintuḷḷa kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟalic cintittuk kaḷikka, itiviṭa vēṟu evvāṟu kūṟamuṭiyum?
nīlat tirikkaṭal arukē niṉṟukoṇṭu pāratiyār katiravaṉ maṟivatik kāṇkiṉṟār;  avar appoḻutu uṇarnta uṇarcci vimmitaṅkoṇṭu virikiṉṟatu pāñcāli capatattil;  arccuṉaṉ vāymoḻiyāka avar kaṇṭa kāṭciyik kūṟukiṉṟār.  pāṇṭavarkaḷ vituraṉ mūlam turiyōtaṉaṉ aṉuppiya aḻippip peṟṟu attiṉapurattiṟku varukiṉṟaṉar.  vaḻiyil mālippoḻutil pārttaṉ pāñcālikkup paritiyiṉ eḻili viḷakkukiṉṟāṉ.
aṭivāṉat tēaṅku paritik kōḷam
aḷappariya viriviṉoṭu cuḻalak kāṇpāy;
iṭivāṉat toḷimiṉṉal pattuk kōṭi
eṭuttu avaṟṟi oṉṟupaṭa urukki vārttu
muṭivāṉa vaṭṭattik kāḷi āṅkē
moykuḻalāy, cuṟṟuvataṉ moympu kāṇāy!
vaṭivāṉatu oṉṟākat takaṭu iraṇṭu
vaṭṭamuṟac cuḻaluvati vaḷintu kāṇpāy. 9 
parācakti kāḷitēvi pattukkōṭi miṉṉalip piṭittu vārtta pantutāṉ katiravaṉ eṉṟu kaṟpaṉi ceytiruppatu kaviñaṉatu kaṟpaṉi ceyyum āṟṟali viḷakkukiṉṟatu.  iṉi, pāratiyiṉ aṭiccuvaṭṭil varum pāratitācaṉ kāṭṭum muḻumatiyak kāṭciyiyum pārppōm.
  nīlavāṉ āṭikkuḷ uṭalma ṟittu
nilāeṉṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟāy oḷimu kattik
kōla muḻutuṅ kāṭṭiviṭṭāl kātaṟ
koḷḷiyilē ivvulakam cāmō? vāṉac
cōliyilē pūttataṉip pūvō nītāṉ!
cokkaveḷḷip pāṟkuṭamō? amuta ūṟṟō?
kālivanta cemparitik kaṭalil mūḻkik
kaṉalmāṟip kuḷiraṭinta oḷippi ḻampō! 10
iyaṟkiyākiya peṇ taṉatu uṭaliyellām nīlavāṉa mākiya āṭiyil maṟittukkoṇṭu mukatti maṭṭilum kāṭṭu kiṉṟāḷām;  muḻuvaṭiviyum kāṭṭiviṭṭāl ivvulaka makkaḷ kātaṟ koḷḷiyil iṟantu viṭuvārkaḷām;  maṟṟum,  kaviñaṉukkuc cantiraṉ cōliyilē pūtta taṉippūvākavum, cokkaveḷḷip pāṟkuṭamākavum, amuta ūṟṟākavum, kāliyil tōṉṟiya katiravaṉ kaṭalil mūḻki eḻuntatāl kuḷiraṭinta oḷip piḻampākavum kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟāṉ.
caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu taṟkālattil tōṉṟi varum ilakkiyaṅkaḷvari uḷḷa tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ittakiya kāṭcikaḷit tammakattē koṇṭuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷ eṇṇaṟṟavi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻmoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkariṉ aṟivunili, paṭṭaṟivu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu avaṟṟit tērnteṭuttuk kaṟpikkavēṇṭum.  avaṟṟik kaṟpikkumpoḻutē kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟaliyum murukuṇarc -ciyiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭum; kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟalukku aṭippaṭi uḷḷattil paṭintukiṭakkum uṇarcciyē.  uṇarcciyum kaṟpaṉiyum oṉṟiyoṉṟu piṉṉik kiṭakkum: oṉṟu kuṟintālum  ataṟkēṟṟavāṟu piṟitoṉṟaṉ āṟṟal kuṟintu viṭum. cintaṉiyum kāṭcittiṟaṉum kalantu uṇarccikaḷi veḷippaṭuttum ikkaṟpaṉiyāṟṟal uyarnta kaviñarkaḷiṭam kāṇappaṭum ōr aruḷāṟṟal.  ittakiya āṟṟal potintuḷḷa pāṭalkaḷik kaṟpikkum poḻutu māṇakkarkaḷiṭam meymaṟantu pāṭṭukkaḷit tuykkum āṟṟali uṇṭākkiviṭṭāl, avarkaḷiṭam atu vaḷarvatuṭaṉ avarkaḷākavē pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paṭittut tuykkum tiṟamiyiyum peṟuvar.  tāymoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ iti nōkkamākak koṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum.
vaḻivaḻi varum camūka iyaliyum paṇpāṭṭiyum aṟiyum tiṟavukōl:  ellā moḻikaḷilumē ammoḻi pēcukiṉṟavarkaḷiṉ paṇṭic camutāya vāḻviyum paṇ pāṭṭiyum aṟivataṟkuriya nūlkaḷ irukkum; perumpālum avi ilakkiyamākavum, varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷākavum tikaḻum.  āṉāl, tamiḻil vāḻvi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa, ilakkaṇa nūl iruppatu ammoḻiyiṉ taṉic ciṟappākum; vāḻkkikkē ilakkaṇam vakuttuk kāṭṭiya perumi tamiḻaṉuṭiyatu.  ivvulakil emmoḻiyilu mirāta perumi tamiḻ moḻiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.  puṟanāṉūṟu, akanāṉūṟu pōṉṟa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu paṇṭit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camūka vāḻkkiyiyum paṇpāṭṭiyum aṟintukoḷḷalā meṉṟālum ippaṭittāṉ kuṟikkōḷ vāḻkki amiya vēṇṭum. eṉṟu tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattil tiṭṭamāṉa kuṟippukaḷ kāṇap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻkkiyi akavāḻkki eṉṟum, puṟavāḻkki eṉṟum iru kūṟiṭṭup pēcuvar: vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōṭ poruḷākiya aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṟa nāṉku pakutikaḷil iṉpatti akattilum ēṉiya mūṉṟaṉip puṟattilum aṭakkiṉar.  kātali aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa vāḻkkiyi akapporuḷ eṉṟum, pōr, koṭi, naṭpu pōṉṟavaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa vāḻkkiyip puṟapporuḷ eṉṟum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil pirittup pēcappaṭum.  kātaliyum pōriyum kaliyāka uyarttiya perumi tamiḻarkaḷuṭiyatu. akapporuḷ, kuṭumpa vāḻk kiyip paṟṟiyatu:  uṇarcciyit talimi yākak koṇṭu taṉivāḻkkiyiṉ palvēṟu iyalpukaḷip paṟṟip pakuttup pēcuvatu. puṟapporuḷ, ulaka vāḻkkiyākiya potu vāḻkkiyip paṟṟiyatu; ceyali aṭippaṭiyākakkoṇṭu ulaka vāḻkkiyiṉ palvēṟu tuṟikaḷip pakuttup pēcuvatu.
iṉpatti nalkum kāmatti mūṉṟu pirivākap pirittu ilakkaṇa nūlār vakippaṭuttiyuḷḷaṉar. āṭavarilō makaḷirilō yārēṉum oruvar māttiram maṟṟoruvarmītu kātal koḷvatākac colvatu oru vaki; itu kikkiḷi  eṉappaṭum.  valiya iṉpam nukartal maṟṟoru vaki;  itaṉip peruntiṇi eṉṟu ilakkaṇa nulār kuṟippar.  mūṉṟāvatu vakitāṉ aṉpuṭik kāmam.  itu kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉṟu aintu tiṇikaḷākap pakuttup pēcap paṭuvatāl itaṉi aintiṇi neṟi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu.  tiṇi eṉṟa col tamiḻil oḻukkattik kuṟikkum.  aintu tiṇiyilum nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷi muṟiyē puṇartal, pirital, iruttal, ūṭal, iraṅkal eṉṟu paṇṭiyōr kuṟippiṭṭaṉar.  ippaṭiaintu vakiyākap pirittālum kati pōlat toṭarntu varum kātal vāḻkkiyil pala kaṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  ovvoru kaṭṭattilum pala ciṟu nikaḻccikaḷ nikaḻak kūṭum.  avaṟṟit tuṟi eṉṟu kūṟuvar ilakkaṇa nūlār.  kiṭṭattaṭṭa nāṉūṟu tuṟikaḷi akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷil kāṇalām.  kātal vāḻkkiyil paṭittuk kāṭṭappeṟum talivaṉum taliviyum kuṟikōḷ vāḻkki naṭattum puṉitamāṉavarkaḷ; avarkaḷ oru kālattil ōriṭattil irukkak kūṭiyavarkaḷ allar; eṉṟum ellā viṭattum nirantaramāka iruppavarkaḷ.  avarkaḷip peyar cuṭṭip kūṟuvatu kūṭat tavaṟu eṉṟu karutiṉar tamiḻarkaḷ.  ātalāltāṉ akapporuḷ pāṭalkaḷil peyar cuṭṭik kūṟappeṟum māntarkaḷik kāṇpatē illi.
puṟapporuḷil aṭaṅkiya kuṟikkōḷ poruḷkaḷ aṟam, poruḷ, vīṭu eṉṟa mūṉṟumākum.  puṟattavarkaḷukku veḷippaṭumpaṭiyāṉa nikaḻccikaḷ nikaḻvataṉāl itu puṟam eṉṟu cuṭṭappeṟṟatu.  akapporuḷiluḷḷa tiṇi tuṟikaḷip pōlavē puṟapporuḷilum tiṇi, tuṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  puṟap poruḷiṉ tiṇi, tuṟikaḷi vakuttuk kūṟuvatil iraṇṭu vakiyāṉa koḷkikaḷ uḷa.  akattiyam, tolkāppiyam eṉṟa nūlkaḷ puṟapporuḷ kaḷukku ēḻu tiṇikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avi veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki, kāñci, pāṭāṇ eṉpavi, veṭcittiṇi pakivaruṭiya pacuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷi avar aṟiyāmal koṇarntu pātukāttalic  ceyvatu. nāṭu piṭikkum āciyāl ōraracaṉ maṟṟōraracaṉmītu taṇṭeṭuttup pōrukkuc cellutal vañcittiṇi.  pakivar araṇi muṟṟukiyiṭuvatākac colvatu uḻiñittiṇi.  vīracciṟappu veḷippaṭum poruṭṭu vēntar porutali, eṭuttiyampuvatu tumpi.  pōril taliyiṭum aracarkaḷ mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ toḻilic ciṟappittu ōtutal vāki.  kāñci niliyāmiyi pulappaṭuttuvatu.  pukaḻtal, vāḻttutal vakiyāl palaric ciṟappittup pāṭutal pāṭāṇ tiṇiyākum.  paṉṉirupaṭalam, puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ tolkāppiyar kūṟiya tiṇikaḷil cilavaṟṟi iraṇṭākap pirittum, akattic cārtti vitta kikkiḷi peruntiṇi kaḷik kūṭṭiyum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟic cērttum puṟapporuḷip paṉṉiraṇṭākap pakuttup pēcukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟiṉ virivukaḷi uriya nulkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.11  akapporuḷ, puṟapporuḷ tuṟikaḷi vittup pāṭip piṟkālap pulavarkaḷ tam kāviyaṅ kaḷic ciṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭiruntu iṉṟu variyil tōṉṟiyuḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷip paṭikkumpoḻutu avi nērmukamākavum maṟimukamākavum avvak kālaṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ camūka iyaliyum paṇpāṭṭiyum viḷakkuvati aṟiyalām. 
vāḻkkiyi vaḷappaṭutti nītikaḷip pukaṭṭutal:  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil moḻi payaṉpaṭāta tuṟikaḷē illi. pēcutal, kēṭṭal, eḻututal ākiya mūṉṟu ceyalkaḷum payaṉpaṭāta vāḻkkit tuṟikaḷē illi eṉalām.  kalvi niliyaṅkaḷi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiya māṇakkarkaḷ vāḻvil nuḻiyumpoḻutu naṉṟākap pēcavum,  paṭikkavum, eḻutavum āṟṟal peṟṟirukkavēṇṭum.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyilum immūṉṟu tiṟaṉkaḷum peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  irupattoru āṇṭu muṭinta yāvarum vākkurimi peṟṟu makkaḷālēyē aracāṅkam niṟuvappeṟum ikkālattil ivi payaṉpaṭāta nēramē illi.  iḷamiyil tāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉṟēl ciṟanta maṉap payiṟci amiyātu; nāṭṭu varalāṟṟic cariyāka aṟintu koḷḷa iyalātu;  moḻippaṟṟum nāṭṭuppaṟṟum oruvariṭam amiyā;  nalloḻukkamum iṟiyaṉpum ēṟpaṭu vataṟkum tāymoḻip payiṟci mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  vāḻvi vaḷappaṭuttum ip paṇpukaḷil ovvoṉṟiyum kuṟittu oru ciṟitu ārāyvōm.  tāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉāl ivi  evvāṟu kivarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatiyum aṟivōm. 
eṇṇa vaḷarccikku moḻi iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  eṇṇamum  moḻiyum oṉṟiviṭṭu oṉṟupirikka muṭiyātapaṭi amintirukkiṉṟaṉa.  oṉṟiviṭṭup piṟitoṉṟi vaḷarkkavum iyalātu.  pēcappaṭum collukkum atu kuṟikkum poruḷukkum; pēcuvōṉukkum kēṭpōṉukkum nērāṉa toṭarpi ēṟpaṭuttuvatu moḻiyē.12  oru kuḻanti pacuvip pārkkiṉṟatu; iḵtu ataṟku oru putiya paṭṭaṟivu (Experience). ovvoru  taṭaviyilum atip pārkkumpōtu putiya paṭṭaṟivu akkuḻantikku ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. oru taṭavi pacu pullit tiṉpatiyum, maṟṟoru taṭavi akkuḻantiyiṉ aṉṉi pacuviṟkut tīṉi vittup pāl kaṟappatiyum, piṟitoru taṭavi pacu kaṉṟukkup pāl ūṭṭuvatiyum kuḻanti kāṇakkūṭum. intak kāṭcikaḷ yāvum akkuḻanti yiṉ maṉattil pativu peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  pacu eṉṟa collik kēṭṭavuṭaṉēyē pacuviṉ uruvam akkuḻantiyiṉ maṉattiriyil amikiṉṟatu; pacuvip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ yāvum ataṉ maṉattāl cēkarikkap peṟṟu ataṉuṭiya aṟivu mukaṭṭil vikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  toṭarpāka eḻum karuttukkaḷ aṉittiyum orē kālattil maṉattiṟkuk koṇṭuvara muṭikiṉṟatu.  oru moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷyāvum ippaṭippaṭṭaviyē.  moḻivaḷarcciyē nākarika vaḷarcciyākum.  tāymoḻiyiṉ vāyilāka eṇṇa vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭuvatu iyalpātalāl, atu mutalil kaṟpikkap peṟutal vēṇṭum.
maṉattiluḷḷa karuttit teḷivākak kāraṇakāriya muṟiyil veḷippaṭuttuvataṟkuc ciṟanta maṉappayiṟci vēṇṭaṟpālatu.  itit tāymoḻi vāyilākattāṉ eḷitil peṟa iyalum.  tāymoḻip paṭippē kalvikku aṭippaṭiyāka amintu nilittatum āḻamāṉatumāṉa uyar paṇpukaḷi oruvariṭam ēṟpaṭuttukiṉṟatu.  oru tiṭṭappaṭi vēli ceytal, maṉattiluḷḷa karuttukkaḷik kōviyāka veḷiyiṭal, kalittuṟikaḷil iṟaṅki uḻittal mutaliya naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷ yāvum tāymoḻip paṭippāltāṉ kivarappeṟum eṉṟu moḻimuṟi aṟinta mūtaṟiñarkaḷ moḻikiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē, tāymoḻip paṭippu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tāymoḻi ilakkiyattip paṭikkumpoḻututāṉ annāṭṭu varalāṟṟippaṟṟit teḷivāka aṟiyamuṭikiṉṟatu.  ilakkiyam vāḻkkiyiṉ kaṇṇāṭi eṉṟum, vāḻkkiyē moḻiyil ilakkiyamāka amikiṉṟatu eṉṟum mēṉāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ kūṟuvar.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka puṟanāṉūṟu pōṉṟa caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷāltāṉ paṇṭit tamiḻakattiṉ varalāṟu, paṇṭit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkimuṟi avarkaḷiṉ āṭcimuṟi,  camayanilikaḷ, kalikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  varalāṟṟup paṭippu, kalveṭṭukkaḷ,  ceppup paṭṭayaṅkaḷ, paḻaṅ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷ, nāṇayaṅkaḷ, ayalnāṭṭiṉar eḻutivitta kuṟippukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyamuṭiyāta uṇmikaḷiyellām ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl aṟiya muṭiyum.  kallilum cempilum eḻutivikka muṭiyāta  itaya pāvaṅkaḷi yellām ilakkiyamtāṉ nuṭpamāka virittuk kāṭṭum.  etirkāla vāḻvi amikkak kaṭantakāla vāḻvu peritum tuṇi puriyumātalāl, tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam atit teḷivāka aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
moḻippaṟṟum nāṭṭuppaṟṟum uṇṭāvataṟkut tāy moḻippaṭippu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  ellā makkaḷum tattam tāymoḻiyic ciṟappittup pēcutal iyalpeṉiṉum, emmoḻiyum peṟāta ciṟappiṉit tamiḻ moḻitāṉ peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  moḻiyi iṟiyuṭaṉ iyipupaṭuttip pēcum vaḻakkam nam nāṭṭiltāṉ uṇṭu.
	kaṇṇu taṟperuṅ kaṭavuḷum kaḻakamōṭu amarntu
 paṇṇu ṟatterintu āyntip pacuntamiḻ13 
eṉṟu tamiḻmoḻi pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  civaperumāṉē naṉpāṭṭup pulavaṉāyc caṅkam ēṟi tamiḻ āynta perumiyit tēvāram eṭuttiyampukiṉṟatu.  pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷi tamiḻ moḻikkup paramporuḷi uvami kūṟiyuḷḷār.  ōr elliyaṟṟa paramporuḷ pal uyirkaḷiyum pala ulakaṅkaḷiyum paṭittu, aḷittu, aḻittālum; tāṉ yātoru māṟupāṭiṉṟi, aṉṟum, iṉṟum, eṉṟum, iruppatupōlavē, namatu tamiḻ moḻiyum muṉṉip paḻamoḻikkum muṉṉip paḻamoḻiyāyum; piṉṉip putumoḻikkum piṉṉip putu moḻiyāyum polikiṉṟatu `eṉṟu pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār,  vaṭamoḻiyum, teṉmoḻiyum, civaperumāṉiṉ irukaṇkaḷ eṉṟu oru pulavar kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.  iṉṉoru pulavar,
	ōṅkal iṭivantu uyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālattu iruḷkaṭiyum-āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatir oṉṟu; ēṉiyatu
taṉṉēr ilāta tamiḻ. 14
eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.  ic ceyyuḷ pakalavaṉukkum tamiḻukkum cilēṭiyāy amintirukkiṉṟatu.  piṟitoru pulavar,
	poruppilē piṟantu, teṉṉaṉ
	pukaḻilē kiṭantu, caṅkattu
iruppilē iruntu, viyi 
	ēṭṭilē tavaḻnta pēti
neruppilē niṉṟu, kaṟṟōr
	niṉivilē naṭantuōr ēṉa
maruppilē payiṉṟa pāvi 
	maruṅkilē vaḷaru kiṉṟāḷ, 15
eṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟār.  ic ceyyuḷil tamiḻi oru kuḻantiyāka uruvakittu ataṉ vaḷarcciyi muṟiyē kāṭṭiyiruppatu aṟintu iṉpuṟattakkatu.  tamiḻ viṭu tūtu eṉṉum nūlil tamiḻiṉ ciṟappu virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  annūlāciriyar,
tittikkum teḷḷamutāyt teḷḷamutiṉ mēlāṉa
muttik kaṉiyē!eṉ muttamiḻē!-  puttikkuḷ
uṇṇap paṭuntēṉē! 16
eṉṟu tamiḻik kuḻintu pōṟṟuvatik kāṇka.
nāṭṭup paṟṟiṉ mutircciyāl viṭutaliyaṭinta makkaḷ moḻippaṟṟāl nikaḻvatik kāṇkiṉṟōm.
yāmaṟinta moḻikaḷilē tamiḻmoḻipōl
iṉitāva teṅkum kāṇōm
eṉṟu pāratiyiṉ kural tamiḻarkaḷiṭam tamiḻppaṟṟiṉit tūṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu.  tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷi naṉku cuvitta pāratitācaṉ kaṉṉal poruḷtarum tamiḻē nī ōr, pūkkāṭu; nāṉōr tumpi! eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.
tāymoḻi ilakkiyaṅkaḷ nāṭṭuppaṟṟiyum uṇṭākkum.  tamiḻiṉ āṇi nilaviya pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṟku vaṇakkam terivikkum pāṭal oṉṟil innāṭṭup paṟṟu veḷiyākiṉṟatu.
āviyan teṉṟal veṟpiṉ
	akattiyaṉ virumpum teṉpāl
nāvalan tīvam pōṟṟi
	nāvalan tīvan taṉṉuḷ 
mūvarkkum ariyāṉ niṟpa
	muttamiḻ teyvac caṅkap
pāvalar vīṟṟi rukkum
	pāṇṭinaṉ ṉāṭu pōṟṟi. 17
eṉpatu kaviñariṉ vākku.  tarumiyiṉ poruṭṭu vātāṭa vantapoḻutu civaperumāṉ niṟkavum, caṅkap pulavarkaḷellām vīṟṟirukkavum ceytatu tamiḻallavā? kampar, cēkkiḻār pōṉṟa peruṅ kaviñarkaḷiṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ittakiya pāṭalkaḷ pala uḷḷaṉa.  kaṉinta nāṭṭuppaṟṟākiya amutak kaṭalil tōṉṟiya pāratiyāriṉ pāṭṭukkaḷ nāṭṭuppaṟṟi eṉṟum vaṟṟātu curantu niṟkum ūṟṟukkaḷākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. 
	centamiḻ nāṭeṉṉum pōtiṉilē-iṉpat
tēṉvantu pāyutu kātiṉilē
eṉṟa aṭikaḷukku īṭāṉavi enta moḻiyilāvatu irukka muṭiyumā?
aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu aṭital nūṟpayaṉē18
eṉṟu ilakkaṇam vakutta perumi tamiḻmoḻikkut tāṉ uṇṭu.  nalloḻukkamum kaṭavuḷ aṉpumē kalviyiṉ kaṉinta payaṉkaḷ eṉṟu koṇṭa perumi tamiḻmaṟikkuttāṉ uṇṭu.
  kaṟka kacaṭaṟak kaṟpavi; kaṟṟapiṉ
niṟka ataṟkut taka.19
kaṟṟataṉāl āya payaṉeṉkol, vālaṟivaṉ
naltāḷ toḻāreṉiṉ?20
eṉpaṉapōṉṟa maṟimoḻikaḷ māṇākkarkaṭkuk kalviyiṉ muṭinta nōkkatti uṇartti avarkaḷ vāḻvic ceḻikkac ceykiṉṟaṉa.   tamiḻ moḻiyil aṟanūlkaḷukkum iṟi vaḻipāṭṭu nūlkaḷukkum kuṟivē illi; vāḻkkiyiṉ palvēṟu uṇmikaḷik kativaṭivil vittuk kāṭṭakkūṭiya kāviyaṅkaḷum pala uḷḷaṉa.  nēraṭiyāka nītiyip pukaṭṭum pākkaḷit tavira maṟimukamāka nīti kūṟum kavitikaḷiyum ērāḷamākak kāṇalām.
	nallatu ceytal āṟṟīr āyiṉum
allatu ceytal ōmpumiṉ; atutāṉ
ellārum uvappatu aṉṟiyum
nallāṟṟup paṭūu neṟiyumā ratuvē. 21
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa aṭikaḷ makkaḷākap piṟanta aṉivarukkumē ciṟantatōr oḻukkattip pukaṭṭum aṟavuriyāka miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.  civa camaya kuravarkaḷ nalkiya pāṭalkaḷum āḻvār maṇikaḷ aruḷiyuḷḷa pācuraṅkaḷum, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pāṭiyuḷḷa tiruvaruṭpākkaḷum, kalneñcattiyum karikkavallavi:  ūṉiṉic curukki uḷḷoḷi perukki uvappilā āṉantatti aḷikkak kūṭiyavi.
taṭittōr makaṉit tantikaṇṭu aṭittāl
	tāyuṭaṉ aṇippaḷ:  tāy aṭittāl
piṭittuoru tanti aṇippaṉ; iṅkueṉakkup
	pēciya tantiyum tāyum
poṭittiru mēṉi ampalattu āṭum
	puṉita! nī ātalāl eṉṉi
aṭittatu pōtum aṇittiṭal vēṇṭum
ammiyap pāiṉi āṟṟēṉ.22
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa iṟi vaḻipāṭṭik kūṟum pākkaḷ nantamiḻ moḻiyil eṇṇiṟantavi uḷḷaṉa.  tamiḻmoḻi payilvōr attakiya pāṭalkaḷip paṭittu iṉpuṟṟup payaṉaṭiyalām.
payiṟṟumoḻi:  tamiḻ moḻi ēṉiya pāṭaṅkaḷip payiṟṟum moḻiyāka (Medium) vantapiṟaku ammoḻiyik kaṟpikkum nōkkamum virivaṭin tirukkiṉṟatu.  ettaṉiyō kalit tuṟikaḷit tamiḻil kaṟpikkum nili ēṟpaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu; kallūrikaḷilum tamiḻ payiṟṟu moḻiyāka amiya vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar; atupaṟṟi ārāyntum varukiṉṟaṉar.  tāymoḻiyil poruḷuṇarntu aṟivi vaḷarttuk koḷvatil kālaccelavu, paṇaccelavu muyaṟci ākiyavi curuṅkum eṉpatu kūṟāmalē pōtarum.  puṭṭippāli viṭat tāyppāl ciṟantataṉṟō?  eṉavē, tāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉṟi paḷḷikaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum uṇmiyāṉa aṟivu vaḷarcciyē illiyeṉṟu kūṟiviṭalām.
ovvoruvaruṭiya uyarvukkum tāḻvukkum avaravaruṭiya aṟivē kāraṇamākum.  aṟivuṭiyār ellām uṭiyār eṉṟa vaḷḷuvar vākki aṟika.  āṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷiṭam iyalpāka aminta aṟivu ceyaṟki muṟiyāl vaḷarcciyaṭi kiṉṟatu; oruvaruṭiya paṭṭaṟivālum piṟar paṭṭaṟintavaṟṟik kēṭpatālum avaṟṟippaṟṟiya nūlkaḷip paṭippatālum vaḷarvatiyē ceyaṟki muṟiyāl vaḷarvatu eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum.  āḻntu cila nūlkaḷip paṭittu aṟivi vaḷarkkum vāyppukaḷ ikkālattil atikamāka uḷḷaṉa; kalvi paṉmuka virivi eyti yirukkiṉṟatu.  eṉavē, piṉṉar uyirkku uṟuti payappataṟku maṭṭilum nōkkamākak koṇṭa paṇṭik kālattu tāymoḻip paṭippip pōlaṉṟi ikkālat tāy moḻip paṭippu, makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, uṭalnalam, poruḷ nili, aṟiviyalkaḷ mutaliya pala tuṟikaḷippaṟṟiyum aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa nōkkat til amintirukkiṉṟatu.  ikkālat tēvikaḷiyoṭṭi kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarum ellāk kalikaḷiyum kaṟṟu aṟintu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa iyalpāṉa uṇarcci māṇākkarkaḷiṭam ēṟpaṭakkūṭum.  tāy moḻiyil uḷḷa puttakaṅkaḷi mēmpōkkākap paṭikkum paḻakkatālēyē iḵtu eḷitil niṟivēṟum. eṉavē, ikkālattiṉ aṟivuttuṟikku ēṟṟavāṟu coṟ celvamum naṭip poruttamum uṭiya tamiḻ moḻiyi vaḷarkkum muṟiyil  tamiḻk kaṟpittal amiyavēṇṭum.  ilakkiya naṭiyum ilakkiyak karuttukkaḷi uṇarttum tamiḻum piṟa pāṭak karuttukkaḷi uṇarttuvataṟku ēṟṟatāka irā.  tēvik kēṟṟavāṟu putu naṭiyiyum putiya kalic coṟkaḷiyum ākkac coṟkaḷiyum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyirukkum.  tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ innōkkattiyum naṉku aṟiya vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
tamiḻmoḻi payiṟṟu moḻiyāka vantapiṟaku paḷḷiyil perumpālāṉa nikaḻccikaḷ tamiḻilēyē naṭipeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ivviṭattil `jārj cāmcaṉ eṉpār āṅkila moḻikkuk kūṟiyuḷḷatu tamiḻukkum poruntum.  avar kūṟukiṉṟār:  tāymoḻip paḷḷi vāḻkkikkē iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  avvāḻkkiyiliruntu atip pirikka iyalātu; atu paḷḷiyākiya uṭalukku uyirāka iruppatu.  ovvoruvaruṭiya vāḻvilum atu toṭṭililiruntu cuṭukāṭu maṭṭum puṟakkaṇikka muṭiyāta oru niliyip peṟṟiruppatu.  aṟivi vaḷarttuk koḷḷum oru karuviyāka iruppatu maṭṭumaṉṟik kuḻantiyi vāḻkkiyil īṭupaṭuttum iṉṟiyamiyāta uṟuppākavum irukkiṉṟatu.23  eṉavē, tamiḻmoḻip payiṟci ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷukkum aṭippaṭiyāṉa potup payiṟci eṉṟum, tamiḻmoḻi, kalvit tiṭṭattil (Curriculum) kāṇappeṟum ēṉiya pāṭaṅkaḷip pōl oru pāṭam maṭṭilum aṉṟu eṉṟum, aḵtu aṅku iṅku eṉātapaṭi eṅkum pirakācamāyp paḷḷi vāḻkkiyiṉ cūḻniliyilellām ūṭuruvic cellum cātaṉam eṉṟum tamiḻāciriyar maṭṭumaṉṟi ellā āciriyarkaḷum talimiyāciriyar kaḷum araciṉarum uṇartal vēṇṭum.  uṇarntu, virivil kuṟikaḷik kaḷintu ākkavēlikkuriya aṉittiyum niṟivēṟṟat tuṇiyavēṇṭum.
tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ:  iṉṟu uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟi varum tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷil perumpālōr āṅkila aṟivu illātavarkaḷ: perumpālōr paḷḷiyiṟutit tērvuvari kūṭap potukkalvi peṟātavarkaḷ.*  ēṉiya pāṭaṅkaḷākiya kaṇitam, camūka iyal, aṟiviyal, āṅkilam ākiyavaṟṟik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷippōl ivarkaḷum paṭṭatārikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  avarkaḷippōla ivarkaḷum payiṟcik kalluriyil āciriyar payiṟciyip peṟṟu appayiṟciyilum paṭṭatārikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  iṉṟuḷḷa tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ paṭṭatārikaḷiviṭa moḻiyaṟivu peṟṟiruntālum, paṭṭatārikaḷukkirukkum vāyppukaḷ ivarkaḷukkilli.  paṭṭatārikaḷ peṟum payiṟci muṟiyi ivarkaḷ peṟa muṭivatilli.  kalvittuṟipaṟṟiya pala putiya karuttukkaḷi āṅkila nūlkaḷiliruntu terintu koḷḷum vāyppukkaḷum ivarkaḷuk killi.  kaṟpikkum muṟipaṟṟiyum, kalvittuṟiyil pal vēṟu poruḷ kaḷip paṟṟiyum tāymoḻiyil nūlkaḷ mikak kuṟivu; nalla nūlkaḷ illi yeṉṟē tuṇintu kūṟi viṭalām.  tāymoḻiyil ittakiya nūlkaḷ perukumvari tāy moḻiyik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ āṅkila aṟivu peṟṟup paṭṭatārikaḷāka iruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum. paṭṭatārikaḷāka illāviṭṭāl āṅkilam, kaṇitam, aṟiviyal, camūka iyal pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷippōl tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ pala putiya karuttukkaḷi aṟintukoḷvataṟku vāyppukaḷ irā; ēṉiyōr putiya karuttukkaḷit terintu koḷvatip pōl ivarkaḷ terintu koḷḷa iyalātu.
ēṉiya pāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ peṟṟuḷḷa kalviyaṟivu, payiṟṟum muṟi ākiyavaṟṟip pōl tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷum peṟṟiruntāl ivarkaḷiṭam vērūṉṟiyirukkum tāḻvuṇarcci ōraḷavu naṉṟākavē nīṅkiviṭum.  avvāciriyarkaḷip pōlavē ivarkaḷum paḷḷiyil naṭipeṟum ellā nikaḻccikaḷilum kalantu koḷḷavēṇṭum.  ivarkaḷ kuṭimip payiṟci, cāraṇarpaṭi, ceñciluvic caṅkam, cuṟṟulā ākiyavaṟṟilellām paṅku koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  palatiṟappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷip paṟṟipēcavum, uriyāṭavum vallavarkaḷākat tikaḻa vēṇṭum.  tāvara iyal, nila iyal, pirāṇi iyal, araciyal nūl, putika iyal, vētiyal, vāṉiyal pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷpaṟṟi yellām māṇavarkaḷiṭam uriyāṭum aṟivuṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntāl, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam nalla celvākkuṭaṉ tikaḻalām;  tām kaṟpikkum nūlkaḷil avvakik karuttukkaḷ varumpaṭi pāṭaṅkaḷ tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉar: akaṉṟa paṭippukkuriya nūlkaḷ palvēṟu poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiyaṉavāka veḷi varukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟik kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya niliyiluḷḷavarkaḷ ōraḷavu paḷḷiyiṟutit tērvu variyilāvatu potuk kalviyum palkalik kaḻakap paṭippum paṭṭamum peṟṟiruntāltāṉ ciṟappuṭaṉum tiṟami yuṭaṉum paṇiyāṟṟa muṭiyum.
tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ itukāṟum ilakkiya iṉpam aṭivatē kalviyiṉ nōkkam eṉṟu eṇṇiyiruntaṉar.  anta nōkkatti avarkaḷ ciṟitu māṟṟik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  moḻiyik kaṟpatu ilakkiyam paṭittu nukarvataṟkāka maṭṭum aṉṟu eṉpati uṇara vēṇṭum.  kalvi maṉitaṉi muḻu vaḷarcci aṭiyac ceyyum cātaṉam eṉpatiyum, ciṟanta kuṭimakaṉākac ceyvataṟkut tuṇipurivatum atuvē eṉpatu pōṉṟa nōkkaṅkaḷiyum avarkaḷ niṉivil iruttavēṇṭum.  uḷaviyal kaṟṟu, kuḻantiyiṉ maṉattip paṟṟiya navīṉak karuttuk kaḷi aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  pala kalvi aṟiñarkaḷ tam aṉupavattil kaṇṭaṟinta muṟikaḷiyellām terintu avaṟṟit tam moḻip payiṟṟalil evvevvāṟu kiyāḷalām eṉpatic cintittal vēṇṭum, ceviyiṉ vāyilākap payiṟṟu vatuṭaṉ ēṉiya pulaṉkaḷ vāyilākavum uyarttiṉāl māṇākkarkaḷ pāṭatti naṉku uṇarvar eṉpati aṟintu ataṟkēṟṟa muṟikaḷik kiyāḷavēṇṭum.  paṭaṅkaḷ, nāṭṭup paṭaṅkaḷ, ōviyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum. ēṉiya āciriyarkaḷippōl māṇākkarkaḷukkup pāṭattil evvevvāṟu cuviyūṭṭi ārvatti eḻuppalām? entat tuṇikkaruvikaḷi mēṟ koṇṭāl avarkaḷ kavaṉattip pāṭattil niliniṟuttalām? uṭaṟcōrvip pōlavē maṉaccōrvum uṇṭu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟit tam uḷḷattil amintu avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟavāṟu kaṟpittali māṟṟiyamittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
tāymoḻiyik kaṟpataṟkum vāḻkkikkum uḷḷa toṭarpi aṭikkaṭi māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarumpaṭi ceytal vēṇṭum.  ēṉiya āciriyarkaḷip pōlavē ceyalkaḷ mūlamum tām kaṟpittali mēṟkoḷḷalām.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyiyum kuḻantikaḷ vāḻum cūḻniliyiyum naṉku kavaṉittāl ettaṉiyō nikaḻccikaḷ itaṟkut tuṇi ceyyum; avaṟṟip payaṉuḷḷa vāṟu amittuk koḷvatiltāṉ āciriyarkaḷiṉ tiṟaṉ  irukkiṉṟatu.  paḷḷi nikaḻccikaḷilellām paṅkeṭuttukkoḷḷum āciriyarkaḷukku ivi yāvum eḷitil tōṉṟupaviyē.  iṉṟu ettaṉiyō tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ karum palakiyikkūṭac cariyākap payaṉpaṭuttuvatilli.  kaṭiṉac coṟkaḷukkup poruḷ eḻututal, ilakkaṇa vitikaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ tarutal, cila poruḷkaḷip paṭampōṭṭu viḷakkutal ākiyavaṟṟiṟkuk karumpalakiyip payaṉpaṭuttalām.  kaṟpittalil karumpalakiyiviṭac ciṟantatōr tuṇik karuvi illi eṉṟu kūṭac collumpaṭi atu payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.  kavaṉak kuṟivuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷ, kēḷvi uṇarcci kuṉṟiyuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷukkuk karumpalakic cātaṉam ciṟanta tuṇiyāka irukkum eṉpati aṉupavattāl aṟiyalām.
tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷum ēṉiya āciriyarkaḷip pōlavē niṉṟu koṇṭu kaṟpittal vēṇṭum.  niṉṟukoṇṭu kaṟpittal māṇākkarkaḷukkum āciriyarkaḷukkum iṭiyēyuḷḷa kaṇṇāl kāṇamuṭiyāta māṉacīka uṟavi naṉku vaḷarkkum. amarntu kaṟpittāl aṭikkaṭikkarum palakiyip payaṉpaṭuttutal, paṭaṅkaḷiyum ēṉiya tuṇikkaruvikaḷiyum tiṟaṉuṭaṉ kiyāḷutal  pōṉṟavaṟṟi naṉku niṟivēṟṟa muṭiyātu.  aṭikkaṭi rakara-ṟakara vēṟupāṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ, lakara-ḷakara-ḻakara vēṟupāṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟik karumpalakiyil eḻutikkāṭṭiṉāl, māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttup piḻikaḷ kuṟiyum.  kaṭpulap payiṟciyiṉālaṉṟi vēṟu muṟikaḷiṉāl eḻuttup piḻikaḷik kuṟikka muṭiyātu.
tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ viṉākkaḷiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyi  naṉku uṇartal avaciyam; kalvi payiṟṟalil viṉākkaḷiṉṟi nallaveṟṟiyik kāṇamuṭiyātu.  pāṭattit toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉ tērnteṭuttuḷḷa cila viṉākkaḷi viṭuttu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ muṉṉaṟivic cōtittal vēṇṭum; muṉṉaṟivi aṟiyātu pāṭattit toṭaṅkip payaṉilli.  pāṭa vaḷarcciyiṉ poḻutu aṭikkaṭi viṉākkaḷi viṭuttu viṭiyaḷikkac coṉṉāl tāṉ māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyarip piṉpaṟṟuvatu teriyum; pāṭattip purintukoḷḷukiṉṟaṉarā eṉpati aṟiyavum muṭiyum.  pāṭatti muṭitta piṉṉarum viṉākkaḷi viṭuttāl tāṉ pāṭattil evvaḷavu kaṟṟuk koṇṭuḷḷaṉar  eṉpati  aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum.  aṉṟiyum, viṉākkaḷi aṭikkaṭik kiyāṇṭāltāṉ māṇākkar tam karuttit teḷivāka uṇarttalil nalla payiṟciyip peṟavum muṭiyum; kavaṉak kuṟivuṭaṉiruppavarkaḷik kavaṉikkac ceyyavum,  tūṅkum cilari viḻippuṭaṉirukkac ceyyavum ivviṉākkaḷ peruntuṇi puriyum.
nalla muṟiyil viṉākkaḷ viṭuttal eḷitaṉṟu.  nallapaṭṭaṟivuḷḷa āciriyarkaḷtām avaṟṟi naṉku kiyāḷa muṭiyum.  nalla viṉākkaḷ nalla cintaṉiyiṉiṉṟu tāṉ tōṉṟa muṭiyum.  nalla coṟkaḷāl teḷivāṉa muṟiyil viṉākkaḷi uruvākkic ciṟanta muṟiyil kiyāḷa vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṭam viṉākkaḷi viṭukkumpoḻutu avaṟṟi aṭikkaṭi māṟṟiyamittāl avarkaḷiṭam mayakka uṇarcciyi uṇṭākkum; cintaṉiyiṉ viḷivākat tōṉṟiya viṉākkaḷi māṟṟi yamikka vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmi irātu. nalla muṟiyil viṉākkaḷit tayārittaṟkuk kālac celavum cintaṉiyum vēṇṭum; māṇākkarkaḷiṉ viṭikaḷi ēṟṟamuṟiyil payaṉpaṭutta aṉupavam vēṇṭum.  muṉ āyattamiṉṟi vakuppiṟkuc cellum moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ ittuṟiyil veṟṟikāṇa muṭiyātu.  viṉākkaḷit tayārittal ēṉiya āciriyarkaḷiviṭa moḻiyāciriyar kaḷukku mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu. moḻittuṟiyil viṉākkaḷi āyattam  ceytal eḷitaṉṟu.
itiliruntu tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ vakuppiṟkuc cellu vataṟkumuṉ muṉ-āyattam ceyyavēṇṭiyataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi teḷivākiṉṟatu.  piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷip payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ muṉ-āyattam ceykiṉṟaṉarā eṉpatu vēṟu viṣayam; āṉāl avarkaḷ muṉ-āyattattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi maṟuppatilli.  āṉāl, tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷukku muṉ-āyattam ceytal eṉpatu putiya tuṟi.  avarkaḷ pāṭaputtaṅkaḷukkuttāṉ mukkiyattuvatti aḷikkiṉṟaṉar; puttakattil uḷḷavaṟṟittāṉ māṇākkar maṉattil tiṇippatu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  kaṟpikkum nēratti yellām antak kiṅkariyattiltāṉ celaviṭu kiṉṟaṉar.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil moḻi evvevvāṟu payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatit tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ  cintittup pārttāl, tām kaṟpikkum muṟiyi māṟṟikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya taṉ avaciyatti uṇarvar; māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉa nilik kēṟṟavāṟu ev vevvāṟu muṉ-āyattam ceyyappeṟutal vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum aṟivar.
ivaṟṟit tavira moḻipayiṟṟutaṟkeṉac ciṟappāṉa cila vitikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟit tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟintirukkavēṇṭum.  avarkaḷ moḻikaṟpippataṉ ciṟappāṉa nōkkaṅkaḷip paricīlaṉi ceyya vēṇṭum. pēccu, oppuvittal, vāyviṭṭup paṭittal. kaṇṇōṭṭamākap paṭittal, poruḷuṇartal, (Comprehension), coṟkaḷañ ciyappēṟu,  eḻututal, paṭippāṟṟal pōṉṟa moḻit tiṟaṉkaḷip paricīlaṉi ceyya vēṇṭum.  ceyyuḷ , urinaṭi eṉṟa iruvēṟu moḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷip pākupāṭu ceytu avaṟṟi moḻi kaṟpippataṉ nōkkattiṟkēṟpa evvevvāṟu kaṟpikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟikaḷit tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.
uyarnilippaḷḷikaḷil moḻi kaṟpittali mēṟkoṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟi varum tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ toṭakka nilip paḷḷikaḷil mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya vitikaḷiyum muṟikaḷiyum kiyāḷavēṇṭiya vāyppukaḷip peṟuvatilli.  māṇākkarkaḷ toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil aintāṇṭu moḻippayiṟci peṟṟu mutaṟpaṭivattil vantu cērkiṉṟaṉar.  immāṇākkarkaḷ uri naṭiyi vāyviṭṭup paṭikkavum, cātāraṇa ceyyuṭkaḷit taṭiyiṉṟi virivākap paṭikkavum payiṟci peṟṟiruppatuṭaṉ kaṇṇōṭṭamākap paṭippatilum ōraḷavu payiṟci peṟṟiruppar.  ippayiṟci kuḻantikaḷiṉ taṉippaṭṭa tiṟami, avarkaḷ vāḻum cūḻnili, peṟṟa payiṟci mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa māṟupaṭum.  kuḻanti ilakkiyaṅkaḷi eḷitil peṟṟup paṭittup payiṟciyaṭinta nakarppuṟac ciṟuvarkaḷ avvita ilakkiya vacatikaḷip peṟamuṭiyāta nāṭṭuppuṟac ciṟuvarkaḷiviṭa atikattiṟamiyuṭaṉiruppar.  ivaṟṟiyellām tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟintu tam payiṟṟum muṟiyi ēṟṟavāṟu māṟṟiyamittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum,  aṅṅaṉam māṟṟiyamittuk koḷḷāviṭil ellā māṇākkarkaḷum tām etirpārkkum aḷavu moḻiyaṟivu peṟa muṭiyātu.
iṉṟiya niliyil paṭṭatārikaḷ tamiḻi naṉku payiṉṟu tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟa muṉvantāl, tamiḻp payiṟṟum muṟi pala tuṟikaḷil virivaṭiyum.  āṉāl, paṭṭatārikaḷ iṉṟu tamiḻ payiṟṟu muṟiyi viruppappāṭamāka eṭuttukkoṇṭāl taṅkaḷ nilikkuk kuṟivu ēṟpaṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  innili virivil māṟavēṇṭum.  āṅkilam paṭittup paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyip payiṟṟal perumi eṉṟa karutti makkaḷiṭiyē kāl koḷḷac ceyyavēṇṭum.  āṅkila aṟivuṭaṉ paṭṭam peṟṟa tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷukku ūtiya aḷavilum piṟa tuṟikaḷilum ciṟitu atikac calukikaḷ aḷikkap peṟṟāl, pala paṭṭatārikaḷit tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟac ceyyamuṭiyum.  āṅkilam payiṟṟu moḻiyāka irunta poḻutu āciriyar payiṟci peṟṟa ovvoruvarum kaṭṭāyamāka āṅkila moḻiyik kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷilum payiṟci peṟṟaṉar.  atupōlavē tāymoḻi payiṟṟu moḻiyāka vantirukkum ik kālattil aṉivarum tāy moḻippayiṟṟalil kaṭṭāyamākap payiṟci peṟa vaḻiceyya vēṇṭum.  āṉāl, iṉṟiya nili eṉṉa? piṟapāṭaṅkaḷip payiṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ tāymoḻiyip paṟṟic ciṟitum kavali koḷḷuvatilli.  tām tāymoḻiyil cātāraṇamākac ceyyum piḻikaḷippaṟṟiyum kavalip paṭuvatilli. tāymoḻiyil eppaṭi vēṇṭumāṉālum eḻutalām eṉṟa maṉappāṉmi mēlum kēṭu payakkiṉṟatu.  inniliyi iṉi vaḷaraviṭakkūṭātu.  araciṉar ittuṟiyil ciṟitu taṉikkavaṉam celuttiṉāl virivil ciṟanta tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷit tōṟṟuvikka muṭiyum.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ avaciyatti naṉkuṇarnta uyartiru.ti. cu. aviṉāciliṅkam avarkaḷ tām kalviyamiccarāka irunta kālattil uyarnilippaḷḷik kalvit tiṭṭattil tāymoḻikku mutaliṭam aḷittārkaḷ.  tāymoḻi kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷiṉ niliyiṉiyum ōraḷavu uyarttiṉārkaḷ.  itukāṟum kalvit tuṟiyiṉ varalāṟṟilēyē irāta perumiyit tāymoḻi āciriyarukku aḷittārkaḷ.  kallūrikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟivarum tāymoḻiyillāta piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷukku iruntuvarum aracitaḻil pativu peṟum niliyit (Gazetted rank) tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷukkum mutaṉmutalāka vaḻaṅkiya perumi ivaric cārum. 
tamiḻum piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷum: iṉṟu āṅkilattit tavira uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷiṉ kalvit tiṭṭattiluḷḷa pāṭaṅkaḷ yāvum tāymoḻiyilēyē kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. inniliyil tamiḻi oru taṉippāṭam eṉṟu karututal tavaṟu. piṟapāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkumpoḻutum cariyāṉa moḻiyik kiyāḷa vēṇṭum; appāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ moḻiyuṇarcciyuṭaṉ tam viḻumiya paṇiyi āṟṟavēṇṭum. āṅkilam payiṟṟu moḻiyāka iruntapoḻutu eṅṅaṉam ellā āciriyarkaḷum pāṭa āciriyarkaḷum avvappōtu māṇakkarkaḷ āṅkila moḻiyil ceyyum piḻikaḷik kaḷintaṉarō, atu pōlavē iṉṟu ellā āciriyarkaḷum tamiḻmoḻiyil māṇākkar ceyyum piḻikaḷik kaḷiya muyalavēṇṭum. curuṅkak kūṟiṉāl ellā āciriyarkaḷum, pāṭa āciriyarkaḷāka iruppatuṭaṉ moḻiyāciriyar kaḷākavum paṇiyāṟṟavēṇṭum. māṇākkar vaḻaṅkum tāy moḻiyil kāṇum piḻikaḷukku tamiḻāciriyar maṭṭilum poṟuppāḷikaḷ ākār; piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷum ōraḷavu poṟuppāḷikaḷāka irukkattāṉ vēṇṭum. innili ēṟpaṭa vēṇṭumāṉāl paṭṭatārikaḷ tamiḻik kaṟpikkum nili varavēṇṭum. piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaṭku oru vakuppiṟkāvatu tāymoḻiyik kaṟpikkum vāyppiṉi nalka vēṇṭum; avarkaḷi ataṟkut takutiyāḷarkaḷākavum ākkavēṇṭum.
uṭaṉaṭiyākac ceyyappeṟa vēṇṭiyavi: tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcci karutic cila cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ uṭaṉaṭiyākac ceyyap peṟal vēṇṭum. kallūrit tamiḻp pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷi māṟṟiyamittup paṭṭatārikaḷiṭam nalla tamiḻaṟivu ēṟpaṭum vaḻivakikaḷi vakukkavēṇṭum. tāymoḻi payilātu paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ āciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyēṟka varumpoḻutu avarkaḷi appaṭṭattiṟkuriya tamiḻt tērvil tammit takutiyākkik koṇṭutāṉ varavēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāya viti ceyyavēṇṭum. iyaṉṟavari, paṭṭat tērvuvari tamiḻ payilātavarkaḷit tamiḻil piṟapāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟum poṟuppukaḷik kuṟittal vēṇṭum. pi.ē. variyilāvatu tamiḻ payilātavarkaḷ piṟapāṭaṅ kaḷit tamiḻil kaṟpittal poruttamaṉṟu; pi. eci. paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ* vittuvāṉtoṭakkanilit tērvu variyilāvatu tamiḻ payiṉṟāltāṉ nalla tamiḻmoḻi aṟivippeṟa muṭiyum. uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil talimiyāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟu vōr tāy moḻip paṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum. tāmum koñcam tamiḻ payiṉṟu tamiḻil aṟivu peṟātavari tamiḻmoḻiyiṉmītu eṅṅaṉam ārvaṅ kāṭṭamuṭiyum? eṉavē, talimiyāciriyarkaḷāka niyamaṉam peṟupavarkaḷ ēṉiya tiṟamikaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻmoḻip pulamiyum ōraḷavu peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum. talimiyāciriyarkaḷ tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarcciyil akkaṟi kāṭṭātavari paḷḷikaḷil nalla tamiḻi etirpārkka muṭiyātu. iṉṟu pi. ṭi. vakuppil tamiḻ kaṟpittali viruppap pāṭamāka eṭuttup payiṉṟavarkaṭkut tamiḻāciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟum vāyppiṉi nalkavēṇṭum. pi.ṭi. tērvil tamiḻk kaṟpittal viruppap pāṭamāka iruppati nīkkik kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākac ceyyavēṇṭum.+ tāymoḻiyil nalla aṟivu peṟātavari, tāymoḻiyil piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷit tiṟamiyākak kaṟpittal iyalātu. tāymoḻiyil piṟapāṭaṅ kaḷik kaṟpippavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyiyum kaṟpittal arumiyaṉṟu. eṉavē, pi. ṭi. tērvukkuc cellum aṉivarukkum tāymoḻi (maṇṭala moḻi) kaṟpikkum payiṟcikaḷi aḷittal porut tamāṉatē. nāḷaṭivil uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ eṉṟa taṉi vakuppiṉarē illātu ceytalvēṇṭum. pi. ō. el. paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ piṟapāṭaṅkaḷip payiṉṟu paṭṭam peṟavum, piṟapāṭaṅkaḷil paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyil paṭṭam peṟavum vāyppukkaḷi nalka vēṇṭum. iṉip paṭṭam peṟṟu uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil āciriyarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟupavarkaḷ tāymoḻiyilum piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷilum nalla pulami aṭiyum vāyppukkaḷip peṟuvataṟkēṟṟavāṟu ovvoru paṭṭaṅkaḷuk kuriya pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷil māṟṟam ceyyap palkalik kaḻakaṅkaḷ muṉvarutal vēṇṭum. kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ, palkalik kaḻakattiṉar, araciṉar ākiya mūvakiyiṉarum oruṅku ārāyntu ceyyappeṟāta entak kalvit tiṭṭaṅkaḷum naṟpayaṉi viḷivikkā.

pirivu-2: tiṭṭaṅkaḷum payiṟṟu muṟikaḷum
kalvit tiṭṭam eṉpatu, toṭakka nili, uyar nili, kallūri nilip paṭippukaḷil kaṟka vēṇṭiya pāṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟum oru tiṭṭam. pāṭat tiṭṭam eṉpatu, attiṭṭattil uḷḷa pāṭap poruḷ oṉṟil amiya vēṇṭiya pāṭa vivaraṅkaḷik kuṟippatu. muṉṉatu kalvit tuṟikaḷik kuṟikkiṉṟatu; piṉṉatu attakiya tuṟikaḷ ovvoṉṟilumuḷḷa pāṭaṅkaḷpaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷit terivikkiṉṟatu. tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ kalvit tiṭṭam, moḻippāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷit teḷivāka aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷik karuvikaḷākak koṇṭu payiṟṟuṅkāl tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ payiṟṟaliṉ cila aṭip paṭiyāṉa vitikaḷi aṟintu koḷḷavēṇṭum. ūkkuvittal, ceyalmūlam kaṟṟal, kaṟpaṉavaṟṟi vāḻkkiyuṭaṉ iṇittuk kāṭṭal, kaṟṟalil akkaṟiyi uṇṭākkal, kaṟpittalil mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya aintu paṭikaḷ ākiyavi avaṟṟuḷ cila. paṇṭiruntu iṉṟu vari ettiṉiyō payiṟṟu muṟikaḷi aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ māṇakkarkaḷ kuḻuvāka niṉṟu payilvataṟkum, taṉittaṉiyāka iruntu payilvataṟkum eṉat taṉittaṉi muṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. immuṟikaḷ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷic cērnta kalvi nipuṇarkaḷāl vakukkap peṟṟavi. āciriyar vakuppaṟiyiliruntu kaṟpikkumpoḻutu cila upāyaṅ kaḷikkiyāṇṭu kaṟpittāl payiṟṟuvikkap peṟum poruḷ māṇākkar maṉattil pacumarat tāṇipōl patiyum; māṇākkar kaṭkuk kaṟpatilum ūkkammikum; uṟcākam kiḷarnteḻum. āciriyarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷum upāyaṅkaḷuḷ vāy moḻiviṉākkaḷ, viḷakkan tarutal, tuṇik karuvikaḷ, vīṭṭu vēli ākiyavi kuṟippiṭat takkavi; ellāvaṟṟiṟkum iṉṟiyamiyātavi. icceytikaḷ ip pirivil aintu iyalkaḷil viḷakkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
2. kalvittiṭṭamum moḻippāṭattiṭṭamum
tamiḻp payiṟṟuvataṉ nōkkaṅkaḷi aṟinta āciriyarkaḷ annōkkaṅkaḷi niṟivēṟṟuvatu eṅṅaṉam eṉpatic cintikkavēṇṭiyatu avaciyamākum. ataṟkēṟṟa oru kalvit tiṭṭamum antat tiṭṭattil moḻip pāṭattiṭṭamum eṅṅaṉam amikkappeṟal vēṇṭum eṉpatippaṟṟiya oru cila karuttukkaḷi īṇṭu ārāyvōm.
kalvit tiṭṭattit takkavāṟu vakuttuk koṇṭutāṉ kaṟpittalit toṭaṅkavēṇṭum. toṭakkanili, naṭunili, uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷukkēṟṟa kalvit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ nalla muṟiyil uruvākkap peṟutal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟi vakuppatil mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiya aṭippaṭik karuttukkaḷi ēṉiya āciriyarkaḷip pōlavē tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷum naṉku terintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. caṟṟuc cintittup pārttāl, tāymoḻi payiṟṟumoḻiyāka aminta piṟaku avarkaḷtāṉ avaciyam aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉpatu teriya varum. ovvoru nilikkuriya kalvittiṭṭattil tāy moḻippāṭattiṉ iṭam eṉṉa? tāymoḻip pāṭattiṭṭam evvāṟu vakukkappeṟa vēṇṭum?  eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vivaraṅkaḷiyum avarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ikkaruttukkaḷi aṟiyātu tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ kuḻantikaṭkuk kaṟpittāl atil payaṉ oṉṟum irātu; ivi taliyaminta yāṉikku viṉiyamitta pākaṉ pōlavum, aḷappariya ākāyattiṟku viḷakkamākiya tiṅkaḷum ñāyiṟum pōlavum avarkaḷkaruttil amintu avartam paṇiyip payaṉuṟum vaḻiyil ciṟappikkum.  ivaṟṟi aṟiyātu kaṟpippavar kuṉṟu muṭṭiya kuruvipōlavum, kuṟicci pukka māṉpōlavum iṭarppaṭuvatuṭaṉ kaṟpikkappeṟum māṇāk karkaḷum virumpiya payaṉi eytamuṭiyātu.  iṉi, kalvittiṭṭatti vakuppatil tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ tam maṉattil irutta vēṇṭiya orucila karuttukkaḷik kāṇpōm.
kalviyiṉ avaciyam: kuḻantikaḷ takka cūḻniliyil nalla muṟiyil uṭal uramum, maṉa vaḷamum peṟṟuc ciṟanta kuṭimakkaḷākat tikaḻavēṇṭu meṉpatē kalviyiṉ nōkkamākum.  ataṟkēṟṟa kalvit tiṭṭam vakukkappeṟal vēṇṭum.  ettakiya kalvittiṭṭamum iṉṟiya camūka nilikkup poruntumāṟum, etirkālac camūka nilikku ēṟṟavāṟum irukkumpaṭi amiya vēṇṭum.  camūka niliyum kuḻantikaḷiṉ iyalpumē kalvit tiṭṭattiṉ mukkiya ellik kōṭukaḷi variyaṟi ceyyum.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ pala vaḷarccippaṭikaḷ, camūkat tēvikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ poruntāta kalvit tiṭṭattāl yātoru payaṉum irātu.
mūvakippaṭṭa karuttukkaḷ.  kalvi niliyaṅkaḷil naṭimuṟiyil amiya vēṇṭiya kalvit tiṭṭaṅkaḷik kuṟittup paṇṭiruntu iṉṟuvari pala kalvi nipuṇarkaḷum aṟiñarkaḷum pala vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷit terivittuḷḷaṉar.  kuḻantiyip paṟṟiyavi, vāḻkkiyil payaṉ tarattakkavi, kuṟikkōḷ taṉmiyiyuṭiyavi eṉṟu avaṟṟi mūṉṟu vakiyil aṭakkalām.  ovvoṉṟiyumpaṟṟi oru ciṟitu īṇṭu viḷakkuvōm.
(a) kuḻantiyip paṟṟiyavi  kalvit tiṭṭatti uruvākkuvōr kuḻantikaḷiṉ palvēṟu iyalpukaḷi mukkiya nōkkamākak koḷḷavēṇṭu meṉṟu ikkoḷkiyi yuṭiyār vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉar.  kuḻantiyiṉ paṭṭaṟivu, ceyalkaḷ, viruppaṅkaḷ ākiyavikaḷē paḷḷi naṭimuṟiyit tīrmāṉikka vēṇṭumeṉṟum ivarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  kuḻanti kaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa vaḷarcciyil vayatāṉavarkaḷ tam paṭṭaṟivi oṭṭiyō tam viruppatti oṭṭiyō pala karuttukkaḷi valintu pukuttutal kūṭātu.
im muṟiyil oru tiṭṭamāṉa muṭivē illi; kuḻantikaḷ viṭutali uṇarcciyuṭaṉum, uṟcākattuṭaṉum, makiḻcciyuṭaṉum vaḷarntu iyalpāṉa muṟiyil aṟivi aṭitalē itaṉ nōkkamākak koḷḷalām.  aṟivi nōkkamākak koṇṭa oru tiṭṭamāṉa muṭivu itil illi.
