viḷiyāṭṭu eṉpatu vāḻkkiyiṉ āyattam eṉṟu poruḷkoṇṭu maṟṟoru koḷki tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu.  mēli ḵpirāṉci eṉpār ati mutalil toṭaṅkiṉār; kārl kurū eṉpār ati naṉku ātarittu viḷakkam tantār.  paṭimuṟi vaḷarcci  (Evolution) ēṇiyil uyarntapaṭiyiluḷḷa pirāṇikaḷiṭantāṉ viḷiyāṭṭu teḷivākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu avar kūṟukiṉṟār.  pūṉiyum nāyum piṟantavuṭaṉē vāḻkkikkup pakkuvamaṭivatilli.  pakkuvam aṭiyum kālam eytumvari pātukāppiṟkum uṇaviṟkum avi peṟṟōriyē nāṭukiṉṟaṉa.  akkālatti avi viḷiyāṭṭil kaḻikkiṉṟaṉa.  kīḻppaṭikaḷil uḷḷa eṟumpum tēṉīyum cūḻniliyi virivil aṉucarittuk koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa; uyarniliyiluḷḷa pirāṇikaḷ tam vaḷarnta paruvattil kuṟukkiṭum tīviramāṉa ceykikaḷiyellām avi nam kuḻantip paruvattilēyē etirpārttu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. eṉṟu kurū karutukiṉṟār.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, pūṉikkuṭṭi aciyumporuḷkaḷit toṭarntu viraṭṭukiṉṟatu; atu taṉ piṟkāla vāḻvil eliyip piṭikkum payiṟciyi aṭikiṉṟatu.  avvāṟē nāykkuṭṭiyum caṇṭi viḷiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭu kiṉṟatu; atutaṉ piṟkāla vāḻviṟkup payaṉpaṭum muṟiyil payiṟciyiṉip peṟukiṉṟatu.  eṉavē, viḷiyāṭṭu uyirppirāṇikaḷiṉ tēviyi yoṭṭiya uṭaṉ piṟanta paṇpu eṉpatu kurūsiṉ kōṭpāṭu.  tīviramākap payaṉpaṭuvataṟku muṉṉar pirāṇikaḷiṉ iyalpūkkaṅkaḷ etirkālattil vēṇṭiya tiṟaṉi yoṭṭip payiṟciyaṭikiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu avar karutukiṉṟār.  akkoḷkiyum ellāvita viḷiyāṭṭukaṭkum viḷakkam taravilli.  vēṇṭu māṉāl, ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉāl pōr vīraṉāka viḷiyāṭap paṭum viḷiyāṭṭiyum ciṟumi oruttiyāl viḷiyāṭappaṭum pommip parāmarippu viḷiyāṭṭiyum vāḻkkiyiṉ āyatta viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḷākak karutalām: āṉāl, ellā vaṟṟiyum ivvāṟu karutuvataṟkilli; karutavum iyalātu.  āyiṉum, kurū iṉṉateṟkeṉṟu aṟintu koḷḷāmalēyē kuḻantikaḷ piṟkālattiṟkuriya cikkalāṉa vāḻkkikkut tammi āyattam ceytu koḷkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu vātiṭukiṉṟār.
ṭāṉli hāl eṉṟa amerikka uḷaviyaṟkaliñar kuḻantikaḷ viḷiyāṭuvati vāḻkki-āyattam eṉṟu karutuvativiṭa, maṉita iṉam pallūḻikālattil aṭinta aṉupavaṅkaḷik kuḻantip paruvattil tirumpavum aṭikiṉṟatu eṉṟu karutuvatu poruttam eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.  pūṉik kuṭṭiyiṉ viḷiyāṭṭiṟkum nāyk kuṭṭiyiṉ viḷiyāṭṭiṟkum akkoḷkiyāl nalla viḷakkam taralām.  turatti viḷiyāṭutal, vēṭṭiyāṭal, mīṉpiṭittal, kalleṟital, vīṭukaṭṭutal, kukikaḷil ārvaṅkāṭṭal ākiya aṉittum vaḻivaḻiyāka varum kuṭivaḻip paḻakkatti naṉku niṉivūṭṭum ceyalkaḷ eṉpatu hāliṉ karuttu.
hāliṉ koḷkiyi yoṭṭiyē jēm rā eṉpār kālutaṟ koḷki (Cathartic theory) eṉṟa koḷkiyik kuṟippiṭukkiṉṟār. Catharsis eṉṟa colli mutaṉ mutal kiyāṇṭavar ariṭāṭṭil eṉṟa aṟiñar.  tuṉpiyal nāṭakattit tiṟaṉāyntapoḻutu accol kiyāḷap
paṭṭuḷḷatu.  pēti maruntu uṭaliliruntu vēṇṭāta poruḷkaḷi akaṟṟuvatu pōlavē, āṉmāvic cūḻntuḷḷa aḻukkup poruḷkaḷit tuṉpiyal nāṭakaṅkaḷ akaṟṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu avar koḷki.  oru tuṉpiyal nāṭakattik kāṇum poḻutu nammiṭam tēṅkik kiṭakkum (pent-up) oru cila uṇarvukaḷ ceyaṟpaṭṭu veḷippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  nāmum katit talivaṉuṭiya uṇarvukaḷip peṟukiṉṟōm; avaṉ paṭum tuṉpaṅkaḷiyellām nāmum ōraḷavu aṉupavikkiṉṟōm.  avaṉiṭam nilipeṟum uṇarvuppōrāṭṭam nammiṭamum tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.  jēm rā eṉpāriṉ karuttuppaṭi viḷiyāṭṭum immātiric ceyalip purikiṉṟatu.  kuḻantip paruvattilum cari, kumarapparuvattilum cari, viḷiyāṭṭu nammiṭiyē aṭaṅkik kiṭakkum (pent-up) iyalpūkkaṅkaḷum uṇarvukaḷum veḷipaṭat tuṇiceykiṉṟatu.  nākarika vāḻk kiyil pōriṭum iyalpūkkam (Instinct of  pugnacity) ceyal paṭuvataṟku atika vāyppilli.  nākarika ulakil viḷiyāṭṭil avvāyppu kiṭikkiṉṟatu.  ovvoru viḷiṭṭum pōlic caṇṭiyē; atil kuruti cintuvatilli; ciṉamum veḷip paṭuvatilli.  āṉāl, anta iyalpūkkattiliruntu āṟṟali veḷippaṭuttuvataṟku vāyppiṉi nalkukiṉṟatu.  pāvaṉi ulakil kuḻantikaḷ viḷiyāṭum viḷiyāṭṭilum avvāṟ ṟalkaḷ veḷippaṭuvataṟku nalla vāyppukaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
koḷkiyiṉ orumippāṭu: ik koḷkikaḷi yellām ārāyntāl aviyāvum muraṇpaṭṭavi alla eṉpatum pulaṉākum.  kurūsiṉ vāḻkki āyattak koḷki jēm rā kuṟipṭum kālutaṟ koḷkiyāl viḷakka maṭikiṉṟatu.  aṉṟiyum, akkoḷki hāliṉ kuṭivaḻik koḷkiyiṉ nīṭṭam eṉṟum karutalām.  vaḻivaḻiyāka nammiṭiyē vantu koṇṭirukkum maṉappāṉmikkuk kāraṇamākavuḷḷa iyal pūkkaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum pirikkamuṭiyāta oru cila uṇarvukaḷ viḷiyāṭṭil veḷippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivvāṟu avi veḷippaṭā viṭṭāl avi citikkappaṭṭu vēṟu vaṭivattil kēṭu viḷivikkum.  eṉavē, kālutaṟ koḷki kurū avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōkkattiyum (forward look) hāl avarkaḷiṉ piṉṉōk kattiyum (backward look) oṉṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ kuṭivaḻip paṇpukaḷiyum pulappaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉa; kumarapparuva vēlikaḷukku āyattam ceyvaṉavākavum uḷḷaṉa.
vēliyum viḷiyāṭṭum:  vēliyiyum viḷiyā ṭṭiyum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuvateṉpatu eḷitaṉṟu.  avaṟṟiṟku viḷakkan taruvatum kaṭiṉamē.  vēli vāḻvuṭaṉ iṇinta oru puṟampāṉa ceyki; oru kuṟippiṭṭa vāḻkkit tēviyi vēli muṟṟuvikkiṉṟatu; āṉāl, viḷiyāṭṭil puṟampāṉa nōkkamoṉṟum illi; aḵtu oruvaruṭiya makiḻcciyiṉ poruṭṭē mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟatu.  vēli makiḻcciyuṭaṉ virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭuvatilli; viḷiyāṭṭu makiḻcci yuṭaṉ virumpi ēṟṟuk koḷḷap paṭukiṉṟatu.  vēliyiṉ payaṉik kaṇṭapiṟaku tāṉ makiḻcci uṇṭākiṉṟatu; viḷiyāṭṭil īṭupaṭṭirukkumpoḻutē makiḻcci ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.  vēli pala puṟampāṉa kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷāl mēṟ koḷḷappaṭuvatu; viḷi yāṭṭil pala kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷ iruppiṉum avi viḷiyāṭu pavarkaḷē ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭavi.  vēli uṇmi vāḻvi oṭṭiyatu;  viḷiyāṭṭu pāvaṉi ulakic cārntatu.  ivvāṟu oṉṟi yoṉṟu pirittaṟiyalām eṉiṉum, aṭip paṭiyāṉa uṇmi oṉṟu uṇṭu.  anta vēṟupāṭukaḷ yāvum ceyalkaḷil illi; avaṟṟil īṭupaṭupavarkaḷiṉ maṉappāṉmiyip (Attitude) poṟuttirukkiṉṟatu.  ettoḻiliyum nāmē ēṟṟu makiḻcciyuṭaṉ āṟṟat toṭaṅkiṉāl, aḵtu oru paḷuvākat tōṉṟu vatilli; attoḻili mēṟkoḷḷumpoḻutu nam iyal pūkkaṅkaḷ tiruptiyaṭintāl, atu namakku viḷiyāṭ ṭākavē tōṉṟum.  eṉavētāṉ ulakil naṭipeṟum ceyalkaḷa ṉittiyum āṇṭavaṉ, alakilā viḷiyāṭṭākac ceytu varukiṉṟār eṉṟa uṇmiyiyum ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇkiṉṟōm.
ulaka varalāṟṟip pārttāl maṉitaṉuṭiya aruñ ceyalkaḷ (Achievements) yāvum viḷiyāṭṭiṉ aṭip paṭiyil tōṉṟiyavi eṉpatu teriyavarum.  āṅkila moḻiyil viḷiyāṭṭu eṉap poruḷpaṭum Play eṉṟacol uyarnta nāṭakattik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟa cekappiriyariṉ nāṭakaṅkaḷ yāvum cāvā ilakkiyaṅkaḷ.  ilakkiyam, ōviyam, ciṟpam, ici, aṟiviyal ākiya tuṟikaḷil maṉitaṉ ārvattuṭaṉum makiḻcciyuṭaṉum ceyalkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu kipuṉintiyaṟṟiya pala uyarnta poruḷkaḷip paṭittuḷḷa nikaḻccikaḷ kāṇakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.  eṉavē, viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyik kaṟṟalil mēṟkoṇṭāl, nalla payaṉi etirpārkkalām eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu.  putiya kalvi muṟikaḷ yāvum viḷiyāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭiyiltāṉ tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa.  pala āṇṭukaḷākak kalvi aṟiñarkaḷ karuttil teḷintu veḷippaṭṭavi avi.  anta aṟiñarkaḷ pallāṇṭu kaḷākak kalvit tuṟiyil uḻittup peṟṟa paṭṭaṟiviṉ viḷivukaḷ avi.  eṉavē, putumik kaviñar pāratiyārum,
         ōṭi viyiṭu pāppā,--nī
ōyntirukka lākātu pāppā,
kūṭiviḷi yāṭu pāppā,--oru
kuḻantiyi viyātē pāppā.
kāli yeḻuntavuṭaṉ paṭippu,--piṉpu
kaṉivu koṭukkum nalla pāṭṭu;
māli muḻutum viḷiyāṭṭu--eṉṟu
vaḻakkap paṭuttik koḷḷu pāppā.
eṉṟu pāppāvip pārttup pāṭi ati viḷiyāṭṭil viṭukiṉṟār.   ceyal tiṭṭa muṟi, ṭālṭṭaṉ tiṭṭam, māṇṭisāri muṟi, kiṇṭarkārṭṭaṉ muṟi, naṭippu muṟi pōṉṟa putiya muṟikaḷ yāvum viḷiyāṭṭi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭavi.  ivaṟṟiṉ virivip piṉṉark kāṇpōm.
viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyiṉ niṟikaḷ:  im muṟiyāl pala naṉmikaḷ viḷikiṉṟaṉa.  kaṭṭāya miṉmiyāl kuḻantikaḷ ārvattōṭum paṟṟōṭum kaṟṟalil īṭupaṭuvar.  kalvipaṟṟiya maṉappāṉmi avarkaḷiṭam māṟi aṟivu naṉku vaḷarat toṭaṅkum.  paṭippil paṟṟum kavarcciyum ēṟpaṭṭu atikamākak kaṟpar.  paṭippatil veṟuppum calippum ēṟpaṭuvatilli.  viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyāl kuḻantikaḷ palvēṟu paṭṭaṟivukaḷi yaṭikiṉṟaṉar.  avarkaḷ palvēṟu tuṟikaḷil ceyalāṟṟavum, palvēṟu poruḷkaḷik kāṇavum, palvēṟu makkaḷiṭam paḻakavum vāyppukkaḷ uṇṭākum.  aimpoṟikaḷ vāyilāka aṟivu peṟum vāyppiṉiyum avarkaḷ peṟalām.  viḷiyāṭṭu muṟi ottuṇarcci, vitikaḷukkaṭaṅkal, talivarukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭal, puṟaṅkūṟāmi, piṟar karutti aṟital, veṟṟi tōlvikaḷi oṉṟupōl pāvittal pōṉṟa paṇpukaḷi vaḷarkkiṉṟatu.  immuṟiyāl māṇākkar kavaṉattip paṭippil īrkkavum, āciriyar pēcum aḷavik kuṟittuk koḷḷavum vāyppukkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.  aṉṟiyum, immuṟiyāl ōḻukkamum (Character) vaḷarkiṉṟatu eṉṟu nampukiṉṟaṉar.  maṉitaṉ taṉiyāka iyaṅkum poḻutu vēṇṭiya tuṇivu, nuṇṇuṇarvu, viṭā muyaṟci, taṉṉampikki, taṉṉaṭakkam pōṉṟa paṇpukaḷiyum, camūkattuṭaṉ cērntu iyaṅkum poḻutu vēṇṭiya naṭpu, piṟar karuttukku matipput tarutal, poṟumi pōṉṟa paṇpukaḷiyum vaḷarttuc ciṟanta kuṭimakaṉākat tikaḻat tuṇipurikiṉṟatu immuṟi.
 cila kuṟikaḷ:  im muṟiyil cila kuṟikaḷum kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  všyh¥ ghl§fisí« ï«KiwÆš f‰ã¤jš ïayhJ.; oru pāṭattiṉ ellāp pakutikaḷiyum immuṟiyiṉ vāyilākak kaṟṟalum aritē.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil immuṟiyik kiyāḷa muṭivatip pōl mēl vakuppukaḷil kiyāḷa muṭivatilli.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kavaṉam pāṭaṅkaḷil pativataṟkup patilāka avarkaḷ kavaṉam tuṇik karuvikaḷilum, paṭaṅkaḷilum piṟavaṟṟilum citaṟippōka iṭamuṇṭu.  pāṭaṅkaḷil toṭarcci aṟṟup pōkavum kūṭum.  ellā āciriyarkaḷum immuṟiyik kiyāḷum tiṟamiyuṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu colla iyalātu.
viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyil tamiḻ kaṟpittal:  toṭakka nili, iṭinili, uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil tamiḻ moḻiyik kaṟpittalil viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyi evvāṟu mēṟ koḷḷa lām eṉpati īṇṭu oru ciṟitu kavaṉippōm.  avaṟṟik karuttuṭaṉum nampikkiyuṭaṉum tiṟamiyuṭaṉum kiyāṇ ṭāl nalla payaṉi etirpārkkalām.


toṭakka nilip paḷḷikaḷukku ēṟṟa viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ:
  (1) oru vakuppi iru camamākap pirittukkoḷka; oru pirivuk kuḻantikaḷiṭam cila coṟkaḷ eḻutap peṟṟa miṉṉaṭṭikaḷiyum (Flash cards) iṉṉoru piriviluḷḷavarkaḷiṭam accoṟkaḷ kuṟikkum paṭaṅkaḷ eḻutiya miṉṉaṭṭikaḷiyum koṭuttiṭuka.  āciriyar oru poruḷik kuṟippiṭṭu aḻittāl apporuḷiṉ peyar eḻutiyuḷḷa aṭṭiyi vittuk koṇṭirukkum kuḻantiyum apporuḷiṉ paṭam eḻutiyuḷḷa aṭṭiyi vittukkoṇṭirukkum kuḻantiyum cērntu vantu āciriyar pakkattil vakuppi nōkki niṟka vēṇṭum.  immātiri toṭarntu naṭattuka.
(2) pala poruḷkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru periya paṭattik kuḻantikaḷiṭam koṭuttu appaṭattil oru kuṟippiṭṭa eḻuttil toṭaṅkum poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi eḻutac ceyyalām.
(3) oru palaki allatu keṭṭiyāṉa aṭṭiyil pala coṟkaḷ allatu eḻuttukkaḷip paṭukki variciyāka eḻutuka.  ovvoru col allatu eḻuttiṟkuk kīḻē avi aṭaṅkumāṟu kāliyiṭam amittiṭuka.  kuḻantikaḷiṭam taṉittaṉiyākac ciṟuciṟu tuṇṭu aṭṭikaḷil eḻutappeṟṟa antac coṟkaḷ allatu eḻuttukkaḷ irukkum.  kuḻantikaḷ taṅkaḷiṭamuḷḷa coṟkaḷ allatu eḻuttukkaḷip periya aṭṭiyiluḷḷa accoṟkaḷ allatu eḻuttukkaḷukkuk kīḻ amikka vēṇṭum.
(4) iḵtu eṉṉa? oru vakuppuk kuḻantikaḷi vaṭṭamāka uṭkāravittiṭuka.  oru kuḻantiyi vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil uṭkārac ceyka.  ovvoru kuḻantiyum taṉ kaṟpalakiyil ētēṉum oru colli eḻutavēṇṭum.  eḻuti muṭintavuṭaṉ ellōrum tattam kaṟpalakikaḷi vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil uṭkārntu koṇṭirukkum kuḻanti kāṇumāṟu tūkkip piṭikkavēṇṭum.  akkuḻanti ellāk kaṟpalakikaḷilum eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟi variciyākap paṭittu varavēṇṭum.  oru collip paṭikka muṭiyāviṭṭālum, paṭikkat tavakkam ēṟpaṭṭālum accolli eḻutiya kuḻanti vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviṟkuc cella vēṇṭum; atukāṟum vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvilirunta kuḻanti vaṭṭattiṟkuc cellavēṇṭum.  collukkup patilāka coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, etirccoṟkaḷ, orumi paṉmi coṟkaḷ, viṉā-viṭi ākiyavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭutti ivviḷiyāṭṭi naṭattalām.  oru kuḻanti ellāvaṟṟiyum paṭittuviṭṭāl, vēṟoru kuḻantiyi vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ aṉuppi ivviḷiyāṭṭit toṭarntu naṭattalām.  vakuppu peritāka iruntāl ati iru pirivukaḷākap pirittukkoṇṭu ivviḷiyāṭṭi taṉittaṉiyāka mēṟkoḷḷalām.
(5) kaṇṇāmpūcci allatu oḷintāṭal:  pala aṭṭikaḷil vevvēṟu coṟkaḷi eḻuti vittuk koḷka.  oru kuḻantiyi aṟikku veḷiyē aṉuppiviṭṭu ēṉi yōrik kaṇkaḷi mūṭikkoḷḷac ceyka.  coṟkaḷuḷḷa aṭṭikaḷi vakuppil pala iṭaṅkaḷil parappi vittiṭuka.  piṟaku vakuppiluḷḷavarkaḷ kaṇkaḷit tiṟantu koḷḷalām.  veḷiyilirukkum kuḻanti kaṇṭu piṭikkavēṇṭiya colli niccayittuk koṇṭu ati oru kuḻantiyiṉ mūlam veḷiyilirukkum kuḻantikkuc colli aṉuppuka.  uṭaṉē akkuḻanti vakuppiṟku vantu accollik kaṇṭupiṭikkum.  collukkup patilākac coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum, muṟṟuppeṟāta coṟṟoṭar kaḷiyum kiyāḷalām.  āciriyar tam aṟivukkum taku vaḻikāṇum tiṟamikkum ēṟṟavāṟellām iv viḷi yāṭṭi amittukkoḷḷalām.
(6) āṇiyiṭal.  oru vakuppuk kuḻantikaḷi vaṭṭamāka uṭkāra vittiṭuka,.  oru kuḻantiyiṭam ētāvatu oru kaṭṭaḷiyi ōciyiṉṟik kūṟuka.  akkuḻanti taṉ arukiluḷḷa maṟṟoru kuḻantikku ati metuvākat terivikkum.  kaṭiciyiluḷḷa kuḻanti ak kaṭṭaḷiyi niṟivēṟṟum.  itiyē iṉṉoru vitamākavum mēṟkoḷḷalām.  oru kuḻantiyi vakuppaṟikku veḷiyil aṉuppuka.  maṟṟoru kuḻantiyiṉ mūlam ak kuḻantiyiṭam terivikkumāṟu ētavātu oru kaṭṭaḷiyi ellōrum aṟiyumāṟu kūṟi aṉuppavum.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ nilikku ēṟṟavāṟu kaṭṭaḷikaḷik kaṭiṉamākkalām.  kuḻanti akkaṭṭaḷiyi evvāṟu oḻuṅkākat terivikkiṉṟatu eṉpati veḷiyilirukkum kuḻanti atic cariyāka niṟivēṟṟu vatiliruntu aṟintu koḷḷalām.  ētāvatu tavaṟu ēṟpaṭṭāl tavaṟu eppaṭi nērntatu eṉpati avviruvariyum viṉavi aṟiyalām.
(7) eṉṉiṭam oru pi irukkiṉṟatu.  oru vakuppuk kuḻantikaḷi vaṭṭamāka uṭkāravittukkoḷka.  oru kuḻanti eṉṉiṭam oru pi irukkiṉṟatu eṉṟu collum.  g¡f¤âÈU¡F« FHªij ‘mâš v‹d ïU¡»‹wJ? eṉṟu viṉavum, mutaṟkuḻanti aval eṉṟu kūṟum.  immātiriyē iraṇṭāvatu kuḻantiyum mūṉṟāvatu kuḻantiyum colluvar.  iraṇṭāvatu kuḻanti āppam eṉṟu collum.  ippaṭiyē eḻuttu variciyil (allatu eppaṭiyāyiṉum) pala coṟkaḷik kūṟuvar.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ varici muṟiyil oru varici muṭintavuṭaṉ mīṇṭum āṭṭam toṭaṅkum.  appoḻutu putiya coṟkaḷit tāṉ kūṟavēṇṭum.
(8) coṟṟoṭar amittal.  pala coṟkaḷit taṉittaṉiyākap pala aṭṭikaḷil eḻutuka.  accoṟkaḷ pala coṟṟoṭarkaḷic cērntavikaḷāka irukkavēṇṭum.  ovvoru kuḻantiyiṭamum oru aṭṭiyik koṭuttiṭuka.  ētāvatu oru coṟṟoṭari aṉivarum kēṭkumpaṭi paṭittiṭuka,  uṭaṉē accoṟkaḷi vittuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ virivāka muṉvantu coṟṟoṭariluḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉ variciyil niṉṟu coṟṟoṭari amittu niṟpar.  vakuppi iru pirivākap pirittuk koṇṭu iraṇṭu vakic coṟkaḷik koṇṭu iv viḷiyāṭṭi naṭattalām.  inta viḷiyāṭṭi iṉṉoruvitamākavum mēṟkoḷḷalām.  pala coṟkaḷik karumpalakiyil eḻutuka.  antac coṟkaḷik koṇṭu palvēṟu coṟṟoṭarkaḷi amikkumpaṭip payiṟṟalām.  avaṟṟil vanta collē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum varalām.
(9) ovvoru aṭṭiyilum ovvoru viṉāvākap pala aṭṭikaḷil eḻutit tayārāka vittukkoḷka.  vakuppuk kuḻantikaḷ varuvataṟku muṉpu anta aṭṭikaḷi vakuppil pala iṭaṅkaḷil maṟittu vittiṭuka.  epporuḷip paṟṟi yāyiṉum viṉākkaḷ irukkalām.  kuḻantikaḷ vantavuṭaṉ aṭṭikaḷit tēṭi eṭukkumpaṭi ceyka.  piṟaku avarkaḷi avaravar iṭattilirukkumpaṭi ceytu viṉākkaḷukku viṭi eḻutum paṭi ceyka.  cariyāka viṭiyeḻutiya kuḻanti viḷiyāṭṭil veṟṟiyaṭintatākak karutappaṭum.  inta viḷiyāṭṭi vakuppiṟku veḷiyilum naṭattalām.
(10) `anniyaṉik kaṇṭupiṭi:  orē iṉattic cērnta pala coṟkaḷaṭaṅkiya pala tokutikaḷit tayār ceytu koḷka.  ovvoru tokutiyilum anta iṉattic cērāta oru collic cērttiṭuka.  kuḻantikaḷ iṉattic cērāta collik kīṟiṭṭuk kāṭṭavēṇṭum.  allatu taṉiyāka eṭuttu eḻuta vēṇṭum.
	(e-ṭu).		(a)	kātu, kaṇ, mūkku, nākku, pākku, ki, kāl;
			(ā)	karumi, cemmi, erumi, mañcaḷ, ūtā, 						nīlam, pacci;

(11) karumpalaki ōṭṭam: aintu karumpalakikaḷit tayār ceytu koḷka.  oru vakuppuk kuḻantikaḷi aintu pirivukaḷākap pirittiṭuka.  ovvoru karumpalakiyiṉ piṉpuṟamum cila coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷi eḻutuka.  ovvoru coṟṟoṭarilum oru cila coṟkaḷ viṭupaṭṭirukkavēṇṭum.  viḷi yāṭṭukkut tayār eṉṟu terintu kuṟippuk kāṭṭiyavuṭaṉ ovvoru pakutiyilu muḷḷa mutaṟkuḻantikaḷ ovvoruvarumāka ōṭi, karumpalakiyiṉ piṉṉāl uḷḷa coṟṟoṭarkaḷik paṭittuviṭṭu muṉpuṟamāka vantu viṭupaṭṭuḷḷa collik karumpalakiyil eḻutiviṭṭu avaravar iṭatti aṭiya vēṇṭum. aṭuttapaṭiyāka ovvoru pakutiyilumuḷḷa iraṇṭāvatu kuḻantikaḷ immātiri ceyvar.  iṅṅaṉam toṭarntu naṭipeṟum.  viṭupaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷiyum mutalil cariyāka eḻutiya pakutiyic cārnta kuḻantikaḷ veṟṟiyaṭintavarkaḷ.  etirccoṟkaḷ, orumi-paṉmic coṟkaḷ, āṇpāl-peṇpāl coṟkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟik koṇṭu itē viḷiyāṭṭi naṭattalām.
(12) oru vakuppuk kuḻantikaḷi vaṭṭamāka uṭkāra vittiṭuka.  naṭuvil oru kuḻantiyi amarttuka.  naṭuvil irukkum kuḻanti kiyil oru ciṟu pantiyō oru kālaṇā nāṇayattiyō vittuk koṇṭu vaṭṭattil niṟkum oruvari nōkki eṟintuviṭṭu nilam, nir, kāṟṟu eṉṟu ētāva toṉṟic collavēṇṭum.  pantiyō nāṇayattiyō vāṅkum kuḻanti pattu viṉāṭikkuḷ mēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa mūṉṟiṉuḷ oṉṟil vāḻum pirāṇiyiṉ peyaric colla vēṇṭum.  naṭuviluḷḷa kuḻanti pattu eṇṇuvataṟkuḷ vaṭṭattilirukkum kuḻanti viṭi collāviṭṭālum allatu tavaṟākac coṉṉālum, uṭaṉē akkuḻanti vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil vantu viṭavēṇṭum; atukāṟum vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvilirunta kuḻanti vaṭṭatti aṭiyavēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu āṭṭam toṭarntu naṭakkum.  ilakkaṇap pāṭattil iv viḷiyāṭṭi mika naṉṟāka mēṟkoḷḷalām.  eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuviluḷḷa kuḻanti peyar, viṉi,  iṭi, uri, eṉṟu ētāvatu oṉṟic colla, vaṭṭattiluḷḷa kuḻanti ataṟkēṟṟa collik kūṟavēṇṭum.
(13) pirayāṇam ceytal.  vaṭṭamākak kuḻantikaḷi uṭkāra vittiṭuka.  āciriyar: nāṉ veḷiyūrukkup payaṇam. nāṉ oru cāttukkuṭiyik koṇṭu celvēṉ.  mutaṟ kuḻanti:  nāṉ veḷiyūrukkup payaṇam.  nāṉ oru mātuḷam paḻattik koṇṭu celvēṉ.  iraṇṭāvatu kuḻanti:  nāṉ kaṟpalakiyik koṇṭu celvēṉ.  mūṉṟāvatu kuḻanti:  nāṉ pommiyik koṇṭu celvēṉ.  ivvāṟu ovvoru varum ovvoru kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷiṉ peyaric colla vēṇṭum.  ellōrum colli muṭintavuṭaṉ, āciriyar nāṉ cāttukkuṭiyit tiṉpēṉ eṉṟu coṉṉavuṭaṉ ellōrum ovvoruvarāka avaravar kūṟiya poruḷi niṉivil vittuk koṇṭu poruttamāṉa coṟṟoṭar amikka vēṇṭum.  cāttukkuṭi tiṉṉum poruḷāṉatāl āciriyar cāttuk kuṭiyit tiṉpēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyatu poruttamāka amiyum.  atēpōl, karumpalakiyil eḻutuvēṉ, pommiyi vittu viḷiyāṭuvēṉ eṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷē porut tamāṉavi.  kuḻantikaḷ viḻippāka irāviṭṭāl iraṇṭāvatu kuḻanti nāṉ kaṟpalakiyit tiṉpēṉ eṉṟum, mūṉṟāvatu kuḻanti nāṉ pommiyit tiṉpēṉ eṉṟum collak kūṭum.  inta viḷiyāṭṭit toṭarntu naṭattalām.
(14) oru vakuppiluḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ ovvoruvaruṭiya caṭṭiyilum mutukup puṟamāka oru pirāṇi allatu oru poruḷiṉ peyar eḻutiya aṭṭiyi allatu kākitattip porutti vittiṭuka.  aṭṭiyi vittuḷḷa kuḻantikku accol eṉṉaveṉpatu teriyak kūṭātu.  ovvoruvarum viṉākkaḷiṉ mūlam piṟariṭamiruntu terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  viṭiyaḷikkum kuḻanti ām illi eṉpatit tavira vēṟu vaṭivil collakkūṭātu.  oru vakuppic ciṟuciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap (Teams) pirittukkoṇṭu ivviḷiyāṭṭi naṭattalām.
(15) kati amittal:  oru katiyil mutal vākkiyattic colluka.  kuḻantikaḷ ovvoruvarum ovvoru vākkiyamākac collik katiyi muṭippar.  cila camayam. katiyiṉ muṟpātiyik kūṟi piṟpātiyik kuḻanti kaḷik koṇṭu muṭikkumpaṭi ceyyalām.
(16) añcal viḷiyāṭṭu:  māṇākkar mukavari eḻutiya aṭṭikaḷi antanta māṇākkaṉiṭam koṭukkac ceytal, allatu poruḷkaḷiṉ peyar eḻutiya aṭṭikaḷi avvap poruḷkaḷiṉmēl vikkac ceytal.
cila viḷiyāṭṭup payiṟcikaḷ:  iḷam āciriyarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭum poruṭṭu oru cila payiṟcikaḷ īṇṭut tarap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ovvoru viḷi yāṭṭiyum āciriyar yuttik kēṟṟavāṟu māṟṟi māṟṟi viḷiyāṭac ceyyalām.  viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḷil pōṭṭi iruntāltāṉ cuvi ēṟpaṭum.
(1) iṉap payiṟci:  oru vīṭṭiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭṭi oṉṟiṉik kuḻantikaḷiṭam koṭuttiṭuka.  apporuḷkaḷ camiyal aṟi, paṭukki aṟi, paṭippu aṟi, uṇṇum aṟi ākiyavaṟṟil ententa aṟiyil evi evi irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu pirikkac ceyka.  ilakkaṇap pāṭattil peyarc col, viṉiccol, iṭic col, uriccol ākiyavaṟṟuḷ coṟkaḷip pirittu aṭakkum payiṟcikaḷit taralām.  nilam, nīr, kāṟṟu eṉṟu pirivukaḷil aṭaṅkum pala coṟkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru paṭṭiyik koṭuttu atilaṭaṅkiya coṟkaḷi iṉap paṭuttumpaṭi collalām.
(2) iṇippu viḷiyāṭṭu:  itil palavaki uṇṭu.
	(a)	paṭaṅkaḷiyum avi kuṟikkum coṟkaḷiyum iṇikkac ceytal.
	(ā)	coṟ poruttam:  poruttamāṉa coṟkaḷi iṇikkumpaṭi ceytal.

	(i)	peyar-viṉip poruttam:  peyar viṉi kaḷip poruttumāṟu payiṟcikaḷ tarutal.
	(e-ṭu)	kōḻi	ūḷiyiṭum
				nāy		piḷiṟum
				kutiri	kurikkum
				yāṉi	akavum

				ciṅkam	kaṉikkum
				nari		karccikkum
	(ī)	viṉā-viṭipporuttam: viṉāvukkuriya viṭiyip poruttumāṟu ēvutal.
	(u)	uvamāṉa-upamēyap poruttam: uvamāṉaṅkaḷiyum upamēyaṅ kaḷiyum iṇittu viḷi yāṭal.
	(ū)	iḷamip peyarp poruttam:  cilavaṟṟiṉ iḷamip peyarkaḷip poruttumāṟu payiṟcikaḷ taralām.

	(e)	etirc col poruttam:  iraṇṭu coṟ paṭṭikaḷit tayār ceyka.  oru paṭṭiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ maṟṟoru paṭṭiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷukku etirc coṟkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum: avi muṟimāṟiyum eḻutap peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.  kuḻantikaḷiṭam avaṟṟik koṭuttut takka vaṟṟi iṇikkumpaṭi ceyyalām.
	(e-ṭu)	mikuti		muṭivu
            			naṉmi		naṇpaṉ
	            	pakivaṉ		tīmi
            			toṭakkam	kuṟivu
orumikkup paṉmiyum, āṇpālukkup peṇpālum, taṉmikkup piṟa iṭamum ākiya coṟkaḷi iṇikkum payiṟcikaḷ taralām.  kīḻ vakuppukaḷil ilakkaṇattil ittakiya payiṟcikaḷit taralām.
(3) collākka viḷiyāṭṭu:  itilum palavaki uṇṭu.
	(a)	taṉittaṉi eḻuttaṭṭikaḷik kuvittuk koṇṭu, poruttamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷip porukkip pala coṟkaḷi uṇṭākkal.
	(ā)	vaṭṭamākak kuḻantikaḷ amarntukoṇṭu āḷukkoru eḻuttik kūṟic coṟkaḷi uruvākkal.
	(i)	vērc colliliruntu piṟakkum piṟa coṟkaḷ oṉṟiraṇṭu kūṟac ceytal.
	(e-ṭu) kaṇ=kaṇṭu, kaṇṭāṉ, kāṇ, kāṭci, kāṇal; kuti=kutiri, kutittal, kutittāṉ.
	(ī)	oru poruṭpala col amittu viḷiyāṭal.
	(e-ṭu)yāṉi, tanti, kari, vēḻam, tumpi, kaḷiṟu, piṭi, kajam mutaliyaṉa.
(u) kāli iṭatti nirappic collākkal.

(ū) eḻuttuc curukkam;  oru nīṇṭa collilirukkum eḻuttukkaḷik kiyāṇṭu vēṟu cila ciṟu coṟkaḷi ākkalām.
	(e-ṭu)	vīra cutantiram-vīram, curam, tiram, taram, ratam, vīti,cuti, tati, rati, vīcu, vīcum, vītam, tantiram mutaliyaṉa.
	(e)	eḻuttup perukkam:   paṭam itil muṟiyē oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu eḻuttukkaḷip peytu collākkal.
	(e-ṭu) 	paṭam, pavaḻam, paravacam mutaliyavi.
	(ē)	mōṉi amittal: mōṉi eṉpatu mutal eḻuttu orē eḻuttāka , allatu iṉa eḻuttāka iruttal.
	(e-ṭu)pāṭṭi paṭutta pāyip pār eṉpatu pōṉṟavi.
	(ai)	 etuki amittal:  etuki eṉpatu mutal eḻuttu māttiri aḷavil ottu niṟka, piṟa eḻuttukkaḷ ōciyil oṉṟu paṭalām.
	(e-ṭu) 	pāṭṭi vīṭṭil pāṭṭuk kēṭṭu iruntēṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟavi.
	(o)	iyipu eṉṉum aḻakuṭaṉ coṟṟoṭar muṭittal.
	(e-ṭu) 	appā tantār	        tuṭṭu

	pōvōm puṟap		paṭṭu 
			eṉpatu pōṉṟavi
	(ō)	ōr eḻuttu māṟṟam:  koṭuttuḷḷa oru collil ōr eḻutti maṭṭilum māṟṟi vēṟoru colli uṇṭākkutal.
	(e-ṭu) kuṭam-paṭam, kulam, kuṭal pōṉṟavi.
(4) varicippaṭuttal: māṟi uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷi allatu coṟkaḷi varicippaṭuttac ceytal.
	(a)	ammā, ili, āṭu, ōṇāṉ, ūci, eli, ural, īram, ēṇi, oṭṭakam pōṉṟa coṟkaḷi eḻuttu variciyil collac ceytal allatu eḻutac ceytal.
	(ā)	māṟi uḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷic collākkac ceytal.
	(e-ṭu) ta, m, pa, ci, ra, m=citamparam pōṉṟavi.
	(i)	muṟimāṟi eḻutiya cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷil uḷḷa coṟkaḷi muṟiyāka varicippaṭuttic cariyāṉa coṟṟoṭarkaḷi uṇṭākkac ceyyalām.
(e-ṭu) eṉṟu ōr irukkiṉṟatu ūr kārikkaṭi,
kārikkuṭi eṉṟu ōr ūr irukkiṉṟatu.
immātiri ‘iṭṭali kuṭittāṉ nīr mōr tiṉṟu’, ‘pul pacittāl pulitiṉṉumā?  pēcuvatu moḻi nām tamiḻ eṉpavaṟṟic cariyāka eḻutac ceyyalām.
(5) cari-tappu:  oru viṉāvum ataṉ kīḻē viṭiyumāka eḻutuka.  viṭi cariyāka iruntāl ataṉ kīḻē kuḻantikaḷ cari eṉṟum, tavaṟāka iruntāl tavaṟu eṉṟum eḻuta vēṇṭum.
	(e-ṭu)	nī paḷḷikku ēṉ cellukiṉṟāy?
		nāṉ paḷḷikku viḷiyāṭap pōkiṉṟēṉ.
		cevvāykkiḻamikku aṭutta kiḻami eṉṉa?
		cevvāykkiḻamikku aṭuttatu putaṉkiḻami.
immātiri aṟivukkum tiṟaṉukkum takkavāṟu kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṟivu niliyi yoṭṭip pala coṟṟoṭarkaḷi amikkalām.
(6) poruttamāṉa coṟkaḷ amittal:  oru coṟṟoṭaril oru col irukkavēṇṭiya iṭattik kāliyāka viṭṭu, arukil piṟik kuṟikaḷiṭiyē pala coṟkaḷik koṭuttu, kāli iṭattil porutta vēṇṭiya coṟkaḷik kaṇṭaṟiyac ceyyalām.

			(kiḷi, puṟā, kōḻi)
		maravēli ceypavar ________
			(kollar, kottar, taṭṭār, taccar,.)
(7) katiyil coṟkaḷip poruttutal: oru katiyil cila coṟkaḷik kāliyāka viṭuka.  iccoṟkaḷaṭaṅkiya paṭṭiyik kuḻantikaḷiṭam koṭuttuk kāli iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa coṟkaḷi nirappumpaṭi ceyka.
iṭinili, uyarnili vakuppukaḷukku ēṟṟa viḷi yāṭṭukkaḷ:  makiḻcciyuṭaṉ ēṟṟu naṭattum ceyalkaḷ yāvum viḷiyāṭṭākat tōṉṟum eṉṟa uṇmiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ivi mēṟkoḷḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  cuṟṟulā cellal, avarkaḷ kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷik kaṭṭuriyāka variyac ceytal, avaṟṟi ōviyamākat tīṭṭac ceytal, katikaḷ nikaḻccikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi naṭittuk kāṭṭal, viḷamparaṅkaḷ, ciṟukatikaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ, āṭṭappāṭalkaḷ, katip pāṭalkaḷ, ciṟu ceyyuṭkaḷ, ōraṅka nāṭakaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi eḻuta tūṇṭutal, ciṟu coṟpoḻivukaḷ, coṟpōrkaḷ, ceyyuṭkaḷi oppu vittal, paḷḷi itaḻkaḷi naṭattutal mutaliyavi pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷ yāvum viḷiyāṭṭiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amintaviyē.



3. ceyal tiṭṭa muṟi
navīṉa kalvi muṟikaḷiṉ uyirnāṭi kuḻantiyic cuṟṟi (‘Paedocentric’) iyaṅkukiṉṟatu eṉpati ikkālattil evarum aṟivar.  kalviyiṉ nōkkam kuḻantikaḷi vāḻkkikku āyattam ceyvatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  itil nampikki irukkumāyiṉ vāḻkkiyiṉ palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷip paḷḷiyil puttakaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilākap paṭippatu maṭṭilum pōtātu; an nikaḻccikaḷ paḷḷikaḷilēyē naṭipeṟac ceytum kāṭṭap peṟutal vēṇṭum.  paḷḷiyil māṇākkarkaḷē oru ceyalit tērnteṭuttu iyaṟkiyāṉa muṟiyil ati muṟṟa muṭiyak koṇṭu celuttuvatittāṉ ceyal tiṭṭa muṟi eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  inta muṟiyil paḷḷi naṭimuṟi, kalvit tiṭṭam, pāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavi yāvum kuḻantiyi naṭuvāka vittē mēṟkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  tāṉākavē oru ceyalit tērnteṭuttu, tāṉākavē atic ceytu muṭittu, tāṉākavē ataṉ payaṉik kaṇṭu makiḻtalē im muṟiyiṉ aṭippaṭi eṉṟu kūṟalām.  im muṟi viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu.
aṟiñarkaḷiṉ karuttu:  ṭākṭar kilpāṭrik eṉṉum pērāciriyar, paḷḷiyil orē takutiyuṭiya kuḻantikaḷ oru vakuppil kūṭiyirukkuṅkāl orumaṉattuṭaṉ oru ceyali allatu vēliyit tam nōkkattiṉālēyē tērnteṭuttu ataṉ mēlēyē kaṇṇum karuttumāyiruntu ceytukoṇṭu varumpoḻutē ataṉ vāyilāy muḻu maṉitarkaḷāvataṟku vēṇṭiya muḻuk kalviyiyum āciriyar utaviyik koṇṭu vaḷarttuk koḷvatē ceyal tiṭṭa muṟiyiṉ vāyilākak kaṟkum kalviyākum eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.  ṭīveṉsaṉ eṉṟa kalvi nipuṇarum, kuḻantikaḷ tam nōkkamāy oru ceyali āṟṟumpoḻutu ati muṟṟum muṭiyak koṇṭu celutti iṭam poruḷ ēval ākiya mūṉṟiyum takka muṟiyil taḻuvikkoṇṭu eti enta iṭattil eppaṭic ceytāl veṟṟiyaṭiyalām eṉpati aṟintu, ati muṭivu peṟac ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu valiyuṟuttukiṉṟār.  kuḻanti kaḷukkuc ceyal tammuṭiyatu eṉṟa uṇarcci ēṟpaṭṭāl tāṉ avarkaḷ ati muḻup poṟuppuṭaṉ niṟivēṟṟi muḻup payaṉiyum eytuvār.
im muṟiyiṉ paṭikaḷ: inta muṟiyil nāṉku paṭikaḷ uḷa.  mutalāvatu: nōkkam, māṇākkarkaḷē taṅkaḷ tiṟamikkum nilimikkum poruttamāṉa oru ceyalit tērnteṭuttuk koḷḷuvar.  āciriyariṉ kaṭṭāyamiṉṟiyē itu tērnteṭukkap peṟum. paṭṭaṟivil kuṟivuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷukkup pala poruḷkaḷil yōcaṉi kūṟi, payaṉuḷḷa ceyalit tērn teṭukkumpaṭi ceyyalām.  iraṇṭāvatu: tiṭṭamiṭal.  āciriyar vaḻikāṭṭa māṇākkarkaḷē acceyalit tiṟaṉuṭaṉ muṭikkum vaḻi muṟikaḷi ārāyvar.  ovvoru vivarattiyum variciyākak kuṟittu vittuk koḷḷumpaṭi ceyyavēṇṭum.  ovvoṉṟiyum kuṟittu vittuk koṇṭāltāṉ ceyal virivāka muṭiyum; ceytikaḷ teḷivāka viḷaṅkum; kuṟinta kālattil niṟinta payaṉi eytavum muṭiyum.  mūṉṟāvatu: ceytu muṭittal.  itutāṉ māṇākkarkaḷukku uṟcākatti aḷippatu; itil kālamum āṟṟalum vīṇākāmal, kaṇkāṇittu varuvatu āciriyar kaṭamiyākum; ceyaliliruntu vaḻi vilakip pōkātu pārttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  tiṭṭattiṉ ovvoru kuṟippum kavaṉikkappaṭukiṉṟatā eṉpati viḻippuṭaṉ kavaṉikka vēṇṭum.  tiṭṭam ceyaṟpaṭuṅkāl āciriyar naṇpaṉpōla kūṭa iruntu yōcaṉikaḷik kūṟavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, etiyum kaṭṭāyap paṭuttutal kūṭātu.  etiyum tiruppati moṭṭi pōl ceytu viṭātu orēcamayattil muṟṟa muṭiyak koṇṭu celutta vēṇṭum eṉpatik kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.  nāṉkāvatu: cīrtūkkal allatu matippiṭal, itilum āciriyar taliyiṭak kūṭātu.  māṇākkarkaḷik koṇṭē tiṟaṉāyac ceytal vēṇṭum.  itutāṉ nalla payaṉiyaḷikkum.  naṭimuṟiyil nēriṭṭa tavaṟukaḷiyum, muṭiviṉ payaṉkaḷiyum naṉṟāka ārāyalām.  curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, inta nāṉku paṭikaḷiyum, 
`tērnteṭuttut tiṭṭamiṭṭuc ceytataṟpiṉ cīrtūkki 
āyntaṟital nāṟpaṭikaḷ ām.
eṉṟa kuṟaṭpāvil aṭakki niṉivil vittuk koḷḷalām.
immuṟic ceyalkaḷiṉ vakikaḷ:  ceyal tiṭṭa muṟiyil āṟṟakkūṭiya ceyalkaḷi māṇākkar taṉit taṉiyākac ceyyak kūṭiyavi eṉṟum, kūṭṭākac ceyyak kūṭiyavi eṉṟum iruvakiyākap pirikkalām.  kāntiyaṭikaḷ nāṭṭu nalaṅkarutum poruḷ, oru māṇākkaṉ alikaṭalukku appāluḷḷa taṉ tantikkuk kaṭitam eḻututal pōṉṟavaṟṟi mutal vakikkum, irāmaṉum avaṉ cēṉiyum orumaṉappaṭṭu irāvaṇaṉuṭaṉ pōriṭṭuc cītiyi mīṭṭal, oru vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu oru katiyic cuyamāka naṭittuk kāṭṭal ākiyavaṟṟi iraṇṭām vakikkum eṭuttukkāṭṭuk kaḷākak koḷḷalām.  intac ceyalkaḷip paṭip pōrkkāṉa vaki, tuyppōrkkāṉa vaki allatu aḻakuṇar vaki, peruṅkēḷvi vaki, paṉmuṟip payiṟci peṟuvaki, viḷiyāṭṭu vaki eṉavum pirikkalām.
tamiḻ payiṟṟalil immuṟi:  oru ceyali māṇāk karkaḷē tēnteṭuttut tiṭṭam vakuttuc ceytu muṭittu matippiṭuvataṉmūlam avvēliyil payaṉpaṭum poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷi avarkaḷ naṉku aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  ap peyarkaḷi eṭutteḻutavum, ceyya vēṇṭiya ceyalkaḷic coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷil eḻutip paṭikkavum vāyppukkaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ceyal tiṭṭa vēlipaṟṟit talimiyāciriyarukku vēli toṭaṅka iciviyum cila poruḷkaḷik kōri eḻuta vēṇṭiya kaṭitam pōṉṟavaṟṟi itaṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭuk kaḷākak  koḷḷalām.  ovvoruvarum kaṭitam allatu viṇṇappam eḻutalām; oṉṟiraṇṭi maṭṭilum aṉṉārukku aṉuppac ceyyalām.  mēl vakuppukkaḷil oru toḻilik kaṟpataṟkut tēviyāṉa pala nūlkaḷip paṭikka nēriṭum toḻilip paṟṟiya viḷamparaṅkaḷ, kaṭitaṅkaḷ, uriyāṭalkaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ, ceymuṟi vivaraṅkaḷ, kaṭṭaḷikaḷ, piṟa kuṟippukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi āyattam ceyyumpoḻutu moḻip payiṟcikku iṭam uṇṭu.  moḻippayiṟciyiṉ ellāp pirivukaḷiyum im muṟiyil kaṟpikka iyalāviṭiṉum, immuṟiyil payiṟṟa iyalātaṉavaṟṟi piṟamuṟikaḷil payiṟṟalām.  immuṟiyil mēṟkoḷḷum vēli etuvāyiṉum muṟiyiṉ nāṉku paṭikaḷilum tamiḻp pāṭattiṟkuriya vāymoḻip payiṟci, eḻuttu, ceyyuḷ, urinaṭip paṭippu, ilakkaṇam ākiyavi avvēliyiliruntē tōṉṟi vaḷaruvataṟkāṉa nalla vāyppukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉpati uyttuṇaralām.
niṟikaḷ:  kaṟṟalil vaḻaṅkum āyattaviti, payiṟciviti, payaṉviti ākiya vitikaḷ7  mēṟkoḷḷappeṟuvatāl palavakit tiṟaṉkaḷil payiṟci ēṟpaṭṭu iṉṟiyamiyāta aṟivu vaḷarcci uṇṭākiṉṟatu.  uṟṟu nōkkal, cintaṉi, karututal, tīrmāṉam, kaṟpaṉi mutaliya maṉattiṉ paṉmuka vēlikaḷukku nalla payiṟci ēṟpaṭṭu nalla maṉappāṉmiyum naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.  paṭippil ārvatti uṇṭākkuvatuṭaṉ vāḻkkiyil nēriṭum nikaḻccikaḷukkup paḷḷiyilēyē pala payiṟcikaḷip peṟa vāyppiṉi aḷikkiṉṟatu.  kalviyē vāḻkkiyākum eṉpatik kaṟpavarum piṟarum naṉku uṇarkiṉṟaṉar. kuḻantikaḷ vāḻkkikkup payaṉpaṭum kalviyit tāṉ kaṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu teḷivākap pulaṉākiṉṟatu.  potuk kāriyattiṟkup pāṭupaṭukiṉṟōm eṉpatu veḷippaṭiyākat terivatāl, māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyē oṟṟumi uṇarcciyiyum kūṭṭuṟaviyum vaḷarkkiṉṟatu; māṇākkarkaḷ tāmākavē kaṟpatāl taṉmuyaṟciyum taṉṉampik kiyum vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivukkum tiṟaṉukkum ēṟṟavāṟu ceyalāṟṟa vāyppukkaḷiruppatāl, avarkaḷiṭiyē paṭippāṟṟal naṉku vaḷarkiṉṟatu. coṉṉaticcollum kiḷippiḷḷip paṭippukku iṅku iṭamilli; māṇākkarkaḷ ceymmuṟiyilēyē kalvi kaṟkiṉṟaṉar. cuṟucuṟuppu, kāytal uvattaliṉṟi uṇmikāṇum āval, piṟar karuttukku matippuk koṭuttal, kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa maṉap pāṉmi mutaliya paṇpukaḷ immuṟiyāl vaḷara iṭamuṇṭu. nāḷukku nāḷ māṟivarum ulaka nākarikattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu māṟivarum kalvinōkkam, paṭippu muṟikaḷ, kalvit tiṭṭam, pāṭattiṭṭam, tērvukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kellām immuṟi ēṟṟa muṟiyil vaḷintu koṭukkum taṉmiyip peṟṟuḷḷatu.
kuṟikaḷ:  immuṟiyil cila kuṟikaḷum kāṭṭap peṟu kiṉṟaṉa.  ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyum ōr oḻuṅkil kaṟpikka iyalātu; oḻuṅkākak kaṟpikka vāyppukkaḷi uṇṭākkuvatum kaṭiṉam.  itaṉāl im muṟiyi mēṟ koḷḷa āciriyarkaḷ piṉvāṅkukiṉṟaṉar.  toṭakka nilik kalvi muṭintavuṭaṉ ovvoru māṇākkaṉukkum ovvoru pāṭattilum ōraḷavu tērcciyāvatu kivarappeṟṟirukka vēṇṭum.  āṉāl, aḵtu im muṟiyāl ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟa iyalātu.  ceyal tiṭṭa vēlikkup puṟampākak kaṟkum oru tiṭṭattiyum ittuṭaṉ iṇittukkoṇṭāl kaṟṟal oḻuṅkuṭaṉum, toṭarccyiṉṟik kāṇappaṭum piḷavukaḷiṉṟiyum naṭipeṟum.  toṭakkanilip paḷḷikkāla aḷavil kaṟkakkūṭiyavaṟṟi atē kālattil immuṟiyiṉ vāyilākak kaṟkamuṭiyum eṉṟu colvataṟkilli.  kuṟippiṭṭa vēliyit tiṭṭamiṭṭapaṭi kuṟippiṭṭa kāla aḷavil muṭikkakkūṭum eṉṟu colla iyalātu.  im muṟiyil māṇākkarkaḷākavē vēlikaḷit tērnteṭuttalum arumi; tērnteṭutta piṟaku vēli ceyaṟpaṭumpoḻutu avarkaḷiṭiyē oḻuṅki niṟuvuvatum ciramam.  vaṟumi mikka namatu nāṭṭil mikunta paṇaccelavil ovvoru paḷḷiyilum immuṟiyi mēṟkoḷḷa iyalātu.  āṉāl, itu muṟiyiṉ nērmukamāṉa kuṟiyākātu.  im muṟiyil naṭipeṟum oru cila paḷḷikaḷ nam nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟil paṇiyāṟṟip paṭṭaṟivuḷḷa āciriyarkaḷum immuṟiyiṉ mēṉāṭṭu vallunarkaḷum akkuṟikaḷi yellām eḷitākak kaḷintu im muṟiyi mikavum muṟpōkkuṭiyatāka ākkalām.  eṉṟu uṟuti kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar.  toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil immuṟiyi ōraḷavu atikac celaviṉṟi mēṟkoḷḷalām.



4. vārtā kalvimuṟi
vārtā kalvimuṟiyit toḻil muṟik kalvi eṉṟum `ceymuṟik kalvi eṉṟum kūṟalām.  ittiṭṭatti nam nāṭṭut tanti kāntiyaṭikaḷ mattiyap piratēcattiluḷḷa vārtā eṉṟa ūril mutaṉ mutalākat toṭaṅkiyatāl ataṟku appeyar ēṟpaṭṭatu.  1937-ām āṇṭu akṭōpar mātam naṭipeṟṟa kalvi mā nāṭṭiṉ tīrmāṉatti yoṭṭi at tiṭṭam tayār ceyyappaṭṭu jākīr hīsiṉ kamiṭṭiyārāl uruvam koṭukkappeṟṟut tiruttiyamikkappeṟṟatu.  nam nāṭṭiṉ paṇpāṭṭiyum tēvikaḷiyum oṭṭi attiṭṭam uruvākkap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  nūṟṟimpatu āṇṭukāḷaka oru cilarukkāka eṉṟē tayārikkappeṟṟa kalvit tiṭṭam pōlaṉṟi, nāṭṭu makkaḷ aṉivarukkumē payaṉpaṭumpaṭiyāka it tiṭṭam tayārikkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ kivaṇṇattiyum karuttu vaṇṇattiyum it tiṭṭattil kāṇalām.
im muṟiyiṉ mukkiyak kūṟukaḷ:  payaṉ paṭattakka ētāvatu oru kittoḻili miyamāka vittukkoṇṭu atic cuṟṟiyē tēviyāṉa piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpittal immuṟiyiṉ mukkiya nōkkamākum.  iṅku, kittoḻilip poṟi iyaṅkuvatupōl kaṟpippatu illi; kittoḻil mūlamāka varalāṟu, nilanūl, kaṇakku, aṟiviyal, moḻi, vaṇṇa ōviyam, ici mutaliya piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷ kaṟpikkappeṟum.  kittoḻil oru katiravaṉ pōlirukka ēṉiya pāṭaṅkaḷ atic cuṟṟi iyaṅkum piṟa kōḷkaḷpōl toṭarpu koṇṭirukkum.  katiravaṉiṭamiruntu veppam, oḷi, āṟṟal mutaliyavaṟṟip piṟa kōḷkaḷ peṟuvatippōl piṟa pāṭaṅ kaḷum tērnteṭukkap peṟṟa kittoḻililiruntu tattamakku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷippeṟum.  ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷum tāymoḻi vāyilākavē kaṟpikkappeṟum; aṉṟiyum, kalvit tiṭṭattil tāymoḻikkē mutaliṭam tarappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  intutāṉi moḻikku iraṇṭāvatu iṭam aḷikkap peṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  āṅkilattiṟku ik kalvit tiṭṭattil iṭamē illi.  ēḻiliruntu patiṉāṉku vayatuvari ēḻāṇṭukaḷ kaṭṭāyak kalvi ilavacamāka aḷikkappeṟal vēṇṭum.  ēḻāṇṭukaḷil kuḻantikaḷ toṭakka nilik kalviyuṭaṉ uyarnilik kalviyiyum peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu eṇṇip pārttaṟkuriyatu.  ittiṭṭappaṭi toḻiṟkalviyāl māṇākkarkaḷ uṟpatti ceyyum poruḷkaḷiliruntu āciriyarkaḷiṉ ūtiyattiṟkup pōtiya varuvāy ēṟpaṭumpaṭi ceytal vēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum akimci, vāḻkkiyuṭaṉ oṭṭik kalvi payiṟṟal, ciṟanta kuṭimakaṉāka vaḷarntu taṉ kaṭamikaḷiyum urimikaḷiyum payaṉuḷḷa tuṟikaḷil vaḻaṅkal pōṉṟaviyum im muṟik kalviyiṉ mukkiya nōkkaṅkaḷākum.
vārtā kalvi muṟippaṭi (1) ētāvatu oru kittoḻil (2) tāymoḻi (3) kaṇakku (4) camūkappāṭam (5) potu aṟiviyal (6) vaṇṇavēliyum ōviyamum (7) ici (8) intutāṉi (9) tēviyāṉa kuṟinta aḷavu nūl nūṟṟal ākiyavi kaṟpikkappeṟutal vēṇṭum.  necavu, nūlnūṟṟal, taccuvēli, kāykaṟi paḻattōṭṭam, uḻavuttoḻil, vevvēṟu iṭattiṟkēṟṟa ētāvatoru vēli ākiyavaṟṟil ētāva toṉṟiṉit tērnteṭuttuk koḷḷalām.  aintām vakuppuvari āṇ piḷḷikaḷukkum peṇpiḷḷikaḷukkum orē pāṭattiṭṭamtāṉ uṇṭu; 6, 7 vakuppukkaḷil tiṭṭattil ciṟitu māṟṟam uṇṭu.  peṇ piḷḷikaḷukku kuṭumpa aṟiviyal pāṭamākac cērkkappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭavāṟu oru nāḷiṉ pāṭa vēḷippaṭṭi amiyum.  
	kittoḻil		3 maṇi 20 maṇittuḷikaḷ
	ici, ōviyam, kaṇakku             	 40  	,,
	tāymoḻi				40 	,,
	aṟiviyal, camūkam			30 	,,
	uṭaṟpayiṟci				10	,,
	ōyvu					10	,,
					_________
					mottam 		5 maṇi 30 maṇittuḷikaḷ.           
					_________
iṉṟiya kalvittiṭṭattil kāṇappeṟum tērvu muṟiyi at tiṭṭam ātarikkavilli; ati aṭiyōṭu nīkkiviṭa virumpukiṉṟatu.  ataṟkup patilāka aṭikkaṭi caripārttal, puṟanilip payaṉaṟi āyvukaḷ (Objective achievement tests), māṇākkariṉ pativup puttakaṅkaḷ, ciṟṟūr makkaḷukku māṇākkar ceyta camūkattoṇṭu ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭu avarkaḷit tēṟalām eṉṟu yōcaṉi kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  kalvik kuḻuviṉar kalvi paṟṟiya ēṟpāṭu, puṟanili āyvukaḷ, muṉṉēṟṟap payiṟṟu muṟikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil aṭikkaṭi putuppikkum ārāycci vakuppukaḷ (Refresher courses) naṭatti āciriyarkaḷiṉ aṟivip perukkuvar.  paḷḷikaḷi mēṟpārvi pārttuk kaṇkāṇikka mēṟpārvi yāḷarkaḷiyum parinturittuḷḷaṉar.  ivarkaḷ iṉṟuḷḷa kalvittuṟik kaṇkāṇippāḷarkaḷ pōlallātu, āciriyarkaḷukku naṭimuṟiyil tuṇi purivarēyaṉṟi, paḷḷi āṭciyil atikamākat taliyiṭār. 
it tiṭṭam veṟṟiyuṭaṉ ceyaṟpaṭavēṇṭumāṉāl āciriyarkaḷ tām poṟuppuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟavēṇṭum eṉpati it tiṭṭatti vakuttavarkaḷ uṇarntuḷḷaṉar.  paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ aṟivu, tiṟami, uṟcākam, nāṭṭuppaṟṟu ākiya paṇpukaḷuṭaṉ taṉippayiṟciyum peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum.  mutal nili (Senior) iraṇṭām nili (Junior) eṉṟa iraṇṭu nili āciriyar payiṟciyiyumpaṟṟi it tiṭṭam kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.  ittakiya payiṟṟumuṟi iṉṟu ṭilli, ājmīr, srīnakar, vārtā, nākapuri, alakāpāt, pūṉā, macūlippaṭṭiṉam ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilum aḷikkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
1945-ām āṇṭu caṉavari mātattil vārtāvil naṭi peṟṟa tēcīyak kalvi mānāṭṭiṉ viḷivāka ik kalvit tiṭṭam ciṟanta kavaṉattip peṟṟuḷḷatu.  eṉavē, it tiṭṭattil iṉṟu paḷḷi varuvataṟku muṉ kuḻantikaḷ kavaṉam peṟa vēṇṭiya toṭakkamuṉ nili, toṭakka nili, toṭakkap piṉnili, vaḷarntōr nili, (Pre-basic, Basic, Post-basic and Adult) eṉṟu nāṉku nilikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  ēḻāṇṭukaḷvari kuḻantikaḷ kuḻantip paḷḷikaḷil  (Nursery schools) uṭal, uḷa, camūka vaḷarccikaḷil kavaṉikkap peṟuvar.  toṭakkap piṉnilik kalviyip paṟṟiya virivāṉa tiṭṭam iṉṉum vakukkap peṟavilli.  eṉiṉum, attiṭṭam kirāmap puṉar amippup paṟṟi irukkum eṉṟum, avvēli uḻavut toḻil, pālpaṇṇi, kaṭṭiṭavēli, ulōka vēli, taccuvēli, necavu, vīṭṭu āṭci mutaliya tuṟikaḷ vāyilāka irukkum eṉṟum etirpārkkappeṟukiṉṟatu.  ataṟkup payiṟci aḷikka vēṇṭiya kallūrikaḷ ṭeṉmārk nāṭṭil niṟuvap peṟṟirukkum makkaḷ kallūrikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyi yoṭṭi niṟuvap peṟum eṉṟum etirpārkkap peṟukiṉṟatu. attakiya kallūri yoṉṟu ṭillik karukiluḷḷa nāṭṭuppuṟattil niṟuvappeṟṟirukkiṉṟatu.  aikkiya nāṭṭu aviyiṉ ātaravil aṉuppap peṟṟa kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ iṅkup paṇiyāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar.  ciṟitu kālam naṭimuṟiyiliruntu taṟkālikamāka niṟutti vikkap peṟṟirukkum vaḷarntōr kalvit tiṭṭattilum vārtāk kalvittiṭṭamtāṉ aṭippaṭi yākak koḷḷap peṟṟiruntatu.
ittiṭṭattiṉ niṟikaḷ:  tiṭṭattik kūrntu nōkkiṉāl atu navīṉa kalvi muṟiyiṉiyum uḷaviyaṟ koḷkikaḷiyum oṭṭi amikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpatum, namatu nāṭṭiṉ camūka, paṇpāṭṭu, araciyal, poruḷātāra nilikaḷi yoṭṭit tiṭṭam uruvākkap peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉpatum teriyavarum.  uḷaviyaṟpaṭi pāṭaṅkaḷ kaṟpikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ceymmuṟik kalviyiṉāl pulaṉkaḷukkup payiṟci peṟum vāyppu uḷḷatu.  kittoḻil mūlam kalvi peṟumpoḻutu māṇākkar kaḷiṭam tiṭṭam pōṭum muṟi, āyvukaḷ ceyyum muṟi, cintaṉi mutaliya paṇpukaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. ākkum iyalpūkkam nalla payiṟciyip peṟavum, kuḻanti muḻu āḷumiyuṭaṉ vaḷaravum vāyppukaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.  navīṉa payiṟṟu muṟikaḷi yoṭṭik kaṟpikkum muṟi amintuḷḷatu.  kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ karuttuppaṭi kuḻantikaḷ uṭal, uḷḷa, āṉmavaḷarcci peṟa it tiṭṭam mikavum tuṇipurikiṉṟatu.  tiṟamiyuṭaṉ kiyāṇṭāl it tiṭṭam kirāmap puṉaramippiṟkum, vēliyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭattip pōkkavum, ōyvu nēraṅkaḷip payaṉuḷḷa muṟikaḷil kaḻikkavum, toḻilāḷarkaḷiṉ uṟpattit tiṟaṉi vaḷarkkavum tuṇi ceyyakkūṭum.  uṭalvēlikkum maṉavēlikkum toṭarpu ēṟpaṭutti uṭaluḻippiṟkup perumiyuṇṭu eṉṟa maṉappāṉmiyiyum ēṟpaṭuttu kiṉṟatu.  nava intiyāvil nalla kuṭimakaṉukku vēṇṭiya paṇpukaḷi yellām vaḷarkkat tuṇipurikiṉṟatu ittiṭṭam.
kuṟikaḷ:  ik kalvimuṟiyil cila kuṟikaḷum kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  āciriyar vāḻkkikkāka māṇākkar uḻikkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟa eṇṇam virumpattakkatāka illi.  iḵtu it tiṭṭattiyē kīḻnilikkukkoṇṭu varukiṟatu eṉṟa karutti nilavac ceykiṉṟatu.  payaṉik karutic celaviṭap peṟum tokikku ēṟṟavāṟu varuvāyi etirpārkka muṭiyātu.  aṉṟiyum, kuḻantikaḷ uṟpatti ceyyum poruḷkaḷ poṟi uṟpattip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭi pōṭṭu, cantiyil viṟpaṉiyākum eṉṟu colvataṟkilli.  vēṟu paṇpukaḷi yellām maṟantu kittoḻil maṉappāṉmikku maṭṭilum mukkiyattuvam aḷikkiṟatu immuṟi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  kittoḻil mūlam kalvi payiṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatē yaṉṟi, kit toḻil oru toḻilpōl kaṟpikkappeṟavilli eṉpati uṇarntāl ik kuṟiyiṉik kūṟār.  paṇpāṭṭi vaḷarkkum ilakkiyam, camūkiyal pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷum immuṟik kalvit tiṭṭattil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  poṟikaḷ palkiyuḷḷa ikkālattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu ik kalvimuṟi amiyavilli.  toḻil nuṇukka vaḷarccikku ikkalvi muṟi tuṇipuriyā tiruppatuṭaṉ taṭiyākavum irukkiṉṟatu.  iḵtu ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyum iyaṟkiyāṉa muṟiyil toṭarpupaṭuttik kaṟkum vāyppukkaḷi nalkavilli; tiṟamiyuṭaṉ kiyāṇṭāl ik kuṟiyi nīkkalām.  im muṟiyil kalvi payilum māṇāk karkaḷ piṟamuṟiyil kaṟpikkappeṟum paḷḷikaḷukku māṟa vacatikaḷ illi.  naṭimuṟik kalvit tiṭṭattil kuṟikaḷi ruppiṉum ati aṭiyōṭu nīkkiviṭa muṭiyātu.  takka pāṭap puttaṅkaḷ illātatāl ovvoru āciriyarum avaravar maṉappāṉmik kēṟṟavāṟu karuttukkaḷit tiṇikkak kūṭum.  im muṟi ulaka moḻiyākiya āṅkilattukku iṭantaravilli.  iḵtu īṭu ceyya muṭiyāta peruṅ kuṟiyākum.  uṭaṟ payiṟci, kuḻantikaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭuṇarcci, camayappāṭam, putiya muṟit tērvukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi ittiṭṭam puṟakkaṇik kiṉṟatu.  it tiṭṭam virumpum āciriyarkaḷi uṇṭākkuvatum kaṭiṉam.  atuvum it tiṭṭam kuṟippiṭum ūtiyattip pārttu uṟcākamuḷḷa iḷiñarkaḷ kalvittuṟiyil paṇipurivatiyē virumpār.


5. naṭippu muṟi
naṭippumuṟi viḷiyāṭṭumuṟiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu; maṉattiṉ āṟṟalkaḷil mukkiyamāṉatākiya kaṟpaṉiyiṉ viḷivāka naṭipeṟuvatu.  kaṟpaṉi ūṟṟup perukku iṉṟēl, entak kaliyum vaḷarccipeṟa iyalātu.  kaṟpaṉi payiṟṟalāltāṉ kuḻantikaḷ aritāṉa ceyalkaḷi eḷitāka niṟivēṟṟukiṉṟaṉar; ūṉakkaṇṇāl kāṇa muṭi yātaṉavaṟṟiyellām maṉakkaṇṇāl kāṇkiṉṟaṉar; muṉṉark kaṇṭa vaṟṟit tirumpavum niṉittup pārkkiṉṟaṉar.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭu ulakil uṇmikkum pāvaṉikkum atika vēṟṟumi illi.  pāvaṉiyiṉ āṟṟal elliyaṟṟatu.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ pāvaṉi ulakil oru ciṟukaḻi mōṭṭārākum; marakkaṭṭikaḷ vaṇṭikaḷākum; nāṟkāli kappalākum; ūñcal puki vaṇṭiyākum. kuḻantikaḷ peṟṟōrākavum, āciriyarākavum, aracaṉākavum, āṇṭiyākavum, kaṭikkāraṉākavum, pāṟkāra ṉākavum, vaṇṭi ōṭṭiyākavum naṭippatil pēriṉpam aṭikiṉṟaṉar.   cātāraṇamāka vīṭukaḷil āṇ kuḻantikaḷ ivvāṟu naṭippatiyum,  peṇ kuḻantikaḷ ciṟu cōṟu camittal, ciṟṟilpuṉital, pommit tirumaṇam pōṉṟa viḷiyāṭṭuk kaḷil īṭupaṭuvatiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm.
āciriyarkkuc cila kuṟippukaḷ:  kuḻantikaḷiṭam kāṇap peṟum inta naṭippu uṇarcciyip payaṉuḷḷa vaḻikaḷil tiruppuvatutāṉ āciriyariṉ kaṭami. atil tāṉ avar tiṟami naṉku pulaṉākum.  tamiḻ kaṟpippatil vāymoḻip payiṟci, eḻuttu ākiya iraṇṭiṟkum vakuppil naṭippatip payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.  kuḻantikaḷiṭam oru tiruttamāṉa nāṭakatti etirpārkka vēṇṭiyatilli; etirpārttalum tavaṟu.  nāṭakatti uruvākkal, nāṭakattiṟku vēṇṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ, pēccukkaḷ tayārittal, naṭikarkaḷit tīrmāṉittal, naṭikarkaṭku vēṇṭiya āṭikaḷ tayārittal, ottiki vittal ākiya ceyalkaḷāltāṉ immuṟiyiṉ muḻup payaṉiyum eyta muṭiyum.  potu makkaḷukku nāṭakatti naṭittuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu nōkka miruntālum, mēṟkaṇṭa ceyalkaḷiṉ mūlantāṉ kuḻantikaḷ niṟinta paṭṭaṟivip peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ eṉṟum niṉivil vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  ciṟu kuḻantikaḷik koṇṭu aṭikkaṭi vaḷarnta varkaḷ muṉṉāl naṭikkac ceyvatum ciṟantataṉṟu.  vēṇṭu māṉāl tam vayatuḷḷa pārviyāḷarkaḷ muṉṉar naṭikka ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyyalām.  vaḷarntavarkaḷukku muṉ naṭikkap pōkiṉṟōm, eṉṟa niṉiviyē kuḻantikaḷiṭam eḻac ceyvatu tavaṟu.
ciṟu kuḻantikaḷ naṭippataṟkut tērnteṭukkap peṟum katikaḷ mikavum eḷiyaṉavāka irukka vēṇṭum; virivāṉa araṅku amippil naṭikkak kūṭiyaviyākattāṉ iruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatilli.  kuḻantikaḷ tāmāka viḷiyāṭu vatik kūrntu kavaṉittāl ettakiya katikaḷ avarkaḷ naṭippataṟku ukantavi eṉpatu teriya varum.  ōnāyum āṭṭuk kuṭṭiyum, ciṅkamum cuṇṭeliyum, cīcī intap paḻam puḷikkum,, nariyum kākkiyum,, kuraṅkum pūṉikaḷum. eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa cātāraṇak katikaḷē kuḻantikaḷ naṭippataṟkut takutiyāṉa nikaḻccikaḷit tarum.  īcōp katikaḷiyum pañcatantirak katikaḷiyum ciṟanta muṟiyil ciṟu kuḻantikaḷiṉ naṭippiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttalām.  mukkiyamāka āciriyar kavaṉikka vēṇṭiyatu itu. kuḻantikaḷukkākat tērnteṭukkappeṟum katikaḷ avarkaḷ paṭṭaṟivi oṭṭiyaṉavāka irukka vēṇṭum. katiyil varum nikaḻccikaḷ avarkaḷ naṉku purintu koḷḷumpaṭi irukkavēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, katiyil varum nikaḻccikaḷ teḷivākavum, kuṟikkōḷi yuṭiyaṉavākavum irukka vēṇṭum.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivi mīṟiya katikaḷik kuḻantikaḷik koṇṭu naṭikkac ceyvatil payaṉ illi.
tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa katiyi nāṭakamākkuvatil kuḻantikaḷiṉ paṅku atikamiruttal vēṇṭum.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷukku iṭam koṭuttal vēṇṭum. āciriyariṉ muṉ kūṭṭiya tiṭṭappaṭik katiyi nāṭakamākkuvatāl payaṉ oṉṟum irātu.  ivvāṟu kuḻantikaḷik koṇṭu tīrmā ṉittalil vaḻi vilakippōkakkūṭumeṉṟu āciriyar karutiṉāl, tammuṭiya yōcaṉikaḷik kūṟi avarkaḷic cariyāṉa muṟiyil koṇṭuceluttalām.  perumpālum ciṟu kuḻantikaḷ tam tavaṟukaḷi eḷitākak kaṇṭukoḷvar.  nāṭakam uruvākik koṇṭirukkum poḻutu nēriṭum uccarippup piḻi, eḻuttup piḻi, coṟṟoṭar amippil nēriṭum piḻikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil āciriyar cilacamayam taliyiṭṭut tiruttalām.  nāṭakam uruvākum poḻutu itic ceyvatum tavaṟu; ottikiyiṉ poḻutu it tavaṟukaḷit tiruttalām.  perumpālum kuḻantikaḷē nāṭakattit tayārittal naṉṟu.  āṉāl, cila camayaṅkaḷil kuḻantikaḷiṉ nilikkum, paṭṭaṟiviṟkum ēṟṟapaṭi eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa oru cila nāṭakaṅkaḷiyum naṭikkac ceyyalām.  perumpālum avaṟṟip puttakaṅkaḷiliruntu poṟukkuvati viṭa, āciriyarē eḻututal naṉṟu.  an nāṭakaṅkaḷ kuḻantikaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷit tirutti nērāṉavaḻikaḷil uyppatit tavira, tamakku ukantavi ettakiyaṉavāka irukka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum kāṭṭum.
āciriyar muṉāyattamillāmalēyē oru katiyi naṭikkum vāyppiṉi vakuppiṟku nalkalām.  oru katiyi oru muṟi vakuppil paṭittuṇarntapiṉ vakuppilēyē māṇāk karkaḷik koṇṭu nērāka naṭittalil iṟaṅkac ceyyalām. tēviyāṉa naṭikarkaḷip poṟukkutal, yār yār ententap pāttiramāka iruntu naṭikkavēṇṭum eṉpatit tīrmāṉittal ākiyavi muṭivuṟṟatum kuḻantikaḷ naṭittalil iṟaṅkuvar.  avaravar iṭam vantavuṭaṉ vaḻakkampōl uriyāṭalkaḷi neṭṭuruc ceytu oppuvikkāmal iṭattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu eṅṅaṉam pēcavēṇṭum eṉpati avaravarkaḷē taṅkaḷāl iyaṉṟavari kaṟpaṉiyil uṟuti ceytu koṇṭu pēcavēṇṭum.  uriyāṭalkaḷ mutalil āciriyar etirpārkkum nilikku amiyāviṭiṉum paḻakap paḻaka itil nalla payaṉik kāṇalām.  itil kaṟpaṉi vaḷarccikku mikka iṭam uḷḷatu.  āciriyarkaḷ im muṟiyik kiyāḷuvataṟku niṟinta paṭṭaṟivuṭiya varkaḷākavum, im muṟiyil tērntavar kaḷākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.  ittakiya naṭippi iraṇṭām vakuppiliruntē toṭaṅkalām.  atikamāṉa pēccukku iṭamillāta mikac ciṟiya katiyil toṭaṅkiṉāl nāḷaṭivil nalla payaṉi eytalām; oru tiṭṭappaṭi payiṟci tarappeṟiṉ, kuḻantikaḷ aintāvatu vakuppiṉi aṭiyumpoḻutu itil nalla tiṟaṉi yaṭivar.  immuṟiyil vakuppiluḷḷa oru kuḻu naṭippil iṟaṅkum poḻutu mītiyuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ  ati naṉku kavaṉittuk kuṟikaḷik kaḷintu nalla tiruttaṅkaḷit taralām.  ciṟitu nēram payiṟci peṟṟatum, nāṭakam nalla muṟiyil uruvākum; pāttiraṅkaḷiṉ pēccu ciṟanta muṟiyil amiyum.  im muṟiyil uruvākum nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ uriyāṭalkaḷ iyaṟkiyāka irukkum; kuḻantikaḷiṉ kaṟpaṉikku nalla vāyppukaḷ kiṭikkum.  tām mēṟkoṇṭa pāttiraṅkaḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu kuḻantikaḷ coṟkaḷiyum naṭippiṉiyum kiyāḷuvar.
kuḻantikaḷ tattam pakutikaḷi maṉappāṭam ceytapiṉ naṭikkum naṭippilum payaṉ uṇṭu.  itil niṉivāṟṟal vaḷarum.  itiyum vakuppilēyē naṭikkalām.  vakuppil nāṭaka uṭi, tiriccīlikaḷ, oppaṉikaḷ, kāṭcikaḷ mutaliyaṉa vēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāyam illi.  ellā naṭippukaḷilum uriyāṭalkaḷil eṭuttal, paṭuttal, nalitalkaḷik koṇṭa ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷiyuṭiya kural amiyavēṇṭum; cemmiyāṉa uccarippu, teḷivāṉa olippu mutaliyaṉa mukkiyamāṉa kūṟukaḷ, naṭippu valintum nalintum pōkāmal iyalpāka, uyiruḷḷatāka irukka vēṇṭum.  uṇarcciyum, maṉaveḻuccikaḷum, uḷaccuvikaḷum, meyppāṭukaḷum poṅki niṟka vēṇṭum.  icikku aḷavāṉa vāyppukkaḷittaralām; aḷavukku mīṟakkūṭātu.  pāṭat terintavartāṉ naṭippil kalantu koḷḷalām eṉṟa eṇṇamē kuḻantikaḷiṭam vaḷara viṭakkūṭātu.  aṉivarumē naṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟi vēṇṭum.  paḷḷi āṇṭuviḻā,  ilakkiyak kaḻaka viḻā pōṉṟavaṟṟil maṭṭilum kati nikaḻnta kālattiṟku ēṟṟa tiriccīlikaḷ, oppaṉikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi mēṟ koḷḷalām.
immuṟiyāl viḷiyum niṟikaḷ:  iyalpākak kuḻantikaḷiṭam kāṇappeṟum accam, kūccam, naṭukkam, paṭapaṭappu, colliṉ kaṭi eḻuttiyum coṟṟoṭariṉ kaṭic colliyum viḻuṅkal ākiya kuṟikaḷip pōkka nalla vāyppukkaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  pēccu, uriyāṭal mutaliyavi vāymoḻip payiṟcikku vāyppiṉi nalkukiṉṟaṉa.  viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyil kaṟpatāl kaṟṟalil paṟṟi ṉi ēṟpaṭuttuvatuṭaṉ kaṟṟa poruḷkaḷum pacumarattāṇi pōl maṉattil naṉku patiyum.  immuṟi poṟikaḷukkum kaṟpaṉikkum niṉivāṟṟalukkum uṇarccikkum nalla muṟiyīṭu ceykiṉṟatu eṉṟu collām.  maṉa eḻuccikkuriya meyp pāṭukaḷ naṉku veḷippaṭa vāyppiṉi aḷikkiṉṟatu.  vāḻkkiyiṉ uṇmikaḷiyum muṟikaḷiyum paṭṭaṟivāl aṟintu koḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.  nallataṉ nalaṉiyum tīyataṉ tīmiyiyum nēraṭiyākak kāṇpatāl kuḻantikaḷiṉ oḻukka nili nalla muṟiyil amiyum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉap pāṉmiyi virivaṭiyac ceytu avarkaḷiṭam paṭip pāṟṟali naṉku vaḷarkkiṉṟatu.  curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, immuṟi muttamiḻiṉ uṇmiyi orē camayattil naṉku aṟiyat tuṇiceykiṉṟatu.
mēlē kuṟippiṭṭaṭa aintu muṟikaḷilum kuḻantikaḷ kuḻuvāka iruntu kaṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu karuttiliṟutta vēṇṭiya toṉṟu.
5. payiṟṟu muṟikaḷ - (2)
ceṉṟa iyalil ārāyntavi pōka eñciyuḷḷa aintu muṟikaḷip paṟṟi inta iyalil ōraḷavu aṟintu koḷḷuvōm.
1. taṉip payiṟci muṟi
vakuppu muṟiyiṉ kuṟikaḷ:  uḷaviyaṟ kaliyiṉ vaḷarcciyāl kuḻantikaḷip paṟṟip pala uṇmikaḷi aṟinta piṟakum kuḻuvākak kaṟpikkum muṟi naṭimuṟiyil irukkat tāṉ ceykiṉṟatu.  uḷaviyaṟkali kuḻantikaḷip paṟṟip pala uṇmikaḷi namakkut tantirukkiṉṟatu.  paḷḷiyil payilum kuḻantikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉ orē mātiriyāka irātu.  cilar niṟimati yuṭiyavarkaḷākavum, cilar kuṟimatiyuṭiyavarkaḷākavum, cilar naṭunili aṟivuṭiyavarkaḷākavum iruppar.  ivvāṟu vevvēṟu vitat tiṟaṉkaḷi yuṭiya māṇākkarkaḷaṭaṅkiya vakuppil orē vitamākak kaṟpittal naṭi peṟṟāl, ellōruṭiya tērcciyum ōraḷavu pātikkappeṟum; niṟimati yuṭiyōr virivāka muṉṉēṟuvataṟku vakiyiṉṟi, vēliceyya vāyp piṉṟip piṟarukkākak kāttukkoṇṭiruntu cōmpit tirivar.  kuṟimati yuṭiyōr tērccik kuṟiviṉāl uṟcākatti iḻappar.  naṭunili aṟivuṭiyavar ippaṭiyum appaṭiyu miṉṟit tiṇṭāṭuvar.  aṉṟiyum, vakuppiluḷḷa ellā māṇākkarkaḷum orē vēkattil kaṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟavum iyalātu.  kuḻantikaḷ kaṟpatu avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉaṟivu, uṟcākam, maṉappāṉmi mutaliya pala kūṟukaḷip poṟuttatu.  oru māṇākkaṉ tavirkka muṭiyāta kāraṇattāl vakuppiṟkup pala nāṭkaḷ  vara muṭiyātu pōyviṭṭāl, avaṉ vakuppumuṟik kalviyāl viṭupaṭṭa pakutiyik kaṟka iyalātu.  naṭunili māṇākkarukkuriya kalvit tiṭṭamum pāṭa vēḷippaṭṭiyum (Time table) ēṉiya iru nili māṇākkarkaḷukkum ottu varātatāl vakuppil avarkaḷiṭam kuṟumpu, cōmpal, muyaṟcik kuṟivu, kalviyil kavaṉakkuṟivu mutaliya keṭṭa paḻakkaṅkaḷ peruki vakuppu āṭcikkut taṭiyākavum irukkum.
taṉip payiṟci muṟiyi mēṟkoḷḷum vaḻi:  mēṟ kūṟiya kuṟikaḷi nīkkuvataṟkākavē taṉippayiṟci muṟi mēṟ koḷḷap peṟukiṉṟatu.  itil māṇākkarkaḷ viruppappaṭi virivākavō metuvākavō kaṟkum vāyppukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa;  māṇākkarkaḷum tāmākavē kaṟkum vāyppiṉip peṟukiṉṟaṉar.  paḷḷiyil kaṟkavēṇṭiya aṉittum immuṟiyil kaṟka iyalātu pōkalām.  āṉāl perumpakutikaḷi immuṟiyil kaṟka iyalum.  oppaṭippukaḷiṉ (Assignments) tuṇiyāl immuṟiyi nalla pōkkil koṇṭu celuttalām.  āṇṭiṉ toṭak kattil ovvoru māṇākkaṉiṭamum oppaṭippukaḷiṉ aṭṭiyum āciriyariṭam oppaṭippu viḷakka aṭṭiyum irukkavēṇṭum.  tamiḻāciriyar oru vakuppil ōr āṇṭil kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya pāṭaṅkaḷip pala ciṟu pirivukaḷākavum ovvoru ciṟu piriviyum pala uṭpirivukaḷākavum pirittuk koṇṭu ovvoru uṭpiriviṟku taṉittaṉi oppaṭipput tāḷkaḷ tayārittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.
āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil āciriyar mutal oppaṭipput tāḷi māṇākkar kaḷiṭam koṭuttu, koṭutta tētiyit taṉṉiṭamuḷḷa oppaṭippu viḷakka aṭṭiyil māṇakkarkaḷiṉ peyarukku nērākak kuṟittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  cilar oppaṭip pukkaḷi virivil muṭittu viṭakkūṭum.  avarkaḷ maṟṟavar kaḷukkākak kāttirāmal iraṇṭāvatu oppaṭipput tāḷip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum.  vakuppu muḻuvatum ovvōr oppaṭipput tāḷiyum orē camayattil peṟa muṭiyātu.  ovvoruvaruṭiya aṟivunili, maṉappāṉmi, ārvam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟṟavāṟu atip pala camayaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttuvar.  ovvoruvarum oppaṭippukkaḷi muṭitta tētiyi āciriyar tam oppaṭippu viḷakka aṭṭiyil kuṟittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  ivvāṟē māṇākkarkaḷum tattam oppaṭippu aṭṭikaḷil tāmtām oppaṭippukkaḷip peṟṟa tēti, avaṟṟi muṭitta tēti ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ aṉṟāṭap pāṭatti āyattam ceyyumpoḻutum, vīṭṭu vēliyic ceyyumpoḻutum, paḷḷiyil tamiḻppāṭa vēḷiyilum iv voppaṭippu vēliyac ceyvar.  paḷḷikku veḷiyē evveppoḻutu iv vēliyic ceyyalām eṉṟa yōcaṉikkuṟippi āciriyar māṇākkar kaḷiṭam terivikkalām.  naṭunilippaḷḷi vakuppukaḷil paḷḷip pāṭavēḷi kaḷilēyē iv vēliyic ceytu muṭittu viṭalām.  uyarnilippaḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil vīṭṭu vēliyic ceyyum kālamum, paḷḷiyiṉ tamiḻp pāṭattukkuriya pāṭa vēḷiyum ivvoppaṭippu vēlikkup pōtumāṉatu.  oppaṭippukkaḷit tayārikkum poḻutu oru carācari māṇākkaṉi maṉattiṟkoṇṭu tayārikka vēṇṭum.  oppaṭippukkaḷ tēvikku vēṇṭiya aḷavukkuc ciṟitāka iruppiṉum tavaṟu illi; peritākap pōkātu pārttuk koḷḷutal nalam.
paḷḷiyil tamiḻppāṭavēḷikaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ oppaṭippu vēliyil īṭupaṭṭirukkumpoḻutu avarkaḷ āciriyar utaviyi vēṇṭuṅkāl vāyppukkaḷit tārāḷamāka avarkaḷukku nalkavēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ oruvari yoruvar kalantu yōcikkavum iṭantaralām.  āṉāl, avarkaḷ oruvariyoruvar pārteḻutāmalirukkap pārttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ nūlakattiluḷḷa puttakaṅkaḷit tārāḷamākappayaṉpaṭuttum vāyppukkaḷ irukkavēṇṭum.  evarum enta oppaṭippiṉi vēṇṭumāṉālum eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.  ētāvatu ōr oppaṭippil pala māṇākkar kaḷukku viḷaṅkāta nili ēṟpaṭṭāl avarkaḷit taṉiyē kūṭumpaṭi ceytu avarkaḷukku ap pakutiyit teḷivāka viḷakkalām.  oru māṇākkaṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa oppaṭip piṉi muṭittuviṭṭāl āciriyar avaṉ muṭitta pakutiyil vāymoḻi viṉākkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu, avaṉatu eḻuttu vēliyi matippiṭalām.  ciṟitu paṭṭaṟivu ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku āciriyar iv vēliyi virivil muṭikkalām.  māṇākkaṉ ceyta vēliyil āciriyar tirupti aṭintāl aṭutta oppaṭippiṉi avaṉukkuk koṭukkalām.  aṭutta oppaṭippu vēliyit toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉpu, muṉ oppaṭippil kāṭṭappeṟṟa tavaṟukaḷi avaṉ tiruttikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa nipantaṉi irukkavēṇṭum.  aṭutta oppaṭippu tiruttattiṟku varuṅkāl muṉ oppaṭippil kuṟippiṭappeṟṟa tavaṟukaḷ tiruttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉavā eṉpati āciriyar kavaṉikkavēṇṭum.  eḻuttu vēlikaḷik kuṟippēṭṭiṉ (Note book) valatu puṟattilēyē ceytāl, iṭatu puṟattit tirutta vēlikku vittukkoḷḷalām.
oru māṇākkaṉuṭiya oppaṭippu vēliyil āciriyar maṉaniṟivu aṭiyāviṭṭāl, enta iṭattil cariyāka illi eṉpati eṭuttukkāṭṭi avaṉukku vēṇṭiya tuṇi puriyalām; piṟaku cariyāka muṭittu varumāṟu ēvalām.  āciriyar ovvoru varuṭiya aṟivunilikkēṟṟavāṟu vēliyiṉ aḷaviyum niliyiyum uyarttavum ceyyalām; kuṟikkavum ceyyalām.  taṉṉiṭamuḷḷa oppaṭippu viḷakka aṭṭiyik kavaṉittu virivākak kaṟpavarkaḷiyum metuvākac kaṟpavarkaḷiyum aṟintu, metuvākak kaṟpavarkaḷukkut tēviyāṉa aḷavu utavi puriyalām.  cila camayam pala māṇākkarkaḷ orē camayattil oppaṭippu vēliyi muṭittuviṭṭuk kūṭṭamāka varutal kūṭum.  appaṭippaṭṭa camayattil āciriyar paḷḷi vēli muṭinta piṟaku oru kuṟippiṭṭa nērattil ittirutta vēliyi vittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  nāḷtōṟum in nili ēṟpaṭātu.
oppaṭippukkaḷ:  oppaṭippukkaḷ kaṟpittalukkut tuṇiyāka irukkum kuṟippukkaḷē.  oppaṭippukkaḷil oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattil oru māṇākkaṉ ceyya vēṇṭiya vēli vivarikkappeṟṟirukkum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, oru vārattil allatu oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattil paṭikkavēṇṭiya pakuti, eḻutavēṇṭiya aḷavu, vāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉ aḷavu mutaliyavi kāṇappeṟum.  māṇākkaṉuṭiya cintaṉiyit tūṇṭuvataṟkum avaṉākavē pala poruṭkaḷik kaṇṭaṟi vataṟkum kuṟippukkaḷ tarappeṟṟirukkum.  taṉ aṟivip payaṉpaṭutti muṭivukaḷik kāṇumāṟu cila kuṟippu viṉākkaḷ avaṟṟil kāṇappaṭum.  āḻntu paṭittaṟkut tēviyāṉa kuṟippukkaḷum nūlakap puttaṅkaḷilum pāṭapputtaṅkaḷilum kāṇāta cila nuṭpamāṉa kuṟippukkaḷum avaṟṟil iṭam peṟṟu irukkum..  taṉ muyaṟci vēliyi māṇākkaṉ eṅṅaṉam toṭaṅkavēṇṭum eṉṟa viḷakkamum, kaṭiṉap pakutikaḷiṉ poruḷviḷakkamum col viḷakkamum avaṟṟil aṭaṅkiyirukkum.  naṉku cintippataṟkum paṭippataṟkum eṉṉeṉṉa puttakaṅkaḷiliruntu eṉṉeṉṉa poruḷkaḷip peṟalām eṉṟum, eṉṉeṉṉa puttakaṅkaḷip paṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum oppaṭippukkaḷil puttakaṅkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal koṭukkap peṟṟi rukkum.  oppaṭippukkaḷi āciriyar mikak karuttuṭaṉum cintaṉiyuṭaṉuṭam āyattam ceytal vēṇṭum.  māṇāk kariṉ maṉanili, viruppuveṟuppukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟintu avaṟṟi āyattam ceyyavēṇṭum.
immuṟiyiṉ niṟikaḷ:  taṉip payiṟci muṟiyāl māṇākkarkaḷ avaravar tiṟamikkēṟṟavāṟu virivākavum metuvākavum viruppappaṭiyum kaṟpataṟku vāyppukkaḷip peṟukiṉṟaṉar.  ataṉāl ellōriṭamum kaṟpatil viruppamum paṟṟum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.  piṟar kaṟpikkum poruḷkaḷik kēṭṭup payaṉaṭivatil mūḷikku atika vēli illi; āṉāl tāṉākap payiṟci peṟum vēliyil mūḷi atikamāka īṭupaṭukiṉṟatu.  mūḷi, maṉam, ceyal  ākiya mūṉṟum cuṟu cuṟuppāka iyaṅki uṇmiyāṉa kaṟṟal naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu.  immuṟiyil māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivu nili, maṉappāṉmi, vayatu, viruppam, vīṭṭiṉ cūḻnili mutaliyavaṟṟi yoṭṭik kaṟṟal vaḷarcciyaṭikiṉṟatu.  im muṟiyil māṇākkarkaḷik kavaṉikkum vāyppu āciriyarukku atikam uṇṭu; avarkaḷ viḷakkam kāṇātu tikikkum iṭaṅkaḷik kaṇṭaṟivataṟkum vāyppukkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.  immuṟiyil kuṟinta kālattil atikappakuti kaḷik kaṟka muṭikiṉṟatu.  mēl vakuppukkaḷil kiṭṭattaṭṭa kaṟṟal vīṭṭil naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu; tērcciyi aṟital maṭṭilumtāṉ paḷḷiyil naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu.  im muṟiyil kaṟkavēṇṭiya pakutikaḷ tiṭṭamākat terivatāl, māṇākkar tam vēliyil tiruptiyum paṭippil kavarcciyum koḷvar.  taṉ kiyē taṉakku utavi eṉṟa koḷkiyi vaḷarttup piṟar tuṇiyi etirpārttu niṟkum maṉap pāṉmiyik kuṟikkiṉṟatu immuṟi.
cila kuṟikaḷ:  im muṟiyil cila kuṟikaḷum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  āciriyariṉ vēli mikutiyākiṉṟatu.  aṭikkaṭi oppaṭippukaḷip putiyaṉa putiyaṉavākat tayārip patilum eḻuttu vēlikaḷit tiruttuvatilum āciriyar atika nēram paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭiyatākavuḷḷatu.  vakuppukkaḷ periya ṉavāka iruntāl, āciriyar vēli ceyvatu arumiyumākum.  muppatu māṇākkarkaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa vakuppukaḷil āciriyarukkuc ciramam atikamākattāṉirukkum.  cila camayaṅkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ oruvariyoruvar pārttu eḻutiviṭakkūṭum; āciriyar viḻippāka iruntu atik kavaṉittal vēṇṭum.  aṭikkaṭi ciṟu tērvukaḷi naṭatti māṇākkar vēliyi aḷantaṟital vēṇṭum.  oppaṭippukkaḷi acciṭuvatil atikac celavu ēṟpaṭum.  nalla nūlakaṅkaḷum immuṟikkut tēviyāyi ruppatāl, puttakaṅkaḷ vāṅkap paṇaccelavu ēṟpaṭum.  paḷḷikaḷil nūlakaṅkaḷi vaḷarttuttāṉ ākavēṇṭum.  nūlakattil aṭikkaṭi nūlkaḷ keṭṭuppōkum; kāṇāmaṟ pōkavum kūṭum.  im muṟi meḷḷappōkiṉṟatu.  orē vita oppaṭippuk kaḷi ellā aṟivu niliyiluḷḷavarkaḷukkum payaṉpaṭuttu vatu cariyaṉṟu.  taṉip payiṟci muṟiyil taṉṉalamē mikutiyākat taliyeṭuttu, kūṭṭuṟavup paḻakkamum ottuṇarcciyum illāmaṟ pōkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.
cintittup pārttāl ivi yāvum kuṟikaḷ alla eṉpatu teriyavarum.  ivi yāvum āṭci muṟiyil nēriṭum caṅkaṭaṅkaḷē.  pala paḷḷikaḷ oṉṟu cērntāl acciṭum celaviṉaṅkaḷik kuṟittuk koḷḷalām.  nūlakattil nēriṭum piracciṉikaḷi oruvāṟu camāḷikkattāṉ vēṇṭum.  paṭippaṭiyākavuḷḷa cila oppaṭippukkaḷit tayārittu cila kuṟikaḷi nīkkikkoḷḷalām; iti muṟiyiṉ kuṟi eṉṟu eṇṇutal tavaṟu.  etilum kuṟikaḷ irukkattāṉ ceyyum.
 kuṇam nāṭik kuṟṟamum nāṭi avaṟṟuḷ
miki nāṭi mikka koḷal.1
eṉṟa poyyāmoḻik kiṇaṅka, kuṇaṅkaḷiyum kuṟi kaḷiyum cīrtūkki, kuṇaṅkaḷi maṭṭilum mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  immuṟiyit tiṟamiyuṭaṉ mēṟkoṇṭāl ataṉāl aṭiyum perumpayaṉkaḷ oru cila kuṟikaḷi maṟakkac ceytu viṭum.  āṭci muṟiyil nēriṭum caṅkaṭaṅkaḷi eṇṇi muṟiyik kuṟi kūṟutal kūṭātu.

2. ṭālṭṭaṉ tiṭṭam
ṭālṭṭaṉ tiṭṭam taṉip payiṟci muṟiyi aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭa oru muṟi.  oṉṟupaṭṭa amerikka nāṭukaḷil mecācuceṭ eṉṟa mānilattil ṭālṭṭaṉ eṉṉum ūril immuṟi tōṟṟuvikkap peṟṟatu.  helaṉ pārk harṭ eṉṉum ammiyār itaṉit tōṟṟuvittār.  vayatil vēṟupaṭṭa muppatu māṇākkarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkum poṟup piṉi ēṟṟa poḻutu cilarukku oppaṭippukkaḷik koṭuttum, cilarukkuk kuḻuk kuḻuvākak kaṟpittum paṇiyāṟṟi vantār.  māṇṭisāri ammiyāriṉ muṟiyi ivar naṉku aṟinti runtār; eṉavē. ammuṟiyiṉ toṭarcciyē taṉatu muṟi eṉṟu anta ammiyār kūṟukiṉṟār.  intat tiṭṭattip peyaraḷavil nām muṟi eṉṟu kūṟiṉālum pāk harṭ ammiyār iti orumuṟi eṉṟō kalvit tiṭṭam eṉṟō kūṟātu itu kalvipaṟṟiya oru cīrtiruttam eṉṟum, kalvi kaṟṟal, kaṟpittal eṉṟa iru ceyalkaḷukkum oru toṭarpu ēṟpaṭuttum cātaṉamē eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟār.
immuṟiyiṉ viḷakkam:  paḷḷiyiṉ naṭimuṟiyip putiya muṟiyil amittu, im muṟiyi eḷitil mēṟ koḷḷalām.  it tiṭṭam pāṭattiṭṭattil māṟṟam virumpa villi.  kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷil oru muṟiyiyum taḻuvavilli.  taṉippaṭṭa māṇākkaṉiṉ tēvikaḷiyoṭṭi immuṟiyit toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.  itaṉāl ellā aṟivunili māṇākkar kaḷum payaṉ aṭivar.
ṭālṭṭaṉ tiṭṭattil vakuppu aṟikaḷ illi; pāṭavēḷippaṭṭikaḷ illi; tērvukaḷ, vakuppu māṟṟam mutaliya atirccitarum nikaḻccikaḷ illi; vakuppu āciriyar eṉṟu oruvar irār.  iṅku vakuppu aṟikaḷ pāṭa āyvakaṅ kaḷāki viṭum; āciriyarkaḷtām pāṭavallunarkaḷ; māṇāk karkaḷ āyvu naṭattuvōr.  pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷukkup patilāka oppaṭippukkaḷ, tērccip pativut tāḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik kiyāḷuvar.  ovvoru pāṭattiṟkum taṉittaṉi āyvakam uṇṭu.  aṅku appāṭattiṟku vēṇṭiya ellāp poruḷ kaḷum, ellā nūlkaḷum, ellāt tuṇik karuvikaḷum, piṟa tēvip poruḷkaḷum irukkum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka tamiḻ āyvakattil tamiḻppērakarāti  (Tamil Lexicon), palvēṟu akarāti nūlkaḷ, apitāṉa cintāmaṇi, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, aivaki ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ, cillaṟip pirapantaṅkaḷ, kamparāmāyaṇam, villipāratam, taṉippāṭal tiraṭṭu mutaliya mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷ irukkum.  āciriyaratu pēccum kaṟpittalum paḻiya muṟipōl iṅku illi.  āciriyar āḻntu akaṉṟa kalvi aṟivuṭiyōrāy kallāliṉpuṭiyamarnta teṉmukak kaṭavuḷ pōl vīṟṟiruppār.  paṭikkum cūḻniliyi nilavac ceytal, oppaṭippukkaḷi viḷakkutal, kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷ kaḷi evvāṟu paṭittal eṉa vaḻikāṭṭal, tuṇik karuvikaḷ paṟṟiya yōcaṉi kūṟal, oru kuṟippiṭṭa poruḷiyum pāṭattil ataṉ toṭarpiyum viḷakkutal pōṉṟa ceyal kaḷi mēṟ koḷḷalē avaratu ceyalāka irukkum.  māṇākkarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi avariṭam ceṉṟu tammiṭiyē tōṉṟum aiyaṅkaḷi akaṟṟikkoḷḷuvar.
naṭimuṟi:  im muṟiyil pāṭavēḷippaṭṭi eṉṟa oṉṟuilli.  paḷḷi kāli 8-45 maṇiyiliruntu māli 4-30 maṇivari naṭipeṟum.  piṟpakal 1-maṇiyiliruntu 2-maṇivari uṇavukoḷḷa ōyvu nēram uṇṭu.  ettaṉip pāṭaṅ kaḷuḷḷaṉavō attaṉi vitamāṉa oppantaṅkaḷ (Contracts) irukkum.  ovvoru māṇākkaṉum ōrāṇṭiṟkuḷ ittaṉi oppantaṅkaḷ ceytu muṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu oppantam ceytu koḷvatāl iḵtu ip peyar peṟṟatu.  oru paḷḷiyāṇṭil ettaṉi mātaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉavō attaṉi oppantaṅkaḷ oru pāṭattiṟku uṇṭu.  oṉpatu mātaṅkaḷ koṇṭa oru paḷḷi āṇṭil tamiḻukku maṭṭilum oṉpatu oppantaṅkaḷ taravēṇṭiyirukkum.  ovvōr oppantamum nāṉku pirivu kaḷākavum (Periods), ovvoru pirivum aintu uṭpirivu kaḷākavum (Units) pirikkap peṟṟirukkum.  ovvoru vāra, māta, āṇṭukkum āciriyar muṉṉatākavē oppaṭippukkaḷi acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkiviṭuvār.  ivvoppaṭippukkaḷē it tiṭṭattiṉ uyirnili eṉṟu collavēṇṭum.  taṉip payiṟci muṟiyil oppaṭippukkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟiya vivaram iṅku poruntum2.  ovvoru oppaṭippum oru curuṅkiya mukavuri, paṭikka vēṇṭiya pakutiyil eḻutavēṇṭiya talippu, atip paṟṟi eḻuta vēṇṭiya kuṟippukkaḷ, viṭiyiṟukkavēṇṭiya viṉākkaḷ, avaṟṟiṟkellām vēṇṭiya mēṟkōḷ nūl vivaraṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭatāyirukkum.
muṟpakalil māṇākkarkaḷ tām virumpum āyvakaṅkaḷil oppaṭippu vēliyil īṭupaṭṭiruppar.  piṟpakalil pāṭa vallunariṉ ucitappaṭi ciṟu kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, vāymoḻip payiṟcikaḷ, uṭaṟ payiṟcikaḷ mutaliyavi naṭipeṟum.  appoḻutu pāṭavallunar māṇākkariṉ paṭṭaṟivukku appāṟpaṭṭa, mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷil kāṇamuṭiyāta, cila ariya karuttukkaḷ eviyēṉu miruntāl avaṟṟik kūṟuvār.  ovvoru vakuppiṟkum ovvoru pāṭattilum oru mātattiṟ kuriya oppaṭippukkaḷ muṉṉarē koṭukkappeṟum.  oru mātattiṟkuriya ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiṉ oppaṭippukkaḷiyum muṭittuviṭṭut tāṉ aṭutta mātattiṟkuriya oppaṭippuk kaḷip peṟutal vēṇṭum.  āṉāl, oru mātattiṟkuḷ pāṭaṅkaṭkuriya oppaṭippukkaḷi māṇākkarkaḷ tattam viruppappaṭi ceyyalām.  tēvikkēṟṟavāṟu oru pāṭattiṟku atikak kālattiyum iṉṉoru pāṭattiṟkak kuṟivāṉa kālattiyum celaviṭalām.  oppaṭippu vēliyil īṭupaṭṭirukkum poḻutu māṇākkarkaḷ avaravar viruppappaṭi ōr āyvakattiliruntu maṟṟoru āyvakattiṟkup pōkalām; pōy āciriyariyō vēṟu māṇākkariyō aṇukit tam aiyaṅkaḷip pōkkik koḷḷalām.
tērcci:  paḻiya muṟiyil uḷḷa tērvukaḷ pōṉṟavi iṅku illi.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tērcci varipaṭaṅkaḷ (Graphs) mūlam aḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu.  nāṉku vari paṭaṅkaḷ kiyāḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avaṟṟil iraṇṭu paṭaṅkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṭamum, iraṇṭu paṭaṅkaḷ āciriyarkaḷiṭamum irukkum.  māṇākkaḷiṭamiruppavaṟṟil oṉṟu vārattiṉ pāṭa vaḷarcciyiyum maṟṟoṉṟu mātattiṉ pāṭa vaḷarcciyiyum kāṭṭum.  āciriyarkaḷiṭa miruppaṉavaṟṟil oṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷ vanta nāṭkaḷiyum, maṟṟoṉṟu vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷaṉivariṉ pāṭa vaḷarcciyiyum terivikkum.  māṇākkarkaḷ tammiṭa muḷḷa varipaṭaṅkaḷiṉāl tām ep pāṭaṅkaḷil evvaḷavu tērcci peṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatiyum, evaṟṟil atikak kavaṉam celutta vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum terintu koḷḷalām.   āciriyar tamatu varipaṭaṅkaḷiliruntu em māṇākkaṉ taṉiyākak kavaṉikkap peṟavēṇṭum eṉpatit terintu koḷḷa iyalum.  ivaṟṟiṟkellām kaṇṇum karuttumākak kaṇkāṇikkum āciriyarkaḷiṉ mēṟpārvi mikavum vēṇṭaṟ pālatu.  naṭunili uyarnilip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil tamiḻp paṭippu, ilakkaṇam, kaṭṭuri, ceyyuṭ pāṭaṅkaḷil immuṟiyi mēṟkoḷḷalām.  iṉṟiya vakuppumuṟip payiṟṟalilēyē it tiṭṭattic ciṟitu kiyāḷalām.  ovvoru pāṭam toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉpu ap pāṭam paṟṟiya cila viṉākkaḷi āciriyar tayārittu acciṭṭu allatu karumpalakiyil eḻuti avaṟṟiṉ viṭikaḷik kaṇṭaṟiyumāṟu ceyyalām.  itu pāṭamuṉ oppaṭippu (Pre - lesson assignment) eṉappaṭum.  ivvāṟē pāṭam muṭinta piṟaku cila payiṟci viṉākkaḷit tantu viṭi eḻutac ceyyalām.  itu pāṭap-piṉ oppaṭippu  (Post-lesson assignment) eṉappaṭum.  immātiriyāṉa payiṟcikaḷ cila pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟāl māṇāk karkaḷiṉ taṉ muyaṟci tūṇṭappeṟum; vāykkuṭ paṭikkum paḻakkamum naṉku vaḷarum.
it tiṭṭattiṉ niṟikaḷ:  it tiṭṭattil ovvoru māṇākkaṉum taṉakkuriya vēkattil kaṟkiṉṟāṉ.  māṇākkarkaḷ poṟuppuṭaṉ kaṟpatāl uṇmiyāṉa tērcciyi aṭiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  iṅku māṇākkarkaḷē taṉ muyaṟciyāl kaṟpatāl, avarkaḷiṭam nalla payiṟci ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu; puttakaṅ kaḷip paṭippatil ārvam uṇṭākiṉṟatu.  tattam tēvik kēṟṟāṟ pōl cila pāṭaṅkaḷil atikak kavaṉattiyum, cilavaṟṟil kuṟinta kavaṉattiyum celutta muṭikiṉṟatu.  iṅkuppeṟum taṉ muyaṟci, poṟuppu, paṭṭaṟivu mutaliya paṇpukaḷ piṟkāla vāḻkkikkup payaṉpaṭum.  etirpārāta kāraṇaṅkaḷāl oru māṇākkaṉ paḷḷikku vara iyalāviṭil, ataṉāl peruṅkēṭu oṉṟum viḷivatilli; vanta piṟaku avaṉ viṭṭa iṭattil toṭaṅkip paṭikkum vāyppu iṅku uḷḷatu.  vakuppu māṟṟam iṅku illātatāl, ellōrum paṭittu muṉṉēṟa muṭikiṉṟatu.  oruvaruṭiya kālamum muyaṟciyum iṅku vīṇāvatilli.  vīṭṭu-vēliyiṉ (Home-work) kaṭṭāyam iṅkilli; ātaṉāl māṇākkarkaḷ tām atic ceyya villiyē eṉṟu añcat tēviyilli.  oḻuṅku muṟipaṟṟiya piracci ṉikaḷē iṅku eḻuvatilli; veṟuppu, calippu, kaṭṭāyam, accam mutaliyavi iṅku irātatāl oḻuṅku muṟi tāṉāka naṉku amintu viṭukiṉṟatu.  āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷit taṉittaṉiyāka naṉku aṟiyum vāyppu uḷḷatu; cutantiram mikkiruppatāl māṇākkar āciriyari aṭikkaṭi neruṅkip paḻaki aiyaṅkaḷi akaṟṟikkoḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.  āciriyar māṇākkariṉ naṇparākavum, vaḻikāṭṭiyākavum, neṟiyāḷa rākavum iruntu māṇākkarkaḷi naṉṉeṟikaḷil koṇṭu celutta muṭikiṉṟatu.  im muṟiyil kiyāḷappeṟum tērcci muṟi varipaṭaṅkaḷ, māṇākkar tam tērcci niliyit tāmē aṟiyavum, piṟpōkku, muṟpōkku māṇākkarkaḷi āciriyar aṟiyavum, māṇākkar oruvarōṭoruvar pōṭṭi yiṭṭuk koṇṭu vēli ceyyavum tuṇi ceykiṉṟaṉa.
cila kuṟikaḷ:  it tiṭṭattil cila kuṟikaḷum kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  māṇākkarkaḷukkut tēviyāṉa aḷavu puttakaṅkaḷ vāṅkuvataṟkup paṇaccelavu atikamākum; tēvi yāṉa ellāp puttakaṅkaḷum tāymoḻiyil kiṭikkum eṉṟum colvataṟkilli.  tēviyāṉa tuṇikkaruvikaḷic cēkarip patum kaṭiṉam.  nam nāṭṭil atikac celavil im muṟiyik kiyāḷuva teṉpatu iyalāta kāriyam.  oppaṭippukkaḷi āyattam ceyvatum eḷitaṉṟu; paṭṭaṟivum āḻnta pulamiyum uṭiya āciriyarkaḷālēyē avaṟṟi āyattam ceyya muṭiyum.  uṭaṟpayiṟci, ici, ceyyuḷ, vāymoḻip payiṟci mutaliyavaṟṟi im muṟiyāl kaṟka iyalātu.  āṉāl kuṟikaḷiviṭa niṟikaḷē atikamāka iruppatālum, kiṭikkum payaṉkaḷum atikamāka iruppatālum immuṟiyik kiyāḷalām eṉṟu muṟivallār kūṟuvar.
paḻamiyil tōyntu kiṭakkum āciriyarkaḷ putiya muṟi etiyum virumpuvatilli.  mēlē kuṟikaḷākak kūṟappeṟṟavi aṉittum āṭci muṟip piracciṉi kaḷēyaṉṟi uṇmiyāṉa kuṟikaḷalla eṉpati eṇṇip pārppavarkaḷukku eḷitil pulaṉākum.
3. māṇṭisāri muṟi
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil taṉittaṉik kaṟṟal muṟi celvākkup peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu.  uḷaviyaṟ kaliyiṉ vaḷarcciyāl kuḻantikaḷip paṟṟiya pala ariya uṇmikaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ovvoru kuḻantiyum ovvoruvita taṉivīṟuṭaṉ uḷḷatu eṉṟa uṇmi kalvi nipuṇarkaḷiṉ karuttik kavarntuḷḷatu.  vakuppil pala kuḻantikaḷukkuc cērntāṟpōl kaṟpittāl kuḻantikaḷit taṉittaṉiyākak kavaṉittal iyalātu eṉṟu avarkaḷ karutalāyiṉar.  iṅṅaṉam kuḻuvākak kaṟpikkum muṟi nīkkappaṭa vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākavē māṇṭisāri muṟi tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu.  immuṟiyiṉ nōkkam ovvoru kuḻantiyiyum taṉittaṉiyākak kavaṉikkavēṇṭum eṉpatē.
māṇṭisāri ammiyār:  māṇṭisāri ammiyār ittāli nāṭṭic cārntavar; ki.pi. 1870il piṟantār.  urōm palkalik kaḻakattil em..ṭi. paṭṭam peṟṟavuṭaṉatapalkalikkaḻakattil uḷaviyaṟkala ārāyccit tuṟip pakutiyilkuṟimatiyuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷik kaṇkāṇikkum vāyppiṉip peṟṟār.  avvāṟu paṇiyāṟṟum poḻutu kuṟimatiyuṭimik kuk kāraṇam pulaṉkaḷiṉ cariyāṉa vaḷarcciyiṉmiyē eṉpatik kaṇṭār.  poṟikaḷukku nalla payiṟci koṭuttu ak kuḻanti kaḷic ciṟitu aṟivuṭiyavarkaḷākac ceyya muṭiyum eṉpati aṟintār.  ivvāṟu kuṟimatiyuṭiya kuḻantikaḷiṉ kalviyil paṇiyāṟṟiya ammiyār naḷaṭivil niṟimatik kuḻantikaḷiṉ kalviyilumnaṭṭam bcaluttiṉār. pallāṇṭukaḷ ittuṟiyil arumpaṇiyāṟṟiyataṉ viḷivākap pulaṉkaḷiṉ payiṟci vāyilākak kuḻantikaḷukkuk kaṟpikkum putiyatoru muṟiyik kaṇṭaṟintār.  atuvē māṇṭisāri muṟi eṉpatu.  ammiyār taṉ paḷḷiyik kuḻantikaḷiṉ illam eṉṟu peyariṭṭār.  ammiyāriṉ aṟiviyaṟ kaliyaṟivutāṉ ip putiya muṟiyik kaṇṭaṟiyum vāyppiṉi nalkiṟṟu eṉṟukūṭac collalām.
im muṟiyiṉ tattuvam:  māṇṭisāri ammiyār ḵpirāpelip pōlavē kuḻantikaḷic ceṭikaḷākak karutiṉār: kuḻantikaḷiṉ piṟappilēyē avarkaḷiṉ piṟkāla vāḻviṟku vēṇṭiya aṉittum maṟinta niliyil amin tuḷḷaṉa eṉpatu ammiyāriṉ kōṭpāṭu, avvāṟu maṟintu kiṭakkum āṟṟal malarcciyuṟuvataṟku vēṇṭiya vacatikaḷic ceyvatutāṉ āciriyaratu paṇiyākum; avi iyalpāka malarvataṟku vēṇṭiya cūḻniliyi maṭṭilumtāṉ āciriyar ceyya vēṇṭum; kuḻantikaḷi ecceyalilum kaṭṭāyappaṭuttak kūṭātu; avarkaḷukku muḻu urimi tara vēṇṭum; evvitattilum avarkaḷik kaṭiyak kūṭātu.  ovvoru kuḻantiyum ovvoruvitamāka irukkum; ataṉuṭiya taṉivīṟi evvitattilum citikkak kūṭātu; atic citikkak kūṭiya vakuppu muṟik kaṟpittali mēṟkoḷḷak kūṭātu.  kuḻanti tāṉākak kaṟkavēṇṭum eṉṟum āciriyar aṭikkaṭi atil taliyiṭutal kēṭupayakkum eṉṟum ammiyār karutu kiṉṟār.  ovvoru kuḻanti kaṟpatilum ovvoru vitamāka viriviṉ aḷavu iruttal kūṭum; virivu karuti āciriyar avarkaḷākak kaṟpatil taliyiṭak kūṭātu.  immuṟiyil kaṟpataṟku āciriyar tēviyilli; āṉāl kuḻantikaḷ tammittāmē cariyāṉa muṟiyil koṇṭu celuttum āṟṟalip peṟātiruppatāl, avarkaḷukkeṉa aṟivūṭṭat takka tuṇikkaruvi (Didactic aparatus) oṉṟik kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷār; kuḻantikaḷ ceyyum tavaṟukaḷ avaṟṟāl tāmākavē kaḷiyap peṟṟu viṭum.  tāmākak kaṟkum kalvitāṉ uṇmiyāṉa kalviyākum eṉpatu ammiyāriṉ nampikki.  iḷaṅkuḻanti kaḷukkut tacikaḷiṉ iyakkattiṟkum vāyppu aḷikka vēṇṭum eṉpati ammiyār mukkiyamākak karutukiṉṟār; ip payiṟciyāl kuḻantikaḷ tammittāmē kavaṉittukkoḷḷum āṟṟalip peṟuvar eṉṟum, eḻututal, naṭattal, ōṭutal mutaliya ceyalkaḷ nalla muṟiyil amiyum eṉṟum nampukiṉṟār.  ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mēlākak kuḻantikaḷukkup pulaṉvāyilākak kalvi pukaṭṭuvatiyē mukkiyamākak koḷḷu kiṉṟār.  pulaṉkaḷukku nalla payiṟci aḷikka vēṇṭum eṉpatu ammiyāriṉ kōṭpāṭu; pulaṉkaḷē veḷiyulaka aṟiviṉip peṟum vāyilkaḷ eṉpatu avar karuttu. mūṉṟu vayatiliruntu ēḻu vayatuvari kuḻantikaḷukku ip payiṟciyi avaciyam aḷikkavēṇṭum eṉṟum, apparuvattiltāṉ kuḻantikaḷ atikamākak kaṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum ammiyār tam paṭṭaṟivāl kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷār.  pulaṉkaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyāltāṉ aṟivu vaḷarcci eyta muṭiyum eṉpatu ammiyāriṉ karuttu.
immuṟiyiluḷḷa pirivukaḷ:  ammiyār kaṇṭa kalvi muṟiyil mūṉṟu pirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
mutalāvatu:  iyakkak kalvi, itil kuḻantikaḷukku avaravar viruppappaṭi viḷiyāṭuvataṟku vāyppukkaḷ aḷikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  kuḻantikaḷ illattiṉ cūḻniliyik kavaṉittuk koḷḷum poṟuppu avarkaḷiṭamē viṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.  iyakkak kalviyāl kuḻantikaḷiṭam oḻuṅku (Discipline) naṉku amikiṉṟatu.  naṭattal, uṭkārutal, poruḷkaḷip paṟṟal, tam uṭampik kavaṉittuk koḷḷutal, illattik kavaṉittuk koḷḷutal, tōṭṭavēli, tāḷatti yoṭṭiya iyakkaṅkaḷ (Rhythmic movements) ākiyavaṟṟil kuḻantikaḷ iyakkak kalviyiṉip peṟukiṉṟaṉar.  tammuṭiya āṭikaḷik kaḷintu koḷvatilum uṭuttik koḷvatilum kuḻantikaḷ payiṟciyiṉip peṟu kiṉṟaṉar.  itaṟkāka ammiyār tamatu aṟivūṭṭum tuṇik karuviyil pala caṭṭaṅkaḷi amittu avaṟṟil pala tuṇit tuṇṭukaḷiyum tōltuṇṭukaḷiyum poruttiyuḷḷār.  avaṟṟiṟkup pottāṉkaḷip poruttutal, avaṟṟik kaṭṭutal, māṭṭutal mutaliyavaṟṟil kuḻantikaḷ payiṟci peṟuvar.  uṇkalaṉkaḷi oliyiṉṟi eṭuttup payaṉpaṭuttal, tam mukaṅkaḷit tāmē kaḻuvik koḷḷutal, naṭiyaṉkaḷit (Sandals) tūymiyāka vittukkoḷḷutal, tōṭṭavēli, kaḷimaṇ pommikaḷ ceytal ākiyavaṟṟik kuḻantikaḷ makiḻcciyuṭaṉ mēṟkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  pāṭṭuṭaṉum pāṭṭiṉ ṟiyum tāḷattiṟkotta payiṟcikaḷ tarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iraṇṭāvatu:  pulaṉkalvi, aṟivūṭṭum tuṇik karuvikaḷāl iḵtu aḷikkappeṟukiṉṟatu.  aimpulaṉkaḷukkum payiṟci aḷikkak kūṭiya cātaṉaṅkaḷ it tuṇikkaruvikaḷil uḷḷaṉa.  iṅku orē mātiriyākavuḷḷa poruḷkaḷi aṟital, palvēṟu poruḷkaḷiliruntu avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷi aṟintukoḷḷutal, kiṭṭattaṭṭa orēmātiriyākavuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumikaḷi nuṇuki uṇartal ākiya vaṟṟil kuḻantikaḷ payiṟci peṟukiṉṟaṉar.  vaṭṭam, caturam, cevvakam, cāyvu caturam pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷiyum palvēṟu nuṇṇiya vaṇṇaṅkaḷiyum, palvēṟu nuṇṇiya olikaḷiyum, vaṉmi meṉmittāḷaṅkaḷiyum, nīḷa akala uyara vēṟupāṭu kaḷiyum, iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum kuḻantikaḷ tāmākavē naṉku aṟintukoḷḷum vāyppiṉip peṟukiṉṟaṉar.  māṇṭi sāri ammiyār oru pulaṉ payiṟci yaṭiyum poḻutu kūṭiyavari piṟa pulaṉkaḷ vāḷāyirukkumpaṭi ceytal nalam eṉṟu karutukiṉṟār.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kaṭpulaṉum cevip pulaṉum tēviyirātapoḻutu avaṟṟi maṟittup piṟavaṟṟiṟkup payiṟci tarutal ciṟappu eṉpatu ammiyāriṉ karuttu.
mūṉṟāvatu:  moḻi kaṟṟal, kaṇakkum moḻiyum tuṇik karuvikaḷālēyē kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  pulaṉ payiṟci muṭivuṟṟatum paṭittal, eḻututal, kaṇakku ākiyavaṟṟik kaṟkum ceyalkaḷ toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa; avi pulaṉ payiṟciyiliruntu nērāka vaḷarcciyuṟukiṉṟaṉa; mutalil ammiyār ap pāṭaṅkaḷit tam kalvi muṟiyil cērkkavilli.  piṟkālattil tāṉ avaṟṟic cērttār.  immuṟiyil eḻututal mutalil toṭaṅkap peṟu kiṉṟatu. upput tāḷil (Sand paper) eḻuttukkaḷ veṭṭap peṟṟu avaṟṟi kuḻantikaḷ viralkaḷāl taṭavip payiṟci peṟukiṉṟaṉar.  nāḷaṭivil paṭippaṭiyāka eḻutu kōli iyakkum āṟṟalip peṟukiṉṟaṉar,  kaṇṇālum kiyālum eḻuttukkaḷi aṟiyum āṟṟal virivil kivarap peṟukiṉṟatu.  eḻuttukkaḷik kaṟkumpoḻutu avaṟṟiṉ olikaḷum kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉavē kaṭpulaṉum, taci iyakkamum, toṭṭuṇarum pulaṉum eḻuttoliyuṭaṉ toṭarpu paṭuttap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  paṭippataṟku muṉṉar eḻutuvatē eḷitu eṉṟu ammiyār karutukiṉṟār.
immuṟiyiṉ ciṟappiyalpukaḷ:  im muṟi aṟiviyal muṟiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amintuḷḷatu.  āyvukaḷālum paṭṭaṟivālum kaṇṭa meymikaḷukku mukkiyattuvam aḷikkap peṟukiṉṟatu.  viṭutaliyuṇarvuṭaṉ kuṭiyāṭci muṟiyil kalvi pukaṭṭum muṟiyi mutaṉmutalil ulakiṟku uṇarttiyavar inta ammiyār avarkaḷē.  kalvit tuṟiyil taṉivīṟu vaḷarcci peṟuvataṟku vaḻikōliyavarum ivarē.  tacikaḷiṉ iyakkattil karuttiṉic celutti eḻutum ceyalil muṉṉēṟṟa vaḻiyi ēṟpaṭuttiṉār.  paṭippatilum eḻutuvatilum aḷaviṭṭa paṭikaḷi vakuttu iraṇṭum oṉṟiyoṉṟu taḻuvic cellumpaṭi ceytuḷḷār.  pulaṉkaḷukku mukkiyattuvam koṭuttu uḷaviyaṟ koḷkippaṭi kaṟpikka vēṇṭum eṉpatiyum valiyiṟuttiyuḷḷār.  ivar amitta oḻuṅku taṉṉaṭakkattiliruntu taṉṉōkkac ceyalkaḷiliruntum ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu; vaḷarntavarkaḷ taliyiṭāmal kuḻantikaḷē tāmākap peṟum kalvitāṉ uṇmiyāṉa kalvi eṉṟu ammiyār nampukiṉṟār. taṉ kiyē taṉakku utavi eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyiṉ karuttik kuḻantikaḷ tāmākavē paṭṭaṟivu mūlam peṟukiṉṟaṉar.
cila kuṟikaḷ:  im muṟiyil cila kuṟikaḷiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.  vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya aṉittiyum aṟivutarum tuṇikkaruvikaḷāl peṟa iyalātu.  vāḻvil nēriṭum ettaṉiyō nikaḻccikaḷāl uṇmik kalviyip peṟa muṭiyum.  ammiyāriṉ taṉivīṟupaṟṟiya karuttu uṭaṟ kūṟiṉiyoṭṭiyē uḷḷatu; maṉattiṉ aṭippaṭiyil kāṇak kiṭakkum cila mukkiya uṇmikaḷukku ammiyār iṭantaravilli.  pulaṉkaḷukkut taṉiyāka aḷikkappeṟum payiṟci navīṉa uḷaviyal uṇmikaḷukku ukantatāka illi.  mūṉṟu vayatiliruntu ēḻu vayatu variyilumuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ aimpulaṉkaḷukkum appāṟpaṭṭa viyattaku viṉākkaḷik kēṭpataṟku ammiyāriṉ koḷki viḷakkantaravilli.  ak kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉam catā tuṭippuṭaṉiruppataṟkuriya kāraṇatti ikkoḷki pulappaṭuttavilli.  ammiyāriṉ tuṇik karuvikaḷ kuḻantikaḷ tam uṇarccikaḷi veḷiyiṭuvataṟku nalla vāyppukaḷit taravilli.  viruppappaṭi palavitamākap pēca virumpum kuḻantikaḷ it tuṇikkaruvikaḷāl payaṉ aṭivatu mikak kuṟivu eṉṟutāṉ colla vēṇṭum.  ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷiyum oṉṟuṭaṉoṉṟu poruttik kaṟpikkum vāyppukkaḷ im muṟiyil illi; ellām tuṇik karuvikaḷi yoṭṭiyē iyaṅkavēṇṭiyuḷḷaṉa.  āciriyar yuktippaṭi ceyyum tuṇikkaruvikaḷi iṅkup payaṉpaṭuttum vāyppu illi.  namatu nāṭṭup paḷḷikaḷil immuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟuvatu eḷitaṉṟu.  tuṇikkaruvikaḷiṉṟi im muṟiyik kiyāḷa muṭiyātu; tuṇikkaruvikaḷum malivākak kiṭip patilli.  eṇṇip pārttāl ivi yāvum muṟiyiṉ kuṟikaḷ alla eṉpatu teḷivākum.


4. mēṟpārvip paṭippu
mēṟpārvip paṭippu eṉpati oru kaṟpikkum muṟi eṉṟu colluvativiṭa, paṭippil nalla paḻakkaṅkaḷi uṇṭākka mēṟkoḷḷum oruvaḻi eṉṟu collalē poruntum.  mēṉāṭṭuk kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ itik kuṟittup palvēṟu karuttukkaḷit terivittuḷḷaṉar.  piruṉvel eṉṟa aṟiñar tamatu nūlil mēṟpārvip paṭippu patiṉāṉku vakippaṭum eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār.3  pāciṅ eṉṟa muṟivallār ellāvita mēṟpārvip paṭippukkaḷiyum iraṇṭu vakiyil aṭakkiviṭalām eṉṟu karutukiṉṟār.
mutalāvatu:  māṇākkarkaḷukkup paṭikkum muṟikaḷil ataṟkeṉa otukkappeṟṟuḷḷa pāṭa vēḷiyil āciriyar vaḻikāṭṭi avarkaḷip paṭikkumāṟu ceytal.  aḵtāvatu, antap pāṭa vēḷiyil āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷōṭu cērntu paṭittu, taṉittaṉi māṇākkarkaṭkut tēviyāṉa tuṇipurintum, āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷukku eppaṭip paṭippatu eṉpatik kāṭṭiyum kaṟpittalē mēṟpārvip paṭippākum. ituvē perumpāṉmi yōriṉ karuttumākum.
iraṇṭāvatu:  oru kuṟippiṭṭa nērattil paṭippati maṭṭilum mēṟpārvip paṭippu eṉṟu kūṟuvatu poruntātu; māṇākkariṉ ellāp paṭippu vēlip pakutikaḷiyum itu kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  ṣrev (Shreve), kilsar (Kilzer) eṉpār ak karuttiyē koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl mutalāvatu vakiyic cariyāṉa mēṟpārvip paṭippu eṉṟu pāsiṅ oppuk koḷḷukiṉṟār.  oru tiṭṭamum teḷivu millāta paṭippi mēṟpārvip paṭippu eṉṟu kūṟutal poruntātu eṉpatu avar karuttu.  eṉavē, avar karuttuppaṭi, tiṟaṉuḷḷa paṭippiṉ pala vaḻikaḷiyum māṇākkar uṇarntu paṭikka āciriyar utavi ceytu payaṉuḷḷa muṟiyil vakuppil putuppāṭa oppaṭippuk kaḷip paṭippatē mēṟpārvip paṭippākum.  ik karuttuppaṭi mēṟpārvip paṭippil mūṉṟu paṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avi: (1) payaṉuṭaṉ paṭippataṟkuriya ciṟanta vaḻikaḷiyum muṟikaḷiyum māṇākkar aṟiyumpaṭi ceytal; (2) māṇākkarkaḷ paṭikkum poḻutu āciriyarē vaḻikāṭṭiyāka niṉṟu ammuṟikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉi avarkaḷ eytumpaṭi ceytal; (3) vakuppaṟiyil oru pāṭa vēḷiyil putuppāṭa oppaṭippukkaḷik kaṟkum poḻutu āciriyar tuṇiyāka iruttal.  
mēṟpārvip paṭippu nikaḻuṅkāl, āciriyar māṇākkaratu paṭippil aṭikkaṭit taliyiṭakkūṭātu.  viḷiyāṭṭukaḷil oru naṭuniliyāḷar mēṟkoḷḷum ceyalip pōṉṟatoru poṟuppiṉittāṉ avar mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭum.  paṭippil māṇakkarkaḷukku nēriṭum aiyaṅkaḷip pōkkalām; tavaṟāṉa muṟiyil avarkaḷ cellumpoḻutu atit taṭuttu nērāṉa vaḻiyil koṇṭuceluttalām.   paṭippil palavakit tiṟaṉkaḷip peṟavum, uḷnōkkam peṟavum, pala pakutikaḷil cuvi yaṟintiṉ puṟavum vēṇṭiya tuṇikaḷic ceyyalām.  vakuppil pāṭam naṭipeṟum poḻutu āciriyar vāymoḻi, paṭaṅkaḷ, collōviyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl viḷakkan tantum; naṭi muṟi, karuttukkaḷ, koḷkikaḷ, ārāyvu nōkkaṅkaḷ,  maṉappāṉmikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyum; paṭippil tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarkkalām.  mēṟpārvip paṭippil taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil tiṟamiyuṭaṉ taṉ vēliyic ceyyum vaḻiyi māṇākkaṉukku eṭuttukkāṭṭit tuṇiceyvatiyē āciriyariṉ ciṟanta vēliyākak kūṟalām eṉṟu kālviṉ eṉṟa muṟivallār karutukiṉṟār.  eṉavē, iṟṟināḷil putumuṟikaḷil mēṟpārvip paṭippiyum, ataṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷiyum, mēṟpārvip paṭippi naṭattum muṟikaḷiyum, ataṉāl viḷiyum naṟpayaṉkaḷiyum aṭikkaṭi nām kēḷviyuṟukiṉṟōm.
mēṟpārvip paṭippiṉ avaciyam:  kalvi pukaṭṭat toṭaṅkiyanāḷ toṭṭu putiyapāṭatti ōraḷavu muṉṉarē paṭittuvaravēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttu nilaviyapōtilum, inta muṉ tayārippilum āciriyarukkup poṟuppuḷḷatu eṉṟa koḷki mika aṇmiyilēyē karukkoṇṭu urup peṟṟatu.  irupatāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutiyiltāṉ mēṟ pārvip paṭippu paṟṟiya pala puttakaṅkaḷ veḷiyāyiṉa; aṟiñar palar atu paṟṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.  nāḷaṭivil ataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi ellōrum uṇaralāyiṉar.4  ataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmikku nāṉku kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa., avi:
tērcci peṟāmi:  iṉṟu kalvi paṉmuka virivi aṭintuḷḷaṉatu; ērāḷamāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷilum kallūrikaḷilum payiṉṟu varukiṉṟaṉar.  avarkaḷuḷ tērcci peṟṟu mēlē celluvōr mikac cilarē.  perumpāṉmi yōr tērcciyuṟātu pōkiṉṟaṉar; palar kalvi niliyaṅkaḷit tuṟantu tāmākavē niṉṟu pōkiṉṟaṉar.  ivvāṟu tērcciyuṟā tiruppataṟkum iṭiyil niṉṟu pōvataṟkum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉa vaḷarcci kāraṇam aṉṟu eṉṟum, avarkaḷiṭam paṭikkum naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷa carivara amiyāmiyē ataṟku mukkiya kāraṇamākum eṉṟum ārāyccikaḷiṉ mūlam kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar.
paṭikka vacatikaḷiṉmi:  nam nāṭu vaṟumiyil āḻn tuḷḷatu.  perumpāṉmiyāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ ēḻmi niliyil uḷḷaṉar. nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷiliruppiṉum nakarp puṟaṅkaḷi liruppiṉum, avarkaḷ vīṭṭil paṭikkum cūḻ niliyum illi; paṭikkum vacatikaḷum illi. nalla kāṟṟōṭṭamum veḷiccamum uḷḷa taṉi aṟikaḷ avar kaḷukkilli.  peṟṟōrkaḷum uṭaṉpiṟantōrkaḷum piṟarum cūḻa vīṭṭu aluvalkaḷuk kiṭiyil avarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam paṭippil kavaṉam celuttamuṭiyum?  appaṭip paṭittālum paṭippil maṉa orumippāṭu amiyumā?  celvantarkaḷ vīṭukaḷil vacatikaḷ iruppiṉum vāṉōliyiṉ iriccal, aṭikkaṭi pala aluvalkaḷ kuṟukkiṭutal, vīṭukaḷil uḷḷōr aṭikkaṭi paṭak kāṭcikaḷ kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup pōtal pōṉṟavi māṇākkariṉ paṭippukkup perun taṭikaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.
paṭikkum paḻakkaṅkaḷ:  orēvaḻi vacatikaḷ iruntu vīṭṭil avarkaḷ paṭittālum vīṭṭup paṭippu keṭṭa paṭippup paḻakkaṅkaḷi uṇṭupaṇṇi viṭukiṉṟatu.  iṉṟiya māṇākkarkaḷiṭam kuṟitta kālattil paṭikkum paḻakkam illi.  cilar maṉam pōṉavāṟu oru tiṭṭamiṉṟip paṭikkiṉṟaṉar.  tērvuk kālaṅkaḷil cilar tēnīr paruki naḷḷiravu vari paṭippatiyum, uṇavukku muṉṉum uṇavukkup piṉṉum paṭippatiyum, cilar tūkkattiliruntu eḻuntu naḷḷiravil paṭittu atikāliyil tūṅkuvatiyum, cilar paṭippit tiṭīreṉṟu niṟutti viṭṭu naṇparkaḷuṭaṉ paṭak kāṭcikaḷukkum kēḷikki kaḷukkum celvatiyum, paṭikkumpoḻutu peṟṟōrkaḷ pala vīṭṭu aluvalkaḷik kavaṉikkumpaṭi kūṟuvatiyum nām kāṇattāṉ ceykiṉṟōm.  kalvi eṉpatu palvēṟu tiṟaṉkaḷaṭaṅkiya oru cikkalāṉa ceyal eṉṟum, avvāṟu paṭikkun tiṟaṉ maṉitarukku maṉitar māṟupaṭum eṉṟum ārāyccimūlam kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar.  itupaṟṟiya oru cila karuttukkaḷ vāykuṭ paṭippippaṟṟik kūṟumpoḻutu kuṟippiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟi āṇṭuk kaṇṭu koḷka.
