āciriyariṉ āyattam: kati colluvataṟkum āciriyariṉ āyattam mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  kati collum āciriyar uṟcākamāvum, mukamalarcciyuṭaṉum, makiḻcciyuṭaṉum irukka vēṇṭum.  iṉṟēl, kuḻantikaḷukkut katikaḷi ūṭṭa muṭiyātu.  āciriyar tammi maṟantu kati coṉṉāltāṉ kuḻantikaḷ uṟcākamāka avaṟṟik kavaṉippar.  tāṉ āciriyar eṉṟa uṇarcciyi viṭṭu vīṭṭiluḷḷa pāṭṭiyiṉ niliyi liruntu koṇṭu parivuṭaṉum aṉpuṭaṉum katikaḷic collavēṇṭum.  collappōkum kati āciriyarukku naṉku terintirukka vēṇṭum.  katikaḷiṉ uṭkaruttukkaḷiyum amippuk kaḷiyum uṇarātu katikaḷic cuviyuṭaṉ colla iyalātu.  kaṟpaṉi āṟṟal, varuṇikkum tiṟaṉ poruntiya āciriyarkaḷtām ippaṇikku em muṟiyilum ēṟṟavarkaḷ varuṇaṉikaḷi viḷakkumpoḻutu avi uṇmi yāṉavi pōlat tōṉṟac ceyyavēṇṭum.  katiyiluḷḷa pala nikaḻccikaḷ, avi naṭakkum kaḷaṉ, aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki, avarkaḷuṭiya paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ mutaliya aṉittiyum āciriyar naṉku terintuvittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  vakuppukkum kuḻantikaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivukkum ēṟṟa katikaḷit tērnteṭuttu avaṟṟi eḷiya iṉiya naṭiyil colluvataṟkēṟṟavāṟu āyattam ceytukoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  cila camayam collum muṟiyil eḻutiyum paḻakikkoḷḷa vēṇṭivarum; tamakkut tāṉē vāyviṭṭuc colliyum paḻakikkoḷḷalām.  puttakat tiliruppatu pōl katikaḷi neṭṭuruc ceytu oppuvittalāl payaṉ irātu.  vēṇṭumāṉāl cila pakutikaḷi avaṟṟiṉ nayam koṭamāliruppataṟku neṭṭuruc ceytu koḷḷalām.  ellāvaṟṟiyum maṉappāṭam ceytu colvatāl kiḷippiḷḷi colvatupōl amintu kēṭpōrukku uṟcākam irātu.  cilacamayam ciṟiya katikaḷip perukkiyum, periya katikaḷic curukkiyum tēvik kēṟṟavāṟu amittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭiyirukkum.  avaciyamāṉa pakutikaḷi viḷakkak kūṭiya paṭaṅkaḷi muṉṉarē varintu vittukkoṇṭāl nalam; tēviyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil karumpalakiyil vaṇṇac cuṇṇak kāmpukaḷik koṇṭu virintu variyum āṟṟal peṟṟiruntāl mikavum nalam.
āciriyar katiccollum muṟi: kati colluvataṟku muṉ āciriyar kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ uriyāṭi kati kēṭpataṟku avarkaḷatu maṉattip pakkuvappaṭutta vēṇṭum.  avvāṟu ceyvatāl kuḻantikaḷ kati kēṭpataṟku āvalākavum uṟcākattuṭaṉum iruppar.  katiyip puttakaṅkaḷiliruntu appaṭiyē paṭippati viṭa colluvatutāṉ nallatu.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ mukattip pārttu uyirttaṉmiyuṭaṉum uṟcākattuṭaṉum vāy moḻiyākak katikaḷic coṉṉāl avi niṟinta payaṉiaḷikkum.  collumpoḻutu aṭik kaṭip puttakaṅkaḷip pārkkum paḻakkattit tavirkka vēṇṭum.  ovvoru katiyilum ciṟappākap payaṉpaṭuttavēṇṭiya coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum terintu kiyāḷa vēṇṭum.  ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil, iyalpāṉa kuralil, eṭuttum paṭuttum nalintum katippōkku amiyavēṇṭum; katiyiluḷḷa  nikaḻccikaḷ oru nāṭakampōl kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉakkaṇmuṉ tōṉṟumpaṭi collavēṇṭum.  cātāraṇak kāṭcikaḷi varuṇikkum poḻutu nitāṉamākac cellavēṇṭum.  arumiyāṉa kaṭṭaṅkaḷ, tuṇikarac ceyalkaḷ, tiṭukkiṭum nikaḻccikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟic collumpoḻutu mikunta uṟcākat tuṭaṉum apinayattuṭaṉum iṭattiṟkēṟṟa toṉiyuṭaṉum urikka vēṇṭum; kati collutal coṟpoḻivupōl irukkak kūṭātu.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil kuḻantikaḷ aṟinta moḻi naṭiyil katiyik kūṟutal avaciyam.  kati cellum poḻutē katiyiṉ nīti veḷippaṭavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, atit taṉiyāka veḷippaṭiyāka eṭuttukkāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttak kūṭātu.  nakiccuvikaḷik kūṟumpoḻutu māṇākkarkaṭku naṉku tuykkum vāyppukkaḷi aḷikkavēṇṭum; ataṟku āciriyarum nakiccuvikaḷit tuyppōrāka iruntāl mikavum nalam.  katikaḷic collak kēṭpōrkaḷiṭam pala katikaḷip paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvam eḻuppappeṟṟāl atuvē kati collum āciriyariṉ veṟṟiyākum.  
katikaḷiṉ vakikaḷ:  katikaḷil palavakikaḷ uḷa.  iyaṟkik katikaḷ, nakiccuviyuḷḷa vēṭikkik katikaḷ, mōkiṉik katikaḷ, varalāṟṟuk katikaḷ, purāṇak katikaḷ ākiya katikaḷ avaṟṟuḷ cilavaki.  avi ovvoṉṟip paṟṟiyum āciriyarkaḷ ōraḷavu aṟintiruttal avaciyam.
iyaṟkik katikaḷ:  ik katikaḷp perumpālum āciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟivar.  māṉiṭa vāḻkkiyit tavira, ēṉiya uyirp pirāṇikaḷiṉ vāḻkki varalāṟukaḷip paṟṟiyum ōraḷavu naṉku aṟivataṟku avi tuṇipuriyum.  paṭcikaḷ,  mirukaṅkaḷ, tāvaraṅkaḷ ākiyavi pēcuvaṉapōla ikkatikaḷ amintirukkum.  īcōppuk katikaḷ, pañca tantirak katikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi ippiriviṉuḷ aṭakkalām.  cātāraṇa maṉitaṉ palavakiyāṉa vāḻkkikaḷit tuykka vāyppukaḷ illi; paṭṭaṟivillāta vāḻkkiyil paṭṭaṟivu eytuvatu kaṭiṉam.  appaṭṭaṟivi, naṉku eḻutappeṟṟa ikkatikaḷiṉ mūlam peṟalām.  katikaḷi varuṇikkumpoḻutu kāṭcikaḷ appaṭiyē  maṉakkaṇmuṉ naṉku tōṉṟa vēṇṭum.  aṉṭrākilsum ciṅkamum eṉṟa katiyik kuḻantikaḷ paṭikkumpoḻutu ciṅkattiṉ naṉṟiyaṟivi aṟivar.  ak katikaḷ palvēṟu uyirppirāṇikaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil karuṇi koḷḷavum, avaṟṟiṉ vāḻkki niliyi uṇaravum vāyppukkaḷi nalkik kuḻantikaḷiṭam ōr uyarnta paṇ pāṭṭiyum vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa.
nakiccuvik katikaḷ:  it talippil palavakiyāṉa vēṭikkik katikaḷic cērkkalām.  avaṟṟip paṭikkum poḻutu kuḻantikaḷ uṟcākam aṭivar; maṉattiṟku makiḻcci yūṭṭavum ullāca maṉappāṉmiyi nalkavum avi peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  cirippu maṉita iṉattiṟku maṭṭilum ēṟpaṭṭi rukkum ōr aṟputappaṇpu; nintaṉiyum vikāramumaṟṟa cirippu uṭalukku nallatu.  mākṭukāl karuttuppaṭi nām cirikkā viṭṭāl oru kuṟippiṭṭa nikaḻcci nammi alikkaḻittu viṭum.  oru camayam ciṉattiṟkum, maṟṟoru camayam karuṇikkum cirippu muṟivāka utavukiṉṟatu.  nakiccuvik katikaḷ kuḻantikaḷukkuc cirippi uṇṭākki vāḻkkiyiṉ tarātaraṅkaḷi uṇarvataṟkut tuṇiyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.  piratāpa mutaliyār carittiram pōṉṟa katikaḷil varum cila kati māntarkaḷukku nēriṭum nikaḻccikaḷi iṉṟu niṉittālum nām vayiṟu kuluṅkac cirikkiṉṟōm; teṉṉālirāmaṉ katikaḷil varum pala nikaḻccikaḷ avaṟṟi niṉikkum poḻutē nammi makiḻvaṭiyacceykiṉṟaṉa.  nakiccuvik katikaḷiṉ mukkiya nōkkam vēṭikkiyē. avaṟṟiluḷḷa nītip paṇpu iraṇṭām paṭiyē.   makiḻnaṉ eḻutiyuḷḷa kaṭalum kaṭaliyum, yatārttavāti lōka virōti, vaṇṇārap periyāṇṭi pōṉṟa kaṭṭurikaḷiluḷḷa nakiccuvi ilakkiyamaṇam kamaḻac ceytu avaṟṟi ōr uyarnta ilakkiya nilikku uyartti viṭukiṉṟatu.  nakiccuvi iṉṟēl vāḻvu cuvikkātu eṉṟu nāṭṭiṉ tanti kāntiyaṭikaḷē ōriṭattil colli yirukkiṉṟār.  nakimukam koṇṭu vāḻum vāḻvē nākarika vāḻvu.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil nakittu niṟkiṉṟa niliyē aṭippaṭiyāka amiyumāṟu avarkaḷik paḻakkutal vēṇṭum.  nakiccuvik katikaḷ itaṟkut tuṇiyāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
mōkiṉik katikaḷ:  ivi kuḻantikaḷiṭam ilakkiyap paṇpāṭṭi vaḷarkkum pērāṟṟal vāyntavi.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ uḷḷak kiḷarccikku ivi mukkiyamāṉavi.  kuḻantikaḷip pāvaṉi ulakiṟkuk koṇṭu celvataṟku ivi tuṇiyāka uḷḷaṉa.  ettaṉiyō kuḻantikaḷiṉ uḷḷaṅkaḷik kaḷippittuk koṇṭu iruppatāl iṉṟu ulakiṭiyē pala moḻikaḷilum ittakiya mōkiṉik katikaḷ cāvā ilakkiyaṅkaḷāka, kuḻantikaḷiṉ paramparic cottukkaḷāka, iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ik katikaḷik kuḻanti ulakam ārvattuṭaṉ varavēṟkiṉṟatu.  kaṟpaṉikaḷiṉ mūlam uṇarttappeṟum pala uṇmikaḷik kuḻantikaḷ ikkatikaḷiṉ vāyilāka uṇarkiṉṟaṉar.  vāḻkkiyiṉ paluṇmikaḷ, aṉṟāṭa vāḻvil nēriṭum aṉupavaṅkaḷiṉ potut taṉmikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kuḻantikaḷ aṟikiṉṟaṉar.  mōkiṉik katikḷip paḷḷi vāḻviṉ toṭakka niliyil collāviṭṭāl, kuḻantikaḷ tam piṟkāla vāḻvil mutirnta ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷic cariyāka matippiṭum āṟṟalip peṟamuṭiyātu eṉṟu kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  toṭakka niliyil ittakiya viyappūṭṭum katikaḷil maṉattip paṟikoṭuttavarkaḷē piṟkālattil uyarnta pala ilakkiyaṅ kaḷip paṭikkum āṟṟalkaḷip peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatip pala aṟiñarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki varalāṟu kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.  caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum kampaṉ pōṉṟa ilakkiya mētikaḷiṉ paṭippukkaḷiyum paṭittu uṇarāta tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki vaṟaṇṭa taṉmi yuṭiyatu. atupōlavē mōkiṉik katikaḷiṉ taṉmiyi uṇarāta kuḻantiyiṉ vāḻvu vaṟaṇṭa niliyi yuṭiyatu.  nāṭṭuk katikaḷilum mōkiṉik katikaḷilum kāṇappaṭum viṟuviṟuppu, iṉpapperukku, kapaṭamaṟṟa taṉmi, ilakkiyaccuvi ākiya paṇpukaḷ piṟkālattil amiya vēṇṭiya ilakkiya vāḻviṟku aṭippaṭiyāka uḷḷaṉa.  eṉavē, ik katikaḷi oruvita kaṭṭuppāṭiṉṟip payaṉ paṭuttalām.
varalāṟṟuk katikaḷ;  ivi nam mūtātiyariṉ vāḻkkiyip paṟṟiyavi.  oru ciṟanta varalāṟṟukkati paṇṭiya nikaḻccikaḷukku uyir koṭuttu vaṭivam amikkiṉṟatu.  antanta nāṭṭuk katikaḷip poṟutta maṭṭilum itu muṟṟilum uṇmiyākum.  oru nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ oṟṟumiyi valiyuṟutti makkaḷiṭam nāṭṭuppaṟṟi vaḷarppataṟku ik katikaḷ peruntuṇi purikiṉṟaṉa.  ovvoru nāṭṭu varalāṟṟilum taliciṟanta pala periyārkaḷ iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.  avar kaḷiṭam pakti koḷvatu iḷañciṟārkaḷiṉ iyalpākum.  ceyaṟkaruñceyalkaḷip purintavarkaḷ, muṭivāṉa tīrmāṉam uḷḷavarkaḷ poṟumiyiyē aṇikalaṉākak koṇṭavarkaḷ ākiyōr ulaka varalāṟṟil palar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.  avarkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷ ovvoṉṟum ovvoru katiyākum.  tamiḻnāṭṭilirunta caṅkakāla vaḷḷalkaḷ, puravalarkaḷ, perum pulavarkaḷ ākiyōriṉ varalāṟukaḷ iḷam uḷḷaṅkaḷik koḷḷi koḷpavi.  paṇṭiya vāḻvi iṉṟiya vāḻvuṭaṉ iṇittuk kāṭṭuvataṟku avvaralāṟṟuk katikaḷ peruntuṇi  puripavi.  mullikkut tērum, mayilukkup pōrviyum īntu paṇṭiya vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ vāḻviyum, tām kuṭiyirunta vīṭṭiyē kāṅkira aluvalakattiṟkum makaḷirk kallūrikkum īnta ikkāla vaḷḷalkaḷiṉ2 vāḻviyum iṇittuk kāṭṭumpoḻutu māṇākkarkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ uṇmikaḷiyum, koṭi maṭattiyum ōraḷavu teḷivāka uṇarvar.  civājiyiṉ tuṇikarac ceyalkaḷiyum kaṭṭa pommaṉiṉ vīrac ceyalkaḷiyum muṟiyē vināyaka tāmōtara cāvarkkāriṉ tuṇikarac ceyalkaḷōṭum nētājiyiṉ vīrac ceyalkaḷōṭum oppiṭṭuk kaṟkumpoḻutu uṇmiyilēyē avi iḷam māṇākkarkaḷiṉ uḷḷaṅkaḷil nāṭṭup paṟṟuk kaṉalit tōṟṟuvikkum.  ivaṟṟiṉ mūlam nāṭṭup paṇiyil īṭupaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvamum eḻum.
purāṇak katikaḷ:  ivi nam nāṭṭiṉ cintaṉik karuvūlaṅkaḷ, vaḷarntavarkaṭkum vāḻkkiyil īṭupaṭṭavar kaṭkum vaḻikāṭṭikaḷāka utavupavi.  nam paṇṭip periyārkaḷ ettaṉiyō uṇmikaḷik katikaḷiṉ mūlam viḷakkiyuḷḷaṉar.  ikkāla māṇākkarkaḷukku avaṟṟik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu aṟiviyal maṉappāṉmiyil katiyiṉ pōkki ārāya vēṇṭum.  katikaḷil potintuḷḷa uṇmip poruḷkaḷit teḷivāka eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉāl kaṭṭāyam avikaḷi māṇākkarkaḷ virumpuvar.  irāmāyaṇam, makāpāratam, pākavatam pōṉṟa itikācaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟum nikaḻccikaḷum, aṅkuḷḷa itikāca māntarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷum māṉiṭa vāḻkkiyi vaḷamuṟa amittuk koṇṭu vāḻa eṉṟum iṇiyaṟṟa vaḻikāṭṭikaḷāka iruppavi.  irāmaṉ, aric cantiraṉ, naḷaṉ, tarumaṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ etta ṉiyō aṟiñarkaḷiṉ vāḻvic cemmipaṭutti yuḷḷaṉa.
katikaḷiṉ paṇṭip perumi: paṇṭiruntē katikaḷ māṉiṭa vāḻkkiyil oru ciṟanta iṭattippeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ellāp paruvattilum katikaḷ maṉitaṉiṉ itayattik koḷḷi koḷḷum taṉmiyip peṟṟiruntuḷḷaṉa.  namatu nāṭṭil kati colluvatu oru kaliyākavē matikkappeṟṟu vantiruk kiṉṟatu.  ciṟukuḻantiyāka iruntapoḻutu civāji aṉṉiyiṉ maṭiyiliruntu koṇṭē pala katikaḷik kēṭṭatāka varalāṟṟu mūlam aṟikiṉṟōm.  tiruviḻāk kālaṅkaḷilum piṟa camayaṅkaḷilum kōyilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum purāṇac coṟ poḻivukaḷ ceyyap peṟṟu varuvati iṉṟum kāṇalām.  katā kālaṭ cēpaṅkaḷ ceyvatu iṉṟu oru ciṟanta kaliyākap pōṟṟap paṭukiṉṟatu*.  pāmara makkaḷukku uyarnta camaya uṇmikaḷiyum, vāḻkkit tattuvaṅkaḷiyum kaṟpippataṟku ik katikaḷ peritum utavukiṉṟaṉa.  ettaṉiyō pēr ik katikaḷik kēṭṭu uḷḷatteḷivu aṭintuḷḷaṉar; aṭintum varukiṉṟaṉar.  itikācattilum palar ivvāṟu teḷivu peṟṟatik kāṇkiṉṟōm.  irāmāyaṇattil kucaṉum lavaṉum vālmīka riṭamiruntu irāma ilakkumaṇar pala katikaḷik kēṭṭatiyum, pāṇṭavarkaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭu vaṉattil vāḻnta poḻutu pala muṉivarkaḷ avarkaṭkup pala uṇmikaḷik katikaḷiṉ mūlam uṇarttiyatiyum nām aṟikiṉṟōm.  eṉavē, makkaḷukku iyalpāka uḷḷa kati ārvatti ciṟu kuḻantikaḷiṉ moḻip payiṟciyil tiruppiviṭṭāl niṟinta payaṉi aṭiyalām eṉpati moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ aṟiya vēṇṭum.


4. naṭippu
naṭippatiyum vāymoḻip payiṟcikku naṉku payaṉ paṭuttalām.  kuḻantikaḷukku naṭippatil āval atikam.  naṭittal avarkaḷukku iyaṟkiyāka amintuḷḷatu.  naṭippiṟ kēṟṟa ciṟu pāṭalkaḷiyum katikaḷiyum tērnteṭuttu naṭikkak kaṟpikkalām.  kuḻantikaḷ naṭikka vēṇṭiya pakutiyiṉ karuttiyuṇarntu naṭittāl nalla payaṉ viḷiyum.  eṉavē, ciṟu kuḻantikaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟiviṟku appāṟpaṭṭavaṟṟi naṭikkum pakutikaḷākat tērnteṭuttalkūṭātu.  ciṟu nikaḻccikaḷ, toḻilkaḷ, pirāṇikaḷ, pirayāṇaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi naṭikkac ceyyalām.  aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭa pāṭalit toṭakkanilip paḷḷiyil iraṇṭām vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷikkoṇṭu naṭikkac ceyyalām.  inta āṭṭatti mutalil āciriyar māṇākkarkaḷukku viḷakkiyapiṉ āṭṭattit toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.  mutalil vakuppiluḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷi āṟu ciṟu kuḻukkaḷākap pirittukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  āṟu kuḻukkaḷukkum talivaṉāka oruvaṉit tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.
ellōrum:	muttu ceyta peṭṭi itutāṉ,  itu tāṉ.
talivaṉ: 	inta roṭṭi teriyumā?
mutaṟkuḻu:	muttu ceyta peṭṭiyil vitta nalla roṭṭiyum itutāṉ, itutāṉ.
talivaṉ: 	inta eli teriyumā?
iraṇṭām kuḻu:	muttu ceyta peṭṭiyil vitta nalla roṭṭiyit tiṉam tiṉṟa eliyum itu tāṉ, itutāṉ.
talivaṉ:	intap pūṉi teriyumā?
mūṉṟām kuḻu:	muttu ceyta peṭṭiyil vitta nalla roṭṭiyit tiṉam tiṉṟa eliyik koṉṟu tiṉṟa pūṉiyum itutāṉ, itutāṉ
talivaṉ:	inta nāy teriyumā?
nāṉkām kuḻu: 	muttu ceyta peṭṭiyil vitta nalla roṭṭiyit 	tiṉam tiṉṟa eliyik koṉṟu tiṉṟa pūṉiyik kulittiṭṭa nāyum itutāṉ, itutāṉ.
talivaṉ:	intap pacu teriyumā?
aintām kuḻu:	muttu ceyta peṭṭiyil vitta nalla  roṭṭiyit 	tiṉam tiṉṟa eliyik koṉṟu tiṉṟa pūṉiyik kulittiṭṭa nāyi utittiṭṭa pacuvum itutāṉ, itutāṉ
talivaṉ: 	intak kōṉār teriyumā?
āṟām kuḻu:	muttu ceyta peṭṭiyil vitta nalla roṭṭiyit 	tiṉam tiṉṟa eliyik koṉṟutiṉṟa pūṉiyik kulittiṭṭa nāyi utittiṭṭa pacuvik kaṟantiṭṭa kōṉār ivartāṉ, ivartāṉ.
inta āṭṭattiṟkup peṭṭi, eli, pūṉi, nāy, pacu, kōṉār ākiya paṭaṅkaḷ vēṇṭum.  inta āṭṭattiyē āciriyar kaṟpaṉikku ēṟṟavāṟu vevvēṟu vitamāka naṭattik kāṭṭalām.  itaṉāl kuḻantikaḷukkut toṭarcciyāka oru poruḷik kuṟittup pēcum paḻakkam kivarappeṟum.
ivvita āṭṭaṅkaḷi naṭikkumpoḻutu pāṭalkaḷiyum takunta apinayattuṭaṉ pāṭalām.  iḷañciṟārkaḷukkuc ciṟunāṭakaṅkaḷum naṭippataṟku ēṟṟavi.  naṭippatil toṭakkattil nalla tērcciyi etirpārkka muṭiyātu.  nāḷaṭivil paṭippaṭiyāka nalla tērcciyi etirpārkkalām.  mutalil ciṟukatikaḷi-nariyum kākkiyum pōṉṟavi-naṭippataṉ mūlam māṇākkarkaḷukkup payiṟci taralām.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil naṭippu maṭṭum mukkiyamaṉṟu.  iyaṟkiyāṉa colvaṉmiyil nalla vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭuvatē mukkiyamākum.  naṭikkumpoḻutu uṇarcci tatumpa naṭikkumpaṭi payiṟci tara vēṇṭum.
mēl vakuppukkaḷilum naṭippu vāymoḻip payiṟcikku nalla vāyppiṉi nalkukiṉṟatu.  iṅku muṉ-āyattamillā naṭippu, payiṟci peṟṟapiṉ naṭippu eṉa iruvakiyāka naṭippik kiyāḷalām.  ivipaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷi naṭippu muṟi eṉṟa talippil piṟitōriṭattil kāṇka.  iḷam māṇākkarkaḷ katikaḷi nāṭakamāka naṭittal avarkaḷukku atika makiḻcciyi viḷivikkum.  avaṟṟi naṭikkum poḻutu avarkaḷiṉ uṇarccikaḷ koppuḷittu niṟkum.  putiya coṟkaḷiyum. coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum tāmākavē amittup pēcum vāyppukkaḷ avarkaṭkuk kiṭṭum.  naṭikkumpoḻutu avarkaḷ muḻu maṉa āṟṟalip payaṉpaṭuttik kuralmāṟṟam, kuraliṉ ēṟṟat tāḻvukaḷ, iṭattiṟ kēṟṟa cikikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi mēṟkoḷvar.  avvāṟu ceyvatil āciriyar avvap poḻutu avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya utaviyic ceyyalām.
ellāk katikaḷum naṭikkum muṟiyil irā naṭittaṟ kuriya nikaḻccikaḷik koṇṭa katikaḷi āciriyar iraṇṭu mūṉṟu kāṭcikaḷāka vakuttu nāṭaka muṟiyil amittuk koṭuttu avaṟṟi naṭikkumpaṭi ceyyalām.  iṭattiṟ kēṟṟārpōl oru cila pāṭṭukkaḷiyum iṭi iṭiyē cērttuk koḷḷalām.  kuḻantikaḷiṉ viruppappaṭi naṭikkap peṟa vēṇṭiya pakutikaḷip pirittukkoṭukka vēṇṭum.  in naṭippukaḷil atika aḻakāṉa āṭikaḷiyum oppaṉi kaḷiyum etirpārttalākātu.  piḷḷi kaḷiṉ maṉappōkki yoṭṭi naṭikkac ceyvatē kīḻvakuppukkaḷil mukkiya māṉatu.  ciṟu kuḻantikaḷi eḷitākap pāvaṉi ulakattiṟkuk (Make-believe world) kūṭṭic cella muṭiyum.  avarkaḷum etiyum kaṟpaṉiyil niṟivu ceytu koḷvar.  paḷḷi āṇṭu viḻā, peṟṟōr nāḷ pōṉṟa ciṟappāṉa nāṭkaḷil maṭṭum uriya uṭikaḷuṭaṉum oppaṉikaḷuṭaṉum naṭikkac ceytāl pōtumāṉatu.


5. paṭaṅkaḷ
vāymoḻip payiṟciyi aḷippataṟkup paṭaṅkaḷ peritum tuṇiyāka uḷḷaṉa.  paṭaṅkaḷip pārttu makiḻvatil iḷañciṟārkaḷukku ārvam atikam.  ellā niliyilum paṭaṅkaḷ moḻi kaṟpittalukkup payaṉpaṭum.  paṭaṅkaḷi uṟṟu nōkkuvataṉ mūlam kūrntu nōkkuntiṟaṉ, kaṟpaṉi āṟṟal, ōviyakkaliyil ārvam, aḻakuṇarcci ākiyavi ēṟpaṭum.  paṭaṅkaḷil kāṇum ceytikaḷ maṉattil naṉku patiyum.  iḷañ ciṟārkaḷiṉ maṉam ellāc ceytikaḷiyum coṟkaḷiṉ mūlam ēṟṟuk koḷvatil naṉku payiṟci peṟṟirukkātu.  coṟkaḷik koṇṭē kaṟpaṉiyi vaḷarttup poruḷkaḷip purintu koḷḷutal kaṭiṉam.  vēṇṭumāṉāl oru cilar purintu koḷḷakkūṭum.  palar kaṭṭāyam purintu koḷḷa muṭiyātu.  coṟkaḷik koṇṭu coṟkaḷi viḷakkuvati viṭa accoṟkaḷ kuṟikkum paṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu avaṟṟi viḷakkiṉāl kuḻantikaḷ nalla viḷakkam peṟuvar.  oru cilar paṭaṅkaḷik kāṭṭuvativiṭa ap paṭaṅkaḷ kuṟikkum poruḷ kaḷiyē kāṭṭiviṭalāmē eṉṟu kūṟalām.  uṇmi tāṉ; āṉāl ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum vakuppaṟikkuk koṇṭu varutal eṉpatu iyalāta ceyal ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum koṇṭu varavum tēviyilli.  perumpāṉmiyāṉa kuḻantikaḷ aṭikkaṭi pārkkātavaṟṟip pārppataṟkuk kuḻantikaḷi apporuḷkaḷ uḷḷa iṭattiṟku aḻittuc ceṉṟu viḷakkalām.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka oru pukivaṇṭi poṟiyi (Engine) ivvāṟu viḷakkalām.  āṉāl, oru kuṭiciyi viḷakka māṇākkarkaḷi veḷiyē kūṭṭic cella vēṇṭiyatilli.  kuṭicip paṭam itaṟkup pōtumāṉatu.  māṇāk karkaḷ tām aṭikkaṭi pārtta kuṭiciyippaṟṟip paṭattik koṇṭē nalla viḷakkam peṟamuṭiyum.
payaṉ paṭuttu muṟi:  paṭaṅkaḷi ivvāṟu payaṉ paṭuttalām; paṭaṅkaḷil kāṇum ceytikaḷippaṟṟik kuḻantikaḷi viṉavat tūṇṭalām.  paṭaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam katikaḷi viḷakkalām.  paṭaṅkaḷi varuṇittup pēcalām.  paṭaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam paṭippu kaṟpikkalām; paṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu cila pāṭaṅkaḷiyum viḷakkakkūṭum.  paṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum peyarkaḷik kaṟpittu kuḻantikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷañciyattip perukkalām.  ivvāṟu kaṟpittalil paṭaṅkaḷ ettaṉiyō kūṟukaḷil tuṇiyāka uḷḷaṉa.
paṭaṅkaḷippaṟṟi uriyāṭalām.  māṇākkarkaḷ paṭattip pārttup paṭikkum poḻutu mutalil coṟ kaḷākavum piṟaku coṟṟoṭarkaḷākavum kaṭiciyil paṭatti luḷḷa ceytikaḷi oru katiyākavum-im mātiri paṭip paṭiyākac collap paḻakkalām.  kati kēṭpatupōla paṭaṅ kaḷik kāṇpatilum kuḻantikaḷukku viruppam uṇṭu; ākiyāl accam, kūccam ākiyavaṟṟi nīkkip pēcap paḻakuvataṟkup paṭaṅkaḷ naṉku payaṉpaṭum.  mutal iraṇṭu vakuppukkaḷukku im mātiri yāṉa payiṟcikaḷit taralām.
palavakip paṭaṅkaḷ:  ikkālattil vaṇṇan tīṭṭap peṟṟa paṭaṅkaḷum tīṭṭappeṟāta paṭaṅkaḷum ērāḷamāka acciṭap peṟṟu malivākak kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.  avaṟṟit tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ naṉku payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.
pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷip paṭikkat toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉ ciṟu kuḻantikaḷuk keṉṟē āyattam ceytu veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷa pāṭapputtaṅkaḷi vāṅkik koṭuttu avikaḷip pārttu makiḻac ceytāl iḷañciṟārkaḷukkup paṭippil cuvi uṇṭākum.  ip paṭap puttakaṅkaḷ pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭiyāka amiyum.  paṭaṅkaḷum coṟkaḷum acciṭṭa aṭṭikaḷ putumuṟippaṭi paṭippu kaṟpippatil peritum payaṉ paṭukiṉṟaṉa.  iṉṟu ampikā vācakam pōṉṟa pāṭap puttataṅkaḷil aḻakāṉa vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷ cērkkap peṟṟuḷ ḷaṉa*.  attakiya paṭaṅkaḷip pārttuk koṇṭiruppatu vīṇ poḻutupōkku eṉṟu āciriyarkaḷ cilarum peṟṟōrkaḷ palarum eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.  atu tavaṟāṉa karuttākum.  ap paṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟi uriyāṭip pāṭattiṟku aṭippaṭiyāka avikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttalām.  pāṭattil varum kaṭiṉa coṟkaḷiyum ceytikaḷiyum viḷakka avi payaṉpaṭum.  kaṇṇiṉāl kāṇum paṭaṅkaḷ karuttukkaḷi maṉattil āḻap patikkat tuṇiceyyum.  periya iyaṟkip poruṭpaṭaṅkaḷ, katip paṭaṅkaḷ, toḻilmuṟi viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavi taṉiyāka acciṭappeṟṟu vilikkuk kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.  avi kaḷi moḻik kaṭṭurip pāṭaṅkaḷil payaṉ paṭuttalām.  pukivaṇṭi niliyam, paṭakkāṭcik koṭṭaki kaḷ, kaṭivīti ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟum viḷamparap paṭaṅkaḷik kavaṉikkavum, appaṭaṅkaḷil acciṭṭuḷḷaṉavaṟṟip paṭittu varavum kuḻantikaḷit tūṇṭalām.  ceytit tāḷkaḷilum itara veḷiyīṭukaḷilum kāṇappeṟum paṭaṅkaḷik kattarittu paṭattokuppuppoṭṭikaḷil (Picture album) cēkarittu vittiruntāl avaṟṟi moḻi kaṟpittalil payaṉpaṭuttalām.  ivaṟṟit tavira purāṇak katikaḷi viḷakkavum, teyva paktiyi ūṭṭavum, aḻakuṇarccikaḷi eḻuppavumvalla iravivarmā paṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa ōviyac ciṟappu vāynta paṭaṅkaḷ paḷḷiyil irukka vēṇṭiyatu mikavum iṉṟiyami yātatu.  mēṟkūṟiya palavakip paṭaṅkaḷiyum vakuppaṟikaḷil cuvaril toṅka viṭṭu vikkalām.  avikaḷi atika uyarattil irāmal kuḻantikaḷ pārttup paṭikkakkūṭiya uyarattil toṅka viṭavēṇṭum.  vārattukku vāram avi iṭam māṟit toṅkaviṭappeṟṟāl kuḻantikaḷ avaṟṟik kūrntu nōkka vāyppukkaḷ uṇṭākum.


6. ciṟu coṟpoḻivukaḷ
toṭakka nilip paḷḷikaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ avar kaḷaṟinta poruḷkaḷippaṟṟi naṉṟākavum, teḷivākavum uriyāṭalil āṟṟal kivarappeṟṟiruppatāl avarkaḷ naṭunilippaḷḷi vakuppukkaḷukku varumpoḻutu avarkaḷaṟinta poruḷil toṭarcciyākap pēcuvatil payiṟcikaḷ taralām.  kālṭuvel kuk eṉpavar ivvāṟu toṭarntu pēcuvatic ciṟu coṟpoḻivukaḷ (Littleman lectures) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟār.3 ippēccukkaḷ toṭakka niliyil oruvitak kaṭṭup pāṭiṉṟi irukkalām; nāḷaṭivil paṭippaṭiyākak kaṭṭup pāṭāṉa nilikku varavēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ virumpum poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi, ciṟu coṟpoḻivukaḷ ceyvataṟku āyattam ceytu varumāṟu colli, kūṭṭattiṟku oru māṇākkat talivaṉit tērnteṭuttu ikkūṭṭatti naṭatta vēṇṭum.  coṟ poḻivu muṭintatum coṟpoḻivik kēṭṭuk koṇṭirunta māṇākkarkaḷ pēcappaṭṭa poruḷippaṟṟi viṉākkaḷ viṭukkalām; vēṟu vitamākavum pēccit tiṟaṉāyalām.  potuvākap pēcappeṟṟa poruḷik kuṟittuk kalantu āytal naṭipeṟum.  coṟpoḻivāḷar taṉ pēccip paṭaṅkaḷālum piṟa tuṇikkaruvikaḷālum viḷakkalām.  vēṇṭu māṉāl orucila vakupput tōḻarkaḷit tuṇiyākak koṇṭu mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷiliruntu mēṟkōḷkaḷik kāṭṭalām.
ittakiya coṟpoḻivukaḷ mika nīḷamāka amiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāyam illi.  iṉṉa poruḷip paṟṟittāṉ pēca vēṇṭum eṉṟa kaṭṭāyamum iṅkuk kūṭātu.  mēl vakuppukkaḷukkup pōkappōka ic coṟpoḻivukaḷum ciṟitu ciṟitāka nīḷalām.  ittakiya poḻivukaḷāl māṇākkarkaḷiṉ poruḷaṟivu vaḷarcci peṟum.  kūṭṭatti naṭattum poṟuppukkaḷ yāvum māṇākkarkaḷiṭamē viṭappeṟutal vēṇṭum.  kuṭimip payiṟcit tiṭṭam paḷḷi naṭimuṟiyil irukkum ikkālattil ik kūṭṭaṅkaḷi mikat tiṟamiyāka naṭipeṟumpaṭi ceyyalām; ceyyavum vāyppu uṇṭu.
kūṭṭam naṭipeṟumpoḻutu āciriyar aṭikkaṭi atil taliyiṭak kūṭātu.  avarum oru pārviyāḷar niliyil tāṉ irukkavēṇṭum.  pēccil kāṇum poruṭkuṟi, coṟpōkkil kāṇum kuṟi mutaliyavaṟṟik kavaṉittuk kuṟippukkaḷ eṭukkavēṇṭum.  āciriyar ivvāṟu ceyvati māṇākkarkaḷ aṟiyakkūṭātu.  evvitattilum māṇākkarkaḷ pēcumpoḻutu tām tēṟum muṟiyil kavaṉikkap peṟuvatāka eṇṇam kūṭātu: itaṟku āciriyar vāyppē tarakkūṭātu.  coṟpoḻiviṉ iṟutiyil piṟa māṇākkarkaḷippōlavē āciriyarum pēcappaṭṭa poruḷik kuṟittu viṉākkaḷ viṭukkalām.  toṭakkattil oru pāṭavēḷiyil (Period) oru poruḷik kuṟittutāṉ coṟpoḻivu naṭipeṟum; eñcum nēram viṉākkaḷ viṭuppatilēyē kaḻintuviṭum.  āṉāl pāṭavēḷi muṭikiṉṟatē eṉṟu viṉākkaḷ viṭuppatik kuṟittuk koḷḷakkūṭātu.  eṉavē, coṟpoḻivukkuriya pāṭavēḷi orunāḷiṉ iṟutip pāṭavēḷiyāka amittuk koḷḷap peṟṟāl coṟpoḻivit toṭarntu naṭattavum vacatiyāka irukkum.
intap pāṭavēḷiyil coṟpoḻivukkup patilākap puttaka matippurikaḷiyum (Reviews) ceyyumpaṭi ēṟpāṭu ceyyalām.  oru māṇākkaṉ tāṉ paṭitta oru puttakattip paṟṟi vakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku eṭuttuk kūṟalām.  ippoḻutu puttakam pēccupporuḷāka amikiṉṟatu.  puttakattiṉ matippuri tarum māṇākkaṉ tāṉ apputtakatti virumpu vataṟkum allatu virumpātataṟkum uriya kāraṇaṅkaḷi eṭuttu viḷakkavēṇṭum.  apputtakattiliruntu cila aḻakāṉa pakutikaḷip paṭittum kāṭṭalām.   apputtakattippaṟṟi viṉavappeṟum viṉākkaḷukku viṭiyiṟukka avaṉ tayārāka irukkavēṇṭum.  puttakattiṟku matippuri taruvati aintām vakuppiliruntē  āciriyar toṭaṅkalām.  toṭakkattil puttakattil paṭitta oru katiyik kūṟumāṟu ivvēliyit toṭaṅkalām. paṭippaṭiyāka itaṉ taratti uyarttik koṇṭē pōkavēṇṭum.


7. kalantu āytal
kalantu  āytaliyum (Discussion) vāymoḻip payiṟcikku naṉku payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām ceyaltiṭṭa muṟiyil itaṟkup palavāyppukkaḷ kiṭikkum.  tavira, ētāvatu oru cuṟṟulāviṟku (Excursion) āyattam ceyyum poḻutu cella vēṇṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ, āṅkāṅku taṅkum vacatikaḷ, uṇavu muṟikaḷ, pirayāṇavacatikaḷ, pala iṭaṅkaḷilum pārkka vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟippaṟṟi ārāyntu teḷiyalām.  paḷḷi āṇṭu viḻā, viḷiyāṭṭup pōṭṭi, ilakkiyak kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻā, periyōrkaḷiṉ tiru nāṭkaḷik koṇṭāṭutal ākiyavaṟṟi evvāṟu ciṟappuṟa naṭattalām eṉpatip paṟṟi  ārāyalām.  vakuppuk kiyeḻuttu māta itaḻ, paḷḷi āṇṭumalar ākiyavaṟṟi evvāṟu veḷiyiṭuvatu eṉpatu paṟṟi ārāyalām.  ivaṟṟit tavira vakuppilēyē kalantu āytalukkup pala vāyppukkaḷ kiṭikkum.  ētāvatu oru periya pāṭappakutiyik kaṟpittu muṭintatum māṇākkarkaḷ cariyāka viḷakkam peṟāta iṭaṅkaḷi viḷakku māṟu āciriyari viṉavalām.  ippoḻutu āciriyar viḷakkam peṟṟa orucila māṇākkarkaḷi avaṟṟiṟku viḷakkam tarumāṟu ēvalām.  avaṟṟiṟkup putiya karuttukkaḷit taramuṭintāl tarumāṟum tūṇṭalām. ellā pāṭaṅkaḷilum (Subjects) ivvita vāymoḻip payiṟcikku iṭam uṇṭu.  piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkum poḻutum, moḻiyuṇarcciyuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum.  paḷḷikkē potuvāka uḷḷa cila piracciṉi kaḷik kuḻu āyvāka (Panel discussion) naṭattalām.  kuḻu āyvu eṉpatu oru kuḻu ārāyum ceytikaḷip palar aṟiyumāṟu naṭattuvatākum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, paḷḷi ilakkiyak kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻā naṭattuvatu paṟṟi ivvāṟu ārāyappeṟum; paḷḷi māṇākkariṉ amiccaravi (School cabinet) paḷḷip potu maṇṭapa mēṭiyil vantu kūṭum.  paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷ yāvarum vakuppu variciyil pārviyāḷarāka vantu maṇṭapattil kūṭuvar.  paḷḷi āciriyarkaḷ oru puṟattil amarntiruppar.  amiccaravi poruḷi eṭuttu ārāyum; avarkaḷ ārāycciyi ellōrum kēṭpar.  pārviyāḷarkaḷukku ārāycciyil kalantukoḷḷum urimi illi.  tām colla vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi yellām muṉṉarē vakuppu māṇākkat talivaṉ mūlāka amiccaravikkut terivittu viṭa vēṇṭum.  āṇṭu viḻāvi naṭattuvatil potu maṇṭapatti aṇi ceytal, irukki amippu, naṭikka vēṇṭiya nāṭakam, paricaḷippu, aḻippitaḻ acciṭal, aḻippitaḻkaḷi veḷiyārkaḷukku aṉupputal, āṇṭu aṟikki tayārittal pōṉṟa ceytikaḷ ovvoṉṟum virivāka ārāyappeṟum ovvoru amiccarukkum viḻāvēli pirittuk koṭukkap peṟum.  ovvoru vakuppilumuḷḷa māṇākkar kuḻukkaḷiṉ ottuḻippāl ovvoru amiccarum tam kaṭamiyit tiṟamiyāka niṟivēṟṟuvar.  immātiriyākap palarum aṟiyum paṭi ceytikaḷi ārāyvatāl ellōrum āṇṭu viḻāvik kuṟittuc cintikka vāyppu kiṭikkiṉṟatu; ārāyum muṟi yiyum ellōrum aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.
8. coṟpōr
uyarnilip paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷukkum kallūri māṇākkar kaḷukkum vāymoḻip payiṟci peṟuvataṟkuc coṟpōr (Debate) ciṟantatoru cātaṉamāka amikiṉṟatu.  pala poruḷkaḷikkuṟittuc coṟpōr naṭipeṟum poḻutu māṇākkarkaḷ irupirivākap pirintu poruḷi oṭṭiyum veṭṭiyum pēcuvar.  iṉṟiya paḷḷi naṭimuṟiyil kuṭimip payiṟci oru mukkiya iṭattip peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itaṉ ātaravil paḷḷiyil amikkappeṟṟuḷḷa paḷḷip pārāḷum maṉṟam pala coṟpōrkaḷukku vāyppiṉi nalkum.
9. coṟpoḻivukaḷ
pala poruḷkaḷik kuṟittu nīṇṭa coṟpoḻivukaḷ āṟṟuvatālum vāymoḻip payiṟciyi naṉku peṟa muṭikiṉṟatu.  oru poruḷikkuṟittu toṭarntāṟpōl pēcuvataṟkup poruḷaṟivum, paṭṭaṟivum, moḻiyaṟivum, mikavum avaciyam.  kīḻvakuppu māṇākkarkaḷiṭam ivaṟṟik kāṇpatu aritu.  uyarnilippaḷḷi māṇākkarkaṭkum kallūri māṇākkar kaṭkum maṭṭiluntāṉ nīṇṭa pēccukkaḷ (Long speeches) ēṟṟavi.
iṟutiyākak kūṟappeṟṟa coṟpōrkaḷiyum coṟ poḻivukaḷiyum paḷḷi māṇākkar ilakkiyak kaḻakattiṉ mūlamum kiṭṭumpaṭic ceyyalām.  eṉavē, ilakkiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ moḻip payiṟcikkup peritum tuṇiyāka iruppati nām aṟikiṉṟōm.  ākavē, ittakiya kaḻakaṅkaḷippaṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
māṇākkar ilakkiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ: tāymoḻikku mutaliṭam aḷikkappeṟṟa piṟaku paḷḷi vāḻkkiyil oru putiya uyir nāṭiyik kāṇalām.  makkaḷāṭci ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku eṅkum araciyal vātikaḷum piṟarum tāy moḻiyil pēcuvatik kēṭkum māṇākkarkaḷ tāmum avvāṟu pēcip paḻaka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇutal iyaṟki.  tāymoḻiyil pala ceytit tāḷkaḷum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷum (Periodicals) atika eṇṇikkiyil tōṉṟiyuḷḷatāl avikaḷip paṭittup pala ceytikaḷi aṟintu koṇṭa piṟaku avikaḷippaṟṟip pēcavēṇṭum eṉṟa tuṭippu māṇākkarkaḷiṭam atikamākat tōṉṟuvati āciriyarkaḷ aṟivar.  ittuṭippuk kēṟṟa vāyppukkaḷiyum mēṭiyiyum paḷḷikaḷil māṇākkar ilakkiyak kaḻakamum ēṉiya kaḻakaṅkaḷum aḷikkiṉṟaṉa.  cātāraṇamāka iṉṟu paḷḷikaḷil aṟiviyal kaḻakam, camūka iyal kaḻakam, kaṇitak kaḻakam pōṉṟa palvēṟu kaḻakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟik koṇṭu varuvatik kāṇalām.
uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil perumpālum ikkaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ naṭimuṟikaḷ yāvum tamiḻiltāṉ naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.  perum pāṉmiyāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ tamiḻil tāṉ pēcukiṉṟaṉar.  māṇākkarkaḷ tamatu karuttukkaḷip palarkkut terivikkavum, pala poruḷkaḷippaṟṟi ārāyavum, coṟpōr puriyavum ikkaḻakaṅkaḷ iṭam aḷikkiṉṟaṉa.  tāymoḻiyāciri yarkaḷum piṟapāṭa āciriyarkaḷum ottuḻittāl ikkaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilāka māṇākkarkaḷiṭam nalla tāymoḻiyaṟivi vaḷarkkalām.  ik kaḻakaṅkaḷil pēciyum veḷiyārkaḷi varavaḻittup pēcac ceytum māṇākkarkaḷukkup pēcum muṟikaḷik kāṭṭalām.  eppoḻutu pēciṉālum ep poruḷip paṟṟip pēciṉālum nalla naṭiyil piḻi kaḷiṉṟip pēca vēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  naṭunili vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷum uyarnili vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷum maṭṭilun tāṉ cātāraṇamāka ik kaḻakaṅkaḷiṉ naṭavaṭikkikaḷil kalantu koḷḷuvar.  mutalil pēcupavarkaḷiṭam avik kūccam, uṭal naṭukkam, taṭumāṟṟap pēccu ākiya kūṟukaḷil maṭṭilum kavaṉattic celutti; nāḷaṭivil karuttōṭṭam, ceyti kaḷik kōvip paṭuttutal, teḷivāṉa pēccu, nalla uccarippu, iṉiya eḷiya naṭi mutaliya kūṟukaḷil kavaṉattic celuttalām. pēccukaḷilēyē ittakiya kuṟikaḷi nīkki viṭṭāl, avarkaḷ eḻutum poḻutu piḻikaḷ kuṟiyum.  pēccut tiruttam eḻuttut tiruttattiṉ aṭippaṭi eṉṟa uṇmiyi āciriyarkaḷ aṟiyavēṇṭum.
ikkaḻakaṅkaḷil palvēṟu poruḷkaḷippaṟṟip pēca vāyppukkaḷ aḷikkalām.  nāṉ kaṇṭa kampaṉ, nāṉ kaṇṭa vaḷḷuvaṉ, eṉ maṉattik kavarnta puttakam,  nāṉ ceṉṟa mukkiya iṭam, kāppiyap peṇmaṇikaḷ, kāppiyat talivarkaḷ eṉṟa pēccu variciyil pēccukkaḷi amittup pēcac ceyyalām. aṟiviyal kaḻakattil miṉcā rattiṉ vaḷarcci, vāṉavūrtiyiṉ varalāṟu,. miti vaṇṭiyiṉ varalāṟu, tottu nōykaḷi nīkka vaḻikaṇṭa periyārkaḷ, viṇ veḷic celavu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa talippukkaḷil pēcum vāyppukkaḷit taralām.  camūka iyal kaḻakattil camayap periyārkaḷ, viṭutali nalkiya vīrarkaḷ. varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷ, naṭimuṟi araciyal piracciṉikaḷ mutaliya talippukkaḷil pēca iṭan taralām.
tāymoḻikku mutaliṭam aḷitta piṟaku ovvoru vakuppukkum ovvoru ilakkiyak kaḻakamum, paḷḷikkeṉa oru potu ilakkiyak kaḻakamum iruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  ēṉiyap pāṭaṅkaḷilirukkum viruppatti viṭa ilakkiyat tiluḷḷa viruppam ellōruṭiya maṉattiyum īrkkam āṟṟaluṭiyatāl ilakkiyattiṟkut taṉic caluki kāṭṭutal tavaṟākātu.  atikamāṉa māṇākkarkaḷum itil paṅku koḷvar.  pāṭavēḷip paṭṭiyilum ikkaḻakattiṟkeṉa oru pāṭa vēḷiyik kaṭṭāyam otukkit taravēṇṭum potu ilakkiya kaḻakattil ellā māṇākkarkaḷukkum pēcavēṇṭiya poruḷik kuṟintatu pattu nāṭkaḷukku muṉṉarē aṟivittu viṭal vēṇṭum.  ovvoru vakuppu āciriyarum tattam vakuppil pēcamuṉvarum māṇākkarkaḷukkup poruḷik kōvip paṭuttip pēcuvatilum, teḷivākap pēcuvatilum, pēcum poruḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi mēṟ koḷ nūlkaḷ, takaval nūlkaḷ, piṟanūlkaḷiliruntu tiraṭṭuvatilum tuṇiyāka iruntu payiṟci aḷikka vēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu payiṟci peṟṟa māṇākkarkaḷukku maṭṭilum potu ilakkiyak kaḻakattil ellōr muṉṉiliyilum pēcum vāyppukkaḷ aḷikkap peṟum.  patiṉintu nāṭkaḷuk korumuṟi potu ilakkiyak kaḻakam kūṭiṉāl pōtum.  patiṉintu nāṭkaḷukkorumuṟi vakuppukkaḷil pēcum payiṟci aḷikkappeṟum.  pēcum poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiya takavalkaḷi aṟiya nūlakatti nalla muṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttalām.  ellōrukkum potuvāṉa poruḷkaḷ pēcappaṭum poḻutum, veḷiyiliruntu periyōr kaḷ vantu pēcumpoḻutum ilakkiyak kaḻaka mēṭiyip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum.
intak kaḻakaṅkaḷil naṭimuṟiyil māṇākkarkaḷukkup poṟuppukkaḷ tarappeṟutal vēṇṭum.  nalla ceyalāḷarkaḷit tērnteṭuttuviṭṭāl kaḻakaṅkaḷ naṉku ceyaṟpaṭum.  tāymoḻi payiṟṟu moḻiyāka vanta piṟaku ellā āciriyarkaḷum tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷē eṉṟa peru nōkkattuṭaṉ ottuḻittāl ,ik kaḻakaṅkaḷ tāymoḻi vaḷarccikkup pallāṟṟāṉum mikat tiṟamiyuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟum.


10. tiruntiya pēccu
oruvar uḷḷattil tōṉṟiya karuttukkaḷip piṟarukku eṭuttuk kūṟum nōkkattuṭaṉēyē moḻi makkaḷāl paṭittuk koḷḷap peṟṟatu.  makkaḷatu maṉattil eḻum karuttukkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvataṟkuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷākakkoṇṭa coṟkaḷiṉ tokutiyē moḻi eṉṟu kūṟalām.  pēcuvōriṉ moḻi tiruttamāka iruntāltāṉ kēṭpōruṭiya uḷḷattil pēcuvōriṉ karuttukkaḷ teḷivākap pulaṉākum.
teḷivāṉa olippu,  tiruttamāṉa uccarippu, iṭattiṟ kēṟṟa kural eṭuppu, ākiyavi tiruntiya pēcciṉ nalviyalpu kaḷākum  pēcumpoḻutu virivu, accam, kūccam, paṭapaṭappu mutaliyaṉaviyiṉṟit taṉṉampikkiyuṭaṉum uṇarcci tatumpavum pēciṉāl pēccu ciṟakkum.  teḷḷiyatīntamiḻc coṟkaḷik koṇṭa ciṟuciṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷ pēccil amital mikavum vēṇṭaṟpālatu.  pēcuṅkāl uṭalāṭṭam, uṟuppacivu mutaliyavi kūṭā.  tikkal,  tiṇaral, ūṭē ā ū eṉa ōciyiṭal pōṉṟavaṟṟit tavirkka vēṇṭum.  pēcuvōriṉ pēccil kūṟiyatu kūṟal, māṟupaṭak kūṟal, kuṉṟak kūṟal. mikipaṭak kūṟal, veṟṟeṉat toṭuttal, ceṉṟu tēyn tiṟutal, maṟṟoṉṟu virittal mutaliya kuṟṟaṅkaḷi nīkki, curuṅkac collal, viḷaṅkavittal, naṉaviṉṟōrkkiṉimi, āḻa muṭittātal mutaliya ilakkaṇa nūlār kūṟum aḻaku kaḷi amittup pēciṉāl pēccil karuttoḻuṅku amiyum.  iṉiya uḷavāka iṉṉāta kūṟalāl nēriṭum viḷivukaḷi ōrntu vinayamāy viḷampi veṟṟi kāṇum paṇpōṭu pēca vēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu eḷiya, iṉiya, tūya naṭiyil ilakkaṇap piḻiyiṉṟi tuṭippum āṟṟalum tatumpap pēciṉāl kēṭṭār piṇippak kēḷātārum vēṭkum muṟiyil pēccu amiyum.
tiruttamaṟṟa pēcciṉ kuṟikaḷik kaḷiyum muṟikaḷ:  paḷḷikku varum iḷam māṇākkarkaḷiṉ pēccil pala vitamāṉa kuṟikaḷirukkum.  avaṟṟi āciriyarkaḷ toṭakkattiliruntē kaḷivataṟku muṉiya vēṇṭum.  itaṟku āciriyar cila muṟikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷalām.
kuḻantikaḷiṉ pēccu tiruttamākavum teḷivākavum amivataṟku mutal tuṇi āciyaratu pēccākum.  iḷañciṟārkaḷ pōlacceytalāltāṉ (Imitation) atikam kaṟkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu uḷaviyal kāṭṭum uṇmiyākum.  eṉavē, āciriyaratu pēccu teḷivākavum tiruttamākavum irukkavēṇṭum.  mutaṉmutalāka āciriyar tammiṭam varum kuḻantikaḷiṭam tōḻami uṇṭākkik koḷvataṟkuk kocci moḻiyik kiyāṇṭālum virivil ati nīkkiviṭṭu, tām avarkaṭku vaḻikāṭṭi eṉṟa eṇṇat tuṭaṉ nalla muṟiyil pēcat tuṇiya vēṇṭum.  vīṭṭil nalla pēccukkaḷik kēṭka vāyppillāta kuḻantikaḷukku āciriyariṉ pēccutāṉ ciṟanta vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amiyum.  eṉavē, āciriyariṉ pēccu māṇākkar piṉpaṟṟuvataṟku ēṟṟatōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāka amiyavēṇṭum.  avaratu pēccu tiruntiya pēcciṉ takutikaḷikkoṇṭu amiya vēṇṭum.
peṟṟōrkaḷiṉ pēccum maṟṟōrkaḷiṉ pēccum vīṭṭil kuḻantikaḷ piṉpaṟṟuvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu amiya vēṇṭum.  aṭikkaṭi āciriyarkaḷ kuḻantikaḷiṉ peṟṟōrkaḷuṭiya toṭarpu peṟṟuk kuḻantikaḷiṉ tiruttamāṉa pēccil avarkaḷiṉ ottuḻippi nāṭavēṇṭum.  cila kuḻantikaḷ mūkkaṭic cati (Adenoids) toṇṭiccati (Tonsil) ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kāraṇamākac cariyāka uccarikkātiruttalkūṭum.  avaṟṟit takka aṟuvi muṟi cikicciyāl pōkkumāṟu peṟṟōrukku yōcaṉi kūṟutalvēṇṭum.  iyalpākac cila kuḻantikaḷiṭam kāṇappeṟum cōrvu, narampumiku kiḷarcci, naramput taḷarcci mutaliyavaṟṟi ēṟṟa maruntukkaḷālum, ōyvu koṭuttalālum nīkkavēṇṭum.  vēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷ iruntāl avaṟṟiyum pōkkavēṇṭum.
cila iṭiyūṟukaḷāl cariyāka uccarittup pēca iyalāta kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉam puṇpaṭumvakiyil kuṟikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi ēḷaṉam ceytalākātu.  piḻi ceypavarkaḷic cuṭṭikkāṭṭāmal piḻikaḷip potuvāka vakuppukku eṭuttukkāṭṭi avaṟṟit tiruttalām.  naramput taḷarcciyuḷḷa oru kuḻantiyiṉ kuṟikaḷi nīkkuvataṟku vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷil oru cilarukku uccarippup payiṟcikaḷik koṭuttu avarkaḷ peṟum payiṟciyiṉik kāṇac ceytal vēṇṭum.  ek kāraṇattālum naramput taḷarcciyuḷḷa kuḻantiyiṉ kuṟikaḷit taṉiyāka eṭuttukkāṭṭa lākātu.  ittakiya kuḻantikaḷik kivēli, varital, ōviyam, eḻuttuvēli pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil amartti moḻivāyilāka veḷippaṭutta iyalāta eṇṇaṅkaḷi icceyalkaḷiṉ mūlam veḷippaṭuttalām.
vīṭṭilum paḷḷiyilum kuḻantikaḷi atikamākak kaṭintu payamuṟuttuvatāl avarkaḷ maṉamviṭṭut tāḷarāḷamākap pēcuvatilli.  tuṇivākavum tārāḷamākavum pēcāta kuḻanti kaḷiṭam tāṉ colliṉ iṟutiyi viḻuṅkutal, cilavaṟṟi iciyātu viṭuttal pōṉṟa kuṟikaḷ kāṇappaṭum.  teṟṟuvāy (Stuttering) accattiṉāltāṉ viḷikiṉṟatu eṉṟu uḷaviyal nipuṇarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  vakuppil kuḻantikaḷ oruvarō ṭoruvar cuyēcciyākap paḻakavum, āciriyarkaḷuṭaṉ tuṇivākap paḻaki viṉākkaḷi viṭukkavum vāyppukkaḷ aḷikkap peṟutal vēṇṭum.  vakuppil kuḻantikaḷukku vāyppūṭṭu pōṭak kūṭātu.  kūccattiṉāl pēcat tayaṅkum kuḻantikaḷit tuṇivākap pēcat tūṇṭavēṇṭum.
perumpālum māṇākkarkaḷiṭam la, ḻa, ḷa; ra, ṟa; na, ṇa, ṉa ākiya eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭa coṟkaḷi uccarippatil tāṉ tavaṟukaḷ ēṟpaṭum.  avaṟṟi nānekiḻ payiṟcikaḷik koṭuttup pōkkalām.  nāviṉ nuṉi mēlum kīḻum aciyu māṟuḷḷa olikaḷip paṉmuṟi uccarikkac ceyvatāl nākku tiruntum.
la,la,la,la,lā; li,li,li,li,lī; le,le,le,le,lē;
ḻa,ḻa,ḻa,ḻa,ḻā; ḻi,ḻi,ḻi,ḻi,ḻī; ḻe,ḻe,ḻe,ḻe,ḻē;
ḷa,ḷa,ḷa,ḷa,ḷā; ḷi,ḷi,ḷi,ḷi,ḷī; ḷe,ḷe,ḷe,ḷe,ḷē;
ra,ra,ra,ra,rā; ri,ri,ri,ri,rī; re,re,re,re,rē;
ṟa,ṟa,ṟa,ṟa,ṟā; ṟi,ṟi,ṟi,ṟi,ṟī; ṟe,ṟe,ṟe,ṟe,ṟē;
na,na,na,na,nā; ni,ni,ni,ni,nī; ne,ne,ne,ne,nē;
ṉa,ṉa,ṉa,ṉa,ṉā; ṉi,ṉi,ṉi,ṉi,ṉī; ṉe,ṉe,ṉe,ṉe, ṉē; 
ṇa,ṇa,ṇa,ṇa,ṇā; ṇi,ṇi,ṇi,ṇi, ṇī; ṇe,ṇe,ṇe,
ṇe, ṇē;
eṉṟa muṟiyil ariya olikaḷip paṉmuṟi payiṉṟāl nākku tiruntum.
nākku cariyākap piṟaḻvataṟkēṟṟa payiṟcikaḷiyum taralām.  otta ōcikaḷ payilum coṟkaḷi amittup paṉmuṟip payiṟci tantāl nākku tiruntum.
	kaṭalilē maravural uruḷutu piṟaḻutu.
tēr uruḷa tēṅkāy uruḷa
tēraṭi maṇi nuṉi uruḷa
malimēl maḻi peytatu
avaḷ aval tiṉṟāḷ
aṭaṭā! nari kāṭēṟutu
ōṭum narikaḷil orunari kiḻanari
kiḻanari mutukiṉil orupiṭi narimayir
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷi āyattam ceytu paṉ muṟip payiṟci tantāl nākku tiruntum.
oli piṟappiyal aṟivik kiyāṇṭum ik kuṟikaḷip pōkkalām.  oli piṟappiyali aṟiyāta āciriyar uṭaṟ kūṟṟiyali aṟiyāta maruttuvarukku ōppāvar.  ariya olikaḷi olittu oli uṟuppukaḷiṉ niliyit tam vāyiṉāl kāṭṭalām; takka paṭaṅkaḷi varintum viḷakkalām;  kuḻantikaḷip periya nilik kaṇṇāṭikku muṉ niṟutti avaravar vāyilum kaṇṭu uṇarac ceyyalām.  na,ṇa ṉa; ra,ṟa; la,ḻa,ḷa; pōṉṟa olikaḷi uccarikkap payiṟṟumpoḻutu immuṟikaḷik kiyāḷalām.  itaṟku āciriyar olipiṟappiyali naṉku aṟintavarāka iruttal vēṇṭum.
perumpālāṉa kuṟikaḷ āciriyar kūṟuvati māṇāk karkaḷ cariyākak kēṭkātatāl nēriṭukiṉṟaṉa.  takka kēḷvip payiṟcikaḷāl avaṟṟippōkkalām.  uccarittaṟku ariya olikaḷi āciriyar uccarittukkāṭṭa, māṇākkarkaḷ avarip piṉpaṟṟi uccarittalē kēḷvip payiṟciyākum.  mutalil avi payiṉṟuvarum muḻuc coṟkaḷiyum piṉṉar taṉi olikaḷiyum uccarittuk kāṭṭalām.
uccarippataṟku arumiyākavuḷḷa cila olikaḷ aṭaṅkiya peyarkaḷil avvolikaḷ muṟiyē mutal iṭi kaṭikaḷil payiṉṟu varumāṟu amittu avi kuṟikkum paṭaṅkaḷik kāṭṭip paṭittup payilac ceytu uccarippiṉ kuṟikaḷik kaḷiyalām.  olittaṟkariya olikaḷ aṭikkaṭi payiṉṟuvarum ciṟu pāṭalkaḷi maṉappāṭam ceyyattūṇṭi oppuvikkac ceytum ikkuṟikaḷi akaṟṟalām.
nāḷoṉṟukku ōriraṇṭu taṭavikaḷāvatu mūccup payiṟcikaḷ koṭuttu oliyuṟuppukkaḷukku valimi aḷikkalām.
11.  oli piṟappiyal
iḷam māṇākkarkaḷiṭam tiruntiya pēccip payiṟṟuvikkum āciriyar olipiṟappiyal aṟivu peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum.  ataṟku avar pala olipiṟappiyal nūlkaḷip payiṉṟirukkavēṇṭum.  olipiṟappiyalpaṟṟiya orucila ceytikaḷi maṭṭilum īṇṭut taruvōm.
moḻimutaṟ kāraṇamām aṇuttiraḷ oli eḻuttākum.  ivveḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappupaṟṟiyum ilakkaṇam paṟṟiyum terivikkum pakutiyē olipiṟappiyalākum; moḻiyiyal iḵtu oru mukkiyap pakutiyākum.  ivviyali naṉku aṟintiruntāl tāṉ tamiḻmoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ eḻuttu, col ākiyavaṟṟiṉ cariyāṉa uccarippi naṉku aṟintu olikka muṭiyum.  māṇākkariṉ uccarippil nikaḻum piḻikaḷit tiruttavum, avarkaḷukkuc cariyāṉa uccarippik kaṟpikkavum kūṭum.  nuriyīral, kāṟṟuk kuḻal, toṇṭi, vāy ākiya uṟuppukkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātavi.  olittalukku mikavum iṉṟiyamiyāta karuviyākiya vāyi oli piṟappiyalār iciyaṟi eṉṟu ciṟappittup pēcuvar.
eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ:  tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷi ilakkaṇa nūlār uyireḻuttu eṉṟum, meyyeḻuttu eṉṟum peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvar.  ippeyar ciṟantatoru kāraṇam paṟṟiyē iṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  taṉittiyaṅkum āṟṟaliyuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷ uyir eḻuttukkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa; uyiruṭaṉ kūṭiyē iyaṅka valla eḻuttukkaḷ meyyeḻuttukkaḷ eṉap peyariṭap peṟṟaṉa.  meyyeḻuttu-valleḻuttu, melleḻuttu, iṭiyeḻuttu eṉa mūvakippaṭum.  uṭalum uyirumpōla cērntu iyaṅkum eḻuttukkaḷi uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu kuṟiyīṭu ceytuḷḷaṉar.  uyirum meyyum mutaleḻuttu eṉappaṭum.  taṉittatoru taṉmiyiṉṟic cārntu varutalē tamakku iyalpākavuṭiya eḻuttukkaḷ mūṉṟaṉic cārpeḻuttu eṉpar tolkāppiyar.4  avi kuṟṟiya likaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam eṉpaṉa.  naṉṉūlār cārpeḻuttukkaḷi uyirmey, āytam, uyiraḷapeṭi, oṟṟaḷapeṭi, kuṟṟayalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, aikārak kuṟukkam, aukārak kuṟukkam, makarak kuṟukkam, āytak kuṟukkam eṉṟu pattuvakiyākap pākupāṭu ceytu pēcuvar.5  ivvāṟu eḻuttukkaḷ pākupāṭu ceyyap paṭṭiruppataṉ kāraṇatti oru ciṟitu ārāyvōm.
piṟappum pākupāṭum:  namatu uṭalil kaḻuttiṉuḷ iraṇṭu kuḻalkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  oṉṟu, uṇavuk kuḻal; maṟṟoṉṟu, kāṟṟukkuḻal.  kāṟṟukkuḻal mārpiṉuḷ uḷḷa kāṟṟup piyuṭaṉ cērkiṉṟatu.  kāṟṟup piyiliruntē kāṟṟu veḷip paṭukiṉṟatu.  moḻikku uṟuppākāmal veḷippaṭuṅ kāṟṟu ōci eṉṟum, moḻikku uṟuppāki veḷippaṭum kāṟṟu oli eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.  kāṟṟup piyiṉiṉṟu puṟappaṭuṅ kāṟṟu, kuralvaḷiyi yaṭintu kaṇṭam, tālu (vāyiṉuḷ irupakkam), mēlvāy mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil paṭṭu veḷic celkiṉṟatu.  avvāṟu cellumpoḻutu nikaḻum cila ceykikaḷāl oli vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu.  iv vēṟupāṭṭiṉ kāraṇamākavē palavita eḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkiṉṟaṉa.  uḷḷirun teḻum kāṟṟu miṭaṟṟiṉ muyaṟciyāl vāyiṉ vaḻiyākat taṭiyiṉṟi veḷippaṭumpoḻutu uyireḻuttukkaḷum, atu vāyil taṭippaṭumpoḻutu meyyeḻuttukkaḷum piṟakkum eṉṟu oli piṟappiyalār kūṟuvar.
eḻuttukkaḷ piṟappatil nikaḻum ceyalkaḷ:  namatu kuralvaḷiyiṉuḷ iru pakkattum puripōṉṟa cavvukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟik kural nāṇkaḷ (Vocal cords) eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar.  nām cila eḻuttukkaḷi olikkumpoḻutu avi irupakkattum pirintirukkum; cila eḻuttukkaḷi olikkum poḻutu cērntirukkum.  kural nāṇkaḷ pirintu niṟkum poḻutu nikaḻum kāriyattiṟkuc cuvācam (Breathed) eṉṟu peyar; avi cērntirukkumpoḻutu nikaḻum kāriyattiṟku nātam (Voiced) eṉṟu peyar.  valleḻuttukkaḷ maṭṭilum cuvāca kāriyattāl piṟappaṉa.  maṟṟa ellā eḻuttukkaḷum nātakāriyattāl piṟappaṉa.  ivaṟṟāl valleḻuttut taṉiyiṉa māṉataṉ kāraṇam aṟiyappaṭum.
namatu mārpiṉuḷ uḷḷa kāṟṟuppiyiṉiṉṟu puṟappaṭum kāṟṟuk kaṇṭatti aṭintapiṉ veḷippaṭuvataṟku iraṇṭu vaḻikaḷ uḷa.  oṉṟu, vāy; maṟṟoṉṟu, mūkku.  mēṟkūṟiya nātatti mūkkiṉ vaḻiyāka veḷiviṭṭāl melleḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum.  ataṉi vāyiṉ vaḻiyāka veḷiviṭṭāl iṭiyeḻuttum, uyireḻuttum tōṉṟum.  ivaṟṟāl melleḻuttut taṉiyiṉamāṉataṉ kāraṇam aṟiyappaṭum.
kāṟṟi veḷiviṭuvatil muṟi vēṟupāṭum uṇṭu.  itaṉi uḷ muyaṟci eṉpar.  kāṟṟi nāviṉatu nuṉi, iṭi, aṭi, pakkam ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉāl kaṇṭam, tālu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil taṭuttup piṉpu veḷiviṭutal kūṭum; avvāṟu taṭukkāmalum veḷiviṭutal kūṭum.  taṭukkumpoḻutu muḻuvatum taṭukkalām; ciṟitum taṭukkalām.  nāviṉāl taṭukkāmal veḷiyē viṭum kāṟṟiṉāl uyireḻuttukkaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.  uyireḻuttukkaḷi olikkiyil tālu, mēl vāy mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷi nākkut toṭiṉum kāṟṟiṉit taṭuttal illi.  valleḻuttukkaḷiyum melleḻuttukkaḷiyum olikkiyil kaṇṭam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil nākkiṉ aṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāl kāṟṟi muḻutum taṭukkiṉṟōm.  taṭiyi nīkkumpoḻutu uṇṭākum oliyē valleḻuttu, melleḻuttukkaḷāy veḷippaṭukiṉṟatu,  iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷi olikkiyil, nākkiṉāl kāṟṟic ciṟitu taṭuttu veḷiviṭukiṉṟōm.
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu, kāṟṟu taṭiyiṉṟi veḷivarutaliṉ, uyireḻuttukkaḷ tāmē olikka iyalkiṉṟaṉa.  taṭippaṭṭu varutaliṉ valleḻuttu melleḻuttukkaḷ tāmē olikka iyalvaṉa alla.  ciṟitu taṭippaṭutaliṉ iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷ ōrāṟṟāl tāmē olikka iyalutalum kūṭum.  olikkuntiṟattil uyireḻuttukkaṭkum iṉaveḻuttukkaḷākiya valliṉa melliṉa veḻuttuk kaṭkum iṭippaṭṭiruttaliṉ, ivi iṭi yeḻuttu eṉappaṭṭaṉa.  ivaṟṟi ariyuyir (Semi-vowels) eṉṟu āṅkilattil vaḻaṅkutal aṟiyattakum.
mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, uḷḷiruntu veḷippaṭum kāṟṟi nāviṉāl taṭukkātu viṭṭāl uyireḻuttukkaḷum, ciṟitu taṭuttu viṭṭāl iṭiyeḻuttukkaḷum, muḻutum taṭuttu viṭṭāl valleḻuttukkaḷum, muḻutum taṭuttu mūkkiṉ vaḻiyē viṭṭāl melleḻuttukkaḷum olikkum eṉpatu pōtarum.
kāṟṟuppiyiliruntu kaṇṭamvari vanta kāṟṟiṉi nāviṉ nuṉi, iṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāl vāyiṉuḷ ōrōriṭattut taṭuttum taṭukkāmalum veḷiviṭuvati mēlē kaṇṭōm.  avaṟṟāl, kāṟṟut taṭipaṭum iṭaṅkaḷum taṭippaṭātu uṟṟuc cellum iṭaṅkaḷum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappiṭamākum.  avi aṭiyaṇṇam, iṭiyaṇṇam, nuṉiyaṇṇam, nuṉi yaṇṇatti yaṭutta iṭam, pallum palliṉ mēliṭamum, utaṭu eṉṉum āṟiṭaṅkaḷiyum oli piṟappiyalār piṟappiṭaṅkaḷākak koḷḷuvar.  ivaṟṟōṭu, kāṟṟuppiyuḷḷa mārpiṉiyum melleḻuttiṉ olivarukiṉṟa mūkkiṉiyum cērttu eṭṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvōrum uḷar.  inta āṟu iṭaṅkaḷil, aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭavāṟu eḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkum:

1. aṭiyaṇṇattil piṟakkum 	 	a, ā;
	eḻuttukkaḷ		ka, ṅa.	
	 			  	
2. iṭiyaṇṇattil piṟak		i, ī, e, ē, ai;
   kum eḻuttukkaḷ		ca, ña, ya;
		
3. nuṉiyaṇṇattil piṟak	
   kum eḻuttukkaḷ		ṭa, ṇa.
				
4. nuṉiyaṇṇatti yaṭutta		
   iṭattil piṟakkum eḻuttukkaḷ		ṟa, ṉa; ra, ḻa.
			
5. palliṉaṭiyil piṟakkum		ta, na;
   eḻuttukkaḷ		la, ḷa.
		
6. utaṭṭil piṟakkum eḻuttukkaḷ		u, ū, o, ō, au; 					pa, ma, va.
		
ip piṟappiṭam nōkki eḻuttukkaḷ neṭuṅ kaṇakkil niṟuttap peṟṟaṉa.  cārpeḻuttukkaḷuḷ āytam oḻinta eḻuttukkaḷellām tattam mutaleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭattiyē piṟappiṭamāka uṭiyaṉa.  āytam, taṉmuṉ niṟkum valleḻuttiṉ piṟappiṭattiyē taṉakkup piṟappiṭamākak koḷḷum.  oli piṟappiyalpaṟṟiya muḻu vivaraṅkaḷi attuṟi nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka; avaṟṟit tolkāppiyam, naṉṉūl mutaliya nūlkaḷiluḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappippaṟṟik kūṟum nūṟpākkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu uṇarka.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāyk kiṭakki muṟiyiṉic civañāṉa muṉivar viḷakkik kūṟum kāraṇattiyum6 īṇṭu aṟital cālap payaṉtarum.
8.  paṭippu
tāymoḻip paṭippil vāymoḻip payiṟcikku aṭutta mukkiyamāṉa pirivu paṭippākum.  ōtuvatoḻiyēl eṉṟa pāṭṭiyiṉ vākki eṇṇi uṇarka.  ōtuvatu - paṭippatu.  kiyeḻuttil allatu acceḻuttil uḷḷatik kaṇṇāl pārttu vāyāl uccarittuc collip poruḷ uṇarvatip paṭippu (Reading) eṉkiṉṟōm.  paṭippu eṉpatu eḷitāṉa ceyal aṉṟu.  varivaṭivattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷi olivaṭivamāka māṟṟi uccarikkum uṟuppukkaḷum, coṟkaḷi nōkkum kaṇkaḷum ottuḻittāltāṉ paṭippu cariyāka naṭipeṟum.  eṉavē paṭittalil coṟkaḷik kāṇal, uccarittal, poruḷuṇartal eṉṟa mukkūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  paṭikkum paḻakkatti mutal vakuppiliruntē toṭaṅkavēṇṭum.
nōkkam:  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil paṭippatil tiṟamiyāṉa paḻakkaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉar.  tāṉākap paṭikkum paḻakkatti uṇṭākka vēṇṭum.  mēlvakuppuk kaḷil ippaḻakkam iṉṉum naṉku uṟutiyaṭiyum.  mēl vakuppil paṭikkum nōkkaṅkaḷi nāṉku vakiyākap pakukkalām.  avi karuttuṇartal, ilakkiya iṉpam nukartal, kaṟpaṉi āṟṟali, vaḷarttal, moḻittiṟam aṭital eṉpavi.  inta nāṉku nōkkaṅkaḷum kivarappeṟuvataṟku vēṇṭiya payiṟci muṟikaḷi āciriyarkaḷ kiyāḷa vēṇṭum.
perumpālum nām karuttuṇarvataṟkākavē paṭikkiṉṟōm.  pala puttaṅkaḷip paṭippatāl palvēṟu ceytikaḷi aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya ceytikaḷ, cuṟṟuppuṟattil naṭakkum nikaḻccikaḷ, aṟiviyal uṇmikaḷ, navīṉa poṟinuṭpa cātaṉaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi aṟivataṟkākap pala puttakaṅkaḷiyum, nāḷ, vāra, māta itaḻkaḷiyum māṇākkar paṭikkiṉṟaṉar.  itu karuttu ṇartal nōkkam kivarap peṟuvataṟkup paṭippatākum.  paṭippatil nalla paḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭup pala puttaṅkaḷip paṭippatāl ilakkiyac cuvi perukum; ceñcoṟ kavi iṉpattilum tiḷikkalām.  nūliṉāl peṟum cuvi teviṭṭāta teḷḷa mutamāy mēlum mēlum vaḷarntu niṉiyuntōṟum putiya iṉpattit tarum.  māṇākkarkaḷ tattamakku vēṇṭiya katip puttaṅkaḷ, taṉ - varalāṟukaḷ, vāḻkki varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ, kāviyaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip paṭippatāl ilakkiya iṉpam perukum.  kaṟpaṉi āṟṟal iṉṟiyamiyāta paṇpukaḷil oṉṟu. iḷam māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyē avvāṟṟal tōṉṟutal mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.  kaviti ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ (Novels), ciṟanta ciṟukatic celvaṅkaḷ, uyarnta nāṭakaṅkaḷ mutaliyavi ik kaṟpaṉi āṟṟali naṉku vaḷarkkum.  uḷḷattil uṇmi oḷi uṇṭāṉāl vākkiṉil oḷi tāṉākat tōṉṟum.  paṭittalil nāṉkāvatākak kivarap peṟuvatu moḻittiṟam aṭital. pala puttaṅkaḷip paṭippatāl māṇākkarkaḷiṉ col vaḷam perukum. palaputiya coṟkaḷi aṟivar.  māṇākkarkaḷ cintiyil oṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟāka uḷḷa mūṉṟu kaṟpaṉi vaṭṭaṅkaḷiṉuḷ avarkaḷuṭiya coṟkaḷi aṭakkalām.  uḷ vaṭṭattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ neruṅkiya col naṇparkaḷ; tēvippaṭumpoḻutu avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuvar.  māṇākkarkaḷ viruppappaṭi accol naṇparkaḷ iyaṅkuvar.  uḷ vaṭṭattiṟku aṭuttuḷḷa veḷi vaṭṭattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷukku aṟimuka māṉavarkaḷ; koñcam paḻakkamtāṉ avarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu.  pala puttakaṅkaḷip paṭikkuntōṟum paḻakkappaṭṭa iccoṟkaḷ ovvoṉṟāka naṇparkaḷāki uḷ vaṭṭattiṟku varakkūṭum.  mūṉṟāvatāka veḷivaṭṭattiluḷ coṟkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷukkup putiyavarkaḷ.  avarkaḷi pṟṟi māṇākkarkaḷ atikak kavali koḷḷuvatilli; avarkaḷic cantippataṟkum ciratti eṭuttukkoḷvatilli.  āḻnta paṭippiṉpoḻutu ipputiyavarkaḷuḷ evariyāvatu cantikka nērntāl āciriyarkaḷ avari māṇākkarkaḷukku aṟimukam ceytu vippār; naṇparkaḷākavum ceytuviṭuvatuṇṭu.  aḵtellām avarkaḷic cantikkum iṭattiyum kālattiyum poṟuttatu.  akaṉṟa paṭippiṉpoḻutu (Extensive reading) ettaṉiyō aṟimukamāṉavarkaḷ naṇpar- kaḷākavum putiyavarkaḷ aṟimukamāṉavarkaḷākavum ātal kūṭum.  eṉavē, eṉṟum collulakattil māṇākkarkaḷiṭam naṇparkaḷum, iruppar; aṟimukap paṭṭavarkaḷum iruppar; putiyavarkaḷum iruppar.


1.  paṭippiṉ vakikaḷ
kuṟikkōḷit teḷivākak koṇṭāltāṉ entac ceyaliyum veṟṟiyuṭaṉ koṇṭu celutta iyalum.  āciriyarkaḷum paṭippiṉ nōkkaṅ kaḷit teḷivākac cintiyiṟ koṇṭāltāṉ payiṟṟu muṟikaḷit tiṭṭamāka amittu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ koṇṭuceluttalām.  nōkkaṅkaḷi aṉucarittup paṭippi mūṉṟu vakiyākap pirittuḷḷaṉar aṟiñarkaḷ.
tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarkkum paṭippu (Develomental reading):  paṭittalil uḷḷa palvēṟu tiṟaṉkaḷiyum aṭivataṟku mēṟkoḷḷum muṟiyiyē ippeyarāl kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar.  kaṇnakarcci, kaṇpāyccal, kaṇpārvi mutaliya tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarttu, paṭippil virivi uṇṭākkal, karuttuṇartal, coṟ kaḷañciyattip perukkutal, niṉivāṟṟali vaḷarttal, vāyviṭṭup paṭittaliluḷḷa palvēṟu kūṟukaḷilum payiṟci peṟutal mutaliyavaṟṟip paṭippiluḷḷa tiṟaṉkaḷākak karutalām.
poḻutu pōkkup paṭippu (Recreational reading):  ilakkiya iṉpaṅkarutiyum, ōyvu nēraṅkaḷik kaḻikkak karutiyum,  vāyviṭṭup paṭittup piṟarukku makiḻcci yūṭṭak karutiyum paṭippati ippeyarāl kuṟippiṭalām.
payaṉkarutiya paṭippu (Functional reading):  payaṉ karutip paṭikkum ellāp paṭippum ittalippil aṭaṅkum.  toli pēciyiṉ eṇṇik kaṇṭaṟivatu, puki vaṇṭi cellum nērattik kāṇpatu, pañcāṅkak kuṟippukkaḷik kaṇṭaṟivatu pōṉṟa ceyalkaḷiliruntu karuttuṭaṉ pāṭapputtaṅkaḷip payilvatu variyilumuḷḷa ellāvitamāṉa paṭippiyum itaṉuḷ aṭakkalām.
inta mūvakip paṭippum taṉittaṉiyākavē irukkum eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.  oṉṟi nōkkamākakkoṇṭu paṭittālum ēṉiya iraṇṭum kivarappeṟum.  paṭippiṉ tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarkkum nōkkattuṭaṉ paṭikkumpoḻutu kuḻantikaḷ poḻutu pōkkiṟkākap paṭittaṟkum, payaṉ karutip paṭittaṟkum uriya pakutikaḷi paṭittal kūṭum.  poḻutu pōkkiṟkākap paṭikkappeṟum katiyē paṭikkum tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarkkum nōkkattiṟkākap paṭikkap peṟalām; tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarkkum pāṭattiṭṭamē aṟiviyalkaḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi aṟiyavum payaṉpaṭalām.  eṉṟālum, ovvoruvita paṭippiṉ nōkkamum āciriyariṉ karuttil teḷivāka nilava vēṇṭum.
īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭappeṟṟa mūṉṟu paṭippu vakikaḷum ellā niliyilum paṭippik kaṟpikkat tuṇiyāka irukkum; avaṟṟi aṭippaṭi nōkkaṅkaḷākak koṇṭāltāṉ ellā niliyilum paṭippic cariyāṉa muṟiyil koṇṭu celuttalām.



2.  paṭippu toṭaṅka vēṇṭiya nili
kuḻantikaḷ toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil cērntavuṭaṉē cila āciriyarkaḷ paṭippit toṭaṅki viṭukiṉṟaṉar.  paṭippiṉ taṉmiyiyum nōkkattiyum kavaṉittāl itu tavaṟu eṉpatu teriyavarum.  accil kāṇum coṟkaḷi aṟiya vēṇṭumāṉāl, kuḻantikaḷiṉ coṟ kaḷañciyam (Vocabulary) peruki yirukka vēṇṭum.  avarkaḷ teḷivāṉa vākku vaṉmiyiyum peṟṟirukka vēṇṭum.  cuṟṟup puṟaṅkaḷiyum pala iyaṟki nikaḻccikaḷiyum nēril pārttup pōtumāṉa paṭṭaṟivu peṟāta kuḻantikaḷ coṟkaḷ uṇarttum poruḷkaḷi aṟiya iyalātu.  tam paṭṭaṟivil pārttum kēṭṭum tuyttu varum poruḷkaḷik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷi mutalil paṭikka vēṇṭum.  nēril kaṇṭa nikaḻccikaḷtām kuḻantikaḷukku uṟcākatti aḷikkum.  āciriyarkaḷō peṟṟōrkaḷō pala iṭaṅkaḷiyum kāṭcikaḷiyum nēril kāṇpataṟkuk kuḻantikaḷi aḻittuc cella vēṇṭum.  vāymoḻip payiṟcikku aṉu kūlamāyirukkum katikaḷ, uriyāṭalkaḷ, pāṭaṅkaḷ, paṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttic coṟkaḷ coṟṟoṭarkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ karutti avarkaṭku naṉku viḷakka vēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu tarum payiṟcikaḷiṉāl kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉam pakkuvappaṭṭu avarkaḷiṭam karuttuṇarum āṟṟal ciṟitu ciṟitāka vaḷarat toṭaṅkum.  āciriyarkaḷum, peṟṟōrkaḷum, piṟarum puttakaṅkaḷip pārppatiyum paṭippatiyum kuḻantikaḷ kaṇṇuṟum poḻutu paṭippil avarkaḷukku ārvam eḻutal kūṭum.  ivvāṟu ārvam eḻum kālantāṉ kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭippit toṭaṅkuvataṟku ēṟṟa kālamākum.
aimpatu āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉirunta jāṉ ṭyūyi eṉṉum pērāciriyar eṭṭu āṇṭukaḷ niṟi veytiya kuḻantikaḷukkut tāṉ paṭippu toṭaṅkap peṟavēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.  uḷaviyaṟkali ōraḷavu naṉku viḷakkam peṟṟirukkum iṉṟu uṭal vayativiṭa maṉavayatik koṇṭutāṉ iti aṟutiyiṭal vēṇṭum eṉpati aṟikiṉṟōm.  kuḻantikaḷ paṭikkum āṟṟali aṭivatu avarkaḷ vaḷarum cūḻniliyiyum avarkaḷ maṉappakkuvam aṭintiruppatiyum poṟuttatu.  amerikkak kalvip paricīlaṉik kuḻu oṉṟu āṟari vayatu muṭinta piṟakutāṉ kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭippu toṭaṅkavēṇṭum eṉṟu cōtaṉikaḷāl muṭivu ceytuḷḷatu.1  kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṟivu nili, avarkaḷ vāḻum cūḻnili ākiya iraṇṭu kūṟukaḷiyum nīkkittāṉ immuṭivi nām ēṟṟuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  āṟari vayatu niṟiveytiya kuḻantikaḷum paṭikkakkūṭiya maṉavayati (Mental age) eytāmalirukkalām; paṭikkum maṉavayati aṭintālum vāḻum cūḻniliyiṉ kāraṇamāka avarkaḷukkup paṭippil ārvam eḻāmalum irukkalām.  cūḻnili vacatiyāka iruntu pāppāp pāṭṭukkaḷ, cevilip pāṭṭukkaḷ, katikaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷi aṭikkaṭik kēṭkum vāyppuk kaḷum, caṟṟu virivākavum naṉṟākavum pēcum vāyppukkaḷum peṟṟuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ āṟari vayatu niṟivataṟku muṉpē paṭippil ārvattik kāṭṭakkūṭum.  eṉavē, kuḻantikaḷ paṭippataṟku ārvaṅkāṭṭum vayatu avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu, avarkaḷ vāḻum cūḻnili, avarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ ākiya kūṟukaḷip poṟuttuḷḷatu eṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum.
kaṟṟōrkaḷum paṭikkum cūḻnilikaḷum aminta illaṅ kaḷiṉiṉṟum varum aṟitiṟaṉ mikka kuḻantikaḷ paṭippi enta muṟiyil kaṟṟukkoṭuttālum ati virivākap paṟṟik koḷḷuvar; avarkaḷukku enta muṟiyum tēviyilli eṉṟukūṭak kūṟiviṭalām.  moḻi vaḷam (Linguistic resources) illāta allatu kuṟivāyuḷḷa illaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum varum aṟitiṟaṉ mikkakuḻantikaḷ paṭippiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi uṇarat toṭaṅkiyatum paṭippil ārvaṅkāṭṭip paṭikkat toṭaṅkiviṭuvar.  nalla cūḻniliyuḷḷa paḷḷikaḷil itaṉi eḷitil eytuvikkalām.  nalla cūḻniliyil vāḻum kuṟimati yuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ palvēṟuvakit tūṇṭutalkaḷāl viri vākavō metuvākavō paṭippil ārvattik kāṭṭak kūṭum; paṭittāltāṉ matippuṭaṉ vāḻalām, paṭikkāviṭṭāl piṟar tammik kuṟivāka matippar eṉṟa uṇmi eppoḻutāvatu pulaṉākip paṭippatil ārvattik kāṭṭalām.  nalla cūḻ niliyil vāḻa vacatiyaṟṟa kuṟimatiyuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷtām āciriyarkaḷukkuc catā ētāvatu piracciṉikaḷit tantu koṇṭēyiruppar.  kaṟpikkum tuṟiyil putiya puttakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟuvataṟkum, putiya muṟikaḷ eḻuvataṟkum ivarkaḷē kāraṇamāka irukkiṉṟaṉar.  ivarkaḷiṉ maṉa niliyiyum vāḻum cūḻniliyiyum aṟiyātu avarkaḷukkuc cātāraṇa muṟiyil paṭippik kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ payaṉkāṇa muṭiyātu.  aṭippaṭiyākac ceyyavēṇṭiya cilavaṟṟic ceytapiṟaku kaṟpittāltāṉ ōraḷavu payaṉik kāṇalām.
paṭippil kuḻantikaḷiṉ ārvam:  paṭṭaṟivu mutirnta evarum kuḻantikaḷ paṭippataṟku eppoḻutu āyattamāka uḷḷaṉar eṉpatik kūṟiviṭuvar.  nalla cūḻniliyil aintari yiliruntu āṟu maṉa vayatu muṭivataṟkuḷ paṭikkum ārvam kuḻantikaḷiṭam naṉku tali kāṭṭum.  appoḻutu kuḻantikaḷ katikaḷik kēṭpatil ārvaṅ kāṭṭuvar; paṭaṅkaḷuḷḷa puttaṅkaḷip puraṭṭuvatil viruppaṅ kāṭṭuvar.  puttaṅkaḷil pārkkum oru cila coṟkaḷik kāṭṭi iḵtu eṉṉa? m~J v‹d? eṉṟu viṉavuvar.  cila kuḻantikaḷ tamakkup paṭikkat teriyum eṉṟu pācāṅku ceyvatu muṇṭu; iṉṉum cila kuḻantikaḷ tammarukēvuḷḷa puttakam allatu ceytittāḷit talikīḻākap piṭittukkoṇṭu niṉiviliruntu muṉ kēṭṭaṉa vaṟṟit tāṟumāṟāka uḷaṟuvar; intac ceyalkaḷi āciriyar payaṉuṟum vaḻikaḷil tiruppiviṭa vēṇṭum.
vaḷarntavarkaḷ paṭittaliṉāl payaṉ peṟuvatik kaṇṭu kuḻantikaḷum paṭippatil viruppaṅ kāṭṭuvar.  tamatu naṇparkaḷ paṭippatikkaṇṭu tāmum avvāṟu paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu ācippaṭuvar.  paṭaṅkaḷiṉkīḻ eḻutappeṟṟi ruppatit terintu koṇṭāl appaṭaṅkaḷ evaṟṟi uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟiyalām eṉpatik kuḻantikaḷ aṟiyum poḻutu avarkaḷum accoṟkaḷit terintu koḷḷa virumpuvar.  tām kēṭkum katikaḷ puttakattiluḷḷavi eṉpati aṟintāl, ap puttakattip paṭikka āval tūṇṭap peṟuvar.  potu iṭaṅkaḷil vikkap peṟum viḷamparaṅkaḷālum, aṟivippuk kaḷālum uḷḷa payaṉkaḷi aṟiyumpoḻutu tāmum avaṟṟip paṭittut terintukoḷḷa avākkoḷḷuvar.  eṉavē, acciṭṭa coṟkaḷi aṟintu koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa tēviyi avarkaḷē uṇarntu koṇṭāl tāṉ paṭippatil iyalpāka ārvam eḻum.  colvatik kavaṉattuṭaṉ kēṭka iyalāta kuḻantikaḷiṭamum, kēṭkum orucila coṟkaḷit tirumpakkūṟa iyalatāta kuḻantikaḷiṭamum acceḻuttu uṇarttum poruḷi uṇarum āṟṟal vaḷara iṭam illi.


3.  paṭippil ūkkuvittal
kuḻantikaḷāyiṉum, iḷiñarkaḷāyiṉum, avarkaḷiṭam paṭippil ūkkatti ūṭṭiviṭṭāl kaṟṟal eḷitil naṭi peṟum eṉpatu paṭṭaṟivil kāṇum uṇmi.  toṭakka nilip paḷḷikaḷil mutaṉ mutalākap paṭippit toṭaṅkum poḻutu ati oru viḷiyāṭṭākak karutum muṟiyil toṭaṅkavēṇṭum.  paṭippatāl peṟakkūṭiya iṉpattiyum kuḻantikaḷi uṇarac ceyvatāl avarkaḷ atil paṟṟu vippar; paṭikkum ārvamum iyalpāka avarkaḷiṭam eḻum.  uḷaviyalukku otta cila muṟikaḷik kiyāḷa vēṇṭu mēyaṉṟi kāraṇakāriya muṟikku ēṟṟaviyeṉṟu karuti, uḷaviyal muṟikku māṟākavuḷḷa muṟikaḷik kiyāḷutal cariyaṉṟu.  iṉṟu perumpālum kalvittuṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟu vōr kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷil karuttiṉic celuttik kuḻantikaḷi aṟavē maṟantuviṭu kiṉṟaṉar; atu tavaṟu.  avarkaḷ kavaṉamellām kuḻantikaḷippaṟṟiyē iruttal vēṇṭum.  aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭavaṟṟic ciṟu kuḻantikaḷip paṭippil ūkkuvikkum muṟikaḷākak karutalām.
(1) paṭaṅkaḷip pārkka virumpāta kuḻantikaḷē irār.  vaṇṇantīṭṭiya paṭaṅkaḷ iḷam uḷḷaṅkaḷi eḷitil kavara vallavi.  eṉavē, kaṇkavar paṭaṅkaḷiṉ vāyilākap paṭippi vaḷarkkalām.  ittakiya paṭaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻ avaṟṟiṟku viḷakkam tarum muṟiyil cila coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭar kaḷiyum eḻutip paṭaṅkaḷi vakuppaṟiyil toṅka viṭṭiruntāl, avaṟṟik kaṇṇuṟum kuḻantikaḷ iṉpam aṭivar.  kaṇṇāṟ peṟum iṉpam karuttiṉāṟpeṟum iṉpattiṟkut tūṇṭu kōlāka amintu ap paṭaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻ eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa vaṟṟit terintukoḷḷa avarkaḷ viḻivar.  kuḻantikaḷuk keṉṟu tayārittu aḻakāka acciṭṭa vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷuḷḷa puttakaṅkaḷi vāṅkik koṭuttu avaṟṟiluḷḷa paṭaṅkaḷip paṟṟip pēcac ceyyalām.2
(2) vakuppaṟikaḷilum paḷḷittōṭṭaṅkaḷilum āṭukaḷaṅkaḷilum cila kaṭṭaḷi allatu vēṇṭukōḷ aṭṭikaḷit toṅkaviṭalām.  kaṭṭaḷi kaḷiṉ paṭi kuḻantikaḷ ceyyat tavaṟiṉāl avarkaḷi aḻittu ak kaṭṭaḷi allatu vēṇṭukōḷ toṭarkaḷi urakkappaṭittu avaṟṟiṉpaṭi naṭakkat tūṇṭalām.  variciyākap pō, eccil tuppātē, kālik kaḻuviyapiṉ uḷḷē vā, cattam ceyyātē eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷākak koḷḷalām.
(3) paḷḷiyiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum, paḷḷittōṭṭattiluḷḷa maram, ceṭi, koṭikaḷiṉ peyar kaḷiyum periya eḻuttukkaḷāl aṭṭi allatu takarattil eḻuti ap poruḷkaḷiṉmītu toṅka viṭalām.  poruḷkaḷip pārkkumpoḻutu avaṟṟik kuṟippiṭum coṟkaḷiyum kuḻantikaḷ aṟivar.  (e-ṭu) caṉṉal, katavu, mutal vakuppu, iraṇṭām vakuppu, talimiyāciriyar aṟi, āciriyarkaḷ aṟi mutaliyavi; teṉṉi maram, vāḻi maram, palā maram, pākku maram, vēppa maram, mallikic ceṭi, rōjāc ceṭi, mullik koṭi, avarik koṭi mutaliyavi.
(4) aṟamoḻi aṭṭikaḷiyum paḻamoḻi aṭṭi kaḷiyum āṅkāṅkut toṅkaviṭṭup paṭikkat tūṇṭalām.  cila kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil nirantaramāka irukkumpaṭi cila paḻamoḻikaḷiyum nītimoḻikaḷiyum aḻakāka eḻuti viṭalām.  vēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil aṭikkaṭi māṟṟi māṟṟi vikkak kūṭiyavāṟu aṭṭikaḷilō takaraṅkaḷilō aḻakāka eḻuti vikkalām.
(5) paḷḷi aṟivippup palakiyil cila poruḷkaḷi eḻutivittu avaṟṟip paṭikkat tūṇṭalām. (e-ṭu) nāḷi poṅkal paṇṭiki; paḷḷikkūṭam illi, ñāyiṟṟuk kiḻami paḷḷi āṇṭu viḻā eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavi.  oruvārattiṟku oru paḻamoḻi allatu nītimoḻiyip paḷḷi aṟivippup palakiyil eḻuti vikkalām.  putitāka viḷiyāṭum viḷi yāṭṭukkaḷ, āṭṭap pōṭṭikaḷ campantamāṉa ceytikaḷi eḻuti viḷamparam ceyyalām.
(6) ceytit tāḷkaḷ, tuṇṭup piracuraṅkaḷ, paṭakkāṭcikaḷ, vilippaṭṭikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip paṭikkac ceyyalām.  kuḻantikaḷi veḷiyil kūṭṭic cellumpoḻutu terukkaḷ, kaṭikaḷ, ciṟṟuṇṭiccālikaḷ, pūmpoḻilkaḷ, pukivaṇṭi niliyam, paṭakkāṭci viḷamparam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷip paṭikkat tūṇṭalām.  ivaṟṟil oru cuviyi uṇṭākki viṭṭāl kuḻantikaḷ tām kāṇpaṉavaṟṟi yellām paṭittu aṟintukoḷḷum paḻakkatti mēṟkoḷḷuvar.
(7) ciṟiya katip pāṭalkaḷ, pāppāp pāṭalkaḷ, cevilip pāṭalkaḷ, āṭṭap pāṭalkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi iciyuṭaṉ paṭittuk kāṭṭip paṭikkat tūṇṭalām.  kaṇṇaṉ, pūñcōli, kalkaṇṭu, ampulimāmā pōṉṟa kuḻantikaḷiṉ paruva veḷi yīṭukaḷi itaṟku ōraḷavu payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām.  āciriyarkaḷē pāṭṭukkaḷi iyaṟṟi nalla tāḷil periya eḻuttukkaḷil aḻakāka eḻutik kuḻantikaḷiṉ kavaṉattiṟkuk koṇṭu varalām.
(8) katikaḷ kuḻantikaḷiṉ karutti īrkkak kūṭiyavi.  puttakaṅ kaḷiliruntu ciṟiya katikaḷik kuḻantikaḷukku aṭikkaṭip paṭittukkāṭṭik katikaḷil avarkaḷukku ārvatti ūṭṭi avipōṉṟa pala katikaḷi avarkaḷiyē paṭikkumāṟu tūṇṭalām.  oru katiyiṉ pātiyic colli, ēṉiya pātiyi paṭikkumpaṭi tūṇṭal, cila katikaḷik kūṟi avi pōṉṟavi koṇṭa puttakattik kāṭṭal pōṉṟa muṟikaḷik kiyāḷalām.
immuṟikaḷi yellām mēṟkoḷḷuvataṟku muṉṉar kuḻantikaḷ teḷivākap pēcavum, karuttuṇaravum āṟṟal peṟṟirukkavēṇṭum.  paṭippati aṟivataṟkum paṭippil cuvi uṇṭāvataṟkum payiṟciyi aḷippataṟku muṉṉar ōraḷavu paṭṭaṟivum peṟṟu ap paṭṭaṟivil kaṇṭavaṟṟippaṟṟiya coṟkaḷiyum kuḻantikaḷ aṟintirukka vēṇṭum.  piṟantatu mutal vīṭṭilēyē palavita paṭṭaṟivum pēccup paḻakkamum cumār 300 coṟkaḷiṉ aṟivum cātāraṇamāka aintāṇṭu nirampiya kuḻantikaḷ peṟṟiruppar.  āciriyarkaḷ mutaṉ mutalākat tammiṭam varum kuḻantikaḷi avarkaḷ vīṭṭilum paḷḷiyilum kaṇṭaṟintirukkum poruḷkaḷippaṟṟiyum paṭṭaṟintirukkum nikaḻccikaḷ paṟṟiyum viṉavi avaṟṟiṟku viṭiyiṟukkac ceyya vēṇṭum.  kuḻantikaḷ kūccattiviṭṭu uriyāṭat toṭaṅkiya piṟaku paṭippit toṭaṅkutal ciṟantatu.


4.  paṭippu payiṟṟum muṟikaḷ
uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟum tāy moḻiyā ciriyarkaḷ toṭakka nilip paḷḷikaḷil kuḻantikaḷukkuk kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷit terintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  aṭip paṭi evvāṟu amikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu eṉpati aṟiyātu tam paṇiyit tiṟamiyāka āṟṟa iyalātu.  ammuṟikaḷik kiyāḷuvatil tiṟamiyuṭiyavarkaḷāka ākāviṭiṉum, am muṟikaḷiṉ neṟiyit (Principle) terintu avi evvāṟu kiyāḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatip paṟṟiya mēmpōkkāṉa aṟiviyāvatu peṟṟiruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  kīḻ vakuppuk kaḷil payiṟṟal naṉṉiliyiliruntāltāṉ āciriyarkaṭku evvāṟu nalla muṟiyil tam vēliyit toṭaṅkalām eṉṟa karuttukkaḷ tōṉṟum; payiṟṟal cariyaṟṟatāka iruppiṉ aṅkup paṭittu veḷivarum kuḻantikaḷiṉ tēviyi yaṟintu avarkaḷ tam muṟikaḷi māṟṟiyamittuk koḷḷalām.  immātiriyē ovvoru vakuppu āciriyarum kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil kiyāḷap peṟum muṟikaḷiyum mēlvakuppukkaḷil kiyāḷa vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷiyum karuttil amittukkoṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟiṉāl, paṭippu nalla muṟiyil cellum.  paṭippu toṭarntu naṭipeṟa vēṇṭiya toṉṟātalāl, āciriyarkaḷ iti uḷaṅkoṇṭu paṇiyāṟṟavēṇṭum.
iṉṟu ovvoru nilip paḷḷikaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟi varum āciriyarkaḷ toḻil muṟiyil uṟavukoḷḷātu uḷḷaṉar.  uyarnilip paḷḷiyil vēli ceypavarkaḷ toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟupavarkaḷiviṭat tammi aṟivilum piṟavaṟṟilum uyarntavarkaḷākak karutik koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  kallūrikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟuvōrō tammi uyarnta cātiyiṉarākak karutikkoṇṭu inta iraṇṭu nilip paḷḷiyāciriyarkaḷuṭaṉ cātāraṇamākak kūṭa kalantu paḻaku vatilli.  ivarkaḷ piṟavilēyē āciriyarātaṟkuriya takuti kaḷaṉittum peṟṟuviṭṭatākak karutik koṇṭiruppavarkaḷ.  ivarkaṭku āciriyap payiṟci iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉṟu kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ vaṟpuṟuttāmiyē itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu karutalām.  toḻil tuṟiyil ovvoruvarum tattam piracciṉi kaḷip potu mēṭikkuk koṇṭu vantu ārāyntāl tāṉ tavaṟukaḷit tiruttikkoḷḷavum, oruvar kiyāṇṭu veṟṟi kaṇṭa putiya muṟiyip piṟar kiyāḷavum vāyppukkaḷ ēṟpaṭum.  araciṉarum ippiḷavi atikarittu viṭṭaṉar eṉṟutāṉ collavēṇṭum.  āciriyar tokutikku vākkaḷip pavarkaḷil toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil paṇiyāṟṟu pavarkaḷic cērkka villi.  ellā nili āciriyarkaḷum, toḻil muṟiyil oṉṟucērntu tattam piracciṉikaḷi ārāyum nāḷ virivil ēṟpaṭa vēṇṭum.  .
atu kiṭakka āti maṉitaṉ moḻi kaṟṟa varalāṟṟiliruntu ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭippu kaṟpikkum muṟiyi oruvāṟu aṟutiyiṭalām.  maṉita camūkam pallāyira āṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarttup paṇpaṭuttiya moḻiyi oru kuḻanti cila āṇṭukaḷil kaṟṟuk koḷḷukiṉṟatu.  kaṟṟalil namatu mūtātiyar aṭinta paṭṭaṟivip payaṉpaṭutti namatu muṟiyi aṟutiyiṭukiṉṟōm.  āti maṉitaṉ mutalil vāyppēcciṉi maṭṭilum kaṟṟāṉ.  piṟaku ōviyaṅkaḷi eḻutit taṉ karuttukkaḷip piṟarukku aṟivittāṉ; nāḷaṭivil ōviyaṅkaḷukkup patilāka coṟkaḷik kiyāḷum tiṟamiyip peṟṟāṉ.  piṟaku taṉittaṉi eḻuttukkaḷik kaṇṭu moḻiyiṉ neṭuṅ kaṇakki variyaṟi ceytāṉ.  inta muṟiyilēyē kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭikkum paḻakkatti uṇṭākkiṉāl cālap payaṉtarum.  ivviṭattil ovvoru moḻiyāciriyarum moḻi varalāṟṟippaṟṟi ōraḷavu terintu koḷvatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉ atu mikiyaṉṟu.
iṉṟu kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭippu kaṟpippatil pala muṟikaḷ kiyāḷap peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ivi eḻuttu muṟi, colmuṟi, pārttuc collum muṟi, coṟṟoṭarmuṟi, kati muṟi eṉap palavākum.  vaṉitiyar āṭiyil nākarikam aṭikkaṭi māṟuvatippōl, kalvit tuṟiyil kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ aṭikkaṭi māṟuvatilli.  āṉāl, kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭippu kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟik kavaṉittāl puraṭcikaramāṉa muṟi eṉṟu karutakkūṭiyatum kūṭa ciṟitu kālattiṟkuttāṉ celvākkuṭaṉ tikaḻkiṉṟatu; atu nīkkappeṟṟup putiya muṟi kiyāḷap peṟukiṉṟatu.  kuḻantikaḷukkuk kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷil etu ciṟantatu eṉṟu iṉṉum kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ tuṇinta pāṭilli.  ciṟanta muṟiyit tuṇivatu eḷitāṉa ceyalum aṉṟu.  ataṟku nāṉku kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa.  mutalāvatu:  oru nalla āciriyar ōr eḷiya celvākkaṟṟa muṟiyik kiyāṇṭum nalla palaṉi aṭital kūṭum; cātāraṇa āciriyar oruvar nalla muṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭu peṟum payaṉiviṭa atikamākavē ivar peṟak kūṭum.  vakuppukkaḷil ettaṉiyō etirpārāta cantarppaṅkaḷ eḻukiṉṟaṉa.  kuḻantikaḷiṭam uṟcākatti eḻuppi avarkaḷit toṭarntu paṭikkavikkum kūṟu enta muṟiyilum illi.  iraṇṭātu:  paṭippu kaṟpikkak kiyāḷap peṟum muṟi paḷḷi naṭimuṟiyi oṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum.  iḵtum eḷitāṉa ceyalaṉṟu.  muṟikaḷukku vēṇṭum tēvikaḷi muṟṟuvikkap paḷḷiyiṉ naṭimuṟi oru peruntaṭiyākavē irukkum.  irupatu yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉar vakuppaṟic cūḻniliyil kiyāḷap peṟṟa muṟikaḷ yāvum iṉṟiya vakuppaṟic cūḻalukkuc cariyāṉaviyāka irā.  mūṉṟāvatu:  paḷḷiyirukkum iṭatti yoṭṭi muṟikaḷiṉ celvākkum māṟum.  nāṭṭup puṟattil orē aṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyar nakarppuṟattil pala vacatikaḷuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarippōl tiṟamiyuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟa iyalātu.  celvar vāḻum pakutip paḷḷikaḷil nalla celvākkup peṟṟirukkum muṟikaḷ vaṟiyar vāḻum pakutip paḷḷikaḷukku ottuvarā.  nāṉkāvatu:  cōtaṉikaḷāl muṟikaḷiṉ uyarvu tāḻvukaḷi aṟutiyiṭutalum eḷitaṉṟu.  ittakiya cōtaṉikaḷ ceyyap peṟumpoḻutu cūḻnili, itara cātaṉaṅkaḷ mutaliyavi orēmātiriyāka iruttal muṭiyātu.
iṉi, paṭippu kaṟpittaṟku naṭi muṟiyil kiyāḷap peṟum oru cila muṟikaḷi ārāyvōm:
(1) eḻuttumuṟi:  itu mikap paḻiya muṟi.  paṇṭik kālattiliruntu ulakil ellā nāṭukaḷilum immuṟi vaḻakki liruntu varukiṉṟatu.  paḻaṅkālattil ḵpōṉīṣiyarkaḷ, kirēkkarkaḷ, urōmāṉiyarkaḷ ākiyōr inta muṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar.  iṉṟum namatu nāṭṭil ellāp pakutikaḷilum toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil im muṟi mēṟkoḷḷappeṟṟu varukiṉṟatu.  ceṭṭimār nāṭṭiluḷḷa ēṭṭup paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷil, immuṟiyiṉ celvākki iṉṟum kāṇalām.  immuṟippaṭi tamiḻ neṭuṅ kaṇakku varici muṟi tavaṟātu eṇkaḷippōl kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟatu.  eḻuttukkaḷi mutalil kaṟpittu ataṉ piṟaku paṭikkak kaṟpippatāl iḵtu `eḻuttu muṟi eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu.  eḻuttukkaḷ muḻu vatiyum kaṟpataṟkup pala mātaṅkaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa.  piṉṉar coṟkaḷ kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  mutalil īreḻuttuc coṟkaḷiyum piṟaku mūṉṟu, nāṉku eḻuttuc coṟkaḷiyum, ataṉ piṟaku coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum kaṟpikkiṉṟaṉar.
muṟivallār immuṟiyil pala kuṟikaḷik kāṭṭu kiṉṟaṉar.  immuṟi kāraṇakāriya muṟikkup poruntu vatāka iruntālum, uḷaviyal uṇmikkuc ciṟitum poruntātu.  kuḻantikaḷi uṟṟunōkkiṉāl, avarkaḷ coṟkaḷiyum poruḷkaḷiyum muḻu vaṭivattil tokuttup pārppatu teriyavarum; piṉṉartāṉ taṉittaṉip pakutikkup pōy vakuttup pārkkiṉṟaṉar.  vīṭṭil kuḻantikaḷ coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum pēcukiṉṟaṉar.  ati oṭṭic cellāmal taṉittaṉi eḻuttukkaḷik kaṟpittal iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa ceyalākum.  im muṟi terintavaṟṟiliruntu teriyāta vaṟṟiṟkup pōtal eṉṟa uṇmikku muraṇākavum uḷḷatu.  aṉṟiyum, immuṟi moḻi vaḷarcciyaṭinta muṟikkum māṟāṉatākum.  tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa 247 eḻuttukkaḷi eḻutap paṭikkak kaṟṟukkoḷvataṟku atikakkālam ākum; mutalil poruḷaṟṟa eḻuttukkaḷik kaṟpippatil kālam vīṇākak kaḻikiṉṟatu.  paḷḷiyil piṟar paṭippatik kaṇṇuṟṟut tāṉum avarkaḷippōl paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvam mikkuḷḷa kuḻantikku immuṟi mikka ēmāṟṟattik koṭuppatuṭaṉ paṭippiluḷḷa āvaliyum kuṟittal kūṭum; paṭippilum veṟuppiyum calippiyum maṉaniṟi viṉmiyiyum tarutal kūṭum.  eḻuttuk kūṭṭippaṭippatil muḻukkavaṉamum celuttappeṟuvatāl, paṭikkum pakutiyiṉ karutti naṉku uṇaramuṭiyātu; itaṉāl karuttuṇarāmaṟ paṭikkum paḻakkam vaḷarntu paṭippiṉ nōkkattiyē kikūṭāmaṟ ceytu viṭavum kūṭum.  kuḻantikaḷ eḻuttu, uccarippu, col ākiya mūṉṟiyum orē camayattil kaṟkavēṇṭiyiruppatāl, kaṟṟal kaṭiṉamāka irukkum.  tamiḻneṭuṅ kaṇakkiluḷḷa ellā eḻuttukkaḷiyum ippaṭip paṭippatu vīṇē.  cila tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ cātāraṇa vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷil varuvatilli.  ārvattuṭaṉ paṭikkat toṭaṅkumpoḻutu yu, pu, ṅa varici, ña varici mutaliyavikaḷi oppuvippatālum, eḻutu vatālum paṭippatiluḷḷa ārvam kuṉṟukiṉṟatu; paṭikkat tuṭikkum kuḻanti manta niliyi yaṭintu viṭukiṉṟatu.  ellāvaṟṟiyum viṭa immuṟiyil kavarcciyum uṟcākamum illi.  āṉāl, kuḻantikaḷiṉ kavaṉatti īrttu avarkaḷukku uṟcākamūṭṭac cila upāyaṅkaḷ kiyāḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ovvoru eḻuttiyum immuṟiyil kaṟpikkumpoḻutu avveḻuttik koṇṭu toṭaṅkum peyarōṭu kūṭiya oru poruḷiṉ paṭattiyum ataṉ kīḻ avveḻuttiyum acciṭuvar.  cila camayam paṭattiṉ peyariluḷḷa mutal eḻuttu vēṟu niṟattāl kāṭṭappeṟuvatumuṇṭu.  kuḻantikaḷ paṭaṅkaḷip pārttu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vari vaṭivaṅkaḷiyum olikaḷiyum uṟcākamākak kaṟpar.  piṉṉar eḻuttukkaḷik kūṭṭic coṟkaḷik kaṟpar.  paṭaṅkaḷip pārkkum variyiltāṉ uṟcākam irukkum; eḻuttukkaḷik kūṭṭip paṭikkum poḻutu uṟcākam kuṉṟiviṭum.
immuṟiyil niṟikaḷē illi eṉṟu colliviṭavum muṭiyātu.  āṅkilam pōlaṉṟi, tamiḻ moḻiyil uccarippatu pōlavē eḻutukiṉṟōm; eḻutuvatu pōlavē uccarikkavum ceykiṉṟōm.  ōr oli orē eḻuttāl tāṉ kuṟikkap peṟukiṉṟatu; ōr eḻuttu ōr olikkē aṟikuṟiyākavum uḷḷatu.  ātalāl immuṟi tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷi eḷitākak kaṟpataṟku naṉku tuṇi ceykiṉṟatu.  kuḻantikaḷ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ cariyāṉa uccarippit terintu koḷvatāl, eḻuttup piḻiyillāmal paṭikkavum eḻutavum kaṟka iṭamuṇṭu.  eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ piṟappu muṟippaṭi uyir, mey, uyir mey eṉṟa variciyil paṭippatāl, eḻuttuvarici muṟiyi aṟivataṟkum, piṟkālattil olipiṟappiyali aṟivataṟkum eḷitāka irukkum.
(2) colmuṟi:  nāṉūṟu yāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉatāka kāmeṉiya (Comenius) eṉpār col  muṟiyik kaṇṭaṟinta tākak kūṟuvar.  iṉṟu im muṟi paṭipput tiṭṭaṅkaḷil mikavum celvākkup peṟṟuḷḷatu.  col eṉpatu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ tokutiyaṉṟu.  ataṟkeṉat taṉi matippu uṇṭu eṉṟa uḷaviyal uṇmiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amintuḷḷatu im muṟi.  kuḻantikaḷ pacuvik kāṇumpoḻutu taliyi vēṟākavum, uṭali vēṟākavum, vāli vēṟākavum, nāṉku kālkaḷi vēṟākavum kāṇpatilli; ati oru taṉippaṭṭa muḻup pirāṇiyākavē kāṇkiṉṟaṉar.  immātiriyē coṟkaḷum kuḻantikaḷukkut taṉippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷi uṇarttum.  oru taṉi eḻuttik kaṟṟaṟital pōlavē oru muḻuc colliyum kuḻantikaḷ eḷitākak kaṟṟuk koḷvar.  collip poru ḷuṇarttum aḷavākak koṇṭu kaṟpippatāl itaṟkuc colmuṟi eṉṟu peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu.
immuṟippaṭi āciriyar oru collik karum palakiyil eḻuti, vakuppiluḷḷa kuḻantikaḷukku uccarittuk kāṭṭuvār; piṟaku kuḻantikaḷi ovvoruvarāka uccarikkac ceyvār. ivvāṟu cila coṟkaḷik kaṟpitta piṟaku, avaṟṟic ciṟu coṟṟoṭarkaḷāka iṇittuk kaṟpippār.  coṟkaḷ eḻutap peṟṟa miṉṉaṭṭikaḷik koṇṭu avaṟṟi viri vākavum piḻiyiṉṟiyum paṭikkum tiṟamiyi vaḷarkkalām.  iṉṉoru vitamākavum āciriyar im muṟiyik kiyāḷu vatuṇṭu.  oru paṭattik kāṭṭi atik kuṟikkum collik karumpalakiyil eḻuti uccarittuk kāṭṭik kuḻantikaḷiyum uccarikkumpaṭi ceyvār.  paṭaṅkaḷi maṭṭilum taṉit taṉiyākak koṇṭa miṉṉaṭṭikaḷ, avaṟṟik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷi maṭṭilum taṉittaṉiyākak koṇṭa miṉṉaṭṭikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭu viḷiyāṭṭu muṟikaḷil iṇikkac ceytu paṭikkum tiṟamiyi vaḷarkkalām.  nalla paṭipput tāḷkaḷ vacatiyuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil im muṟiyi mikavum veṟṟikaramākak kiyāḷalām.
im muṟiyiṉāl pala niṟikaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa.  nām pēcum poḻutu taṉi eḻuttukkaḷākap pēcuvatilli; coṟ kaḷākavē pēcukiṉṟōm, ātimaṉitaṉ, muḻuc coṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyē tāṉ karutiya poruḷkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭāṉ.  kuḻantikaḷum pēcat toṭaṅkum poḻutu muḻuc coṟ kaḷākavē pēcukiṉṟaṉar.  ākavē, muḻuc coṟkaḷik kaṟpittal iyaṟki yāṉatum uḷaviyal muṟikku ēṟṟatumākum.  oru poruḷi ataṉ ōviyattik koṇṭu evvāṟu aṟikiṉ ṟōmō atupōla avvō viyatti ataṉ collō viyamākiya eḻuttukkaḷālāṉa collik koṇṭu aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu.  muḻuc coṟkaḷik kaṟṟapiṉṉar avaṟṟiluḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷik kuḻantikaḷ eḷitil kaṟkiṉṟaṉar.  coṟkaḷiṉ vaṭivattiyum, karuttiyum, uccarippiyum teḷivāka aṟintu koḷvataṟku im muṟi tuṇipurikiṉṟatu.  coṟkaḷiṉ karutti aṟintu paṭippatāl, kuruṭṭup pāṭamiṉṟip paṭippataṟkum, paṭippil uṟcākam ēṟpaṭuvataṟkum iṭam uṇṭu.  kuḻantikaḷ paḷḷikku varumpoḻutu aintu vayatiṟkuḷ pala coṟkaḷi aṟintavarkaḷākavum pala karuttukkaḷit terintavarkaḷākavum, cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷi amikkum āṟṟal vāyntavar kaḷākavum viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.  im muṟiyil avarkaḷukku aṟimukamāṉa coṟkaḷi mēlē vivarittavāṟu kaṟpittu, piṟaku eḻuttukkaḷik kaṟpittāl paṭippu eḷitākap pōkum.  itaṉāl terintavaṟṟiliruntu teriyātavaṟṟiṟkup pōtal, muḻutiliruntu pakutikkup pōtal eṉṟa uḷaviyal koḷki kaḷiyum ceyaṟpaṭutta muṭikiṉṟatu.
immuṟiyil cila kuṟikaḷum illāmal illi.  eṉiṉum, avaṟṟi eḷitil camāḷittuk koḷḷalām.  eḻuttuk kūṭṭip paṭikkum paḻakkam illātatāl putiya coṟkaḷip paṭikkumpoḻutum, pārkkāmal eḻutum poḻutum eḻuttup piḻikaḷ uṇṭākum eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  terinta coṟkaḷil peṟṟa payiṟcikaḷip pōlavē, putiya coṟkaḷip payilumpoḻutum coṟkaḷip paṉmuṟi uccarittal, eḻuttuk kūṭṭutal, eḻututal, aṭikkaṭi muḻuc coṟkaḷākavē uṟṟunōkkutal mutaliya payiṟcikaḷāl ap piḻikaḷ ēṟpaṭāmal ceyyalām. coṟkaḷi muḻumiyāka uccarittup paṭittuk karuttuṇarntapiṉ avaṟṟiṉ uṭpakuti kaḷiyum taṉittaṉiyāka uccarittu eḻuttuk kūṭṭik kaṟpittāl ikkuṟiyē nēriṭātu.  āṅkilattil kuṟi ēṟpaṭalām; uccarikkiṉṟapaṭiyē eḻutum tamiḻ moḻiyil ikkuṟikku iṭamē illi eṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalām.  kuruṭṭup pāṭamākac coṟkaḷiṉ variciyip paṭaṅkaḷiliruntu kaṟṟuk koḷvar eṉṟum, avaṟṟik karuttuṇarāmalum pārāmalum virivākap paṭittukkoḷvar eṉṟum maṟṟoru kuṟi kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  ikkuṟiyiyum eḷitil nīkkalām.  uccarikkum poḻutu coṟkaḷiṉmītu viralkaḷi vittukkoṇṭuvaracceytal, taṉic coṟkaḷi uccarikkumpaṭi aṭikkaṭik kēṭṭal, pāṭattil varum coṟkaḷi muṟi māṟṟi eḻutippaṭikkac ceytal mutaliyavaṟṟāl ikkuṟi nīṅkum.  maṇal, caṇal, taṇal; maram, varam, caram; pākku, nākku, cākku; ēr, vēr, kār pōṉṟa ēṟakkuṟiya ōr ōcic coṟkaḷ aṭuttu varumpoḻutu māṟṟip paṭippar eṉṟu iṉṉoru kuṟiyum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  ittakiya coṟkaḷiṉ naṭuvil māṟṟōcic coṟkaḷi amittu ikkuṟiyi nīkkalām.  im muṟiyāl paṭippu virivāka vaḷarntālum, eḻuttukkaḷi uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ kaṟṟuk koḷḷa iyalātu eṉṟa kuṟi kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu.  iḵtu uṇmi tāṉ; paṭikkak kaṟṟukkoṇṭa piṉṉarē eḻuttukkaḷ kaṟpikkappaṭa vēṇṭum.  tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil ellā eḻuttukkaḷiyum toṭakkattilēyē kaṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa nipantaṉi oṉṟum illi.  mūṉṟām vakuppil neṭuṅ kaṇakkic cariyāka aṟintāl pōtumāṉatu.
(3) kaṇṭu collum muṟi:  eḻuttukkaḷik kūṭṭāmal coṟkaḷiyō coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyō pārttatumē muḻu miyāka appaṭiyē uccarittup paṭippatik kaṇṭu collum muṟi eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  im muṟiyāl muḻuc coṟkaḷ kuḻantikaḷukku aṟimukam ceyyappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  col muṟiyil kiyāḷappaṭum paṭaṅkaḷi immuṟiyil payaṉpaṭuttu vatilli.  āṉāl, kaṟṟuk koṇṭa coṟkaḷit tokuttu coṟṟoṭarkaḷākak kaṟpippar.  im muṟiyāl kuḻantikaḷukku paṭippil uṟcākam kuṟiyum.  toṭakka niliyil paṭaṅkaḷiṉṟic coṟkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷi aṟintu koḷvatu aritu.  immuṟiyil coṟkaḷ oli aḷavilēyē niṉṟuviṭak kūṭum.  col muṟiyi im muṟiyuṭaṉ kalantu kiyāṇṭāl nalla payaṉik kāṇalām.
(4) coṟṟoṭar muṟi:  karuttuṇarntu paṭippatil vaṟpuṟuttuvataṟkākak kaṇṭaṟinta putiya muṟi itu.  coṟṟo ṭariyē karuttiṉit terivikkum aḷavu kōlākak koṇṭuḷḷa tāl im muṟikkuc coṟṟoṭar muṟi eṉṟa peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu.  im muṟippaṭi kuḻantikaḷ mutaṉ mutalākac coṟṟoṭar kaḷiyē kaṟkiṉṟaṉar.  coṟṟoṭarkaḷip paṭikkap paḻakiya piṟaku at toṭarkaḷiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiyum eḻuttukkaḷiyum kaṟkiṉṟaṉar.  im muṟi kaṇṭu collum muṟiyiṉ vitiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭuḷḷatu.  nām pēcuvatil cila pala coṟkaḷaṭaṅkiya toṭarkaḷiyē mēṟ koḷḷukiṉṟōm.  coṟṟoṭar oru muḻuk karuttit terivippatāl atuvē moḻiyiṉ mukkiya uṟuppākum.  kuḻantikaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷālēyē tam karuttukkaḷit terivikkiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē, im muṟi kuḻantikaḷukku iyal pāṉatum uḷaviyalukku ēṟṟatum ākum.  oru ciṟiya katiyic ciṟiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷāl amittuk kaṟpittāl aḵtu uḷaviyalukkum kuḻantikkum ēṟṟatāy amintu niṟinta payaṉiyum tarum eṉpatil aiyamilli.
immuṟiyil kaṟpittal ivvāṟu toṭaṅkap peṟum:  vakuppaṟiyilō paḷḷiyilō uḷḷa ētāvatu oru poruḷip paṟṟiyāvatu, kuḻantikaḷukkuc contamāṉa ētāvatu oru poruḷip paṟṟiyāvatu paṭippit toṭaṅkalām.  ap poruḷip paṟṟi kuḻantikaḷiṭamiruntu cila coṟṟoṭar kaḷi varuvittup puttakaṅkaḷākat tokukkalām.  periya tāḷkaḷil mēlē paṭam pōṭuvataṟku iṭamviṭṭu cila poruḷkaḷippaṟṟit taṉit taṉiyākac coṟṟoṭarkaḷi eḻutalām.  it tāḷkaḷil mēl vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷik koṇṭō āciriyarō paṭaṅkaḷi variyalām.  ovvoru nāḷum kuḻantikaḷimiṭaruntu varuvikkum coṟkaḷip puttakaṅ kaḷākavum paṭattāḷkaḷākavum tokukkalām.  aṭik kaṭik kuḻantikaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟiviluḷḷa coṟkaḷum, coṟṟoṭarkaḷum ivikaḷil amiyavēṇṭum.  mutaṟ puttakattil 50-liruntu 150 vari coṟkaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyirukkalām.  ovvoru colliyum ēḻiliruntu irupatu taṭavi vari mīṇṭum mīṇṭum kiyāḷalām,  ivvāṟu kiyāṇṭāltāṉ ac coṟkaḷ, kāṇpatālēyē maṉattil naṉku patiyum.  ivvāṟu tēviyāṉa puttakaṅkaḷiyum paṭattāḷkaḷiyum tayāritta piṉṉar kuḻantikaḷukku ac coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kaṟpikkalām.  itaṉāl coṟṟoṭarkaḷiluḷḷa coṟkaḷiyum, piṉṉar coṟkaḷiṉ uccarippiyum, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ oli vēṟu pāṭṭiyum kuḻantikaḷ kaṟpar.
vakuppaṟi, paḷḷittōṭṭam, viḷiyāṭum tiṭal ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil cila ceyalkaḷic ceyyumpaṭi tūṇṭum kaṭṭaḷi kaḷip periya eḻuttukkaḷāl aṭṭiyil eḻuti māṭṭi vikkalām.  katavit tiṟa, caṉṉali mūṭu, kāl kaḻuvu, tali vāru, variciyākap pō eṉpaṉa pōṉṟavaṟṟi eḻutalām.  avaṟṟiṉpaṭi kuḻantikaḷ naṭakkāviṭṭāl, avikaḷi avarkaḷukkup paṭittuk kāṭṭalām; piṟaku paṭikkac ceyyalām.  paṭippataṟku āval ūṭṭak kūṭiya cātaṉaṅkaḷ vakuppaṟiyil irukka vēṇṭum.  ciṟiya katikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷ, pāppāp pāṭalkaḷ, ceyal muṟikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip paṭaṅkaḷāl viḷakkip periya eḻuttukkaḷāl eḻuti vakuppaṟikaḷil toṅkaviṭalām.
immuṟi iyaṟkiyāṉatu; itu kuḻantikaḷ uṟcākat tuṭaṉ paṭippataṟkut tuṇipurikkiṉṟatu.  kuḻantikaḷ taṅkaḷukku viḷaṅkak kūṭiyavaṟṟiyum tāṅkaḷ virumpuvaṉa vaṟṟiyum kaṟpatāl, kaṟṟalil avarkaḷukku nalla paṟṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu.  muḻup poruḷik kuḻantikaḷ tam pulaṉuṇarccikaḷāl aṟintu ataṉ pakutikaḷik kaṟpatu pōlavē, muḻuc coṟṟoṭarkaḷi mutalil kaṟṟu, piṟaku coṟkaḷik kaṟkiṉṟaṉar.  ātalāl itu kuḻantiyiṉ uḷḷattiṟku ēṟṟa muṟi eṉpataṟkut taṭiyilli.  immuṟiyil paṭikkap paḻakiṉāl, pārviyi yoṭṭip paḻakum kāraṇattāl pala coṟkaḷ kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉattil naṉku patiyum; teḷivuṭaṉ karuttuṇarntu paṭikkum tiṟamiyum avarkaḷukku ēṟpaṭum.
immuṟiyil pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷi eḻutik kaṟpittalil oḻuṅkilliyeṉṟum, coṟkaḷañciyattip paṭippaṭiyāka eḷimiyiliruntu arumikkup perukkikkoṇṭu pōka vaḻiyilli yeṉṟum, oru tiṭṭamāṉa muṟippaṭi acciṭṭa pāṭap puttakattip paṭippataṟku iḵtu īṭākātu eṉṟum cila kuṟikaḷum kāṭṭappeṟum.  avi ōraḷavu uṇmi yēyāyiṉum, kuḻantikaḷ paṭippil kāṭṭum uṟcākam kāraṇamāka ak kuṟikaḷ nīṅkum.  avarkaḷ eḷitil paṭikkum tiṟamiyiyum aṭivar.  im muṟiyik kavaṉattuṭaṉ kiyāṇṭāl āṇṭiṉ iṟutikkuḷ mutal vakuppuk kuḻantikaḷ kaṟka vēṇṭiya coṟkaḷi yellām naṉṟākak kaṟpittu viṭalām eṉpatil taṭi yoṉṟumilli.
immuṟiyil kaṟpikkappeṟum kuḻantikaḷ pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭa coṟṟoṭarkaḷi appaṭiyē neṭṭuruc ceytu kuruṭṭuppāṭamāka oppuvippar eṉṟum, vēṟu putiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷi aṟiya vāyppirātu pōkum eṉṟum, taṉittaṉic coṟkaḷiyum eḻuttukkaḷiyum aṟiya iyalātu pōkum eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  mēl vakuppu māṇākkar kaḷik koṇṭum tāmākavum āciriyar pala putiya puttakaṅ kaḷit tayārittuk kiḷippiḷḷipōl oppuvikkum paḻakkat tit taṭukka immuṟi utavukiṉṟatu.  tavira, ellāk kuḻanti kaḷum kuruṭṭuppāṭamāka oppuvikkiṉṟaṉar eṉṟum, avarkaḷ taṉic coṟkaḷi aṟiya māṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟuvataṟ killi.  āciriyarkaḷ coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kaṟpittapiṟaku taṉic coṟkaḷiṉ uccarippu, taṉic coṟkaḷiṉ eḻuttuk kūṭṭal, eḻuttukkaṉiḷ uccarippu eṉṟa muṟiyil kaṟpittāl oruvita kuṟiyum nēriṭātu.  eṅkō ōriraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷil ikkuṟi nērntāl, aḵtu im muṟiyic cariyākak kiyāḷāta āciriyarkaḷiṉ kuṟiyēyaṉṟi muṟiyiṉ kuṟiyaṉṟu.  tamiḻil ik kuṟiyē nēriṭātu eṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟalām.
coṟṟoṭar muṟiyil paṭikkak kaṟṟukkoṇṭa kuḻantikaḷ eḻuttuk kūṭṭalil tiṟamiyaṟṟavarkaḷāka iruppar eṉṟu iṉṉoru kuṟiyum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  orōvaḻi iḵtu uṇmiyākavum irukkalām.  āṉāl, atuvum muṟiyiṉ kuṟiyāl nēriṭuvataṉṟu; aḵtu ammuṟiyi muṟṟum muṭiyak koṇṭuceluttic carivara naṭattik kāṭṭāta āciriyariṉ kuṟiyeṉṟē colla vēṇṭum.  im muṟiyil iṟutinili variyilum viṭātu payiṉṟa kuḻantikaḷum eḻuttuk kūṭṭutalil tiṟamiyaṟṟavarkaḷāka iruppar eṉṟu colluvataṟkilli.  orōvaḻi cila kuḻantikaḷiṭam akkuṟi kāṇappaṭṭāl avarkaḷiṭam taṉik kavaṉam celutti āciriyar ataṉik kaḷiyalām.  olipiṟappiyalukku otta eḻuttukkaḷillāta āṅkila moḻiyil akkuṟi eḻa iṭamuṇṭu; tamiḻ moḻiyil ataṟkuc ciṟitum iṭamē illi.
pala coṟkaḷit toṭarntu orē kālattil uccarittuk karuttuṇarntu paṭikkak kaṟṟukkoḷvatu kuḻantikaḷukku eḷitaṉṟu eṉṟu piṟitoru kuṟiyum kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu.  āṉāl, muḻuc coṟṟoṭarkaḷākap pēcuvatē iyalpāṉa muṟi; uḷaviyal uṇmikkum ēṟṟatu.  aṉṟiyum, kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṟivukkum āṟṟalukkum paṭṭaṟivukkum uṭpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiyē pāṭaṅkaḷ amivatāl avaṟṟip paṭittuk karuttuṇartal arumiyāka irātu.
(5) kati muṟi:  katikaḷiṉ vāyilākap paṭippu kaṟpippatik kati muṟi eṉṟu collukiṉṟōm.  itu coṟṟoṭar muṟiyiṉ virivēyākum.  coṟṟoṭar muṟiyil katiyik kaṟpippatu tāṉ katimuṟi.  oru katiyip pala ciṟu coṟṟoṭar kaḷāl amittut toṭarcciyākak kaṟpikkalām.  katiyi ōraḷavu muṉṉatākavē aṟintuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ coṟṟoṭar kaḷit terintu kaṟpar.  coṟṟoṭarkaḷi aṟinta piṟaku, palvēṟu cantarppaṅkaḷil coṟkaḷi aṟiyat tuṇivar; coṟkaḷi yaṟinta piṟaku eḻuttukkaḷi aṟiyat toṭaṅkuvar.  eṉavē, kati muṟi eṉpatu coṟṟoṭar muṟi, col muṟi, eḻuttu muṟi ākiyavaṟṟi uṟuppukkaḷākak koṇṭa muṟiyē eṉpatu aṟiyappaṭum.
mēṟkūṟappeṟṟa muṟikaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷum niṟikaḷum immuṟikkup poruntuvatāl avaṟṟi īṇṭu mīṇṭum kuṟippiṭat tēviyilli.  āṉāl, kati muṟi kuḻantikaḷiṉ iyaṟki āvalittūṇṭi paṭippiṟku oru tēviyi aḷikkiṉṟatu; vāymoḻip payiṟcikkum ciṟanta vāyppukkaḷi nalkukiṉṟatu; im muṟiyil pala kōviyāṉa coṟṟoṭar kaḷik kaṟpatil veṟupput tōṉṟalāmeṉiṉum avi katiyi viḷakkuvatāl paṭippatil āvali ūṭṭi veṟuppip pōkkiviṭum.
āciriyarukkuk kuṟippu:  ellā muṟikaḷiṉ nalla kūṟukaḷit tokutta toru kalavi muṟiyē ciṟantatu.  paṭṭaṟivum kaṟpikkum āṟṟalum kivarappeṟṟa āciriyarkaḷ tam aṟivuttiṟamiyāl ovvoru muṟiyilumuḷḷa nalla kūṟukaḷit takka aḷavilum vakiyilum cērttuk kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉanilikku ēṟṟavāṟu kiyāṇṭāl paṭikkum paḻakkam kuḻantikaḷukku eḷitil kiṭṭum; kuḻantikaḷum kuṟinta kālattil niṟinta payaṉi aṭivar.


5.  paṭippil payiṟci taravalla tuṇikkaruvikaḷ
kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭippil payiṟci tarakkūṭiya tuṇik karuvikaḷ kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉanili, paṭṭaṟivu, aṟivu, āṟṟal ākiyavikaḷukku eṭṭakkūṭiyaviyāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  kuḻantikaḷ virumpum poruḷkaḷ, āciriyarkaḷ avarkaḷukku ēṟṟaṉavākak karutum poruḷkaḷ, katip pāṭalkaḷ, pāppāp pāṭalkaḷ, āṭṭappāṭalkaḷ, cevilip pāṭalkaḷ mutali yavaṟṟi āciriyarkaḷē ēṟṟa viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷōṭu vēṟu paṭṭa palavakip paṭipput tāḷkaḷil eḻuti āṇṭu muḻuvataṟkum vēṇṭiya aḷavil tokuttu vittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.
