paṭipput tāḷkaḷi uṇṭākka vēṇṭum eṉṟa āvalik kuḻantikaḷiṭam eḻuppi, etippaṟṟi eḻutalām eṉpati viṉākkaḷ mūlam avarkaḷiṭamiruntu varuvittu vēliyit toṭaṅkalām.  avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭum poruḷkaḷippaṟṟip paḻuppu allatu mañcaḷ tāḷkaḷil kaṟuppu allatu nīla niṟattil periya eḻuttil cila ciṟiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷi eḻutavēṇṭum; paṭam tīṭṭuvataṟku mēlē iṭam viṭṭu ivaṟṟi eḻutavēṇṭum.  itippōl ciṟu katikaḷ, katip pāṭalkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum paṭipput-tāḷkaḷāka amikkalām.  ciṟiya aḷavil ovvoru kuḻantikkum vēṇṭiya paṭikaḷākavum āyattam ceyyalām; allatu vakuppil ovvoru ciṟu pirivukkum oru paṭiyāka eḻutalām.  itupōl nāṉku allatu aintu tāḷkaḷi ōrāṇṭup paṭippukku vēṇṭiya aḷavil āyattam ceytu vittuk koḷḷalām.  accāki veḷivarum puttakaṅkaḷiviṭa ivi kuḻantikaḷiṉ kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarum eṉpataṟku aiyamilli.
ivaṟṟit tavira vēṟu tuṇikkaruvikaḷiyum āciriyar āyattam ceytu vittukkoḷḷalām.  kuḻantikaḷ vāḻum cūḻnilikaḷiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ, ceykic coṟkaḷ, aṟimukamāṉa coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip payaṉpaṭutti ivaṟṟit tayārikka vēṇṭum.  kīḻkkaṇṭavikaḷi ēṟṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷākak karutalām:
(1) paṭaṅkaḷum coṟkaḷum uḷḷa aṭṭikaḷ:  uṟaviṉar, vīṭṭup pirāṇikaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, maram ceṭi koṭikaḷ, vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ, paḷḷikkūṭap poruḷkaḷ, ūrtivakikaḷ, uṭaluṟuppukkaḷ ākiyavikaḷip paṟṟik kuḻantikaḷukkut terintuḷḷavaṟṟit tēnteṭuttu ovvoṉṟiyum viḷakkum paṭaṅkaḷit taṉittaṉiyāka varintu ovvoṉṟiṉ kīḻum ataṉataṉ peyarip periya eḻuttukkaḷil eḻuti aṭṭikaḷi āyattam ceytu koḷḷavēṇṭum.  paṭaṅkaḷiṉṟic coṟkaḷ maṭṭum eḻutappeṟṟa aṭṭikaḷiyum āyattam ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  toṭakka nilippaḷḷikku vantu cērnta kuḻanti kaḷuṭaṉ āciriyarkaḷ aḷavaḷāvi uṇarvoṟṟumi ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭa piṟaku paṭikkum payiṟciyit taralām; orumāta kālam ippayiṟciyi nīṭṭikkalām.  mutalil paṭamum collum cērntuḷḷa aṭṭiyikkoṇṭu collip paṭikkac ceytum, piṟaku colmāttiram eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷa aṭṭiyik koṇṭu colli māttiram niṉivu paṭuttip paṭikkumāṟum kuḻantikaṭkup payiṟci taravēṇṭum.  ivaṟṟil nalla payiṟci ēṟpaṭṭa piṟakutāṉ accup puttakaṅkaḷik koṭukka vēṇṭum.
(2) paṭaṅkaḷiyum peyarkaḷiyum iṇittal:  kuḻantikaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivukkuṭpaṭṭa poruḷkaḷiṉ paṭaṅkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷum taṉittaṉiyāka eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa aṭṭikaḷit tayār ceyyavēṇṭum.  inta aṭṭikaḷik kuḻantikaḷiṭam koṭuttup paṭattiṟkup poruttamāṉa peyarip poṟukkac ceyyavēṇṭum; peyarukkup porut tamāṉa paṭattip poṟukkavum takka payiṟcikaḷik koṭukkalām.
(3) mukavaric cīṭṭukkaḷ:  vakuppiluḷḷa kuḻantikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷit taṉittaṉiyāka aṭṭikaḷil eḻutit tayārittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  inta aṭṭittuṇṭukaḷi ovvoru kuḻantiyiṭamum koṭuttu avaṟṟi uriyavariṭam koṭukkac ceyyalām.  itit tapālkāraṉ viḷiyāṭṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.
(4) coṟṟoṭar aṭṭikaḷ:  coṟṟoṭar muṟippaṭi paṭippataṟkup paṭaṅkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum koṇṭa aṭṭi kaḷit tayārikkalām.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kutiri ōṭukiṉṟatu, pacu mēykiṉṟatu, vāttu nīntukiṉṟatu eṉpaṉapōṉṟavaṟṟi itaṟkup payaṉpaṭuttalām; paṭaṅkaḷic caṟṟu māṟṟi eḻutip paṉmic coṟṟoṭarkaḷākavum payaṉpaṭuttalām.  murukaṉ paṭikkiṉṟāṉ, kōvintaṉ pantu āṭukiṉṟāṉ, cīti kummi aṭikkiṉṟāḷ eṉpaṉapōṉṟa toṭarkaḷiyum paṭaṅkaḷ eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttalām.
(5) kaṭṭaḷi aṭṭikaḷ:  kuḻantikaḷ aṉṟāṭam ceyyakkūṭiya cila ciṟiya ceyalkaḷikkoṇṭa kaṭṭaḷit toṭarkaḷi aṭṭiyil periya eḻuttukkaḷil eḻuti acceyalkaḷi niṟivēṟṟac ceyyalām.  eḻuntiru, uṭkārntu koḷ, valap puṟam tirumpu, nērē cel, variciyākap pō eṉpaṉapōṉṟa toṭarkaḷik koṇṭa aṭṭikaḷit tayārittu avaṟṟik kuḻantikaḷiṭam miṉṉaṭṭikaḷip pōl kāṭṭi acceyalkaḷi niṟivēṟṟac ceyyalām.
(6) vakuppiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ, paḷḷiyiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷ, paḷḷittōṭṭam, viḷiyāṭumiṭam ākiyavaṟṟiluḷḷa poruḷ kaḷiṉ peyarkaḷip periya eḻuttukkaḷil aṭṭiyil allatu takarattil eḻuti apporuḷkaḷil toṅkaviṭa vēṇṭum.  kuḻantikaḷ apporuḷkaḷik kaṇṇuṟumpoḻutu accoṟ kaḷip paṭikkap paḻakuvar.
(7) aṟivippup palakiyilum, viḷamparap palakiyilum aṭikkaṭi mukkiya ceytikaḷi eḻuti avaṟṟip paṭikkum vāyppukkaḷ taralām.  nāḷi poṅkal paṇṭiki; paḷḷik kūṭam illi, nāḷi paḷḷi āṇṭu viḻā, nāḷi kānti piṟanta nāḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ceytikaḷukku ivviṭaṅkaḷi aḷikkalām.
(8) katip paṭaṅkaḷ:  ōrāṇṭil vakuppil kaṟpikkak kūṭiya irupatiṟkuk kuṟiyāta katip paṭaṅkaḷit tayār ceytuvikkalām.  ovvoru katikkum nāṉku allatu āṟu pakutikaḷāka viḷakkappaṭaṅkaḷ amiya vēṇṭum.  ovvoru paṭattiṉ kīḻum oṉṟiraṇṭu ciṟiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷ katiyi viḷakkum muṟiyil eḻutap peṟal vēṇṭum.
(9) paṭipputtāḷkaḷ:  mēlē kuṟippiṭṭapaṭi kuḻanti kaḷiṉ tuṇik koṇṭu oru paṭatti allatu katiyip paṟṟiya pala coṟkaḷ allatu coṟṟoṭarkaḷip periya tāḷkaḷil periya eḻuttukkaḷil eḻutittoṅka viṭṭu vikkalām.  avaṟṟip puttaṅkaḷukkup patilākap paṭip pataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttalām.
(10) tētip peṭṭi:  coṟkaḷum paṭaṅkaḷum eḻutiya kākitac curuḷit tētip peṭṭiyil amittu vikkalām.  peṭṭiyiṉ kippiṭiyic cuḻaṟṟumpoḻutu ovvoru collum paṭamum kuḻantikaḷukkut teriyum.  avaṟṟi avarkaḷ āvaluṭaṉ paṭippar.
(11) taṉip payiṟci aṭṭikaḷ:  taṉip payiṟci aṭṭi kaḷik koṭuttup paṭikkac ceyyumpoḻutu vakuppi āṟu pirivukaḷākap pirittu ovvoru pirivukkum ovvoru vaki aṭṭikaḷik koṭukkalām.  āṟu taṉip payiṟci aṭṭi kaḷāvaṉa:  (a) muṟi māṟṟi eḻutappeṟṟa coṟkaḷic cariyāṉa muṟiyil amittuc coṟṟoṭarkaḷākkutal; (ā) viṭu katikaḷ eḻuti avaṟṟip paṭittu viṭuvikkac ceytal; (i) viṭikaḷukku ēṟṟa viṉākkaḷiyum viṉākkaḷukku ēṟṟa viṭikaḷiyum poruttik kaṇṭupiṭittal; (ī) ovvoru variciyilum avvaricikkup poruttamillāta collip paṭittu nīkkic cariyāka vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, maraṅkaḷ, ūrtikaḷ mutaliya vakikaḷākap pirittal; (u) nēretirc coṟkaḷiyum, āṇpālukkup peṇpāṟ coṟkaḷiyum, orumikkup paṉmic coṟkaḷiyum, oru poruḷ kuṟitta pala coṟkaḷiyum, pala poruḷ kuṟitta oru coṟkaḷiyum amittal; (ū) cari, tavaṟu eṉṟa iruvakik kūṟṟukkaḷiyum amittuc cariyāṉavaṟṟik kaṇṭupiṭikkac ceytal.
paṭittalil tiṟami:  paṭittal vāḻkki muḻuvatum payaṉpaṭak kūṭiyatu.  ākavē, ellā nilip paḷḷikaḷilum paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ attiṟaṉi māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyē vaḷarkkavēṇṭum.  moḻiyāciriyarkaḷēyaṉṟi ellāppāṭa āciriyarkaḷum itil karuttic celuttip paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum.  ellāt tuṟikaḷilumuḷḷa coṟkaḷañciyam kuḻantikaḷiṭam perukiṉāltāṉ paṭittalil virivum cuviyum ēṟpaṭum;  atil oruvita uṟcākamum uṇṭākum.
vāyviṭṭup paṭittal, vāykkuḷ paṭittal eṉap paṭippil iraṇṭu nilikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  paṭittalil tiṟami uṇṭāka vēṇṭumāyiṉ inta iraṇṭu nilikaḷilum tiṟamiyi vaḷarkkavēṇṭum.  vāḻkkikku inta iraṇṭu nilip paṭippum vēṇṭum.  paṭippil virivi uṇṭākka vāykkuṭ paṭippiṟku mukkiyattuvam aḷikkappeṟṟirukkiṉṟatēyaṉṟi vāyviṭṭup paṭittali aṟavē nīkkavēṇṭum eṉṟa poruḷil aṉṟu.  inta iraṇṭu nilip paṭippiyum tēvikku ēṟṟavāṟu aḷavaṟintu paḷḷikaḷil māṇākkarkaḷiṭam vaḷarkka vēṇṭum.  iṉṟu paḷḷikaḷil inta iraṇṭiyum cariyāṉa muṟikaḷil vaḷarppatil takkavāṟu kavaṉam celuttappeṟavilli.


6.  vāyviṭṭup paṭittal

puttakattilirukkum eḻuttukkaḷik kaṇṇālpārttu vāyāl uccarittuc collip poruḷ uṇartaliyē vāyviṭṭup paṭittal, (oral reading) eṉkiṉṟōm. icceyalil mukkūṟukaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa; coṟkaḷik kaṇṇāṟ kāṇal, utaṭu, nākku1, aṇṇam, pal mutaliya oli uṟuppukaḷāl cīrpaṭa uccarittal, uḷḷattāṟ karuttuṇaral eṉpavi avi. inta mukkūṟukaḷiyum vāyviṭṭup paṭikkumpoḻutu teḷivākak kāṇalām. vāyviṭṭup paṭittalil tiṟami uṇṭāka vēṇṭu māyiṉ inta mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷilum taṉittaṉiyē tiṟami uṇṭāka vēṇṭum ; takka payiṟcikaḷāl ivaṟṟi naṉku vaḷarkkalām. toṭakkattil vāyviṭṭup paṭittaliyē naṉku vaḷarkka vēṇṭum. vāyviṭṭup paṭittalāltāṉ kuḻantikaḷ paṭippil tēviyāṉa aḷavu tiṟamiyaṭintuḷḷaṉarā eṉpatic cōtikkavum muṭiyum.
vāyviṭṭup paṭittaliṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi: vāy viṭṭup paṭittal iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷukkāka mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟatu. mutalāvatu : pēcuvataṟku atu peruntuṇi purikiṉṟatu. puttakattiliruntu pēcuvataṟkup pala coṟkaḷi eṭuttuk koḷḷukiṉṟōm. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, oru nāṭakatti naṭippatākak koḷvōm. nāṭakattil ettaṉip pāttiraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉarō attaṉi māṇakkarkaḷ atil paṅku eṭuttuk koḷḷa nēriṭum. appoḻutu, ovvoruvarum iyalpākap pēcuvatil nalla payiṟciyiṉip peṟuvar. an nāṭakam neṭṭuṟac ceyyappeṟṟu naṭikkappeṟum poḻutu māṇakkar peṟum vāymoḻip payiṟciyiṉ aḷavu atikamākum. naṭippatil uṇṭākum paḻakkam vēṟu katikaḷi nāṭakamākkinaṭikkavum, eḷiya uriyāṭalkaḷi āyattam ceyyavum tūṇṭum. itaṉāl iṉṉum nalla vāymoḻip payiṟci ēṟpaṭuvataṟku vāyppu uṇṭākum. iraṇṭāvatu : vāykkuṭ paṭittalil celuttappeṟum kavaṉam vāyviṭṭup paṭittalukku naṉmi payakkiṉṟatu. vāyviṭṭup paṭittalil peṟum payiṟcikku ēṟṟavāṟu vāykkuṭ paṭittalil nalla palaṉik kāṇalām. atil payiṟci peṟum māṇakkarkaḷ itil mikka tiṟaṉuṭaṉ tikaḻvar. aṉṟiyum, ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukku mutaṉmutalil vāyviṭṭup paṭittalē iyalpākavum eḷitākavum irukkum ; appaṭip paṭittaliṉālēyē avarkaḷ karutti uṇarkiṉṟaṉar. vaḷarntavarkaḷukkum vāyviṭṭup paṭittaliṉāl tāṉ ōci nayattiyum ceyyuḷ iṉpattiyum cuviyiyum naṉku aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu. uṇarcciyiyum uḷac cuviyiyum taṭṭi yeḻuppu vataṟku ippaṭippu tuṇiceykiṉṟatu. peruṅ kūṭṭattil kūccamiṉṟip pēcap paḻakavum itu vākkuvaṉmiyiyaḷik kiṉṟatu. vāyviṭṭup paṭittal eṉpatu puttakattuṭaṉ pēcutalē yākum; pēcuvataṟku vēṇṭiya coṟkaḷip puttakam tarum. aṉṟiyum, karuttōṭṭattiṟkum kuṟivu irātu; karuttuk kaḷiyum puttakamē tantu utavukiṉṟatu.
iḷañciṟārkaḷiṭam nalla uccarippi vaḷarkkavum, niṟuttavēṇṭiya iṭattil niṟuttip paṭippatil payiṟci taravum ip paṭippu utavukiṉṟatu. cariyāṉa muṟiyil teḷivāṉa vāymoḻip payiṟci taruvataṟku vāyviṭṭup paṭittal perun tuṇiyāka uḷḷatu. māṇakkar paṭikkum tiṟaṉi aḷantaṟiyavum iḵtu utavukiṉṟatu. cilar toṭakka niliyil maṭṭilum vāyviṭṭup paṭittal tēvi yeṉṟum, piṟkālap paṭippil appayiṟci tēviyilli yeṉṟum tavaṟākak karutukiṉṟaṉar. muṟkālattil kaṟpittal vāyviṭṭup paṭittaliltāṉ toṭaṅkiyatu. nāḷaṭivil vāykkuṭ paṭittal celvākku aṭiyavē, iti aṭiyōṭu puṟakkaṇittaṉar. mutaliraṇṭu vakuppuk kaṭkumēl iti vaḷarppatil muyaṟci eṭuppatilli. itaṉ viḷivāka coṟkaḷit tavaṟāka uṇartal, eḻuttup piḻikaḷ, teḷivaṟṟa pēccu mutaliya kuṟikaḷ malivākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
vāyviṭṭup paṭittal ēṟpaṭṭa kāraṇaṅkaḷ: vāy viṭṭup paṭittal ēṟpaṭṭa kāraṇaṅkaḷi oruvāṟu ūkittu aṟiyalām. varivaṭivaṅkaḷi oli vaṭivaṅkaḷāka aṭiyāḷam aṟintu ataṉ tiruttatti uṇarvataṟkum, taṉivaṭivaṅkaḷik kūṭṭic coṟkaḷāka olittaṟkum, paṭittavarkaḷ ēṭṭiliruntu paṭippatip paṭikkat teriyātavarkaḷ toṭarntu aṟiyavum, paṭikkum pakutikaḷi maṉappāṭam ceyyavum vāyviṭṭup paṭittal utaviyirukkak kūṭum. mutal iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷum paṭippukku aṭippaṭiyāka eṉṟum aḻiyāmal iruppavi. ēṉiya iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷum iṉṟupōl accup puttakaṅkaḷ perukiyirātatālum, perumpālāṉa makkaḷ kalviyaṟivu illā tirunta paḻaṅkālattil paṭittavarkaḷ tam paṭippip piṟarukku utavum poruṭṭuk kikkoṇṭa muṟikaḷ eṉavum aṟiya vēṇṭum. inta iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷum iṉṟu eṅkeṅkē ententa aḷavukku iṉṉum irukkiṉṟaṉavō antanta aḷavukku vāyviṭṭup paṭittalil paḷḷi māṇakkarkaḷukkup payiṟcit tēviyākum.
vāyviṭṭup paṭittaliṉ paṇpukaḷ : vāyviṭṭup paṭittal vāykkuṭ paṭittaliviṭa kaṭiṉamāṉa ceyalākum. vāykkuṭ paṭittaṟku vēṇṭiya tiṟaṉkaḷuṭaṉ vēṟu pala tiṟaṉkaḷum itaṟkut tēviyāka uḷḷaṉa. vāyviṭṭup paṭittalil māṇakkarkaḷ coṟkaḷi aṟital, paḻakkamillāta coṟkaḷi uyttuṇartal, karuttuṇartal, coṟkaḷiṉ uccarippu3 avaṟṟi oru kuṟippiṭṭa muṟiyil veḷiyiṭal, puttakatti vittukkoṇṭu etiriliruppavarkaḷik kūcca miṉṟip pārttal mutaliyavi atikamāka vēṇṭappaṭupavi. vāykkuṭ paṭittalil putiya coṟkaḷic camāḷikkum nili vēṟu; vāyviṭṭup paṭittalil avaṟṟic camāḷikkum nili vēṟu. vāyviṭṭup paṭittalil avi cila camayam paṭippiyē niṟutti viṭavum kūṭum; poruḷ teriyātu mēlē celluvatum kaṭiṉam. vāykkuṭ paṭittalil innili ēṟpaṭiṉum veḷiyil teriyātu. eṉavē pala kūṟukaḷil vāyviṭṭup paṭittal kaṭiṉamāṉatu.
vāyviṭṭup paṭittal paṭippōrip pala caṅkaṭaṅkaḷuk kuḷḷākkum; palarukkumuṉ aviyil vantuniṟpatu kaṭiṉam tāṉ. paṭṭaṟivu kivarappeṟṟa vaḷarntavarkaḷum accattuṭaṉum naṭukkattuṭaṉum iruppatip pārkkat tāṉ ceykiṉṟōm.  avarkaḷ cātāraṇap pakutikaḷiyē pala tavaṟukaḷuṭaṉ paṭikkiṉṟaṉar. paḻakkappaṭāta coṟkaḷ payiṉṟu varutal, kaṇ cāṇiṉ kuṟivu. kūṭṭattiṉ muṉṉar vantuniṟkum paḻakkamiṉmi mutaliyavi kuḻantikaḷiṭam uḷḷak kiḷarcciyi uṇṭākki viṭum. 
mutal ottikiyāl coṟkaḷil paḻakkam, karuttuṇarvu mutaliyavi ēṟpaṭiṉum, uccarippu, veḷiyiṭuntiṟaṉ, puttakattip paṭittukkoṇṭu palarmuṉ niṟkum nili mutaliyavaṟṟil ciramam irukkattāṉ ceyyum. palavita payiṟcikaḷāl ciramaṅkaḷik kuṟikkalām; paṭip paṭiyāka avikkūccattiyum pōkki viṭalām.
vāyviṭṭup paṭittali vaḷarkkum vāyppukaḷ : paḷḷi vāḻkkiyil vāyviṭṭup paṭittali vaḷarppataṟkup pala vāyppukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kūṭṭattil paṭippataṟku muṉṉar cariyāṉa ottiki tarappeṟutal vēṇṭum. paḷḷiyil naṭipeṟum vāṉolip pēccu, ceytikaḷip paṭittal, nāṭakattiṟku muṉṉuri paṭittal, coṟpōril kalantu koḷḷutal pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷāl māṇakkarkaṭku vāyviṭṭup paṭittalil nalla vāyppukaḷit taralām. makkaḷāṭciyil ciṟanta kuṭimakaṉukku vāyviṭṭup paṭittaliṉ tiṟaṉ mika mika iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.
paḷḷiyil uṇarcciyuṭaṉ vāyviṭṭup paṭikkun tiṟaṉ ellōriṭamum amiyātu. itaṟkut taṉippaṭṭa payiṟci aḷikkappeṟal vēṇṭum. itil tiṟaṉ illāta varkaḷi vaṟpuṟuttāmal avarkaḷ taṉmik kēṟṟa vēṟu ceyalkaḷil ūkkuvittiṭalām.


7. vāykkuḷ paṭittal 
iṉṟiya vāḻkkiyil atikamākat tēviyuḷḷatu vāykkuṭ paṭippu. ōlic cuvaṭikaḷ, kiccuvaṭikaḷ, accu nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi maṉattiṟkuḷḷēyē, vāytiṟavāmal, utaṭukaḷ aciyāmal, toṇṭiyiṉ acivukūṭa illāmal, olittal uccarippu iṉṟi muṇu muṇuppukūṭa illāmal, paṭittalittāṉ vāykkuṭ paṭittal4 (Silent reading) eṉkiṉṟōm. ivvitap paṭippi mika aṇmiyiltāṉ mukkiyameṉak karutalāyiṉar. ceṉṉi mānilattil 1939-il veḷiyāṉa toṭakka nilippaḷḷi moḻippāṭat tiṭṭattil tāṉ itu kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. eṉṟālum, itu ciṟitum kavaṉam peṟavilli eṉṟu colluvataṟkilli. eṅkō oru cila iṭaṅkaḷil atti pūttamātiri āciriyarkaḷ itil kavaṉam celutti vantaṉar; cila māṇakkarkaḷ tāmākavē itaṉi vaḷarttuk koṇṭaṉar. palar iti vaḷarttuk koḷḷāmal viṭṭatāl, vāḻkkiyil ētāvatu ceytiyip paṭikka nēriṭum poḻutum, ceytit tāḷkaḷip paṭikka nēriṭum poḻutum vāyviṭṭup paṭittāltāṉ karuttuṇarum niliyiluḷḷaṉar. iṉṟu tāymoḻiyil maṟumalarcci ilakkiyaṅkaḷ atikamākak tōṉṟi varuvatāl, paḷḷiyil immuṟiyil payiṟci tarāviṭṭālum, māṇakkarkaḷiṭam ippaḻakkam tāṉāka vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu.
vāykkuṭ paṭittaliṉ avaciyam : iṉṟiya ulakil puttakaṅkaḷum, kaṭṭurikaḷum, veḷiyīṭukaḷum, ceytit tāḷkaḷum, piṟavum kaṭalpōl perukik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. kalvi kariyila; kaṟpavar nāḷ cila` eṉpatu kaṇantōṟum niṉivukku vantu koṇṭuḷḷatu. `kaṟṟatu kimaṇṇaḷavu; kallātatu ulakaḷavu eṉpati ellōrum uṇarkiṉṟaṉar. iviyaṉit tiyum vāy viṭṭup paṭittuṇartal eṉpatu iyalāta ceyal. itil muyaṟci, kālaccelavukaḷ uṇṭāvatuṭaṉ, cōrvum ēṟpaṭum; karuttuṇartalum taṭippaṭum. paṭippataṉ nōkkam karuttuṇartalē yātaliṉ, ati eḷitil eytuvikkum vāykkuṭ paṭippē itaṟkup peruntuṇi purikiṉṟatu; virivum virivum aminta ivvakip paṭippiṉāltāṉ karuttuṇartal eḷitil kivarap peṟukiṉṟatu.
meyyuṇarvuk kalviyilum iṟivaṉōṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalakkat tuṇiceyyum mōṉa niliyilum pēccu nili yiliruntu pēccaṟṟa ñāṉanilikkum pōkum paṭikaḷ iruppatik kāṇalām. 
	kallāliṉ puṭiyamarntu nāṉmaṟiā
	ṟaṅkamutal kaṟṟa kēḷvi
vallārkaḷ nālvarukkum vākkiṟanta
	pūraṇamāy maṟikkap pālāy
ellāmāy allatumāy iruntataṉi
	iruntappaṭi iruntu kāṭṭic
collāmal coṉṉavari 5 
eṉṟa aṭikaḷiṉāl piṟicūṭiya piññakaṉ caṉakāti muṉivarkku ñāṉaniliyip pēcāmal kuṟippitta ceyti aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. meyyuṇarvu peṟṟa tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷum kaṇmūṭi muṉiyākic cummā irukka niṉippatiyē tam kuṟik kōḷ eṉa iṟivaṉ tirumuṉ muṟiyiṭṭa ceytiyiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. ivaṟṟāl ulaka uṇarvuk kalviyilum vāykkuṭ paṭippē niṟinta payaṉittarum eṉṟu aṟiya vēṇṭum.
vāykkuṭ paṭittaliṉ toṭakkam : vāykkuṭ paṭittalik kīḻ vakuppukkaḷiliruntē paṭippaṭiyāy nuḻikkalām. mutal vakuppil perumpālum vāyviṭṭup paṭittalē naṭimuṟiyil irukkum. iraṇṭāvatu vakuppiliruntu vāykkuṭ paṭittalit toṭaṅka vēṇṭum. nāṟpatu catavikitam iraṇṭām vakuppilum, aṟupatu catavikitam mūṉṟām vakuppilum, ataṟku mēl iṉṉum ciṟitu ciṟitāka mēl vakuppukkaḷilum itaṉi vaḷarkkalām. nāṉkām vakuppil utaṭṭacivik kuṟittu, vāyviṭṭuk kūṟuvati aṟavē nīkka vēṇṭum. aintām vakuppil caṟṟēṟat tāḻap periyavarkaḷippōlavē ciṟitum olikkāmalum utaṭṭi acikkāmalum paṭikkum paḻakkatti uṇṭākka muṉiya vēṇṭum. itic cila moḻip payiṟcikaḷāl eytuvikkalām.
vāykkuṭ paṭittaliṉāl viḷiyum naṉmikaḷ : pēccuk karuvikaḷukku vēliyillātatāl uṭaṟcōrvu ēṟpaṭātu. kaṇṇum maṉamum maṭṭilumē ceyaṟpaṭuvatāl, paṭikkum poruḷkaḷil atikak kavaṉam cellum; itaṉāl paṭippu virintu celvatuṭaṉ karuttuṇartalilum āṟṟal vaḷarum. kuṟinta kālaaḷavil atikamāṉa pakutikaḷip paṭikkalām. itil cintaṉi, kaṟpaṉi. nalla maṉappayiṟci mutaliya paṇpukaḷ vaḷara iṭamuṇṭu; aṭuttiruppōrukku iṭiyūṟāka illāmal paṭikkavum iyalum. itaṉāl ellā māṇakkarkaḷum orē camayattil kavaṉam celuttip paṭikka iyalum. iṉṟiya vakuppup payiṟṟalil itutāṉ mēṟ koḷḷap peṟa vēṇṭiyatoṉṟu; mikavum iṉṟiyami yātatuṅkūṭa. itaṉāl taṉmuyaṟci atikam tūṇṭappeṟṟu taṉṉampikkiyum vaḷarum. muṟpōkkāṉa māṇakkarkaḷum tattam tiṟamik kēṟṟavāṟu taṉit taṉiyākap paṭikka itil vāyppu uṇṭu. ilakkiyac cuvikaḷi uṇarntu iṉpuṟṟup paṭippatuṭaṉ, uyarnilik karutti uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ aṟintu koḷḷavum itil vāyppu uḷḷatu. paṭippil ārvattiyum paṟṟiyum uṇṭākkip paṭikkum paḻakkattiyum pāṅkuṭaṉ vaḷarkkum. ip paṭippu muṉṉirunta coṟ kaḷañciyatti uṟuti ceyvatuṭaṉ atip perukkavum tuṇipurikiṉṟatu.
itiluḷḷa cila kuṟikaḷ : vāykkuṭ paṭittalil kural payiṟcikkum uccarippil tiruttam peṟuvataṟkum vāyppukaḷ illi. uccarippup piḻikaḷik kēṭṭaṟintu avaṟṟik kaḷiya āciriyarukku vāyppu itil illi. moḻip pāṭattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta vāymoḻip payiṟcikku iṅku iṭam illi. naṭittal, uriyāṭal, pēccu, coṟpoḻivukaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟil payiṟci aḷikka ip paṭippu tuṇi ceyyātu. taṉiyāka maṉattiṟ kuḷḷēyē paṭippatāl ceyyuḷip paṭittu iṉpuṟu vataṟkum, iṭattiṟkēṟṟa, cuvikkēṟṟa naṭiyikkēṭṭu iṉpuṟu vataṟkum iṅku iṭam illi. ceyyuḷi oppuvikkum vāyppi itaṉāl aṭiyamuṭiyātu.
virivāṉa paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka iruppavi : virivāṉa paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka irukkum kāraṇaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ mūlattiyum kaṇṭaṟital kaṭiṉam. paṭittal mikac cikkalāṉa kūṟukaḷiyuṭiyatu ; akkūṟukaḷi aḷakkum muṟikaḷum teḷivaṟṟaṉavāka uḷḷaṉa. kalvi nipuṇarkaḷum uḷaviyaṟ kaliñarkaḷum kīḻkkāṇpaṉavaṟṟi paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka iruppavi eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar :
(i) aṟitiṟaṉukkum  (Intelligence) paṭippiṟkum toṭarpu iruppatākak karutappeṟukiṉṟatu. aṟitiṟaṉ īvu kuṟiyak kuṟiya paṭippu meḷḷap pōkum eṉṟum aḵtu uyara uyara paṭippum virivākac cellum eṉṟum, kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
(ii) ikkālattil kuḻantikaḷukkut taṉit taṉiyākak kaṟpikkum vāyppu illi; oru vakuppil palarukkuc cērntāṟ pōl kaṟpikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl ovvoru kuḻantiyiṉ tēviyi āciriyar aṟintu koḷḷa iyaluvatilli; avarkaṭku ep pakuti viḷaṅkavilli eṉpati avar terintu koḷḷa muṭivatumilli. itaṉālum paṭipput taṭipaṭalām.
(iii) uṭalil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa cila kōḷāṟukaḷāl paṭippu taṭipaṭakkūṭum eṉṟum karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṇkaḷil cātāraṇa mākak kāṇappaṭum tūrappārvi, pakkappārvi, uruṭcip piḻi (Astigmatism) niṟak kuruṭu (Colour blindness) pōṉṟa kuṟikaḷālum, toṇṭic cati (Tonsils), mūkkaṭic cati (Adenoids) pōṉṟa cati vaḷarcciyāl pēccuṟuppukaḷil nēriṭum kōḷāṟukaḷālum, kātukaḷil uḷḷa kuṟiyālum maṭṭilumē taṭi ēṟpaṭum eṉṟu karutalāyiṉar. aṇmiyil mūḷiyil ēṟpaṭakkūṭiya cila kōḷāṟukaḷālum it taṭi nēriṭalām eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar. kuḻanti karuvil irukkumpoḻutu tāykku ēṟpaṭum kāyccalālum, piṟakkum poḻutu maruttuvakkaruvikaḷ kiyāḷumpaṭi nēriṭṭāl avaṟṟālum, piṟantapiṟaku kuḻantikku varum kāyccalālum mūḷiyiṉ cila pakutikaḷ pātikkap peṟum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya nīkkakkūṭiya kuṟipāṭukaḷit takka maruttuva muṟikaḷāl nīkka vēṇṭum.
(iv) uṭalil ēṟpaṭum vēṟu cila kōḷāṟukaḷum paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka irukkalām eṉṟu karutappeṟukiṉṟatu. cila kuḻanti kaḷukku iṭakki naṉku ceyaṟpaṭum. iṭakkaṇ naṉku ceyaṟpaṭum. uṇmiyāka ivi kuṟipāṭukaḷ alla veṉiṉum, paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu cōtaṉikaḷ   mūlam meyppikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ik kuṟipāṭukaḷi pakka ātikkam (Lateral dominance) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar.
(v) maṉattiṉ cila kuṟipāṭukaḷum paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu cōtaṉikaḷāl meyppikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. paṭippil cila kuḻantikaḷukkuk kavaṉa millātiruttal, cila kuḻantikaḷ kavaṉam celutta iyalātiruttal pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟ kellām uḷaviyaṟ kaliñarkaḷ kūṟum kāraṇaṅkaḷ oppuk koḷḷattakkavi. takka nipuṇarkaḷik koṇṭu cila kuṟippāṭukaḷik kaḷintuviṭalām.
(vi) uṭalilum maṉattilum nalla vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭuvataṟku muṉpu kuḻantikaḷukkup paṭippik kaṟpittalum paṭippukkut taṭiyāka uḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iḷamparuvattil uṭalilum maṉattilum ciṟuvarkaḷi viṭac ciṟumikaḷukku virivil mutircci ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. pārvi, kēḷvi mutaliya pulaṉkaḷil mutircci ēṟpaṭāta poḻutu paṭippu kaṭṭāyam taṭippaṭattāṉ ceyyum.
(vii) kuṟivāṉa paṭṭaṟivum paṭikkum niliyi virivil uṇṭākkuvatilli. paḷḷikku varuvataṟkumuṉ cila kuḻanti kaḷukku uriyāṭal, kati kēṭṭal, paṭaṅkaḷiyum paṭap puttaṅkaḷiyum kāṇal, piṟa kuḻantikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiyirukkum vāyppu mutaliyavaṟṟili paṭṭaṟivu mikak kuṟivāka iruppatāl paṭikkum viruppam mikavum tāmatappaṭṭē eḻukiṉṟatu. ik kuḻantikaḷ paṭippil virivākac cella muṭivatilli.
(viii) paṭippil takka ārvam ūṭṭat tavaṟiṉāl atuvum paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka irukkum eṉṟu karutap paṭukiṉṟatu. kuḻanti vāḻvil oruvita nōkkamumiṉṟip paṭippit toṭaṅkiṉāl atu vaḷarcci peṟātu. tavaṟāṉa muṟiyil ārvam ūṭṭiṉālum kēṭu payakkum eṉṟu uḷaviyaṟ kaliñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. ataṟkup pala eṭuttuk kāṭṭuk kaḷiyum tarukiṉṟaṉar. oru ciṟuvaṉ paṭittuk koṇṭirunta poḻutu avaṉiviṭa vayatukkuṟivuḷḷa iraṇṭu ciṟumikaḷ paṭippil avaṉ ceyta vaḻukkaḷic cuṭṭi nakiyāṭiyataṉ kāraṇamāka acciṟuvaṉ paṭippil veṟuppu kāṭṭiṉāṉām; oru kuḻanti tāṉāka maṉattiṟkuḷ paṭittuk koṇṭirunta poḻutu avaṉuṭiya aṉṉi avaṉi aḻittu urakkap paṭittuk kāṭṭa atiliruntu akkuḻanti paṭippataṟkē maṟuttāṉām kuḻantikaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷiyaṟintu ārva mūṭṭiṉāl tāṉ paṭippil nalla palaṉik kāṇamuṭiyum.
(ix) paṭittaliluḷḷa pala nuṭpat tiṟaṉkaḷ (Techniques) kivarappeṟāmiyālum avaṟṟi virivil kaṟṟukkoḷḷa muṉiyātatālum paṭippil taṭi ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ paṭittalip pākupāṭu ceytu palvēṟu nuṭpat tiṟaṉkaḷik kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar. avaṟṟuḷ oru kuḻantiyiṭam illātaṉavaṟṟi eytuvittalum, cemmiyuṟā tiruppaṉavaṟṟic cemmiyuṟac ceytum, tiripaṭintuḷ- ḷaṉavaṟṟi nēriyaṉa vākkiyum paṭittalil virivi uṇṭākkalām.
(x) payiṟṟum muṟikaḷiluḷḷa tavaṟukaḷiṉālum paṭippil taṭi ēṟpaṭakkūṭum. nilikku mīṟiya puttakaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttal, uṟcākamaṟṟa poruḷkaḷik kaṟpittal, kuḻantikaḷiṉ iyalpāṉa ārvattic citikkakkūṭiya muṟikaḷik kiyāḷal, collilak kaṇattilum piṟavaṟṟilum avaciyamaṟṟapaṭi karuttic celuttutal, tēviyaṟṟa moḻip payiṟci tarutal, toṭakkattil eḷitākavuḷḷa puttakaṅkaḷip payaṉ paṭuttāmi, paṭittalil iṭamiruntu valamākap pōka vēṇṭum eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vaḻikaḷik kāṭṭāmi pōṉṟa pala kūṟukaḷ paṭippiṟkut taṭiyāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.
iṉṉum vēṟu kāraṇaṅkaḷum kūṟappaṭakiṉṟaṉa. viḻippuṭaṉum karuttuṭaṉum paṇiyāṟṟum āciriyarkaḷ ikkuṟikaḷil perumpālāṉavaṟṟi eḷitil kāṇakkūṭum. kuṟipāṭukaḷiṉ kāratti aṟiyakkūṭāviṭiṉum kuṟipāṭu kaḷiyāvatu kaṭṭāyam kāṇamuṭiyum. tammāl kaḷiya muṭiyāta kuṟikaḷiyuṭiya kuḻantikaḷit takka nipuṇarkaḷiṭam aṉuppi āciriyar avaṟṟik kaḷiya vaḻitēṭalām.
vāykkuṭ paṭippiṉ mukkiya kūṟukaḷ : vāykkuṭ paṭittalil pala mukkiya kūṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ovvoru kūṟilum nalla tiṟaṉi eytiṉāltāṉ ivvakip paṭippil niṟinta payaṉi etirpārkka muṭiyum. uṟuppukkaḷil acivu illāmi, virivāṉa kaṇ nakarcci, piṟpōkkukaḷum niṟuttalkaḷum kuṟital, nērmiyāṉa kaṇpāyccal, nalla kaṇpārvi, karuttuṇarum āṟṟal coṟkaḷañciyap perukkam ākiyavi vāykkuṭ paṭippiṉ mukkiyak kūṟukaḷākum. ikkūṟukaḷi iraṇṭu pirivukaḷākap pirikkalām. oṉṟu, karuttuṇarum āṟṟal; maṟṟoṉṟu, vēkam. ivviru pirivukaḷilum takka kavaṉam celutti avaṟṟiṟkuriya pala nuṇṇiya tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarppataṟku vēṇṭiya payiṟcikaḷit tantāl vāykkuṭ paṭittalil tiṟaṉ nalla muṟiyil vaḷarum. ivaṟṟic caṟṟu virivāka ārāyvōm. 
3. karuttuṇarum āṟṟal
itu vāykkuṭpaṭittalilum vāyviṭṭup paṭittalilum mukkiyamāka vēṇṭappaṭuvatu. paḷḷi vāḻkkiyilum ulaka vāḻkkiyilum itu kivarappeṟuvataṟkākattāṉ palavita upāyaṅkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ, paṭippiṉ uyir nāṭi itutāṉ. oruvar pēcuvatik kavaṉittō acciṭṭa pakutiyip paṭittō aṟintu koḷvatutāṉ karuttuṇartal eṉṟum, ellōrum ati aṟivatu eḷitu eṉṟum ōreṇṇam nilavi varukiṉṟatu. tārṉṭik eṉṟa kalvi nipuṇar avvāṟu karutuvatu tavaṟu eṉṟu meyppittuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. karuttuṇartalil oru karuttu allatu oru cila karuttukkaḷi maṭṭilum poṟukkuvatil māttiram maṉattiṉ ceyal niṉṟu viṭuvatilli; mikac cikkalāṉa muṟiyil maṉam ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. oru kuṟippiṭṭa pakutiyip palar paṭittāl, ovvoruvarum ovvoru vitamāka ati aṟikiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟu aṟivatu avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉiya paṭṭaṟivu, maṉattil patiyum tiṟaṉ, paṭikkumpoḻutu maṉam viḻippuṭaṉirukkum aḷavu ākiya pala nilikaḷip poṟuttuḷḷatu. eṉavē, karuttuṇartal eṉpatu mikac cikkalāṉa oru tiṟaṉ; aḵtu eppoḻutum kuṟivuṭivatu; niṟivu peṟātu; tērnteṭukkum iyalpuṭiyatu.
paṭittalum kēṭṭalum vīṇāṉa ceyalkaḷ alla. ētāvatu oru nōkkattik koṇṭutāṉ avi mēṟ koḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. tārṉṭik eṉpār karuttuppaṭi paṭittal eṉpatu cintittal. ovvoruvarum tām tām karutukiṉṟapaṭitāṉ paṭittali mēṟkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar ; paṭikkum muṟiyum tēvik kēṟṟāṟpōl māṟum. eṉavē, karuttuṇarum āṟṟalum tēvik kēṟṟāṟpōl māṟak kūṭiyatē. oru katiyiṉ pōkki aṟiyavō oru kuṟippiṭṭa piracciṉiyiṉ mukkiya kūṟiṉi aṟiyavō paṭittal mēṟkoḷḷap peṟalām. cila kuṟippiṭṭa vivaraṅkaḷi aṟivataṟkum, oru cila kuṟippukkaḷi aṟivataṟkum, oru nūli matippiṭuvataṟkum, paṭikkum pakutiyic curuṅkak kūṟuvataṟkum, niṉivāṟṟali vaḷarttaṟkum ākiya ivipōṉṟa palvēṟu nōkkaṅkaḷukkākap paṭippu mēṟkoḷḷappeṟukiṉṟatu; ovvoṉṟilum nōkkatti yoṭṭik karuttuṇarum tiṟaṉum māṟum. ivvāṟu tēvik kēṟṟavāṟu vevvēṟu aḷavil payaṉ paṭakkūṭiya karuttuṇarum tiṟaṉil māṇākkarkaḷ nalla payiṟci aṭiyavēṇṭum.
karuttuṇarum tiṟaṉ kivarappeṟa vēṇṭumāṉāl, kuḻantikaḷiṉ coṟkaḷañciyam perukavēṇṭum. kuḻantikaḷiṉ coṟ kaḷañciyattip perukkum vaḻikaḷ piṉṉark kūṟap peṟum. coṟkaḷañciyak kuṟiviṉāl karuttuṇartal taṭip paṭum. kuṟivāṉa aṟivuniliyum karuttuṇartalil piṟpōkku niliyi viḷivikkum. pattikaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum paṭikkum nuṭpat tiṟaṉkaḷiṉ kuṟiviṉālum, iṭattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷuṇartalil kavaṉam celuttātu coṟkaḷi aṟivatilēyē mukkiyattuvam taruvataṉālum karuttuṇartal pātikkappeṟum. kīḻk kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷa upāyaṅkaḷit tēvik kēṟṟavāṟu vāyppukaḷ varuṅkāl kiyāṇṭu karuttuṇartalil tiṟamiyi vaḷarkkalām. vakuppu muṟiyil kaṟpittalukku ivi yōcaṉikaḷēyaṉṟi muṭinta muṭivukaḷalla.
(1) kuḻantikkup paṭikkak koṭukkappeṟum puttakaṅkaḷ avarkaḷukku ārvamūṭṭakkūṭiyavikaḷākavum, karutti īrkka vallavi kaḷākavum iruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. ari nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉatākak katikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum puttakaṅkaḷ mūlamāka maṭṭiluntāṉ aṭiya muṭintatu. iṉṟu avaṟṟi vāṉoli, pēcum paṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa piṟa cātaṉaṅ kaḷālum peṟa muṭikiṉṟatu. eṉavē, āciriyarkaḷ tammuṭiya aṟivut tiṟaṉiyellām payaṉpaṭuttip puttakaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam pala nalla ceytikaḷip peṟa muṭiyum eṉṟu kuḻantikaṭkut teḷivupaṭuttavēṇṭum; teḷivu paṭuttuvatuṭaṉ niṉṟu viṭātu ataṟkeṉat taṉik kavaṉattic celuttip pala nallaputtakaṅkaḷit tērnteṭukkavēṇṭum. metuvākap paṭippavarkaḷukku uṟcākam, putuppōkku, nakiccuvi vīram mutaliya karutti īrkkum paṇpukaḷaṭaṅkiya ceytikaḷik koṇṭa puttakaṅkaḷit tērnteṭuttup paṭikkum vāyppukaḷi nalkavēṇṭum.
(2) toṭakkattiliruntē kuḻantikaḷip palvēṟu vakip paṭippukkaḷil naṉku payiṟṟa vēṇṭum. iyalpākavē kuḻantikaḷ katikaḷip paṭikka viḻivar. ivaṟṟit tavira, nāḷaṭivil payaṉpaṭum takavalkaḷiyuṭiya nūlkaḷ, naṭi muṟiyil vāḻkkiyil payaṉ peṟattakka poruḷkaḷik kūṟum nūlkaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ, pirayāṇa nūlkaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ, nāṭṭu vaḷaṅkaḷ nakic cuvi ākiyavaṟṟit tarum nūlkaḷ, eḷiya muṟiyil eḻutappeṟṟa aṟiviyal nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi yellām paṭippatil cuviyi eḻuppi viṭutal vēṇṭum. iṉṟiya niliyil tamiḻilum kuḻantikaḷukkeṉap pala nalla itaḻkaḷ veḷivaruvatāl, avi iḷam māṇākkarkaḷukkuk kiṭṭumpaṭi ceytal vēṇṭum. kavarccitarum puttaṅkaḷiyum itaḻkaḷiyum tērnteṭuppatilum, palvēṟu vitamāṉavaṟṟip poṟukki eṭuppatilumtāṉ āciriyarkaḷiṉ tiṟamiyik kāṇamuṭiyum; atiyoṭṭitāṉ veṟṟiyum aṭiyamuṭiyum.
(3) kuḻantikaḷiṉ taṉi ārvattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu puttakaṅkaḷip poṟukka vēṇṭum. ovvoru kuḻantikkum ovvoru poruḷil ārvam irukkum. cila kuḻantikaḷ vimāṉaṅkaḷi virumpalām; cila kuḻantikaḷ payirttoḻilkaḷi viḻiyalām; cila kuḻantikaḷukkuk kittoḻilkaḷil nāṭṭam cellum; iṉṉum cilar ilakkiyavaki nūlkaḷil āci koḷvar; cilar aṟiviyal puttaṅkaḷi virumpakkūṭum. ivaṟṟik kuṟikkum puttakaṅkaḷik kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṟivu nilikkēṟṟavāṟu tērnteṭuttu avarkaḷukkuk kiṭṭumpaṭi ceytalvēṇṭum. āṅkilattil arumiyāṉa nūlkaḷ pala vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷāl viḷakkam tarappeṟṟu ērāḷamāka veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa; veḷivantu koṇṭumirukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻil ammātiri nūlkaḷ iṉittāṉ veḷivaravēṇṭum.* nalla nūlkaḷi eḻutuvataṟkum āṭkaḷ illi; eḻutiṉālum veḷiyiṭuvōr avaṟṟip patippikka muṉvaruvatilli. eḻutuvōrum veḷiyiṭuvōrum kalviyip paṟṟi nalla karuttukkaḷi aṟintiruntāltāṉ, ittuṟi naṉmuṟiyil vaḷaramuṭiyum.
(4) paṭittavaṟṟi māṇākkarkaḷ kalantu pēcuvataṟkum, veḷiyiṭuvataṟkum, piṟa muṟikaḷil uṭaṉē payaṉpaṭuttu vataṟkum paḷḷiyil vāyppukaḷi ēṟpaṭutta vēṇṭum. ivvita vāyppukkaḷiṉāl iyalpākap paṭikkum māṇākkarkaḷukku uṟcākam ēṟpaṭṭu iṉṉum atikamākap paṭikka muṉivar; paṭippatil nāṭṭam celuttā maṇākkarkaḷum atil nāṭṭam celutta iṭam uṇṭu. ilakkiyak kaḻakaṅkaḷ, piṟa kaḻakaṅkaḷ, paḷḷi itaḻkaḷ, ceytikaḷ tarutal pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil vāyppukkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttalām. tavira, vāṉoliyil pala kavarccikaramāṉa nikaḻccikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttuvatippōlavē paḷḷikaḷilum avvita nikaḻccikaḷi ēṟpaṭutti oru tiṭṭap paṭi oru kuṟippiṭṭa nāḷil avaṟṟi oli perukkiyiṉ mūlam oli parappalām; ellā māṇākkarkaḷum avaravarkaḷ vakuppu aṟikaḷiliruntu koṇṭum, viḷiyāṭum tiṭal, pūntōṭṭaṅ kaḷ ākiya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭum ivaṟṟik kēṭkum paṭit tūṇṭalām. oṉṟilum kaṭṭāya muṟiyē kūṭātu. kuṭimip payiṟcit tiṭṭam naṭimuṟiyil uḷḷa innāḷil māṇākkarkaḷ innikaḻkccikaḷic cevimaṭukkumpoḻutu vakuppu māṇākkat talivarkaḷik koṇṭē nalla amitiyi nilavac ceyyalām. paṭittavaṟṟi ettuṟiyilāyiṉum uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ payaṉpaṭuttum vāyppukaḷ iruntāltāṉ, karuttuṇarum āṟṟal virivil vaḷara vaḻi ēṟpaṭum.
(5) paḷḷip potu nūlakattilum, vakuppu nūlakaṅkaḷilum tuṇip paṭippukkeṉa ērāḷamāṉa puttaṅkaḷi vāṅki vikka vēṇṭum; nalla nalla itaḻkaḷiyum ceytit tāḷkaḷiyum taruvikkavēṇṭum. avaṟṟi alamārikaḷilum, paṭikkum mēcikaḷilum nalla muṟiyil parappivikka vēṇṭum; nūlakattil nalla irukki vacatikaḷi ēṟpaṭuttavēṇṭum. puttakaṅkaḷ nūlakattil iruppati aṉivarum eḷitil aṟiyumpaṭi ceytal vēṇṭum. puttakak kaṭiyil kavarccitarum muṟiyil puttakaṅkaḷi alamāriyil parappi vittiruppatu pōṉṟa muṟikaḷi nūlakattilum kiyāḷalām; nūlakattiṟkup putitāka vantuḷḷa nūlkaḷi ivvāṟu parappivikkalām. ēṉiya nūlkaḷukkup pala puttakap pērēṭṭup paṭikaḷi (Catalogues) vittu nūlakattil irukkum puttakaṅkaḷi ellārum aṟiyum paṭi ceyyalām.
(6) oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkeṉa cila nikaḻccikaḷit tayār ceytu avaṟṟic ceyal muṟikkuk koṇṭu varalām. nēril kāṇavalla nāṭakaṅkaḷ, oli parappumūlam kēṭkum nāṭakaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi nikaḻccikaḷil pukuttalām ; paṭitta puttakaṅkaḷippaṟṟiya tiṟaṉāyvu, cila ici nikaḻccikaḷ, cuṟṟulāpaṟṟiya oru cila pēccukaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi ivaṟṟil iṭampeṟac ceyyalām. paḷḷi naṭimuṟi āṇṭiṉ mūṉṟu paruvaṅkaḷilum (Terms) paruvam oṉṟiṟku māli nērattil ovvoru vāramum innikaḻccikku vāyppukkaḷi nalkalām.
(7) ciṟu kuḻantikaḷāka iruntāl uriyāṭalkaḷ, kati collac ceytal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku vāyppu aḷikkavēṇṭum. katip puttakaṅkaḷiyum uriyāṭalkaḷ aṭaṅkiya puttakaṅ kaḷiyum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ nilikkēṟṟa vāṟu tēviyāṉa aḷavu nūlakaṅkaḷil vāṅki vikka vēṇṭum.
(8) iḷam māṇākkarkaḷ paṭikkum puttakaṅkaḷil kaṭiṉa coṟkaḷum coṟkaḷum poruḷ viḷaṅkāta karuttukkaḷum atikam iruttal kūṭātu. avi atikamāka iruppiṉ karuttuṇartal taṭippaṭum; avvāṟu atikamāka iruppatu terintāl, paṭikkat toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉ āciriyarkaḷ avaṟṟi viḷakkalām. intap putiya coṟkaḷum karuttukkaḷum paravalāka irukkumpaṭi eḻutappeṟa vēṇṭum; avi ōriṭattil atikamākac cērntirukkalākātu. nūṟu coṟkaḷukku aintu allatu āṟu putiya coṟkaḷ irukkalām.
(9) mēl vakuppukkaḷil mēṟpārvip paṭippu, ceyaltiṭṭa muṟi, taṉippayiṟci muṟi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ mūlam pala puttakaṅ kaḷip paṭikkac ceyyalām. cila camayaṅkaḷil paṭikkum puttakaṅkaḷil oru cila viṉākkaḷi āyattam ceytu avarkaḷ karuttuṇarum āṟṟalit tēṟalām. paṭippataṟkum ērāḷamāṉa vāyppukaḷik koṭukkavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, paṭippatil kaṭṭāyap paṭuttuvatiyō paṭittavaṟṟic cōtippatiyō kuṟittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
(10) pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷiliruntu cila eḷiya pakutikaḷi vakuppil naṭattumuṉṉarē orōvaḻi vīṭṭil paṭittuk koṇṭu varumpaṭiyum ceyyalām. immuṟi tāmākap paṭittuk karuttuṇarvataṟku oru payiṟciyāka iruppatuṭaṉ tāmākap paṭippataṟkut tūṇṭukōlākavum irukkum; paṭippatil āvaliyum uṇṭu paṇṇum. akarātippaḻakkam, apitāṉa cintāmaṇi pōṉṟa mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷip puraṭṭum paḻakkam ākiyavaṟṟi māṇākkarkaḷiṭam uṇṭākki viṭṭāl karuttu ṇarum tiṟaṉ virivil vaḷara ētuvākum.
immuṟikaḷil ellām veṟṟikāṇa vēṇṭumāṉāl palaruṭiya kūṭṭuṟavum ottuḻippum vēṇṭum. mutulil ellā āciriyarkaḷum itil  ōraḷavu tiṟamiyum nāṭṭamum koṇṭiruppatuṭaṉ, uṟcākattuṭaṉ ottuḻikkavum vēṇṭum; paḷḷikaḷik kaṇkāṇip pōr celavukaḷip pārāmal tārāḷamāka nitiyi nalki ellāvacatikaḷiyum ēṟpaṭuttit tarutalvēṇṭum. aracāṅkamum ittuṟikaḷil nēriṭum celaviṉaṅkaḷi ēṟṟuk koṇṭu tārāḷamāka māṉiyam vaḻaṅkutalvēṇṭum; celvarkaḷ itaṟku naṉkoṭikaḷ aḷikka muṉvarutal vēṇṭum.


4 paṭippil vēkam
vāykkuṭ paṭippil karuttuṇarum āṟṟaluṭaṉ vēkamākap paṭikkum paḻakkamum kivarap peṟutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. paṭikkum pakutikaḷil vēkattit taṭippaṭuttakkūṭiya kūṟukaḷi nīkki, vēkatti vaḷarkkum muṟiyil kiyāḷa vēṇṭum. paṭippu mellac celluvataṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa. oru cilavaṟṟi muṉṉark kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷōm. taṭiyāka iruppavaṟṟik kaḷintāl virivu tāṉāka ēṟpaṭum. oru cilar paṭippil virivi uṇṭākkiṉāl karuttuṇartal taṭippaṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. atu tavaṟāṉa karuttākum.
paṭippiṉ vēkam paṭikkum poruḷukkēṟṟavāṟum tēvikkēṟṟavāṟum māṟupaṭum. ivaṟṟit terintāl vēkattiṉ aḷaviṉi ōraḷavu aṟutiyiṭalām. ellāvitap puttakaṅkaḷiyum orē vēkattuṭaṉ paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa eṇṇam māṟavēṇṭum. orē aḷavuḷḷa oru katiyiyum ōr aṟiviyaṟ kaṭṭuriyiyum paṭittup pārttāl, iraṇṭiyum paṭittu muṭikka ākum kālattil vēṟupāṭu iruppati aṟiyalām; aṟiviyaṟ kaṭṭuriyip paṭikka atikak kālam tēviyāka iruppati uṇaralām.
paṭippiṉ virivi aḷantaṟivataṟku mēṉāṭukaḷil pala cōtaṉikaḷi naṭatti muṭivu kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar. cātāraṇamāka uyarnilippaḷḷikaḷ, allatu kallūrikaḷil māṇākkaṉ oru maṇittuḷikkuc caṟṟēṟak kuṟiya 250 coṟkaḷ vītam paṭikkalām eṉṟu kaṇṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. vēkamākap paṭippōr katip pakutiyip paṭikkumpoḻutu maṇittuḷikku 400 coṟkaḷ vītam paṭikkalām eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭaṟiyappeṟṟuḷḷatu.    maṇit tuḷikku 1400 coṟkaḷ vītam paṭikkum oru cilarum iruppatāka aṟintuḷḷaṉar. paṭittalil taṉippaṭṭavar kaḷiṉ vēka aḷavukaḷil atika vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. takka payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkap peṟṟāl ellōruṭiya paṭippiṉ vēkamum atikamākum. ittakiya payiṟciyiṉāl vēkamākap paṭippōr oruvariṉ vēkam maṇittuḷikku 450 coṟkaḷiliruntu 700 coṟkaḷukku atikarittuḷḷatu eṉṟum, metuvākap paṭippōr oruvariṉ vēkam maṇittuḷikku 175 coṟkaḷiliruntu 250 coṟkaḷukku atikarittuḷḷatu eṉṟum, aimpatu kallūri māṇākkarkaḷukku aḷikkappeṟṟa oru cōtaṉiyiṉ mūlam kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷatu. paṭippil vēkatti uṇṭākkum oru cila kūṟukaḷi īṇṭuk kavaṉippōm.
nām puttakattip paṭikkumpoḻutu namatu kaṇkaḷ accu variyiṉmītu nērākac celvatilli; avi māṟi māṟi niṉṟu cellukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟik kaṇ niṟuttaṅkaḷ (Eye fixations) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar; avi cumār kāl viṉāṭi avvāṟu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. avvāṟu niṟkum poḻututāṉ kaṇkaḷ eḻuttuk kaḷip pārkkiṉṟaṉa. kaṇkaḷ niṟkum kālatti kaṇ niṟutta aḷavu (Eye fixations limit) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. oru variyil atikak kāla aḷavu niṟuttupavarkaḷ metuvākap paṭippōr eṉṟum., kuṟinta kāla aḷavu niṟuttupavarkaḷ virivākap paṭippōr eṉṟum karutappeṟuvar. nām paṭittuk koṇṭē oru variyiṉ iṟutiyi aṭintatum kaṇkaḷ mika eḷitākat toṭarntu piṉpuṟamāka nakarntu aṭutta vari toṭaṅkum iṭattiṟkup pāykiṉṟaṉa. itip piṉpāyccal (Return sweep) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. itu nērmiyākavum virivākavum celvataṟkup paḻakkam vēṇṭum. accu variyōṭu iḵtu ōṭip piṉpu cariyāka aṭutta varikkup pōkavēṇṭum. itil kālak kaḻivu ēṟpaṭṭāl vēkam taṭippaṭum.
nām paṭikkumpoḻutu ovvoru eḻuttiyum taṉit taṉiyākap paṭippatilli; ovvoru niṟuttattiṟkum oṉṟu allatu ilaṇṭu coṟkaḷip paṭikkiṉṟōm. oru niṟuttattāl kaṇ pārkkakkūṭiya coṟkaḷiṉ nīḷatti niṉivukūr cāṇ (Recognition span)mšyJ kaṇcāṇ (Eye span) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. kaṇcāṉ akaṉṟiruntāl vēkam atikarikkum. kaṇcāṇiṉ akalam atikamāṉāl kaṇ niṟuttaṅkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki kuṟiyum. akalamāṉa kaṇcāṇiṉāl orē taṭaviyil atikac coṟkaḷip paṭittup poruḷuṇaralām. cilacamayam kaṇkaḷ cila iṭaṅkaḷil cariyākap pārttuk karuttuṇarntu paṭikkāmaṟ pōyviṭṭup piṉṉar avviṭaṅ kaḷukkē tirumpi varuvatuṇṭu. kaṇkaḷ pārtta vaṟṟiyē tirumpap pārkkiṉṟaṉa; kāraṇam, muṉṉark karuttuṇarāmaṟ pārttaṉa; ati uṇarvataṟkāka mīṇṭum pārkkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu kaṇkaḷ piṉnōkki varuvatip piṟpōkku (Regression) eṉṟu kūṟuvar. piṟpōkkukaḷ kuṟiyak kuṟiyap paṭippiṉ virivu perukum. eṉavē, akaṉṟa kaṇcāṇ, oru variyil kuṟinta eṇṇikkiyuḷḷa niṟuttaṅkaḷ, kuṟinta piṟpōkkukaḷ iruntāl ati nalla paṭippu eṉṟu collalām; ivvāṟu iruntāl paṭippu virintu cellakkūṭiyatākavum irukkum.
kaṇcāṇ akaṉṟāl niṟuttaṅkaḷum piṟpōkkukaḷum kuṟiyum; kālakkaḻivum nēriṭātu. karuttum coṟkaḷum aṟintavikaḷāka iruntāl niṟuttaṅkaḷum piṟpōkkukaḷum kuṟintu kaṇcāṇ virivaṭiyum. kaṇcāṇ virivaṭivataṟkuk kīḻvakuppukkaḷiliruntē cila payiṟcikaḷi aḷikka vēṇṭum. miṉṉaṭṭip payiṟcikaḷ itaṟku naṉku payaṉpaṭum. coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷa aṭṭikaḷi vakuppukku ēṟṟavāṟu tokuttu avaṟṟi virivāka māṟṟik kāṭṭip payiṟcikaḷi aḷittāl kaṇcāṇ virivaṭiyum.
kaṇkaḷil cātāraṇamākak kāṇakkūṭiya tūrap pārvi, kiṭṭappārvi, uruṭcippiḻi (Astigmatism) pōṉṟa kōḷāṟukaḷ iruppiṉum paṭippiṉ vēkam kuṟiyum. māṇākkarkaḷiṭam kaṇkōḷāṟukaḷ iruppatāka āciriyarkaḷ kaṇṭāl kaṇ maruttuvariṭam aṉuppic cōtikkac ceytu takka kaṇṇāṭikaḷ pōṭṭuk koḷḷac ceyya vēṇṭum.
ettakiya payiṟciyilum nalla paḻakkam ēṟpaṭuvataṟkumuṉ virivi uṇṭākka muṉiyak kūṭātu; paṭippilum appaṭittāṉ. paṭippil virivi uṇṭākkat toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉ mutalil caraḷamākap paṭikkap paḻakikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. caraḷamākap paṭippataṟkut taṭiyāka irukkum pala kūṟukaḷi muṉṉatākak kaḷiya vēṇṭum.. cila kuḻantikaḷ coṟkaḷiyē pirittup pirittup paṭittu, piṟaku cērttup paṭikkiṉṟaṉar; coṟkaḷip paṭittālum avaṟṟi virivākap paṭittuk koṇṭu pōvatilli. tavira, coṟkaḷit taṉittaṉiyākap paṭittalum nalla paḻakkam aṉṟu. coṟṟoṭarkaḷākap paṭittuk karuttuṇarum paḻakkattit toṭakkattiliruntē kivarac ceyyavēṇṭum.
māṇākkarkaḷ uṭkārum niliyum aṟiyiṉ oḷiyum vēkattit taṭippaṭuttak kūṭiyavi. kuṟivāṉa oḷi kaṇ pārvikkup pōtātu; atikamāṉa oḷiyāl kaṇ kūcum; iraṇṭum paṭippiṉ virivik kuṟippavi. eṉavē, vacatiyāṉa niliyil iṭappuṟattiliruntu veḷiccam varumpaṭi ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum. paṭikkumpoḻutu muṉpuṟattaliruntāvatu piṉpuṟattiliruntāvatu veḷiccam varutalit tavirkka vēṇṭum. nērāka nimirntu uṭkārntu puttakatti mārpukku nērākap paṉṉiraṇṭu aṅkula aḷavil piṭittuk koṇṭu kuḻantikaḷip paṭikkac ceyyavēṇṭum.
paṭikkumpoḻutu poruḷaṟiyāta coṟkaḷ aṭikkaṭi taṭṭuppaṭṭāl karuttuṇartal taṭippaṭuvatuṭaṉ paṭikkum virivum kuṟikiṉṟatu. paṭikkum payiṟci yaḷikkap payaṉpaṭuttum puttakaṅkaḷ karuttuṇarntu virivākap paṭikkakkūṭiyaviyāka iruttal vēṇṭum. puttakaṅkaḷil putiya coṟkaḷi aḷavu mīṟik kiyāḷak kūṭātu. puttakattil coṟcumi (Vocabulary burden) mikak kuṟivāka iruttal vēṇṭum. coṟcumi eṉpatu, oru puttakattil putiya coṟkaḷ enta vikitattil nuḻikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpatik kuṟikkum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka 1000 coṟkaḷuḷḷa oru pāṭanūlil 120 palvēṟu putiya coṟkaḷ nuḻikkap peṟṟiruntāl apputtakattiṉ coṟcumi 1000 / 120 = 8.3% aḵtāvatu apputtakattil eṭṭuc coṟkaḷukku oru putiya col vītam irukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. oru puttakattiṉ coṟcumiyik kuṟikka vēṇṭumāṉāl terinta coṟkaḷi atikamākkip putiya coṟkaḷik kuṟikka vēṇṭum. itaṉāl oru periya puttakattiliruppativiṭa oru ciṟiya puttakattil coṟcumi atika miruttal kūṭum eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. āṉāl, tamiḻil coṟcumiyi aḷavu paṭutti eḻutappeṟṟa puttakaṅkaḷ illi; eḻutuvōr itik kavaṉittu eḻutuvatē illi. cātāraṇamākap pāṭap puttakattil 25 catavikitam putiya coṟkaḷ iruppatip pārkkalām. māṇākkarkaḷ paṭikkum puttakaṅkaḷ eḷiyaṉavāy, tāmē karuttuṇarntukoḷḷak kūṭiyaṉavāy, paṭippil paṟṟi ūṭṭuvaṉavāy iruntāl paṭippu virintu cellum.
kuḻantikaḷ kuralpōṭṭup paṭippatip paṭippaṭiyāka akaṟṟi, utaṭukaḷ toṇṭi mutaliyavi kūṭa aciyā tirukkumāṟu paḻakkavēṇṭum. kaṇpārvi maṭṭilum ceyaṟ paṭumāṟu ceytal vēṇṭum. kuṟippiṭṭa kālattil kuṟippiṭṭa pakutiyip paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṟiyil payiṟci aḷittum, ivvāṟu paṭippati vāykkuḷ paṭikkumpaṭi ceytum paṭippil virivi eytuvikkalām. itaḻkaḷ acital mutaliyaṉa paṭippiṉ virivik kuṟikkum eṉpatiyum, kaḷippi virivil uṇṭākkum eṉpatiyum māṇākkarkaḷ aṟiyum paṭic ceyyalām. aṉṟiyum, vāykkuṭ paṭippi vaḷarkkak kiyāḷum upāyaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum mēṟkoṇṭāl, paṭippil virivu ēṟpaṭum.
5. coṟkaḷañciyap perukkam
moḻi kaṟṟalil coṟkaḷañciyap perukkam oru mukkiya kūṟākum. pālar kalvi mutal palkalik kaḻakak kalvivari paṇiyāṟṟupavarkaḷ coṟkaḷañciyap perukkattiṉ iṉṟiyami yāmiyi uṇarntu ati vaḷarkkap pala upāyaṅkaḷik kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar. māṇākkarkaḷiṉ coṟ kaḷañciyattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷi iraṇṭu vakiyākap pirikkalām. avi nāḷ tōṟum vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ, nāḷtōṟum vaḻaṅkāc coṟkaḷ eṉappaṭum. nāḷtōṟum vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi māṇākkarkaḷ naṉku aṟivar. avaṟṟi avarkaḷ tam vākkilum eḻuttilum vittuk kiyāḷuvar. avaṟṟuḷ cila coṟkaḷ mikavum aṭikkaṭi payiluvaṉavākavum irukkum. nāḷtōṟum vaḻaṅkāc coṟkaḷip potuvāka uṇarvār kaḷēyaṉṟi, avaṟṟip pēccilum eḻuttilum mikutiyāka vittu vaḻaṅkār. avi perumpālum ceyyuṭ pāṭaṅkaḷilum orōvaḻi urinaṭip pāṭaṅkaḷilum varalāṟu, aṟiviyal pōṉṟa cila pāṭaṅkaḷil cila viṭaṅkaḷilum payiṉṟu varum.
mutal aintu vakuppukkaḷil nāḷtōṟum vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷiṉ paṭṭikaḷ, avaṟṟuḷḷum aṭikkaṭip payiṉṟu varum coṟkaḷiṉ paṭṭikaḷ, nāḷtōṟum vaḻaṅkāc coṟkaḷiṉ paṭṭikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi naṉ muṟiyil ārāyntu tokukka vēṇṭum. tamiḻil itukāṟum āyvukaḷ mūlam āyattam ceyta paṭṭikaḷ tōṉṟavilli. āṅkila moḻiyil pala paṭṭikaḷ tayār ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ammuṟikaḷ tamiḻc coṟ paṭṭikaḷ tayār ceyvataṟku vaḻikāṭṭikaḷāka amiyalām. aṅṅaṉam āyattam ceyyap peṟṟu iṉṟu eṅkum peru vaḻakkāka iruppatu tārṉṭik eṉpār tiraṭṭiya āciriyar coṟputtakam6 eṉṟa paṭṭiyākum. atil āṅkila moḻiyil aṭikkaṭi vaḻakkattil payaṉpaṭum 10,000 coṟkaḷ tiraṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. avi caṟṟēṟakkuṟiya nālari ilaṭcam coṟkaḷaṭaṅkiya puttakaṅkaḷi yellām ārāyntu tiraṭṭap peṟṟavi. ap puttakaṅkaḷil viviliyam, mukkiya āṅkila uyartara ilakkiyaṅkaḷ (Classics), kuḻantikaḷāl mukkiyamākap paṭikkappeṟum puttakaṅkaḷ, vaḷarntavarkaḷāl aṭikkaṭi payaṉpaṭuttappeṟum vaḻikāṭṭi nūlkaḷ, ceytittāḷkaḷ, kaṭitaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi aṭaṅkum. avvāṟu tokukkappeṟṟa 10,000 coṟkaḷum akaravaricippaṭi varicippaṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru collukkum arukē kāṇappeṟum eṇ aḵtu aṭikkaṭi payilappeṟum pāṉmiyik kāṭṭum. ataṉāl colliṉ mukkiyattuvamum teriyum. tām tokutta coṟpaṭṭiyāl āciriyarkaḷ eccoṟkaḷi mukkiyamākak kaṟpikkavēṇṭum eṉpati aṟiyalām eṉṟu tārṉṭik karutukiṉṟār. vēṟucila coṟpaṭṭikaḷum cila kalvi nipuṇar kaḷāl āyattam ceyyappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
kalviyiṉ mukkiya uṟuppākiya paṭippu naṉku kivarap peṟutaṟkum, paṭippil virivu ēṟpaṭuvataṟkum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷañciyam peruka vēṇṭum. coṟkaḷi virivil niṉivukūrtaltāṉ paṭippil virivi vaḷarkkum eṉṟa karuttu nilavi vantatu. eṉavē, paḻaṅkālattil coṟkaḷik kaṟpippatiltāṉ perumpakutiyāṉa kālam celavaḻikkap peṟṟatu. ivvāṟu kaṟpikkappeṟṟa māṇākkarkaḷuṭiya paṭippiṉ virivum karuttuṇartalum kuṟintiruppati nāḷaṭivil aṟiyalāyiṉar. eṉavē, coṟkaḷik kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ celvākkiḻantu karuttuṇartalik kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷ celvākkaṭintaṉa. itaṉāl palar putiya coṟkaḷi aṟivatil taṭumāṟṟam aṭintaṉar; tavaṟākavum poruḷuṇarn taṉar. iṉṟu coṟkaḷañciyattil aṟivuk kuṟivu iruntāl karuttuṇartalil tiṟaṉ naṉku ēṟpaṭātu eṉṟa karuttu nilavukiṉṟatu. eṉavē, coṟkaḷañciyap perukkattil āciriyarkaḷ kavaṉam celuttal vēṇṭum.
mutalil kuḻantikaḷukkuc coṟkaḷik kēṭṭup poruḷuṇartalil tāṉ paḻakkam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. perum pālāṉa kuḻantikaḷ tām coṟkaḷik kiyāḷuvataṟku muṉpē piṟarmūlam kēṭkum coṟkaḷi virivāka aṟintu koḷvati aṉupavattil kāṇkiṉṟōm. oruvaruṭiya vāḻkki muḻuvatilumkūṭak kēḷviyaṟiviṉāl peṟakkūṭiya coṟkaḷiṉ tokitāṉ mikutiyāka uḷḷatu. kuḻantikaḷ paṭikkat toṭaṅkumpoḻutu pārviyiṉāl pala coṟkaḷi aṟikiṉṟaṉar. ippoḻutu avarkaḷ, tām kēḷviyaṟiviṉāl muṉṉarp peṟāta pala putiya coṟkaḷiyum terintu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. nāḷaṭivil eḻutuvataṉālum eḻuttuk kūṭṭuvataṉālum pala coṟkaḷi aṟikiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, kuḻantikaḷ kēṭpatālum, pēcuvatālum, paṭippatālum, eḻutuvatālum pala coṟkaḷip poruḷuṇarntu kaṟkiṉṟaṉar. toṭakka nilip paḷḷik kuḻantikaṭku eḻutappeṟum puttakaṅkaḷil kuḻantikaḷ ēṟkeṉavē aṟintuḷḷa coṟkaḷiyum, eḷitil viḷakkavalla coṟkaḷiyumtāṉ kiyāḷa vēṇṭum. itupaṟṟiya vitikaḷ piṉṉark kūṟappeṟum. īṇṭu māṇakkarkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷañciyattip perukkum oru cila vaḻikaḷi maṭṭilum ārāyvōm.
karuttuṇarum paṭṭaṟivu uṇṭākkal. paṭikkum aṟivu uṇṭāvataṟku muṉpu kuḻantikaḷ toṭṭu, kaṇṭu, kēṭṭu, mukarntu, cuvittu aṟiyakkūṭiya pulaṉuṇarvu aṉupavaṅkaḷi atikam uṇṭākka vāyppukkaḷit tarutal vēṇṭum. peṟṟōr kaḷum āciriyarkaḷum aṭikkaṭik kuḻantikaḷip pala iṭaṅkaḷukku aḻittuc ceṉṟu pala poruḷkaḷippaṟṟiya aṉupavaṅkaḷ kiṭṭum paṭi ceytal vēṇṭum. kaṭitteru, pukivaṇṭi niliyam, canti, pūmpoḻilkaḷ, kōvilkaḷ, tiruviḻākkaḷ naṭipeṟum iṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟiṟkuk kuḻantikaḷi aṭikkaṭik kūṭṭicceṉṟu avarkaḷ kēṭkum viṉākkaḷukkup poṟumiyuṭaṉ viṭiyaḷittu avarkaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivi vaḷarkkavēṇṭum. pala iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu pala poruḷkaḷik kāṇpatāl pulaṉkāṭciyum (Perception), pala poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi viṉākkaḷ mūlamum nērilum aṟivatāl karuttuk kōṭalum (Conception) vaḷarntu uṟutiyaṭi kiṉṟaṉa. perumpālāṉa āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷi im mātiriyāka veḷiyil kūṭṭic cellum vāyppukkaḷi aḷittālum, pala poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiya aṟiviṉi vaḷarppatil kavaṉam celuttu vatilli.
nērmuka aṉupavam aḷikka vāyppu illāta poḻutu, pēcum paṭaṅkaḷ, aciyum paṭaṅkaḷ, naḻuvaṅkaḷ (Slides), cātāraṇap paṭaṅkaḷ, viḷakkappaṭaṅkaḷ (Diagrams) pōṉṟavi kaḷāl pala poruḷkaḷippaṟṟiya aṉupavaṅkaḷit tarutal vēṇṭum. kati collal, āciriyarkaḷ vāyviṭṭu naṉṟākap paṭittal pōṉṟavi mutaliraṇṭu vakuppukkaḷil ivvita aṉupavaṅkaḷukku mēlum peruntuṇiyāka amiyum. iyalpāṉa pēccukaḷ, uriyāṭalkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟāl toṭakka niliyil pala poruḷpaṟṟiya paṭṭaṟivukaḷi uṇṭākkalām. kuḻanti kaḷukku immātiriyāṉa vāyppukkaḷ aḷikkum poḻutu avarkaḷ teriyātaṉavaṟṟi urimiyuṭaṉ viṉava vāyppukkaḷit tarutal vēṇṭum.
paṭittalāl coṟkaḷañciyattip perukkal: kuḻantikaḷ paṭikkat toṭaṅkiyavuṭaṉ coṟkaḷañciyattip perukkuvatil pala upāyaṅkaḷik kiyāḷalām. miṉṉaṭṭikaḷ, kaṭṭaḷic coṟṟoṭarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil aṭikkaṭip payilum coṟkaḷ, iṉṟiyamiyātavāṟu payaṉpaṭakkūṭiya coṟkaḷ, vīṭu paḷḷi cellum iṭaṅkaḷ mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇakkūṭiya poruḷ kaḷip paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ, uṇpaṉa tiṇpaṉa parukuvaṉa pōṉṟa uṇavup poruḷkaḷ, uṟaviṉar, vīṭṭup pirāṇikaḷ, maram ceṭi koṭi vakikaḷ ūrtikaḷ uṭaluṟuppukkaḷ viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik kaṟpikkalām, cila nikaḻccikaḷiyum immuṟiyil kaṟpikkalām. coṟkaḷic cila cilavākap poruḷuṇarumpaṭiyākak kaṟpikka vēṇṭum; palavaṟṟi orē camayattil kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkiṉāl karuttuṇar talil mayakkam ēṟpaṭa iṭam uṇṭu, coṟkaḷit taṉit taṉiyākak kaṟpippatuṭaṉ.,iṇimoḻikaḷākavum ,toṭar moḻikaḷil vittum, maraput toṭarkaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ, aṟamoḻikaḷ, uvamikaḷ vaṭivattilum kaṟpikka muṉiya vēṇṭum. mikavum aṭippaṭiyākavuḷḷa iṉṟiyamiyāta toḻilkaḷip paṟṟiya peyar viṉic coṟkaḷi mutaṉ mutalil toṭaṅkalām.
puttakaṅkaḷip paṭikkum paḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭavuṭaṉ mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu kaṟpippatuṭaṉ vēṟu cila muṟikaḷiyum mēṟkoḷḷalām. kuḻantikaḷ ārvattuṭaṉ paṭikkak kūṭiya poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi avarkaḷaṟinta coṟkaḷik koṇṭa puttakaṅkaḷi mutalil paṭikkak koṭukka vēṇṭum. eḷitākavuḷḷa puttakattil oru cila putiya coṟkaḷ kiyāḷap peṟṟiruppiṉum, iṭaṉōkki māṇākkarkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ poruḷi uṇarntu koḷḷakkūṭum. iṉṟiyamiyāta coṟkaḷāka iruntāl, avi aṭikkaṭip payiṉṟu varutalikkoṇṭu iṭaṉōkki avaṟṟiṉ poruḷit teḷivāka aṟintu koḷvar. avvāṟu payilumpoḻutu oru cila coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi aṟiyā viṭiṉum kuṟṟam illi. coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷit taṉit taṉiyāka aṟintu koḷḷuvativiṭa iṭaṉōkki aṟital tāṉ ciṟappuṭittu.
pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟum aruñcoṟkaḷik kaṟpippatil cila muṟikaḷik kiyāḷalām. aruñcoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷip piṟitoru collāl kūṟuvatil payaṉ illi. avi payilum pala iṭaṅkaḷilum avaṟṟi āciriyarkaḷ tam vākkil vittu vaḻaṅkik kaṟpikka vēṇṭum. māṇākkariṉ vākkilum piṟitoru iṭattil vittu vaḻaṅkumāṟu ēvip payiṟci koṭukkalām. nalla pāṭapputtakamāka iruntāl putuc coṟkaḷ pala iṭaṅkaḷil palvēṟu muṟiyil vaḻaṅkappeṟṟu irukkum. cila ariya collāṭcikaḷ, maraputtoṭarkaḷ, ilakkaṇa amitiyuḷḷa toṭarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi neṭṭuruc ceyyac colli avaṟṟi appaṭiyē tam vākkil vittu vaḻaṅkumāṟu māṇākkarkaḷi ēvalām. coṟkaḷi iṉap paṭuttik kaṟkum muṟikaḷiyum kiyāḷalām; coṟkaḷi nēretirc coṟkaḷōṭum, oru poruḷ kuṟitta pala coṟkaḷi oṉṟākat tokuttum, pala poruḷ kuṟitta oru coṟkaḷic cērttum iṉappaṭuttik kaṟpikkalām. orē vēriṉiṉṟō pakutiyiṉiṉṟō piṟakkum pala coṟkaḷi oṉṟu cērkkum payiṟcikaḷiyum taralām. oru poruḷi, oru toḻili, oru taliyi, oru niliyatti oru vēliyi, oru nikaḻcciyi-ivi pōṉṟa piṟavaṟṟi viḷakkum coṟkaḷiyum, camaya uṇmikaḷi viḷakkum coṟkaḷiyum tokutit tokutiyāka vakippaṭuttik kaṟpikkalām. kuṟil neṭil toṭaṅkum coṟkaḷiyum, mutal nīṇṭu toḻil peyarākum coṟkaḷiyum, orē muṭipuṭiya coṟkaḷiyum iṉappaṭuttik kaṟpikkalām. māṇākkarkaḷiṉ uṟcākatti aṉucarittu ivvāṟu kaṟpittali mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭum. uṟcākam illiyeṉat terintāl, vēṟu muṟikaḷik kiyāḷa vēṇṭum.
putitākak kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷik koṇṭu tokic coṟkaḷi uṇṭākki avaṟṟiṉ poruḷi valiyuṟuttalām. ra,ṟa; la,ḻa,ḷa; ṇa, na,ṉa; mutaliya otta ōcikaḷāl poruḷpaṭum coṟkaḷi iṇi iṇiyākat tokuttuk kaṟpikkalām; perumpālum avaṟṟic coṟṟoṭarkaḷil vaḻaṅkip poruḷ vēṟupāṭukaḷi uṇarttik kaṟpittal cālap payaṉtarum. aṭimoḻikaḷi mutal kaṭikaḷiṟ cērttu vēṟu putiya coṟkaḷi uṇṭākki avaṟṟik kaṟpikkalām. kāli-ucci-māli cērntu oru pakal ākiṉṟatu; aṭivāram-naṭu-kuṭumi cērntu maliyākiṉṟatu; iyal-ici-nāṭakam cērntu muttamiḻākiṉṟatu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷāl mūṉṟu coṟkaḷ taṉittaṉi cērntu oru muḻupporuḷik kuṟikkum collip piṟappikkumāṟum putuc coṟkaḷi ākkik kāṭṭalām. viṭukatikaḷi avaṟṟiṉ viṭikaḷ vāyilākavum, ciṟiya katikaḷic ciṟu toṭarkaḷākat taṉit taṉiyāka eḻutic cērttuk katiyip paṭikkac ceytum putiya coṟkaḷik kaṟpikkalām.
kuḻantikaḷukku akarātiyip puraṭṭutal, aruñ col variciyip pārttal ākiyavaṟṟil paḻakkatti uṇṭākkiviṭṭāl tāmākak karuttuṇarntu paṭikkum paḻakkam vaḷarum. putiya coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷiyum tāmāka aṟintu koḷḷum vāyppu ēṟpaṭum. nāṉku, aintu vakuppukaḷil ippayiṟciyit toṭaṅkalām. kaḻakak kiyakarāti pōṉṟa ciṟu akarātiyi ovvoru māṇākkaṉum vittuk koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ perumpālum akarātip paḻakkattiyē vaḷarppatilli. paḻaṅkālattil kurumukamākak kalviyi peṟṟu nikaṇṭukaḷi maṉappāṭam ceytu vantapoḻutu appaḻakkam tēviyirātu iruntirukkalām; aṉṟiyum,akkālattil akarātikaḷum illi. iṉṟiya cūḻniliyil, kalvi paṉmuka virivi aṭintirukkum poḻutu, akarātip paḻakkam mikavum iṉṟi yamiyātatu. ovvoṟu paḷḷiyilum palavita akarātikaḷ mēṟkōḷ nūlakattil irukka vēṇṭum. vīramāmuṉivar caturakarāti, katirivēṟpiḷḷi akarāti, tamiḻp pērakarāti, (Tamil Laxicon) apitāṉa cintāmaṇi, putitāka veḷivantuḷḷa kalikkaḷañciyam ākiya nūlkaḷ ciṟappāka iṭam peṟutal vēṇṭum.
tamiḻ moḻi mikavum nuṭpamāṉatu. tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa ākupeyar āṭcikaḷ, tokic coṟkaḷiṉ āṭcikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nayatti māṇākkarkaḷ naṉkaṟiyac ceyya vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟit tavira, kampi nīṭṭutal, kayiṟu tirittal, vayiṟṟilaṭittil pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa maraput toṭarkaḷ pēccu vaḻakkiluḷḷaṉa. avi piṉṉar ilakkaṇam eṉṟa iyalil virivāka ārāyappeṟum. avi yāvum ēṭṭu vaḻakkiṟku vantāl tamiḻ uri naṭi puttuyir peṟum; putiya uṇarccikaḷ tuḷirkkum. avaṟṟit tokuttu, tamiḻ pātukāppuk kaḻakam veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu.7 āciriyarkaḷ avaṟṟi māṇākkarkaḷ aṟiyumpaṭi ceytal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.


9. kiyeḻuttum eḻuttukkūṭṭalum
moḻi kaṟṟalil pēcutal, paṭittal, eḻututal eṉṟa muṟitāṉ iyalpāṉa muṟiyākum. nalla pēccup paḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭa piṟakutāṉ coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum paṭikkum paḻakkattit tarutal vēṇṭum. piṟaku tāṉ eḻutum payiṟci tarappeṟutal vēṇṭum. ivvitam paṭippaṭiyāka payiṟcikaḷ tarappeṟavēṇṭum eṉiṉum, oṉṟi muṭitta piṟaku tāṉ aṭuttatit toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutavēṇṭā. paṭikkat toṭaṅkiyavuṭaṉ pēccup payiṟciyi niṟutti viṭalām eṉṟō, eḻutum payiṟci toṭaṅkiyavuṭaṉ paṭikkum payiṟci tēviyilli eṉṟō niṉittal tavaṟu. mūṉṟiyum toṭarntē kaṟpittal vēṇṭum. pēccup payiṟci taruvatu paṟṟiyum, paṭittalil payiṟci aḷippatu paṟṟiyum mēlē kaṇṭōm. īṇṭu eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ taruvatupaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi oru ciṟitu ārāyvōm.
eḻuttiṉ āṟṟal: nēril iruppavarukkup pēcuvataṉ mūlam eṇṇaṅkaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṉṟōm. nēril illātavarukku eḻuttumūlamāka avaṟṟit terivikkiṉṟōm. pēcciṉi nilipeṟavippatu eḻuttē ākum. eḻuttu moḻiyi makkaḷ aṟivataṟku muṉṉaravarkaḷ tam niṉivāṟṟali nampiyē iruntaṉar. namatu nāṭṭil nāṉmaṟi eḻutāk kiḷaviyāyt talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vantu koṇṭuḷḷatu. iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal uri eṉṟa oru nūl pattut tali muṟiyāyt tamiḻnāṭṭil vāymoḻiyākac collap paṭṭu vantatu eṉpati annūliṉ pāyirattāl aṟiyalām.1 tali muṟit talimuṟiyāy vāy vaḻiyākavum cevi vaḻiyākavum vāḻntu varum pāṭalkaḷ nantamiḻ moḻiyil ērāḷamāka uḷḷaṉa. makkaḷ tam niṉivil avaṟṟi vittirukkum āṟṟalālum avaṟṟip piṉṉōrkkuk kaṟpikkum takutip pāṭṭiṉālumē avi kālak kaṭalik kaṭantu nammiṭam vantuḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, kālattiyum iṭattiyum kaṭantu piṟkālattil vāḻpavarkaḷuṭaṉ pēcuvatu eḻuttu vaḻiyākat tāṉ muṭiyum. eṉavē, makkaḷ iṉattik kāla vēṟṟumiyum iṭavēṟṟumiyum iṉṟi oṉṟākak kāṇum kāṭci eḻuttiṉ tirumuṉpē eḻukiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatil tavaṟu oṉṟum illi. eḻutumpoḻutu nammiyē viḷakkivippatupōla, eṇṇi eṇṇi eḻutukiṉṟōm. pēcuvataṟku vēṇṭiyati viṭa eḻutuvataṟku atikak kālam vēṇṭum.  ātalāl karuttiṉit tiraṭṭi iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavaṟṟi maṭṭumē eḻuti vikkiṉṟōm. kampaṉiyum, iḷaṅkōviyum, tiruttakkat tēvariyum, cēkkiḻāriyum paṭikkumpoḻutu avarkaḷ uḷḷatti nām uḷḷavāṟu uṇaruvatupōla avarkaḷuṭaṉ nērē pēca vāyppuk kiṭittiruntālum uṇarntirukka muṭiyātu.
eḻuttu moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu: eḻuttu moḻiyiṉ varalāṟṟi virivāka ārāyvataṟku iṅku iṭamilli. eḻuttu moḻitāṉ kāṭṭumirāṇṭi niliyilirunta maṉitaṉip paṇpaṭutti nākarika nilikku uyarttiyatu. eḻuttukkali maṉitaṉi aṟivu nilikkum āṉma nilikkum uyarttiyatu pōla vēṟu entac cātaṉamum uyarttavilli. itaṉāltāṉ piḷēṭṭō eṉṟa aṟiñar itaṉi tēvakali (Divine Art) eḷṟu kuṟittup pōntār. eḻuttuk kaliyiṉṟēl nam mūtātiyariṉ aṟivuk kaḷañciyatti nām peṟṟiruttal muṭiyātu; nāmum nam piṉvaruvōrukku nam karuttukkaḷit tara muṭiyātu. paṇpaṭāta ettakiya kuṟiyīṭukaḷiyum koṇṭu eḻuttuk kaliyip peṟṟa entakkūṭṭattu makkaḷāka iruppiṉum avarkaḷ nākarika ēṇiyil atika uyarattukkuc ceṉṟuḷḷaṉar eṉṟē colla vēṇṭum.
pēccu moḻiyik kaṟṟa maṉitaṉukku eḻutak kaṟkumāṟu tūṇṭiyatu etu eṉpati eṇṇippārttāl taṉṉetirilō aṇmiyilō illāta oruvarukkut taṉatu karuttit terivikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇamē yākum eṉpatu teriya varum. iṭattiṉ cēymiyik karuti ivvāṟu ceyyap paṭṭa muyaṟci kālattiṉ cēymiyik karutiyum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. tamatu kālattil illāta makkaḷukku-piṟkālattil vāḻap pōkum tam piṉvaruvōrukku-tamatu karuttukaḷit terivikka vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattāl avaṟṟik kallilum, cempilum, piṟavaṟṟilum, makkaḷ poṟittuvikkat talip paṭṭaṉar. ivvāṟu cēymiyil uḷḷavarkaḷukkut tamatu karuttukaḷi aṟivikka makkaḷ mutaṉmutalākak kiyāṇṭa eḻuttukkaḷ evi? avaṟṟi avarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam kaṟṟaṉar? iṉṟu vaḻakkatti luḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷit toṭakkattilēyē avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintaṉarā? allatu vēṟuvitamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷik kiyāṇṭiruntāl avi evvevvāṟellām vaḷarcci yaṭintu iṉṟiya niliyi yaṭintaṉa? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉākkaḷi ārāyntāl ōraḷavu eḻuttukkaliyiṉ tōṟṟattiyum vaḷarcciyiyum aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyum.
kukikaḷil vāḻantu vanta āti maṉitarkaḷum ōviyam varintu vantaṉar eṉpatu avarkaḷ viṭṭup pōyuḷḷa aṟikuṟikaḷāl aṟiyalākum. ivaṟṟuḷ cila iṉṟu koṭikaḷ mutaliya ciṉṉaṅkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ōviyattiṉ mūlam tam karuttukaḷi veḷiyiṭalām eṉṟu aṟinta makkaḷ toliviliruppavarkaḷukkum niliyāka eḻutivippataṟkum itiyē vaḻiyāka amittuk koṇṭaṉar. inta muyaṟciyē ōviya eḻuttukkaḷāka vaḷarntatu. iraṇṭoru karuttuk kaḷiyaṉṟip pala karuttukkaḷi eḻutivarumpoḻutu virivu kāraṇamāka ōviyaṅkaḷ iraṇṭoru varikaḷākac curuṅkiṉa. ivi muḻunili ōviyamāka viḷaṅkāmal ōviya aṟi kuṟikaḷākavē amintaṉa. cīṉa eḻuttukkaḷip pārttavarkaḷ inta uṇmiyi aṟivar. ekiptiya eḻuttukkaḷum, mokañcatārō eḻuttuk kaḷum oru ciṟitu ittakiyaṉavē ākum. nāḷaṭivil anta aṟikuṟi ōviyamum oru collāka niṉṟu antac colli aṟiviyātu antac colliṉ mutal eḻuttiṉimaṭṭilum aṟivikka vantatu. piṟaku colliṉ poruḷiyaṉṟic colliṉ eḻuttukkaḷiyum makkaḷ pirittaṟiya viḻintaṉar. anta viḻivē oli eḻuttukkaḷāka vaḷarcci peṟṟatu. āṉāl vaṭamoḻiyilum, tamiḻil pala iṭaṅkaḷilum eḻuttukkaḷ eṉpavi uyirmey eḻuttukkaḷāka irukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. alakunili peṟuvaṉavaṟṟiyē perumpāṉmiyum taṉittaṉi eḻuttāka eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟi alaku eḻuttukkaḷ (Syllable writing) eṉalām. inta nilikkup piṉṉar āṅkilam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟpōlak kūṭṭeḻuttām alakeḻuttukkaḷip pirittut taṉittaṉi eḻuttāka eḻuti vantaṉar. ōviya eḻuttukkaḷ, ōviya aṟikuṟi eḻuttukkaḷ, oli eḻuttukkaḷ, alaku eḻuttukkaḷ, taṉi eḻuttakkaḷ eṉṟa palavaki nilikaḷāka eḻuttiṉ varalāṟu vaḷarntatu. ivipaṟṟiya virivāṉa vivaraṅkaḷi yeparcaṉ, veṇṭirī pōṉṟa aṟiñar nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.2
kuḻantikaḷiṉ eḻuttup payiṟcikkuriya paruvam:  kuḻanti kaṭku eḻuttup payiṟci aḷippataṟku muṉṉar avarkaḷ ataṟ kēṟṟa paruvatti eytiviṭṭaṉarā eṉṟu kavaṉittal iṉṟiyami yātatu. eḻutukōli viralkaḷāl piṭittu ōr eḻuttiṉ uruvatti amikkum vakiyil tacikaḷ iṇaṅkavēṇṭum. paḷḷikaḷukkuk kuḻantikaḷ vantu cērntavuṭaṉ ellāk kuḻantikaḷiṭamum eḻutuvataṟkāṉa paruvam vantirukkakkūṭum eṉṟu karututal tavaṟu. viralkaḷāl poruḷkaḷip piṭittup payaṉpaṭuttum tiṟaṉ cilarukku virivil kivarappeṟum: cilarukku metuvākattāṉ amiyum. eḻutumpoḻutu kikaḷiṉ tacikaḷum, eḻutu karuvikaḷum, kaṇkaḷum maṉamum oru cēra iyaṅkavēṇṭum. ātalāl ciṟu kuḻantikaḷukku eḻutak kaṟpikkumuṉ palavita pulap payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkavēṇṭum.
tacikaḷiyum piṟa pulaṉkaḷiyum orucēra iyakkuvataṟ kēṟṟavāṟu viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷi amittuk koṭuttāl, kuḻantikaḷiṉ tacikaḷ tam viruppatti niṟivēṟṟum vakiyil iṇaṅkic ceyaṟpaṭum. in niliyil eḻutukōlip piṭittu viruppappaṭi iyakkum tiṟaṉ eytiviṭṭatu eṉṟu karutalām. cātāraṇamākak kuḻantikaḷ paḷḷikku vantu cērnta iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu vāraṅkaḷil eḻuttu vēli taralām. mutal vārattil pēccu, paṭaṅkaḷ vittu viḷiyāṭuvatu; iraṇṭām vārattil atikap pēccu, atika viḷiyāṭṭu, paṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟum coṟkaḷip paṭippatu, paṭaṅkaḷiṉṟip paṭippatu; mūṉṟām vārattil iṉṉum atikap pēccu, viḷiyāṭṭu, atikac coṟkaḷip paṭippatu, eḻutat toṭaṅkuvatu eṉṟavāṟu naṭimuṟit tiṭṭaṅkaḷi amittuk koḷḷalām. cantarppattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟum ivaṟṟi āciriyar amittukkoḷḷalām. eṭutta eṭuppil kuḻantikaḷi eḻutum vēliyil kaṭṭāyappaṭuttakkūṭātu eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ aṟiyavēṇṭum. paṭittaliṉmūlam coṟkaḷum eṇṇaṅkaḷum ēṟpaṭṭa piṟakē avaṟṟi eḻutak kaṟpittal vēṇṭum. paṭippiṟkup piṟakē eḻuttu eṉṟa uṇmiyi āciriyar niṉiviliruttal vēṇṭum. pēccumoḻi tōṉṟiya piṟakē eḻuttumoḻi tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu eḻuttuk kaliyiṉ varalāṟṟiliruntu aṟiyum uṇmi. itiliruntum paṭikkum paḻakkam ōraḷavu kivarappeṟṟa piṟakē eḻuttukkaḷi eḻutak kaṟpittal nalam eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu.
paḻiya muṟippaṭi moḻip payiṟṟalil mutalil kuḻanti kaṭku eḻuttukkaḷi eḻutak kaṟpittaṉar; atilum avaṟṟi neṭuṅ kaṇakku varicippaṭiyum kaṟpittaṉar. kaṟṟa eḻuttuk kaḷik kūṭṭip paṭittalil piṉṉar payiṟci aḷikkappeṟṟatu. immuṟi uḷaviyal muṟikkuc ciṟitum ukantatu aṉṟu. aṉṟiyum, immuṟiyil cuviyum viruppamum ēṟpaṭuvataṟku iṭamumilli. immuṟi moḻik kaṟṟalil veṟuppiyē viḷivikkum. putiyamuṟiyil, pēccup paḻakkam kivarappeṟṟa piṟakum, ciṟitaḷavu paṭikkum paḻakkam kivarappeṟṟa piṟakum eḻuttup payiṟcikku vaḻikōlap peṟukiṉṟatu. tiṭṭamāṉa eḻuttup payiṟci taruvataṟku muṉṉar muṟ payiṟcikaḷāka viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyil cila payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
eḻutuvataṟkumuṉ mēṟkoḷḷattakka muṟpayiṟcikaḷ: niṉittavuṭaṉ kuḻantikaḷāl eḻutamuṭiyātu. eḻutuvataṟku muṉpatākap poruḷaṟivu vēṇṭum; collaṟivu vēṇṭum. ataṟku mutalil pēccum, paṭippum, piṉṉar eḻuttum varuvatē muṟiyākum. eḻutat toṭaṅkuvataṟkumuṉ kuḻantikaḷiṉ ki, muṉki, maṇikkaṭṭu,viralkaḷ, taci nārkaḷ mutaliyavi viruppappaṭi iyaṅka valla payiṟcikaḷip peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum. eḻutukōlkaḷ, kaṟpalaki mutaliya eḻutu karuvikaḷi aṭakki yāḷum vaṉmi peṟavēṇṭum. aintu vayatuvari vīṭṭilirunta kuḻantikaḷ ōraḷavu ittiṟaṉip peṟṟiruttalkūṭum. cevilip paḷḷikaḷilum, kuḻantippaḷḷikaḷilum itaṟkeṉat takka pulap payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ap paḷḷikaḷil payila vāyppillāta kuḻantikaḷiṉ tokitāṉ mikuti. avarkaḷ muṟippaṭi ap payiṟcikaḷip peṟṟirāviṭiṉum vīṭukaḷil viḷiyāṭum oru cila viḷiyāṭṭukkaḷiṉ mūlam oru cila payiṟcikaḷ avarkaḷiṭam iyalpāka amintuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil vantu cērum kuḻanti kaṭku takka viḷiyāṭṭup payiṟcikaḷ koṭuttu eḻutum uṟuppukaḷukkup payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkavēṇṭum. eḻutuvataṟku muṟpaḻakkamākac cīmic cuṇṇak kāmpiṉāl karumpalakiyil tamakku viruppamāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷi variyavum kiṟukkavum vāyppukkaḷ taralām. itaṟku toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil vakuppu aṟic cuvarkaḷil kuḻantikaḷ kiyeṭṭum variyil kaṟuppu cīmikkāri (Cement) pūciya cuvark karumpalaki amikkap paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. itil kuḻantikaḷ tārāḷamāka eḻutuvataṟku mikavum virumpuvar. piṉṉar nērk kōṭukaḷ, vaḷivukaḷ, vaṭṭaṅkaḷ, nēr paṭukkik kōṭukaḷ mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷikkoṇṭu karumpalakiyilum, palappattik koṇṭu kaṟpalakiyilum, eḻutukōlik koṇṭu (Pencil) cāṇit tāḷkaḷilum eḻutac ceyyalām.
tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷi eḻutak kaṟpippataṟku muṉ av veḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruvam maṉattil naṉku amiyavum, viralkaḷiṉ tacikaḷ naṉku payiṟci peṟavum ippayiṟcikaḷit taralām. kuccikaḷ, vitikaḷ, vaḷiyaṅkaḷ ivikaḷik koṇṭu eḻuttukkaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum vēṟu virumpiya uruvaṅkaḷiyum amikkac ceyyalām. avvappoḻutu māṟṟakkūṭiya vakiyil taṭṭil maṇali vittuk kōlkaḷikkoṇṭum eḻutac ceyyalām. ippayiṟci muḻuk kiyum iyaṅkuvataṟku vāyppu aḷippatāl piṉṉar maṇikkaṭṭimaṭṭilum iyakkuvataṟku muṟpayiṟciyāka amikiṉṟatu. eḻuttukkaḷi upput tāḷiṉālum taṭitta paṭṭiṉālum veṭṭi aṭṭikaḷil oṭṭi avveḻuttuk kaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷi viralkaḷiṉāl taṭavip pārkkac ceyyalām. veṭṭa veḷiyilum mutukilum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷi viralāl eḻutik kāṭṭac ceyyalām.  ivvāṟu eḻuti oru ciṟitu paḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭa piṉṉar karumpalakiyil āciriyar caṟṟu maṅkalāka eḻutik kuḻantikaḷ avaṟṟiṉmītu kōliṉik koṇṭē cīmic cuṇṇak kāmpiṉik koṇṭō viḷampum payiṟciyi mikutiyākak koṭukkavēṇṭum.  karumpalakiyil eḻuttukkaḷi aḻiyāta vaṇṇattāl tīṭṭiviṭṭāl pala kuḻantikaḷ avaṟṟiṉmītu eḻutiyum aḻittum payiṟci peṟuvataṟku eḷitāka irukkum.
eḻututal palvēṟu ciṟu iyakkaṅkaḷik koṇṭa mikavum cikkalāṉa oru ceyal. ataṉ oru pakuti iyakkap poṟinuṭpattiyum (Motor mechanism) maṟṟoru pakuti maṉattiṉ vēliyiyum (Work of the intellect) poṟuttuḷḷaṉa.  kuḻantikaḷiṭam eḻutum ceyal evvāṟu naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu?  eḻututalil uḷḷa kūṟukaḷ yāvi?  avaṟṟi evvāṟu kiyāḷavēṇṭum? eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vivaraṅkaḷi māṇṭi sāri ammiyār teḷivāka viḷakkiyuḷḷār.3 ovvoru tamiḻāciriyarkaḷum, annūli, ciṟappāka 26-ām iyalik kaṭṭāyam paṭikka vēṇṭum.
eḻutumpoḻutu uṭkārum nili:  kuḻantikaḷ eḻutum poḻutu uṭalik kōṇalākac cāyttukkoṇṭu uṭkāru vatit toṭakkattiliruntē tavirkka vēṇṭum. toṭak kattil innili tiruttappeṟāviṭiṉ kuḻantikaḷiṉ uṭalil vikāramāṉa amippu ēṟpaṭak kūṭum; mutukum kūṉiviṭum.  nērāka uṭkārntu eḻutumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttavēṇṭum, avarkaḷ uṭkāruvatu avarkaḷukkuc cukamāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  muṉpuṟamāy atikam cāyakkūṭātu.  eḻutukōlik kuḻantikaḷ piṭikkum muṟiyum kavaṉikkappaṭavēṇṭum.  mikavum kiṭṭa allatu eṭṭap piṭittaliyum, mikavum taḷarvāka allatu iṟukap piṭittaliyum tavirkkavēṇṭum.  eḻutu kōli naṭuviralil tāṅkumpaṭi vittu cuṭṭu viralālum kaṭṭi viralālum piṭittukkoṇṭu kaṟpalakiyi iṭatu kiyāl piṭittu valatu muḻaṅkālmītu vittu, caṟṟu iṭappuṟam cāyttuk koṇṭu eḻututal ciṟappuṭiyatu.  kaṇakkap piḷḷi-cāyvu mēciyiṉmītu vittu eḻutap paḻakuvatē cālac ciṟantatu.
cila kiyeḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ
māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttu nallamuṟiyil ami vataṟkut toṭakka nilippaḷḷi vakuppukkaḷiṉ cila kiyeḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ aḷikkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avi:  (1) pārttu eḻututal;  (2) acciṭṭa payiṟcip puttakaṅkaḷil eḻututal; (3) colluvati eḻututal ākiyavi. ivi mūṉṟumē cātāraṇamāka naṭimuṟiyil kiyāḷap peṟṟuvarum muṟikaḷākum.  ivi ovvoṉṟiyumpaṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu ārāyvōm.
pārttu eḻututal:  iḷañciṟārkaḷ etip pārttālum ataṉpaṭiyē pārttu eḻuta virumpukiṉṟaṉar.  ivviruppatti āciriyarkaḷ vaḷaracceytu payaṉpaṭum muṟikaḷil koṇṭu celutta vēṇṭum.  mutalil peyarkaḷ eḻutiya aḻakiya paṭaṅkaḷik kuḻantikaḷukkuk kāṭṭi avaṟṟip pārttu avarkaḷi variyac ceyyalām; appaṭaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiyum kīḻē allatu etirppuṟattil eḻutat tūṇṭalām.  nāḷaṭivil puttakattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷi allatu piṟar eḻutiya coṟkaḷip pārtteḻutac ceyyalām.  paṭaṅkaḷiṉkīḻ eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷ,  miṉṉaṭṭikaḷil kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi muḻuc coṟkaḷākavē eḻutap paḻaka vēṇṭum.  ciṟuciṟu coṟkaḷi eḻutuvatiliruntu vākkiyaṅkaḷi eḻutap paḻakalām.  kaṭṭaḷi aṭṭikaḷ,  viḷamparaṅkaḷ, miṉṉaṭṭikaḷ,  kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiluḷḷa coṟṟoṭarkaḷi appaṭiyē eḻutum payiṟciyit taralām.  ivaṟṟil ōraḷavu tiṟami kivarappeṟṟa piṟaku, iraṇṭām vakuppiliruntu pārttu eḻututalit toṭaṅkalām.  nāḷtōṟum nāṉku allatu aintu varikaḷ vīṭṭil eḻuti varumāṟu vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum. iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu kōṭukaḷ amitta kaṟpalakiyil eḻutac ceyyalām.  coṟkaḷukku iṭiyilum varikaḷukku iṭiyilum ciṟitu iṭamviṭṭu eḻutum paḻakkattit toṭakkattiliruntē vaṟpuṟuttavēṇṭum.  eḻuttukkaḷukkuk kōṭuviṭāmal moṭṭiyāka eḻutum tavaṟāṉa muṟiyi avvappoḻutē tavirkkumpaṭi ceytu tirutta vēṇṭum.  niṟuttaṟkuṟiyi viṭāmal kuṟikkap paḻakkavēṇṭum. mukkiyamāṉa ovvoru vākkiyattiṉ muṭivilum muṟṟup puḷḷiyik kuṟippati vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.
ittakiya eḻuttup payiṟciyik kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil pattu maṇittuḷikkumēl tarutal ākātu. iḷañciṟārkaḷukku eḻuttu vēli virivil maṉakkaḷippiyum viraṟ cōrviyum uṇṭu paṇṇumātaliṉ avarkaḷi atika nēram eḻutac ceytal kūṭātu. aṉṟiyum, aḵtu avarkaḷukku eḻuttil veṟuppu uṇṭākkavum kūṭum.  taṇṭaṉiyākap palamuṟi eḻutac ceyvati āciriyar aṟavē tavirttal vēṇṭum.
pārttu eḻututalic caṟṟu māṟṟiyamittu cila payiṟcikaḷik koṇṭu viḷiyāṭṭu muṟiyilum kaṟpikkalām.  orumiyip paṉmiyākavum paṉmiyi orumiyākavum māṟṟi eḻutum payiṟcikaḷ, kāla vēṟupāṭukaḷi māṟṟi eḻutum payiṟcikaḷ, ēṟṟa aṭimoḻikaḷip peyarkaḷukku viṉikaḷukkum koṭuttut toṭar moḻikaḷi virikkac ceyyum payiṟcikaḷ mutaliyavi itaṟku mikavum ēṟṟavi.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil ivi pōṉṟa mikavum eḷiya payiccikaḷik koṭuttu avarkaḷuṭiya coṟkaḷañciya aṟiviyum, ilakkaṇa marapukaḷit terintu vaḻaṅkum āṟṟaliyum oruvāṟu tēṟalām.  iṭinili vakuppukaḷil ivaṟṟic caṟṟu virivāṉa muṟiyil naṭattalām.
naṭattum muṟipaṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ: nalla naṭiyum poruḷum potinta pakutikaḷip poṟukki eṭuttup payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum. kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil naṭanta pakutikaḷiyē poṟukki eṭuttalvēṇṭum. iṭinili vakuppukkaḷil neṭṭuruc ceyyattakka nalla urinaṭi ceyyuṭ pakutikaḷip pārttu eḻutac ceytāl ciṟanta payaṉi viḷivikkum. eḻuttuk kūṭṭalil tavaṟakkūṭiya coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum uḷḷaṭakkiya pakutikaḷip pārttu eḻutac ceytalāl naṟpayaṉ viḷiyum.  māṇākkarkaḷic collikkoṇṭē eḻutac ceytu piḻikaḷiruppiṉ avaṟṟit tiruttumpaṭi tūṇṭa vēṇṭum. mikavum kuṟinta aḷavil toṭaṅki āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil uyarnta aḷavukku varumpaṭi payiṟcikaḷi amittuk kōṭal ciṟantatu. ivvakiyil pārttu eḻutuvatu, kēṭṭu eḻutuvatu, niṉiviliruntu eḻutuvatu eṉa mūṉṟuvitamāṉa payiṟcikaḷit taralām.  ovvoru payiṟciyum kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkuḷ muṭiya vēṇṭum.  toṭakkattil aintu maṇit tuḷiyākavum, nāḷaṭivil paṭippaṭiyākap pattu allatu paṉṉiraṇṭu maṇittuḷi variyilum eḻutum payiṟcikaḷ ciṟantavi.
ip payiṟciyāl ēṟpaṭum naṉmikaḷ:  kiyeḻuttu tiruttamāka amivataṟku iḵtu oru vaḻiyākum; teḷivākavum vēkamākavum amivataṟku oru tūṇṭukōlākavum amikiṉṟatu.  ip payiṟci māṇākkarkaḷiṉ uṟṟunōkkum paḻakkattiyum āṟṟaliyum vaḷarttu eḻutum pakutikaḷ teḷivāka viḷakka maṭintu uḷḷattil amiyap payaṉpaṭum.  itaṉāl nalla coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, maraput toṭarkaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ, ariya collāṭcikaḷ mutaliyavi paḻakkattil amittu avi vaḻaṅkum iṭaṅkaḷum muṟikaḷum viḷakkam eytum.  itu kavaṉak kuṟivākavuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷukkuk kavaṉatti ūṭṭavallatu.  niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷiṉ payaṉi naṉku uṇarvataṟku itu tuṇiyāka irukkum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttu vēliyil nērum piḻikaḷ maṟimukamāka ippayiṟciyāl nīṅka iṭam uṇṭu.  itik kaṭṭurip payiṟcikku oru vaḻikāṭṭiyākavum muṟpayiṟciyākavum koḷḷavum kūṭum.
(2) acciṭṭa payiṟcip puttaṅkaḷil eḻututal: ip payiṟci mikavum arumiyāṉatum mikunta muyaṟciyi vēṇṭu vatum ākum.  mūṉṟām vakuppiliruntē itit toṭaṅka vēṇṭum.  mutalil nāṉku kōṭukaḷ uḷḷa payiṟcip puttakaṅkaḷi vaḻaṅkalām.  eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uṭal, viciṟi, vilaṅku ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uyaraṅkaḷik kāṭṭa ikkōṭukaḷ utavum.  mutalil periya eḻuttukkaḷāl ākiya varikaḷiyum, piṟaku varavarac ciṟiya eḻuttukkaḷākik koṇṭu varum varikaḷiyumuṭiya puttaṅkaḷip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum.  caṟṟup paḻakiya piṉṉar iraṇṭu kōṭuḷḷa payiṟcip puttakaṅkaḷiyum, piṉṉar oru kōṭuḷḷa puttakaṅkaḷiyum koṭukkalām. piṉṉar kōṭu illāta puttakaṅkaḷil eḻutac collām.
payiṟcip puttakaṅkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutumpoḻutu kīḻiruntu mēlē eḻutikkoṇṭu cellum muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟavēṇṭum.  mēliruntu vantāl, mutalil uḷḷa accu variyic cariyākak kavaṉiyātu tām oruvēḷi tavaṟāka eḻutackūṭiya muṉ uḷḷa variyiyē pārttu tavaṟākavē eḻuta nēriṭum.  tamiḻukku uriya ciṟanta ceṅkuttāka eḻutum muṟiyit toṭakkattiliruntē vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  ceṅkuttāka eḻutiṉāl tāṉ tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ aḻakākavum tōṟṟap polivuṭaṉum tikaḻum.
im muṟiyi ikkāla muṟivallār virumpuvatilli.  āciriyar karumpalakiyil eḻutik kāṭṭa atip pārttu eḻututalē ciṟantatu eṉṟu ivarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  eḻutum poḻutu āciriyariṉ ki vaḷital, eḻuttu eṅkut toṭaṅki eppaṭi pōkiṉṟatu, eḻutukōlip piṭikkum muṟi, eḻuttiṉ uruvam mutaliyavaṟṟi māṇākkar kaṇkūṭākak kavaṉittu āciriyarip piṉpaṟṟalām eṉṟu avarkaḷ eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.
(3) colluvati eḻututal: āciriyar colvatik kēṭṭu māṇākkarkaḷ pārātu eḻutuvatu colluvati eḻututal (Dictation) eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum.  pārttu eḻutuvativiṭak kēṭṭu eḻutuvatil cuṟucuṟuppum virivum atikamāka uṇṭu.  coṟ kaḷiluḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷip piḻiyaṟa māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutu kiṉṟaṉarā eṉṟu cōtikkavum, piḻiyiṉṟi eḻutuvatil payiṟci taravum iti ovvoru vakuppilum kiyāḷuvatu avaciyamākum.  pāṭa vēḷip paṭṭiyil (Time-table) oru pāṭa vēḷi itaṟkeṉa amikkap paṭṭiruppiṉum perumpālāṉa paḷḷikaḷil itu cariyāka naṭipeṟātatāl paḷḷit taṇikkiyāḷarkaḷ itu cōmpēṟi yāciriyarkaḷukku oru poḻutupōkkāka amikiṉṟatu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar; ataṉāl avarkaḷ itaṟkeṉat taṉippāṭa vēḷi amippati oppuk koḷḷuvatilli.
iṉṟu naṭimuṟiyil colluvati eḻutum payiṟci cariyāṉa muṟiyil mēṟkoḷḷappaṭuvatilli.  itic cariyāka naṭattuvataṟku āciriyarkaḷ ik kuṟippukkaḷit tuṇiyākak koḷḷalām:  ippayiṟcikkeṉat tērn teṭutta pakutiyi āciriyar orumuṟi paṭittuk kāṭṭa vēṇṭum; māṇākkarkaḷip paṭittuk koḷḷumpaṭi ceytālum ceyyalām.  collum poḻutu āciriyarkaḷ coṟkaḷit tirumpat tirumpac collakkūṭātu.  poruḷ toṭarntu ceṉṟu niṟkum iṭaṅkaḷil maṭṭilumē niṟuttavēṇṭum.  naṉṟāka muḻuvatum kēṭṭuk karuttuṇarnta piṉṉarē eḻutat toṭaṅkavēṇṭum eṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷukku aṟivuṟutta vēṇṭum.  mutalil kēṭṭa coṟkaḷi eḻutat toṭaṅki aṭuttu varum coṟkaḷ teriyātu viḻikkakkūṭātu eṉa aṟivuṟuttutal perumpayaṉ viḷikkum.  eḻutumpoḻutē āciriyar coṉṉa coṟkaḷi mella vāykkuṭ collik koṇṭē eḻutumpaṭi ceyyavēṇṭum.  immuṟi mēṟ paṭippilum vāḻkkiyilum peritum payaṉpaṭum.  kiyeḻuttu, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷ, kūṭiyavari, coṟkaḷip poruḷ nōkkip pirittal mutaliyavaṟṟik kavaṉikkumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttavēṇṭum.
kēṭkum tiṟaṉik kaṇṭaṟivatiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, ilakkaṇa marapukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi vaḻaṅkum āṟṟaliyum itil kalantu colluvati eḻututali māṟṟi yamikkalām.  āciriyar oru coṟṟoṭari ōr uruvattil eṭuttuc colluvār.  māṇākkarkaḷ atik kēṭṭu māṟṟumpaṭi kūṟappaṭum uruvattil māṟṟi eḻutuvar.  immuṟiyi iṭinili vakuppukkaḷil naṉku kiyāḷalām.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷukku eḻuttuk kūṭṭum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷip payiṟcikaḷākak koḷḷalām.  viṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷiyum coṟkaḷiyum nirapputal, coṟkaḷ kaṭṭutal, otta ōciyuḷḷa coṟkaḷik kaṇṭaṟital, etirc coṟkaḷi yaṟital, collēṇi amittal, putiya coṟkaḷi akara variciyil tokuttal mutaliyavi avaṟṟuḷ cila payiṟcikaḷākum. avaṟṟi āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpippatil tattam tiṟamik kēṟṟavāṟu ciṟappāṉa muṟikaḷik kiyāḷalām.
ip payiṟciyiṉāl viḷiyum naṉmikaḷ: pēcum moḻikaḷi virivākavum cariyākavum karuttuṇarntu koḷvataṟku iḵtu oru ciṟanta payiṟciyākap payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.  kēṭṭavi uḷḷattilum naṉku amikkappaṭum.  itu kavaṉikkum tiṟaṉiyum kēṭkum tiṟaṉiyum maṉa orumiyiyum naṉku vaḷarkkiṉṟatu.  niṟuttaṟkuṟikaḷic cariyāka vaḻaṅkuvatil payiṟci aḷikkiṉṟatu.  kiyeḻuttiṉ viriviyum teḷiviyum vaḷarppataṟkut tuṇiyāka uḷḷatu.  āciriyar kaṟpitta uccarippi māṇākkar cariyākap piṉpaṟṟukiṉṟaṉarā eṉpatik kaṇṭaṟiyavum, eḻuttuk kūṭṭaliṉ aṟivik kaṇṭaṟiyavum, eccoṟkaḷit tavaṟāka eḻutukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatik kaṇṭaṟiyavum itic ciṟanta vaḻiyākac ceyyalām.


nalla kiyeḻuttiṉ paṇpukaḷ
kiyeḻutti ikkālattil cariyākak kavaṉippavarkaḷ illi.  kaṟṟut tuṟipōkiya perum pulavarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttum naṉṟāka iruppatilli.  iḷamiyil tiruntiya kiyeḻuttu amiyātatē itaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṟu collavēṇṭum.  namatu nāṭṭut tanti kāntiyaṭikaḷ tam kiyeḻuttippaṟṟi ivvāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār:
uṭaṟ payiṟciyi vilakkiyataṉāl nāṉ atikam ciramappaṭavilli.  vēṟōṉṟi vilakkiyataṉāl iṉṉum ciramap paṭukiṉṟēṉ.  nalla kiyeḻuttu kalvikku iṉṟiya miyātatu aṉṟu eṉṟa maṉappāṉmi eṉṉiṭam evvāṟu amintatō teriyavilli. iṅkilāntu ceṉṟapōtukūṭa iḵtu eṉpāl nilittiruntatu.  piṉṉar nāṉ teṉ āḵpirikkāvilirunta poḻutu aṅkup piṟantu aṅkēyē kalvikaṟṟa iḷiñarkaḷiṭamum vaḻakkaṟiñarkaḷiṭamum nalla aḻakāṉa kiyeḻuttiṉik kaṇṭapoḻutu nāṉ mikavum nāṇappaṭṭēṉ; kiyeḻuttil karuttiṉic celuttātatu kuṟittu mikavum varuntiṉēṉ.  kuṟipāṭāṉa kalviyāl tāṉ nalla kiyeḻuttu amiyavilli eṉak karuta vēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇmi eṉakkup pulappaṭṭatu.  piṉṉar eṉ kiyeḻut tic cīrpaṭutta muyaṉṟēṉ; āṉāl, atu kālaṅkaṭanta ceyal.  eṉṉuṭiya kuṟipāṭu ovvoru iḷiñaṉum yuvatikkum ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka irukkaṭṭum.  nalla kiyeḻuttu amivatu kalviyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta pakuti eṉpati avarkaḷ aṟiyaṭṭum.  kuḻantikaṭku eḻutak kaṟpippataṟkumuṉ ōviyam variyum kaliyik kaṟpikka vēṇṭum.  malarkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ piṟa poruḷkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik kuḻantikaḷ uṟṟu nōkkuvatip pōlavē ovvōr eḻuttiyum uṟṟu nōkki aṟiyavēṇṭum.  apporuḷ kaḷi ōviyamāka variyak kaṟṟapiṟakutāṉ eḻutak kaṟkat toṭaṅkavēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu kiyeḻuttik kaṟṟāl aḻakāṉa kiyeḻuttu amiyum.4
ivvāṟu pala aṟiñarkaḷ tam kiyeḻuttuc cariyāka amiyāmiyik kuṟittu varuntiyuḷḷaṉar.  cila aṟiñarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttu mika naṉṟākavum amintirukkiṉṟatu.  aṟiñar kaḷuṭiya vāḻkki varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷik kaṟpikkum poḻutu intak kūṟiṉi āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷukku valiyuṟuttik kūṟavēṇṭum.
iṉṟiya ulakil eḻuttu vēli kaṭalpōl parantu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.  accup poṟikaḷum taṭṭaccup poṟikaḷum kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟiruntālum kiyiṉāl eḻutappeṟum vēli kuṟiyavē illi.  vēliyiṉ mikutiyāl makkaḷ virivāka eḻuti eḻuttiṉ aḻakiyum teḷiviyum paṟi koṭukkiṉṟaṉar.  nalla kiyeḻuttu nalla kalvikkum maṉap payiṟcikkum aṟikuṟi eṉṟu collām.  nalla kiyeḻuttu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tacinārkaḷi aṭakki yāḷvatu maṭṭumaṉṟi maṉattiyum nalla muṟiyil payilvikkiṉṟatu.  aḻakāṉa nalla muttup pōṉṟa kiyeḻutti yārum virumpip paṭippar; kaṇkaḷil oṟṟik koḷvar.  aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭavaṟṟi nalla kiyeḻuttiṉ paṇpukaḷāka koḷḷalām.
1. teḷivu: oruvar eḻutuvatu piṟar paṭippataṟkākavē.  eṉavē, piṟarukku viḷaṅkumpaṭi teḷivāy eḻutuvatē mutaṟkaṇ vēṇṭap peṟuvatu.  taṉittaṉiyāka eḻutiṉāltāṉ tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ teḷivāka viḷaṅkum.  cilar āṅkilak kiyeḻuttup pōl kōviyāyc caṅkilipōl cērttu eḻutuvar.  ivvāṟu eḻutiṉāl tāṉ virivik koṭukkum eṉpatu avar koḷki.  cērttu eḻutikkoṇṭē pōṉāl ovvōr eḻuttukkum eṭutteṭuttu eḻuta vēṇṭiyirātu.  itaṉāl kālak kuṟivum virivum uṇṭākattāṉ kūṭum.  āṉāl, taṉittaṉiyāka eḻutuvatō tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukkut teḷivuṭaṉ poliviyum tarum; ituvē tamiḻ muṟiyum marapum ākum.  aṉṟiyum, coṟkaḷukkiṭiyilum iṭam viṭappeṟutal vēṇṭum.  eḻuttukkaḷik kiṟukkic cellāmal muttu muttāka eḻuta vēṇṭum; moṭṭiyāka irāmal kompukaḷ, vaḷivukaḷ, viciṟikaḷ, cuḻikaḷ ivikaḷic cariyāka amittu eḻuta vēṇṭum.  toṭakkanili vakuppukkaḷil eḻutak kaṟpippatil āciriyarkaḷ eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷik kuḻantikaḷukkuc cariyāka uṇartta vēṇṭum.  eḻuttukkaḷi amippatil tavaṟāṉa paḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭāl, piṉpu at tavaṟāṉa paḻakkattip pōkkuvatu kaṭiṉam.  ākavē, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruva amippip payilac ceyvatil karuttūṉṟik kavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum.  cilar kompi mēliruntu eḻutuvar.  cilar ma vic cuḻiyiliruntu toṭaṅkuvar.  ṉi, ḷi, li, ṇi, kaḷi eḻutumpoḻutu ṉa, ḷa, la, ṇa kkaḷi eḻutip piṉṉar lavic cērppar cilar; cilar mēṟpuḷḷiyi viṭṭuviṭukiṉṟaṉar.  ittakiya piḻikaḷi avvappoḻutu eṭuttuk kāṭṭit tirutta vēṇṭum.
neṭuṅ kaṇakku variciyil eḻuttukkaḷik kaṟpikkāmal ṭa, pa, ma mutaliya nērkōṭṭu eḻuttukkaḷit toṭaṅkuvatē virivilum eḷitilum eḻuttup paḻakkam viḷivikkum eṉṟu karutuvatu ārāyattakkatu.  āciriyarkaḷ ṭa, pa, ma, ḻa, ya mutaliya eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟiliruntu maṟṟoṉṟu amiyum muṟiyi varuvittuk kāṭṭalām.  eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruva oṟṟumi paṟṟi avaṟṟi aintu iṉaṅkaḷāka pirikkalām eṉṟu orucilar karutukiṉṟaṉar.  avi: (a) ṭa, pa, ya, ma, ḻa; (ā) ī, ra, na, ṅa, ai, ka, ca, ta; (i) e, ē, ña, o, ō, au; (ī) ḷa, va, la, ṉa, ṇa; (u) a, ā, i, u, ū, ivaṟṟik kaṟṟapiṉ uyir mey eḻuttukkaḷi eḻutuvatu eḷitākum.5  uyir meyyeḻuttiṉ amippil kāl (), veḷḷikkāl (ḷa), oṟṟik kompu (), iraṭṭik kompu (), vaḷivukaḷ, cuḻikaḷ, viciṟikaḷ mutaliya kūṟukaḷit tiruttamāka eḻutap payiṟṟavēṇṭum.  ka, ca, ta pōṉṟa eḻuttukkaḷi moṭṭiyāka eḻutātu mūkku vittu eḻutumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttavēṇṭum.
eḻutap paḻakumpōtu āciriyar eḻutuvatip pārttē kuḻantikaḷ eḻutat toṭaṅkuvar.  coṟkaḷi uccarittuk koṇṭē āciriyarkaḷ karumpalakiyil eḻutik kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.  āciriyar eḻutum muṟiyip pārttu, avarkaḷ ki cellum pōkkik kavaṉittuk kuḻantikaḷum eḻutuvar.  acciṭṭa payiṟcip puttakaṅkaḷip pārttu eḻutuvativiṭa immuṟi mikavum ciṟantatu.  itaṟku āciriyariṉ kiyeḻuttu aḻakāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  avaruṭiya kiyeḻutti mātiriyāka amittukkoṇṭu kuḻantikaḷ piṉpaṟṟutal eḷitu.  eṉavē, āciriyar taṉ kiyeḻutti mutalil cīrpaṭuttik koḷḷavēṇṭum.
2. virivu: kālam karuntaṉam eṉpatu ōr āṅkilap paḻamoḻi.  kālam virintu cellukiṉṟatu. ciṉaṉāṭ palpiṇi ciṟṟaṟivuṭiya makkaḷ kariyaṟṟa kalviyik kaṟka vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.  kaṟṟatu kimaṇṇaḷavākavum kallātatu ulakaḷavākavum uḷḷatu.  eṉavē, kaṟpaṉavaṟṟiyum eḻuta vēṇṭiyaṉa vaṟṟiyum virivākac ceyya vēṇṭum.  teḷivip paṟikoṭukkātu virivāka eḻutum paḻakkam ēṟpaṭavēṇṭum.  cila payiṟcikaḷāl ivvirivik kivarac ceyyalām; eḻuttiṉ viriviyum cila āyvukaḷāl tēṟalām.  āciriyar eḻutu eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭatum māṇākkarkaḷi tērnteṭutta oru pakutiyi eḻutat toṭaṅkac ceytu, niṟuttu eṉṟatum niṟuttac ceytu, akkāla aḷavil oru maṇittuḷikku carācari ettaṉi eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭalām.  immuṟiyāl pala māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttu vēkatti oppiṭṭup pārkkalām.  tiṅkaḷ oṉṟukku immātiri iraṇṭu mūṉṟu āyvukaḷ naṭatti māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttu vēkam muṉṉēṟṟam aṭikiṉṟatā eṉpatic cōtik kalām.  vakuppu uyara uyara eḻuttiṉ virivum atikarittal vēṇṭum.  mūṉṟām vakuppil oru maṇittuḷikku 35-40 eḻuttukkaḷiyum, nāṉkāvatu vakuppil 50-60 eḻuttuk kaḷiyum, aintām vakuppil 60 eḻuttukkaḷiyum eḻutalām eṉṟu kaṇak kiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  vaḷarntavarkaḷ 100 eḻuttukkaḷ variyil eḻutalām.
3. aḻaku: eḻuttukkaḷ aḻakāka iruntāltāṉ avaṟṟip pārttup paṭippatil makiḻcci uṇṭākum.  eṉavē, teḷiviṟkum viriviṟkum aṭuttapaṭiyāka kavaṉikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyatu aḻaku ākum. intap paḻakkattit toṭakkattiliruntē ceyvatu naṉṟu. eḻuttukkaḷ aḻakāka irukka vēṇṭumāṉāl avaṟṟiṉ uruvam cariyāka irukkavēṇṭum. avi mēlum kīḻum toṭum iṭaṅkaḷum, mēl vilaṅku kīḻ vilaṅkukaḷum, eḻuttukkaḷiṉ uruva aḷavukaḷum orē aḷavil irukka vēṇṭum. coṟkaḷil eḻuttukkum, vākkiyattil collukkum collukkum, oru pakkattil varikkum varikkum viṭappaṭum iṭiveḷiyiṉ aḷavu orē aḷavāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  varikōṇā tiruttalum, tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷic cāyāmal ceṅkuttāka eḻututalum avaciyam kavaṉikkappaṭa vēṇṭiya kūṟukaḷ ākum.6  kaṭikaḷiṉ kaṇakku eḻutu kiṉṟavarkaḷum piṟarum eḻuttukkaḷukkillāta curuḷkoṭuttu eḻutuvatip pōṉṟa paḻakkam ēṟpaṭātirukkumāṟu pārttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  ivi eḻuttukku oruvita aḻakik koṭuppiṉum virivik keṭukkum.
māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ aḻakāka amivataṟkup paḷḷikaḷil cila muṟikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷalām.  acciṭṭa payiṟcip puttakaṅkaḷip pārtteḻutap paḻakiya oṉṟiraṇṭu tiṅkaḷukkup piṟaku, nāḷtōṟum pārtteḻutum payiṟci taravēṇṭum; pattu maṇittuḷi aḷavu kālam itaṟkup pōtumāṉatu.  oru muḻup pāṭa vēḷi tēviyilli.7  nāḷtōṟum toṭakkanili vakuppumāṇākkarkaṭku pattu maṇittuḷi aḷavu kiyeḻuttup payiṟci iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  itit tamiḻppāṭa nērattiltāṉ eḻuta vēṇṭum eṉṟa variyaṟi vēṇṭuvatilli.  ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷum tāy moḻiyil naṭipeṟuvatāl iti eppoḻutu vēṇṭu māṉālum aḷikkalām.  māṇākkarkaḷē tattam kiyeḻuttuk kaḷi matippiṭṭup pārkka vāyppukkaḷ taruvatu naṉṟu.  vāra iṟutiyil ellōruṭiya kiyeḻuttut tāḷkaḷiyum poruṭkāṭciyil vittu avarkaḷiyē taram pirikkat tūṇṭalām.  mutal taram eṉṟu cilavaṟṟip poṟukki eṭuttu vakuppu viḷamparap palakiyil vittuc ciṟappikkalām.  ciṟanta kiyeḻuttu vakikaḷic cērttu āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil paḷḷipporuṭkāṭci viḻāvil viḷamparap paṭutta vēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu ovvoru vakuppiṟkum ēṟṟamātiri matippu aḷavu amittukkoṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttil aḻaku paṟṟiya kūṟukaḷi vaḷarkkalām.  vakuppuc carācari aḷavu matippu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ muṉ iruntāl avarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttil āṇṭutōṟum muṉṉēṟṟattik kāṇalām.  ivvitam payiṟci aḷikkappeṟṟāl aintu āṇṭup paṭippukkup piṟaku aḻakum teḷivumuḷḷa ciṟanta kiyeḻuttu māṇākkarkaḷiṭam amiyum.  tiṭṭamāka oru muṟiyi amittukkoḷḷātu kiyeḻuttuc cīrpaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟu maṭṭilum virumpuvatil payaṉ illi.
nalla kiyeḻutti vaḷarkkum vaḻikaḷ: toṭakka nilip paḷḷikaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ eytum tiṟaṉkaḷil kiyeḻuttu mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu.  āṉāl, ati oruvarum cariyākak kavaṉippatilli.  tamiḻp pāṭattilaṉṟi ēṉiya pāṭaṅkaḷilum kiyeḻuttiṉ teḷiviyum viriviyum aḻakiyum vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ iyalpāka eḻutum ellāvaṟṟiyum kavaṉittāl, avarkaḷ avaṟṟik kavaṉattuṭaṉ eḻutuvar.  nalla urinaṭi, pānaṭip pakutikaḷi eḻutac ceytum, colluva teḻutali cila camayam mēṟkoṇṭu, pārttavaṟṟi eḻutac ceytum nalla kiyeḻuttu vaḷarkkalām.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kiyeḻuttil kāṇum kuṟikaḷi avarkaḷik koṇṭē tiruttavēṇṭum.  cariyāka eḻutātavarkaḷi mīṇṭum eḻutac ceytum, mikak kēṭāka eḻutuvōrukkut taṉiyāka vēli koṭuttum nalla kiyeḻutti vaḷarkkat tuṇi ceyyalām.  māṇākkarkaḷ kiyeḻuttil teḷivum virivum paṟikoṭuk- kātirukkumvari avarkaḷ kiyeḻuttil kāṇum taṉittaṉmiyit taṭukkakkūṭātu.  viṭiyēṭukaḷi matippiṭuvatil kiyeḻuttiṟkum tuppuraviṟkum taṉiyāka matippeṇkaḷi vaḻaṅkum paḻakkatti mēṟkoḷḷalām.  māṇākkarkaḷiyē avaravarkaḷ kiyeḻuttit taram pirittuk kāṭṭumpaṭi kūṟalām.  ivaṟṟip poruṭkāṭci viḻāvil vittu avarkaḷi ūkkuvikkalām.
kīḻvakuppukkaḷil eḻuttup pāṭattiṭṭam: araciṉar vakuttuḷḷa toṭakkanilip paḷḷip pāṭat tiṭṭattil aṭiyiṟkaṇṭavi kiyeḻuttu paṟṟiya pāṭattiṭṭamākum: 
mutal vakuppu: kuḻantikaḷ tāṅkaḷ paṭitta coṟkaḷiyum ciṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷiyum karumpalaki, paṭam, miṉṉaṭṭi, piṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil eḻutiyiruppatip pārttu atip pōl tāmum eḻutat tārāḷamāyp paḻakutal.
iraṇṭām vakuppu: eḻutukōlic cariyāṉa muṟiyil piṭikkavum, teḷivāyum virivāyum eḻutavum kaṟpati vaṟpuṟuttal.  paṭitta pāṭaṅkaḷil viṉavappeṟum viṉākkaḷukku ovvoru vākkiyattil viṭi eḻutum tiṟamiyi vaḷarttal.
