iṉṟum āṅkilap paṭippil moḻipeyarppi oru kuṟiyākak kiyāṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉār.  atil kuṟikaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟu evvaḷavu valiyuṟuttik kūṟappaṭiṉum, ammuṟi iṉṉum vaḻakkattilirukkattāṉ ceykiṉṟatu.  ayal moḻiyil viḷaṅkāta karuttup poruḷi coṟkaḷiyum, coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum, moḻi peyarttalāl kūṭiyavari eḷitil viḷakkiṭa muṭikiṉṟatu; kālam, muyaṟci mutaliyavi vīṇākāmal māṇākkarkaḷ poruḷi uṇarntu koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṟku aiyam ciṟitu illi.  moḻi peyarkkappaṭumpoḻutu ayal moḻic coṟkaḷ maṉattil naṉku patintu ammoḻiyilum aṟivu kivarappeṟukiṉṟatu eṉṟu karutuvatil uṇmi illāmal illi.
moḻipeyarppil iruvaki uṇṭu.  oṉṟu, ayal moḻiyiliruntu tāymoḻiyil peyarppatu; maṟṟoṉṟu, tāymoḻiyiliruntu ayal moḻiyil peyarppatu.  muṉṉataṟkut tāymoḻiyaṟivu atikamum, piṉṉataṟku ayal moḻiyaṟivu atikamum iruttal vēṇṭum.  muṉṉatāl piṟamoḻic celvaṅkaḷ tamiḻ moḻikku varavum, piṉṉatāl tāy moḻiyiluḷḷa tirukkuṟaḷ, tiruvācakam, kamparāmāyaṇam pōṉṟa kalik celvaṅkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷil paravum vāyppukkaḷ ēṟpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  iṉṟu āṅkila moḻi pōṉṟa veḷināṭṭu moḻikaḷiliruntu ettaṉiyō aṟiviyaltuṟic celvaṅkaḷum, vaṅkāḷam, kucarātti, marātti, inti mutaliya uḷnāṭṭu moḻikaḷiliruntu ettaṉiyō putiṉam pōṉṟa putiya ilakkiya vakikaḷum moḻipeyarkkappeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.  muṉṉatik kīḻ, mēl vakuppukkaḷilum, piṉṉati mēl vakuppukkaḷilum kaṟpikkalām.  tamiḻiliruntu āṅkilattukkup peyarkkum payiṟcikaḷ naṭimuṟiyil tarappeṟuvatilli.  avaciyam avi tarappeṟutal vēṇṭum.
moḻi peyarppil nāṉku paṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  mutaṟpaṭiyil ayal moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷum coṟṟoṭarkaḷum karutti yaṟivataṟku maṭṭilum moḻi peyarkkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  iraṇṭāvatu paṭiyil ivvāṟu moḻi peyarkkum paḻakkam kuṟintu pakutikaḷip paṭikkum poḻutē cantarppaṅkaḷi yoṭṭic coṟkaḷiṉ allatu coṟṟoṭarkaḷiṉ poruḷ kaḷi aṟintu koḷḷa muṭikiṉṟatu.  mūṉṟāvatu paṭiyil iru moḻiyiṉ vākkiya amippukkaḷiyum, coṟṟoṭar maraput toṭar nuṭpaṅkaḷiyum aṟintu koḷḷukiṉṟōm.  nāṉkāvatu paṭiyil iru moḻikaḷiṉ poḻutuppōkku, naṭippōkku, uyirōṭṭam mutaliya paṇpukaḷi aṟiyum tiṟaṉ kivarap peṟukiṉṟatu.  
moḻipeyarppil kavaṉikka vēṇṭiya cila kuṟippukkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟi īṇṭu nōkkuvōm: collukkuc col moḻipeyarttal cariyāṉa moḻi peyarppākātu.  piṟamoḻic coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, maraput toṭarkaḷ, paḻamoḻikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷic collukkuc col kūṟutal eṉpatu iyalātatoṉṟu. appaṭip peyarppiṉum appakuti uyiraṟṟa cavampōl tōṉṟum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, āṅkila moḻiyiliruntu tamiḻ moḻikku oru pakutiyip peyarttuk kūṟumpoḻutu annāṭṭiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkam, naṭi uṭi pāvaṉi, eṇṇam, uṇarcci, cūḻnili, moḻit toṭaramippu mutaliyavaṟṟi nammavaṟṟuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭum.  āṉāl, collukkuc col cila coṟṟoṭar kaḷukkup poruḷ taralām.  eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka Turn the back-òw mutuku kāṭṭu, allatu venniṭu; Face is the index of the mind-mf¤â‹ aḻaku mukattil teriyum eṉpavaṟṟik kūṟalām.
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu ellāt toṭarkaḷiyum ivvāṟu moḻi peyarttal oṇṇātu.  Take the seat, breathed his last eṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷi aṅṅaṉam moḻi peyarttal ākātu.  ivaṟṟi muṟiyē amar, āvi pirintatu eṉṟu moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭum. Arms in arms eṉpatip puyattoṭu puyam eṉpativiṭa tōḷōṭu tōḷ eṉpatē ciṟantatu.  Nose-cut eṉpati avamāṉa maṭital eṉṟu moḻi peyarttalē cari.9 “Money makes many things” eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟṟoṭari īṭṭi eṭṭiṉa maṭṭum pāyum, paṇam pātaḷam maṭṭum cellum eṉṟō, paṇam pattum ceyyum eṉṟō moḻi peyarkkalām.  “As clear as a crystal” eṉpati uḷḷaṅki nellikkaṉi pōla eṉṟu tamiḻ marapi oṭṭi moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭum. “All that glitters is not gold” eṉṟa coṟṟoṭari miṉṉuvatellām poṉṉalla eṉṟu moḻipeyarppatiṉum veḷuttatellām pālalla eṉṟu tamiḻ marapup paṭi moḻi peyarppatē ēṟpuṭittu.  ‘Face is the index of the mind’ eṉṟa āṅkilac coṟṟoṭari akattiṉ aḻaku mukattil teriyum eṉṟu moḻi peyarttal cariyāṉa moḻi peyarppu ākum.  eṉavē, moḻipeyarppil marapu muṟi piṟaḻakkūṭātu eṉpati uṇaravēṇṭum.  moḻi peyarppip paṭittāl aḵtu iyalpākavum toṭarpākavum kōviyākavum irukkavēṇṭum.  moḻi peyarkkappaṭum pakuti putitākat tamiḻil oruvarāl eḻutappaṭṭatupōl amiyavēṇṭum; paṭippavarukku mūlappakutiyiṉ niṉivē varakkūṭātu. ji. yū. pōp, va.ve.cu. aiyar, kavimaṇi, cakkaravartti irācakōpālāccāriyār, vipulānanta aṭikaḷ pōṉṟa moḻipeyarppukkaḷ aṟiñarkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarpput tuṟiyil pukukiṉṟavarkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka amiyum irumoḻikaḷiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷi naṉkaṟintālaṉṟi moḻi peyarppuc ciṟappāka amiyātu.
vakuppil moḻi peyarppi naṭattumpoḻutu āciriyar cila paṭikaḷi niṉivilirutta vēṇṭum.  moḻi peyarkka vēṇṭiya āṅkilap pakutiyi āciriyar oru muṟikku mummuṟi muṟṟilum karuttuṇarum variyil teḷivāka niṟuttip paṭikka vēṇṭum.  iṭiyiṭiyē aruñcoṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷik kūṟikkoṇṭē cellalām.  iṟutip paṭiyiltāṉ iti collavēṇṭum.  aṭuttu oru cila māṇākkarkaḷit teḷivāka, niṟuttip paṭikkum paṭic ceyyavēṇṭum.  pakutiyiṉ uṭkiṭakkiyi māṇākkar aṟintuḷḷaṉarā eṉpati oru cila viṉākkaḷāl terintu avarkaḷ aṟintiruntāl moḻi peyarppi vāymoḻiyākat toṭaṅkālam.  ovvoruvariyum ovvoru vākkiyamāka moḻi peyarkkumpaṭi ceytu avaṟṟiluḷḷa tavaṟukaḷip piṟa māṇākkarkaḷik koṇṭu tiruttalām.  pakuti muḻuvatum vāymoḻiyāka nalla muṟiyil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa piṟaku, eḻutum payiṟciyit taralām.  toṭakkattil ivvāṟu virivāṉa muṟiyik kiyāḷa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi paṭippaṭiyāka ivaṟṟik kuṟittu māṇākkarkaḷ tāmāka eḻutum paḻakkatti uṇṭu paṇṇa vēṇṭum.
māṇākkar eḻutum moḻi peyarppu eḷiya iṉiya tūya tamiḻ naṭiyil amiyavēṇṭum.  eḻuttup piḻikaḷ, coṟṟoṭarp piḻikaḷ, karuttu muṭipup piḻikaḷ mutaliyavi nēriṭāmal pārttukkoḷḷavēṇṭum.  eḻuti muṭintapiṉ māṇākkar eḻutiyavaṟṟit tirumpap paṭikkum paḻakkatti mutaliliruntē vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  ippaḻakkam iyalpāka amiyappeṟiṉ piṟaku pala tavaṟukaḷi avarkaḷē tirutti viṭalām.  piṟaku āciriyar avaṟṟit tiruttavēṇṭum.  kaṭṭurip payiṟcikaḷ tiruttumpoḻutu kiyāḷavēṇṭiya kuṟippukkaḷiyē īṇṭum kiyāḷalām9.
māṇākkar eḻuttu vēliyil kāṇappeṟum kuṟikaḷum piḻikaḷum: cātāraṇamāka māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutum kaṭṭurikaḷil ippiḻikaḷik kāṇalām: evvaḷavu coṉṉālum cilar varantiyil (Margin) payiṟci eṇ, payiṟci koṭukkappeṟṟa tēti, kaṭṭuriyiṉ talippu ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟippatilli.  cilaruṭiya kaṭṭurikaḷil ra-ṟa; la-ḷa-ḻa; pōṉṟa eḻuttup piḻikaḷ malivāka irukkum; cantippiḻikaḷ ērāḷamākak kāṇappeṟum.  oru cila kaṭṭurikaḷil orumi-paṉmi mayakkam.  ceyviṉi-ceyappāṭṭu viṉikaḷil tavaṟukaḷ pōṉṟa ilakkaṇap piḻikaḷik kāṇalām.  cilar coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum poruḷaṟintu kiyāḷuvatilli; cilar vākkiya amippic cariyākak kavaṉittu eḻutuvatilli. kaṭṭurikaḷil tiṭṭamāṉa toṭakkam, cemmiyāṉa naṭi, cariyāṉa muṭivu ākiyavi iruppatilli.  kaṭṭurip poruḷamippilum kāraṇakāriyat toṭar piṉmi, talippukkup poruntāta poruḷkaḷip pukuttutal, tavaṟāṉa karuttukkaḷip pukuttutal, ceytik kuṟivu pōṉṟa poruṭ piḻikaḷum kāṇappaṭum. cila kaṭṭurikaḷil uṇarcciyē irātu.  cila māṇākkarkaḷ pattikaḷip pirippatilli. cilar pattikaḷiṉ amippik kavaṉiyātu piritteḻutuvar.  cilar niṟattaṟ kuṟikaḷic cariyākavē iṭuvatilli.  cilar tām eḻutum kiyeḻuttik kavaṉittē eḻutuvatilli.
māṇākkar ceyyum piḻikaḷi aṟiyāmiyāl ēṟpaṭuvaṉa, kavaṉakkuṟiviṉāl nēriṭuvaṉa eṉa iraṇṭu pirivukaḷākap pirikkalām.  aṟiyāmiyiṉāl nēriṭum piḻikaḷi māṇākkarkaḷukkut taṉit taṉiyākavō, kuḻu muṟiyilō eṭuttuk kāṭṭittāṉ āciriyar tiruttavēṇṭum.  kavaṉak kuṟiviṉāl ēṟpaṭuvaṉavaṟṟik kavaṉakkuṟivu illātavāṟu ceytu nīkkalām.  eḻuttukkaḷik kavaṉittu niṟutti eḻutum paṭi ceytal, eḻutiya kaṭṭuriyit tirumpap paṭikkac ceytal, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷi ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil iṭac ceytal pōṉṟa vaṟṟik kavaṉikkac ceytāl piḻikaḷik kuṟikkalām.  aiyan tarat takka coṟpaṭṭikaḷik kiyāḷutal, kīḻvakuppukkaḷil colluvateḻutum payiṟcikaḷ tarutal, akarātip paḻakkam ēṟpaṭuttal pōṉṟa vaḻikaḷāl eḻuttuppiḻikaḷiyum cantippiḻikaḷiyum nīkkalām.  takka moḻippayiṟcikaḷiṉāl ēṉiya piḻikaḷiyum kaḷiyalām. avi nēriṭāmalum taṭukkalām. kuṟippuc caṭṭakam eḻutiya piṟaku kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻututal, tēviyāṉa aḷavu vāymoḻik kaṭṭurip payiṟci ākiya vaḻikaḷamittāl poruṭ ṭoṭarpup piḻikaḷ nēriṭā.  kaṭṭurikaḷit tiruttiya piṟaku ivaṟṟi ēṟṟa muṟiyil eṭuttuk kāṭṭi viḷakkiṉāl piḻikaḷ nāḷaṭivil kuṟintu kaṭṭurikaḷ naṉmuṟiyil amiyum.
eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷit tiruttutal
māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷit tiruttum vēli āciriyat toḻilil mikak kaṭiṉamāṉatoru pakuti.  ippakuti arumiyāṉatum iṉṟiyamiyātatumākum.  eḻuttup payiṟci māṇākkarkaṭku evvaḷavu iṉṟiyamiyātatō, atu pōlavē āciriyarkaḷ kaṭṭurikaḷit tiruttik kuṟikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatum mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  tām ceyta tavaṟu kaḷit tiruttikkoḷḷa vāyppu illāviṭil,  eḻuttup payiṟciyiṉāl māṇākkarkaḷukku yātu payaṉ? tavaṟukaḷi avvappoḻutu āṅkāṅku eṭuttukkāṭṭavēṇṭum.  oru muṟi ceyta tavaṟukaḷi mīṇṭum ceyyāmal irukka muyaṟci ceyyavēṇṭum.  uḷaviyaṟpaṭi, tavaṟukaḷi mīṇṭum mīṇṭum ceytāl atu nīkkamuṭiyāta paḻakkamākavē amintu viṭakkūṭum.  kaṭṭuri tiruttuvatu eṉpatu oru taṉikkali; ciṟappāṉa kali.  kaṭṭuri tiruttuvatu tērvu viṭittāḷkaḷi matippiṭṭu matippeṇkaḷ taruvatu pōṉṟatalla; atu tērcci peruka utavum oru cātaṉamumākum.  kaṭṭuriyit tiruttumpoḻutu ati eḻutiya māṇākkaṉi arukil vittukkoṇṭu tiruttiṉāl niṟinta payaṉ viḷiyum.  ciṟcila camayamtāṉ itu  cāttiyap paṭakkūṭum; iṉṟiya cūḻniliyil itu perumpālum iyalātatoṉṟu eṉṟē collavēṇṭum.  eṉavē, ētāvatu oru cariyāṉa muṟiyi mēṟkoṇṭu iti payaṉuḷḷatākac ceyvatup mikamika iṉṟiyamiyāta tākum.
kaṭṭuri tiruttum vēli aluppum aruvaruppum tarakkūṭiyatāka iruntālum ati puṟakkaṇikkātu poṟuppāṉa vēliyākavē mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭum.  perumpālāṉa paḷḷikaḷil ivvēli puṟakkaṇikkappaṭṭē varukiṉṟatu.  itaṟkup pala kāraṇaṅkaḷ uḷa.  cila talimiyāciriyarkaḷ ivvēliyi ēḷaṉamākak karuti ataṉit tamakkup piṭikkāta, allatu mikka paṭṭaṟivu illāta, āciriyarkaḷ taliyil aḷavukkumēl oru cumiyākak kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.  allatu, tamiḻāciriyarkaḷē tamiḻk kaṭṭurikaḷiyellām tirutta vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷkiyi mēṟkoṇṭu avarkaḷiyē tiruttumpaṭi ēvukiṉṟaṉar.  vārattiṟku 200 allatu 300 payiṟci ēṭukaḷit tiruttumpaṭi oruvari ēvutal kaṭumiyāṉa taṇṭaṉiyākum.  immuṟikaḷ virivil māṟavēṇṭum.  ekkāraṇattālum kaṭṭuri tiruttum vēliyi oruvar mītu aḷavukkumēl cumattakkūṭātu.  tamiḻk kaṟpikkātu kaṭṭuriyi ōr āciriyarik kaṟpikka ēvutalālum payaṉ irātu.
cila paḷḷikaḷil āṇṭukku oru vakuppukku 12 mutal 20 kaṭṭurikaḷ payiṟcikaḷākat taruvar.  tiṅkaḷ oṉṟukku iraṇṭu eḻuttuk kaṭṭurikaḷ koṭukkalām eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭu atic cevvaṉē tiruttiṉāl nalamāka irukkum.  āṇṭiṟkup paṉṉireṇṭu eḻuttuk kaṭṭurikaḷ ceytāl pōtum.  ēṉiyavi vāymoḻik kaṭṭurikaḷāka amiyalām.  pala payiṟcikaḷik koṭuttu avaṟṟit tiruttāmaliruppativiṭa cila payiṟcikaḷi eḻutacceytu avaṟṟi naṉku tiruttip piḻikaḷi māṇākkarkaṭku uṇarttutal mēlāṉatu.  ceyvaṉa tiruntac cey eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ ceyilil mēṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  eṉavē, kaṭṭuriyit tiruttuvatu paṟṟi oru yōcaṉikaḷik kūṟutal ivviṭattil poruntum; avi iḷam āciriyarkaṭku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavumākum.
tiruttuvatupaṟṟi cila kuṟippukkaḷ: kaṭṭuri eḻutum muṉṉar cariyāṉa muṟiyil vāymoḻik kaṭṭurip payiṟci tarappeṟiṉ piḻikaḷ kuṟiyum; tiruttavēṉṭiya vēliyum atikamirātu.  kūṭiyavariyil māṇākkariṉ muṉṉiliyil piḻikaḷit tiruttuvatu nallatu.  ellāp piḻikaḷiyum iṅṅaṉam eṭuttukkāṭṭit tiruttuvateṉpatu naṭimuṟiyil iyalātatoṉṟu.  mikap perum piḻikaḷiyāyiṉum ivvāṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉāl atikap payaṉviḷiyum.  ellāp payiṟcikaḷiyum māṇakkarkaḷi vittuk koṇṭu tiruttuvatum iyalātatu.  oru vārattil oru payiṟciyi aintāṟu māṇākkar kaḷip poṟukki vittuk koṇṭu ivvāṟu tiruttiṉāl, eṭṭu vārattiṟkuḷ oru vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷ ellōriyum orumuṟi uṭaṉ vittukkoṇṭu tirutta muṭiyum.  oru māṇākkaṉiṉ piḻikaḷi avaṉikkoṇṭē vakuppil tiruttumpaṭi ceyvatu payaṉuṭiya muṟiyāka irukkum.  āṉāl, palar muṉṉiliyil ivvāṟu cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvati muṉkumarap paruva māṇākkarkaḷil cilar virumpātiruttal kūṭum; avvāṟu ceyvatāl avarkaḷ maṉampuḻuṅki puṇpaṭṭu varuntavum kūṭum.  avvāṟu maṉam pātikkappaṭāmal immuṟi naṭipeṟṟal mikac ciṟanta payaṉi aḷikkum.  eṉiṉum, oruvar tam kuṟṟattit piṟar muṉṉiliyilō taṉittō uṇarntu koḷvatu vāḻkkiyil aṉivarum mēṟkoḷḷattakka  toṉṟātalāl, āciriyar ārāyntu immuṟiyik kiyāḷalām.  tiruttum poḻutu piḻikaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭutal nalla muṟiyil amiyavēṇṭum; kaṟkum māṇākkarkaḷum virumpi ēṟṟut tirunta muṉ varum muṟiyil aḵtu amintāl cālap payaṉ tarum.
toṭakkanilip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil viṉākkaḷukkuc  ciṟu ciṟu viṭikaḷē eḻutavēṇṭi yiruppatāl eḻuti muṭintatum avaṟṟit tirutti viṭalām.  tiruttik koṇṭē vakuppic cuṟṟiyum varalām.  naṭunili, uyarnilip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil avaṟṟi ōyvu nēraṅkaḷilum viṭumuṟi nāṭkaḷilumē tirutta vēṇṭum.  cilar vakuppoḻinta vēḷikaḷilum viṭumuṟi nāṭkaḷilum tiruttāmal iruntuviṭṭu avaṟṟi vakuppukkuk koṇṭuvantu ovvoruvariyum tam pakkattil vittuk koṇṭu tiruttum muṟi virumpat takkataṉṟu.  ivvāṟu kālatti vīṇākkutal kūṭātu.  immuṟiyāl māṇakkarkaḷ taṉikkavaṉam peṟukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu niṉippatum tavaṟu.  ivvāṟu tiruttum poḻutu  ellāvaṟṟiyum tirutti eḻuta vēṇṭiya avaciyamum illi. cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷik kiyāṇṭāl āciriyariṉ vēli kuṟivatuṭaṉ avi māṇākkariṉ cintaṉiyit tūṇṭuvaṉavākavum irukkum.  kīḻkkāṇum kuṟiyīṭukaḷi mēṟkoḷḷalām.
kuṟi			kuṟippatu
e. pi.		eḻuttup piḻi.
		coṟkaḷ viṭupaṭṭiruppatik 				kāṭṭuvatu.
ta. na. 		tamiḻ naṭi naṉṟāka illi. 
i. pi. 		ilakkaṇap piḻi
 !		uyarvu naviṟci allatu puṉinturi.
?		karuttiṉ uṇmiyil aiyam.
ni.		niṟuttaṟkuṟiyiṭṭatil tavaṟu. 
vi.		viṭṭuviṭuka.
vē.		vēṇṭātavi.
+		iṭam viṭuka.
x		pattiyākat toṭaṅkuka.
V		piḻiyāṉa niṟuttaṟ kuṟi.
x		toṭaril muraṇkāṇum iṭam.
†		kūṟiyatu kūṟiyirukkum iṭam.
ivaṟṟi māṇākkarkaṭku naṉku viḷakkiviṭṭāl, ik kuṟikaḷi aṟintu, kāṭṭiya  tiruttaṅkaḷit tiruttamuṭiyum.  māṇākkariṉ kaṭṭurikaḷil kāṇappeṟum eḻuttup piḻikaḷ, ilakkaṇap piḻikaḷ, cantip piḻikaḷ, vākkiyap piḻikaḷ  mutaliyavaṟṟi maṭṭilum tiruttaviṭṭāl pōtātu.  avaṟṟi eṭuttukkāṭṭa takka moḻip payiṟcikaḷiṉāl nīkka muyala vēṇṭum.  poruḷ varicip poruttattil kuṟikaḷ, uṇarcciyil veḷiyiṭum moḻik kuṟikaḷ, poruḷukkum collukkum poruttam viḷaṅkāta kuṟikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil āciriyar nallakavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum.  perumpālāṉa āciriyarkaḷ ivaṟṟic cariyākak kavaṉippatē illi. ivaṟṟip potuvāka vakuppukkaḷil eṭuttuk kāṭṭit tiruttiṉāl naṟpayaṉ viḷiyum.  poruḷi aḻuttamākavum kōviyākavum uṇarcciyōṭum veḷiyiṭum kaṭṭurikaḷ ciṟu piḻikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭiṉum, avi piḻiyaṟṟu amippuk kulintu poruḷ melintu tōṉṟum kaṭṭurikaḷi viṭa mēlāṉavi.  eṉavē, tiruttum āciriyarkaḷ uṇarcciyik kavaṉikkavēṇṭum.
oru vārattil koṭutta eḻuttuk kaṭṭuriyi aṭutta vārattiṟkuḷ tiruttiviṭṭāl nalam. anta vārattil kaṭṭurip pāṭa vēḷiyil ati māṇākkarkaḷiṭam koṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭum.  tiruttiya āciriyar māṇākkarkaḷ ceyta piḻikaḷi vakippaṭuttit tokuttu vittuk koṇṭu vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷ aṉivarukkum kāṭṭalām; ap piḻikaḷiṉ tiruttaṅkaḷi uḷattil patiyumāṟum viḷakkalām;.  oru cilar naṉṟāka eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷi aṉivarukkum paṭittuk kāṭṭalām;  atē poruḷpaṟṟip puttakattiluḷḷa kaṭṭuriyip paṭittum kāṭṭalām; atē pāṭa vēḷiyil ellōriyum kuṟippēṭṭiṉ (Note book) iṭappuṟattil tiruttaṅkaḷi eḻutumāṟu ēvi, avaṟṟi muṭikkumāṟu ceyya vēṇṭum.  perumpālum māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyar civappu miyiṉāl tiruttiyavaṟṟip pārppatē illi. immuṟiyik kiyāṇṭāl nalla payaṉ viḷiyum. tammuṭiya kaṭṭuri vakuppil paṭikkappaṭum, ārāyappaṭum eṉpatu paṟṟi ovvoruvarum aṟintiruntāl, māṇakkarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṭṭurikaḷi naṉṟāka eḻuta muṉvarutal kūṭum.
cila āciriyarkaḷ kaṭṭurikaḷi matippiṭuvataṟku oruvaki matippeṇ pākupāṭṭik kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar.  poruḷukku ittaṉi,  naṭikku ittaṉi, kiyeḻuttukku ittaṉi eṉṟu pattu matippeṇṇip pākupāṭu ceytukoṇṭu tiruttum muṟiyi aṉivarumē kiyāḷalām.  itu māṇākkarkaḷ tam kuṟikaḷi uṇarntu tam tiṟamiyi vaḷarttuk koḷḷat tūṇṭukōlākavum irukkum.
paḷḷikaḷil eḻuttuvēlipaṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ
toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil: toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil eḻuttu vēlikkeṉat taṉiyāṉa tiṭṭam oṉṟum illi.  avarkaḷ paṭitta puttakaṅkaḷiliruntu cila poruḷkaḷip paṟṟiyum, pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷil kāṇāta-āṉāl, māṇākkar kaḷaṟinta - poruḷkaḷippaṟṟiyum oru patti allatu iraṇṭu pattikaḷ variyum payiṟcikaḷit taralām.
mutal vakuppil kuḻantikaḷ tāṅkaḷ paṭitta coṟkaḷiyum, ciṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷiyum karumpalaki, paṭam, miṉṉaṭṭi, piṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil eḻutiyiruppaṉavaṟṟippārttu atip pōl tāmum eḻutum muṟikaḷiyum paḻakik koṇṭāl pōtumāṉatu.  iraṇṭām vakuppil eḻutu kōlic cariyāṉa muṟiyil piṭikkavum, teḷivāyum, virivāyum eḻutavum kaṟpati vaṟpuṟutta vēṇṭum.  ivvakuppil paṭitta pāṭaṅkaḷil kēṭkum viṉākkaḷukku ovvoru vākkiyattil viṭi eḻutum tiṟami vaḷarntāl pōtumāṉatu.  mūṉṟām vakuppil moḻippāṭapputtakaṅkaḷiliruntum piṟa pāṭaṅkaḷiliruntum kēṭkappeṟum viṉākkaḷukku iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu vākkiyaṅkaḷil viṭiyeḻutum payiṟcikaḷit taravēṇṭum.  nāṉkām vakuppil kaṭṭuri eḻutum payiṟcikku mutaṟpaṭiyāka camūka vāḻkkipaṟṟiya kaṭitaṅkaḷum, contak kaṭitaṅkaḷum eḻutum payiṟcikaḷ taravēṇṭum.  iv vakuppil āṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil conta aṉupavaṅkaḷippaṟṟic curukkamāka variyum payiṟcikaḷit taralām.  aintām vakuppil camūkac= cārpākavum , vāṇikac cārpākavum caṟṟu nīṇṭa kaṭitaṅkaḷi eḻutum payiṟcikaḷit taravēṇṭum.  āṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil muṟiyāṉa kaṭṭuri eḻutum payiṟcikaḷit taralām.  māṇākkar kaḷaṟinta poruḷkaḷpaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi oḻuṅkāka vakuttuk koṇṭu ciṟiya aḷavil kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻutat tūṇṭa vēṇṭum.  iv vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷukku ciṅkam, yāṉi pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷ paṟṟiyum; arici, māṅkāy, palāppaḻam, uṭi pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiyum; vīṭu, paḷḷi, tōṭṭam, viḷiyāṭṭiṭam ākiya aṟinta iṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum; avarkaḷaṟitta cila ūrkaḷ paṟṟiyum; kākitam ceytal, maṭpāṇṭam varital, kūṭi muṭital, uppu uṇṭākkal pōṉṟa ciṟu toḻilkaḷ paṟṟiyum; ūrppūmpoḻil, āṟṟaṅkari, canti, puki vaṇṭi niliyam, malic carivu pōṉṟa kāṭcikaḷpaṟṟiyum; maḻināḷ aṉupavam, kōyil, tiruviḻā, kaṭitteruvukkup pōy varutal pōṉṟa nukarvukaḷip paṟṟiyum kaṭṭurikaḷ variyat tūṇṭalām.  eḷiya paṭaṅkaḷip pārttu kaṭṭuri variyum payiṟcikaḷum ivarkaḷukku ēṟṟavi.  ōnāyum kokkum, kākkiyum nīrk kuṭamum, ōnāyum āṭṭukkuṭṭiyum eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ciṟiya katikaḷi eḻutum payiṟcikaḷiyum taralām.
naṭunilip paḷḷikaḷil: naṭunilip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ āṟṟa vēṇṭiya eḻuttu vēlikaḷi mika mukkiyamāṉa viyākak koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭurikaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ māṇakkarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyi yoṭṭiyaṉavāka irukkavēṇṭum.  māṇakkarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷē veḷiyiṭat takkaṉavākak kaṭṭurip poruḷkaḷ amintāl avaṟṟi avarkaḷ viruppattuṭaṉum ārvattuṭaṉum eḻutuvar.  car.  ḵpilip eṉpāriṉ karuttuppaṭi ovvōr eḻuttu vēliyum ati eḻutukiṉṟavar piṟarukkō allatu taṉ piṟkāla niṉivukkut tuṇiyākavō poruḷit teḷivāka uṇarttum nōkkattuṭaṉ eḻutavēṇṭum.  avar ovvoṉṟiyum niṉivup pativu (Record) ceyti allatu aṟikki (Message) eṉṟa iruvakikkum tokukkakkūṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟār.10 muṉ iyalil collappeṟṟa moḻip payiṟcikaḷi ilakkaṇap pāṭattuṭaṉ iṇittuk kaṟpittāl ataṉāl māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum moḻiyaṟivu avarkaḷiṉ palvēṟu eḻuttu vēli kaḷukkup peruntuṇiyāka irukkum.  kaṭṭuriyiṉ naṭi eḷimi, teḷivu, marapu piṟaḻāmi,  ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil aḻuttam ākiya paṇpukaḷik koṇṭi ruppatiyē nōkkamākak koḷḷavēṇṭum.
vakuppukkaḷil ellāt talippukkaḷiyum eḻutuvatip paṟṟik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu pattiyamippukkaḷ, pattiyiṉ nalliyalpukaḷ, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷiṭutal, kōṭiṭal (Underlining) talippamittal, uḷ talippukkaḷamittal pōṉṟa palvēṟu vaḻivakikaḷiyum avvappoḻutu āṅkāṅkuk kaṟpikkalām. 
uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil: tāymoḻi allatu maṇṭala moḻi mutalmoḻi niliyi aṭinta piṟaku māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttu vēlikaḷ tarattil uyarnta niliyil irukka vēṇṭum.  poruṭpōkkil virintum, naṭippōkkil uyarntum irukka vēṇṭum.  mēl vakuppukkaḷil kūṭiya vari ivi māṇākkar kaḷiṉ paṭippāṟṟalkaḷi veḷippaṭuttum pāṉmiyilum, māṇākkarkaḷiṉ taṉivīṟu veḷippaṭum muṟiyilum amintāl naṉṟu.  curukki varitaluṭaṉ kaṭṭurip poruḷkaḷic cērttu oḻuṅkupaṭutti avaṟṟik kāraṇa kāriya muṟippaṭiyum, tarukka muṟippaṭiyum eḻutum vāyppukkaḷi aṭikkaṭi nalkalām.  in nili māṇākkarkaḷiṭam ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvuk kaṭṭuri kaḷiyum etirpārkkalām.  
in nili māṇākkarkaḷiṉ naṭi iṉiya, eḷiya, marapu neṟi piṟaḻāta taṉmiyuṭaṉ irukka vēṇṭum.  pattiyamippu, niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷiṭutal, talippukkaḷ koṭuttal pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil uyartarap payiṟcikaḷi etir pārkkalām.  māṇākkarkaḷ eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷil nallaṉavaṟṟit tērnteṭuttup paḷḷi āṇṭitaḻil veḷiyiṭalām.
moḻi peyarppukkut tarum āṅkilap pakutikaḷiṉ naṭi eḷimiyākavum nēriyatākavum irukka vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuri kaḷukkākat tērnteṭukkum poruḷkaḷippaṟṟiya pakutikaḷē itaṟkum ēṟṟavi.  ip pakutikaḷi māṇākkar payilum āṅkilat tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷiliruntum poṟukki eṭukkalām.
uyarnilip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷil avarkaḷuṭiya tuṇippāṭap puttakaṅ kaḷiliruntu cila talippukkaḷit tērnteṭuttu oṉṟarip pakkaṅkaḷukku mikāmal kaṭṭurikaḷ variyum payiṟcikaḷ tara vēṇṭum.
paṭippāṟṟalukku vaḻikōlutal
tāy moḻiyil tāṉ paṭippāṟṟali eḷital vaḷarkkalām.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tēvikaḷ, maṉappāṉmi, kavarcci ākiyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu moḻippāṭaṅkaḷik kaṟpikkavēṇṭumāṉāl, cātāraṇamākak koṭukkap peṟum kaṭṭurip payiṟcikaḷ maṭṭilum pōtā.  nāḷitaḻkaḷilum, paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷilum veḷivarum putiya ilakkiya vakikaḷiyum kavitikaḷiyum paṭikkum māṇākkarkaḷukku avikaḷippōl tāmum paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvam eḻutal iyalpu.  tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ ciṟitu muyaṟci eṭuttu it tuṟiyil takka kavaṉam celuttiṉāl uyarnilippaḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷiṭam ciṟu kaṭṭurikaḷ, ciṟu nāṭakaṅkaḷ, ciṟu katikaḷ. ciṟu ciṟu kavitikaḷ mutaliya ilakkiya vakikaḷit tāmākavē eḻutum āṟṟali vaḷarttu viṭa muṭiyum.  paṭippāṟṟalkaḷi vaḷarkkum muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭṭāl iḷam uḷḷaṅkaḷ paṇpaṭṭu nalla palaṉit tarakkūṭum.  am māṇākkarkaḷil palar etirkālattil ciṟanta ciṟukatic celvarkaḷākavum, putiṉa āciriyarkaḷākavum, kaviñarkaḷākavum tikaḻakkūṭum.  āyirattil allatu ilaṭcattil oruvarāvatu piṟkālattil kampaṉ, pārati pōṉṟa peruṅ kaviñaṉāka viḷaṅka iṭamuṇṭu.  eṉavē, ataṟkēṟṟa aṭip paṭiyip paḷḷiyil pōṭuvatum, ataṟku vēṇṭiya vāyppukkaḷik kalvi niliyaṅkaḷil tarutalum avaciyam.  paḷḷi itaḻ ittuṟiyil perun toṇṭum puriyum.  paḷḷi itaḻkaḷil cila māṇākkarkaḷ arumiyāṉa katikaḷ, kavitikaḷ uriyāṭalkaḷ, kaṟpaṉik kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻutiyati in nūlāciriyar naṉku aṟivār11. eṉavē, paḷḷi itaḻip paṟṟi oru ciṟitu īṇṭu kūṟuvatu iṉṟimiyātatākiṉṟatu.
paḷḷi itaḻkaḷ: pala nūlkaḷiluḷḷa katikaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip paṭikkumpoḻutum, pala vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷiyum kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷiyum kaṇṇuṟum poḻutum avaṟṟip pōl tāmum eḻutavēṇṭum, variya vēṇṭum eṉṟa āval māṇākkarkaḷiṭam eḻutal iyalpu.  vāyppukkaḷ takkavāṟu koṭukkappeṟṟāl māṇākkarkaḷiṭamuḷḷa paṭippāṟṟal naṉku vaḷarcci peṟum.  ittakiya vāyppukkaḷi nalkuvatu paḷḷi itaḻ.  tāymoḻi uṇarcci mikkeḻuntirukkum ik kālattil ovvoru paḷḷiyilum paḷḷi itaḻkaḷ tōṉṟip paṇiyāṟṟa iṭam koṭuttal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
paḷḷi itaḻkaḷāl iruvita naṉmikaḷ uṇṭu.  paḷḷiyil naṭipeṟum palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷiyum ceyal muṟikaḷiyum peṟṟōrkaḷukkum potu makkaḷukkum aṟivittu avarkaḷuṭiya ottuḻippip peṟuvatu oruvaki.  maṟṟoruvaki, māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyē maṟintu kiṭakkum paṭippāṟṟal kaḷip palvēṟu muṟikaḷil vaḷarkkat tuṇi purivatu.  iṉṟu naṭimuṟiyil pala paḷḷikaḷil ivvita itaḻkaḷ tōṟṟuvikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  acciṭṭa itaḻkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvataṟku ataṟkeṉa ākum.  celavu toki atikamāvatāl, cila paḷḷikaḷil āṇṭiṟku oṉṟum, mikac cila periya paḷḷikaḷil āṇṭiṟku iraṇṭumāka ivi veḷiyiṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  paḷḷi nikaḻccikaḷ, āṇṭu aṟikkikaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟāl itaḻkaḷi nirappuvativiṭa avaṟṟit taṉiyāka veḷiyiṭṭu viṭṭu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ paṭippāṟṟalukku mutaliṭam taruvatē ciṟantatu.  talimiyāciriyarum piṟa āciriyarkaḷum ivvaḻiyil cintiyic celutti āvaṉa ceyya vēṇṭum.
naṭimuṟiyil paḷḷikkeṉa ōr itaḻum ovvoru vakuppukkum ovvoru vakuppiṉ pirivukaḷukkum ovvōr itaḻum irukka ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytāl ciṟanta palaṉkaḷik kāṇa iyalum.  vakuppu itaḻkaḷik kiyeḻuttup paṭiyākavē veḷiyiṭalām; tiṅkaḷ oṉṟukku ōr eṇ vītamum veḷiyiṭalām.  ivvāṟu  ovvoru vakuppilum veḷivarum itaḻkaḷiluḷḷa katikaḷ, kavitikaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ, ciṟu nāṭakaṅkaḷ, piṟa ceytikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuḷ nallaṉavaṟṟit tērnteṭuttup paḷḷi itaḻkaḷil veḷiyiṭalām.  paḷḷi itaḻ accitaḻāka irukka vēṇṭum; āṇṭiṟku oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu eṇkaḷi vacatik kēṟṟavāṟu veḷiyiṭalām.  tammuṭiya eḻuttōviyaṅkaḷ accil veḷivaruvatik kāṇum māṇākkarkaḷukkum ēṟpaṭum iṉpattiṟku iṇiyoṉṟilli.  anta iṉpamē avarkaḷuṭiya paṭippāṟṟalkaḷit tūṇṭi paṉmaṭaṅku vaḷarac ceyyum.  tammuṭiya karuttōviyaṅkaḷ eppaṭiyum accil veḷivaravēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarcciyāl māṇākkarkaḷ vakuppukkaḷil tiṅkaḷtōṟum veḷivarum itaḻkaḷukku atikamāṉa kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻutuvar.  kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷāl tāṉ ōviyat tiṟaṉ, itaḻkaḷip palvēṟu muṟiyil kōlamiṭṭuc ciṟappikkum tiṟaṉ ākiyavaṟṟi naṉku vaḷarkka muṭiyum.  accitaḻkaḷāl inta naṉmikaḷi aṭiya iyalātu.
kuṟippiṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil ivvitaḻkaḷ veḷi varuvataṟkuc cuṟucuṟuppākac ceyalāṟṟum māṇākkarkaḷaṭaṅkiya kuḻukkaḷ paṇiyāṟṟa vēṇṭum ovvoru vakuppāciriyarkaḷum tiṟamiyuḷḷa māṇākkarkaḷit tērnteṭuttu ceyaṟ kuḻu amittu ip paṇiyiṉi mēṟkoḷḷalām.  ik kuḻukkaḷil, karuttil vallavar, eḻuttil vallavar, ōviyattil vallavar, patippittalil vallavar ākiyavarkaḷ uṟuppiṉarkaḷākap paṇiyāṟṟa vāyppaḷittāl itaḻkaḷ mikac cīriya muṟiyil veḷivarum.  veḷiyil ulavak kūṭiya accitaḻi veḷiyiṭu vataṟku oru taṉic ceyaṟkuḻu vēṇṭum.  itil it tuṟiyil aṉupavamuḷḷa virumpip paṇiyēṟkum āciriyarkaḷum, tiṟamiyuḷḷa oru cila māṇākkarkaḷum uṟuppiṉarkaḷāka iruntu paṇiyāṟṟalām.  itaṉāl putiya tāymoḻip pāṭattiṭṭattil kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷa kaṭṭurikaḷip poruḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu piriviṉi ceytu tokuttal, uḷḷuṟi tayārittal, aṭṭavaṇi tayārittal, piṉṉiṇippukkaḷ tayārittal, accup piḻittiruttam ceytal, eḻuttōviyaṅ kaḷukkum ēṉiya ōviyaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟa talippukkaḷi amittal, kaṭṭurikaḷil uṭ pirivukaḷukkut talippukkaḷit tarutal, kaṭṭuri-katikaḷi oru cila vākkiyaṅkaḷāl tiṟaṉāytal, puttakattiṟaṉāyvu ceytal, ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil niṟuttaṟ kuṟikaḷi iṭutal pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa tuṟikaḷil māṇākkarkaḷukku nalla payiṟciyiṉi aḷikkalām.  itaṟkākac celaviṭap peṟum nēram moḻi kaṟpikkum tuṟiyil ciṟanta palaṉit tarum.  aruñcoṟkaḷukkup poruḷ kūṟutal, collilakkaṇam kūṟutal, canti pirittuc collutal, ceyyuṭkaḷi aṉvayap paṭuttip patavuri, poḻippuri kūṟutal ākiyavaṟṟil maṭṭilum kavaṉam celuttic celaviṭum nērattil etir pārkkum palaṉiviṭap paṉmaṭaṅku atikamākavē itu palaṉ aḷikkum eṉpatil eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli.
12. ilakkiyam
etu ilakkiyam: ilakkiyam eṉṉum peyarukkut takutiyāṉa puttakaṅkaḷi aṟiñarkaḷtām iyaṟṟa muṭiyum.  paṇṭiruntē ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ palavakiyāṉa ciṟappuk kaḷiyum  aṟivāṟṟaliyum uṭiyavarkaḷāka iruntaṉar.  evarum nūliyaṟṟal eṉpatu iyalāta ceyal aṟivāl nirampiyavar mikac ciṟu tokiyiṉa rēyāvar.  
	ārttacapi nūṟṟoruvar āyirattoṉ ṟāmpulavar
  vārtti patiṉā yirattoruvar1 
eṉṟa veṇpāp pakutiyi nōkkuka.  ik kālattil ellōrum puttakam eḻuta vēṇṭum eṉṟu viḻikiṉṟaṉar; eḻutavum ceykiṉṟaṉar.  avarkaḷ eḻutiya aṉittum ilakkiyaṅkaḷākā.  maṉitaṉuṭiya aṟiviṟkup payaṉpaṭupavi eviyō aviyē ilakkiyaṅkaḷ. nūlkaḷ.  māntar maṉakkōṭṭam tīrkkum nūl eṉpati aṟika ilakkiyattiṟkut tāṉ maṉitaṉuṭiya uḷḷuṇarcciyit tūṇṭakkūṭiya āṟṟal uṇṭu. maṉita vāḻvukku araṇāka iruntu pātukākkak kūṭiyatum atuvē.
entap puttakattip paṭitta piṟaku mīṇṭum mīṇṭum paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa vēṭki eḻukiṉṟatō atu tāṉ ilakkiyam; paṉmuṟi paṭittālum ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ paṭikkuntōṟum putupputuc cuviyit tōṟṟuvikkum. āyun toṟuntoṟum iṉpan taruntamiḻ tērun toṟumiṉi tāntamiḻ eṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷak karuttic cintittup pārttāl ivvuṇmi pulappaṭum. kamparāmāyaṇam, cīvaka cintāmaṇi, cilappatikāram, tirukkuṟaḷ pōṉṟa nūlkaḷ nām ettaṉi muṟi paṭittālum avaṟṟiṭam nammuṭiya ārvam kuṉṟuvatilli. aṟivit toṭavalla nūlkaḷ ciṟanta ilakkiyaṅkaḷākā; orumuṟi paṭittu avaṟṟiluḷḷa poruḷ kaḷi aṟintu koṇṭāl mīṇṭum nām avaṟṟip paṭikka virumpuvatilli. āṉāl, uṇarcci niliyip paṟṟum nūlkaḷ aṅṅaṉa maṉṟu; ettaṉi muṟi paṭittālum calipputtarātavi. aṉumaṉatu āṟṟalum, ilakkuvaṉatu toṇṭum, parataṉuṭiya cakōtara vāñciyum, kukaṉatu naṭpum, cītiyiṉkaṟpum, kumpakaruṇaṉuṭiya ceyn naṉṟiyaṟitalum nam maṉatti viṭṭu eṉṟum nīṅkā tiruppavi. ittakiya uyarnta paṇpukaḷip peṟṟiruppavitām kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyātu eṉṟum nilittu niṟpavi. avaṟṟip paṭikkun tōṟum iṉpattip peṟalām.
	naviltoṟum nūlnayam pōlum payiltoṟum
 paṇpuṭi yāḷar toṭarpu.2
eṉṟa vaḷḷuvar vāymoḻiyē ciṟanta ilakkiya uriyāṇi. orunūl payiluntōṟum nayam payavātāyiṉ atu ciṟanta nūlaṉṟu eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟiviṭalām.
orumuṟi paṭitta māttirattil iṉpantarum puttakaṅkaḷum, ettaṉi taṭavi paṭittālum viḷaṅkātavikaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avi ilakkiyaṅkaḷākā. oru muṟi paṭitta māttirattilē poruḷ pulappaṭātu, nuṇuki ārāya, nuṉiyiṟ karumpu tiṉṟaṟṟē eṉpatupōla varavarac cuvi payakkum nūlkaḷē ilakkiyaṅkaḷ. toṭaṅkiṉa nāḷtoṭṭu āyuṭkālam variyilum paṭittālum veṟuppillāmal cuvi payappavi ilakkiyaṅkaḷil uyartaramāṉavi.
avaṟṟuḷ kaviti ilakkiyaṅkaḷ iṉṉum ciṟantavi. kaviyiṉippuk kaṇṭa kaliñaḷ vaḷḷuvaṉ, eṉṉoruvaṉ, cāntuṇiyum kallātavāṟu eṉṟu iraṅkiyiruttali eṇṇi uṇarka. ilakkiyaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷit tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.
cāttiraṅkaḷi mēṉāṭṭār aṟiviyalkaḷ (Sciences) eṉṟum, kāviyaṅkaḷi ilakkiyam (Literature) eṉṟum kūṟuvar; tamiḻil ivi muṟiyē nūl eṉṟum pā eṉṟum vaḻaṅki varuvaṉavākak koḷḷalām. tamiḻ ilakkiya nūlkaḷ perumpālum aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉṉum nāṉku kuṟikkōṭ poruḷkaḷik kūṟum. avaṟṟi veḷiyiṭum muṟikaḷuḷ pala vēṟupāṭukaḷ uḷa. cila nūlkaḷ aṟattiṟkuriya vitikaḷiyum vilakkukaḷiyum nērē nītiyākap pulappaṭuttum; avaṟṟiyē cila nūlkaḷ uvamimukattāṉum utāraṇa mukattāṉum pulappaṭuttum; katippōkkil avaṟṟiyē kuṟippākavum cila nūlkaḷ uṇarttum. ellā nūlkaḷum ciṟantaviyē yeṉiṉum, karuttukaḷi eḷiya muṟiyil cuvi payakkum pāṅkil, katippōkkil, kāppiya vaṭivil kūṟum nūlkaḷē ciṟantavi. vaṭa nūlār kāviyatti maṉiviyiṉ urikku iṇiyākac colluvar. kaṇavaṉukkut taṉatu karutti yaṟivikkappukum maṉivi, mika iṉiya coṟkaḷāl maṉaṅka varum muṟiyil mella aṟivittalip pōla, kāviyaṅkaḷ uyarnta nītikaḷic coṟporuḷ nayaṅkaḷōṭu maṉaṅkavarum vaṇṇam pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. vaṉappiṉippaṟṟik kūṟavanta pērāciriyar ataṉāṟ pala ceyyuḷum uṟuppāyt tiraṇṭu perukiya toṭar niliyatē vaṉappeṉṉum peyarppakuti vakiyāṉ ēṟpa teṉpatu3 eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar. vaṉappu-aḻaku. oru moḻiyiṉ vaṉappukkaḷi ammoḻiyiluḷḷa toṭar-nilic ceyyuṭkaḷākiya kāppiyaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koḷḷalām. kāppiyaṅkaḷip pōl vaṉappiṉip pulappaṭuttuvatil talimi yuṭiyaṉa piṟavaki ilakkiyaṅkaḷalla eṉpatu īṇṭu aṟiya vēṇṭiyatoṉṟu. ivvāṟu ilakkiyattippaṟṟiya pala karuttukkaḷi āciriyarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷ: oru moḻiyiluḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ceyyuḷ vaṭivam, urinaṭi vaṭivam eṉa iru vaṭivaṅkaḷil amiyum. tamiḻ moḻiyiluḷḷa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum pā vaṭivamākavum uḷḷaṉa; uri naṭiyākavum uḷḷaṉa. ivviruvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, vaḷarcci, avaṟṟiṭam kāṇappeṟum vēṟṟumikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟit tamiḻmoḻiyāciri yarkaḷ oruvāṟu potup paṭa uṇarntiruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.4
iṉṟu ceyyuḷ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻaṅkum nūlkaḷ vēṟu; tolkāppiyar kālattil attoṭarāl vaḻaṅkiya nūlkaḷ vēṟu. iṉṟu pāviyum ceyyuḷiyum oru poruḷ kuṟitta coṟkaḷākavē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm. tolkāppiyar kālattil ceyyappaṭuvaṉa ellām ceyyuḷ eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅkap peṟṟaṉa. uri, pāṭṭu, nūl ākiya mūṉṟumē ceyyuḷ eṉṟa collāl uṇarttap peṟṟaṉa. ceyyuḷiyaliṉ mutal nūṟpāvil kūṟappeṟum māttiri, eḻuttiyal, aci mutaliya muppattunāṉkiṉik koṇṭaṉavellām ceyyuṭku uṟuppākum eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttalik kāṇka. pā eṉpatum ceyyuḷ uṟuppukkaḷil oṉṟākum.
tamiḻcceyyuḷit tolkāppiyar ēḻuvakiyākap pirittup pēcuvar. avi pāṭṭu, uri, nūl, vāymoḻi, pici, aṅkatam, mutuccol eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟi,
pāṭṭuṭi nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkata mutuccollō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varippiṉ
nāṟpeya relli yakattavar vaḻaṅkum
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar.5
eṉṟa nūṟpāvāl aṟiyalām.  inta ēḻuvakik ceyyuḷkaḷiyum āciriyar aṭivariyaṟi uḷḷaṉa, aṭivariyaṟi illaṉa eṉṟu iru pirivākavum pirittuk kāṭṭuvar.  aṭivariyaṟi uḷḷaṉa vaṟṟil pākkaḷ yāvum aṭaṅkum; aṭivariyaṟi illaṉa vaṟṟil nūl, uri, pici, mutumoḻi, mantiram,kuṟippueṉṟāṟumaṭaṅkum..6 itovvoṉṟiyumpaṟ¿āṇṭu¡kaṇLkoḷka.  nāḷaṭivil ceyyuḷ eṉṟa col poruḷāl curuṅkikkoṇṭē vantu iṉṟu `pā ceyyuḷ oṉṟiyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu.  tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ `pā ceyyuḷ, uric ceyyuḷ ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟittum, avaṟṟiṉ naṭi vēṟṟumi kuṟittum ōraḷavu aṟintukoḷḷutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
pā naṭiyum uri naṭiyum: em moḻiyilum pānaṭiyē uri naṭikku muṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu āyvāḷarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  ilakkiya naṭikaḷil aṭivariyaṟiyuṟṟu naṭakkum pā naṭiyē mikavum toṉmi vāyntatu.  ceṟivu vāynta ceyyuḷ naṭiyē ciṟanta karuttukkaḷi niṉivil vittukkoḷvataṟku ēṟṟatu.  ārāycci ceyyum aṟiviyal muṟikaḷ ēṟpaṭāta akkālattu iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷit turuvi ārāyntu avaṟṟiṉ uṟuppukkaḷi alacippārkkātu kaṟpaṉiyiyē tuṇiyākak koṇṭu makkaḷ tam maṉak kaṇṇāl avaṟṟik kaṇṭu vantaṉar.  kāraṇakāriya muṟi avarkaḷiṭattu amiyavilli; ātalāl ataṉi viḷakki virittu ārāyntu kūṟum urinaṭiyum tōṉṟavilli.  eṉavē, mkkālattilkkkaḷjmTṟavēṇṭiyamṉittiyumbcayyuḷtṭivilēyēTṟiṉār. katikaḷiyum pā vaṭivil amittaṉar; kaṭavuḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷum, kaṭavuḷukkum makkaḷukkumuḷḷa toṭarpik kūṟum camayak karuttukkaḷum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē amintaṉa. ivvāṟu kūṟuvar kūrthōp eṉṉum āṅkila aṟiñar.  emarcaṉ eṉpārum, iciyum etukiyum kuḻantikku iṉpam aḷippaṉavām eṉṟum, ilakkiya varalāṟṟil kaviti naṭiyē uri naṭikku muntiyatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  immarapiyum varalāṟṟiyum oṭṭiyē nam moḻiyum amintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu.  tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟi aṟivōr pākkaḷē tamiḻil mutalil eḻuntaṉa eṉpatiyum, piṉṉarē uri naṭi vantatu eṉpatiyum eḷitil uṇarvar.  iṉṉum oru cila moḻikaḷiluḷḷaṉapōl tamiḻ moḻiyil palatuṟikaḷilum kalikaḷilum urinaṭi ilakkiyaṅkaḷ palkip perukavilli.
naṭi eṉpatu tamiḻil oḻukkattik kuṟikkum7; cellum celaviyum kuṟikkum. naṭa eṉpatu iraṇṭukkum pakutiyākum.  celavik kuṟikkumpoḻutu atu toḻiṟ peyarākiṉṟatu; piṟa poruḷik kuṟikkumpoḻutu toḻilāku peyarākum.  ac col ceyyuḷ naṭi, uri naṭi eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkum poḻutu ceyyuḷ urikaḷ cellum celavu eṉpatik kuṟikkum.  kāl naṭiyiṉ celavu oruvari ōriṭattiliruntu piṟitōriṭattiṟkuk koṇṭuceluttuvatu pōl innaṭikaḷum ceyyuṭ poruḷiyum urikkum uripporuḷiyum oruvar maṉattiliruntu piṟitoruvar maṉattiṟkuk koṇṭu pōyc cērkkiṉṟaṉa.8  avvāṟu koṇṭupōyc cērkkum karuvikaḷ ceyyuḷ urinaṭikaḷil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷākum.
coṟceṟivu, poruṭṭeḷivu, ici nalam, aṇi nalam mutaliya nalliyalpukaḷ amiya, karutiya poruḷmēl pala coṟkaḷit toṭuttu amippatuvē ceyyuḷ naṭiyākum.  oru poruḷmēl pala col koṇarntu īṭṭal ceyyuḷ ceytal eṉṟum, pala col toṭarntu poruḷ kāṭṭu vaṉavaṟṟuḷ oci taḻuviyavaṟṟip pāṭṭeṉṉalām eṉṟum uriyāciriyarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  ceyyuḷ naṭikkuḷ pukāta niliyil makkaḷ potuvākap pēcummoḻi uri naṭiyākum.  kaṟṟavarkaḷum maṟṟavarkaḷum eḷitil poruḷ uṇarntu koḷḷakkūṭiyatāy ceyyuḷ naṭiyiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭu aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil nām pēcip payiṉṟu varum naṭiyi urinaṭi eṉṉalām.  aḵtu urittup pōtaliṉ uriyum, tēṅki niṟkāmal naṭantu pōtaliṉ naṭiyum āyiṟṟu.  ilattīṉ moḻiyiluḷḷa Oratio Pedestris eṉṟa toṭar tamiḻiluḷḷa pēccu naṭi eṉṟa toṭarōṭu oppiṭattakkatāka uḷḷatu.  aṉṟiyum,
	pūvaru maḻali yaṉṉam
puṉimaṭap piṭiyeṉ ṟiṉṉa
tēvaru maruḷat takka
celaviṉa eṉiṉum tēṟēṉ;
pāvarum kiḻamit toṉmip
paruṇital toṭutta patti
nāvaruṅ kiḷavic celvi
naṭivaru naṭiyaḷ nallōy9
eṉṟu kampa nāṭaṉ kūṟum kāppiya naṭiyuṭaṉum iti oppiṭuka.
iru naṭikaḷukkumuḷḷa aṭippaṭi vēṟṟumikaḷ: pā naṭi, ceṟivum curukkamum koṇṭatu; uri naṭi nekiḻcciyum perukkamum koṇṭatu.  muṉṉatu, kaṟpaṉiyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu kāraṇakāriyattoṭarpik kavaṉiyātu poruḷkaḷi muḻup poruḷkaḷākat tokuttuk kaṇṭu aḻakiṉi viyappatu.  itaṟku aṭivariyaṟiyum uṇṭu.  iḵtu etuki, mōṉi mutaliya toṭi nalaṅkaḷum aṇi nalaṅkaḷum koṇṭatu; yāppu ilakkaṇattiṟkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭu naṭappatu; uḷaccuvikaḷiyum, uṇarccikaḷiyum, maṉaveḻucci kaḷiyum, meyppāṭukaḷiyum taṉṉakattē aṭakkik koṇṭiruppatu; paṭittuc cuvikkavum, murukuṇarcciyi vaḷarkkavum tuṇipurivatu.  piṉṉatu ārāycci, kāraṇakāriyat toṭarpu, aṉumāṉam ākiyavaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu; poruḷkaḷi ārāyntu vakuttup pārppatu.  aṭivariyaṟi itaṟku illi; etuki mōṉi mutaliya toṭi nalaṅkaḷum aṇi nalaṅkaḷum itaṟku vēṇṭuvatilli; yāppilakkaṇattukkum itu kaṭṭuppaṭātu.  pala kalikaḷiyum viritturippatāl, iṉpattukku maṭṭumaṉṟi aṟivu vaḷarccikkē mikavum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟatu.
muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷuṇṭeṉpataṉi viḷaṅka aṟivittaṟ kuriya aṟivu niliyum, piṉ ataṉōṭu vēṟaṟakkalantu niṉṟu iṉpuṟutaṟkuriya uṇarvu niliyum eṉṉum irutiṟa niliyi ṉiyum uyirkaḷukku muṟukuvittup piṉ avvaṟivu niliyi ṉiyum kaḻalac ceytu tūya aṉpuruvāy viḷaṅkum uṇarvu niliyi nilipēṟāk kuvittu uyirkaḷukku aḷantaṟiyap paṭāta perum payaṉit tarutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāk karuviyāvatu tāṉ pāṭṭeṉpatu.  aṟṟēl, uriyum ceyyuḷum eṉṉum iraṇṭaṉuḷ uriyum avvirutiṟa niliyiyum muṟukuvittup payaṉṟarumātaliṉ ceyyuḷ māttiramē ataṉip payakku meṉṟuri kūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ: pāl kaṟanta māttiriyē yuṇpārkkuc cuvi payakkumāyiṉum, ataṉi vaṟṟakkāyccik kaṭṭiyākat tiraṭṭip piṉṉuṇpārkkuk kaḻiperuñ cuvi tarutal pōlavum, muṟṟiṉa karuppaṅkaḻiyi naṟukkip piḻinta māttiriyē ataṉ cāṟṟip parukuvārkku aḵtiṉimi viḷikkumāyiṉum mēlum ataṉip pākutiraḷak kāyccic carukkarik kaṭṭiyāka eṭuttuṇpārkku āṟṟavum pēriṉimi payattal pōlavum, uriyum nalam payappa toṉṟēyāyiṉum ataṉik kāṭṭilum ceyyuḷāṟ peṟappaṭum payaṉ cālavum peritām.  kaṟanta pāl nīrāḷamāy nekiḻntiruttaliṉ ataṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa cuvi mikuntu tōṉṟātu kuṟintē kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu.  atupōla, uriyum oru varampiṉkaṭpaṭātu coṟperukkamuṟṟu naṭipeṟutalāl ataṉkaṭputinta poruḷum āḻamākaliṉṟi ac coṟkaḷōṭu ottu oḻuki mellitāy viṭukiṉṟatu.  maṟṟuk kāyccit tiraṭṭiya pāṟ kaṭṭiyum carukkarik kaṭṭiyum iṟukit tiṇṇeṉṟa uruvuṭi yavāyiruttaliṉ avaṟṟiṉkaṭ cuvi mikamutirntu tōṉṟā niṟkiṉṟatu.  atupōla, ceyyuḷum eḻuttu aci, cīr, taḷi, aṭi, toṭikaḷāṉ aminta pāvāy oru varampuṭpaṭṭuc coṟ curukkamuṭittāy naṭattaliṉ ataṉkaṭ putinta poruḷum avaṟṟōṭu oppa oḻukit tiṭpamum āḻamum uṭiyatākiṉṟatu.  pāṭṭellām aṟivu niliyip paṟṟik koṇṭu pōy uyirkaḷiṉ uṇarvu niliyi eḻuppi viṭuvatākum, uriyellām aṟivu niliyippaṟṟiyē nikaḻumallatu ataṉmēṟ ceṉṟu uṇarvu niliyit toṭamāṭṭātākum.
periyatōr malimuḻiñciṉuṭ poṉṉum maṇiyum citaṟik kiṭattal oru viyappaṉṟu; oru ciṟu kaṟpiḷavilē ariya periya muḻu maṇikaḷ aṭukkaṭukkāyk kiṭantu eṭukkuntōṟum kuṟipaṭātiruttalē peritum viyakkaṟpālatākum.  ciṟiya vāṉmīṉkaḷ akaṉṟa avvāṉattiṟkāṇappaṭutal oru viyappaṉṟu; avvakaṉṟa vāṉum vēṟu māṭamāḷiki kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum oru ciṟiya kaṇṇāṭiyiṉuṭ kāṇappaṭutalē mikavum viyakkaṟkapālatām.  atupōla, periya ōr uriyilē cila poruḷēṉum pala poruḷēṉum kāṇappaṭutal viyappiṉi viḷiviyātu; maṟṟucciṟiyatoru pāṭṭilē palatiṟappaṭṭa āḻnta poruḷellām kāṇappaṭutalē viyakkaṟpālatākum.  uriyil oruvariyaṟi yiṉmiyiṉālē poruṭku vēṇṭu vaṉavum vēṇṭātaṉavumāṉa pala coṟkaḷ nirampi niṉṟu viyappiṉit tārāvāyt tāmeṭutta poruḷi māttiram pulaṅkoḷa viḷakki yoḻikiṉṟaṉa.  maṟṟuc ceyyuḷilō, poruṭcāramāṉa coṟkaḷ māttiram āyntamikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa vākaliṉ, ac coṟkaḷ koṇṭa poruṭkaruvi aṟiyap pukuvāṉoruvaṉ, taṉ aṟivāl ataṉik kūrntaṟiya vēṇṭutaliṉ taṉṉaṟivu mika nuṇukap peṟutalōṭu ac ciṟiya colliṟ periya poruḷaṭaṅki niṟṟali aṟinta māttiri yiṉālē taṉṉiyaṟiyātoru viyappumuṭaṉeyti, ataṉkaṇ ōr iṉpantōṉṟa ataṉ vāypaṭṭuc ciṟakkiṉṟāṉ.  ākavē, pāṭṭu oṉṟumē uyirkaṭku aṟivi viḷakki ataṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa uṇarvi eḻac ceytu iṉpam payappatoṉṟāy nili peṟukiṉṟatu.  urip payiṟciyil aṟivu māttiram viḷakkam eytumē yallatu uṇarviṉ vaḻittāṉa iṉpan tōṉṟutal illiyeṉka.  allatūum, uriyellām iyaṟkiyilēyē ici taḻuvātu naṭappaṉavāmākaliṉ avi uṇarvi eḻuppumāṟu evvāṟṟāṉumilli yeṉka.  pāṭṭukkaḷō, iyaṟki-yākavē iṭaṅkaṭku ēṟṟapeṟṟi yellām pala tiṟappaṭṭa ici taḻuvi naṭakkumākaliṉ avi uyiruṇarvi eḻupputaṟkaṭku piṉṉum ciṟappuṭiyavām.  eṉṉi? iciyik kēṭṭu urukāta poruḷ ulakat tilliyaṉṟē?  ulakiyal ciṟitu maṟiyāta makavi aṉṉi toṭṭiliṟkiṭattit tālāṭṭa aḵtavviciyā liṉpuṟṟu uṟaṅkal kaṇṭāmaṉṟē?  koṭiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷum naccuyirkaḷum pullāṅ kuḻalōci kēṭṭavaḷavāṉē tantoḻil maṟantu iṉpuṟṟut taṉvayamaḻital teḷiyap paṭṭatoṉṟaṉṟō?  itaṉ māṭci iṉitu aṟivuṟuttavataṟkeṉṟē māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyāril,
	ciṟivāṉ puṉaṟṟillic ciṟṟampa lattumeṉ
	cintiyuḷḷum
uṟivāṉ uyarmatiṟ kūṭali ṉāyntavoṇ
	ṭīntamiḻaṉ
tuṟivāy nuḻintaṉiyō? aṉṟi yēḻicic
	cūḻalpukkō?
iṟivā! taṭavarittōṭ keṉko lāmpukun
	teytiyatē?
eṉṟu tiruvāy malarntaruḷiṉar.  ippeṟṟittāṉa ici, tāṉēyum iṉpuṟuttukiṉṟa pāṭṭōṭum cērumāyiṉ avi yiraṇṭiṉ kalappāl tōṉṟum iṉpam ivviyal piṟṟeṉṟu kuṟitturikkal ēlumō?  eṉavē, iciyōṭu piṇintiyaṅkum pāṭṭiṟkum aḵtiṉṟiyiyaṅkum urikkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭum, ivviraṇṭil muṉṉiyatu payaṉ peri tuṭittātalum piṉṉiyatu payaṉ ciṟitē yuṭittātalum teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkā niṟkum.  aṟṟaṉṟu, uri nūlkaḷum orōvaḻi viyappuṇarvu tarak kāṇṭumākaliṉ ataṉic ceyyuḷukkē varintu kūṟiyatu poruntātām piṟaveṉiṉ: naṉṟu kaṭāyiṉāy, orōvōrurinūlkaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē pāṭṭiṟkuriya col nayam poruḷ nayaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭutalāl aṅṅaṉam avviyappuṇarvu tōṉṟiṟṟākaliṉ atupaṟṟi īṇṭik kāvatōr iḻukkilliyeṉpatu.  ip peṟṟippaṭṭa urip pakutikaḷ cila āciriyar nakkīraṉār kaḷaviyaluri yiṉiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum; avikoṇṭu ivvuṇmi aṟiyaṟpāṟṟām.10
kaviti kaṟpittal11
tāymoḻik kalviyil kavitiyē uyirniliyāṉa pakuti.  kaviñarkaḷ tam vāḻkki nukarvukaḷiliruntum, paṭṭaṟiviliruntum eḻum uṇarccikaḷiyum uyar karuttukkaḷiyum ōci iṉimi, col nayam, poruḷ nayam ākiya paṇpukaḷ niṟinta collō viyaṅkaḷāl veḷippaṭuttuvar.  nuṇkalikaḷil kaviti ciṟanta iṭam peṟukiṉṟatu.  paṇṭit tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ tam matinuṭpattāl nuṉittaṟintu veḷiyiṭṭa aṟivuc celvaṅkaḷ yāvum pā vaṭivilēyē uḷḷaṉa.  tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷip poṟuttavari naṭi vēṟupāṭukaḷik kavitikaḷiltāṉ kāṇalām.  oṉpāṉ cuvikaḷum, pattu vaki aḻakukaḷum iṟicci uḷḷuṟi yuvamam pōṉṟa ciṟanta aṇikaḷum, piṟa aṇikaḷum pākkaḷiltāṉ ciṟantu kāṇappaṭum.  paṭippōr maṉatti īrppataṟkuriya ōci nayam, poruḷiṉimi, curuṅkiya paṇpu, ceṟivu mutaliyavi pākkaḷiṉ ciṟanta kūṟukaḷ.  iḷamiyiliruntē māṇākkarkaṭkuk kaviti kaṟpittali mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
kaviti kaṟpittaliṉ nōkkaṅkaḷ: kavitikaḷiṉ uyir nilikkarutti uṇarntu avaṟṟi yātta kaviñaṉiṉ karuttukkaḷ, uṇarccikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum avaṉkaṇṭa iyaṟki eḻilkaḷ, makkaḷ maṉappāṉmi, itayappāṅku mutaliyavaṟṟiyum māṇākkarkaḷ aṟiyac ceytalē ciṟanta nōkkamākak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷañciyattip perukkuvataṟ keṉṟō, moḻiyaṟivu vaḷarccikkeṉṟō, karuttukkaḷi veḷiyiṭum tiṟaṉkaḷi vaḷarppataṟkeṉṟō kavitiyik kaṟpittal kūṭātu.  kavitikaḷ, pala vāḻkki uṇmikaḷi aḻaku porunta eṭutturikkiṉṟaṉa.  avvāṟu kaviñaṉ eṭutturikkum peṟṟiyi māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarumpaṭi ceytuviṭṭāl tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ toṇṭu or elliyi eytiviṭṭatu eṉṟu karutalām; tamiḻ payilum māṇākkarkaḷum kalviyiṉ payaṉi ōraḷavu aṭintu viṭṭaṉar eṉṟum tīrmāṉikkalām.  oru kaviñaṉiṉ enta maṉa eḻuccikaḷum kaṟpaṉi āṟṟalum kaviti piṟakkak kāraṇamāka iruntaṉavō, atē maṉa eḻuccikaḷiyum kaṟpaṉikaḷiyum māṇākkarkaḷiṭam uṇṭu paṇṇumāṟu kaviti kaṟpikkap peṟutal vēṇṭum.  kavitiyip paṭittu nukartalē oru taṉippaṭṭa iṉpam ākum.  pēraṟiñarkaḷiṉ collōviyaṅkaḷip paṭittut tuykkum poḻutu avarkaḷ varuṇikkum kāṭcikaḷ nam maṉattiriyil naṉku paṭiyum; avarkaḷ kuṟippiṭum uṇarccikaḷ nam itayattik kiḷarcci ceyyum.  kavitikaḷik kaviñaṉiṉ maṉappōkki yoṭṭi avaṉuṭaṉ kalantu pāṭuṅkāl irāmaliṅka vaḷḷal kūṟiyavāṟu naṟkaruppañ cāṟṟiṉilē tēṉ kalantu pāl kalantu ceḻuṅkaṉit tīñcuvi kalantu, ūṉ kalantu uyirkalantu uvaṭṭāmal iṉippati aṟiyalām.  ivvāṟu kalantu pāṭi iṉpatti nukartal vāḻkkiyiyē paṇpaṭutti makkaṭ paṇpi vaḷarkkum taṉmiyi eytuvikkum.  aṉṟiyum, kavitikaḷ kaṟpōriṉ kaṟpaṉiyi vaḷappaṭutti, avarkaḷiṭam taṉivīṟu (Individuality) eṉṟa paṇpi vaḷarttu avarkaḷ oḻukkattiyum cīrppaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum collalām.  ikkūṟiyavaṟṟāl kaviti kaṟpittaliṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiyum nōkkattiyum oruvāṟu aṟiyalām.
kavitikaḷit tērnteṭuttal: iṉṟu avacarak-kōlattil aḷḷit teḷitta mātiri kuṟukiya kālattil ciṟitum cintaṉiyiṉṟi oru cila āciriyarkaḷāl oru nerukkaṭiyil eḻutappeṟṟup pāṭapputtakak kuḻuviṟku aṉuppavēṇṭiya nāḷ neruṅki viṭṭatē eṉṟa kavaliyāl kaṉṉāpiṉṉā eṉṟu acciṭap peṟṟu veḷivarum pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷil kāṇappeṟum kavitikaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉanilikkum uḷaccuvikkum ēṟṟaṉavāka illi eṉpati uṇmiyākak kaṟpittali mēṟkoḷḷum moḻi-yāciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟivar.  kavitiyiṉ karuttukkaḷiyum nayaṅkaḷiyum māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarntu tuyttalē kavitip pāṭattiṉ payaṉ eṉpati uṇarāmalēyē kavitikaḷ tērnteṭukkappeṟutal poṟuppaṟṟa ceyalākum.  ivaṟṟik kaṟpikkum kālamum ataṟkuc celaviṭappeṟum poruḷum viḻalukkiṟitta nīrpōlākum.
toṭakka nili: iḷañciṟārkaḷukku viḷaṅkātaṉavum avarkaḷ tuyttu uṇarntirātaṉavum avarkaḷ paṭṭaṟiviṟkap pāṟpaṭṭaṉavumāṉa karuttukkaḷum, nikaḻccikaḷum, kāṭcikaḷum avarkaḷukkuc cuviyaḷikka māṭṭā eṉavē, kīḻvakuppu māṇākkarkaḷukkut tērnteṭukkappeṟum pāṭalkaḷ avarkaḷ maṉanilikkukantaṉavāy iruttal vēṇṭum.  uḷḷak kiḷarcciyik tūṇṭum eḷiya iṉiya coṟkaḷ, uyir tatumpum cantanayam, coṟkaḷ tirumpat  tirumpa vantu olikkak kūṭiya piṉvaru niliyaḻaku, karuttup poruḷiṉṟi kāṭcip poruḷkaḷiṉ varuṇaṉi ākiyavaṟṟik koṇṭu miḷirum pāṭalkaḷ ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ cevikku  iṉpa amiḻtam ūṭṭum; cintikkum ciṟanta viruntāka amiyum.  iḷañciṟārkaḷiṉ maṉam naṉku virintu irukka muṭiyātu; paranta ulakiluḷḷa pala poruḷkaḷi aṟiyum āṟṟal avarkaḷiṭam tōṉṟum paruvam iṉṉum vāykka villi.  avarkaḷ vāḻkkiyil kaṇṭu kēṭṭu uṇṭu uyirttu uṟṟaṟiyak kūṭiya poruḷkaḷi maṭṭilumtāṉ avarkaḷ teḷivupeṟa eḷitil uṇarakkūṭum.  eṉavē, avarkaḷ vāḻkkiyil eḷitil kuṟukkiṭakkūṭiya ceṭi koṭikaḷ, maraṅkaḷ, pūkkaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, uṟṟār uṟaviṉarkaḷ, naṇparkaḷ, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, viṇmīṉkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟit tāṉ avarkaḷ karuta muṭiyum; uṇarakkūṭum.  ātalāl avarkaḷiṉ viḷiyāṭṭuttaṉmikkum maṉap pāṉmikkum ēṟṟa pala vita iṉiya pāṭalkaḷic cērkka vēṇṭum.  avarkaḷ ārvattuṭaṉ kaṟkakkūṭiya iṉṉiciyum eḷimiyumuḷḷa pāppāp pāṭalkaḷ, āṭṭappāṭalkaḷ, cevilippāṭalkaḷ, katippāṭalkaḷ, viṭukatikaḷ mutali yavikaḷi avarkaḷiṉ maṉanilikku ēṟṟaṉavākak koḷḷalām.  avaṟṟi avarkaḷ eḷitil paṭikkak kūṭiyavāṟu coṟpirivu ceytu, periya eḻuttukkaḷiṉ aḻakāṉa tāḷil acciṭṭu vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.  ciṟuvarkaṭkup puttakam eḻutuvōr itil atikak kavaṉam koḷḷavēṇṭum.  kavimaṇi tēcika vināyam piḷḷi avarkaḷiṉ pāṭalkaḷ, cuttāṉantapāratiyāriṉ pāṭalkaḷ, kuḻantik kaviñar aḻa.  vaḷḷiyappāviṉ pāṭalkaḷ, civa cittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakattār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kuḻantip pāṭalkaḷ pōṉṟavi kuḻantikaṭku ēṟṟavi.  ivaṟṟit tavira, avvappoḻutu ciṟuvar pakutiyil kalimakaḷ, āṉanta vikaṭaṉ, amutacurapi, tiṉamaṇik katir, tamiḻnāṭu-ñāyiṟu malar pōṉṟa paruva itaḻkaḷil veḷivarum pāṭalkaḷiyum kaṇṇaṉ, kaṟkaṇṭu, pūñcōli, ampulimāmā mutaliya kuḻanti malarkaḷil varum pāṭalkaḷiyum iḷañciṟārkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkum moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ tokuttu vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
iṉṟu toṭakkanilip paḷḷikaḷil kavitip pāṭaṅkaḷil mikutiyāka mūturi, ulakanīti, ātticūṭi, koṉṟi vēntaṉ, naṉṉeṟi, nalvaḻi, nītineṟi viḷakkam pōṉṟa aṟaneṟip pāṭalkaḷē mikutiyāka kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  toṭakka nilivakuppukaḷukkeṉa veḷivarum pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷilum ippāṭalkaḷē mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  nalvaḻiyum naṉṉeṟiyum aṟiyum paruvam eytappeṟāta iḷañciṟārkaḷukku kuruvittaliyil paṉaṅkāyik kaṭṭi vitta mātiri appāṭalkaḷik kaṟpittalāl yātu payaṉ?  avarkaḷukkut terinta coṟkaḷum poruḷkaḷum mikak kuṟivākavuḷḷa iḷam paruvattil avaṟṟi aṟintu koḷḷa iyalumā?  uḷaviyal muṟikkuc ciṟitum ovvāta immuṟi virivil māṟavēṇṭum.  pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷik kiyālum toṭa virumpāta kuḻanti kaṇṇaṉiyum kaṟkaṇṭiyum pūñcōli yiyum ampulimāmāviyum ārvattuṭaṉ paṭippati liruntāvatu kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷum ciṟārkaḷukkup pāṭap puttakam eḻutuvōrum kuḻantikaḷiṉ maṉappāṅki ōraḷavu aṟintu koḷḷac kūṭātā?
naṭunili-uyarnili: naṭunili vakuppukkaḷil māṇāk karkaḷ iyalpāka virumpum muṟkūṟappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷuṭaṉ tirukkuṟaḷ, nālaṭiyār, tirikaṭukam, aṟaneṟiccāram, nāṉmaṇik kaṭiki pōṉṟa aṟaneṟip pāṭalkaḷi mikutiyākavum, uyarnili vakuppukkaḷil ivaṟṟi iṉṉum caṟṟu mikutiyākavum cērkkalām.  tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tiruvicippā, tiruppukaḻ, nālāyiram ivaṟṟiṉ pāṭalkaḷum tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ, paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ, matāṉ cākipu, vētanāyakam piḷḷi mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ camayap piṇakkamiṉṟip potunōkkāka amintuḷḷa iṟivāḻttup pāṭalkaḷum; ariccantira purāṇam, naḷaveṇpā, kucēlō pākkiyāṉam, tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇam, kāñci purāṇam mutaliya katip pakutikaḷum; kamparāmāyaṇam, periya purāṇam, villiputtūrār pāratam, cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali mutaliya kāviyap pakutikaḷum; pal cuvi niṟinta taṉip pāṭalkaḷum mēṟpaṭiva māṇākkarkaḷukku ēṟṟavi.  piḷḷit tamiḻ, cīṭṭukkavi, tūtu, ulā, paraṇi, paḷḷu, kuṟavañci, cintu pōṉṟa cila pirapantaṅkaḷiliruntum cila eḷiya pakutikaḷic cērkkalām.  aṉṟiyum, nāṭṭup paṟṟu moḻippaṟṟukaḷi ūṭṭavalla ikkālak kaviñarkaḷākiya pāratiyār, pāratitācaṉ, kavimaṇi tēcika vināyakam piḷḷi, pērāciyar cuntaram piḷḷi, pērāciriyar rā. irākavayyaṅkār, tiru. vi. kalyāṇacuntaraṉār, ve. pa. cuppiramaṇiya mutaliyār, cuttāṉanta pāratiyār, nāmakkal kaviñar ākiyōriṉ pāṭalkaḷiyum cērkkalām. aṉṟāṭam veḷiyākum nāḷitaḻkaḷilum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷilum kāṇappeṟum cila uyarnta pāṭalkaḷiyum taṉip puttaṅkaḷil tokuttu vittuk koṇṭu pāṭapputtaṅkaḷil nallaṉavaṟṟic cērkkalām.
kaviti kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ: ceyyuḷ kaṟpikkum nōkkaṅkaḷ cevvaṉē niṟivēṟa vēṇṭumāyiṉ kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ kaviti iṉpam tuyppōrkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  kavitiyi uṇarntāltāṉ piṟarkku avaṟṟi naṉku uṇartta muṭiyum.  oru cila māṇākkarkaḷ kavitikaḷiṉ murukuṇarcciyil tāmākavē īṭupaṭṭut tiḷikkakkūṭum.  āṉāl, perumpālōrkku aḵtu iyalāta toṉṟu; avarkaṭku āciriyar  uṇarttiṉāltāṉ, avarkaḷ kavitiyiṉ pattil īṭupaṭa muṭiyum.  kaṟpikkappōkum kavitiyiṉ uṭ kiṭakkip pulaṉākumāṟu viṟuviṟuppāṉa oru cūḻniliyi uṇṭākkip piṉ ataṉ tuṇiyiṉik koṇṭu kavitiyiṉ uṇarcciyi māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarumpaṭi ceytalvēṇṭum.
	kēṭṭāṉ iḷiyōṉ kiḷarñālam varattiṉālē
mīṭṭāḷ aḷittāḷ vaṉantammuṉi vemmi muṟṟat
tīṭṭāta vēṟkaṇ ciṟutāyeṉa yāvarālum
mūṭṭāta kālak kaṭittīyeṉa mūṇṭeḻuntāṉ.
kaṇṇiṟ kaṭittīyuka neṟṟiyiṟ kaṟṟi nāṟa
viṇṇiṟ cuṭaruṅkeṭa meyyiṉil nīrvi rippa
uṇṇiṟkum uyirppeṉum ūti piṟaṅka niṉṟa
aṇṇaṟperiyōṉ taṉatātiyiṉ mūrtti yottāṉ,
ciṅkak kuruḷikkiṭu tīñcuvi ūṉi nāyiṉ
veṅkaṭ ciṟukuṭṭaṉi yūṭṭa virumpiṉāḷē
naṅkik kaṟiviṉṟiṟam naṉṟitu naṉṟi teṉṉāk
kaṅkik kiṟivaṉ kamalakki puṭittu nakkāṉ.12
eṉṟa pākkaḷik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu irāma ilakkuvaratu cakōtaravāñci, irāmaṉ muṭicūṭṭutalukku ēṟpaṭṭa taṭi, ataṟkuriya kāraṇam ākiyavaṟṟi viṟuviṟuppāṉa coṟkaḷil curuṅkak kūṟiyum, ilakkuvaṉatu cīṟṟaṟtiṟkuk kāraṇaṅkāṭṭiyum pākkaḷi uṇarcciyuṭaṉ paṭittāl māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarcci vayattarāki avaṟṟi naṉku cuvippar; vekuḷic cuviyilum tiḷippar.
kaviti kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ pāṭalkaḷi vāy viṭṭu iciyuṭaṉ paṭikkum paḻakkam uṭiyavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  maṉakkaṇ eṉṟu oṉṟiruppatu pōlavē maṉakkātum (Mind‘s ear) nammiṭam  amintukiṭakkiṉṟatu.  coṟkaḷukku iraṇṭu kuṇaṅkaḷ uṇṭu.  oṉṟu, oli; maṟṟoṉṟu, poruḷ.  accup puttakaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum coṟkaḷ ariyu yirōṭu tāṉ uḷḷaṉa.13  nām ūṭṭukiṉṟa ōciyiṉ mūlam tāṉ eñciya ariyuyirum muṟṟup peṟukiṉṟatu.  ākavē, oliyi ūṭṭittāṉ pāṭalkaḷip paṭikka vēṇṭum.  iṭattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu ōci yūṭṭip paṭittāltāṉ pākkaḷil aṭakki vikkap peṟṟuḷḷa uṇarcciveḷḷam pīṟiṭṭukkoṇṭu veḷivarum.  coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷuṭaṉ avaṟṟiṉ toṉip poruḷum kaṭṭaviḻntu koṇṭu puṟappaṭum.  kavitikaḷi enniliyilum ēṟṟa iciyuṭaṉ vāyviṭṭup paṭittāltāṉ avaṟṟiṉ collum poruḷum cuviyum naṉku tōṉṟi uḷḷattiṟku uvaki yaḷikkum.  tolkāppiyattiṟku uri kaṇṭa pērāciriyar, pā veṉpatu, cēṭpulattirunta kālattum oruvaṉ eḻuttum collum teriyāmal pāṭam ōtuṅkāl, avaṉ collukiṉṟa ceyyuḷi vikaṟpittu iṉṉa ceyyuḷ eṉṟu uṇartaṟ kētuvākip parantupaṭṭuc celvatō ōci14 eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar.  itaṉāl pā eṉpatu icimayamāka iruppatoṉṟu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  poruṭ ciṟappu mikkillāta kavitiyum icip poruttam15 amiyap peṟṟiruppiṉ atu paṭippōriṉ maṉattik kavaravallatu eṉpati kavitiyip paṭippōr paṭṭaṟivāl aṟintu koḷḷalām. iciyil īṭupaṭāta uyirppirāṇikaḷē illi eṉalām.  puṟattē ceṉṟulavum māṇākkariṉ maṉattiyum kavarntu naṭipeṟum kavitip pāṭattil īṭupaṭutta vallatu ici.  iciyeṉṟāl kēḷvikku iṉimiyāka irukku māṟu ataṟkuriya ōciyil kavitiyip paṭippatākum; icip pulavar pōl tāḷam mutaliyaṉa amittuc curattuṭaṉ avaṟṟip pāṭavēṇṭum eṉpatilli.  iḵtu ellōrālum ceyya iyalātu.  eṉavē, kaviti kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ iciyuṭaṉ paṭikkum paḻakkatti yuṭiyavarkaḷāka irukka vēṇṭum.
pala paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟuviṭṭāl pāṭalkaḷic collip piṟarukku uṇarttiviṭa muṭiyum eṉṟu niṉippatu tavaṟu.  kavitiyippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷi aṟivatu vēṟu; kavitiyi uṇarvatu vēṟu.  kavitiyi uṇarvatu eṉṟāl, kaviñaṉiṉ uṇarcciyit tāṉ peṟuvatu. tāṉ peṟṟa av vuṇarcciyip piṟariyum aṭiyac ceytāltāṉ kavitiyi veṟṟiyuṭaṉ kaṟpittatākak koḷḷa muṭiyum.  cilar tērvukaḷil veṟṟi peṟṟavuṭaṉ  kavitikaḷip paṭippatiyē niṟutti viṭukiṉṟaṉar.  itu kūṭātu.  kaṟpittal tuṟiyi mēṟ koṇṭavarkaḷ eṉṟum iṭiviṭātu kavitikaḷip paṭittut tuytta vaṇṇam iruttal vēṇṭum.  appoḻututāṉ kavitikaḷ avarkaḷiṭam uyiruṭaṉ nilavum.  paṭippu niṉṟa aṉṟē kavitikaḷum cettup pōkum.  kaṟpippataṟku vēṇṭiya aṟivu avarkaḷiṭam pōtumāṉa aḷavu uḷḷatu eṉpatu uṇmiyāka irukkalām.  āṉāl, kaviti kaṟpittalil vēṇṭappaṭuvatu aṟivu aṉṟu; uṇarccip perukkutāṉ.  maḻiyiṉṟēl ērinīr vaṟṟip pōkum.  maṇaṟkēṇi toṭṭaṉittuttāṉē ūṟum?  atu pōlat tāṉ kavitiyum, paṭikkum aḷaviyoṭṭit tāṉē kavitikaḷ uyirōṭ ṭattuṭaṉ ilaṅkum?  tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ paṭikkavilli eṉṟa kuṟi talikāṭṭavē kūṭātu.  oru cilar tavaṟāka āciriyat toḻili mēṟkoṇṭu ac camūkattiṟkē iḻukkiṉit tēṭukiṉṟaṉar.  ivarkaḷ tamakkuc ciṟitum porutta maṟṟa toḻililiruntu koṇṭu māṇākkariṉ kālatti vīṇākkuvativiṭa tamakkēṟṟa toḻilukkup pōyviṭuvatu nallatu.
kaviti kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ ivvāṟu pala nūlkaḷip paṭittuk tuykkumpoḻutu tam uḷḷattik kavarnta pākkaḷi oru taṉi kuṟippēṭṭil kuṟittu vittuk koḷḷum vaḻakkatti mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum16.  mutaṉ mutalākak kaṟpikkum tuṟikku varukiṉṟavarkaṭku ippaḻakkam mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷi eḻutum vāyppu varuṅkāl pallāṇṭukaḷ ivvāṟu tiraṭṭivitta cēma nitiyiliruntu ēṟṟa pāṭalkaḷip poṟukki eṭuttuk koḷḷalām.
kavitikaḷik kaṟpippōr kavitikkuriya iṭattiṟkēṟṟa canta nayam, kaṟpaṉiyāṟṟal, aṇi nayam, colnayam, collum kaviñaṉ peṟavikkum kuṟippu ākiyavikaḷi naṉku aṟiyum āṟṟal peṟṟiruttalvēṇṭum.  ivaṟṟi uṇarttavalla tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷik kaṟka vēṇṭum.  mēṟṟici nāṭṭu moḻikaḷiluḷḷavi pōl, tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ nam tamiḻmoḻiyil iṉṉum tōṉṟavilli.  atti pūtta mātiri eṅkō oṉṟiraṇṭu tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
kavitip pāṭattit toṭaṅkum muṟikaḷ: paṭṭaṟivu peṟāta āciriyarkaḷ potuvākap pāṭaṅkaḷi evvāṟu toṭaṅkuvateṉṟum, ciṟappākak kavitip pāṭatti evvāṟu toṭaṅkuteṉṟum aṟiyātu mayaṅkuvar.  innūlāciriyarip poṟuttavari ippaṭittāṉ toṭaṅka vēṇṭum eṉṟa tiṭṭa māṉa muṟiyoṉṟum illi.  palamuṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭu māṇākkariṉ kālattiyum uṟcākattiyum keṭuppativiṭa nēraṭiyākak kavitiyil pukuvatē cariyāṉa muṟiyākum.  cila camayam viḻākkūṭṭaṅkaḷil talivarāyirukkum uḷḷūrp periyār mukkiyamāṉa veḷiyūrc coṟpoḻivāḷari aṟimukappaṭuttumpoḻutu vēṇṭātavaṟṟi yellām kūṟi vīṇ kālattip pōkkumpoḻutu kēṭpōr talivar mītu veṟuppuk kāṭṭi virivil avar pēcci muṭittup pēccāḷarip pēca viṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu viḻivati nām aṭikkaṭi pārttirukkiṉṟōm.  atupōlavē, kaviñaṉ kūṟuvataṟkut taṭiyāka niṉṟukoṇṭu āciriyar vēṇṭāta veṟṟuri pakarntu koṇṭiruppati viṭa, pāṭalukkuḷ nuḻintu kaviñaṉiyē pēcaviṭutal naṉṟu.  kaṟpikkappōkum kavitiyiṉ muḻukkaruttiyum aḻakiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvataṟkāka utavum cātaṉaṅkaḷi avvappoḻutu vēṇṭuṅkāl kiyāḷa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, pāṭattiṟku avaṟṟi muṉṉuriyākak koḷvatu tavaṟu.  eṉiṉum, naṭimuṟiyiluḷḷa oru cila muṟikaḷi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu ārāyvōm.  ciṟanta toṭakkam vēli muṭiviṉ pātiyallavā?  aṉṟiyum, avi kaṟpikkum tuṟiyil putitāka nuḻivārkku vaḻikāṭṭiyākavum amital kūṭum. 
(1) kaviñaṉ varalāṟu kūṟit toṭaṅkum muṟi: paṭṭaṟi vuṭiya āciriyarkaḷāyiṉum cari, payiṟṟal tuṟikkum putiyavar kaḷāyiṉum cari, perumpālum iṉṟu avarkaḷāl im muṟi mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu. itu ciṟitum poruttamaṟṟa muṟi.  pāṭalik kaṟpitta piṟaku, pāṭaliṉ poruḷum cuviyum māṇākkarkaṭku viḷakkam peṟātiruntāl, āciriyar, appāṭalkaḷi eḻutuvataṟkuk kaviñaṉukku nēriṭṭa cūḻniliyik kūṟalām.  appoḻutum kaviñaṉip peṟṟa tāy tantiyar, kaviñaṉ piṟanta ūr mutaliya vēṇṭāta vivaraṅkaḷiyellām kūṟip pāṭaliṉ cuviyik keṭukka vēṇṭiyatilli.  pāṭal pāṭiya cantarppatti viḷakkum aḷavukku varalāṟu kūṟiṉāl pōtumāṉatu; atutāṉ pāṭaliṉ poruḷi viḷakkat tuṇi ceyyum.  kāviyap pakutiyāka iruntāl, appāṭal varum katiyiṉ iṭattic cuṭṭum aḷaviṟkuk kati varalāṟu urittāl pōtum.  aṭiyip piṭiṭā, pārata paṭṭā eṉṟāṟpōl katiyit toṭakkattiliruntu kūṟa vēṇṭiya avaciyamilli.  mēlē kuṟippiṭṭa pēccāḷarukkum kēṭpōrukkum iṭiyil taṭiyāka niṟkum viḻāt talivarpōl kaviñaṉukkum māṇākkar kaṭkum iṭiyil āciriyar taṭiyāka niṉṟu avar kālatti vīṇākkat tēviyilli.  evvaḷavuk kevvaḷavu virivākap pāṭalukkuḷ nuḻiya muṭiyumō avvaḷavuk kavvaḷavu virivāka nuḻintu viṭavēṇṭum; māṇākkarkaṭkuc calippi uṇṭākkum aḷavukku mukavurikūṟip pāṭatti muṭippataṟkuk kālampōtātapaṭi ceytu viṭakkūṭātu.  oru cila taṉip pāṭalkaḷ, kāḷamēkap pulavar yamakaṇṭam pāṭiyapoḻutu pāṭiya cilēṭicceyyuṭkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṟpikka nēriṭuṅkāl orōvaḻi immuṟiyi mēṟkoḷḷalām.  kaviñaṉ varalāṟu kūṟutal evvitattilum kaviti kaṟpittal ākātu eṉpati maṭṭilum āciriyar eṉṟum niṉivil irutta vēṇṭum.  kaviti kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya nērattil kaviñaṉ varalāṟṟiṟkuc ciṟitum iṭamē illi.  kavitic cuvikku kaviñaṉ varalāṟu tuṇipuriyumāyiṉ ati ōr uṟuppākak koḷḷalām; atiyum vēṇṭumaḷaviṟkuttāṉ kiyāḷa vēṇṭum.
(2) viḷakka muṟi: itu palvēṟu vaṭivaṅkaḷil kiyāḷap paṭukiṉṟatu.  oru cila āciriyarkaḷ tām kaṟpikkappōkum kavitiyil varum kaṭiṉa coṟkaḷik karumpalakiyil eḻutik kāṭṭiviṭṭu kavitiyik kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.  cantarppattiṟkup poruntāta muṟiyil ivvāṟu coṟkaḷit taṉiyākap pirippatāl accoṟkaḷ iraṭṭippuk kaṭiṉamāki viṭuvatit tavira vēṟoru palaṉum ēṟpaṭātu.  coṟkaḷ taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil uṇarttum poruḷvēṟu; avi kalantu iṭatti yoṭṭit taṉ ōci vaṉmiyālum poruḷ taṉmiyālum putiyatoru karuttip piṟappikkum nili vēṟu.  taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyil avaṟṟiṟku eṉṟumē irāta oru poruṭ ciṟappu ēṟpaṭṭu viṭukiṉṟatu.  nāṇayaṅkaḷippōl paḻakippaḻaki uruvaḻintu merukēṟippōṉa coṟkaḷ kavitiyil varum poḻutu uyar pataviyip peṟukiṉṟaṉa; āṟṟaliyum aṭikiṉṟaṉa.  ataṉāl avaṟṟiṉ valuvum vēkamum atikarikkiṉṟaṉa.  araṅku kīṟi vaṭṭāṭuṅkāl caturaṅkak kāykaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa ellikkuḷ ulavumpoḻutu eppaṭip putiya āṟṟalip peṟukiṉṟaṉavō, atupōlavē kaviñaṉ yāppu muṟiyil coṟkaḷik kiyāḷumpoḻutu avi putiya āṟṟalip peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  taṉiyāka irukkum kuṇṭukaḷiṉ taṉmi vēṟu; tuppākkikkuḷ vikkap peṟṟirukkum kuṇṭukaḷiṉ taṉmi vēṟu.  atupōlttāṉ taṉic coṟkaḷiṉ porūḷvēṟu; kavitikkuḷḷirukkum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷ vēṟu.  vēkamum appaṭittāṉ.
	aṇṇalum nōkkiṉāḷ; avaḷum nōkkiṉāḷ.17
eṉṟa aṭiyil varum nōkku eṉṟa colliṉ poruḷi nām naṉku aṟivōm.
	kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇiṉi nōkkuokkiṉ vāyccoṟkaḷ
eṉṉa payaṉum ila.18
eṉṟa kuṟaḷāl nōkku eṉṟa colliṉ eṇṇattik tōṟṟuvikkum āṟṟal terivikkap paṭukiṉṟatallavā?  pārttu, kaṇṭu, viḷittu eṉṟa coṟkaḷip pōlattāṉ nōkki eṉṟa collum eṉṟu niṉippatu tavaṟu; nōkki eṉpataṟkut taṉivīṟu uṇṭu.  ikkuṟaḷi niṉittu mēṟkāṭṭiya aṭiyiluḷḷa nōkki eṉṟa colliṉ poruḷi aṟiyum poḻutu tāṉ at taṉivaṉmiyi aṟikiṉṟōm.  kavitiyil varum coṟkaḷiṉ taṉmi ivvāṟirukka avaṟṟit taṉiyāka eṭutteḻutuvatāl yātu payaṉ?  aṉṟiyum, colliṉ poruḷi aṟiyum muṟiyum tavaṟu.  cantarppatti viṭṭu nīkkiṉāl coṟ poruḷ cariyāka viḷaṅkavum ceyyātu.
immuṟiyil kavitiyik kaṟpippatu tavaṟu.  kavitiyi iciyūṭṭi aḻuttam tiruttamākap pala muṟi paṭippatālēyē atiluḷḷa kaṭiṉa coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷi māṇākkarkaḷ ūkittuk koḷḷal kūṭum.  ivvāṟu uṇarntāltāṉ avarkaḷ coṟkaḷiṉ cariyāṉaporuḷi, ciṟappāṉa poruḷi, cantarppattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu putitāka uṇarttum poruḷi aṟiya muṭiyum.  ivvāṟu coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷi aṟiyāviṭṭāl, kavitiyiṉ uyirōṭṭam māṇākkarkaṭkup pulaṉākātu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka āṟāmpaṭiva māṇākkarkaṭku,
	maṇaṅkamaḻ pūviyum mayilum, tamālakkāṭum
	vaṇkiḷiyum, nīlaveṟpum maṭamāṉ kaṉṟum,
iṇaṅkukaṭa luntukirum, kārum miṉṉum,
	yamuṉiyeṉum tiru natiyum ekiṉap pēṭum,
kaṇaṅkuḻiya kōcalitē vakiya cōti
	kaṇmaṇiyum pāviyumpōl kamala vīṭṭiṉ
aṇaṅkaraci yuṭaṉkulavi āṭīr ūcal!
	alaṅkāra māyavarē āṭīrūcal!!19
eṉṟa pāṭalik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu mutalil pāṭali iciyākap pāṭiṉālēyē ataṉ potup poruḷi oruvāṟu uṇarak kūṭum; iraṇṭu mūṉṟu taṭavi iciyuṭaṉ paṭittāl pāṭaliṉ potupporuḷ viḷaṅkāmaṟpōkātu.  piṟaku pāṭap paṭikkumpōtu,
	maṇaṅkamaḻ pūviyum mayilum
tamālakkāṭum vaṇkiḷiyum
nīlaveṟpum maṭamāṉ kaṉṟum
iṇaṅkukaṭalum tukirum
kārum miṉṉum
yamuṉiyeṉum tiru natiyum ekiṉappēṭum
kaṇaṅkuḻiya kōcali tēvaki yacōti
kaṇmaṇiyum pāviyum pōl
eṉṟa toṭarkaḷil taṉittaṉi aḻuttam koṭuttuc coṟkaḷi naṉṟākap pirittup paṭittāl kaviñaṉ pāṭalil kāṭṭum kāṭciyi oruvāṟu uṇarak kūṭum.  vēṇṭumāṉāl maṇaṅkamaḻ pūvi, tamālam, veṟpu, tukir, kār, ekiṉam pōṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷit terivittup pāṭṭi mīṭṭum iciyuṭaṉ pāṭiṉāl pāṭṭil varuṇikkappaṭum kāṭci maṉattil naṉku paṭiyum.  cātāraṇa makkaḷum kavitiyi aṉupavikkumāṟu racikamaṇi ṭi. kē. ci. avarkaḷ ivvāṟutāṉ viḷakkuvārkaḷ.  ittakiya pāṭalkaḷi eṉ ariya naṇpar ulaka ūḻiyaṉār20 pōṉṟavarkaḷ ceyvatupōl iciyūṭṭi viḷakkam ceytāl māṇākkarkaḷ naṉku cuvippar.
cilacamayam puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭalkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟil varum coṟkaḷ mikak kaṭiṉamāka irukkum.  ammātirip pāṭalkaḷ vakuppu nilikku appāṟpaṭṭavi.  avaṟṟit taṉiyāṉa muṟiyāltāṉ (Exceptional treatment) viḷakka vēṇṭum.  attakiya pāṭalkaḷik kaṟpikkum poḻutu pāṭattiṉ toṭakkattilāyiṉum pāṭalip paṭikkum poḻutāyiṉum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷit taṉiyāka eṭuttuc collittāṉ ākavēṇṭum.  iṉṉum cila camayaṅkaḷil pāṭalip purintu koḷḷuvāṉ vēṇṭi katic curukkattiyō nikaḻcciyiṉ curukkattiyō kūṟip pāṭalkaḷ paṭikkappeṟum. kampar, villiputtūrār pōṉṟa cila peruṅ kaviñarkaḷiṉ pāṭalkaḷi ivvāṟu tāṉ kiyāḷa vēṇṭum.  toṭakkattil ciṟu viḷakkam tarappeṟāviṭṭāl ippāṭalkaḷip purintu koḷvatu kaṭiṉam.  ivi yellām cila kaṭiṉamāṉa pāṭalkaḷukkuk kiyāḷap peṟum muṟikaḷākum.
mēṟkūṟiya kaṭiṉamāṉa pākkaḷit tavira ēṉiya pākkaḷi māṇākkarkaṭkuk kaṟpikkumpoḻutellām kaviñaṉiyē pēcumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭutal vēṇṭum.  kaviñaṉiviṭa yārtāṉ avaṉ kūṟum uṇarcci veḷḷatti, aḻakōviyatti, itayap pāṅkit teḷivākak kāṭṭa iyalum?  kaviñaṉ kuralil avaṉ itaya pāvatti uṇarntu āciriyar pāṭali iciyūṭṭip paṭikkumpoḻutu, kaṭṭāyam māṇākkar atik cuvippar; cuviyiṉucciyi eṭṭip piṭikkavum ceyvar.  aṭiyiṟkāṇum pāṭalkaḷ pōṉṟavikaṭku evvita viḷakkamum tēviyilli.
	nāṭip pulaṅkaḷ uḻuvār karamum
	nayavurikaḷ
tēṭik koḻikkum kavivāṇar nāvuñ
	ceḻuṅkaruṇi
ōṭip perukum aṟivāḷar neñcum
	uvantunaṭam
āṭik kaḷikkum mayilē! uṉ pātam
	aṭikkalamē.21
itu kavimaṇiyiṉ kalimakaḷ vaṇakkam.
	teḷivuṟavē yaṟintiṭutal, teḷivutara moḻintiṭutal,
	cintip pārkkē
kaḷivaḷara uḷḷattil āṉantak kaṉavupala
	kāṭṭal, kaṇṇīrt
tuḷivaravuḷ ḷurukkutal, iṅ kiviyellām nīyaruḷun
	toḻilka ḷaṉṟō?
oḷivaḷarum tamiḻvāṇī ! aṭiyaṉēṟ kiviyaṉittum
	utavu vāyē.22
iṭiyiṉṟi aṇukkaḷelām cuḻalumeṉa iyalnūlār
	icittal kēṭṭōm;
iṭiyiṉṟik katirkaḷelām cuḻalumeṉa vāṉūlār
	iyampu kiṉṟār;
iṭiyiṉṟit toḻilpurital ulakiṉiṭip poruṭ kellām
	iyaṟki yāyiṉ		
iṭiyiṉṟik kalimakaḷē niṉa taruḷil eṉatuḷḷam
	iyaṅko ṇātō?23
ivi iraṇṭum pāratiyāriṉ kalimakaḷ vaṇakkaṅkaḷ.

	kāliyiḷam paritiyilē avaḷik kaṇṭēṉ;
	kaṭaṟparappil oḷippuṉalil kaṇṭēṉ; antac
cōliyilē malarkaḷilē taḷirkaḷ tammil
	toṭṭiṭam elāmkaṇṇil taṭṭup paṭṭāḷ;
māliyilē mēṟṟiciyil ilaku kiṉṟa
	māṇikkac cuṭarilavaḷ iruntāḷ; ālañ
cāliyilē kiḷitōṟum kiḷiyiṉ kūṭṭan
	taṉil anta aḻakeṉpāḷ kaviti tantāḷ.
ciṟukuḻanti viḻiyiṉilē oḷiyāy niṉṟāḷ;
	tiruviḷakkiṟ cirikkiṉṟāḷ; nāreṭuttu
naṟumalarit toṭuppāḷiṉ viralva ḷivil
	nāṭakattic ceykiṉṟāḷ ; aṭaṭē! centōṭ
puṟattiṉilē kalappiyuṭaṉ uḻavaṉ cellum
	putu naṭiyil pūrittāḷ; viḷinta naṉcey
niṟattiṉilē eṉ viḻiyi niṟutti ṉāḷ;eṉ
	neñcattil kuṭiyēṟi makiḻcci ceytāḷ.24
ivi iraṇṭum aḻakippaṟṟip pāratitācaṉ puṉinta kavitikaḷ.  ittakiya eḷiya iṉiya kavitikaḷit tēvāram, tiruvācakam, nālāyira tivyap pirapantam, tiruvaruṭpā ākiya nūlkaḷilum kāṇalām.  ippāṭalkaḷi iciyūṭṭip pāṭiṉālē pōtum; māṇākkarkaḷ kaviticcuviyil īṭupaṭṭu viṭuvar.  evvitat tiṟaṉāyvum ceyyappeṟātu māṇākkarkaḷ pāṭalkaḷip pāṭakkēṭka vēṇṭum.  pāṭam kaṟpitta piṟaku, iṟutiyil ētāvatu nayaṅkaḷi aṟiyātu viṭṭaṉarā eṉpati āciriyar terintu koḷḷalām.  cariyāṉa muṟiyil uṇarttappeṟṟal aḻakaṉittiyum māṇākkar uṇarntu koṇṭiruppatikkaṇṭu āciriyar viyappaṭivār.  ivvāṟu māṇākkarkaḷē pāṭalkaḷic cuvikkum tiṟaṉi avarkaḷiṭam vaḷarkka vēṇṭum; palavita nayaṅkaḷiyum aḻakukaḷiyum tāmākavē uṇarum āṟṟali māṇākkarkaḷiṭam uṇṭākka vēṇṭum.
(3) cūḻnili kāṭṭummuṟi: pāṭal eḻunta cūḻniliyi māṇākkarkaṭku oru ciṟitu viḷakkip pāṭattit toṭaṅkuvatu cūḻnili kāṭṭum muṟiyākum.  kaviñaṉ entac cūḻniliyil, entac cantarppattil, pāṭalip pāṭiṉāṉō antac cūḻniliyi, antac cantarppatti, māṇākkarkaḷ aṟintu koṇṭāl pāṭalkaḷi naṉku cuvikka muṭiyum; pāṭalkaḷil varum karuttukkaḷilum col nayaṅkaḷilum tiḷikkamuṭiyum.  kaṟpikkum āciriyar ivvāṟu niṉippatu caritāṉ; nallāciriyarkaḷ aṉivarum perumpālum immuṟiyi veṟṟiyuṭaṉtāṉ kiyāḷukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl, ellāp pāṭalkaḷukkum immuṟi poruntum eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyātu.  cila taṉippāṭalkaḷukku, kāviyattil mukkiya kaṭṭaṅkaḷil varum pāṭalkaḷukku immuṟi naṉku poruntum eṉpatil aiyamilli.  oru cila eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷik kāṇpōm.
	ācu kaviyāl akilavula keṅkum
vīcu pukaḻkkāḷa mēkamē-pūcurā
viṇkoṇṭa centaḻalil vēkutē aiyiyō
maṇ tiṉṟa pāṇameṉṟa vāy.25
eṉpatu oru kiyaṟunilic ceyyuḷ; taṉip pāṭal
iti nāṉkām paṭiva māṇākkarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkum vāyppu nēriṭṭāl, appāṭal eḻunta cantarppattiyum cūḻniliyiyum māṇākkarkaṭku muṉṉatāka aṟivittal vēṇṭum.  appoḻututāṉ maṇtiṉṟa pāṇam eṉṟa vāy eṉṟa coṟṟoṭaril aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa uṇarcci vēkatti, itayattuṭippi, naṉku uṇaramuṭiyum.
mēṟ kāṭṭiya pāṭalip pāṭiyavar iraṭṭiyar.  ivarkaḷiṉ oruvar kuruṭar; maṟṟavar noṇṭi.  iruvarum kaviti pāṭum āṟṟal peṟṟavarkaḷ.  perumpālum ivarkaḷuṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷil mutaliraṇṭaṭikaḷi oruvarum maṟṟa iraṇṭu aṭikaḷi iṉṉoruvarum pāṭutal vaḻakkam.  kuruṭar noṇṭiyit tūkkikkoṇṭu noṇṭi vaḻikāṭṭa, iruvarum ūr ūrākac ceṉṟu iṟi vaḻipāṭu ceyvatu vaḻakkam.  orunāḷ ivarkaḷ tiruvārūrkkuc ceṉṟu civataricaṉam ceytu maṇṭapattil vantu taṅkiyiruntaṉar.  ivaruḷ oruvar,
	nāṇeṉṟāl nañcirukkum naṟcāpam kaṟcāpam
pāṇantāṉ -
eṉṉum aḷavu pāṭiviṭa, maṟṟoruvarukku avar viṭṭa iṭantoṭaṅki maṟṟatip pūrtti ceyyat tōṉṟavilli.  itaṉi avarkaḷ ammaṇṭapac cuvaril eḻutivittu maṟṟum cila pala tiruppatikaḷ ceṉṟu vaṇaṅki mīṇṭum avvūrukku vantaṉar.  avarkaḷ vantapoḻutu veṇpā muṟṟuppeṟṟum, aṭiyil kāḷamēkam eṉṟu kiyeḻuttiṭṭiruppatiyum kaṇṭaṉar.  avarkaḷ elliyaṟṟa viyappeytiṉar.  muṟṟuppeṟṟa 	veṇpā,
	nāṇeṉṟāl nañcirukkum naṟcāpam kaṟcāpam
pāṇantāṉ maṇtiṉṟa pāṇamē-tāṇuvē
cīrā rūrmēvum civaṉē nī eppaṭiyō
nērār purameritta nēr.26
eṉṟu amintiruntatu.  maṇtiṉṟa pāṇam eṉṟu coṉṉa pulavarik kaṇṭu avaruṭaṉ aḷavaḷāva vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvattuṭaṉ aṅkuḷḷavarik kāḷamēkattiṉ iruppiṭatti viṉaviṉar.  avarkaḷ aṉṟutāṉ kāḷamēkam iyaṟki eytiṉār eṉṟum, avaratu uṭal cuṭukāṭṭil ventu koṇṭiruk kiṉṟatu eṉṟum aṟivittaṉar.  atikkēṭṭa iraṭṭiyar cuṭukāṭṭiṟku virintu ceṉṟaṉar; appoḻutu tāṉ kāḷamēkattiṉ vāy ventuk koṇṭiruntatu.  uṭaṉē avarkaḷiṉ cōka uṇarcci mēṟkaṇṭa ācukaviyāl eṉṟa pāṭalāka vaṭiveṭuttatu.
inta mukavurikkup piṉ pāṭali iciyuṭaṉ paṭittāl māṇākkarkaḷ pāṭṭiluḷḷa avalac cuviyi aṟivar; maṇtiṉṟa pāṇam eṉṟa toṭariṉ ciṟappiyum uṇarvar; atil aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa uṇarcciyiṉ koṭumuṭi avarkaṭkut taṭṭuppaṭum.
iṉṉumōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭu.
	eṉṟu varuvāṉ emaṉeṉ ṟetirnōkki
niṉṟu taḷarkiṉṟēṉ nittamumē-maṉṟil
naṭaṅkaṇṭa īcaṉ naṭarācaṉ pātat
tiṭaṅkaṇṭu vi nī emakku.27
ituvum oru taṉippāṭaltāṉ; kiyaṟu nilic ceyyuḷ tāṉ. paṇṭitamaṇi katirēcaṉār iṟanta ceytikēṭṭu kavimaṇi tēcikavināyakam piḷḷi pāṭiyatu.  paṇṭitamaṇi pala nāṭkaḷ vātanōyāl tuṉpuṟṟu 24-10-53 aṉṟu piṟpakalil tamatu 72-vatu vayatil iyaṟki eytiṉār. ataṉ piṟaku cumār ōrāṇṭu kālattil tamatu 78-vatu vayatil iyaṟki yeytiya28 kavimaṇi paṇṭitamaṇi iṟanta camayam `ātmā nōyālum ciṟu ciraṅku nōyālum pīṭikkappaṭṭiruntār. potuvākac civacamayap paṟṟum ciṟappākat tillic kūttaṉpāl īṭupāṭuṅ koṇṭa paṇṭitamaṇiyiṉ pirivik kaṇṭu avaratu keḻutaki naṇparum maraṇatti ovvoru nimiṭamum etir nōkkik koṇṭu iruntavarumāṉa kavimaṇi mēṟkaṇṭa pāṭalip pāṭiṉār.  kavimaṇiyum niṟinta iṟiyaṉpu koṇṭavar.  inta mukavurikkup piṉ mēṟkaṇṭa pāṭali iciyuṭaṉ niṟuttip palamuṟi paṭittāl-illi pāṭiṉāl-māṇākkarkaḷ,
eṉṟu varuvāṉ emaṉeṉ ṟetirnōkki
niṉṟu taḷarkiṉṟēṉ nittamumē
eṉṟa aṭikaḷiṉ pāvatti, atil potintuḷḷa nōyt tuṉpatti, naṉku aṟiyamuṭiyum; cettār oruvari nōkkic cāvār oruvar kāṭṭum itayat tuṭippi, uṇarccip perukki, uṇaramuṭiyum.  ivvāṟu pala eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ taralām.  kāviyap pakutikaḷiliruntum pala eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷik kāṭṭalām.
(4) viṉāmuṟi: itip pērāciriyar hāṭō (Haddow) tokuttaṟi muṟi (Synthetic method) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar.  immuṟiyil kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyē viṉāmārikaḷippoḻintu kavitikaḷi māṇākkar-kaḷiṭamiruntu varuvikka muyalvar.  perumpulami paṭitta kampaṉ pōṉṟa mētikaḷ eṅkē?  moḻiyaṟivu, poruḷaṟivu, kaṟkaṉiyāṟṟal mutaliyavi vāykkappeṟāta iḷam māṇākkarkaḷeṅkē?  perum pulavarkaḷiṉ kavitiyi iḷañciṟārkaḷiṭamiruntu varuvikka iyalumā?  aḵtu avarkaḷiṭam irukkattāṉ muṭiyumā? allatu, kaṟpikkum āciriyariṭantāṉ irukka muṭiyumā? avar viṭukkum viṉāk kaḷilum kaviti irukka muṭiyātu; avaṟṟiṟku māṇākkar kūṟum viṭikaḷil āciriyarāl tērnteṭukkap peṟṟavikaḷilum irukka muṭiyātu.  oru cila eḷiya coṟkaḷ perukiya-āṉāl poruḷāḻam illāta-ceyyuṭkaḷi vēṇṭumāṉāl immuṟiyil kaṟpikkalām.  alli aracāṇimāli, pavaḷak koṭi māli, nallataṅkāḷ kati, pulantiraṉ kaḷavu pōṉṟa nūlkaḷiluḷḷa pakutikaḷukku immuṟi poruntakkūṭum.  im muṟiyik kiyāḷum āciriyar mikat tiṟamiyāḷarāka iruttal vēṇṭum; viṉākkaḷi mikattiṟaṉuṭaṉ ākkit tokukka vēṇṭum; immuṟiyil kaṟpikkappeṟum pāṭam pārppataṟkum kēṭpataṟkum kavarccikaramākat tōṉṟum.  cila camayam āciriyar immuṟiyik kiyāḷuvatil tavaṟu illi; āṉāl atik kaviti kaṟpittal eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅkak kūṭātu; avvaḷavu tāṉ! mukavuriyē tēviyirāta pakutikaḷi immātiri kaṟpikkalām.  āṉāl kaṟpikkum pakutikaḷukku ati mukavuriyākat tāṉ koḷḷavēṇṭum! ivvāṟu cila viṉākkaḷi viṭuttu viṭikaḷi varuvittālum, māṇākkarkaḷ kavitiyi aṟintu koṇṭatākak karutamuṭiyātu.  kaviti eṉpatu varivaṭivu maṭṭumaṉṟu; aḵtu oli vaṭivāyaminta toru collōviyam.  māṇākkar ati iciyuṭaṉ paṭikkak kēṭkātavari, kēṭṭu ataṉ iciyiṉpattilum aḻakuṇarcciyilum tiḷikkātavari, avarkaḷ kavitiyik kāṇavilli eṉṟutāṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu palavēṟu viṉākkaḷi viṭuttu māṇākkaḷiṭa miruntu āciriyar kavitiyi varuvikka eṭukkum muyaṟci āciriyaruṭiya tiṟaṉik kāṭṭuvatāka muṭiyumēyaṉṟi collōviyatti māṇākkar kaṭkuk kāṭṭuvatāka irātu.  kaliyuruvām kavitiyi māṇākkarkaḷ kāṇāta vari, avarkaḷ evvāṟu kavitiyiṉ murukuṇarcciyil īṭupaṭamuṭiyum?  atil tiḷikka muṭiyum?
ōr aḻakiya ōviyattiluḷḷa pala kōṭukaḷiyum taṉit taṉiyāka oruvar kaṇṭu makiḻalām; avi yāvum ōviyattiluḷḷa kōṭukaḷē yaṉṟi ōviyam ākātu.  ovvoru kōṭṭiṉ aḻakum aḵtu ōviyattic ciṟanta kaliyākkum tiṟaṉip poṟuttuḷḷatu; kōṭṭiṟkeṉat taṉi aḻaku oṉṟum illi.  nam ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu ettaṉiyō ciṟanta karuttukkaḷi, nītikaḷi, uṇarttak kūṭiya taṉic coṟ ṟoṭarkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭalām.  kāṭṭi eṉṉa payaṉ?  avaṟṟi avi kūṟum cantarppaṅkaḷiliruntu piritteṭuttu viṭṭāl, avaṟṟiṉ aḻakum kulintuviṭum; uṇarcciyum cappiṭṭup pōkum; ac coṟṟoṭarkaḷ kavitiyil cērntirukkum poḻutu tāṉ aḻakuṭaṉ polivuṟum.
		kallāp
pullarkku nallōr coṉṉa
	poruḷeṉap pōyiṟ ṟaṉṟē29
eṭuttatu kaṇṭaṉar; iṟṟatu kēṭṭār30
eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kamparāmāyaṇac cuviñarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi eṭuttuc colli makiḻvar; avaṟṟik kavitiyiṉ koṭumuṭikaḷ eṉṟum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar.  kaviti kūṟum cantarppaṅkaḷi aṟiyātavarkaḷ avaṟṟic cuvikka muṭiyumā?  ac cantarppaṅkaḷiṉṟi avaṟṟiṟkup poruḷtāṉ uṇṭā?  ittakiya eṇṇaṟṟa toṭarkaḷi tamiḻ ilakkiyak karuvūlattiliruntu ettaṉi vēṇṭu māṉālum eṭuttukkāṭṭalām.
eṉavē, māṇākkaḷiṭam murukuṇarcciyi vaḷarkka vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avarkaḷiṭam kaviñaṉ paṭitta kavitiyik kāṭṭavēṇṭum; accu vaṭivattiluḷḷa coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kāṭṭiṉāl maṭṭilum pōtātu.  āciriyar kavitiyic cuvittiruntāl, ati iciyūṭṭi māṇākkarkaḷiṉ uḷattiṟkuk koṇṭuceluttamuṭiyum.  kavitiyi māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarac ceyyavēṇṭum; aṟiyac ceyvatāl payaṉ illi- iti moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ eṉṟum niṉivilirutta vēṇṭum.
kavitiyi viḷakkum muṟi:  ‘kavitikku evvāṟu viḷakkan taruvatu?   eṉṟa viṉā kaṟpikkum paṇiyil pukum niliyiluḷḷa iḷam āciriyarkaḷiṭiyē eḻutal iyaṟki.  inta nūlāciriyarip poṟuttavari kavitikku viḷakkam taruvataṟku evvitat tiṭṭamāṉa muṟiyum illi.  pērāciriyar hāṭōviṉ karuttuppaṭi kaviti kaṟpittal kātal puritali ottatu.  kātal purivatil muṟiyoṉṟu irukka muṭiyumā? eṉṟa viṉāviṟku eṉṉa viṭiyō atittāṉ kavitikku viḷakkan taruvataṟkum vittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  viḷakkan taruvatil ovvoruvarum tattamakkut tōṉṟukiṉṟa muṟiyil tāṉ viḷakkan tarutal vēṇṭum.  eṉṟālum, oru cila kuṟippukkaḷi īṇṭu eṭuttuk kāṭṭalām.  āṉāl, kuṟippukkaḷik kiyāḷum poḻutu, kaṟpikkum vakuppu, kaṟpikkavēṇṭiya pāṭal ākiyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟavāṟum, kaṟpikkum āciriyariṉ aṟivunili, maṉappāṉmik kēṟṟavāṟum māṟupaṭattāṉ ceyyum.
kavitiyi ataṟku ēṟṟa iciyuṭaṉ coṟkaḷip pirikkāmal cīrpirittup paṭittukkāṭṭa vēṇṭum.  pāṭṭiṉ potuk karutti māṇākkarkaḷ uṇarumvari palamuṟi āciriyar paṭittukkāṭṭalām.  māṇākkarkaḷ pāṭaliṉ karutti uyttuṇarvatuṭaṉ kaviti iṉpattiyum colnayam poruḷ nayaṅkaḷiyum tuykkavēṇṭum eṉpatiyum āciriyar maṉattilirutta vēṇṭum.  ekkāraṇattik koṇṭum kavitiyip paṟṟit tamatu karutti māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉattil valintu tiṇittal kūṭātu.  kavitiyippaṟṟi avarkaḷ cintiyil oru karuttu uruvāvataṟku vēṇṭiya kālam tarutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  ovvoru māṇākkaṉum tattam maṉappāṉmik kēṟṟavāṟu kavitiyip paṟṟi oru karutti uruvākkik koḷḷum urimiyi āciriyar paṟittal kūṭātu.  āciriyar puriyum tuṇiyellām māṇākkarkaḷ koṇṭa karutti vaḷarppataṟkum. uṟutip paṭuttuvataṟkum tuṇiyāka irukkavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi atic citikkavō māṟṟavō tuṇiyāka iruttal kūṭātu.  itik kaviti kaṟpikkum mukkiya nōkkamākak koḷḷa vēṇṭum āciriyarkaḷ.  kavitikaḷit tuykkum payiṟci māṇākkarkaṭku ēṟpaṭṭu viṭṭāl iraṇṭāvatu taṭavip paṭikkumpoḻutē kavitiyi ōraḷavu tam uḷḷattil amittuk koṇṭuviṭuvar.  aṭuttu oṉṟiraṇṭu taṭavi paṭikkumpoḻutu pāṭṭiṉ uyirnilik karuttukkup pōy ati eṭuttuk kāṭṭavēṇṭum.  ak karutti, kāṭciyi allatu kati nikaḻcciyi namakku uṇarttavē appāṭal kaviñaṉāl pāṭap peṟṟatu eṉpati avarkaḷ aṟiyac ceytal vēṇṭum.
pāṭali allatu pāṭalkaḷi oru muḻup poruḷākak karutavēṇṭum.  pāṭalkaḷip pala vivaraṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyavi eṉṟu karuti coṟcētam ceytu collukkuc col poruḷ kūṟiyum.  pāṭalkaḷilvarum aṇikaḷukkum ilakkaṇak kuṟippukkaḷukkum taṉittaṉi viḷakkam kūṟiyum pāṭalkaḷi viḷakkutal tavaṟu.  mutalil col collākap pirittu uri colluvatu, urinaṭiyākkuvatu, uvamip poruḷ kēṭpatu mutaliyavaṟṟik kiyāṇṭu paḻiya muṟiyiṟ kaṟpittal kavitic cuviyikkeṭuttu kaviñaṉiṉ uṇarcciyi aṟiyat tuṇi ceyvatilli eṉṟu ikkālattavar ammuṟiyik kuṟi kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  pāṭalkaḷip paṭittu avaṟṟiṉ uyirnilik karuttiyum, uṇarcci niliyiyum, aḻakiyum aṟinta piṟaku avaṟṟi mīṇṭum naṉku uṇarvataṟkut tuṇipuriyumaḷaviṟku viḷaṅkāta cila aruñcoṟkaḷukkup poruḷ kūṟalām; cila vivarap pakutikaḷi viḷakkalām.  iṟutiyāka oru muṟi pāṭalkaḷi iṉṉōciyuṭaṉ pāṭi māṇākkarkaḷik kavitic cuviyiṉ koṭumuṭikku aḻittuccellavēṇṭum.  cilappatikāram, maṇimēkali, kampa rāmāyaṇam, pāratam pōṉṟa nīṇṭa katip pakutiyāka iruntāl atik karuttu muṭivu koṇṭa ciṟuciṟu pakutikaḷākap pirittukkoṇṭu kaṟpittal vēṇṭum.
pāṭalkaḷukkuc coṟporuḷ, poḻippuri, karutturi ilakkaṇak kuṟippukkaḷ, toṭinayam, aṇinayam mutaliyavaṟṟi naṉku eṭuttukkūṟi viḷakkuvatāl māṇākkarkaḷ pāṭalkaḷiṉ poruḷi naṉku uḷḷattil amippar; tērvilum uyarnta matippeṇkaḷ peṟuvar.  āṉāl, pāṭalkaḷiṉ poruḷi maṭṭilum aṟital orucār nōkkamēyākum.  itiviṭap pāṭalkaḷiṉ nayattiyum aḻakiyum uṇarntu cuvittu makiḻtalē ciṟanta nōkkamākum.  kavitikaḷik kaviñaṉ enta uṇarcciyuṭaṉ pāṭiṉāṉō avvuṇarcci kavitikaḷip kaṟpōriṭattum uṇṭāka vēṇṭum; appoḻututāṉ avarkaḷ kaviti iṉpattil tiḷikkamuṭiyum.  ïj‰F MáÇa® v‹d brŒant©L«?1 kaviñaṉiṉ uṇarcciyi mutalil tām peṟṟu, piṟaku māṇākkarkaṭku vaḻaṅka vēṇṭum.  coṟcuviyum poruṭcuviyum toṭi nayamum aṇinayamum iṭattiṟkēṟṟa canta iṉimiyum koṇṭu viḷaṅkum pāṭalkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷit tiḷikkavittalē tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ mutaṟkaṭamiyum mutalāya kaṭamiyumākum.  kavitikaḷiṉ kaviṉi, uṇarcciyi, aṟiyacceyyātu pāṭal kaḷip payiṟṟuvatāl payaṉoṉṟu milli.  toṭakka niliyil kaṟṟa pāṭalkaḷiyē uyar niliyilum kaṟpikka vēṇṭiya muṟiyil kaṟpittāl kaṭṭāyam putiya poruḷkaḷ tulaṅkum; māṇākkarkaḷ cuvippatilum kuṟivu irātu.1  kavitikaḷik kaṟpippateppaṭi eṉpati oru cila eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷik koṇṭu viḷakkuvōm.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu-1
1.	viruntiṉarum vaṟiyavarum neruṅki yuṇṇa
	mēṉmēlum mukamalarum mēlōr pōlap
paruntiṉamum kaḻukiṉamum tāmē yuṇṇap
	patumamuka malarntārip pārmiṉ! pārmiṉ!!
2.	cāyntuviḻum kaṭakaḷiṟṟi ṉuṭiyē cāyntu
		taṭaṅkuruti micippaṭiyum koṭikaḷ taṅkaḷ
kāntaruṭaṉ kaṉalamaḷi yataṉmēl vikuṅ
		kaṟpuṭimā tariyottal kāṇmiṉ!kāṇmiṉ!!
3.	tarimakaḷtaṉ koḻunaṉṟaṉ uṭalan taṉṉit
			tāṅkāmal taṉṉuṭalāl tāṅki viṇṇāṭ(ṭu)
aramakaḷir avvuyirip puṇarā muṉṉam
			āviokka viṭuvāḷik kāṇmiṉ!kāṇmiṉ!!
4.	paṭavūṉṟu neṭuṅkuntam mārpi ṉiṉṟu
			paṟittaṉi nilattūṉṟit tērmēl niṟpār
paṭavūṉṟi viṭuntoḻilōr eṉṉa muṉṉam
	pacuṅkuruti nīrttōṉṟum paricu kāṇmiṉ!31ivi nāṉku pāṭalkaḷum pōrkaḷak kāṭcikaḷi eṭutturippaṉa.  kaliṅkappēyiṉ vēṇṭukōḷiṉpaṭi kāḷitēvi pēykaḷ cūḻak kaliṅkanāṭṭup pōrkaḷattik kāṇac cellukiṉṟāḷ.  aṅkuk kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷit taṉṉuṭaṉ vanta pēy kaḷukkuc cuṭṭikkāṭṭukiṉṟāḷ.  avaṟṟuḷ nāṉku kāṭcikaḷi ippāṭalkaḷ kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa - eṉṟa ciṟu mukavuriyuṭaṉ pāṭalkaḷi cila taṭavikaḷ āciriyar iciyūṭṭip paṭikkavēṇṭum.32  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ mukak kuṟippāl pōtum eṉṟu aṟintavuṭaṉ paṭippati niṟuttip pāṭalkaḷiṉ uyirnilik karuttukkup pōkavēṇṭum; māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉakkaṇmuṉ pāṭalkaḷ cittirikkum kāṭcikaḷik koṇṭu niṟuttavēṇṭum.  itil āciriyarum māṇākkarum ottuḻittāltāṉ perum payaṉ viḷiyum; viṉāviṭi muṟi kiyāḷappaṭavēṇṭum.  kaviti kaṟpikkum nōkkatti maṟantu viṉākkaḷ tērvu muṟiyilirukkak kūṭātu.  pāṭalkaḷ cittirikkum kāṭcikaḷ -cūḻnili-vakuppaṟiyil nilavumāṟu āciriyariṉ kural, collum muṟi mutaliyavi aṉittum amiya vēṇṭum.  ivi cariyāka amintāl māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyaruṭaṉ naṇparkaḷ pōl ottuḻittuk kaviñaṉ kāṭṭum kāṭcikaḷi mīṭṭum tam maṉakkaṇṇāl kāṇa muṉivar.
mutaṟpāṭal: mutalil kāḷitēvi pēykkūṭṭaṅkaḷukku evaṟṟik kāṭṭukiṉṟāḷ? paruntiṉattiyum kaḻukiṉattiyum, avi eṉṉa ceytu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa? vīrarkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷi uṇṭu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  vīrarkaḷiṉ mukaṅkaḷ eppaṭik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa?  mukamalarcciyuṭaṉ.  ivviṭattil āciriyar vīrarkaḷ vīrattuṭaṉ pōrpurintu, kaḷippuṭaṉ uyir tuṟantamiyāl, avarkaḷ muka malarcciyuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiṉar eṉpatik kūṟalām.  mēlōr eṉpavar yāvar?  illaṟattil uyarntōr.  avarkaḷ mukam malarvataṟkuk kāraṇam?  viruntiṉarum vaṟiyavarum neruṅki yuṇpatāl.  iyalpāka iṟantukiṭakkum vīrarkaḷiṉ mukamalarccikkut tam uṭalaṅkaḷ paruntukaḷukkum kaḻukaḷukkum viruntākap payaṉpaṭuvatāltāṉ eṉṟu kaviñaṉ paṭittuk kāṭṭum kaṟpaṉiyi āciriyar eṭuttu iyampalām.
iraṇṭām pāṭal:  iraṇṭāvatākak kāḷi kāṭṭum kāṭci yātu?  matayāṉikaḷ iṟantu kiṭakkum nili.  anta yāṉikaḷuṭaṉ vēṟu evi kiṭantaṉa?  aracarkkuriya koṭikaḷ.  koṭikaḷum yāṉikaḷum etil tōyntu kiṭantaṉa?  kuruti veḷḷattil.  ikkāṭciyāl kaviñaṉ niṉivupaṭuttum vēṟu kāṭci yātu?
kāntaruṭaṉ kaṉalamaṉi yataṉmēl vikum
kaṟpuṭimā tariyottal.
kāntar, kaṉal amaḷi, kaṟpuṭi mātar-ivarkaḷi niṉivukkuk koṇarntavi yāvi?  kaṭakaḷiṟu, taṅkuruti, koṭikaḷ.  ivviṭattil kaviñaṉ koṭikaḷik koṭi pōṉṟa peṇkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭum nayattiṉic cuṭṭalām.  māṇākkar aṟivu niliyiyum cuvi yuṇarum āṟṟaliyum oṭṭi,
	poṭiyuṭik kāṉa meṅkum
	kurutinīr poṅka vīḻnta
taṭiyuṭi yeyiṟṟup pēḻvāyt
	tāṭaki talikaḷ tōṟum
muṭiyuṭi yarakkaṟ kannāḷ
	muntiyuṟ pāta mākap
paṭiyiṭi yaṟṟu vīḻnta
	veṟṟiyam patāki yottāḷ.33
eṉṟa kampaṉ pāṭali eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, ak kaviñaṉum iṟantu vīḻnta tāṭakiyi veṟṟik koṭikku uvamiyākak kūṟiyiruttali viḷakkalām.
mūṉṟām pāṭal: mūṉṟāvatākak kāḷitēvi eṉṉa kāṭciyik kāṭṭukiṉṟāḷ?  talivi yorutti taṉ kaṇavaṉ uṭalit tāṅkiya vaṇṇam uyir tuṟakkum niliyi.  talivi taṉ kaṇavaṉ uṭalit tāṅkiya vaṇṇam iṟappataṟkuk kaviñar paṭittuk kāṭṭum iraṇṭu kāraṇaṅkaḷ yāvi?  oṉṟu, pūmitēvi taṉ kaṇavaṉ uṭalit tīṇṭakkūṭātu eṉpatu; maṟṟoṉṟu, viṇṇāṭṭu makaḷir taṉ kaṇavaṉ uyirip puṇarakkūṭātu eṉpatu.  itiliruntu eṉṉa aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭum?  tamiḻ nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ kaṟpu mikkavarkaḷ eṉpatum, tam kaṇavar piṟa peṇṭiri nayappatip poṟār eṉpatum.
nāṉkām pāṭal:  vīrarkaḷiṉ mārpil eṉṉa pāyntu niṟkiṉṟatu?  nīṇṭa vēlāyutam.  av vīrarkaḷ eṉṉa ceykiṉṟaṉar?  anta vēlip piṭuṅki ati nilattil ūṉṟikkoṇṭu tērmēl tāṇṭik Fâ¡»‹wd®-Polevalut ceyvatu pōla.  ivvāṟu kutikkum kāṭci kaviñarukku niṉivūṭṭum maṟṟoru kāṭciyātu?  paṭakōṭṭi tuṭuppik koṇṭu paṭaku valikkum kāṭci.  evi akkāṭciyi niṉivu paṭuttat tūṇṭiṉa?  vīrarkaḷ paṭakōṭṭikaḷiyum, tērkaḷ paṭakukaḷiyum, vēlkaḷ tuṭuppukkaḷiyum, pacuṅkuruti yāṟṟu nīriyum niṉivu paṭuttip paṭaku valikkum kāṭciyi niṉivupaṭuttiṉa.
ivvāṟu māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ ottuḻittuk kāṭcikaḷi vakuppaṟiyil nalla muṟiyil tirumpap paṭitta piṟaku, pāṭalkaḷi āciriyar mīṭṭum oru muṟi iciyuṭaṉ pāṭi māṇākkarkaḷic cuviyiṉ koṭumuṭikkuk koṇṭu celutta vēṇṭum.  iḵtu oru muṟiyē yaṉṟi, itutāṉ muṭivāṉa muṟi yeṉṟu collamuṭiyātu.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vayatu, avarkaḷ vāḻum cūḻnili (nakarppuṟam, nāṭṭuppuṟam), aṟivunili ākiyavaṟṟi yoṭṭi immuṟiyic ciṟitu māṟṟiyamittuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.
eṭuttukkāṭṭu- 2
1. 	cūṭukiṉṟa tuḻāymuṭiyōṉ curaruṉē
	muṉivarkaḷum curuti nāṉkum
tēṭukiṉṟa patañcivappat tirunāṭu
	peṟattūtu cella vēṇṭā;
	vāṭukiṉṟa maṭappāvi taṉvaramum
	eṉvaramum vaḻuvā vaṇṇaṅ
kōṭukiṉṟa moḻiyavaṉpāl eṉittūtu
	viṭukiṉik koṟṟa vēntē.
2.	malikaṇṭa teṉaveṉki maṟattaṇṭiṉ
	valikaṇṭum, makavāṉ mintaṉ
cilikaṇṭum, iruvarporun tiṟalkaṇṭum,
	emakkākat tirumāl niṉṟa
	nilikaṇṭum, ivaḷviritta kuḻalkaṇṭum,
	imippoḻutil nērār tammik
kolikaṇṭum, makiḻāmal avaṉkuṭikkīḻ
	uyirvāḻak kuṟikkiṉṟāyē.34
ivi iraṇṭu pāṭalkaḷum kati nikaḻcciyi eṭutturippaṉa,  kiruṭṭiṉaṉit tūtaṉuppa vēṇṭumeṉṟa ēṟpāṭu naṭi peṟukiṉṟatu.  tarumaṉ kiruṭṭiṉaṉit tūtu ceṉṟu vara vēṇṭumeṉa vēṇṭukiṉṟāṉ.  vīmaṉ iṉic camātāṉam kūṭātu eṉṟu ciṉantukūṟa, kaṇṇaṉ avaṉ ciṉattit taṇivikkiṉṟāṉ; tarumaṉ camātāṉam kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.  inniliyil vīmaṉ pēcukiṉṟāṉ.  mēṟkāṭṭiya pāṭalkaḷ iraṇṭum vīmaṉ kūṟṟukkaḷāka varupavi.
itik kati nikaḻcciyil kavitikaḷ varum iṭam cuṭṭiyapiṟaku kavitikaḷi ici yēṟṟik paṭikka vēṇṭum.35  māṇākkarkaḷ pāṭalkaḷiṉ uyir nilikkarutti ōraḷavu uḷattil koḷḷumvari avaṟṟit tirumpat tirumpap pāṭavēṇṭum:  āciriyar taṉṉi vīmaṉākak karutip pāṭavēṇṭum.  appaṭittāṉē kaviñaṉ uṇarntu pāṭiyiruppāṉ?
