naṭiyum payiṟcikaḷum:  urinaṭip pakutikaḷil mēṟ koḷḷap peṟum coṟṟoṭar amippu muṟi, naṭi ākiyavi vakuppu nilikkēṟṟavāṟu māṟumpaṭi amiya vēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum naṭi, iṉimi, eḷimi, tūymi mutaliya paṇpukaḷik koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  vakuppukkut takuntavāṟu naṭi paṭippaṭiyāka uyarntu ceṉṟāl niṟinta payaṉi viḷi vikkum.  ilakkaṇa amitikaḷum, coṟṟoṭarkaḷum, maraput toṭarkaḷum vakuppu uyara uyara paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarntu pōtal nalam.  urinaṭiyil cantaṅkaḷ, mōṉikaḷ, col vittikaḷ pōṉṟa muṟikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷutalit tavirttal vēṇṭum.  vaḻakkaṟṟa coṟkaḷ, paḻiya vaḻakkāṟukaḷ (Usages) pōṉṟavaṟṟi urinaṭiyil pukutti naṭiyiṉ virivāṉa pōkkik keṭuttal kūṭātu.  pāṭaṅkaḷiṉ kīḻttarappeṟum moḻippayiṟcikaḷ pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷi yoṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum1.  māṇākkarkaḷ karuttuṇarntu tam vākkilēyē vittu vaḻaṅkakkūṭiya aruñ coṟkaḷ mutaliyavi moḻip payiṟcikaḷāka amitalvēṇṭum.  avi arumi nōkkip paṭippaṭiyāy oḻuṅku ceyyappeṟalvēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, eḻuttup payiṟcikaḷ ilakkiyac cuviyit tūṇṭak kūṭiyaṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  ovvoru pāṭattiṉ īṟṟilum moḻip payiṟcikaḷum, aruñcol viḷakkaṅkaḷum, piṟa viḷakkaṅkaḷum curukkamāka iṭampeṟac celtal vēṇṭum.  urinaṭiyil kiyāḷappeṟum cila aruñ coṟkaḷ iṭam nōkkip poruḷuṇarat-takkaṉavāyiruttal vēṇṭum.  appaṭiyiruntāl ap paḻakkam avarkaḷi akaṉṟapaṭippiṉpāl īṭupaṭat tuṇi ceyyum.  oru putiya colli oru pāṭattil palvēṟu muṟikaḷil kiyāṇṭāl, māṇākkarkaḷ ataṉ poruḷi eḷitilaṟintu  tāmum atip pala iṭaṅkaḷil payaṉpaṭuttum payiṟciyiṉip peṟuvar.  ittakiya coṟkaḷi ovvoru pāṭattiṉ īṟṟilum tokuttuk kāṭṭavēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, cila ilakkaṇa vaḻakkukaḷ, cantikaḷ, vēṟu ilakkaṇa muṟikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟik kaṟpikkum orucila moḻip payiṟcikaḷum, vāykkuṭ paṭippataṟkāṉa payiṟcikaḷum, vīṭṭu-vēlip pakutikaḷum ippakutiyil iṭampeṟutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
camayam, oḻukkam mutaliyavipaṟṟiya pakutikaḷ:  camayam oḻukkam mutaliyavipaṟṟit tērnteṭukkappeṟum pakutikaḷ camayap piṇakki uṇṭu paṇṇāmalum, pala camayattiṉarukku uriyaviyāyum allatu ottaṉavāyum iruntāl ellōrum puttakaṅkaḷi virumpi ēṟpar.  tirukkuṟaḷ, nālaṭiyār pōṉṟa aṟanūlkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷ ellā camayattiṉarukkum potu vāṉavi.  tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tivyappirapantam, tiruvaruṭpā, tiruvicippā ākiya nūlkaḷum; tāyayumāṉavar, paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ, vīramāmuṉivar, arikiruṣṇapiḷḷi, ve. pa. cuppiramaṇiya mutaliyār, matāṉ cākipu ākiyōriṉ pāṭalkaḷum ippakutikku uriyavi.  tēmpāvaṇi, cīṟāppurāṇam, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mutaliyaviyum ippakutikku ēṟṟavi.  vētanāyakam piḷḷiya varkaḷiṉ camaracak kīrttaṉikaḷiyum tārāḷamāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷalām.  makkaḷil eppakutiyiṉariyāvatu puṇpaṭuttum karuttukkaḷ nūlkaḷil iṭampeṟalākātu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka matuvilakkip paṟṟiya koḷkikaḷ ellōrum oppuk koḷḷattakkavi.  tēnīr, kāḵpi mutaliyaṉa kuṭittal kaḷkuṭi pōṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟuvatit tavirttal vēṇṭum.  maṟṟum tirunāvukkaracar, tiruñāṉacampantar pōṉṟa camayap periyārkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ piṟa camayattiṉarkaḷiṉ maṉam puṇpaṭāta muṟiyil eḻutappeṟal vēṇṭum.
piṟa ceytikaḷ:  pāṭap poruḷkaḷil evvita piḻikaḷukkum iṭantaralākātu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, iyaṟki eytiya periyārkaḷ iṉṟum ivvulakil vāḻvatākak kūṟutalit tavirttal vēṇṭum; patippukkup patippu itik kavaṉittal avaciyam.  aruvikaḷiṉ (Water falls) āḻam, malikaḷiṉ uyaram, natikaḷiṉ nīḷam, periyārkaḷ vāḻnta kālam, kittoḻilkaḷ, toḻil tuṟikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟippaṟṟiya takavalkaḷil piḻi nēriṭāmal pārttuk koḷḷalvēṇṭum.  tamiḻ nūlkaḷil nakiccuviyiṉik kāṇpatu kutirikkompāka uḷḷatu;  oṉṟiraṇṭu attipūtta mātiri kāṇappeṟiṉum, avi mēlnāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷil Humour eṉṟu kūṟappeṟum.  tarattil illiyeṉṟutāṉ collavēṇṭum.  āṉāl, ikkālattil tōṉṟiyuḷḷa maṟumalarcci ilakkiyaṅkaḷil attakiya nakiccuvip paṇpu talikāṭṭat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.  kalvi, nāṭōṭi, e. vi. É., putumippittaṉ pōṉṟār eḻutiyuḷḷavaṟṟilum, ulakamcuṟṟiya tamiḻaṉ ē. kē. ceṭṭiyār eḻutiyuḷḷa pirayāṇap puttakaṅkaḷilum mēṉāṭṭu Humour uṭaṉ oppiṭattakka cila nakiccuvip pakutikaḷik kāṇalām.  avaṟṟip pāṭa nūlkaḷil cērkkalām.  nakiccuvi poruntiya katikaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ, nāṭōṭip pāṭalkaḷ, taṉippāṭalkaḷ, cilēṭic ceyyuṭkaḷ, cīṭṭukkavikaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip pāṭanūlkaḷil mikutiyākac cērkka vēṇṭum.
iṉṟu veḷiyiṭappeṟum pāṭapputtakaṅkaḷ yāvumē maṉaniṟivu peṟakkūṭiya muṟiyil illi.  ellōrumē tamakku ellāt takutiyum iruppatāka eṇṇikkoṇṭu pāṭanūlkaḷi eḻutat tuṇikiṉṟaṉar; māṇākkariṉ nilik kēṟṟavāṟu nūl eḻutuvatu eḷitāṉa ceyalaṉṟu.  pāṭa nūlkaḷiṉ uṟpattiyil kāṇum ūḻalkaḷaṉittiyum uyar nilikkalvi aṟikki eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṟatu pārāṭṭat takkatu.2  nalla nūlkaḷi uṟpatti ceyvatil ivvaṟikki kūṟum yōcaṉikaḷ aṉittum naṭimuṟiyil mēṟ koḷḷat takkavi.  aṟikki kuṟippiṭum kuḻuvoṉṟu uruvāki mēṟpaṭi yōcaṉikaḷ ceyalpaṭṭāl, nalla muṟiyil pāṭa nūlkaḷ tōṉṟum eṉpataṟku aiyamilli.
2. tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷ
āḻnta paṭippit  tavira, virivākavum vāykkuḷḷum paṭikkavēṇṭiya piṟitoru paṭippum uṇṭu.  iti akaṉṟa paṭippu eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar.  itaṉ iṉṟiyamiyā iyalpukaḷ virivāka vāykkuṭ paṭittalum, karuttuṇartalumākum.  akaṉṟa paṭippil mūṉṟu nōkkaṅkaḷ niṟivēṟukiṉṟaṉa.  mutalāvatu: aṟivu nōkkam.  iti veṭ eṉṟa aṟiñar mika aḻakākak kūṟukiṉṟār.3 avar nūliṉ itayattit taṉiyāka eṭuttu viṭutal eṉpatē aṟivu nōkkam eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟār.  m~jhtJ, Éiuthf üÈ‹ âu©l fU¤ij¥ ãÊªbjL¤jny mj‹ bghUshF«.;  virivākap paṭikkumpoḻutē karuttuṇartal, cintaṉik karuttukkaḷip pōṟṟutal, uyir nilik karutti uṭaṉaṟintukoḷḷutal ākiya tiṟaṉkaḷāl innōkkam niṟivēṟum.  iraṇṭāvatu:  ilakkiya nōkkam, ulaka aṟiviyum potu aṟiviyum vaḷarttup paṭippil oru paṟṟi uṇṭākkit tāmākavē paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa āvaliyum paḻakkattiyum tōṟṟuvippatē ilakkiya nōkkamākum.  mūṉṟāvatu: moḻi nōkkam.  moḻiyaṟivi vaḷarttalum coṟkaḷañciyattip perukkutalum moḻi nōkkattiṉ pāṟpaṭum.  āḻntapaṭippil arumiyākak kāṇum putiya coṟkaḷiyum coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum tam vākkil vittu vaḻaṅkum payiṟciyiṉip peṟutal pōlavē, akaṉṟa paṭippil kaṇṇuṟum putiya coṟkaḷ coṟṟoṭarkaḷiṉ āṭcikaḷit teḷivāka aṟintukoḷḷum vāyppiṉippeṟutal vēṇṭum.  iviyum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ coṟkaḷañciyattuṭaṉ cērntu avarkaḷiṉ karuttuṇarum āṟṟali vaḷarkkum.  vāykkuṭ paṭippil peṟum naṉmikaḷiyum nōkkaṅkaḷiyum akaṉṟa paṭippiṟkuriyaṉavāka iṇittuk koḷḷalām.4  akaṉṟa paṭippiṉ nōkkaṅkaḷi niṟivēṟṟum vaḻikaḷil tuṇip pāṭanūṟ paṭippum oṉṟākum.  eṉavē, tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷip paṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu kuṟippiṭuvōm.
nalla tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷ:  pāṭa nūlkaḷiṉ naṟpaṇpukaḷākak kuṟippiṭṭa aṉittiyum appaṭiyē tuṇippāṭa nūlkaṭkum koḷḷalām.  puṟattōṟṟam, akat tōṟṟam, poruḷamippu,  nūlāciriyarkaḷ, urinaṭip pakutikaḷ, naṭi, camayam oḻukkampaṟṟiya kuṟippukaḷ, piṟaceytikaḷ ākiya talippukkaḷil āṇṭuk kūṟiyaṉavaṟṟil poruntuvaṉavaṟṟi yellām īṇṭu appaṭiyē iṇittuk koḷḷalām.  avaṟṟit tavira tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷ vēṟu cila kūṟukaḷiyum koṇṭiruttal iṉṟiyamiyātatākum.
tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ aṟiyāta coṟkaḷ, coṟṟoṭarkaḷ, maraput toṭarkaḷ, vēṟu moḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟik kiyāḷutal kūṭātu.  karuttuṇarvatil iviyum naṭiyum taṭiyāka iruttalkūṭātu.  māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyariṉ tuṇi ciṟitumiṉṟi, vēṇṭumāṉāl akarātit tuṇiyuṭaṉ, nūlkaḷit tāmākavē paṭittu makiḻa vēṇṭum; paṭittu muṭittōm eṉṟa veṟṟiyuṇarcci eḷitākak kivarappeṟutal vēṇṭum.
ittakiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyil atikamākat tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl, perumpālum vakuppukku vikkappeṟum nūlkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivu niliyiṉi oṭṭiyaṉavāka illātiruppatu mikavum varunta;tatakkatu.  pāṭa nūṟ kuḻuviṉarāl ēṟkappeṟṟa nūlkaḷuḷḷum oru vakuppuk kuriyaṉavākavuḷḷa nūlkaḷ pala kūṟukaḷil orē mātiriyāka iruppatilli.  inta nūlāciriyar aintām paṭivattilum, ep. ē. vakuppilum, pulavar vakuppilum orē nūlip paṭikkum vāyppiṉip peṟṟār eṉṟāl, nūlkaḷit tērnteṭuppōr evvaḷavu kavaṉak kuṟivāka iruntirukka vēṇṭum, allatu collakkūṭāta kāraṇaṅkaḷukkuk kaṭṭuppaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
nūlkaḷ palvēṟu tuṟikaḷippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷit taruvaṉavākavum iruttalvēṇṭum.  pāṭa nūlkaḷik kuṟippiṭum poḻutu 	urinaṭip pakutikaḷ eṉṟa talippil kūṟap peṟṟa pala karuttukkaḷiyum tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷukkum ēṟṟaviyākak koḷḷalām.  tamiḻil pirayāṇa nūlkaḷ kuṟivāka uḷḷaṉa.  ulakam cuṟṟum tamiḻaṉ, citpavānanta aṭikaḷ, ti. cu. avināciliṅkam, ṭākṭar mu. varatarācaṉār, cōma. ilakkumaṇaṉ ceṭṭiyār (cōmale) eḻutiyuḷḷavi pōl ivikaḷ cuviyāka eḻutappeṟṟāl māṇākkarkaḷ avaṟṟik kati nūlkaḷip pōlavē virumpip paṭippar.
tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷip payiṟṟum muṟi:  tuṇip pāṭa nulkaḷi āḻnta paṭippiṟkuriya pāṭa nūlippōl vakuppil kaṟpikkavēṇṭiya avaciyam illi.  appaṭik kaṟpittālum, poḻutup pōkkākak kaṟpittāl pōtum.  pāṭanūlik kaṟpikkumpoḻutu mēṟkoḷḷappeṟum aruñcol viḷakkam, coṟṟoṭar maraputtoṭar viḷakkaṅkaḷ, avaṟṟic conta vākkiyaṅkaḷil vittup payiṟṟuvikkum paṉmuṟip payiṟci, ariya collāṭci ilakkaṇakkuṟiyīṭu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa muṟikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatilli.
tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷi māṇākkarkaḷē vīṭṭil paṭittu varavēṇṭum; tuṇippāṭap paṭippukkeṉa pāṭa vēḷi otukkappeṟṟiruntāl atil avarkaḷ annūlkaḷip paṭikkalām.  tēviyāṉāl appāṭa vēḷiyil āciriyar nūliluḷḷa katiyi allatu poruḷippaṟṟip paranta nōkkāka ārāyalām; kati naṭakkum kālam, iṭam, cūḻnili, kati māntarkaḷ, tuṇikkuṟippukkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi muṉṉuriyāka eṭuttukkūṟi annūlip paṭippatil māṇākkarkaḷiṭiyē ārvatti eḻuppiviṭalām.  nulip paṭikkat toṭaṅkumuṉ cintaṉiyik kavarakkūṭiya cila viṉākkaḷit tantu avaṟṟiṟku viṭi kaṇṭu varumāṟu ēvalām.  av viṉākkaḷ ivvāṟu amiyalām; katip pōkku, kati māntarkaḷiṉ kuṇa nalaṉkaḷ, nikaḻccik kuṟippu, varuṇaṉi mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟiya ciṟu kuṟippukkaḷ mutaliyavi eḻutivarumāṟu ēvalām; allatu ciṟu ciṟu viṉākkaḷ tantu avaṟṟiṟku viṭi kāṇumāṟu tūṇṭalām.  putiya muṟi viṉākkaḷiṉ mūlamum avarkaḷuṭiya paṭippaṟivi aḷantu kāṇalām.
vīṭṭil māṇākkarkaḷ annūlkaḷip paṭittuvanta piṟaku mēṟkaṇṭa viṉākkaḷi viṭuttu viṭi kūṟumāṟu ēvalām.  iv viṉākkaḷ yāvum māṇakkarkaḷ karuttuṇarntu paṭittu varukiṉṟaṉarā eṉpati aṟivataṟkut tuṇi ceypavi.  tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷil paṭittaṟintavaṟṟi orucila talippuk kaḷil kaṭṭurikaḷāka varivatilum payiṟcikaḷ taralām.  kaṭṭuri varivataṟkeṉak kūṟappeṟṟa vitikaḷip piṉpaṟṟik kaṭṭuri eḻututal naṭipeṟal vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuri tiruttum muṟikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟi āciriyar tiruttavum vēṇṭum.
3. potuppaṭippukkuriya nūlkaḷ
nūlakap paṭippukkākap pāṭanūṟ kuḻuviṉarāl ēṟkap peṟṟa pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷip potup paṭippukkāṉa nūlkaḷākak karutalām.  perumpālum avikaḷum tuṇip pāṭa nūlkaḷippōlavē eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  iṉṟu patippāḷarkaḷ nūliṉ puṟattōṟṟattil kavaṉam celutti mēṟ caṭṭiyuṭaṉ nūlkaḷip patippikkiṉṟaṉarēyaṉṟi, nūlkaḷiṉ akattōṟṟattil nalla muṟiyil kavaṉam celuttavilli.  akattōṟṟattil kavaṉam celutti orucila āciriyarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟṟa nūlkaḷip patippāḷarkaḷ ātarippatilli; avi accuppoṟi ēṟum pēṟṟip peṟāmal kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
inta nūṟṟāṇṭil paḻiya muṟiyilum putiya muṟiyilum pala urinaṭi nūlkaḷ veḷivantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.  ilakkiya ārāycci nūlkaḷ, kāla ārāycci nūlkaḷ, palvēṟu poruḷkaḷippaṟṟiya urinaṭi nūlkaḷ, ilakkaṇa ārāycci nūlkaḷ ākiyavi paḻiya muṟiyil veḷivantavi.  putiya muṟiyil pala maṟumalarcci ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiya vakikaḷi yoṭṭi veḷivantuḷḷaṉa; nāṭōṟum veḷivantu koṇṭumirukkiṉṟaṉa.  paḻiya muṟiyil veḷivantavaṟṟip pulami mikkavarkaḷ maṭṭiluntāṉ paṭittu iṉpuṟa muṭiyum.  putiyamuṟiyil veḷivantuḷḷavaṟṟip ellōrum paṭittuc cuvikkalām.  putiya muṟiyil veḷivarupavi iraṇṭu vakikaḷil vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa.  mutal vaki nūlkaḷil ciṟukatic celvaṅkaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ, tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ pōṉṟa mēṉāṭṭu urinaṭi ilakkiya vakikaḷic cērnta vaṟṟik kuṟippiṭalām.  ivaṟṟāl tamiḻ urinaṭic celvam vaḷarkiṉṟatu eṉṟu colla vēṇṭum.  iraṇṭāvatu vakiyil ceytittāḷkaḷ, vāra, piṟi, tiṅkaḷ, āṇṭu itaḻkaḷik kuṟippiṭalām.  ceytittāḷ āciriyarkaḷ, pēcuvatupōlavē eḻutavēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattāl eḻutukiṉṟaṉar.  mutalvaki ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kaṟṟavarkaḷiṭiyēyum, iraṇṭāvatu vaki ellōriṭamum celvākkup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  pāmaramakkaḷum ciṟuvar ciṟumikaḷum iraṇṭāvatu vaki ilakkiyaṅkaḷip peritum virumpukiṉṟaṉar.  inta iruvaki ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ēṟkappeṟṟālum peṟāviṭṭālum paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷ avaṟṟip paṭittu maṉappaciyit taṇittuk koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷāka makkaḷ etirnōkkiyirunta vēkamāṉa naṭi ciṟuvarkaḷiṉ itayaṅkaḷik kuvi niṟkiṉṟatu; vaḷarntavar kaḷum an naṭiyil viruppaṅ koḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  anta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ cintikkuriya peruviruntāka uḷḷaṉa.  pāṭanūlkaḷ, tuṇippāṭa nūlkaḷ, nūlakap paṭippiṟkuriya nūlkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟi virumpāta māṇākkarkaḷum innūlkaḷip peru viruppuṭaṉ paṭittu iṉpa muṟukiṉṟaṉar.
maṟumalarcci ilakkiyaṅkaḷil mukkiyamāṉavi putiṉaṅkaḷ, ciṟukatikaḷ, kaṭṭurikaḷ, nāṭakaṅkaḷ, tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ ākiyaviyākum.  ivi ovvoṉṟiyumpaṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
putiṉaṅkaḷ: putiṉam mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiya vakikaḷil oṉṟu.  taṉṉuṭiya ōviyattit tāṉē pārttu makiḻum iyalpu maṉitaṉukku uṇṭu.  taṉ iyaṟki, tāṉ vāḻum vāḻkki ivikaḷiṉ vēṟupāṭukaḷi maṉitaṉ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kaṇṭu tuykka virumpukiṉṟāṉ.  eṉavē, makkaḷ maṉattil tōṉṟum uṇarccikaḷ pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyaviyē ip putiṉaṅkaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa; avi tōṉṟum nilikkaḷaṉkaḷākavum uḷḷaṉa.  putiṉam oru periya kati.  oruvarukkoruvar toṭarpuḷḷa pala kati māntarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil oru perum pakutiyi muḻu vivaraṅkaḷuṭaṉ tarum ōviyam itu.  putiṉattiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta cila uṟuppukkaḷ uḷa. katiyamippu (Plot), kati māntariṉ paṭippu (Characterisation), uriyāṭal (Dialogue), kālam (Time) iṭam (Place of action), vāḻkkit tattuvam (Pilosophy of life) ākiyaviyē ivvuṟuppukkaḷākum.  ivi aṉittum oru putiṉattil orē aḷavākavō, aṉittumō irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟa variyaṟi illi.  avi aṉittum irukkalām; ovvoṉṟum mikkum kuṟintum varalām.  ivaṟṟiṉ virivi mēṉāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷil virivākak kāṇalām.  haṭcaṉ eḻutiya ilakkiya ārāycci nūlil icceytikaḷik kaṇṭukoḷka.5
tamiḻnāṭṭu vāḻkkiyippaṟṟiya ciṟanta putiṉaṅkaḷ iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavilli.  āṉāl, orucila eḻuttāḷarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻikku nalla putiṉaṅkaḷit tantirukkiṉṟaṉar.  vaṅkāḷam, kucarātti, marātti, inti ākiya moḻikaḷiliruntu pala putiṉaṅkaḷ tamiḻil moḻi peyarkkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  mūla nūlkaḷ yāvum nalla ilakkiyap paṇpukaḷuṭaṉum, cuviyuṭaṉum, kati vaḷattuṭaṉum ilaṅkuvatip pōlavē moḻipeyarppu nūlkaḷum nalla muṟiyil ākkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
mēl nāṭṭil putiṉaṅkaḷ oru ciṟanta ilakkiya nilikku uyarntiruppatupōla nam nāṭṭil uyaravilli; kāraṇam, avarkaḷ moḻiyil karuttic celuttiyiruppatupōla nammavarkaḷ celuttavilli.  tāmahārṭi, kēṉaṉṭāyil, ec. ji. vel pōṉṟa putiṉa āciriyarkaḷ tām eḻutum poḻutu vaḻukkaḷaṟṟa naṭiyi mēṟkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.  ākavē, avi palkalik kaḻaka māṇākkarkaḷukkup pāṭamāka amiyum perumiyiyum peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  nam nāṭṭup putiṉa āciriyarkaḷum tām eḻutum moḻiyil karuttic celuttip palavita vaḻukkaḷi nīkki eḻutiṉāl aviyum ciṟanta nilikku uyarum.  nalla muṟiyil aminta oru cila putiṉaṅkaḷ (e-ṭu. ṭākṭar mu. va. eḻutiyavi) tamiḻil illāmal illi.  avaṟṟit tērnteṭuttu māṇākkarkaḷip paṭikkumpaṭiyāka āciriyar tūṇṭalām.
ciṟu katikaḷ:  ciṟu kati oru taṉippaṭṭa ilakkiya vaki.  ciṟu katikaḷ ellām ōviyaṅkaḷ.  avikaḷip paṭippataṟkuk kalittiṟaṉ vēṇṭum.  avaṟṟip paṭippōriṉ uṇarcci uccanilikkup pōkavum kūṭum; cilacamayam avi kavitip pāṉmikkum uyaralām.  appaṭi uyarvatu mēkattiṉ naṭuvil miṉveṭṭut tōṉṟuvatu pōlirukkum.  nīṇṭa varuṇaṉikaḷ, periya kati māntar mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟu katiyil iṭam illi.  aṭakkattuṭaṉ amikkap peṟṟa oru cila coṟṟoṭarkaḷ uṇarcciyiṉ aṉalikkāṭṭum.
āṅkila eḻuttāḷarāṉa rāparṭ lūyi ṭīveṉcaṉ eṉpār ciṟu kati eḻuttāḷarkaḷukku oru yōcaṉi kūṟukiṉṟār:  eṉakkut terintavariyilum ciṟu kati eḻutuvataṟku mūṉṟu vaḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  oru nikaḻcciyit tērnteṭuttu ataṟkuriya kati māntarkaḷip paṭikkalām; allatu oru kati māntarit tērnteṭuttu ataṉmītu nikaḻccikaḷip poruttalām; allatu oru cūḻniliyit tērnteṭuttu ataṟkup poruttamāṉa nikaḻccikaḷiyum kati māntarkaḷiyum tērntu amikkalām.  ivar kūṟum yōcaṉi putiya ciṟu kati eḻuttāḷarkaḷukku oru ciṟanta vaḻikāṭṭiyāka irukkum eṉpataṟku aiyamilli.
tamiḻnāṭṭil ciṟu kati vaḷarccikku mutaṉ mutalil vaḻikāṭṭiyavar kālañceṉṟa va. ve. cu. ayyar avarkaḷ.  avarip piṉpaṟṟi ērāḷamāṉa ciṟukati eḻuttāḷarkaḷ tōṉṟi eṇṇaṟṟa ciṟukatic celvaṅkaḷit tamiḻukku aḷittuḷḷaṉar.  ivaṟṟuḷ pala taḻuvalkaḷākavum moḻi peyarppākavum piṟantavi.  putumippittaṉ, ku. pa. irāja kōpālaṉ pōṉṟa oru cila ciṟu kati eḻuttāḷarkaḷ cāvā ilakkiyattaṉmiyi peṟṟuḷḷa cila ciṟu katic celvaṅkaḷi iyaṟṟiyuḷḷaṉar.  putiṉak kaliyiviṭac ciṟu katik kali tamiḻil nalla vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷatu.
kaṭṭurikaḷ:  kaṭṭuri (Essay) eṉpatu oru poruḷip paṟṟip pala karuttukkaḷi icittuk kōvipaṭa variyum coṟperukku eṉpatākum.  maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ itik kōppuri eṉṟu kuṟippiṭuvar.  ituvum mēṉāṭṭu ilakkiya vakikaḷil oṉṟākum.  urinaṭi naṉṟāka vaḷarāta tamiḻ moḻiyil kaṭṭuri ilakkiyaṅkaḷ mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷil kāṇappaṭupavipōl avvaḷavu atikamāka illi.  iṉṟu tōṉṟiyuḷḷa oru cila kaṭṭurikaḷum ellōrum paṭikkum niliyil amiyavilli; aṟiñarkaḷum pulavarkaḷum paṭikkat takkavāṟutāṉ avi amintuḷḷaṉa.
āṅkila moḻiyil evarum paṭittuc cuvikkakkūṭiya muṟiyil ērāḷamāṉa kaṭṭuri ilakkiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.  palvēṟu poruḷpaṟṟiya kaṭṭurikaḷi aṅku ērāḷamākak kāṇalām.  āṅkilak kalvi peṟṟa oru cila tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷ āṅkila marapi yoṭṭi oru cila kaṭṭurikaḷi varintuḷḷaṉar.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka makiḻnaṉ6 eḻutiyuḷḷa pala kaṭṭurikaḷik kuṟippiṭalām.  kaṭalum kaṭaliyum arañceyya virumpu yatārttavāti lōka virōti maṅki paṅkaṉ vaṇṇārap periyāṇṭi pōṉṟa kaṭṭurikaḷ paṭikkap paṭikka iṉpañ curappavi; paṭippavar vāyil tēṉūṟṟit tarum pāṉmi yuṭiyavi; ilakkiya maṇamum nakiccuviyum tatumpi niṟpavi.  ‘fš»’, vÞ.É.É., ‘ehnlho’, t.uh., â.ja.u., ».t.jafªehj‹ pōṉṟa maṟumalarcci eḻuttāḷarkaḷum pala nalla kaṭṭurikaḷit tōṟṟuvittuḷḷaṉar.  avarkaḷ aṭipaṟṟi iṉṟu ettaṉiyō kaṭṭurikaḷ veḷivantavaṇṇa mirukkiṉṟaṉa.
nāṭakaṅkaḷ:  oru katiyi eṭuttukkoṇṭu atil varum kati māntarkaṭkellām naṭikarkaḷi amittu uriyāṭal kaḷālum ceyalkaḷālum akkatip poruḷip palar kāṇa araṅkattil naṭittukkāṭṭuvati nāṭakam eṉṟu ikkālattil nām kūṟukiṉṟōm.  muttamiḻil oṉṟākiya nāṭakat tamiḻ paṟṟiya paṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṟu kāṇappeṟavilli; avi eppaṭiyō aḻintupaṭṭaṉa.  nāṭakam eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar puṉinturivaki eṉṟu poruḷ virittaṉar.7  iḷampūraṇarum ip poruḷi ṉiyē piṟitoru vakiyāl kūṟiṉar.  āṅkilattil paṭittaṟkum naṭittaṟkum uriya pala nāṭaka nūlkaḷ iruppatu pōl tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavilli.  iṉṟu maṟumalarcci eḻuttāḷarkaḷ eḻutivarum nāṭakaṅkaḷ nilittu niṟkum taṉmi vāyntavi aṉṟu.
iṭikkālattil kōvilkaḷ. nāṭakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟu vataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntaṉa.  mutal irājirājaṉ kālattil irāja rājēcuvara nāṭakam eṉṟa peyarāl āṇṭutōṟum oru nāṭakam naṭikkappeṟṟu vantatākak kalveṭṭu oṉṟāl aṟikiṉṟōm.8  ittakiya oru cila ceytikaḷāl ki. pi. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil tamiḻ nāṭakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.  mēṟ kūṟiyavāṟu cila nāṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉum ivvaki nūlkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil ceḻittu vaḷaravilli.  pāṭṭiyal nūlkaḷilum uḻattippāṭṭu (paḷḷu), kuṟattippāṭṭu (kuṟavañci), noṇṭi nāṭakam eṉṟa mūṉṟu tavira vēṟu nāṭaka nūlkaḷ kāṇappaṭavilli.  ivi ki. pi. 17-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyavi.
pattoṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil mēṉāṭṭuk kalvi nāṭṭil paravat toṭaṅkiyatu.  āṅkilam kaṟṟa oru cilar vaṭamoḻi nāṭaka ilakkaṇaṅkaḷiyum muṟikaḷiyum piṉpaṟṟi nāṭakaṅkaḷi iyaṟṟiṉar.  ivarkaḷil tali ciṟantavar paritimāṟ kaliñar eṉṟa vi. kō. cūriyanārāyaṇa cātiriyār āvār.  avar iyaṟṟiya māṉa vijayam uyarnta centamiḻ naṭiyil amintuḷḷatu.  vēṟu cilar vaṭamoḻi nāṭakaṅkaḷit taḻuvuvatil viruppamiṉṟi ṣēkpiyar, rāsīṉ, mōliyar mutaliya mēṉāṭṭu nāṭaka āciriyarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟi nāṭakam iyaṟṟiṉar.  avarkaḷuḷ pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷi taliciṟantavar.  avar iyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇīyam nāṭakaṅkaḷil taṉicciṟappuṭiyatāka eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavak kūṭiyatu.
paḻantamiḻ ilakkiya nāṭaṅkaḷ illi eṉṟālum, iṉṟiyat tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil nāṭakaṅkaḷ eṉṟu karutat takkavi cila uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟuḷ ilakkiya maṇam evvaḷavu uḷḷatu eṉpati ārāyntutāṉ muṭivu kaṭṭavēṇṭum.  kāla veḷḷatti etirttu niṟkum āṟṟaliyuṭiyavi ettaṉi eṉpatu kālap pōkkiltāṉ teriyavarum.  taṟkālattu nāṭakaṅkaḷ illāta kuṟiyi nīkkiyavar tiru.  pammal campanta mutaliyār.  avar tamiḻil iyaṟṟiya nāṭakaṅkaḷil perum pālāṉavi eḷiya urinaṭiyil uḷḷaṉa.  avar naṭikarākavum uḷḷamiyāl nāṭakaṅkaḷit tiṟaṉāyntuṇarum āṟṟal peṟṟavarākavum tikaḻkiṉṟār.  iṉṟu maṟumalarcci eḻuttāḷarkaḷ pala nāṭakaṅkaḷi eḻuti varukiṉṟaṉar.
mēṉāṭṭut tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷ oru nalla nāṭakattil toṭakkam, kuḻappam, uccanili, vīḻcci, muṭivu eṉṟa nilikaḷ iruppatākac kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  iṅṅaṉam aintu pirivukaḷāka oru nāṭakam amiyavēṇṭum eṉṟa nipantaṉi illi.  tiṟaṉāyvu ceyvataṟkākavē ivvāṟu pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭatu.  ciṟanta nāṭakaṅkaḷil muraṇ eṅku toṭaṅkukiṉṟatō aṅkutāṉ cūḻcciyum toṭaṅkum.  muraṇ muṭiyum iṭattil cūḻcciyum muṭintuviṭum.  ivviraṇṭu miyaṅkaḷiṉ iṭiyiltāṉ katiyiṉ ciṟanta pakuti amintirukkum.  pōrāṭṭattiṉ toṭakkam, naṭappu, muṭivu eṉpaṉa itaṉuḷ amintuviṭum.  etirmāṟāṉa karuttukkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu porumpoḻutu kuḻappam ēṟpaṭukiṉṟatu.  ik kuḻappam vaḷarntukoṇṭē ceṉṟu ōr uccaniliyi aṭikiṉṟatu.  anniliyiṉ piṉṉar muṭivu veḷippaṭat toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu.  immuṭivu naṉmikkuc cātakamākavō aṉṟit tīmikkuc cātakamākavō irukkalām.  uccanili ēṟpaṭṭa piṟaku nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷ aṉittum varappōkum muṭivukku araṇ ceyvaṉavākavē irukkum.  ēṟattāḻa oru paṭampōṭṭu viḷakkakkūṭiya muṟiyil ivi amiya vēṇṭum.
mēṟkūṟiya kūṟukaḷ yāvum niṟinta nāṭaka nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyil mikavum arukiyē kāṇappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl, aṉṟāṭam veḷivarum paruvaveḷiyīṭukaḷil kāṇap peṟum orucila nalla nāṭakaṅkaḷip paṭikkumāṟum, aṭikkaṭi vāṉoli niliyaṅkaḷiliruntu oli parappappeṟum orucila uyarnta nāṭakaṅkaḷik kēṭkumpaṭiyum māṇākkarkaḷit tūṇṭalām.
tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ:  tamiḻ ilakkiyattukku ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvut tuṟi putitu.  eṉavē, tamiḻmoḻiyil tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷē illi eṉṟu tuṇivākak kūṟi viṭalām.  ilakki yattiṉ aṭippaṭiyāṉa uṇmikaḷi ārāyntu atu maṉitaṉukku iṉpam koṭukkum irakaciyatti aṟivatu uṇmiyāṉa ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvākum.  kamparāmāyaṇa racaṉi eṉṟa talippil va.  ve.  cu. aiyarāl eḻutappeṟṟu muṟṟuppeṟāta kaṭṭuriyē tamiḻmoḻiyil mutalil tōṉṟiya tiṟaṉāyvu nūlākak karutalām.  atil avar kamparāmāyaṇattiṉ kāviya amippippaṟṟi nuṇṇiya aṟivuṭaṉum, cuvit taṉmiyuṭaṉum viḷakkiyuḷḷār.  kampaṉi vāṉmīki, hōmar mutaliya peruṅkaviñarkaḷuṭaṉ oppiṭṭu pala kūṟukaḷil kampaṉ avarkaḷi veṉṟuḷḷāṉ eṉṟu kāṭṭum kalittiṟaṉ pōṟṟuvataṟkuriyatu.  avar ceytuḷḷa ārāycci putumiyākavum taṟkāla muṟikaḷit taḻuviyum uḷḷatu.
ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu eṉpatu oru ilakkiyattiṉ uṇmiyāṉa kuṇaṅkaḷi ārāyntu muṭivu kāṇutalumākum.  eṉavē, ilakkiyattiṉ ciṟanta paṇpukaḷ evi eṉpatiyum, nūlil nuvalappeṟum poruḷ, amippu, collum muṟi, nūlāciriyaṉatu kaṟpaṉi āṟṟal, uṇarccikaḷ, itayat tuṭippukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, avi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷa toṭarpukaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum aṟitalumē uṇmiyāṉa ilakkiyat tiṟaṉāyvu ākum.  iṉṟu tamiḻil matippurikaḷ eṉṟa talippil veḷiyākum tiṟaṉāyvukaḷ ellām viruppu veṟuppukkaḷukku uṭpaṭṭavi alla eṉṟu colvataṟkilli.  it tiṟaṉāyvukaḷik koṇṭu nāṭōṟum palkivarum tamiḻ nūlkaḷil nallaṉavaṟṟit tērnteṭukka muṭiyum eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.  tiṟaṉāyvāḷaṉ nūli matippiṭuṅkāl taṉatu viruppu veṟuppukkaḷiyum contak karuttukkaḷiyum kaṭci vēṟṟumikaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum koṇṭu avaṟṟi nūlāciriyaṉiṉ karuttukkaḷuṭaṉum koḷkikaḷuṭaṉum oppiṭṭō maṟuttō nūlkaḷi matippiṭutal kūṭātu.
mēl nāṭukaḷil Literary Criticism eṉṟa peyarāl vaḻaṅkap peṟum nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavi oṉṟiraṇṭu ippoḻututāṉ tamiḻ moḻil tōṉṟat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.  tamiḻiluḷḷa ovvoru ilakkiyattiṟkum ovvoru tiṟaṉāyvu nūlāvatu uyariya muṟiyil virivil tōṉṟavēṇṭum.  ṣēkpiyar nāṭakaṅkaḷukku pirāṭli eḻutiyuḷḷavi pōṉṟa tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ tamiḻil pala tōṉṟavēṇṭum.  āṅkilam kaṟṟa tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ ittuṟiyil ākkavēli ceyya muṉ varutal vēṇṭum.
4. takaval nūlkaḷ
vaḷarntavarkaḷ paṭippataṟkum kuḻantikaḷ paṭippataṟkum ēṟṟa takaval nūlkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavilli; intiya moḻikaḷilēyē iṉṉum atikam tōṉṟavilli eṉṟu kūṭak kūṟalām.
piṟanāṭṭu nallaṟiñar cāttiraṅkaḷ
	tamiḻmoḻiyil peyarttal vēṇṭum;
iṟavāta pukaḻuṭiya putu nūlkaḷ
	tamiḻmoḻiyil iyaṟṟal vēṇṭum.
eṉṟu pāratiyārum mēṉāṭṭu moḻikaḷiliruntu pala nūlkaḷi moḻi peyarkkavēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.  oru cila nalla nūlkaḷ ippoḻututāṉ tōṉṟak toṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa.  paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷukkeṉa avarkaḷiṉ paruva nilikkēṟṟavāṟu putika iyal, uyiriyal, tāvara iyal, vētiyiyal, vāṉiyal, nilaviyal, niluṭkūṟṟiyal, uṭaliyal pōṉṟa tuṟikaḷil Basic Science series, Wonder book series eṉṟa toṭarkaḷil iṉiya, eḷiya, teḷivāṉa āṅkila moḻiyil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa nūlkaḷip
pōl tamiḻ moḻiyil pala nūlkaḷ tōṉṟutal vēṇṭum.  iṉṉum āṅkila moḻiyil veḷiyākiyuḷḷa palvēṟu takaval nūlkaḷippōl tamiḻ moḻiyilum veḷivarutal vēṇṭum.  kalik kaḷañciyam eṉṟa talippil tamiḻil veḷivantuḷḷa nūl arumiyāṉa takavalnūl; uyariya muṟiyil mēṟkōḷ nūlākat tuṇiceyvatu.  tamiḻ vaḷarccik kaḻakam ceytuḷḷa iccīriya paṇi tamiḻ makkaḷāl eṉṟeṉṟum pārāṭṭutaṟkuriyatu.
iṉṟu tamiḻ makkaḷiṭam takavalkaḷit tarum nūlkaḷākap paḻakkattiliruppavi pañcāṅkam, pukivaṇṭik kāla aṭṭavaṇi, orucila tamiḻ akarātikaḷ, tamiḻ leksikaṉ, apitāṉa cintāmaṇi ākiyaviyē.  akarātikaḷum tēvikkēṟṟavāṟu iṉṉum atikamākat tōṉṟavilli.  tāymoḻiyil varalāṟu, camūkam, tarinūl, kaṇitam mutaliya pāṭaṅkaḷip payiṉṟu uyarnilik kalviyi muṭittukkoṇṭa māṇākkarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷi viṭṭu veḷiyēṟiyatum mēlum at tuṟikaḷil aṟivi vaḷarttukkoḷḷa virumpiṉāl tāymoḻiyil attuṟikaḷil takka nūlkaḷ illi.  kiṭikkum oṉṟiraṇṭu nūlkaḷum nalla naṭiyil uṟcākam ūṭṭum muṟiyil eḻutap peṟavilli.  kalvi muṭintatum tamiḻil eḻutappeṟṟa it tuṟi nūlkaḷi māṇākkarkaḷ viruppattuṭaṉ pārppatē illi.  pala tuṟikaḷil evarum virumpip paṭikkum muṟiyil nalla naṭiyil ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷ iṉit tōṉṟutal vēṇṭum.  tāymoḻiyil nalla takaval nūlkaḷ illi eṉpati uyarnilik kalvi aṟikkiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu.9  aṟiñarkaḷ at tuṟikaḷil ākka vēlikaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum; patippāḷarkaḷ avikaḷi aḻakiya muṟiyil acciṭa muṉ varavēṇṭum.  necavut toḻil, maravēli, ulōka vēli, piṉṉal vēli, tōṭṭa vēli pōṉṟa viruppac ceyalkaḷ (Hobbies) paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil iṉṉum tōṉṟa villi.  vārtā kalvit tiṭṭam ōriraṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷil ceyaṟ paṭṭiruppatālum, aṭippaṭik kalvittiṭṭam naṭimuṟikku vantuviṭṭatālum, kuṭicit toḻil vaḷarcciyippaṟṟip palar akkaṟi koḷvatālum tēṉī vaḷarttal, kākitam ceytal, cōppu ceytal pōṉṟa toḻilkaḷippaṟṟiya oṉṟiraṇṭu nūlkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyil veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.  ivi eḷiyamuṟiyil eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ip puttakaṅkaḷi toṭakkam mutal iṟutivari paṭikka vēṇṭiyatilli.  tēvik kēṟṟavāṟu cantarppam nēriṭumpoḻutu cila pakutikaḷip paṭittukkoṇṭāl pōtumāṉatu.
5. payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ
tāymoḻiyil pala payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa.  kaṇitam, camūka iyal, tari iyal pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷil pala payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ veḷivantiruppatik kāṇkiṉṟōm.  mēlnāṭukaḷil ippāṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa payiṟcikaḷ evvaḷavō muṉṉēṟṟam aṭintuḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl, nam nāṭṭil orucila āciriyarkaḷāl eḻutappeṟṟaviyē pala patippukaḷil veḷiyāki varukiṉṟaṉa; putitāka oruvarum ittuṟikaḷil eḻutavilli.  tamiḻppayiṟcikkeṉa ākpōrṭu yūṉivarciṭi pirasārāl putumuṟit tamiḻ appiyācaṅkaḷ eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ mūṉṟu, nāṉku, aintu, vakuppukaḷukku oru varici ēṭukaḷ veḷiyiṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ivaṟṟit tavira vēṟu payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ itukāṟum veḷivantatākat teriyavilli.  āṅkila moḻip payiṟcikkeṉa `cīṭ vork, `ṭek vork, piṟapayiṟcikaḷ eṉṟu veḷi vantuḷḷa moḻip payiṟcik kuṟippēṭukaḷ pōṉṟavi tamiḻmoḻiyil veḷi varutal vēṇṭum.  cila āṇṭukaḷāka kuḻantikaḷukkeṉa veḷivarum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷilum, piṟa veḷiyīṭukaḷil ciṟuvarkkeṉa otukkappeṟṟuḷḷa pakutiyilum viṭukatikaḷ pōṉṟa orucila moḻip payiṟcikaḷ veḷiyiṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avi poḻutupōkkukkākavum, mutalil pārppavarkaṭkuc ciṟitu taṭumāṟṟatti uṇṭupaṇvataṟkākavum utavupavi.  oru cila kuṟukkuc coṟpōṭṭikaḷ ittakiyavi.
itukāṟum ilakkaṇap pāṭanūlkaḷil kāṇappeṟum orucila payiṟcikaḷ moḻip payiṟcikaḷākak karutappeṟṟaṉa.  ilakkaṇa vitikaḷiyum nūṟpākkāḷiyum maṉappāṭam ceytavaṉālum orucila ilakkaṇap payiṟcikaḷic ceyva taṉālum moḻip payiṟci tāṉāka ēṟpaṭum eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa eṇṇam nilaviyatu.  āṉāl, ilakkaṇap payiṟci vēṟu, moḻip payiṟci vēṟu eṉpati mika aṇmiyil tāṉ kalviyāḷarkaḷ uṇarntaṉar.  poruḷaṟivuṭaṉ ceyyum payiṟcikaḷum, palvēṟu moḻi vaṭivaṅkaḷil ceyyum payiṟcikaḷum āṅkila moḻiyiluḷḷavipōl tamiḻilum ceyyap peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi uṇarntaṉar.10  iṉṉum ammuṟiyil pala nūlkaḷ veḷi varavilli.  paḷḷip pāṭat tiṭṭattiyoṭṭi veḷivantuḷḷa nūlkaḷit tavira uyartarak kaṭṭuri ilakkaṇam eṉṟa talippil oru nūl iraṇṭu pakutikaḷāka veḷivantuḷḷatu.11 atu reṉṉum māriṭṭiṉum (Wren and Martin) iṇintiyaṟṟiya āṅkila ilakkaṇa nūlit taḻuvi eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  itupōṉṟa pala nūl tamiḻ moḻiyil veḷivarutal vēṇṭum.  tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ it tuṟiyil āṟṟa vēṇṭiya paṇi mikkuḷḷatu.
pāṭanūlkaḷit tavira ēṉiya nūlkaḷ paḷḷiyil ōr oḻuṅkil cēmittu vikkap peṟṟirukkum iṭam paḷḷi nūlakamākum.  iṉṟu nāṭōṟum veḷivarum ceytit tāḷkaḷ, paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷ mutaliyaṉavaṟṟip paṭikka vacatiyāka amikkappeṟṟuḷḷa maṟṟōr iṭam nūlakattuṭaṉ iṇintuḷḷa paṭippakamākum.  paḷḷikaḷil ivi iraṇṭum evvāṟu ceyaṟpaṭukiṉṟatu? moḻip paṭippiṟku ivi evvāṟu tuṇipurikiṉṟaṉa? eṉpaṉavaṟṟip paṟṟiyum ciṟitu aṟintu koḷḷutalvēṇṭum.  eṉavē, inta iraṇṭu niliyaṅkaḷippaṟṟi ōraḷavu īṇṭu aṟintu koḷḷuvōm.
nūlakam:  nūlakattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi iṉṟu makkaḷ uṇarat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.  nūlakak kaḻakaṅkaḷ māvaṭṭaṅkaḷtōṟum niṟuvappeṟṟup palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil nūlakaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvittuḷḷaṉa.
   maṉitarelām aṉpuneṟi kāṇpa taṟkum
maṉōpāvam vāṉippōl viriva ṭintu
taṉimaṉita tattuvamām iruḷip pōkkic
cakamakkaḷ oṉṟeṉpa tuṇarva taṟkum,
iṉitiṉitāy eḻuntuyar eṇṇa mellām
ilakuvatu pulavartaru cuvaṭic cāli;
puṉitamuṟṟu makkaḷputu vāḻvu vēṇṭil
puttakacā livēṇṭum nāṭṭil yāṇṭum.12
eṉṟa kaviñaṉ kural emmaruṅkum kēṭkap peṟṟuc ceyaṟpaṭat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu.  uyarnilik kalvi aṟikkiyum nūlakattiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi paṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷatu.13  intac cūḻniliyil paḷḷikaḷil nūlakam nalla muṟiyil amintu māṇākkariṉ moḻiyaṟiviṉiyum poruḷaṟiviṉiyum vaḷarkkavēṇṭum.  avvappoḻutu putitāka veḷivarum vāra itaḻkaḷ, piṟi itaḻkaḷ, tiṅkaḷ itaḻkaḷ, āṇṭitaḻkaḷ, vēṟu ciṟappitaḻkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiyum, tāymoḻiyil palvēṟu tuṟikaḷil veḷivarum nūlkaḷiyum māṇākkarkaṭkuk kiṭṭumpaṭi ceytu ivaṟṟi vaḷarkkalām.
nūlakam-vakikaḷ:  cātāraṇamākap paḷḷi nūlakattil potu nūlakam, mēṟkōḷ nūlakam, vakuppu nūlakam eṉṟu muppirivukaḷ irukkum; ivvāṟu pirikkappaṭṭiruntāltāṉ cīriya muṟiyil nūlakam ellōrukkum payaṉpaṭum.  potu nūlakattil ellā vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷum, āciriyarkaḷum paṭikkakkūṭiya pala kalikaḷpaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ irukkum.  mēṟkōḷ nūlakattil potu akarātikaḷ, kaliccol akarātikaḷ, vāḻkki varalāṟṟuc curukka akarātikaḷ, tamiḻp pērakarāti, kalikkaḷañciyaṅkaḷ, apitāṉa cintāmaṇi, nikaṇṭukaḷ, caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, purāṇaṅkaḷ, ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ ākiyavi irukkum.  centamiḻc celvi, centamiḻ, tamiḻppoḻil, tamiḻar nēcaṉ, kalimakaḷ, amutacurapi, kalikkatir, aṇukkatir, tarumacakkaram mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaḻinta āṇṭut tokutikaḷ iruttal avaciyam.  ciṟuvarkku viruppattit tarum kaṇkavar ōviyaṅkaḷik koṇṭa aṟivu vaḷarkkum nūlkaḷum, pukivaṇṭi vaḻi kāṭṭikaḷ, pañcāṅkaṅkaḷ, vēṟu aṭṭavaṇi nūlkaḷ, āṇṭu nūlkaḷ (Year books) ākiyavaṟṟiṟkum iṅku iṭam aḷikkalām.  vakuppu nūlakattil ovvoru vakuppiṟkumuriya nūlkaḷip pirittut taṉittaṉiyāka vittalvēṇṭum.  annūlkaḷi māṇākkarkaḷ vāṅkavum tiruppik koṭukkavum ēṟṟa vacatikaḷ iruttalvēṇṭum.  ataṟkeṉa ovvoru vakuppiṟkum pērēṭukaḷ (Catalogues) vittu avaṟṟil koṭukkal vāṅkalkaḷit tētivāriyākap patintuk koḷḷalām.  mutal iraṇaṭu pirivukaḷum nūlakak kaṇkāṇiyāriṉ pārvi yilum, mūṉṟāvatu pirivu antanta vakuppu āciriyariṉ pārviyilum iruntāl ceyal eḷitākavum tiṟamiyākavum naṭipeṟum.  māṟṟiyamikkap peṟṟa uyarnilik kalvittiṭṭattil tāy moḻip pāṭat tiṭṭattil 1,2,3 paṭivaṅkaḷukku vārattiṟku oru pāṭavēḷiyum, 4,5,6 paṭivaṅkaḷukku iraṇṭu vāraṅkaḷukku oru pāṭavēḷiyum otukkappeṟṟiruppatāl vakuppu āciriyarkaḷ vakuppu nūlakattiṉ poṟuppi ēṟṟāltāṉ nūlakappaṭippu tiṟamiyāka naṭipeṟa iyalum.
ivviṭattil uyarnilik kalvi aṟikki nūlakattip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭum ceytikaḷi niṉivukūrtal mikavum poruttamāka irukkum.14  nūlakattiṟkuriya kaṭṭaṭam mika aḻakāṉatāka amitalvēṇṭum.  kāṟṟōṭṭamuḷḷatākavum pōtumāṉa veḷiccatti yuḷḷatākavum aminta iṭam ēṟṟatu.  nūlakattil pala periyārkaḷ, aṟiñarkaḷiṉ paṭaṅkaḷi vittal vēṇṭum; aṟiyil amarntu paṭippataṟ kēṟṟa irukki vacatikaḷ naṉṟāka amikkappeṟal vēṇṭum.  iraṇṭāvatāka vēṇṭappaṭupavi nalla nūlkaḷ, paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷ, ārāycci itaḻkaḷ mutaliyavi.  ivaṟṟit tērnteṭukkum poṟuppi oru ciṟiya kuḻu ēṟṟu naṭattiṉāl naṉṟu; kuḻuvil paṇiyāṟṟuvōr paṭippatil ārvamuḷḷa varkaḷākavum, nāḷitaḻkaḷil veḷivarum matippurikaḷik kavaṉikkak kūṭiyavarkaḷākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.  mūṉṟāvatāka, nūlkaḷi māṇākkar kikkuk kiṭṭumpaṭi ceyyum muṟi naṉku amitalvēṇṭum.  nūlaka muṟikaḷil naṉku tērccipeṟṟa oruvar pārviyil nūlakam amintu nūlakamuṟikaḷip paṟṟic ciṟitu payiṟcipeṟṟa āciriyarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavāl iti naṉṟāka amikkalām.
vakuppu nūlakattil irukkavēṇṭiya nūlkaḷ:  vakuppu nūlakaṅkaḷukku māṇākkarkaḷiṉ iyalpiyum cuviyiyum tiṟaṉiyum aṟintu avaṟṟiṟkēṟṟa, paṭippil ārvam ūṭṭavalla nūl vakikaḷit tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.  maṉattiṟkuk kavarccitarum nikaḻccikaḷum karutti īrkkum ceyalkaḷum niṟinta ciṟukati nūlkaḷ, putiṉaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tērn teṭukkalām.  vaḻiccelavukaḷ (Travels) paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, putiya nāṭukaḷik kaṇṭupiṭitta varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ cintikku viruntāka irukkum.  aṟiviyal tuṟikaḷil putiyatu puṉinta varalāṟṟik kūṟum kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavaravalla viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya nūlkaḷi māṇākkarkaḷ virumpuvar.  eḷiya iṉiya naṭiyil eḻutappeṟṟuḷḷa nam nāṭṭuk kāṭci kaḷiyum, ayalnāṭṭuk kāṭcikaḷiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum paṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, palporuḷpaṟṟiyaṉavum palvēṟu naṭikaḷ koṇṭuḷḷaṉavumāṉa nūlkaḷ, eḻiluṇar āṟṟali vaḷarkkum pala tiṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ, potu aṟiviyum palatuṟi aṟiviyum vaḷarkkum nūlkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi āciriyarkaḷ tērnteṭuttalvēṇṭum.  ovvoru nūlilum kuṟintatu aintu paṭikaḷāvatu irukkumāṟu nūlkaḷi vāṅkavēṇṭum.
nūlkaḷi vāṅkuvatil āciriyariṉ poṟuppu:  vakuppu nūlakattiṟku nūlkaḷit tērnteṭukkum āciriyarkaḷ cila kuṟippukkaḷi niṉivil vittukkoṇṭu nūlkaḷit tērnteṭukkavēṇṭum.  ovvoru vakuppukkum uriyaṉavākat tērnteṭukkum nūlkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivunili, maṉanili, poruḷaṟivu, maṉappāṉmi ākiyavaṟṟiṟkup porutta māṉavikaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ ārvattip piṇittu iḻuttuc cella valla katinūlkaḷ iṅku iṭampeṟal vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ virumpip paṭikkum nūlkaḷi vāṅkavēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, āciriyarkaḷ iṉṉiṉṉa-vaṟṟittāṉ paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟutti nūlkaḷi vāṅkutal tavaṟu.  kīḻvakuppukkaḷukkuc ciṟu ciṟu katikaḷik koṇṭa nūlkaḷum, mēl vakuppukkaḷukkuc ciṟu katic celvaṅkaḷum putiṉaṅkaḷum irukkalām.  piñcu maṉaṅkaḷik kulikkum nūlkaḷi aṟavē nīkkutal vēṇṭum.  kīḻvakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ virumpip paṭikkum nūlkaḷi vāṅkiṉālum, mēl vakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ kaṭṭāyam paṭikka vēṇṭum eṉṟu āciriyar karutum nūlkaḷi vāṅkalām.  kūṭiyavari oruvarālēyē eḻutappeṟṟa nūlkaḷāyiruppiṉ naṉṟu; palarāl eḻutappeṟṟirukkum pāṭa nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavi kūṭā.  akaṉṟa paṭippukkuriya innūlkaḷ iṉiya eḷiya tūya naṭiyiṉik koṇṭaṉavāyum, eḻuttup piḻikaḷum ēṉiya piḻikaḷumiṉṟi aḻakāṉa kaṇkavar viḷakkappaṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭiruppaṉavākavum iruttal naṉṟu.  vakuppukkēṟṟa periya eḻuttukkaḷil nalla tāḷil acciṭappeṟṟup puṟa, akat tōṟṟaṅkaḷik koṇṭu nalla kaṭṭaṭam aminta nūlkaḷāka iruttal avaciyam.  tāmākavē paṭittaṟintōm eṉṉum makiḻcciyi aḷikkattakkaṉavāy, māṇākkarkaḷukkup paṭippatil ārvam eḻuppakkūṭiya poruḷkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟuvaṉavāy iruntāl ciṟanta naṉmi uṇṭu.  āciriyarkaḷ ceytit tāḷkaḷil veḷivarum matippurikaḷip paṭittu oru taṉik kuṟippēṭṭil kuṟittu vittukkoḷḷavēṇṭum; āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil tēviyāṉa nūṟpāṭṭiyali āyattam ceyyumpoḻutu atil ivaṟṟic cērttalvēṇṭum.  iṉṟu aṟivu vaḷarccipaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, kalikaḷpaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ, aṟiviyal tuṟi nūlkaḷ mutaliyavi ettaṉiyō tāy moḻiyil veḷivantavaṇṇa mirukkiṉṟaṉa.  āciriyarkaḷ avaṟṟip paṭittup paricīlaṉi ceytu ēṟṟavaṟṟi vāṅka vēṇṭiya paṭṭiyalil cērkkavēṇṭum.
paṭippil ārvatti ūṭṭum muṟi:  paḷḷip pāṭa vēḷit tiṭṭattil nūlakappaṭippiṟkāka otukkappeṟṟuḷḷa nērattilum piṟa camayaṅkaḷilum āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkar kaḷiṭiyē paṭikkum ārvatti ūṭṭutali mutaṟ kaṭamiyākak koḷḷavēṇṭum; mutalāya kaṭamiyum atutāṉ.  perumpālum āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṭam avarkaḷ paṭikkum nūlkaḷil kāṇakkiṭakkum mukkiyamāṉa coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum, (Phrase) maraput toṭarkaḷiyum puttakattil paṭikkum poruḷkaḷiyum paṟṟic curukkamāka eḻutumpaṭi colli avarkaḷiṭam iyalpākat tōṉṟum paṭikkum ārvatti vērilēyē citittu viṭukiṉṟaṉar.  jārj sāmsaṉ eṉṟa aṟiñar, ilakkiya maṅki paḷḷi āciriyarkaḷiṉ kiyilum tērvāḷarkaḷiṉ kiyilum cikkuvatu avaḷatu pōtāk kālamē; avaḷatu aḻakiyum poliviyum viṭa avaḷ tarum cītaṉattiyē mukkiyamākak karutum pēr vaḻikaḷ avarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟic ceṉṟati āciriyarkaḷ cintittup pārkkavēṇṭum.15  pala nūlkaḷip paṭittu māṇākkarkaḷ paṭippatil oru cuviyiyum iṉṟiyami yāmiyiyum tammiṭiyē vaḷarttuk-koḷḷukiṟavari āciriyarkaḷ immātiriyāṉa vēlikaḷi avarkaḷukkiṭak kūṭātu.  avi vaḷarntāl ittakiya vēlikaḷi āciriyarkaḷ avarkaḷukkut taravēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmiyum illi.  avarkaḷukkē eṉṉa ceyyavēṇṭum eṉpatu tāṉākap pulappaṭum.  inta uṇmiyi aṟintu āciriyarkaḷ ēṟṟavaṟṟic ceyyavēṇṭum.
nūlakap paṭippi mēṟkoḷḷum muṟi:  ivviṭattil nūlakap pāṭa vēḷiyil tammiṭam varum māṇākkarkaḷukku āciriyar evvāṟu an nērattip payaṉ peṟum vakiyil celaviṭa vaḻikāṭṭavēṇṭum eṉpatippaṟṟi oru ciṟitu kavaṉittal poruttamē.  payiṟṟu muṟikaḷi ārāynta poḻutu ‘mēṟ pārvip paṭippu’ eṉṟa oru muṟiyik kuṟippiṭṭōm aṉṟō?16  āṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa cila ciṟanta kūṟukaḷik kiyāṇṭu nūlakap paṭippip payaṉuṟumpaṭi ceyyalām.  avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi īṇṭu eṭuttuk kūṟuvōm.
innūlaka vakuppil āciriyarkaḷ tērnteṭukkappeṟṟa nūlkaḷi māṇākkarkaḷiṭam eṭuttuk koṭuttu avarkaḷi vāykkuṭ paṭikkumāṟu ceyyavēṇṭum.  oru nūlukkup pala paṭikaḷ irukkumāyiṉ vakuppic ciṟuciṟu pirivukaḷākap pirittu oru pirivukku oru nūliyum, maṟṟoru pirivukku maṟṟoru nūliyum koṭuttup piṉṉar avaṟṟi māṟṟik koḷḷumpaṭi ceyyalām.  kīḻ vakuppukkaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ virumpum nūlkaḷik koṭukkavēṇṭu mēyaṉṟi cila nūlkaḷik koṭuttu avaṟṟittāṉ paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttutal paṭippatil uḷḷa cuviyiyē citittuviṭum.  mēl vakuppukkaḷil āciriyarkaḷ, māṇākkarkaḷ paṭikkavēṇṭum eṉṟu tām karutum nūlkaḷip paṭikkumāṟu tūṇṭalām.
potu vakuppil kaṟpikkumpoḻutu iṉṉa ceytikaḷi iṉṉa mēṟkōḷ nūlil kāṇalām eṉṟu kūṟiyiruntāl anta nūlil ceytikaḷik kāṇumpaṭi vaḻi kāṭṭalām.  nūlakattil nūlkaḷip paṭikkumpoḻutu viḷaṅkāta ceytikaḷ, poruḷ teriyāta coṟkaḷ ākiyavikaḷi mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷ, akarāti nūlkaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷip puraṭṭi aṟintu koḷḷum muṟikaḷiyum kāṭṭalām.  ippaṭi vaḻikaḷik kāṭṭi akaṉṟa paṭippup paḻakkatti naṉku uṇṭākki viṭṭāl atu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muḻuvatum nilipeṟa iṭamuṇṭu.  itaṟkut tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ naṉku kaṟṟu aṟintavarkaḷāka iruttal vēṇṭum.
intap pāṭa vēḷiyil caṟṟup piṟpōkkāṉa māṇākkar kaḷi naṉku kavaṉikkalām.  aṭikkaṭi avarkaḷiṭam ceṉṟu aṉpuṭaṉ naṇpaṉpōl paḻaki avarkaḷ aiyaṅkaḷi akaṟṟa vēṇṭum.  mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷi evvāṟu kiyāṇṭu teriyātaṉavaṟṟit terintukoḷḷalām eṉpati avarkaḷukku naṉku viḷakkavēṇṭum.  paṭippu virivākac ceṉṟāltāṉ atil paṟṟu ēṟpaṭṭup pala cuvikaḷik kāṇalām eṉṟum vaṟpuṟuttutalvēṇṭum.  pala vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷ, viḷakkap paṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tāṅkiyaṉavum, eḷiya iṉiya naṭiyil eḻutap peṟṟaṉavum, periya eḻuttukkaḷil acciṭap peṟṟaṉavumāṉa nūlkaḷikkoṇṭu im māṇākkarkaḷukkup paṭippil cuviyūṭṭalām.  caṟṟu muṟpōkkāṉa māṇākkarkaḷ ip pāṭavēḷiyil niṟinta payaṉi aṭivar; pala nūlkaḷi virivākap paṭittu aṟivup paciyiyum pōkkik koḷvar.
āḻnta paṭippi mēṟkoḷḷumpoḻutu ceyyap peṟum payiṟcikaḷip pōṉṟavikaḷi iṅku vaṟpuṟuttak kūṭātu.  orōvaḻi putiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷiyum maraput toṭarkaḷiyum aruñ coṟkaḷiyum viḷakkalām.  paṭikkum poḻutu karutti maṭṭilum uṇarntāl pōtum eṉṟum, kati allatu kaṭṭuriyiṉ pōkkiyum toṭarcciyiyum aṟintu koṇṭāl pōtum eṉṟum, aṭikkaṭi vaṟpuṟuttutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  toṭarntu vāykkuḷḷēyē paṭikkum calippip pōkka cila camayaṅkaḷil cila nūlkaḷiluḷḷa mukkiya iṭaṅkaḷiyum, ciṟanta naṭiyil eḻutap peṟṟuḷḷa pakutikaḷiyum paṭittukkāṭṭi anta nūlkaḷiṉpāl avarkaḷiṉ kavaṉatti īrkkalām.  itaṉāl palar anta nūlkaḷip paṭittup payaṉ peṟa vaḻiyuṇṭu.
nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷ, nāṭōṭip pāṭalkaḷ, taṉippāṭalkaḷ, kummi, cintu, temmāṅku, kuṟavañci, paḷḷu, noṇṭi nāṭakaṅkaḷ, ciṟappup peyar akarātikaḷ, palporuṭ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kavaṉattit tiruppiviṭṭu anta nūlkaḷil cuviyūṭṭalām.  ilakkiyam, tiṟaṉāyvu nūlkaḷ, moḻi nūlkaḷ, moḻi varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷ, ilakkiya ārāycci nūlkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip paṭikka cila muṟpōkkāṉa māṇākkarkaḷi ēviviṭalām.  ciṟuvarkkāṉa tiṅkaḷ, piṟi, vāra veḷiyīṭukaḷ, kañcikikaḷ, viḷakka itaḻkaḷ mutaliyavi kaḷum, kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarum vaṇṇap paṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭa itaḻkaḷum vakuppu nūlakattil vikkap peṟṟiruntāl, māṇākkarkaḷiṭam paṭippuṇarcci iyalpāka eḻa iṭam uṇṭu.  aṉṟāṭac ceytit tāḷkaḷiyum iṅku iṭampeṟac ceytu ceytikaḷi aṟintukoḷḷum vāyppiṉiyum nalkalām.
ovvoru vakuppu māṇākkaṉuṭiya peyar.  avaṉ eṭukkum nūliṉ peyar, eṭutta tēti, tiruppik koṭutta tēti mutaliya vivaraṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya pativēṭu oṉṟi vittu avvap poḻutu patintu koḷḷalām.  ovvoru māṇākkaṉukkum ovvoru pakkam viṭṭu eḻutiṉāl avaṉ anta āṇṭil eṉṉeṉṉa nūlkaḷip paṭittāṉ, avaṉ ettaṉi nūlkaḷip paṭittāṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa ceytikaḷi āciriyar aṟintu koḷḷalām.  mēṟkōḷ nūlkaḷit tavira, nūlaka vakuppil paṭittu muṭiyāta nūlkaḷi vīṭṭiṟku eṭuttuc cella icivum aḷikkalām.
paṭippakam:  iṉṟu tāymoḻiyil ērāḷamāṉa ceytit tāḷkaḷum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷum nāḷtōṟum veḷi vanta vaṇṇa mirukkiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟuḷ nallaṉavaṟṟit tērnteṭuttu vāṅki māṇākkarkaḷukku avikaḷip paṭikkum vāyppiṉi aḷittal vēṇṭum.  itaṟku amiti nilavakkūṭiya aṟiyoṉṟu irukki vacatikaḷuṭaṉ irukkavēṇṭum.  itaṉ poṟuppi nūlakak kaṇkāṇippāḷar ēṟkavēṇṭum.  itil māṇākkarkaḷ amitiyuṭa ṉiruntu ceytit tāḷkaḷiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum pārkkum paḻakkatti vaḷarttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  paḷḷi māṇākkat talivaṉum vakuppu māṇākkat talivarkaḷum nūlakak kaṇkāṇippāḷaruṭaṉ ottuḻittu ivṟiyil nalla amitiyi nilavac ceyyalām.  paṭikkum tāḷkaḷi vittup pōṟṟum uṇarcci ovvoruvariṭamum vaḷaravēṇṭum.
nūlakattiyum paṭippakattiyum ciṟanta muṟiyil amittuc ceyaṟpaṭac ceytāl tāymoḻip payiṟciyil avaṟṟit tiṟampaṭavum payaṉuṟavum kiyāḷalām.  tāy moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ inta iraṇṭu cātaṉaṅkaḷiyum takka muṟikaḷil payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷavēṇṭum.  nūlkaḷil mukavuriyil collakkūṭiya ceytikaḷ, pāṭaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya āciriyarkkuriya kuṟippukkaḷ, āciriyarkkuriya piṟatēvikaḷ ākiyavi pāṭanūlkaḷil iṭam peṟutal tavaṟu.  ivaṟṟi āciriyar nūl eṉṟa taṉic ciṟu nūlākavō cuṟṟaṟikki mūlamākavō veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭum.
kuḻantikaḷuk keṉavum ciṟuvar ciṟumiyarkaḷukkeṉavum ērāḷamāṉa tamiḻp pattirikikaḷum paruva veḷiyīṭukaḷum cila āṇṭukaḷāka veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.  toṭakka nilip paḷḷikaḷilum oru paṭippakatti ēṟpaṭutti ivi aṉivarukkum kiṭṭumpaṭi ceytalvēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu ceytāl, ettaṉiyō ēḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷiliruntu varum kuḻantikaḷ avaṟṟip paṭittup payaṉaṭiyak kūṭum.  iṉṟu pala uyarnilip paḷḷikaḷil peyaraḷavil paṭippakam eṉa oṉṟu uḷḷatu; pala paḷḷikaḷil peyaraḷavilkūṭa paṭippakam illi.  pala kallūrikaḷil inniliyē nilavukiṉṟatu.  intanili virivil māṟa vēṇṭum.
16. kalvittuṟi aḷaviyal
kalvittuṟic cōtaṉikaḷ:  kuḻantikaḷ paḷḷikaḷilum paḷḷikaḷukku veḷiyilum kaṟṟavaṟṟi aḷantaṟiyap payaṉpaṭuttum cōtaṉikaḷik kalvittuṟic cōtaṉikaḷ (Educational tests) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭalām.  āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpittalāl kuḻantikaḷ aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟiyum, vakuppaṟi, paḷḷi aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭa vaṟṟiyum, paḷḷikku veḷiyil peṟum aṉupavaṅkaḷāl aṟintu koṇṭavaṟṟiyum taṉittaṉiyākap pirittu aṟivatum ovvoru vakiyilum aṟintukoṇṭatu evvaḷavu eṉpatik kaṇakkiṭṭu aṟivatum iyalāta ceyal.  eṉavē, ellāt tuṟikaḷilum kaṟṟavaṟṟi oru cērattāṉ aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum.  cātāraṇamākak kalvittuṟiyil mūvakic cōtaṉikaḷ naṭimuṟiyiluḷḷaṉa.  avi:  (1) vāymoḻic cōtaṉikaḷ; (2) kaṭṭuric cōtaṉikaḷ; (3) puṟavayat tērvukaḷ (Objective examinations) mūṉṟāvatu vakiyil irupirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  oṉṟu, tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷ piṟitoṉṟu, tarappaṭuttāta cōtaṉikaḷ allatu putu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷ.  ivi ovvoṉṟiyumpaṟṟi oru ciṟitu virivāka ārāyvōm.
1. vāymoḻic cōtaṉikaḷ
māṇākkarkaḷ kaṟṟavaṟṟi aḷantaṟivataṟkuc cātāraṇamāka vāymoḻic cōtaṉikaḷ mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl, ivaṟṟāl aḷantaṟiyum muṭivukaḷic cariyāṉavi eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.  vakuppiṟku aintāṟu māṇākkarkaḷ iruntāl ivaṟṟik kiyāḷa muṭiyum.  iṉṟuḷḷatupōl vakuppiṟku nāṟpatu allatu aimpatu māṇākkarkaḷiruntāl vāymoḻic cōtaṉikaḷik kuṟukiya kālattil evvāṟu veṟṟikaramākak kiyāḷa muṭiyum? māṇākkarkaḷ kūṟum viṭikaḷiyum avacara avacaramāka matippiṭa vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.  avaṟṟic cariyā tavaṟā eṉṟu nitāṉittup pārppataṟkukkūṭa nēram irātu.  tavira, ovvoru māṇākkaṉum aṟintuḷḷavaṟṟic cātāraṇamāka ovvoruvaṉukkum vāykkak kūṭiya mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku viṉākkaḷikkoṇṭu aḷantaṟivatu ciṟitum poruttamaṉṟu.  inta mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku viṉākkaḷilum oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭiṉamāka amintālum, allatu avaṟṟiṉ viṭikaḷi avaṉ aṟiyāviṭiṉum avaṉ cōtaṉiyil tavaṟak kūṭumallavā? itaṉāl avaṉi oṉṟum aṟiyātavaṉ eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭuvatu poruntumā? eṉavē, cintaṉiyiṉ kāraṇamāka viṭiyiṟukkak kūṭiya viṉākkaḷik kiyāḷuvataṟku immuṟi ciṟitum poruntātu.  tavira, māṇākkarkaṭkum āḻntu aṟutiyiṭṭu viṭiyiṟuppataṟku vēṇṭiya kālattiyum tara iyalātu.  eṉavē, vāymoḻi viṉākkaḷi aḷavuk karuviyākak koḷvativiṭa kaṟpikkum upāyamākak koḷvatē ēṟpuṭittu eṉṟu kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  ākavē, vāymoḻi viṉākkaḷ iṉṟiya navīṉa aḷaviyalil iṭam peṟavilli.
2. kaṭṭuric cōtaṉikaḷ
kaṭṭuric cōtaṉiyi nām naṉku aṟivōm.  itu pallāṇṭukaḷāka naṭimuyil iruntu varuvatu; mūṉṟu allatu iraṇṭari maṇikkāla aḷavil aintu allatu āṟu viṉāk kaḷik kēṭṭu oru pāṭa muḻuvatilum māṇākkaṉukku irukka vēṇṭiya oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu āṇṭu aṟivi aḷantu aṟiya muyalkiṉṟatu intac cōtaṉi.  putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷ celvākkup peṟṟa piṟaku itaṉ celvākku kuṟiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.  eṉiṉum, intac cōtaṉiyi aṭiyōṭu nīkka vaḻiyēyilli; kallūri vakuppukkaḷil itutāṉ iṉṟum āṭci celuttivarukṉiṟatu.  kalvi nipuṇarkaḷ iccōtaṉi cariyāṉa aḷavu karuviyalla eṉpatip pallāṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē meyppittu viṭṭaṉar.  itiluḷḷa kuṟikaḷiyum naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.  mukkiyamāṉa oru cilavaṟṟi maṭṭilum īṇṭuk kavaṉippōm.
aḷantaṟiyum ellik kuṟivu:  itaṉāl aḷantaṟiyak kūṭiya poruḷaṟivu mikak kuṟivu.  ciṟiya kāla aḷavil āṇṭu muḻuvatum paṭitta pāṭa aṟivi aintāṟu viṉākkaḷik koṇṭu tēṟiviṭa muṭiyum eṉṟu eṇṇuvatu poruntāta toṉṟu; appaṭi aḷappatil oru cila mukkiya ellip parappukkaḷi maṭṭilumtāṉ aṟiya iyalum.  oru viṉāvit tavaṟākap purintukoḷpavar allatu avviṭiyiṉ pakuti kaṟpikkappaṭṭapoḻutu paḷḷikku varātiruntavar cariyāka viṭiyiṟukka muṭiyātu pōtal kūṭum.  itaṉāl avarip poruḷ aṟivil kuṟivuḷḷavar eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭutal poruntātu.
tiruttuvōriṉ maṉanili:  tērvāḷarkaḷ māṇākkar kaḷiṉ viṭiyēṭukaḷi orē potu nōkkamum orēvita nōkkamum koṇṭu tiruttukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli.  viṭikaḷiṉ takutiyi yaṟintu ēṟṟa matippeṇkaḷik koṭuppatu tērvāḷarkaḷiṉ maṉaniliyiyē poṟuttuḷḷatu eṉpataṟku ettaṉiyō aṉupavaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  mukkuṇavacattāl muṟi piṟaḻntupōvatuṭaṉ nillātu ciṉam, makiḻcci mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟpa matippu eṇkaḷ kuṟintum mikuntum pōtalkūṭum.  ittakiya nilikaḷik kaṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷ illāḷ ūṭi niṉṟakālattil intat tērvāḷar ivvāṟu kuṟivāka matippiṭṭār pōlum! eṉṟu eḷḷi nakiyāṭuvati nām kāṇāmal illi.  pērāciriyar pālārṭ ittakiya nikaḻcci oṉṟiṉit tam nūlil kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.1 1920-il amerikka nāṭṭup palkalikkaḻakam oṉṟil varalāṟṟup pāṭattil oru tērvu naṭantatu.  antat tērviṉ viṭiyēṭukaḷi āṟu pērāciriyarkaḷ tirutta vēṇṭum.  100-kku 60 matippeṇkaḷukkuk kuṟivāka vāṅkuvōr tērvil tavaṟiyavarkaḷ.  tavaṟum elliyiluḷḷavarkaḷiṉ viṭiyēṭukaḷi ellōrum pārkka vēṇṭum.  maṉaccāṉṟukkuc ciṟitum māṟātu paṇiyāṟṟum oruvar taṉakku viḻikāṭṭiyāka irukkumporuṭṭu mātiri viṭikaḷi eḻutikkoṇṭu tiruttiṉār.  tavaṟutalāka inta viṭiyēṭu tavaṟum niliyiluḷḷavarkaḷiṉ viṭiyēṭu kaḷuṭaṉ kalantuviṭṭatu.  atiyum oruvariṉ viṭiyēṭu eṉṟu ēṉiyōr niṉittu ataṟkum matippeṇkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉar.  atu 40-liruntu 80 variyilum matippeṇkaḷ peṟṟatu.  taṉṉuṭiya tērvilēyē tavaṟum pērāciriyar niliyik kāṇum poḻutum itu pōṉṟa piṟa cāṉṟukaḷālum ik kaṭṭuric cōtaṉiyil oru peruṅkuṟi iruppatāka uṟuti ceyyavēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.  ivi iraṇṭum poruṅkuṟikaḷ.  vēṟu kuṟikaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  pērāciriyar pālārṭ tiruttuvōrip paṟṟik kūṟum karuttu ivviṭattil cintikkattakkatu.  maruttuvar kaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷ kallaṟikkuk kīḻ maṟintu kiṭappatip pōlavē, tērvāḷarkaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷ potumakkaḷ pārvi yiliruntu maṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.2
piṟa kuṟikaḷ:  kaṭṭuri eṉpatu pala kūṟukaḷik koṇṭa tiṟaṉāl uṇṭākkappaṭṭa ōr paṭippu, ovvoruvarum tattamakkēṟṟavāṟu aḷavukōlkaḷi māṟṟikkoṇṭu atit tiruttuvar.  kiyeḻuttu, moḻinaṭi, poruḷik kōviyākat tokuttukkūṟum pōkku, ariya collāṭci, maraputtoṭar āṭci, niṟuttaṟkuṟikaḷi iṭutal pōṉṟa kūṟukaḷum oru kaṭṭuriyiṉ taratti aṟutiyiṭuvataṟku mēṟkoḷḷap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  iṉṉum vēṟu kuṟikaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟi aṟiñarkaḷ eḻutiya aḷaviyal nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.
niṟikaḷ:  iṉi, ic cōtaṉikaḷāl niṟikaḷē illāmal illi.  mukkiyamāṉavaṟṟi maṭṭilum īṇṭu kūṟuvōm.
(1) ivvakic cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyvatum eḷitu; kiyāḷuvatum eḷitu.  iccōtaṉikaḷiṉ viṉākkaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷi āciriyarkaḷ naṉku aṟivar; avaṟṟiṟku viṭi yiṟuppatiyum māṇākkarkaḷ naṉku aṟivar.  viṉākkaḷukku evvāṟu viṭiyiṟuppatu eṉpatippaṟṟi māṇākkarkaṭku viḷakkattēviyē illi.
(2) paḷḷik kalvit tiṭṭattiluḷḷa ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷukkum immuṟi āyvukaḷi eḷitākak kiyāḷalām.  kaṇakkaṟivut tiṟaṉ, kiyeḻuttut tiṟaṉ, paṭipput tiṟaṉ pōṉṟavaṟṟi iv vāyvukaḷāl aḷantaṟiya iyalāviṭiṉum, vēṟu tuṟikaḷil avi peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(3) poruḷkaḷiṉ variyaṟivukaḷ kūṟuvatu, teḷivu paṭuttik kāṭṭuvatu, tokuppatu, vakuppatu, virippatu, curukkuvatu mutaliya tiṟaṉkaḷi yellām ivvaki āyvukaḷāltāṉ aḷantaṟiya iyalum.  uyarnili vakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku, mukkiyamākak kallūri palkalikkaḻaka māṇākkarkaṭku, iccōtaṉikaḷ payaṉpaṭum.
ivaṟṟit tavira, vēṟu pala niṟikaḷum kāṭṭap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  avi yāvum kūṟuvōriṉ maṉappāṉmiyip poṟuttaṉa vēyaṉṟi, ārāycciyiṉ muṭivukaḷ aṉṟu.  eṉavē, avi īṇṭu eṭuttuk kūṟappeṟavilli.
kaṭṭuric cōtaṉiyic cīrpaṭuttuvatu:  itupaṟṟip pala aṟiñarkaḷ ārāyntuḷḷaṉar.  avarkaḷ kaṇṭa muṭivukaḷ iraṇṭu pirivukaḷil aṭaṅkum.  avi: (i) cōtaṉikkut tērnteṭukkum poruḷum viṉākkaḷi amikkum muṟiyum; (ii) viṭiyēṭukaḷit tiruttum muṟikaḷ.  ovvoruvarum vakuttuḷḷa vitikaḷ yāvum niliyāṉavi alla.  eṉavē, ellāk kālattukkum ellōrālum kiyāḷakkūṭiya mūṉṟu vitikaḷi maṭṭilum īṇṭuk kūṟuvōm.
(1) cōtaṉiyiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa nōkkatti āciriyarkaḷum māṇākkarkaḷum teḷivāka aṟintu koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  kaṭṭuric cōtaṉiyil karuttu. ārāyum āṟṟal pōṉṟa kūṟukaḷukkuttāṉ mukkiyattuvam tarutal vēṇṭum.  kavarcci karamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷ, toṭarpukaḷi uṇarttum iṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi cōtaṉikkuriya pakutikaḷākak koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  muṭivu kūṟutal, tokuttuk kūṟutal, ciṟappāṉa nikaḻccikaḷiliruntu potu vitiyākkik kūṟutal pōṉṟa pakutikaḷit tēṟuvatu kaṭiṉam.  āṉāl, avaṟṟippōl māṇākkarkaḷiṉ maṉappaṇpukaḷit teḷivākak kāṭṭum vēṟu pakutikaḷē illiyeṉṟu kūṟi viṭalām.
(2) cōtaṉi ceyyappaṭum pakuti cōtaṉiyiṉ nōkkatti yoṭṭiyiruttal vēṇṭum.  pāṭa nōkkatti yoṭṭic cōtaṉikaḷ amivatu ciṟappākum.  putumuṟic cōtaṉi viṉākkaḷippōlavē iccōtaṉi viṉākkaḷum avakācam koṭuttu āḻntu cintittu āyattam ceyyap peṟumāṉāl, iccōtaṉikaḷāl māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum matippeṇkaḷum kūṭiyavari tiṭṭamāka irukkum; aḷavīṭṭiṉ taramum perukum.
(3) viṭiyēṭukaḷit tiruttuvataṟkut tiṭṭamāṉa vitikaḷi vakuttuk koḷḷavēṇṭum.  iv vitikaḷ tēviyaṟṟa kūṟukaḷi vilakkuvaṉavāka amital vēṇṭum.  ittakiya vitikaḷ aḷavukōlic cīrpaṭutti aḷavīṭukaḷiṉ tarattiyum uyarttum.



3. tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷ
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷ eṉpatu aṭippaṭiyil tēṟavēṇṭiya poruḷ cārpuṟṟē irukkum; avaṟṟiṟkup potunili aḷavikaḷ (Norms) aṟutiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷ pōtumāṉa aḷavu ērāḷamāṉa pērkaḷukkuk koṭukkap peṟṟu avarkaḷ peṟum matippeṇkaḷik koṇṭu potunili aḷavikaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  intac cōtaṉikaḷi atē vayatu allatu atē aṟivu niliyuḷḷavarkaḷukkuk koṭuttāl, avarkaḷum atē matippeṇkaḷip peṟuvar.  ittakiya cōtaṉikaḷ it tuṟiyil naṉku payiṟci peṟṟavarkaḷik koṇṭu cōtaṉa āyattam ceyyum aṭippaṭiyāṉa vitikaḷit taḻuvi uruvākkap peṟṟavi.  kaṭanta aimpatu āṇṭukaḷāka mēṉāṭukaḷil intac cōtaṉi iyakkam tōṉṟi ceyaṟpaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.  mutalām ulakap perum pōrukkup piṟakutāṉ inta iyakkam nalla muṟiyil vaḷarntatu.
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷ pala tuṟikaḷip paṟṟiyavi.  mutaṉ mutalāka aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉikaḷittāṉ tarappaṭuttu vatil aṟiñarkaḷ kavaṉam celuttiṉar.  iṉṟu aṟivut tuṟi,  paḷḷittuṟi allāta piṟa tuṟikaḷilum cōtaṉikaḷ āyattam ceyyap peṟṟut tarappaṭuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ivvāṟu tarappaṭuttap peṟṟa cōtaṉikaḷ kaṟpittal tuṟiyil aḷattal muṟiyil ciṟanta muṉṉēṟṟattik kaṇṭuḷḷaṉa; aḷattalic cariyāṉa muṟiyilum nēriya muṟiyilum koṇṭucelutti yuḷḷaṉa.  aṇmiyil mēṉāṭukaḷil palvēṟu vitamāṉa tarappaṭuttap peṟṟa cōtaṉikaḷ acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa; avi kalvit tuṟiyil pala  pirivukaḷip paṟṟiyavi.  avaṟṟuḷ aṭiyiṟ kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷa oru cilavaṟṟip paṟṟit tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ aṟintiruttal mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
	(i)	aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉikaḷ
	(ii)	nāṭṭac cōtaṉikaḷ
	(iii)	akkaṟiyaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ
	(iv)	muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ
	(v)	aṭivuc cōtaṉikaḷ
	(vi)	kuṟiyaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ
ivi ovvoṉṟiyumpaṟṟi oru ciṟitu kūṟuvōm.
(i) aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉikaḷ (Intelligence tests):  ivaṟṟil taṉiyāḷ cōtaṉikaḷ, kuḻu allatu kūṭṭac cōtaṉikaḷ eṉa iruvaki uḷa.  muṉṉavaṟṟiṉ tāyakam ḵpireñcu nāṭu.  piṉṉavaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭam amerikka nāṭu.  taṉiyāḷ cōtaṉikaḷ aṟivu niliyic cariyāka aḷakkavalla aḷavu karuvi eṉṟu kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar; accōtaṉikaḷtām iṉṟu mikavum celvākkup peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  cōtaṉiyiṉ nīḷam, palvēṟu tulaṅkalkaḷik kāṇal, ovvoruvarum taṉittaṉiyākak kuṟippukkaḷi aṟiyum muṟi, tērvāḷar ovvoruvariṭamum veḷippaṭum ovvoru tulaṅkaliyum kāṇum vāyppu, cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷuvatil tarappaṭuttappeṟṟa muṟikaḷ, tiruttum muṟi ākiya kūṟukaḷ iccōtaṉikaḷic ciṟanta aḷavu kōlākki yuḷḷaṉa.  payiṟciyum aṉupavamumpeṟṟa nipuṇarik koṇṭu tāṉ cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷa vēṇṭumēyaṉṟi, cātāraṇa āciriyarkaḷāl ivaṟṟik kiyāḷamuṭiyātu.  āyiṉum, iccōtaṉikaḷ kāṇum muṭivukaḷukku evvāṟu viḷakkam taruvatu eṉpati ovvoru tāymoḻi yāciriyarum aṟintirut tal vēṇṭum. 
cōtaṉikaḷiṉ ēṟpuṭimi (Validity) cila aṭip paṭiyāṉa koḷkikaḷip poṟuttuḷḷatu.  aṟitiṟaṉ eṉpatu kuṭivaḻi vanta iyaṟkip paṇpu; cūḻniliyālum payiṟciyālum ataṉi māṟṟa muṭiyātu.  atu maṉa vāḻvip paṟṟiya potup paṇpēyaṉṟi tiṭṭamāṉa paṇpaṉṟu.  atu toṭakkattil uṭal vayatiṟkēṟṟavāṟu vēkamākavum kumarap paruvam eytiya piṟaku mikavum metuvākavum mutiṟciyaṭintu irupatāvatu āṇṭil ataṉ vaḷarcci niṉṟu viṭukiṉṟatu.  aṇmiyil tārṉṭik pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ ceyta ārāycci kāḷal oruvariṉ naṭupparuvam variyilum aṟitiṟaṉ vaḷarcci taṭippaṭavilli eṉṟu kaṇṭaṟiyappeṟṟuḷḷatu.  muṉ-kumarap paruvattil aṟitiṟaṉ vaḷarcci mikak kuṟivāka iruppatāl ṭermaṉ tiruttiyamitta piṉēyiṉ cōtaṉikaḷil paṉṉiraṇṭu, patiṉāṉku vayatiṟku viṉākkaḷum vaḷarntavar kaḷukku iraṇṭu cōtaṉikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
piṉēyiṉ āyvu 1905-il mutaṉ mutalāka ḵpireñcu nāṭṭil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟatu.  piṟaku piṉēyum cimaṉum cērntu 1908-lum, 1911-lum mīṇṭum tiruttaṅkaḷ ceytu veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.  amerikkāvil ivvāyvukaḷit taḻuviyum tiruttiyum 1911-il kāṭārṭ eṉpārum 1912-il kuhalmāṉ eṉpārum, 1916-il ṭermeṉ eṉpārum, 1922-il herri eṉpārum, 1937-il ṭermeṉ, merril eṉpārkaḷum veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.  iṟutiyākak kūṟappaṭṭa cōtaṉikaḷ tām iṉṟu amerikkāvil palarāl aṟintavi yākavum naṭimuṟiyil uḷḷaviyākavum uḷḷaṉa.  piṉē mūṉṟu mutal patiṉmūṉṟu vayatuvari ḵpireñcu nāṭṭuk kuḻavikaḷiṉ naṭattiyik kaṇṭaṟiya āyattam ceyta cōtaṉi kaḷip pōlavē, tamiḻnāṭṭuk kuḻantikaṭkēṟṟa cōtaṉikaḷi nam nāṭṭu uḷaviyalār ārāyntu āyattam ceyya vēṇṭiyatu iṉṟiyamiyāta tākum.
kuḻuc cōtaṉikaḷ (Group tests):  piṉēyiṉ āyvukaḷik kiyāḷuvatu kaṭiṉamāka iruppatālum, avaṟṟi ovvoru kuḻantikaḷukkum taṉittaṉiyākak koṭukka vēṇṭiyiruppatālum avi āciriyarkaḷukkuc ciṟitum naṭimuṟiyil payaṉpaṭavilli.  ākavē, uḷaviyalār pala kuḻantikaḷukkuc cērntāṟpōṟ koṭukkavalla eḻuttu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceytu avaṟṟi tarappaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar.  ivaṟṟiṟkeṉa kuṟippukkaḷ, viṭikaḷ, potunili aḷavikaḷ ākiyavi uḷḷaṉa.  kuṟippukkaḷi mika naṉṟāka aṟintukoṇṭu evarum ivaṟṟi eḷitil kiyāḷalām.  āciriyarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷil ic cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāṇṭu taṉippaṭṭa māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivippaṟṟik kavarcciyāṉa takavalkaḷi aṟintu koḷḷalām.  āciriyarkaḷ nāṭōṟum māṇākkarkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻaku vatāl pala takavalkaḷi aṟintu koḷḷa iyalum eṉṟum, ittakiya cōtaṉikaḷ tēviyilli yeṉṟum orucilar niṉittal kūṭum; atu tavaṟu.  cilacamayam ittakiya cōtaṉi kaḷāl viyattaku muṭivukaḷiyum kāṇṭalkūṭum.  vakuppil tōṟṟattil mantamākak kāṇum māṇākkaṉ cōtaṉiyil nalla matippeṇkaḷip peṟutalkūṭum.  nalla aṟivuḷḷa ammāṇākkaṉ yātu kāraṇattālō paḷḷivēliyil mantamatiyiṉaṉ pōl kāṭciyaḷittut taṉ matiyi āciriyarkaḷ aṟiyātu ceytuviṭṭāṉ.  aṟitiṟaṉkaḷ inniliyit teḷivākkum.  aṉṟiyum, iccōtaṉikaḷāl mikak kuṟukiya kālattil māṇākkarkaḷippaṟṟip pala takavalkaḷi aṟintu koḷḷa iyalum.  ōr āciriyar āṇṭiṉ oru paruvattil tam vakuppu māṇākkarkaḷippaṟṟi aṟiyum takavalkaḷi avar orumaṇi nērattil aṟintukoḷḷa iyalum.
kuḻuc cōtaṉikaḷ kaṭanta mutalām ulakap perum pōrk kālattil amerikkāvil mutaṉ mutalil tōṉṟiṉa.  irāṇuva ālḵpāc cōtaṉikaḷ irāṇuva pīṭṭāc cōtaṉikaḷ avaṟṟuḷ mukkiyamāṉavi.  piṉṉavi ceykic cōtaṉikaḷ; paṭiyāḷarkaḷit tērnteṭuppataṟku iccōtaṉikaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭuttappeṟṟaṉa.  pōrukkup piṟaku oṭṭi, ṭermeṉ pōṉṟa aṟiñarkaḷ paḷḷikaḷil payaṉpaṭak kūṭiyavāṟu attakiya cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceytu 1918-kkum, 1925-kkum iṭippaṭṭa kālattil veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.  1925-kkup piṟaku ettaṉiyō cōtaṉikaḷ veḷivantuḷḷaṉa.
ceykic cōtaṉikaḷ (Performance tests):  piṉē kaṇṭa cōtaṉikaḷ paṭikkat terintavarkaṭku maṭṭilumē payaṉpaṭak kūṭiyavi. paṭiyātavarkaḷiyum, ceviṭarkaḷākavum, ūmiyarkaḷāka uḷḷavarkaḷiyum evvāṟu ārāyvatu? avarkaḷuṭiya ceyalkaḷik koṇṭutāṉ avarkaḷuṭiya aṟivu niliyi ārāyavēṇṭum.  intac ceykic cōtaṉi kaḷil coṟkaḷukkup patilāka pulaṉīṭāṉa poruḷkaḷ (Concrete things) amintirukkum.  avarkaḷukkut tarappeṟum piracciṉi pulaṉīṭāṉaviyāka iruntālum aḵtu ārāyntu muṭivu kāṇa vallatākavē irukkum.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, pala vaṭivamāṉa marattuṇṭukaḷi oru marattuṇṭiṉiṉṟum veṭṭi eṭuttu ivarkaḷiṉ etirē vittu veṭṭi yeṭutta attuṇṭukaḷi mīṭṭum anta marattuṇṭil evvāṟu patiyavikkiṉṟaṉar eṉpataṉik kavaṉittāl porutta muṇarum aṟivu evvāṟu avarkaḷiṭiyē vaḷarntuḷḷatu.  eṉpati aṟiyalām.  ovvoru tuṇṭiṉiyum poruttuvataṟku eṭuttukkoḷḷum kālam, avarkaḷ tavaṟu ceytalkaḷiṉ eṇṇikki ākiyavaṟṟi yellām kuṟittuvittu oppiṭṭu nōkkiṉāl vayatiṟkēṟpa ivi māṟi varuvataṉik kāṇṭalkūṭum.  ittakiya potu nili aḷavukōlikkoṇṭu ellāk kuḻantikaḷiṉ aṟivu niliyiyum aḷakkalām.
(ii) nāṭṭac cōtaṉikaḷ:  paḷḷi yāciriyarkaḷukku ittakiya cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷum vāyppukkaḷē irāteṉiṉum, avarkaḷ ac cōtaṉikaḷiṉ potu iyalpukaḷip paṟṟiyāvatu aṟintiruttal avaciyam.  oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟiyil tiṟaṉi vaḷarttukkoḷḷavalla oruvariṭam amintuḷḷa āṟṟalittāṉ nāṭṭam (Aptitude) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.  oru poṟi nuṭpa nāṭṭac cōtaṉiyik koṇṭu oruvar karuvikaḷiyum piṟa poruḷkaḷiyum mēṟ koṇṭu ceyyavalla oruvaratu kivēlittiṟaṉi aḷantaṟital kūṭum.  potut tiṟaṉukkum taṉittiṟaṉukkum toṭarpu iruntālum, ataṟku vitivilakkum uḷḷati aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar.  ittuṟiyil virivāṉa ārāyccikaḷ naṭatti iyalpāka aminta potuttiṟaṉuṭaṉ tiṭṭamāṉa maṉap pōkkukaḷiyum aṟintuḷḷaṉar.  taṉi nāṭṭac cōtaṉikaḷik koṇṭu āciriyarkaḷum māṇākkarkaḷum toḻil tuṟikaḷilum kalvittuṟikaḷilum ūkkavalla tiṭṭamāṉa maṉappōkkukaḷik (Definite tendencies) kaṇṭaṟiyalām.  iṉṟu pala tuṟikaḷiyum ārāyavalla cōtaṉikaḷ āyattam ceyyappeṟṟu veḷiyiṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ivi yāvum muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉikaḷē.  oru kuṟippiṭṭa cūḻniliyil iccōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷvataṟku āciriyarkaḷ aḷaviyal tuṟiyil aṇmiyil veḷivantuḷḷa nūlkaḷip paṭittaṟiya vēṇṭum; allatu attuṟiyil taṉippayiṟciyum aṉupavamum peṟṟuḷḷavarkaḷiṉ tuṇiyi nāṭal vēṇṭum.
(iii) akkaṟiyaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ:  ivi mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa nāṭṭac cōtaṉikaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyavi.  ivaṟṟikkoṇṭu oruvar ettoḻilil akkaṟi kāṭṭukiṉṟār eṉpati yaṟiyalām.  ivaṟṟi āyattam ceyta aṟiñarkaḷ akkaṟi (Interest) eṉpatu kuṭivaḻi varum paṇpu eṉṟu kūṟavilli.  aṭippaṭiyāṉa maṉappōkkukaḷ mika iḷami yilēyē oruvariṭam nili peṟukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟa uḷaviyal uṇmiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ic cōtaṉikaḷ āyattam ceyyappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ippōkkukaḷ naṭuk kumarapparuvattil allatu piṉ-kumarapparuvattil mika aḻuttamākap paṭintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉavē, ip pōkkukaḷikkoṇṭu oruvar iyalpāka virumpum toḻil tuṟiyi aṟutiyiṭalām.  ṭāṉ pōrṭu palkalik kaḻakattic cērnta ṭrāṅ eṉṟa pērāciriyar ittuṟiyil naṉmuṟiyil ārāyccikaḷ ceytuḷḷār.
(iv) muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ:  piṉṉiya vaḷarcciyi-ṉippaṟṟi muṉṉarē aṟivu koḷuttavalla cōtaṉikaḷ muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ (Prognostic tests) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ivi kuḻantikaḷiṉ takutip pāṭṭiṉi ārāyntu ak kuḻantikaḷ evvettuṟikaḷil muṉṉukku varuvataṟkilli eṉpati uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟik koṇṭu oru kuḻanti kaṇitam, moḻi, ici mutaliya tuṟikaḷil muṉṉukku varakkūṭumā eṉpati ārāyalām.  āṉāl, naṭimuṟiyil ivaṟṟi atikamākap payaṉpaṭuttu vatilli.  kāraṇam, inta aḷavu karuvikaḷil makkaḷ iṉṉum atika nampikki vikkavilli; avarkaḷ nampukiṉṟa aḷavukkum avi iṉṉum cemmi yaṭiyavilli.  āciriyarkaḷum potumakkaḷum avi kuḻantikaḷiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa nāṭṭaṅkaḷi evvāṟu muṉṉarē cariyāka aṟivikkakkūṭum eṉṟu aiyuṟukiṉṟaṉar.
(v) aṭivuc cōtaṉikaḷ (Achievement tests):  mukkiyamākak kalvi niliyaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum aṟiviṉi aḷantaṟiyavalla cōtaṉikaḷittāṉ ippeyarāl kuṟikkiṉṟaṉar.  payaṉṭum nōkkattiṟkēṟṟavāṟu ivi māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  eṉavē, oru tiṭṭamāṉa nōkkatti yoṭṭi ivaṟṟik kiyāḷavēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, attiṭṭamāṉa nōkkatti yoṭṭi avi ākkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉavā eṉpatic cariyāka aṟintutāṉ payaṉpaṭuttavumvēṇṭum.  nōkkatti yoṭṭi ic cōtaṉikaḷ pala vakiyākap pirikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ovvoru tuṟiyilum potuvāṉa aṟivu niliyiṉi aḷantaṟiya valla kaṇakkeṭuppuc cōtaṉikaḷ (Survey tests) oru vaki.  ivaṟṟippōlavē, kaṟṟa pakutikaḷil takavalkaḷi maṭṭilum ārāya valla takaval cōtaṉikaḷ (Information tests) maṟṟāru vaki.  perumpālum ivi vayatiṟku ēṟpa amiyāmal vakuppiṟku ēṟpa amintirukkum.  kalvit tuṟiyil tarappaṭuttiya ic cōtaṉikaḷiṉ payaṉ aḷavaṟṟatu.  māṇākkarkaḷiṉ potu aṟivu niliyi āyavum, taṉippaṭṭōriṉ kuṟikaḷi āyavum māṇākkarkaḷit taram vāriyākap pirikkavum, oru kuḻuviṉ tiṟaṉip piṟitoru kuḻuviṉ tiṟaṉuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārkkavum, kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷiṉ tiṟaṉkaḷi ārāyavum ivi iṉṟu peritum payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivipaṟṟiya muḻu vivaraṅkaḷi aḷaviyal nūlkaḷil (Books on Measurement) kaṇṭu koḷka.
(vi) kuṟiyaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ (Diagnostic tests):  kaṟpatilō kaṟpippatilō uḷḷa kuṟikaḷik kāṇavalla cōtaṉikaḷik kuṟiyaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ eṉṟu muṟi vallār kuṟippiṭuvar.  kaṟpittal tuṟiyil ivvakic cōtaṉikaḷiṉ tēvi iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  navīṉa kalvi muṟiyil ivaṟṟiṉ avaciyam mikavum vaṟpuṟuttapṭukiṉṟatu.  ovvoru pāṭattilum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ tiṭṭamāṉa kuṟikaḷiyum niṟikaḷiyum aḷantaṟiyavalla cōtaṉikaḷ āyattam ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, kaṭṭurit tuṟiyil ittakiya cōtaṉikaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vākkiya amippil kuṟikaḷ, kaṭṭurip poruḷkaḷik kōviyāka  amittalil kuṟikaḷ, teḷivāṉa cintaṉiyiṉmi ākiyavaṟṟik kāṭṭutal kūṭum.  māṇākkar kaḷiṉ ārāyntu pārkkum muṟiyil kuṟikaḷ, oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟiyil uṟṟu nōkkiyaṟitalil kuṟikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi aḷantaṟiya valla cōtaṉikaḷum āyattam ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  tiṭṭamākak kuṟikaḷi aḷantu kāṭṭavalla cōtaṉiḷit tāṉ ciṟanta cōtaṉikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟavēṇṭum.  ittakayi cōtaṉikaḷāl māṇākkarkaḷ kaṟṟalil tam kuṟikaḷit tiṭṭamāka aṟital muṭiyum.  āciriyarkaḷum kaṟpittali evvevvakikaḷil tiṟamiyāka māṟṟiyamittal kūṭum eṉṟum, tām kiyāṇṭa muṟikaḷil eṉṉeṉṉa kuṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷutal kūṭum.
intac cōtaṉikaḷiṉ payaṉkaḷ: piṟa cōtaṉikaḷāl kāṇa iyalāta oru tiṭṭamāṉa kūṟukaḷi tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷāl aḷantaṟiyalām.  avaṟṟip paṟṟi īṇṭu oru ciṟitu nōkkuvōm.
(1) oppiṭutal: ic cōtaṉikaḷ oru vakuppu allatu paḷḷiyip piṟitōr ūriluḷḷa atē vakuppu allatu paḷḷiyuṭaṉ oppiṭṭup pārttaṟkup payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ic cōtaṉikaḷiṉ potunili aḷavikaḷ itaṟku mikavum mukkiyamāṉavi.  oppiṭutalpaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷi aḷaviyal nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷka.
(2) kuṟiyaṟital:  kaṟpittal tuṟiyil kuṟiyaṟital mikavum mukkiyamāṉa ōr kūṟu ākum.  uḷaviyal aṟivu vaḷaravaḷara itaṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi aṟiñarkaḷ naṉku uṇarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.  tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷik koṇṭu oru kuṟippiṭṭa tuṟiyil kuṟikaḷi aṟiyalām.
(3) takuti pirittal: kalviyaṟivuc cōtaṉikaḷi maṭṭilum koṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷi aṟivu nilikkēṟṟavāṟu takuti pirittali aṟiñarkaḷ cariyeṉa oppukkoḷvatilli.  orē mātiriyāṉa pāṭattiṭṭaṅkaḷum pāṭa nūlkaḷum illāmi kāraṇamāka āciriyarkaḷ tām āyattam ceyta cōtaṉikaḷik koṇṭē māṇākkarkaḷit taram pirikkiṉṟaṉar.  iṉṟu kalvittuṟi aṟiñarkaḷ āciriyarkaḷ naṭimuṟiyil taram pirippatil kiyāḷum muṟikku aṉukūlamāka aṟitiṟaṉ cōtaṉikaḷiyum tuṇik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  māṇākkarkaḷip pala tuṟikaḷilum koṇṭu celuttuvataṟku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka iruppataṟku nāṭṭac cōtaṉikaḷ muṉṉaṟi cōtaṉikaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟik kiyāḷa vēṇṭum eṉak karutukiṉṟaṉar.
(4) orē mātiriyāṉa cōtaṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷ: oru cōtaṉiyiṉāl vaḷarcciyi aṟutiyiṭuvativiṭa orē niliyil ākkappeṟṟa iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu cōtaṉikaḷiṉ matippeṇkaḷik koṇṭu aṟutiyiṭuvatu ciṟantatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karukiṉṟaṉar.  tarappaṭuttiya oṉṟiraṇṭu cōtaṉi kaḷik koṇṭu aṟivu vaḷarcciyiṉi oppiṭṭup pārkkalām.
(5) kiyāḷutal: cōtaṉi vaṭivaṅkaḷi eḷitāka amittāltāṉ nampakak kūṟu (Validity factor) uyar niliyil irukkum.  pattup pakutikaḷ uḷḷa oru cōtaṉiyiviṭa, pala pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭāta cōtaṉi ciṟantatu.  ovvoru pakutikkum iṭi iṭiyē viḷakkak kuṟippukkaḷ tantu cōtaṉikaḷit taruvativiṭa, oru cila viḷakkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ eppoḻutu toṭaṅkuvatu, eppoḻutu muṭippatu eṉṟa kuṟippi maṭṭilum tantu cōtaṉi taruvatu naṉṟu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.  cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷuvataṟku muṉ avaṟṟiṉ viḷakkak kuṟippukkaḷi āciriyarkaḷ teḷivāka aṟintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.  iṉṟu perumpālōrālum kiyāḷavalla cōtaṉikaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.  cōtaṉikaḷik koṭuttal; tiruttutal, muṭivukaḷukku viḷakkan tarutal pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ viḷakkak kuṟippukkaḷi aṟintu koṇṭavarkaḷ ivvakic cōtaṉikaḷip payaṉuḷḷa muṟiyil eḷitākak kiyāḷalām.
4. putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷ
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷiṉ  iyakkam celvākkup peṟṟavuṭaṉ atē muṟiyil āciriyarkaḷāl āyattam ceyyap peṟum putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷum celvākkup peṟat toṭaṅkiṉa.  ivi tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷ alla.  ivi naṭimuṟikku vantavuṭaṉ itukāṟum vakuppu aṟikaḷil naṭipeṟṟu vanta tēṟum muṟikaḷum māṟṟam aṭintaṉa; nāḷaṭivil intap putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyvatilum muṉṉēṟṟattik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉpatil kalvi nipuṇarkaḷiṉ kavaṉam ceṉṟatu.  eṉavē, ittuṟiyil naṉmuṟiyil payiṟci peṟṟa aṟiñarkaḷum uḷaviyal vallu narkaḷum putu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyvatil ārāyccikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar.  ivarkaḷ ceyta ārāycci kaḷiṉ viḷivākac cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyvatil cila vitikaḷ vakuppappeṟṟaṉa; attuṟiyil putiya vaḻikaḷum variyaṟi ceyyappeṟṟaṉa.
tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷi naṭimuṟiyil vakuppaṟi kaḷil payaṉpaṭutta iyalātu.  ēṉeṉil, avaṟṟi āyattam ceyta nipuṇar vakuppaṟikaḷil kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷ kaṟpittalil ententak kūṟukaḷil aḻuttam koṭuttuk kaṟpittaṉar eṉpati aṟiya iyalātu.  eṉavē, tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷ paḷḷikaḷi oppiṭṭup pārppataṟkum, orē vakuppukkaḷil pala pirivukaḷi oppiṭuvataṟkum, ōr ūruṭaṉ piṟitoru ūri ottup pārppataṟkum maṭṭilumē payaṉpaṭuttalām.  eṉavē, ataṉ aṭippaṭiyil āyattam ceyyap peṟṟaṉavum tarappaṭuttap peṟātaṉavum āciriyarkaḷāl avvappoḻutu āyattam ceyyap peṟuvaṉavumāṉa putu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷē vakuppaṟikaḷil naṭimuṟiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ivikaḷipṟṟit tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ aṟintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷil kiṭṭattaṭṭa muppattintukku mēṟpaṭṭa vakikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  eṉṟālum, ivaṟṟi yellām aṟiñarkaḷ maṟittuṇar vaki (Recognition type), niṉivu kūrvaki (Recall type) eṉṟu iruṅkūṟiṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.  maṟittuṇar vakiyil cari-tavaṟu cōtaṉikaḷ, palviṭiyiṟ poṟukkuc cōtaṉikaḷ, poruttaṟ cōtaṉikaḷ aṭaṅkum; niṉivukūr vakiyil nirappuc cōtaṉikaḷ mutaliyavi aṭaṅkum.  cōtaṉit tuṟiyil muṉṉēṟṟam aṭi ya aṭiya aṟiñarkaḷ putiya vakikaḷik kaṇṭukoṇṭē varukiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē, ivvakikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkiyum vaḷarntukoṇṭē varukiṉṟatu.  āyiṉum, ēḻu allatu eṭṭuvakik cōtaṉikaḷē naṭi muṟiyil payaṉpaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.  ellā vakikaḷip paṟṟiyum īṇṭu kūṟuvataṟku iṭam illi.  avaṟṟi aḷaviyali viḷakkum aṟiñarkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷil kaṇṭukoḷka.  orucila vakikaḷ maṭṭilum īṇṭu tarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
1. cari-tavaṟu cōtaṉikaḷ:  cila kuṟitta toṭar moḻikaḷik koṭuttu avaṟṟuḷ varum karuttukkaḷ cariyā, tavaṟā eṉpataṟkēṟpa kākkip puḷḷiyiyō cuḻiyiyō iṭumpaṭi kūṟutal.  ivaṟṟiyē viṉāvāka amittu ām illi teriyātu eṉṟa viṭikaḷil oṉṟi eḻutumāṟum ēvalām.
(e-ṭu)-1.
aṭiyiṟ kāṇum coṟkaḷil cilavaṟṟil eḻuttup piḻikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  cariyāṉavaṟṟiṟku nērāka ‘Î’ eṉṟum piḻiyāṉavaṟṟiṟku nērāka ‘x’ eṉṟum kuṟikaḷi iṭuka.

(e-ṭu)-2.
	1.	poṅkalum cuṇṭalum tiṉṟārkaḷ.  (toḻilāku peyar.)
	2.	pūṅkuḻalāḷ eṉap pulavar pōṟṟiṉar.  (aṉ moḻit toki).
	3.	veṇṭāmari malarntatu.  (paṇputtoki.)
	4.	paṉikkālam, iṟantakālam, irākukālam, muṟkālam, nikaḻkālam eṉak kālam āṟu vakippaṭum.
	5.	kāṟṟil aṭipaṭṭa pañcu. (vaṉṟoṭark kuṟṟiya lukaram.)
2. palviṭiyiṟ poṟukkuc cōtaṉikaḷ:  muṟkūṟiya vaṟṟiviṭa ivi caṟṟu arumiyum vaḷarcciyumuṭiyavi.  ivaṟṟuḷ oru poruḷpaṟṟiya pala toṭarmoḻikaḷil etu cari eṉṟu māṇākkarkaḷ oṉṟit tērnteṭukka vēṇṭi varum.  ilakkiya āciriyarkaḷ, avarkaḷ vāḻntakālam, ilakkiya māntarkaḷ, toki nūlkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟi ivaṟṟāl tēṟalām.  putu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷil iv vakiyi māṇākkarkaḷum āciriyarkaḷum peritum virumpukiṉṟaṉar.  ovvoru viṉāvukkum mūṉṟiliruntu aintuvari toṭar moḻikaḷ koṭukkappeṟum.
(e-ṭu.) kīḻkkāṇum viṉākkaḷil ovvoṉṟiṉ kīḻum aintu viṭikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  poruttamāṉavaṟṟiṉ eṇṇi maṭṭilum valappuṟattiluḷḷa pakara vaḷivukkuḷ eṭuttu eḻutuka.
1. naḷaveṇpāvi iyaṟṟiya pulavar:
(a) oṭṭakkūttar
(ā) kampaṉ
(i) pukaḻēnti
(ī) nakkīrar
(u) ceyaṅkoṇṭār			[	]

2. cāttaṉār iyaṟṟiya nūl:

(ā) kaliṅkattupparaṇi
(i) puṟanāṉūṟu
(ī) tiruvācakam

3. pāṇṭavarkaḷiṉ uṭaṉ piṟantavaṉ:
(a) cicupālaṉ
(ā) karṇaṉ
(i) vituraṉ
(ī) acuvattāmaṉ
(u) kaṭōṟkajaṉ			[	]
3. nirappuc cōtaṉikaḷ:  kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa coṟkaḷi amittuk toṭarmoḻiyi muṭittuk kāṭṭal.  ivvakic cōtaṉikaḷ kīḻ vakuppu māṇākkarkaṭku mikavum ēṟṟavi.  ēṟṟa colli eḻutuvataṟku muṉ orumuṟi toṭar moḻiyip paṭittu, piṉ colli amittu, piṉṉum orumuṟi toṭar moḻiyip paṭittut toṭarmoḻi cariyāṉa poruḷik koṭukkiṉṟatā eṉṟu pārkkumpaṭi aṟivuṟutta vēṇṭum.
(e-ṭu). kīḻē varum toṭarmoḻikaḷik kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa coṟkaḷi amittu muṭittuk kāṭṭuka.
	1.	mutalvēṟṟumi ____ eṉappaṭum.
	2.	____ ____ ____ nikaḻkāla iṭi nilikaḷ ākum.
	3.	oru moḻiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttu valliṉa ukaramākavum ayal eḻuttu neṭilākavum iruppiṉ atu ____  ukaramākum.
4. poruttaṟ cōtaṉikaḷ: iraṇṭu varicikaḷākat toṭar moḻikaḷi uṇmiyum poruttamum muṟiyum piṟaḻa amittu avaṟṟi poruttumpaṭi ceytal.  nikaḻccikaḷ, avi nikaḻnta āṇṭukaḷ ; pōrkaḷ, avaṟṟi naṭattiya cēṉit talivarkaḷ; pulavarkaḷ avarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷ; paḻamoḻikaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ karuttukkaḷ; maraputtoṭarkaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavaṟṟi ivvāṟu poruttac ceyyalām.  kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷiyum varicip paṭuttalām.  kuṟitta poruḷaṟivit tēṟa itu ciṟanta muṟiyākum.
(e-ṭu) kīḻkkaṇṭavaṟṟil a piriviluḷḷavaṟṟi ā piriviluḷḷavaṟṟuṭaṉ poruttik kāṭṭuka.  pakaravaḷivukkuḷ viṭiyiṉ eṇṇimaṭṭilum eḻutiṉāl pōtumāṉatu.

5. iṉam pirikkum cōtaṉikaḷ :  ōr iṉam, allatu oru tokutiyil cērāta, aṭaṅkāta coṟkaḷi avvariciyi liruntu aṭittu viṭumpaṭi ceytal.  avaṟṟiṉ kīḻk kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭum paṭiyum ēvalām. (e-ṭu) kīḻkkaṇṭavaṟṟil ovvoṉṟilum avviṉattil aṭaṅkāta oru col uḷḷatu.  accolli maṭṭilum aṭiyil kōṭiṭṭuk kāṭṭuka.
	1.	pūṉi, nāy, āṭu, māṉ, kiḷi
	2.	taliyaṇi, nāṟkāli, peñcu, mukkāli, mēci
	3.	mocci, tuvari, payaṟu, uḻuntu, vāḻi
	4.	kākki, kuruvi, nari, mayil, kōḻi
	5.	aṭuppu, akappi, karaṇṭi, kiṇṇam, puttakam
6. oppuc cōtaṉikaḷ: koṭutta oru poruḷ etaṉi ottuḷḷatu eṉṟu kāṭṭac ceytal.  avvāṟu kūṟuvatil caṟṟu māṇākkarkaṭku utaviyāka irukkumpaṭi iḵtu ati ottiruppatupōl eṉṟu ōr eṭuttuk kāṭṭik kūṟip piṉ koṭuttuḷḷa poruḷ eti ottuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟumpaṭi ēvavēṇṭum.
(e-ṭu) kīḻkkāṇum vākkiyaṅkaḷil kōṭiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷi ēṟṟa coṟkaḷāl nirappuka.
	1.	cantiraṉ pūmiyic cuṟṟuvatupōla pūmi _____——— cuṟṟukiṉṟatu.
	2.	eṉpilataṉi veyil pōlak kāyumē _____ aṟam.
	3.	irāmaṉi ilakkuvaṉ piriyātiruntatu pōlavē catturukkaṉ _____ piriyātiruntāṉ.
cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷutalum tiruttutalum: ic cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷum muṟikaḷippaṟṟi atika viḷakkam tēviyilli.  māṇākkarkaṭkuriya kuṟippukkaḷit teḷivākavum viḷakkamākavum amittu viṭṭāl iccōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷutal eḷitu. ic cōtaṉikaḷi māṇākkarkaṭkuk koṭuttu avarkaḷ viṭiyiṟukkumpoḻutu māṇākkarkaḷ viṉavum evvita aiyaṅkaḷiyum akaṟṟuvatil āciriyarkaḷ taliyiṭakkūṭātu.  ovvoru vakic cōtaṉikaḷilum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷik koṭuttu māṇākkar viṭiyiṟukkum muṟiyit teḷivākaviḷakki viṭṭāl āciriyarkaḷ utaviyi māṇākkarkaḷ nāṭār.  eṅkāvatu accup piḻikaḷiruntāl, āciriyar cōtaṉiyil kalantukoḷḷum aṉivariṉ kavaṉat tiyum īrttu appiḻitaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi aiyatti nīkkalām.
ic cōtaṉikaḷiṉ viṭikaḷit tiruttutal mikavum eḷitu. viṉākkaḷukkēṟṟa viṭikaḷi muṉṉatākavē āyattam ceytu koḷḷavēṇṭum.  ovvoru vakic cōtaṉikkum ovvoru muṟi uṇṭu.  cariyāṉa viṭikaḷi vaṇṇap peṉcilāl aṭiyāḷamiṭṭut tiruttiṉāl piṉṉāl avaṟṟik kaṟpittalil payaṉpaṭuttuvatu eḷitāka irukkum. uṟutiyāka aṟiyāmal uttēcamāka viṭiyiṟuppati oḻippataṟku upāyaṅkaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ellāvakik cōtaṉi kaḷilum uttēcamāka viṭiyiṟuppataṟku vāyppukaḷ illi cari-tavaṟu cōtaṉikaḷilum, pal viṭiyiṟ poṟukkuc cōtaṉi kaḷilum avarkaḷ uttēcamāka viṭiyiṟuttal  kūṭum.  uttēcamāka viṭiyiṟuppatit tavirppataṟku,

eṉṟa vāyppāṭṭip payaṉpaṭutti matippeṇkaḷ kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  itil S eṉpatu māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum matippeṇ; R eṉpatu cariyaṉa viṭikaḷ;  W eṉpatu tavaṟāṉa viṭikaḷ; N eṉpatu uttēca viṭikaḷ.  cari tavaṟu cōtaṉiyil ivvāypāṭum  S=R-W  eṉṟu amintu viṭukiṉṟatu.  pal
