eṉṟu amikiṉṟatu. uttēca viṭikaḷil eṇṇikki atikappaṭa atikappaṭa māṇākkarkaḷ uttēcittu viṭiyiṟuppatu kuṟivati aṟiyalām.
intac cōtaṉikaḷiṉ niṟikaḷ: putu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷil pala niṟikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟi oru ciṟitu kavaṉippōm.
1. aḷantaṟiyum elliyiṉ virivu : atikamāṉa viṉāk kaḷāl mikutiyāṉa poruḷaṟivi aḷakka muṭikiṉṟatu.  ellāp pakutikaḷilum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ poruḷaṟivit tēṟalām.
2. tiruttuvōriṉ maṉappāṉmi: viṉavappeṟum viṉākkaḷukkut tiṭṭamāṉa viṭikaḷiruppatāl tiruttu vōriṉ maṉappāṉmi allatu uḷak cuvikaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu matippeṇkaḷ kuṟivataṟkum mikuvataṟkum iṅku iṭamē illi. 
3. kālac cikkaṉam: kuṟinta kālattil māṇākkarkaḷiṉ mikunta poruḷaṟivic cōtippataṟkum, kuṟinta kālattil āciriyarkaḷ atikamāṉa viṭiyēṭukaḷit tiruttuvataṟkum ic cōtaṉikaḷ tuṇi ceykiṉṟaṉa.  kaṟpikkum pāṭap poruḷi aṟiyāta eḻuttāḷarkaḷum uyiraṟṟa poṟikaḷum avaṟṟit tirutti viṭalām.
4. māṇākkarkaḷ viṭiyaḷittal: im muṟi viṉāk kaḷukku māṇākkarkaḷ atikamāka viṭi eḻutat tēvi yilli; eḻutuvataṉāl uṭaṟkaḷippum maṉac cōrvum uṇṭākā.  kaṭṭurit cōtaṉiyil ceyvatupōl māṇākkarkaḷ tēviyaṟṟa poruḷkaḷi eḻuti  kayiṟu viṭum vaḻakkatti itil ciṟitum mēṟkoḷḷa muṭiyātu.
intac cōtaṉikaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷ: ic cōtaṉikaḷip paṟṟi āciriyarkaḷum tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷum (Critics) pala kuṟikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar.  avaṟṟuḷ mukkiya māṉavaṟṟi maṭṭilum īṇṭu kūṟuvōm.
(1) cila camayam āciriyarkaḷ ip putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷ māṇākkarkaḷ tam karuttukkaḷik kōviyākavum teḷivākavum eṭutturippataṟku vāyppukkaḷ nalkuvatilli eṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.  āṉāl, kaṭṭuric cōtaṉikaḷum ivaṟṟi nalkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu colla iyalātu.  ittakiya vāyppuk kaḷit tarum poṟuppic cōtaṉikaḷukkut tarutal eṉpatu ciṟitum poruntātu.  itaṟkeṉa vēṟu vāyppukkaḷip paḷḷi naṭimuṟiyil amittuk koḷḷalām.
(2) putu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷ takaval tarum aṟivu paṟṟiya pakutikaḷukku atika aḻuttam taruvatāka uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, cintaṉi āṟṟali vaḷarppataṟkuc ciṟitum ivaṟṟil iṭamē eṉṟum kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu.  āṉāl kaṭṭuric cōtaṉikaḷil itaṟku iṭam irukkiṉṟateṉṟu niṉittaṟkum ciṟitum iṭamē illi.  naṭimuṟiyil uḷḷa cila cōtaṉikaḷil illā viṭiṉum, kāraṇa kāriyat toṭarpu, tīrmāṉikkum āṟṟal mutaliyavaṟṟip putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷāl aḷantaṟiya muṭiyātu eṉpataṟkilli.  takka muṟiyil āyattam ceytāl ivaṟṟiṟku iṭam aḷikkalām.
(3) cila āciriyarkaḷum tiṟaṉāyvāḷarkaḷum immuṟic cōtaṉikaḷil uttēcamāka viṭiyaḷippataṟku iṭam uḷḷatu eṉṟu nampukiṉṟaṉar.  iv vakic cōtaṉikaḷiṉ vaṭivam uttēcamāka viṭiyaḷittalukku iṭam tarukiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟalāmēyaṉṟi, ataṟku ātaravu tantu tūṇṭukiṉṟatu eṉṟa colla muṭiyātu.  oru cila viṉakkaḷukku uttēcamāka viṭiyaḷittal kūṭumeṉṟālum, tiruttumpoḻutu ataṟku matippeṇkaḷ kuṟikka vaḻi vakuttiruppatāl ip paḻakkam vaḷarvataṟku atika iṭamē illi.
(4) putu muṟik cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyvatu kaṭiṉam eṉṟum, āyattam ceyvataṟku atikak kālam ākiṉṟatu eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  kaṭṭuric cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyvatu eḷiteṉiṉum tiruttuvatu mikavum kaṭiṉam.  intac cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyvatu kaṭiṉamāka iruppiṉum tiruttuvatu mika mika eḷitu.  intac cōtaṉikaḷāl ēṟpaṭum naṉmikaḷi nōkka ik kūṟu poruṭpaṭuttum aḷavukku atikamāka illi.
(5) putu muṟic cōtaṉikaḷi acciṭṭu vaḻaṅkuvataṟku atikac celavu ākiṉṟatu eṉpatu aṉupavattil aṟiyum uṇmi.  vili kuṟivākavuḷḷa tāḷip payaṉpaṭutti acciṭṭuk koḷvatit tavira vēṟu vaḻiyilli. ic cōtaṉi kaḷil cilavaṟṟic colluvati eḻutum muṟiyil (Dictation) naṭatta muṭiyumātalāl, ammuṟiyik kiyāṇṭu ciṟitu celavik kuṟikkalām.
(6) im muṟikaḷil naṉṟākap payiṟci peṟāta āciriyarkaḷ ic cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceytāl ic cōtaṉi-kaḷiṉāl ēṟpaṭak kūṭiya muḻup payaṉiyum aṭiya muṭiyum eṉṟu muṭiyātu.  ikkuṟiyic cōtaṉikaḷiṉ kuṟiyeṉṟu evvāṟu kūṟu iyalum? ellā āciriyarkaḷum payiṟci peṟṟut tammit takutiyākkik koḷḷattāṉ vēṇṭum.  itu pōṉṟa iṉṉum pala kuṟikaḷ kāṭṭappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  aviyāvum cōtaṉikaḷiṉ kuṟiyaṉṟu. 
intac cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceytal: putumuṟic cōtaṉikaḷil ovvoru vakic cōtaṉiyiyum āyattam ceyvataṟkut taṉittaṉi  vitikaḷ kaṇṭaṟiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  avaṟṟi yellām īṇṭu eṭuttukkūṟutal iyalāta toṉṟu.  avaṟṟi aḷaviyal nūlkaḷil kaṇṭu teḷika.3 āṉāl, ellāvakic cōtaṉikaḷukkum.
potuvāka vuḷḷa cila vitikaḷi maṭṭilum īṇṭu oruciṟitu ārāyvōm.
(1) cōtaṉikaḷiṉ nōkkam mutalil tiṭṭamāka variyaṟi ceyyappeṟutal vēṇṭum.  cōtaṉiyiṉ nōkkam terinta piṟakutāṉ ataṉ ēṟpuṭimi tīrmāṉikkappeṟum. aṟintuḷḷa takavalkaḷi aḷappatā, aṭintuḷḷa ārāyum āṟṟali aḷappatā,oru kuṟippiṭṭa kuṟikaḷi aḷantaṟivatā eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa nōkkaṅkaḷ mutalilēyē tiṭṭappaṭuttap peṟutal vēṇṭum.
(2) cōtaṉikaḷukkēṟṟa vivaraṅkaḷip pāṭattiṭṭaṅ-kaḷiliruntu tērnteṭukka vēṇṭum.  ituvum mēṟkuṟippiṭṭa kūṟum oru cōtaṉiyiṉ ēṟpuṭimiyiyum nampakattiyum aṟutiyiṭuvataṟku mikavum mukkiyamāṉavi.  cōtaṉikaḷiṉ nōkkattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu pāṭattiṭṭaṅ kaḷiliruntu vivaraṅkaḷit tērnteṭuppatil karuttūṉṟa vēṇṭum.  oru kuṟippiṭṭa kuṟiyaṟi cōtaṉiyi āyattam ceyyavēṇṭumāṉāl pāṭattiṭṭattil attakiya kūṟukaḷi maṭṭilum tērnteṭuppataṟkup pāṭat tiṭṭam muḻuvatum pārvi celuttap peṟutal vēṇṭum.  ivvāṟē piṟavaṟṟiṟkum koḷka.
(3) cōtaṉikaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu tērnteṭutta vivaraṅkaḷip palvēṟu cōtaṉikaḷil evvāṟu poruttuvatu eṉpatu aṭuttu aṟutiyiṭavēṇṭiya paṭiyākum.  tērnteṭutta vivaraṅkaḷi avaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu cari-tavaṟu, palviṭiyiṟ poṟukkal pōṉṟa cōtaṉikaḷil poruttic cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyyavēṇṭum.  ārāyum āṟṟalkaḷi cari-tavaṟu pōṉṟa cōtaṉikaḷāl aḷantaṟiya iyalātu. palviṭiyiṟ poṟukkal cōtaṉi iti aḷantaṟiya mikavum ēṟṟatu.  ivvāṟu nōkkattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu cōtaṉikaḷi ākkutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. 
(4) piṟaku avaṟṟi eḷiya teḷivāṉa moḻiyil amittal vēṇṭum.  kūṭiyavari kaṭiṉac coṟkaḷi vilakkavēṇṭum.  kiyāḷum naṭiyilō moḻiyilō evvita aiyamum iruttal ākātu.  pāṭap puttakaṅkaḷiluḷḷa vākkiyaṅkaḷiyō coṟṟoṭar kaḷiyō ciṟitum payaṉpaṭuttal kūṭātu.
(5) cōtaṉikaḷiṉ nīḷamum variyaṟi ceyyap peṟutal vēṇṭum.  cōtaṉikaḷiṉ nampakam avaṟṟiṉ nīḷattip poṟuttatu.  cōtaṉikaḷiṉ nīḷam avaṟṟiṉ nōkkattip poṟuttatu.  poruḷaṟivip paranta niliyil tēṟa vēṇṭu māṉāl cōtaṉikaḷ caṟṟu nīḷamākavē amital vēṇṭum; ovvoru pakutiyilum mukkiyamāṉa vivaraṅkaḷit tēṟuvatāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  cōtaṉikaḷiṉ nōkkam kuṟikaḷi aṟivatāka iruntāl, avi curuṅkiya vaṭivilamintāl pōtumāṉatu.  atu pōlavē, cila nāṭkaḷ kaṟṟa pāṭa aṟivi aḷakkum poḻutum kuṟukiya cōtaṉiyik kiyāṇṭāl pōtumāṉatu.
(6) cōtaṉikaḷiṉ viṉākkaḷi nallatōr oḻuṅkil amittal mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu. toṭakkattil eḷiya viṉākkaḷum paṭippaṭiyākak kaṭiṉa viṉākkaḷum amital vēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu amippatu eḷitāṉa ceyal aṉṟu.  oru cōtaṉiyip pala taṭavikaḷil kiyāḷum poḻutu māṇākkarkaḷ viṭiyiṟuppati uṟṟu nōkki intak kūṟiṉi aṟutiyiṭa vēṇṭum.  aṉṟiyum, viṉākkaḷi amikkum oḻuṅkumuṟi viṭikaḷik kuṟippākap pulappaṭut tuvatāka iruttal kūṭātu; oru viṉā piṟitoru viṉāviṉ viṭiyi aṟivittal kūṭātu.  payaṉaṟṟa vivaraṅkaḷic cōtaṉikaḷil amittalum ākātu.
7. aṟitiṟaṉāl viṭiyiṟukkakkūṭiya viṉākkaḷi aṭivuc cōtaṉikaḷil amittal ākātu.  aṉṟiyum, veḷip paṭiyākat teriyum viṭikaḷik koṇṭa vivaraṅkaḷiyum, kuṟippāṟ pulaṉākakkūṭiya vivaraṅkaḷiyum cōtaṉikaḷil cērttal kūṭātu.
8. cōtaṉikaḷ āyattamākumpoḻutē avaṟṟiṉ viṭikaḷiyum āyattam ceytu viṭutal vēṇṭum.  cōtaṉi kaḷi mutalil āyattam ceytuviṭṭup piṟaku avaṟṟiṉ viṭikaḷi aṟutiyiṭac ceyyum muṟi virumpattakkataṉṟu.  avvappoḻutē viṭikaḷi aṟutiyiṭṭāl cōtaṉikaḷiṉ viṉākkaḷum cariyāṉa muṟiyil amital kūṭum.
9. cōtaṉi nalla muṟiyil acciṭṭu amippatum avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappukkup peruntuṇi puritalkūṭum.  nērttiyāṉa tāḷ, nalla eḻuttukkaḷ, viṉākkaḷi amikkum oḻuṅkumuṟi pōṉṟa vivaraṅkaḷ naṉṟākak kavaṉikkap peṟutal vēṇṭum.  kūṭiyavari viṭittāḷiṉ valappakkat tilēyē māṇākkarkaḷiṉ viṭikku iṭam amittal vēṇṭum.  ovvoru vakic cōtaṉikkum kuṟippukkaḷ viḷakkamākavum teḷivākavum karuttuṭaṉ āyattam ceyyappeṟṟuc cōtaṉikaḷiṉ toṭak kattil teḷivāka irukkumāṟu amikkavēṇṭum.
10. cōtaṉikaḷit tiruttuvatil payaṉpaṭak kūṭiya vitikaḷiyum cōtaṉikaḷi āyattam ceyyum poḻutē vakuttuviṭa vēṇṭum.  viṉākkaḷiṉ tarattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu matippeṇkaḷik kūṭṭiyō kuṟittō aṟutiyiṭutal cariyallaveṉṟum, ovvoru viṭikkum oru matippeṇ koṭuttalē cariyeṉṟum aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.
nalla cōtaṉikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam
entac cōtaṉikaḷāka iruppiṉum avaṟṟik kiyāḷu vataṟku muṉ nalla cōtaṉikaḷiṉ ellā ilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṟkup poruntukiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu pārttaṟital vēṇṭum.  kīḻkkāṇum paṇpukaḷi nalla cōtaṉikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇamākak koḷḷalām.  cōtaṉikaḷi ākkuvōrum avaṟṟik kiyāḷuvōrum ivvilakkaṇatti aṟintiruttal iṉṟiya miyātatu.
ēṟpuṭimi (Validity): oru nalla cōtaṉiyil amiya vēṇṭiya ciṟappiyalpu ēṟpuṭimi eṉpatu.  etaṉi ārāya vēṇṭumō ataṉit tiṟamiyāka ārāyum takutiyum āṟṟalum vāyntatāka antaccōtaṉi viḷaṅkavēṇṭum.  oru cōtaṉi yāl māṇākkarkaḷiṉ eḻuttilakkaṇa aṟivip paṟṟi ārāya virumpiṉāl, antac cōtaṉiyiṉ viṉākkaḷ teḷivāka amital vēṇṭum; viṉākkaḷiṉ moḻi eḷitākavum teḷivākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.  viṉākkaḷil mēṟ koṇṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷē viḷaṅkātiruntāl.  atic cariyāṉa cōtaṉi eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.  tavira, atu coṟkaḷañciyatti ārāyum cōtaṉiyākavum amital kūṭum.  ēṟpuṭimi amiyāta oru cōtaṉiyik koṇṭu māṇākkarkaḷiṉ oru kuṟippiṭṭa aṟivut tuṟiyi āytal niyāmaṟṟa ceyalākum.  ōru cōtaṉi cariyāṉa ēṟpuṭi miyuṭiyatāka irukka vēṇṭumāṉāl, atu kalvit tiṭṭattuṭaṉ naṉku poruntavēṇṭum.  uyar nilip paḷḷikaḷil kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷaṉṟip piṟarāl āyattam ceyyappeṟum cōtaṉikaḷ cariyāṉa muṟiyiliruppatilli.  uyarnilip paḷḷi vakuppukkaḷukkuriya kalvit tiṭṭamē cariyāṉa muṟiyil tarappaṭuttap peṟavilli.  aracāṅkam oru kuṟippiṭṭa pāṭanūlik koṇṭu pāṭam kaṟpikkappeṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu variyaṟi ceytālum, kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷuk kēṟṟavāṟu atu māṟupaṭukiṉṟatu.  eṉavē, kaṟpittaluk kēṟṟavāṟu cōtaṉiyum amitalvēṇṭum eṉpatu peṟap paṭukiṉṟatu itittavira, iṉṉoru piracciṉiyum eḻa iṭam uṇṭu pāṭattiṭṭattiṉ nōkkattip poṟuttum, aṟivu niliyil oru kūṟiṉic cōtippatip poṟuttum cōtaṉi māṟupaṭum.  pāṭattiṭṭattiṉ nōkkam cuvi yuṇar āṟṟali vaḷarkkavēṇṭum eṉpatāka irukkumpoḻutu, ceytikaḷ paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷi aḷappatākac cōtaṉi amintāl ac cōtaṉi ēṟpuṭimi peṟavilli eṉṟutāṉ koḷḷa vēṇṭum.  cuvi yuṇar āṟṟal peṟuvataṟkuc ceytipaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷum tēvitāṉ; cōtaṉi kalvi kaṟṟalāl peṟṟa maṉappāṉmiyattāṉ mutalil cōtikka vēṇṭum.  eṉavē, ēṟpuṭimi eṉpatu oru tiṭṭamāṉa paṇpēyaṉṟip potup paṇpu aṉṟu eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
liṉṭkuṭu eṉṟa aṟiñar oru cōtaṉiyiṉ ēṟpuṭi miyik kuṟittu tamatu nūlil ivvāṟu viḷakkukiṉṟār:4 amerikka aikkiya nāṭukaḷiṉ varalāṟṟil uyarnilip paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷiṉ potu aṟivi ārāyum uyarnta ēṟpuṭimiyip peṟṟuḷḷa cōtaṉiyi atē pāṭattil kallūri māṇākkarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟu aṟivi ārāyum poḻutum, uyarnilip paḷḷi māṇākkarkaḷiṉ amerikka aikkiya nāṭukaḷiṉ poruḷātāra varalāṟupaṟṟiya aṟivi ārāyumpoḻutum, etir kālattil uyarnilip paḷḷiyil āṅkila nāṭṭu varalāṟṟup pāṭatti nuḻittāl peṟum veṟṟiyiṉ aḷavi ārāyumpoḻutum, aikkiya nāṭṭu varalāṟu paṟṟiya aṟiviṉ kuṟipāṭukaḷi ārāyumpoḻutum, iṟutiyāka atikkoṇṭu kivēlip payiṟcik kalvikku matippeṇkaḷ tarumpoḻutum, paṭippaṭiyāka ēṟpuṭimiyil kuṟintu varuvati eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟār.  oru kuṟippiṭṭa niliyil payaṉpaṭumpoḻutu kāṇum oru cōtaṉiyiṉ ēṟpuṭimiyi atu piṟitorucamayam payaṉpaṭuttum poḻutu kāṇṭal aritu.  atu pōlavē, oru kuṟippiṭṭa māṇākkar kuḻuviṟkup payaṉpaṭumpoḻutu peṟṟuḷḷa ēṟpuṭimiyip piṟitoru kuḻuviṟkup payaṉpaṭum poḻutum kāṇal iyalātu.  eṉavē, oru cōtaṉi oru kuṟippiṭṭa niliyil payaṉpaṭum poḻutum oru kuṟippiṭṭa aṟi tiṟaṉuḷḷa māṇākkaḷiṭam payaṉpaṭum poḻutumtāṉ oru tiṭṭamāṉa ēṟpuṭimiyip peṟṟirukkum eṉṟu collavēṇṭum. 
cōtaṉi ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭu uḻitta aṟiñarkaḷ kalvit tiṭṭampaṟṟiya ēṟpuṭimi, puḷḷi vivara ēṟpuṭimi, uḷaviyal paṟṟiyum kāraṇa kāriya muṟi paṟṟiyumuḷḷa ēṟpuṭimi ākiya mūvita ēṟpuṭimikaḷik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉar.5  avaṟṟuḷ kalvit tiṭṭam paṟṟiya ēṟpuṭimiyē mikavum mukkiyamāṉa teṉṟum avarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.  avipaṟṟiya muḻu vivaraṅkaḷi an nūlil kaṇṭukoḷka.
2. nampakam (Reliability) : oru nalla cōtiṉiyiṉ aṭutta ciṟappiyalpu nampakam eṉappaṭuvatu. eti aḷantaṟiya vēṇṭumō ati mikavum tiṟamiyāka aḷantaṟivati nampakam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvar. oru kuṟippiṭṭa cōtaṉiyi orē kūṭṭattiṉari ṭiyil iṭiyiṭṭu iṭiyiṭṭup paṉmuṟip payaṉpaṭutti ṉālum orē vitamāṉa muṭivukaḷittāṉ kāṇalkūṭum. intac cōtaṉiyi muḻutum payaṉpaṭuttiṉālum, pātiyip payaṉpaṭuttiṉālum, cōtaṉiyil varum oṟṟippaṭi eṇṇuḷḷa viṉākkaḷi oru muṟiyum iraṭṭippaṭi eṇṇuḷḷa viṉākkaḷip piṟitoru muṟiyumākap payaṉ paṭuttiṉālum nampakamuṟṟa muṭivukaḷittāṉ kāṇamuṭiyum. ittakiya cōtaṉiyil tāṉ nampakam eṉṟa kūṟu amintuḷḷatu eṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. orumuṟi vakuppil ilakkaṇattērvu oṉṟu naṭattiya āciriyar vakuppil mūvarē tēṟiyatu kaṇṭu, mūṉṟu nāṉku nāṭkaḷ kaḻittu naṭattiya tērvil vēṟōru mūvar tērak kāṇkiṉṟa kāṭciyi entap paḷḷiyilum iṉṟu kāṇalām. kaṇakkut tērvil itu aṭikkaṭi nēriṭum nikaḻcciyākum.  intac cōtaṉikaḷ muṉṉukkup piṉ muraṇāṉa muṭivukaḷit tarukiṉṟaṉa.  nēṟṟu aṟivāḷi, iṉṟu muṭṭāḷ eṉpatu viyattaku muṭivaṉṟō? oru nalla tērvil immuraṇpaṭāp paṇpi atāvatu nampakamaṟṟa kūṟiṉik kāṇa muṭiyātu.
3. iyalumi (Adequacy): kalviyāl peṟakkūṭiya viṉiyāṇmi, tiṟami, maṉappāṉmi mutaliya ellāp paṇpukaḷiyum iṉṟu naṭimuṟiyiluḷḷa cōtaṉikaḷāl aḷantaṟital muṭiyātu.  aṉṟiyum, avi yāvaṟṟiyum aḷantaṟivatil eṭukkum kālamum muyaṟciyum vīṇē.  eṉṟālum, piṟatuṟikaḷil kiyāḷappeṟum cila muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟic cōtaṉiyi āyattam ceytāl atu naṟpayaṉi viḷivittal kūṭum.  ōru kūla vāṇikaṉ oru vaṇṭi nelli vāṅkum poḻutu eṉṉa ceykiṉṟāṉ? anta vaṇṭiyiluḷḷa ellā mūṭṭikaḷilum ciṟitaḷavu eṭuttu avaṟṟit tarappaṭi, vittup pārkkiṉṟāṉaṉṟō? atupōlavē, cōtaṉi āyattam ceyvōrum māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kalviyaṟivu niliyi aḷantu aṟivataṟku avarkaḷ payilum paranta poruḷ elliyil palvēṟu poruḷkaḷit tērnteṭuttu avaṟṟil viṉākkaḷi ākkavēṇṭum.  inta viṉākkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷa cōtaṉiyāl peṟum matippeṇkaḷik koṇṭu tāṉ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kalviyaṟiviṉi aṟutiyiṭal vēṇṭum.  māṇākkarkaḷ payiṉṟa poruḷiṉ ellip parappil evvaḷavutūram virivākap poruḷkaḷit tērnteṭuttu viṉākkaḷ ākkap peṟu kiṉṟaṉavō anta elliyit tāṉ iyalumi eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉar.  tēṟappeṟum poruṭparappiṉ viriviṟ kēṟṟavāṟu iyalumiyum atikappaṭum.  tēṟappaṭum poruṭ parappu virintiruntāl māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum matippeṇkaḷum cariyāṉaviyāka irukkum.  tēṟappaṭum pakuti ciṟitāka iruntāl cila māṇākkarkaṭku atu niyāyamaṟṟatāka irukkum; appakuti mikavum virintiruntāl, ellā māṇākkarkaṭkum atu niyāya māṉatāka irukka iṭam uṇṭu.  eṉavē, cōtaṉi cariyāṉatāka irukkavēṇṭumāṉāl tēṟappaṭum pakuti parantirukkavēṇṭum eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  ākavē, cōtaṉiyiṉ nampakattiṟku iyalumi iṉṟiyamiyāta tākiṉṟatu.  iyalumiyi nampakat tiṉ oru kūṟākavum koḷḷalām.
4. puṟavayam: (Objectivity): oru cōtaṉi ciṟantateṉa viḷaṅkavēṇṭumāṉāl atip palar kiyāḷum poḻutum orēvita muṭivip peṟuvatāka amital vēṇṭum.  kiyāḷu vōriṉ maṉappāṉmikkēṟṟavāṟu māṇākkarkaḷ peṟum matippeṇkaḷ māṟakkūṭātu.  cōtaṉiyik kiyāḷu vōriṉa karuttu, maṉanili, oru taliccārpu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu māṟāniliyuḷḷa cōtaṉiyip puṟavayamuṭiyatākak koḷḷalām.  putu muṟi viṉākkaḷ yāvum ōraḷavu ittaṉmi yuṭiyaṉavākavuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu koḷḷalām.  ivvāṟu puṟap poruḷi vēṟupirittut taṉṉōkkilum vēṟākac cōtaṉikaḷ amital vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇamē taramākkiya cōtaṉikaḷik (Standardised tests) tōṟṟuvittatu.  oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu tiṭṭamāṉa viṭikaḷ varumāṟu viṉākkaḷi amittu avaṟṟiṉ viṭikaḷi yum variyaṟi ceytuviṭṭāl, cōtaṉikaḷ puṟavaya muṭiyaṉavāka ilaṅkum.
5. kiyāḷuvatil eḷimi: cōtaṉikaḷ eḷitāka vaḻaṅkak kūṭiyaṉavākavum, eḷitākat tirumpa vāṅkak kūṭiyaṉavākavum, eḷitākat tiruttak kūṭiyaṉavākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.  oru nalla cōtaṉi im muppaṇpukaḷiyum peṟṟirukkum.  kaṟpittalil collik koṭuttal mukkiya mēyaṉṟi.  tērvu naṭattutal aṉṟu.  āciriyar tērvukku mutaliṭantantu kalviyiṉ nōkkattiyē citittuviṭal kūṭātu.  tērvukaḷippaṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ eḷitākavum teḷivākavum nērākavum amintu avaṟṟi āyattam ceyvatum eḷitāka amintāl mika naṉṟu.  taramākkiya cōtaṉikaḷil ip paṇpukaḷik kāṇalām.  perumpālum tērvukaḷi āyattam ceytal, avaṟṟit takka muṟiyil acciṭal ākiyavaṟṟiltāṉ caṅkaṭaṅkaḷ nēriṭukiṉṟaṉa.  teḷivāṉa kuṟippukkaḷ, nallatāḷ, teḷivāṉa accu, tāḷiṉ aḷavu , payaṉpaṭuttum acceḻuttukkaḷ ākiyavi cōtaṉi kaḷik kiyāḷum eḷimiyi aḷavu paṭuttakkūṭum.
6. tiruttuvatu: cōtaṉikaḷ avaṟṟiṉ mukkiyattuvattiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu eḷitākavum virivākavum cātāraṇa muṟiyilum tiruttak kūṭiyaṉavāka iruttalvēṇṭum.  cōtaṉikaḷiṉ poruḷaṟiyātavarkaḷum eḻuttāḷar paṇiyiluḷḷavarkaḷum (Clerical workers) avaṟṟic cariyāṉa muṟiyil tiruttak kūṭiyaviyāka amitalvēṇṭum.  eḷitākat tiruttuvataṟku iṉṟu pala muṟikaḷik kaṇṭaṟintuḷḷaṉar.  avi taramākkiya cōtaṉikaḷik kiyāḷuṅkāl mēṟ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  āyattam ceyyappaṭṭa viṭikaḷik kiyāl tiruttuvataṟ kēṟṟavāṟu amittal, poṟiyāl tiruttuvataṟ kēṟṟavāṟu amittal ākiyavi avaṟṟuḷ orucila.
7. oppiṭat takum paṇpu (Comparability) : cōtaṉikaḷiṉ muṭivukaḷi oppiṭṭup pārttal mikavum mukkiyamāṉatu.  cōtaṉikaḷ oppiṭattakum kūṟukaḷip peṟṟirā viṭṭāl, avaṟṟāl peṟum mukkiya palaṉkaḷi iḻattal  kūṭum.  taramākkiya cōtaṉikaḷil ivvoppiṭattakum paṇpu iraṇṭu muṟikaḷil niliniṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu.  avi: (i) cōtaṉikaḷiṉ iraṭṭippaṭi kiṭittal; (ii) tēviyāṉa potunili aḷavikaḷ kiṭittal.  tarappaṭuttiya cōtaṉikaḷ paṟṟiya kiyēṭukaḷil (Manual) vayatu, vakuppunili mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kēṟṟavāṟu payaṉpaṭuttum muṟiyil tēviyāṉa potunili aḷavikaḷkoṇṭa aṭṭavaṇikaḷ aṭaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum.  ittakiya potunili aḷavikaḷikkoṇṭu taṉippaṭṭa māṇākkariṉ niliyiṉiyum, vakuppukkaḷiṉ niliyiṉiyum, atē vayatuṭiya māṇākkarkaḷiṉ carācari niliyuṭaṉum, vakuppuṭaṉum, atē pāṭattiṭṭattip payilvōruṭaṉum oppiṭṭup pārkkalām.  oru cōtaṉiyiṉ iraṭṭip paṭiyikkoṇṭu kaṟpittalukku muṉṉum piṉṉum uḷḷa nilikaḷi ac cōtaṉiyi irumuṟi kiyāḷāmalēyē oppiṭṭup pārkkavum iyalum.
taramākkiya cōtaṉikaḷukku maṭṭilum potunili aḷavikaḷum iraṭṭippaṭikaḷum kiṭitta pōtilum, eḷiya puḷḷivivara muṟikaḷik (Statistical procedures) kiyāṇṭu cātāraṇap puṟavayac cōtaṉikaḷiṉ muṭivukaḷiyum oppiṭalām.
8. cikkaṉam (Economy) : nalla cōtaṉiyiṉ mukkiya paṇpu eṉṟu koḷvataṟkilli.  āṉāl, ap paṇpi otukki viṭuvataṟkumilli.  maṉam pōṉavāṟu malivāṉa cōtaṉi
kaḷiyum muṟikaḷiyum kiyāṇṭu āytalil uṇmiyāṉa cikkaṉatti aṭiya muṭiyātu; āṉāl, viliyuyarvuḷḷa aḷavuk karuvikaḷum muṟikaḷum nallavi eṉṟum kūṟuvataṟ
killi.  orukāl nīṇṭa kāla elliyil cōtaṉiyiṉ celavic cōtaṉikaḷiṉ ēṟpuṭimiyi yoṭṭi ivvaḷavu eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭutal kūṭum.  aḷavu muṟiyiṉ tiṟaṉ kuṟiyāmalēyē  cōtaṉiyiṉ celaviṉaṅ kaḷik kuṟippataṟkup pala upāyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  cātāraṇamākac cila cōtaṉikaḷip paṭiyeṭukkum poṟiyik koṇṭu āyattam ceyyalām; cila vaṟṟik karumpalakiyil eḻutiyō, allatu vāymoḻi
yākavō koṭukkalām.  pala paḷḷikaḷ oṉṟu kūṭicōtaṉikaḷi  āyattam ceytu celaviṉaṅkaḷik kuṟikkalām.
9. payaṉ (Utility) : mēṟkūṟappeṟṟa ellā naṟpaṇpukaḷik koṇṭiruntālum cōtaṉi oru kuṟippiṭṭa cantarppattiṟkuc ciṟitum payaṉpaṭātu pōtalum kūṭum.  oru kuṟippiṭṭa cantarppattil tiruptikaramāṉa muṟiyil oru tiṭṭamāṉa tēviyi niṟivu ceytāltāṉ atu nalla muṟiyil payaṉ aḷikkiṉṟatu eṉṟu koḷḷalām. im muṟiyil tērnteṭukkap peṟāta allatu āyattam ceyyap peṟāta cōtaṉikaḷiṉāl payaṉ viḷiyātu pōvatuṭaṉ tīṅkukaḷum nēriṭakkūṭum.  yātoru nōkkamumiṉṟic cōtaṉikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttutal kūṭātu.  putiya muṟikaḷil payiṟcipeṟṟa āciriyarkaḷ oru tiṭṭamāṉa nōkkattuṭaṉtāṉ māṇākkarkaḷic cōtaṉi ceyvar; cōtaṉiyiṉ muṭivukaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ nalaṉukkup payaṉ paṭuttappeṟum.
taramākkiya cōtaṉiyāka iruntāl, muṭivukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷ kuṟippēṭukaḷil teḷivāka mēṟ kōḷuṭaṉ viḷakkap paṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum.  muṭivukaḷik koṇṭu etirkālattil eṉṉa ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu tiṭṭamāka variyaṟi ceyyappeṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum; vakuppup pativēṭukaḷum matippeṇ paṭṭikaḷum cōtaṉikaḷip payaṉu ṭiyaṉavāyc ceykiṉṟaṉa.  āciriyarē āyattam ceyyum cōtaṉikaḷāka iruntāl avaṟṟiṉ payaṉ cōtaṉikaḷi variyaṟi ceyvatil āciriyariṉ muṉyōcaṉiyip poṟuttuḷḷatu; cōtaṉikaḷiṉ muṭivukaḷ vakuppukaḷiṉ tēvikaḷi niṟivu ceyyum niliyilirukkum.  eṉavē, cōtaṉiyiṉ payaṉiyum ataṉ ciṟanta paṇpākak karututal vēṇṭum.  ēṉiya paṇpukaḷippōl itu teḷivāka irāviṭiṉum, itic cōtaṉiyiṉ payaṉi variyaṟi ceyyum aḻuttamāṉa iṟutik kūṟākak koḷḷalām.
tāymoḻi āciriyarkaḷ
ācāṉiṉ ilakkaṇam āciriyarkaḷippaṟṟip potuvāka āṉṟōr kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷavi aṉittum tāymoḻi āciriyarukkum poruntum.  uyarnilip paḷḷi, mēlnilip paḷḷikaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyil āciriyarkaḷtām mikavum cel vākkuḷḷa kūṟu eṉṟu karututal vēṇṭum nallāciriyariṉ iyalpi naṉṉūl,
	kuvaṉaruḷ teyvam koḷki mēṉmi
kalipayil teḷivu kaṭṭuri vaṉmi
nilammavi niṟikōl malarnikar māṭciyum
ulakiyal aṟivōṭu uṇarkuṇam iṉiyavum
amipavaṉ nūluri yāciri yaṉṉē1
eṉṟa kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.  nallāciriyari nilam, mali, tulākkōl, malar ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ oppiṭṭa kāraṇattiyum viḷakkukiṉṟār āciriyar.2  kaṟpikkum muṟikaḷik kūṟum mēṉāṭṭu nūlkaḷum nallāciriyariṉ taṉmikaḷi viritturikkiṉṟaṉa.3 ik karuttukaḷ yāvaṟṟiyum moriyāciriyakaḷ cintittu uṇaravēṇṭiyatu mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  iṉi, moḻiyāciriyarkaḷiṉ paṇi ciṟappataṟku avartam kalviyaṟivu, payiṟciyaṟivu, moḻiyaṟivu, avarkaḷiṭam amiya vēṇṭiya tiṟaṉkaḷ, avarkaḷ āyattam ceyya vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ mutaliyavipaṟṟiya iṉṟiyamiyāta cila kuṟippukaḷ īṇṭut tarappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.
cila paḷḷiyiluḷḷa tamiḻp pāṭattiṭṭam nalla muṟiyil ceyaṟpaṭuvatu tamiḻāciriyarkaḷip poṟuttatu.  eṉavē paṭippu, payiṟci, nalla moḻi-ilakkiyap pulami, uṟcākam mutaliya takutikaḷ peṟṟa āciriyarkaḷit tērnteṭuttal vēṇṭum.
ācāṉiṉ kalviyaṟivu: muṉ talimuṟiyil paḷḷikaḷ atikamāka illi; kuṟivākavuḷḷa  ippaḷḷikaḷil poṟuppum pulamiyum tiyāka uṇarvum mikka tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ iruntaṉar.  moḻi kaṟpikkum nōkkaṅkaḷum iṉṟu iruppaṉapōl aṉṟu illi.  ākavē, avarkaḷ ētō nalla muṟiyil paṇiyāṟṟi vantaṉar.  nalla peruṭaṉum pukaḻuṭaṉum tikaḻntaṉar.  iṉṟiya niliyil eṇṇaṟṟa paḷḷikaḷ, kallūrikaḷ, ittaṉi niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷukkum nallāciriyarkaḷi niyamaṉam ceyvateṉpatu ilēcāṉa ceyalā? eppaṭiyāvatu āciriyarkaḷi niyamittāka vēṇṭum.
pulavar  paṭippiṟkō mutukalip paṭippiṟkō paṭittatuṭaṉ niṉṟuviṭātu.  ‘v‹ xUt‹ rhªJizí« fšyhjthW?4 eṉṟa vaḷḷuvar perumāṉ kuṟippit tali mēṟkoṇṭu iyaṉṟavari tamiḻ ilakkiyap parappiyellām ōraḷavu mēṟtal vēṇṭum.  kaṟṟaṉittu ūṟum aṟivu5 eṉṟallavō vaḷḷuvar peruntaki kūṟic ceṉṟār? avviyārum,
	nīraḷavē yākumār nīrāmpal tāṉkaṟṟa
nūlaḷavē yākumām nuṇṇaṟivu6
eṉṟu kūṟiyuṟṟati īṇṭuc cintittal takum.
payiṟci : oru cilariṭam āciriyarkaḷiṭam amiya vēṇṭiya paṇpukaḷ, tiṟaṉkaḷ yāvum piṟappilēyē amintuḷḷaṉa.  iṅṅaṉam karuvilē tiruvuṭiyār ettaṉi pēr irukkamuṭiyum? eṉavē, takka cūḻniliyil perumpālōrukku nallatoru payiṟciyi aḷittu avarkaḷic ciṟanta āciriyarkaḷākka vēṇṭum.  maḻuṅkiya kattiyiyum cāṇiyāl kūriyatākka muṭiyumallavā? katti, uyarnta raka eḵkiṉāl ceyyappeṟṟāl maṭṭilum pōtātu; kūriyatākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.  naṉku kaṟṟu mutal vakuppil tērcci peṟṟavarkaḷ yāvarumē ciṟanta āciriyarkaḷākat tikaḻa muṭiyātu.  kaṟṟal vēṟu; kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉ vēṟu.  kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉip payiṟcik kallūrikaḷtām āciriyarkaḷiṭam amiyac ceytal vēṇṭum.  kuṟikkōḷkaḷ illāta payiṟcik kallūrikaḷāl yātu payaṉ? payiṟcik kallūrikaḷil payilum moḻiyāciriyarkaḷiṭam tamiḻ maṉappāṉmiyi uṇṭākki ati naṉku vaḷarkka vēṇṭum.  payilvōrkaḷiṉ maṉa niliyi aṟintu kaṟpittal vēṇṭum eṉpati naṉku vaṟpuṟuttutal vēṇṭum.  kaṟpittal tuṟiyil uṟcākatti yūṭṭik kiḷarnteḻac ceytal vēṇṭum.  āciriyar eṉṟum māṇākkaṉākavē iruntu putiya ceytikaḷi aṟinta niliyiliruttal vēṇṭum.  kalvi kariyila.  atuvum, nūlkaḷ palkip peruki varum iṉṟiya niliyil kaṟṟalukku elli ētu?  kaṇṭatu kaṟkap paṇṭita ṉāvāṉ eṉpatu mutumoḻiyaṉṟō?
moḻiyaṟivum moḻippaṟṟum:  iṉṟu tāymoḻiyil ellāp pāṭaṅkaḷum kaṟpikkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa.  itaṉāl collum tiṟami (Expressive ability) tamiḻ moḻikku illi eṉṟa kuṟṟam tamiḻ moḻiyic cārāmal kākka vēṇṭum.  tamiḻ aṟivit tavira piṟa tuṟi aṟiviyum peṟa tamiḻāciriyarkaḷ muṉital vēṇṭum; avvaṟivip peṟa avāvutal vēṇṭum.  tamiḻil potup paṭippukkeṉa (General reading) pala tuṟikaḷil pala nūlkaḷ veḷivanta vaṇṇamuḷḷaṉa.  ivaṟṟip paṭittup purintu tam aṟivuc celvattip perukkik koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.  iṉṟu tāymoḻi, paḷḷiyil kaṟpikkappeṟum pāṭam eṉṟu maṭṭilum koḷḷa iṭam illi.  vāḻvē tāymoḻiyā uḷḷatu eṉpati moḻiyā ciriyarkaḷ uṇartal vēṇṭum.
	payiṟṟip palakalvi tantu - intap
  pāri uyarttiṭa vēṇṭum?
eṉṟa pāratiyāriṉ vākkuppaṭi piṟa kalikaḷiyum tamiḻil payiṉṟu tamiḻi vaḷarkka vēṇṭum.  mēli nāṭṭil vaḷarum pañca pūtac ceyalkaḷiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷ kūṟum puttam putiya kalikaḷit tamiḻukku vantu cērutal vaḻikaḷil ellām tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṉ perumpaṅku iruttal vēṇṭum.
	piṟanāṭṭu nallaṟiñar cāttiraṅkaḷ
	tamiḻpmoḻiyil peyarttal vēṇṭum;
iṟavāta pukaḻuṭiya putunūlkaḷ
	tamiḻmoḻiyil iyaṟṟal vēṇṭum7
eṉṟa pāratiyāriṉ nōkkattik - kuṟikkōḷic ceyaṟ paṭuttutal vēṇṭum.  itutāṉ uṇmit tamiḻp paṟṟāṉariṉ viḻumiya tamiḻt toṇṭākum.
	ceṉṟiṭuvīr eṭṭut tikkum - kalic
celvaṅkaḷ yāvum koṇarntiṅkuc cērppīr8
eṉṟa tamiḻttāyiṉ aṟivuriyip piṉpaṟṟal vēṇṭum.  iṅṅakaṉam pala tuṟikaḷ pāṅkuṭaṉ tamiḻ moḻiyil palkip perukivarum veḷināṭṭuc celvam vīṭṭuc celvatti viḷaṅkac ceytalvēṇṭum.
	tamiḻuyarntāl tamiḻnāṭu tāṉuyarum
aṟivuyarum aṟamum ōṅkum
imayamali pōluyarnta orunāṭum
taṉmoḻiyil tāḻntāl vīḻum9
eṉṟa pāvēntariṉ eccarikkip poṉmoḻiyi moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ eṇṇip pārttal vēṇṭum.
uṟcākam :  tamiḻāciriyarkaḷiṭam irukka vēṇṭiya paṇpukaḷuḷ taliciṟantatu uṟcākamākum.  uṟcākam irukka vēṇṭumāṉāl tām mēṟkoṇṭa paṇiyil nampikki iruttal vēṇṭum.  piḻippiṟkāka mēṟkoṇṭa tuṟiyē yāyiṉum (ellāt tuṟikaḷum ippaṭip paṭṭaviyē; āyiṉum ittuṟi tiyāka uṇarvuṭaṉ kūṭiyatu), nampikkiyuṭaṉ paṇiyāṟṟiṉāl atu ciṟakkum. nammiṭam payila vantuḷḷa kuḻantikaḷ teyva uruvaṅkaḷ eṉṟa eṇṇam vēṇṭum; aṟivu peṟukikkākavē tammiṭam avarkaḷ vantuḷḷaṉar eṉṟa cintaṉi vēṇṭum.  kuḻantikaḷ yāvarum tam viruppappaṭi iyakkavalla navarāttirik kolup pommikaḷ eṉṟa tavaṟāṉa eṇṇam iruntāl ati aṭiyōṭu akaṟṟiviṭal vēṇṭum.  ālayattiṟkuc ceṉṟu āṇṭavaṉi vaḻipaṭu vataṟkup pakti mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu pōla, vakuppaṟikkuc ceṉṟu kaṟpippataṟku uṟcākam mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu.  āciriyarkaḷ tammik kītācāriyaṉ niliyil vittuc cintittāl tam peruṅkaṭamiyit teḷivāka uṇaralām.
uṟcākatti tavaṟāṉa muṟiyil koṇṭuceluttutal ākātu; māṇākkarkaḷiṉ vayatu nilikaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu avarkaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟiviṟ keṭṭiya poruḷkaḷi, vāḻkkiyuṭaṉ oṭṭiya ceytikaḷāka amittuk kaṟpittal moḻiyā ciriya kaḷiṭam amintirukka vēṇṭiya payiṟṟum paṇpākum.  kuruvi taliyil paṉaṅkāyik kaṭṭiyatu pōlap piñcu uḷḷaṅkaṭku ovvāta ceytikaḷit tiṇippatu kaṟpikkum āciriyarkaḷiṭa mēyaṉṟi, payiṟṟappeṟum pāṭattiṉ mītum veṟuppu ēṟpaṭum eṉpatu iyalpu eṉpati āciriyarkaḷ uṇartal vēṇṭum.
	koḷvōṉ koḷvaki
  aṟintavaṉ uḷaṅkoḷak
  kōṭṭayil maṉattiṉ
  nūl koṭuttal eṉpa10
eṉṟa pavaṇantiyāriṉ aṟivurippaṭi oḻukutal vēṇṭum.  paruvamaṟintu ataṟkēṟṟa vakikaḷil kalvi payiṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉi,
	paruvamaṟin tataṟkiyinta kalipayiṟci
  makiḻvit tārāl.11
eṉṟu .rāmaliṅka vaḷḷal kūṟip pōntatiyum cintittup pārppatu iṉṟiyamiyātatu.
uḷaviyal aṟivu : uṭaṟkūṟṟiyalaṟivu maruttuvatti vallunarukkup payaṉpaṭutal pōla, uḷaviyalaṟivu kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriyarukkup peruntuṇiyāka irukkum.  kuḻantiyi miyamākak koṇṭa navīṉak kalvi muṟiyil kuḻantikaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷippaṟṟiya aṟivu āciriyarkaṭku iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉpatic collavum vēṇṭumō?  inta aṟivu naṉku vāykkap peṟṟāl tāṉ kuḻantikaḷiṉ vayatu nilikkēṟṟavāṟu payiṟṟum paṇpiṉi āciriyar vaḷarttuk koḷḷa iyalum.
oru vakuppil palvēṟupaṭṭacūḻalkaḷiṉiṉṟum varum māṇākkarkaḷ niṟintiruppar.  ivarkaḷ vayatu, aṟivu, maṉa nili, purintukoḷḷum āṟṟal ivaṟṟil oruvarukkoruvar māṟupaṭṭiruppar eṉpatu āciriyarkaḷ kāṇum nili, celvarkaḷ vīṭṭiṉiṉṟu varum māṇākkarkaḷ, vaṟiyar vīṭṭiṉiṉṟu varum māṇākkarkaḷ ivarkaḷ vaḷarntuvarum cūḻnilit tākkattāl palvēṟu maṉaniliyil iruppar.  ivarkaḷ uṇarvukaḷum palvēṟu vakippaṭṭirukkum.  pakaṟkaṉavu kāṇpavarkaḷum ivarkaḷiṭiyē iruppar.  uḷaviyalaṟivu naṉku vāykkap peṟṟa āciriyarkaḷtām iṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭṭē. viḷaṅkum eṇṇaṟṟa uḷḷaṅkaḷi vittukkoṇṭē, avaṟṟiyellām orumukappaṭutti kaṟṟal oṉṟiṉiyē nāṭumāṟu ceytal iyalum.  orē eṇṇak kōṭṭil niṟkumāṟu ceytal iyalum.  ivarkaḷē uṇmiyāṉa āciriyarkaḷ eṉappōṟṟal takuti peṟṟavarkaḷ.
takuvaḻi kāṇum tiṟam:  pāṭamēṟiṉum ēṭatu kiviṭēl eṉṟa mutumoḻiyi maṟavātu akaṉṟa niliyil paṭittuk koṇṭēyirukkum.  āciriyarkaḷ ciṟanta āciriyarkaḷākat tikaḻvar.  ecceytipaṟṟi māṇākkar viṉāviṉālum uṭaṉaṭiyāka , cintaṉikkiṭamiṉṟi aṭutta noṭiyil ataṟkuriya  viṭikaḷi āciriyar virintaḷippiṉ avar matippu naṉku uyarum eṉpatic collavum vēṇṭumā?
pala malarkaḷiluḷḷa naṟumaṇaṅkaḷi nāṭit teṉṟal celvatip pōl, eṅkeṅkē ceytikaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉavō aṅkellām ceṉṟu, kallātaṉavaṟṟik kaṟṟup putiya aṟiviṉip peṟutal moḻi āciriyariṉ kaṭamiyāka iruttal vēṇṭum.  ivvāṟu palvēṟu tuṟi aṟiviṉiyum peṟṟu, moḻi āciriyar oru naṭamāṭum kalik kaḷañciyamākat (Walking encyclopedia)  tikaḻntāl avariṭam takuvaḻi kāṇum tiṟaṉ (Resourcefulness)  ciṟappāka amiyum.  iṅṅaṉam tam aṟivuk kuṟivi akaṟṟum moḻiyāciriyarkaḷ,
	poṅkaril nuḻintu vāvi
	pukuntupaṅ kayamtu ḻāvip
 piṅkaṭi mayili mulli
	mallikip pantar tāvik
koṅkalar maṇaṅkūṭ ṭuṇṭu
	kuḷirntumel leṉṟu teṉṟal
aṅkaṅkē kalikaḷ tērum
	aṟivaṉpōl iyaṅkam aṉṟē12
eṉṟu  parañcōti muṉivar pakarvatu pōṉṟu āciriyarkaḷ aṅkaṅkē kalikaḷ tērum aṟivaṉpōl pala paṉṉūlkaḷik kaṟṟuk kaṟṟup pāṭapporuḷip pala kōṇaṅkaḷil naṉkaṟintu pāṭam naṭattiṉāl avarkaṭku nallācāṉ eṉṟa naṟpeyar tāṉāka vantamiyum.  mēṉāṭukaḷil māṇākkarkaḷ āciriyarkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷum muṟikaḷiyum avarkaḷ kaṟpikkum tiṟaṉiyum aḷantaṟiyum pōkku taliyeṭuttu varukiṉṟatu. aḵtu iṉṉum peruvaḻakkāka naṭimuṟikku varavilli13
payiṟṟaliṉ aṭippaṭi vitikaḷi naṭimuṟiyil kiyāḷal:  innūlil palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa payiṟṟu muṟikaḷit tāymoḻi yāciriyarkaḷ pāṅkuṭaṉ mēṟkoḷḷa muyalavēṇṭum.  ēṭṭuc curikkāy kaṟikkutavātu eṉṟu avaṟṟit taḷḷiviṭalākātu.  mutalil māṇākkarkaḷi naṉkaṟintu avarkaḷiṉ uḷappōkkukaḷ, taṉit tiṟamikaḷ, īṭupāṭṭup paṇpukaḷ, ceyalkaḷ ivaṟṟiyellām karuttil koṇṭu teḷivāy eḷimiyāy eṭuttuk kūṟi māṇākkar kaḷiṭam taṉṉampikkiyi vaḷarac ceytāl, avartam toṇṭu viḻumiya toṇṭāka amiyum.  avarum eḻuttaṟivitta iṟivaṉākap pōṟṟappeṟuvar.  payiṟcik kallūrikaḷil payilum māṇākka āciriyarkaḷ toṭakka nilip paḷḷi mutal kallūri, palkalik kaḻakamvari tamakkuk kaṟpitta āciriyar kaḷip piṉṉōkki ārāyntāl avarkaḷuḷ cilarē nallāciriyar kaḷākak kāṭci aḷippar.
ivviṭattil pāratattil oru nikaḻcciyic cintittal poruttamāka amiyum.
	yātumoṉ ṟaṟiyā eṉṉi
	ivaṉavā tiviyeṉ ṟinta

	viṉmutal paṭikaḷ yāvum
tītaṟat tanta uṇmit
	teyvam nī.14
eṉṟu turōṇari vāyāra vāḻttukiṉṟāṉ arccuṉaṉ.  yātu maṟiyātirunta eṉṉi, `ivaṉi vellal yārkkum aritu eṉa ulakam matikkumāṟu ceyta teyvam tāṅkaḷ eṉak kūṟip pukaḻkiṉṟāṉ, pārttaṉ.  nallāciriyarkaḷ yāvariyum māṇākkarkaḷ arccuṉaṉ turōṇarip pukaḻtal pōl pukaḻvar eṉpatu tiṇṇam.
irāma kātiyilum oru nikaḻcci.  cūriya vamicattu aracarkaḷiṉ perumiyic caṉakaṉukku eṭutturikkiṉṟār catānanta muṉivar.  irāma ilakkuvariṉ piṟappu vaḷarppic caṉakaṉukkuk kūṟuṅkāl avarkaḷ kalvi kaṟṟataṉiyum kuṟip piṭukiṉṟār muṉivar.

 maṟiyōṭu vittuivari
	vaḷarttāṉu vaciṭṭaṉkāṇ.15
eṉpatu ivar vākku.  peṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavaṉ tacarataṉ; uṭalukku vēṇṭiya itaṅkaḷi nāṭic ceytavaṉ.  āṉāl, uḷḷattip paṇpaṭutti ellāk kalikaḷilum vallavarkaḷākac ceyta perumi vaciṭṭaṉic cārntatu;  avaṉē ivartam `ñāṉattanti yāvāṉ eṉṟu vaciṭṭaṉukkup perumi taruvati īṇṭuk kāṇalām.  āciriyarkaḷ vaciṭṭaṉ niliyil uḷḷaṉar eṉṟa karuttu cintittaṟkuriyatu.  ēṉiya pāṭa āciriyarkaḷ māṇākkarkaḷiṉ aṟivip perukkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka irukka, ilakkiyam kaṟpikkum tamiḻmoḻi āciriyarkaḷ ivartam uḷḷattip paṇpaṭuttupavarkaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉar.  iḵtu ivartam perukum pēṟākum; iṟivaṉiṉ tiruvuḷḷak kuṟippumākum.  itaṉit tāymoḻiyāciriyarkaḷ cintittut tamatu kaṭamikaḷi-poṟuppukaḷi-uṇarvārkaḷāka.
